 to leave the land were free.

===Chinese===
这些朝圣的旅人困于风涛，
在海上颠簸已久，
待十四天期满，
便可自由离境。

===Original===
 To them by the host were given
 gifts of gold, bright shining;
 By the help of his son’s great kindness
 he hoped a lasting friendship to be winning.

===Chinese===
主人赠给了他们
灿然生辉的黄金；
靠着儿子的一番厚意，
他指望赢得一份长存的情谊。

===Original===
Hagen his maidens never
 henceforth unthought-of leaves;
 Kindly doth he teach them
 ofttimes to bathe in the waves.

===Chinese===
哈根从未
冷落过他的少女们；
他温存地教她们
时时到波浪里沐浴。

===Original===
 He showed himself most loving,
 ever for them caring;
 Rich clothes to them were given,
 and wise beyond his years was all his bearing.

===Chinese===
他显得极尽体贴，
时时为她们挂心；
华美的衣裳赠给她们，
而他年纪轻轻，举止已睿智过人。

===Original===
Now was the youth beginning
 to be a man well-grown.
 He ever showed his kinsmen
 the skill to warriors known;

===Chinese===
如今这少年
渐渐长成魁伟的男儿。
他不断向族人们展示
武士所熟稔的身手；

===Original===
 Whate’er a knight befitteth
 with hand and weapon doing.
 In the land of his father, Sigeband,
 his mighty sway he soon to all was showing.

===Chinese===
凡与骑士相称的本领，
他都亲手持械演习。
在他父亲西格班德的国土上，
他很快向众人显出了威势。

===Original===
Hagen was ever learning
 what doth a king beseem.
 He who of knights is leader
 must ever free from shame

===Chinese===
哈根始终在修习
君王应有的器度。
身为骑士之首的人，
必须永远不染耻辱、

===Original===
 And every stain be living;
 this earns fair women’s praises.
 So gentle was he truly
 that every one with wonder on him gazes.

===Chinese===
不沾任何污点地立身；
这才赢得佳丽们的称美。
他确实温文如此，
人人都惊奇地注视着他。

===Original===
Brave he was and daring,
 (such is the olden song,)
 And ever was he ready
 to right his neighbor’s wrong.

===Chinese===
他英武而果敢，
（古老的歌谣如此传唱，）
并且随时准备
为邻人昭雪冤屈。

===Original===
 He high upheld his honor
 in all things, never fearing;
 Throughout the land, his praises
 were spoken and were sung in all men’s hearing.

===Chinese===
他在一切事上高扬自己的荣誉，
无所畏惧；
他的美名传遍全境，
在众人耳畔被讲述、被歌唱。

===Original===
In a waste he grew to manhood,
 that youthful son of a king,
 Wild beasts his only fellows;
 but none so quick could spring

===Chinese===
他是在荒野中长大成人的，
那王家的娇子，
唯有野兽与之为伴；
但没有哪只野兽能纵跃得

===Original===
 That they to flee were able,
 if he for them was striving.
 I ween both he and his maidens
 had wonders seen, while by the waters living.

===Chinese===
快到足以逃脱，
只要他一心追赶。
我料想他和他的少女们
在水泽之畔度日时，都见过种种奇观。

===Original===
Rightly his name was Hagen;
 but later men did own
 He was “of kings the Devil;”
 so came he to be known

===Chinese===
他的本名是哈根；
但后来人们公认
他是「诸王中的魔王」；
于是这名号渐渐传开，

===Original===
 In every land and kingdom,
 such was his strength in fighting.
 The bold and wild young Hagen
 well did earn his name, his foemen smiting.

===Chinese===
传遍每一片土地与王国，
只因他战力如此雄强。
勇猛狂野的少年哈根
痛击仇敌，果真不负这名号。

===Original===
He oft was begged by his kinsmen
 that he a wife would take;
 One so fair was near him
 that none had need to seek

===Chinese===
族人们常常恳求他
及早娶妻；
他身边就有一位如此姝丽的佳人，
无人再需寻访

===Original===
 A fairer or a lovelier,
 all earthly kingdoms over.
 He himself had taught her;
 with her in sorrow grown, and now her lover.

===Chinese===
比她更美、更可爱的人，
纵览普天之下的国度。
是他亲手教养成她；
在忧患中与她一同长大，如今成了她的恋人。

===Original===
She bore the name of Hilda;
 from India she had come,
 And love she oft had shown him
 under their wretched doom,

===Chinese===
她的名字是希尔达；
她从印度而来；
在那悲惨的命运之下，
她屡屡向他吐露爱意，

===Original===
 Since, in their early childhood,
 he in the cave had found her.
 None better need he wish for,
 or seek in any land the heavens under.

===Chinese===
只因早在幼年之时，
他在岩窟中寻得了她。
普天之下，他无须企盼更好的人，
也无须向任何国度另行寻访。

===Original===
His father bade him hasten
 to be knighted with the sword,
 With a hundred of his vassals.
 He gave, with kindly word,

===Chinese===
他的父亲催他赶紧
受剑册封为骑士，
连同他的一百名封臣。
他和颜悦色地赐下

===Original===
 To him and to his maidens,
 for clothes and horses needed,
 A thousand marks of silver.
 Hagen said that the will of his father should be heeded.

===Chinese===
给他本人与他的少女们、
以充衣装马匹之需的
一千马克白银。
哈根说，父亲的意愿理当遵行。

===Original===
The news of this was bruited
 through many a prince’s land;
 And the day when it should happen
 all did understand.

===Chinese===
这消息传扬开来，
响彻诸位王公的领地；
行礼当在哪一天举行，
人人都了然于心。

===Original===
 Soon the king’s great kindness
 from all won praises golden
 In a year and three days after
 the festival of knighting them was holden.

===Chinese===
不久，国王的宽仁
从众人处赢得金色的颂扬。
一年又三日之后，
为他们册封骑士的庆典如期举行。

===Original===
For this the knights made busy,
 glad to be bidden there.
 Soon they made them bucklers,
 bright and painted fair;

===Chinese===
骑士们为此奔忙，
欣然应邀赴会。
他们很快制成圆盾，
鲜亮而彩绘精美；

===Original===
 In making showy saddles
 the workmen were not idle;
 With gold both red and shining
 the breastplate was bedecked, as was the bridle.

===Chinese===
匠人们赶制华鞍，
也不曾偷闲；
红金与亮金
装点了胸甲，也装点了辔头。

===Original===
Upon a broad green meadow
 the guests of the mighty king
 Were bidden then to gather.
 He left not anything

===Chinese===
在一片开阔的绿茵草地上，
那威势赫赫的国王的宾客们
受邀齐聚一堂。
凡他们可能向他求取的，

===Original===
 That they from him could ask for;
 seats were spread in order,
 And many guests soon after
 were seen to ride to his land from every border.

===Chinese===
他一概没有短少；
座席井然铺陈，
其后不久，又有许多宾客
从四面八方骑马驰赴他的国土。

===Original===
To those from far now ready
 the sword with him to bear
 Fighting-gear was given,
 that beseemed them well to wear.

===Chinese===
对那些远道而来、
预备与他一同受剑的人，
都发给了战具，
穿戴起来甚是相称。

===Original===
 They who from other kingdoms
 into his land were faring
 A thousand men were reckoned;
 to give them clothes and steeds he was not sparing.

===Chinese===
从别的王国
来到他邦土的人们，
计有一千之众；
他赠衣赐马，毫不吝惜。

===Original===
Unto his friends then said he:
 “If now you deem it fit
 That men a king should call me,
 it therefore seemeth meet

===Chinese===
他对朋友们这般说道：
「倘若你们如今认为
世人当以国王称呼我，
那么似乎理当

===Original===
 That she my heart holds dearest
 a crown with me be wearing;
 Never shall I rest happy
 until, for her love to me, she this is sharing.”

===Chinese===
让我心中最珍爱的人
与我同戴王冠；
否则我永不心安意满，
直到她为着对我的爱，同享此荣。」

===Original===
Then asked of him his followers
 who might the lady be,
 Who, riding proudly before them,
 they at court should see?

===Chinese===
于是随从们问他，
那位淑女究竟是谁，
将骑马昂然行在他们前头，
在宫廷之上与他们相见？

===Original===
 He said: “Her name is Hilda,
 in India once living;
 To me and to my kindred
 she, as our queen, no shame will e’er be giving.”

===Chinese===
他说：「她的名字是希尔达，
曾居于印度之地；
她作我们的王后，
绝不致让我与我的族人蒙羞。」

===Original===
Well pleased was now his mother,
 when she the tidings had,
 That they thought to crown the lady;
 his father, too was glad.

===Chinese===
他的母亲此刻满心欢喜，
当她听闻这消息，
说众人有意为那位淑女加冕；
他的父亲也同样高兴。

===Original===
 Of them was she so worthy,
 that high in heart they set her.
 With him the sword was taken
 by full six hundred; the number e’en was greater.

===Chinese===
她如此当得起他们，
以致他们把她珍重奉于心头。
与他一同受剑的
足足六百人；数目甚至更多。

===Original===
As is the way of Christians,
 both of them were bid
 First for the crown to be hallowed;
 this at once they did.

===Chinese===
依照基督徒的礼俗，
他们两人先受命
行加冕前的祝圣；
这事随即便照办了。

===Original===
 King Hagen with Queen Hilda
 in state were soon seen riding;
 Many games of knighthood
 were played at court by his men, at Hagen’s bidding.

===Chinese===
哈根国王与希尔达王后
随即盛仪并辔而行；
他的部众奉哈根之命，
在宫廷上演了诸多骑士竞技。

===Original===
Sigeband, too, rode with them;
 high rose his heart as he went;
 He reckoned very little
 the wealth that must be spent.

===Chinese===
西格班德也与他们同骑；
一路行去，他心潮高扬；
须得耗散多少资财，
他全不放在心上。

===Original===
 When in jousts they had ridden,
 in ways most true and knightly,
 Then were pages busy
 to make the halls for the guests all fair and sightly.

===Chinese===
待他们以最纯正的骑士风度
驰骋比枪已毕，
侍童们便忙碌起来，
为宾客把厅堂装点得焕然可观。

===Original===
Seats were brought together,
 strong, and broad, and long,
 With stools besides, and tables.
 After the mass was sung,

===Chinese===
座席搬拢一处，
坚固、宽阔而修长，
另有坐凳与长案。
弥撒唱毕之后，

===Original===
 U-te, his wife, came riding,
 with women round her thronging;
 These the youthful warriors
 to gaze on, as they rode, right earnestly were longing.

===Chinese===
乌特——他的妻子——骑马而来，
女眷们簇拥在侧；
年轻的武士们
只切盼一睹她们驰马的丰姿。

===Original===
While the great King Sigeband
 sat by U-te’s side,
 And Hagen next to Hilda,
 all looked on with pride;

===Chinese===
伟大的西格班德国王
坐在乌特身侧，
哈根则紧挨着希尔达，
众人满怀自豪地望着；

===Original===
 And said, in his child-belovèd
 happy was their master.
 Before them, while at table,
 the throng was great; the clash of spears grew faster.

===Chinese===
都说道，主人得此爱子，
何等幸福。
席前人潮涌动；
长枪的交击声愈发急密。

===Original===
After the king of Ireland
 at the meal his fill had eat,
 By riders the grass was trodden;
 flowers to dust they beat

===Chinese===
爱尔兰国王
在席上酒足饭饱之后，
草地被骑手们踏遍；
花朵被他们

===Original===
 With rude and heavy trampling,
 while in uproar riding.
 The men best known for bravery,
 before the fair, in knightly jousts were leading.

===Chinese===
粗重地践踏成尘，
他们在喧腾中纵马驰骋。
最负英名的武士们
在佳人面前领队比枪。

===Original===
Four and twenty warriors,
 bearing well the shield,
 Over the plain came riding;
 bold were they in the field,

===Chinese===
二十四名武士，
娴熟地擎着圆盾，
驰马掠过平野；
他们在战阵中剽悍，

===Original===
 And now in many a struggle
 all their strength were spending.
 ’Twas done in sight of the ladies,
 and hard it was of their games to make an ending.

===Chinese===
此刻正把全身气力
倾洒进一次次交锋。
这一切都当着淑女们的面；
他们的游戏实在难以收场。

===Original===
The brave young son of Sigeband
 himself in the onset rode.
 Not loth was she to gaze on him
 who her with hope had wooed;

===Chinese===
西格班德勇敢的少年之子
亲自策马冲入战团。
她并不厌于凝望他——
那曾满怀指望向她求爱的人；

===Original===
 That she to him was friendly
 in a far-off land forsaken,
 For this would he reward her.
 No truer knight the sword had ever taken.

===Chinese===
只因当年在遥远之地失群被弃，
她待他一片友善，
为此他定要报偿她。
再无骑士比他更真诚地佩剑受封。

===Original===
Amid the throngs here riding,
 one thro’ the dust might see
 Men whose birth was princely,
 in number twelve and three;

===Chinese===
在这驰骋的人群之中，
透过飞扬的尘土，可以望见
出身王公之家的贵人，
共计一十五位；

===Original===
 The Christian and the heathen
 to him their fiefs were owing,
 And honor now, right heartily,
 to Sigeband and Hagen they were showing.

===Chinese===
基督徒与异教徒
都向他称臣领受封地，
此刻正满怀诚敬，
向西格班德与哈根致敬。

===Original===
Long those high times lasted;
 their mirth, how loud it rose!
 With crowding and with shouting
 great the bustle grows.

===Chinese===
盛会延续了许久；
欢声何其喧腾！
人潮拥挤，欢呼阵阵，
喧嚣愈发高涨。

===Original===
 The king now bade the champions
 to end the strife so heated;
 And leave to them was given
 that they beside the ladies should be seated.

===Chinese===
国王此刻命众豪杰
停止这场炽烈的争锋；
并赐予他们恩典，
准其安坐到淑女们的身旁。

===Original===
Before his friends and kinsmen
 then spake King Sigeband:
 “Unto my dear son Hagen
 give I now my land,

===Chinese===
当着友人与族人之面，
西格班德国王开言：
「我如今将我的国土
授予我亲爱的儿子哈根，

===Original===
 With the dwellers and the strongholds,
 be they far or nearer.
 Let all my trusty liegemen
 have him now for their lord, and hold none dearer.”

===Chinese===
连同黎民与堡砦，
无论或远或近。
愿我所有忠信的封臣
从此奉他为主，再无更亲之人。」

===Original===
As soon as his father, Sigeband,
 his sway did thus forego,
 Hagen his lands and castles
 began in fief to bestow;

===Chinese===
他的父亲西格班德
一经如此让出权柄，
哈根便开始
以封地颁授他的土地与城堡；

===Original===
 This he did right freely,
 and to those to whom he gave them
 He seemed so true and worthy
 that they indeed from him would gladly have them.

===Chinese===
他行事极为慷慨，
凡从他手中受封的人，
都觉得他如此真诚可贵，
甘愿领受他的封赏。

===Original===
As by feudal law is rightful,
 many stretched the hand
 To the youthful king in fealty.
 To all, from every land,

===Chinese===
依照封建法统的正当之理，
许多人向这位年轻的国王
伸手宣誓效忠。
对所有从各地而来、

===Original===
 Or far or near, then gave he
 clothes and riches hoarded.
 A feast so freely given
 would now the poor not harm, and with thanks be rewarded.

===Chinese===
无论远近的人们，
他都赠以衣装与积储的财货。
如此慷慨赐予的盛宴，
不至损及穷人，反赢得他们的谢忱。

===Original===
At court now dwelt the maidens
 who had before been brought
 With him within those borders;
 of these one now was sought,

===Chinese===
先前随他
进入这片疆土的少女们，
如今就住在宫廷里；
其中一位如今被选中，

===Original===
 And sent to the king and Hilda;
 there she soon was dwelling.
 The maid it was from Iserland;
 of one more fair to see none e’er was telling.

===Chinese===
送到国王与希尔达那里；
不久她便在那里安居。
她正是来自伊瑟兰（Iserland）的少女；
再无人说起过比她更美的人。

===Original===
A princely youth soon wooed her,
 who saw the maiden fair
 Beside the king’s fair daughter.
 Soothly might he swear

===Chinese===
一位王公少年不久便向她求爱，
他见这位美丽的少女
侍立于国王的爱女之侧。
他尽可以郑重起誓：

===Original===
 That she by right was worthy
 to be of a crown the wearer.
 She had erst been Hilda’s playmate;
 of widespread lands she now became the sharer.

===Chinese===
她的确当得起
承戴一顶王冠。
她从前是希尔达的游伴；
如今成了辽阔疆土的共享者。

===Original===
At last the guests were scattered,
 and all now left the king.
 That high-born lady also
 men did straightway bring

===Chinese===
宾客们终于四散而去，
人人都辞别了国王。
那位高贵的淑女也
被即刻护送启程，

===Original===
 Into the land of Norway,
 to her youthful lord’s kind keeping.
 After her heavy sorrows,
 blest with hope, she knew no more of weeping.

===Chinese===
前往挪威之地，
交到她年轻夫君的温存照拂之中。
历经重重哀伤之后，
她满怀希望，再不识哭泣为何物。

===Original===
Now, throughout all Ireland,
 did Hagen his sway begin.
 If ever among his lieges
 a deed of wrong was seen,

===Chinese===
如今，在整个爱尔兰，
哈根开始执掌大权。
只要他的封臣之中
出现一桩恶行，

===Original===
 At once for this the doer
 must pay with pain well dreaded;
 Of such, within a twelvemonth,
 eighty or more were for evil deeds beheaded.

===Chinese===
行恶者立刻就得
领受令人胆寒的严惩；
一年之内，
这等恶徒有八十余人因恶行伏诛被斩。

===Original===
An inroad made he later
 into the lands of his foes.
 He spared the poor, and brought not
 flames, to add to their woes;

===Chinese===
后来他挥师进袭
仇敌们的国土。
他饶过穷人，不曾
纵火以添他们的困厄；

===Original===
 But if with pride and rudeness
 he was by any treated,
 He quick laid waste their strongholds,
 and deadly wounds in bitter wrath he meted.

===Chinese===
但凡有人以倨傲无礼相待，
他便迅速荡平其堡砦，
在狂怒之下
施以致命的创伤。

===Original===
When it came to fighting,
 he was a goodly knight.
 Of heroes high in breeding
 he soon brought low the might;

===Chinese===
论及临阵交战，
他是位出色的骑士。
出身高贵的豪杰，
他不久便挫其锋芒；

===Original===
 To all he showed his bravery,
 whether far or near him.
 Of kings was he the Devil;
 in truth his many foes might greatly fear him.

===Chinese===
无论身侧还是远方，
他向众人昭示了勇武。
他正是诸王中的魔王；
确实，他的众多仇敌尽该惧他三分。

===Original===
The life he led was happy,
 nor of gladness asked he more.
 His wife, from far-off India,
 to her lord and master bore

===Chinese===
他过着幸福的生活，
于欢愉别无所求。
他的妻子，来自遥远的印度，
为她的夫君诞下了

===Original===
 A fair and lovely daughter;
 she also, like her mother,
 Bore the name of Hilda;
 well known is her tale to us, and to many another.

===Chinese===
一位美丽可爱的女儿；
她也如母亲一般，
取名希尔达；
她的故事为我们所熟知，也为许多旁人知晓。

===Original===
Wild Hagen bade his maidens
 so to rear the child,
 That the sun ne’er shone upon her;
 nor were rough winds wild

===Chinese===
狂野的哈根吩咐他的少女们
这样来养育这孩子：
不让太阳照上她的脸；
也不许狂野的烈风

===Original===
 Oft allowed to touch her.
 She was by ladies guarded,
 And cared for by her kinsmen;
 most wisely was the trust to them awarded.

===Chinese===
时时得以袭扰她。
她由贵妇们护卫，
由族人照料；
这份信托付与他们，最为明智。

===Original===
Before twelve years were ended
 the fair and well-born maid
 Was comely more than any,
 and her name was widely spread;

===Chinese===
不满十二年期满，
这位美丽的高贵少女
容颜已冠绝众人，
名声也广为传扬；

===Original===
 Rich and high-born princes
 gladly would have sought her,
 And earnestly were thinking
 how they could win wild Hagen’s lovely daughter.

===Chinese===
富贵的王公们
都巴望前来求娶，
并殚精竭虑地思量
如何赢得狂野哈根的爱女。

===Original===
One of these same princes
 in Denmark had his home,
 Within the land of Waleis.
 When the tale to him had come

===Chinese===
这些王公之中有一位，
家居丹麦，
在瓦莱斯（Waleis）之地。
当那桩传闻

===Original===
 About this lovely maiden,
 his longing ne’er would leave him;
 But he was scorned by Hagen,
 who swore of life and name he would bereave him.

===Chinese===
说到这位可爱少女，
传到他耳中，他的渴念便再难排遣；
但他遭到哈根的轻鄙回绝，
哈根还发誓要夺去他的性命与名位。

===Original===
Whene’er to seek the maiden
 men were by wooers sent,
 In his pride, wild Hagen
 upon their death was bent.

===Chinese===
每逢求婚者们遣人
前来寻访这位少女，
倨傲的狂野哈根
便定要置来人于死地。

===Original===
 He to none would give her
 who than himself was weaker;
 Of the tale of the mighty Hagen,
 far and near, was every man the speaker.

===Chinese===
他绝不肯把她许给
任何比他孱弱的人；
关于强雄哈根的传说，
远近之人都争相谈论。

===Original===
He bade that more than twenty
 of those sent there be hung;
 None might wreak his anger,
 though sore his heart was wrung.

===Chinese===
他下令把二十余名
来使统统吊死；
无人能消他的怒火，
纵使有人为此心痛如绞。

===Original===
 When all had done their errand,
 for Hagen’s daughter suing,
 “Enough,” soon went the saying:
 “ ’Twere best that none should go for her a-wooing.”

===Chinese===
待所有人办完了
为哈根之女求亲的差事，
「够了」之说旋即传开：
「最好再无人前去向她求爱。」

===Original===
But still by high-born warriors
 the maid was not unsought.
 Let pride be ne’er so lofty,
 as we have long been taught,

===Chinese===
但这位少女仍不乏
出身高贵的武士前来求娶。
任凭傲气何等凌云，
正如我们久受的教诲，

===Original===
 There always is another
 with just as high a bearing;
 While to win her kindness
 his yearning grows, and his toil he is never sparing.

===Chinese===
总会另有其人，
气度同样高华；
为赢得她的芳心，
他渴望日增，辛劳也永不吝惜。

## Tale V How Wâ-te Was Sent to Ireland as a Suitor / 第五诗章 瓦特如何奉命前往爱尔兰求亲

===Original===
Hettel, king of the Hegelings, a suitor for Hilda, summons his vassals, Wâ-te, Horant, and Fru-te, and sends them to obtain her for his wife. They go, purporting to be merchants, carrying seven hundred warriors concealed in the hold of the ship. They send rich gifts to Hagen, and at the request of his daughter, Hilda, they are kindly received at court.

===Chinese===
黑格林人（Hegelings）之王黑特尔（Hettel）向希尔达（Hilda）求亲，召集封臣瓦特（Wâ-te）、霍兰特（Horant）与弗鲁特（Fru-te），派他们前去为他娶她为妻。三人假扮商人前往，船舱中暗藏七百武士。他们向哈根（Hagen）进献厚礼，又应其女希尔达之请，在宫廷受到殷勤接待。

===Original===
Hettel was lord in Daneland; to be its king he rose;
’Twas in the Sturmisch marches, as many a one well knows;
There abode his kindred, who ways of honor taught him.
Ortland also served him. His might and worth high fame with all soon brought him.

===Chinese===
黑特尔本是丹麦兰（Daneland）之主，后来登基为王；
那是在施图尔姆边区，世人多半知晓；
他的亲族世居彼处，以荣誉之道教诲于他。
奥特兰（Ortland）也臣服于他。他的威权与德望，不久便为他赢得众口交誉。

===Original===
One among his kinsfolk the name of Wâ-te bore;
He for his lands and castles fealty to Hettel swore.
As kinsman of his master, he careful teaching gave him
In all things good and worthy, and in his watchful care did ever have him.

===Chinese===
亲族中有一人，名唤瓦特；
他为自己的领地与城堡，向黑特尔宣誓效忠。
身为君主的尊长亲眷，他谆谆施教，
授以一切善良高贵之事，并将他时时置于自己的悉心看顾之下。

===Original===
A landed knight in Daneland was Wâ-te’s sister’s son,
The brave and upright Horant. Later his faith was shown
Unto his lord, King Hettel, who for his worth did crown him.
This to him he grudged not, but ever for a prince was glad to own him.

===Chinese===
丹麦兰有位拥有领地的骑士，是瓦特的外甥，
那勇敢正直的霍兰特。后来他的忠贞
彰显于其君主黑特尔王之前，君主因他的贤德为他授爵加冕。
他对此毫无怨言，反倒终生庆幸得奉这样一位君王。

===Original===
Hettel, rich and mighty, at Hegeling held his seat,
Not far from the lord of Ortland; this is true, I weet.
He there owned many castles, eighty at least or over;
They who these strongholds guarded in truest faith and honor held them ever.

===Chinese===
富有而威武的黑特尔，在黑格林坐拥王座，
离奥特兰之主不远；此话千真万确，我深知晓。
他在那里坐拥众多城堡，至少八十座犹不止；
守卫这些要塞的人们，以最真的忠义与荣誉长久保着它们。

===Original===
Lord he was of Friesland, its waters and its land;
Ditmarsh, as well as Waleis, were swayed by his kingly hand.
Hettel was truly mighty; his kinsmen they were many;
Bold was he and daring, and ’gainst his foes he plotted, well as any.

===Chinese===
他又是弗里西亚（Friesland）之主，统辖其海陆；
迪特马什（Ditmarsh）连同瓦莱斯（Waleis），都受他王者之手的节制。
黑特尔着实权势煊赫；他的亲族为数众多；
他勇武果敢，对付仇敌的谋略，不输当世任何豪杰。

===Original===
Hettel was an orphan, and so he felt the need
That he a wife should find him. To him, at last, were dead
Father as well as mother, who their lands had left him.
He friends in truth had many, yet found he much in life that of bliss bereft him.

===Chinese===
黑特尔是个孤儿，因而深感
有必要为自己觅一妻子。到他那时，父母
皆已亡故，只把领地留给了他。
他的朋友确实很多，却仍发觉人生中颇多事物，使他失去了欢愉。

===Original===
The best of these besought him some maiden’s love to seek,
Who of his birth was worthy. The knight did answer make:
“I here know none who fitly should be o’er the Hegelings seated,
Nor is there any lady who, brought from far, should as my queen be greeted.”

===Chinese===
其中最亲近的朋友们劝他寻访某位少女的爱情，
要与他的出身相称。骑士如此答道：
「我于此间实不知有哪位女子，配君临黑格林人之上，
也没有哪位淑媛，能从远方迎来、被尊为我的王后。」

===Original===
Then spake a knight of Nifland, Morunc, a youthful lord:
“I know of a lovely maiden, of whom I oft have heard;
She in truth is fairer than all on earth now living.
Her will we gladly sue for, that she her troth to you may soon be giving.”

===Chinese===
这时一位尼夫兰（Nifland）骑士开言，莫伦克（Morunc），一位年轻的贵胄：
「我知道一位可爱的少女，常听人说起她；
她实在比世上一切在世之人都更美。
我们乐意代您向她求亲，好让她早日把终身许给您。」

===Original===
Then quoth the king: “Who is she? her name I pray you tell.”
Then said Morunc: “ ’Tis Hilda, in Ireland she doth dwell;
Her father’s name is Hagen; King Ger was her forefather.
If to this land she cometh, your life will then be blissful altogether.”

===Chinese===
国王又道：「她是谁？请把她的名字相告。」
莫伦克答道：「她就是希尔达，居住在爱尔兰；
她父亲名叫哈根；格尔王是她的先祖。
她若来到这片国土，您的一生从此圆满幸福。」

===Original===
Then spake the young King Hettel: “I oft have heard it said,
Whoever woos this maiden her father’s wrath must dread.
Many a worthy suitor his life for her has ended;
But none among my vassals must meet his death for having me befriended.”

===Chinese===
年轻的黑特尔王说道：「我常听人说，
谁若向这位少女求亲，就必畏惧她父亲的震怒。
多少高贵的求亲者，为她断送了性命；
但我的封臣之中，谁也不得因为与我为友而送命。」

===Original===
Morunc quickly answered: “Then send to Horant’s land,
And bid that he come hither; he well doth understand
The ways and moods of Hagen, for often has he seen them.
Unless his help he gives you, ’twill come to nought, howe’er your friends demean them.”

===Chinese===
莫伦克马上答道：「那就遣使去霍兰特的封地，
召他来此；他深谙
哈根的习性与脾性，因为他屡屡见识。
没有他出手相助，任凭您的亲友如何尽力，也是徒劳。」

===Original===
He said: “Your will I follow, since she is so fair;
But if my friends shall seek her, yourself the suit must share;
And if unto your friendship the task I’ve trusted fitly,
Wealth shall you have and honor, when as the Hegeling’s queen she’s greeted rightly.”

===Chinese===
他说：「既然她如此美貌，我愿依从你的主意；
但若由我的友人们去求她，你自己也须分担这桩使命；
我既把这差事托付给你的情谊，
待她被堂堂正正迎为黑格林人的王后，你必得财富与荣耀。」

===Original===
He quickly sent out riders through the Danish land to haste;
By them was the mighty Horant, his nephew, found at last,
And to the court was bidden; to come must he be speedy,
Within seven days, not later, if he to help his lord in truth were ready.

===Chinese===
他火速派出骑士，疾驰遍及丹麦之地；
他们终于寻到豪杰霍兰特，他的外甥，
并宣他进宫；他必须从速前来，
七日之内，不得延误，若他当真愿意相助他的君主。

===Original===
When Horant met the heralds, and did their errand hear,
Then for friendly service himself he would not spare.
Right gladly did he listen to the bidding of his master;
But this, on a day thereafter, to him brought sorrow great, and sore disaster.

===Chinese===
霍兰特迎接信使，听罢他们的使命，
为尽朋友的情谊，他决不惜己身。
他欣然领受君主的吩咐；
然而日后某天，这事却给他带来巨大的悲伤与严酷的灾殃。

===Original===
To the court he soon went riding, with sixty of his men;
Of friends at home young Horant to take his leave was seen.
He then made haste the faster, when now the tale was told him
How he must help his master, if for a faithful knight he now would hold him.

===Chinese===
他很快便骑马赴宫廷，带着六十名部众；
人们看着年轻的霍兰特在家乡辞别亲友。
当来意向他言明，他如何必须帮助君主——
若君主仍视他为忠贞的骑士——他便愈发兼程赶路。

===Original===
Upon the seventh morning he came to Hettel’s land;
Decked in finest clothing was he and all his band.
The king to welcome Horant rode forth, most glad to greet him,
And saw that with him Fru-te, another Danish knight, was there to meet him.

===Chinese===
第七天早晨，他抵达黑特尔的国土；
他与全体随从都身披华服。
国王为迎霍兰特而出驰，喜不自胜，
又看见与他同来的还有弗鲁特，另一位丹麦骑士。

===Original===
Good news it was of their coming, of which all men now spoke;
Glad was the king to see them; from him a share it took
Of the deep and heavy sorrow which his heart was filling.
“Welcome, Cousin Fru-te!” cried he, the while he looked upon him smiling.

===Chinese===
他们到来的喜讯，如今人人传扬；
国王喜见他们；这喜讯也从他心头
卸下了几分深沉而沉重的忧伤。
「欢迎你，弗鲁特贤弟！」他望着来客含笑高呼。

===Original===
When Horant now with Fru-te before the king did stand,
Then he asked for tidings of their home in the Danish land.
Both of them now answered: “Not many days are ended
Since we in stormy battle with many deadly blows our lives defended.”

===Chinese===
当霍兰特与弗鲁特同立于国王面前，
他便询问他们丹麦故乡的消息。
两人齐声答道：「距今没有几天，
我们还在险恶的鏖战中，顶着致命的刀剑护卫性命。」

===Original===
He asked whence they had ridden from off the stormy field.
They said: “It was from Portugal, where the strife was held;
There the mighty ruler from fighting would not spare us;
Daily within our borders he did us wrong, and much ill-will did bear us.”

===Chinese===
他问他们从哪片鏖战之野驰来。
他们答道：「是从葡萄牙，战事就发生在那里；
那强横的君主不肯放过我们，不容我们休战；
他日日侵犯我们的边境，加害于我们，对我们深怀恶意。」

===Original===
The young King Hettel answered: “Now cast all care away;
I know that the aged Wâ-te will never yield the sway
He holds o’er the Sturmisch marches; he of the land is owner;
Who wins from him a castle will earn high praise and long be held in honor.”

===Chinese===
年轻的黑特尔王答道：「如今且把忧心统统抛开；
我知道年迈的瓦特绝不会放弃
他握有的施图尔姆边区的权柄；他是那片土地的主人；
谁能从他手里夺得一座城堡，必赢得盛誉，长久受人尊崇。」

===Original===
Within the roomy palace the guests then took their seats.
Both Horant and Sir Fru-te with thoughtless, merry wits,
Of the loves of high-born ladies began to gossip gaily.
To them the young king listened, and costly gifts he gave unto them freely.

===Chinese===
宽敞的宫殿里，宾客们随即落座。
霍兰特与弗鲁特爵士兴致盎然，妙语连珠，
兴致勃勃地闲谈起高贵淑媛们的情事。
年轻的国王听他们谈笑，慷慨地赠给他们贵重的礼物。

===Original===
Hettel turned to Horant, and thus to ask began:
“If aught hath reached your hearing, then tell me, if you can,
How ’tis with Lady Hilda, King Hagen’s lovely daughter?
To her would I send most gladly, and would that words of love from me were brought her.”

===Chinese===
黑特尔转向霍兰特，如此问道：
「你若有所耳闻，就请告诉我，倘若你能，
希尔达小姐、哈根王的爱女近况如何？
我很乐意遣人去她那里，恨不得把我的爱语捎给她。」

===Original===
The youthful knight then answered: “She is to me well known;
A maid so fair and lovely my eyes ne’er looked upon
As she, that maid of Ireland, Hilda, the rich and stately,
The daughter of wild Hagen; to wear a crown with you would befit her greatly.”

===Chinese===
年轻的骑士答道：「她与我相熟；
如此美丽可爱的少女，我的眼睛从未见过
像她那样：那位爱尔兰少女，希尔达，雍容华贵，
暴烈的哈根之女；她与您共戴王冠，再相称不过。」

===Original===
On this King Hettel asked him: “Now think you, can it be
That ever her lordly father will give this maid to me?
If I deemed he were so friendly, I would seek to win her,
And would reward him ever who gave to me his ready help to gain her.”

===Chinese===
于是黑特尔王问他：「依你看，可有指望，
她那威严的父亲终有一天肯把这少女许给我？
我若认为他这般友善，我便去设法赢得她，
并永远酬谢那肯助我赢得她的人。」

===Original===
“That can never happen,” to him young Horant said:
“No rider with this errand to Hagen need be sped.
To hasten thither boldly I feel, myself, no longing;
The man sent there to seek her is either slain with blows, or dies by hanging.”

===Chinese===
「那绝无可能，」年轻的霍兰特对他说：
「担着这桩差事的骑士，不必被派往哈根那里。
要我奋勇赶去那边，我自己也毫无热望；
被派去求她的人，不是死于乱刃之下，就是被绞死。」

===Original===
Then spake again King Hettel: “Not so for her I care;
To hang my trusty vassal should Hagen ever dare,
Then he, the king of Ireland, himself must death be facing.
Be he ne’er so boastful, he’ll find his rashness is to him no blessing.”

===Chinese===
黑特尔王又说道：「为了她，我不在乎这些；
哈根若敢绞死我忠勇的封臣，
那么他这爱尔兰之王，自己就得面对死亡。
任他如何自夸，他终将发现，鲁莽于他绝非幸事。」

===Original===
Then spake the knightly Fru-te: “If Wâ-te deigns to go
Unto the king of Ireland, to woo this maid for you,
Lucky will be our errand, and we shall bring the lady;
Or wounds throughout our bodies, e’en to the heart, to take shall we be ready.”

===Chinese===
骑士弗鲁特于是说：「倘若瓦特肯屈尊
前往爱尔兰国王那里，为您向这位少女求亲，
我们的差事便算走运，我们定会把小姐迎来；
不然，纵使浑身创痕，直抵心口，我们也甘愿领受。」

===Original===
Then said to him King Hettel: “My men I now will send
With word to the lord of Sturmland; I do not fear the end,
For Wâ-te will hasten gladly wherever I shall bid him.
Bring Irold, too, from Friesland, with all his men, for sorely do I need him.”

===Chinese===
黑特尔王对他说：「我这就派人
传话给施图尔姆兰（Sturmland）之主；我不怕这结局，
因为无论我差遣瓦特去哪里，他都欣然从命。
也把伊罗尔德（Irold）连同他的全部人马从弗里西亚带来，我正急需他。」

===Original===
His riders then went quickly into the Sturmisch land,
Where the brave old Wâ-te they found among his band.
Then the word they gave him, now to the king to betake him;
But Wâ-te felt great wonder, to know for what the Hegeling king did seek him.

===Chinese===
他的骑士们随即疾驰进入施图尔姆之地，
在部众之中寻见了豪勇的老瓦特。
他们传下口信，命他即刻去见国王；
瓦特心中大为诧异，不知黑格林人之王找他所为何事。

===Original===
He asked if it were needful to bring, when he should go,
His breastplate and his helmet, and any followers, too?
One of the heralds answered: “We did not hear it spoken
That he had need of fighters; for you alone did his words a wish betoken.”

===Chinese===
他问此去是否需要
带上胸甲和头盔，还要不要带些随从？
一名信使答道：「我们未曾听说
他需要武士；他的话里，只表达了请您一人前往的意愿。」

===Original===
Wâ-te would be going, but left behind a guard,
To care for lands and castles. Then taking horse, at his word,
Twelve of his followers only with him from home now started;
Wâ-te, the brave old warrior, at once on his way to court in haste departed.

===Chinese===
瓦特准备启程，但留下一支卫队，
照管领地与城堡。随后他一声号令，策马上路，
只有十二名部下随他离家启行；
豪勇的老将瓦特，当即兼程驰向宫廷。

===Original===
He reached the land of the Hegelings. When he now was seen,
As he came near Kampatille, but little sorrow, I ween,
Was felt by the kingly Hettel; with speed he went to greet him,
And thought of the kindly welcome he would give his friend, old Wâ-te, when he met him.

===Chinese===
他抵达黑格林人的国土。当他的身影一现，
行近坎帕蒂勒（Kampatille）时，据我看来，君王黑特尔
心中几乎不存一丝忧烦；他疾趋相迎，
盘算着与老友瓦特相见时，要如何殷勤接待。

===Original===
Right glad was he to see him; with hearty speech he says:
“Sir Wâ-te, thou art welcome; many are the days
Since I have looked upon you, when on our horses sitting,
Side by side together, we proudly met our foes with blows befitting.”

===Chinese===
见到他，国王满心欢喜；他热忱地说道：
「瓦特爵士，欢迎你；距离我上一次见到你，
已过去多少时日；那时我们跨坐马上，
并肩而行，以相称的刀剑骄傲地迎战仇敌。」

===Original===
Then answered him old Wâ-te: “Ever should good friends
Be glad to be together; that fight the better ends
Where, before the foeman, friends as one are fighting.”
Then by the hand he held him, to him his love and friendship warmly plighting.

===Chinese===
老瓦特答道：「好友相聚，
本该永远欢欣；面对仇敌，
众友如一人并力奋战的战斗，才有好的收场。」
说罢他握住国王的手，向他热烈地申明爱与情谊。

===Original===
They took their seats together, nor place to other gave.
Hettel, he was mighty, and Wâ-te, he was brave;
He yet was also haughty, and proud in all his bearing.
Hettel now was thinking how Wâ-te could be brought to Ireland to be faring.

===Chinese===
他们并肩落座，不把位子让给旁人。
黑特尔权势煊赫，瓦特则骁勇非凡；
瓦特却又性子倨傲，举止间处处透着骄矜。
黑特尔此刻思忖着，如何能促成瓦特前往爱尔兰之行。

===Original===
Then spake the knightly Hettel: “For this I bade thee come;
Need have I of riders, to send to Hagen’s home.
Truly I know of no one whom I would send the sooner
Than thee, my good friend Wâ-te, or who in this could bring me greater honor.”

===Chinese===
骑士黑特尔说道：「我召你来，正为此事；
我需要骑士，差往哈根的家邦。
说实话，我不知还有谁比你更让我愿遣，
我的好友瓦特，也没有谁能在此事上为我赢得更大的荣光。」

===Original===
Then said the aged Wâ-te: “Whatever I can do
To show my love and fealty, I’ll gladly do for you.
Herein I may be trusted, to be for you bold-hearted;
And to bring about your wishes, unless in this by death I should be thwarted.”

===Chinese===
年迈的瓦特说：「凡我所能做的、
能表明我的爱与忠忱之事，我都乐为您效劳。
这一点尽可信任我，我必为您奋不顾身，
定要成全您的心愿，除非死亡从中阻挠。」

===Original===
Then quoth the kingly Hettel: “Many friends have said
That if the mighty Hagen will my wooing heed,
And give to me his daughter, she, as my queen, would honor
Me and my kingdom also; my heart is bent as a wife and queen to own her.”

===Chinese===
君王黑特尔又道：「许多朋友说过，
倘若强横的哈根肯垂顾我的求亲，
把女儿许给我，她成为我的王后，
将为我和我的王国增光；我心意已决，要娶她为妻、立她为后。」

===Original===
Angrily spoke Wâ-te: “Whoever this has said
Would truly feel no sorrow if I this day were dead.
’Tis Fru-te, he of Denmark, I know it is no other,
Who to this has stirred you, to send me to the maid, your suit to further.

===Chinese===
瓦特怒道：「说这话的人，
倘若我今天死了，准不会有一丝难过。
那是弗鲁特，丹麦的那位，我知道不是别人，
是他撺掇您这样，派我去那少女那里，好推进您的求亲。

===Original===
“This young and lovely maiden is guarded now with care;
Horant and Fru-te also, who say she is so fair,
And speak to you her praises, must go with me to seek her.
Never shall I rest easy unless they strive with me your own to make her.”

===Chinese===
「这位年轻可爱的少女，如今被严加守护；
霍兰特和弗鲁特也一样，他们既把她夸得那般美，
又在您面前称颂她，就必须随我同去求娶。
不与他们一同出力使她归于您，我永远不得安心。」

===Original===
Both these faithful vassals King Hettel sent for soon;
To others good and trusty they also made it known,
That by their king and master they at court were wanted.
No more their thoughts men whispered, but freely spoke of the coming raid, undaunted.

===Chinese===
黑特尔王随即召来这两位忠心的封臣；
他们还向其他善良可靠的部属透露，
君王与主上有意召他们进宫。
人们不再窃窃私语，而是无所畏惧，公然谈论起即将到来的远征。

===Original===
When Wâ-te, the brave old warrior, did on Horant look,
And on the Danish Fru-te, how sharply then he spoke!
“Brave knights, may God reward you, to me you are so friendly,
And of my fame so careful, and my trip to court this time you help so kindly.

===Chinese===
当豪勇的老将瓦特目光扫向霍兰特，
又扫向丹麦的弗鲁特时，他言辞何等尖刻！
「勇敢的骑士们，愿上帝酬谢你们，你们待我如此友爱，
又这般顾惜我的名声，还这样好心地促成我此番进宫。

===Original===
“You are, forsooth, most willing that I this errand do;
But both of you are bounden with me thereon to go,
To serve the king, our master, even as our duty calleth.
He who my life endangers himself the risk must share, whate’er befalleth.”

===Chinese===
「你们当真巴不得我去办这桩差事；
但你们二人都责无旁贷，须与我同去，
一同侍奉我王、我们的君主，正如职责所召。
谁连累我涉险，谁就得同担风险，无论落下什么结局。」

===Original===
“For this I now am ready,” answered Horant the Dane;
“If leave the king will grant us, I then will shun no pain,
Nor aught of toil will grudge me. Only to see this lady,
For me and for my kinsman, were happiness enough, and bliss already.”

===Chinese===
「此事我已备好，」丹麦人霍兰特答道；
「只要国王恩准，我便不辞任何苦痛，
也不惜一切辛劳。单是得见那位小姐，
对我和我的亲眷而言，已是足够的幸福，甚至是至福。」

===Original===
“Then we ought,” said Fru-te, “to take upon our way
Seven hundred warriors. No man doth honor pay
To Hagen without grudging. He is overweening, truly;
If he thinks that he can crush us, he soon must lay aside his boasting wholly.

===Chinese===
「那么我们应当，」弗鲁特说，「在途中带上
七百名武士。人们向哈根行礼致敬，
无不心存勉强。他着实傲慢狂妄；
他若以为能压垮我们，就趁早彻底收起他的大话。

===Original===
“Sir king, you should bid your workmen a ship of cypress-wood
To build upon the river; strong must it be and good,
So your band of warriors shall shipwreck ne’er be ruing.
From timber white as silver the lofty masts your men must soon be hewing.

===Chinese===
「君主啊，您该吩咐工匠在河边
造一艘柏木船；它必须坚固精良，
好让您的一队武士永不为海难抱恨。
您的人还须尽快从白银般洁白的木材中，砍下高高的桅杆。

===Original===
“Also food for your fighters you must now bespeak;
And bid that men be busy helmets for us to make,
And hauberks strong for many; when we these are wearing,
Then wild Hagen’s daughter we shall the easier win by craft and daring.

===Chinese===
「您还须即刻为您的武士们置办粮秣；
吩咐匠人们赶制头盔，
并为众人锻打坚固的锁子甲；待我们披挂整齐，
便更容易凭智谋与胆魄，赢得暴烈的哈根之女。」

===Original===
“Also my nephew Horant, who is shrewd and wise,
Must go with us as a shopman; (I grudge him not his guise)
There must he to the ladies be clasps and arm-bands selling,
With gold and costly jewels; thus greater trust in us will they be feeling.

===Chinese===
「还有我的外甥霍兰特，他机敏又睿智，
必须扮作店伙随我们同去；（我不委屈他这身装扮）
他要在那里向淑媛们兜售饰扣与臂环，
连同黄金与贵重的珠宝；如此她们对我们便会更添信任。

===Original===
“For sale we also must carry weapons and clothing, too;
And since wild Hagen’s daughter it is such risk to woo,
That only now by fighting one can hope to wed her,
Let Wâ-te choose the warriors to go with him, and home to the king to lead her.”

===Chinese===
「我们还须带上兵刃与衣裳，以供出售；
既然求娶暴烈的哈根之女如此凶险，
如今唯有凭战斗才有望娶她，
那就由瓦特挑选同去的武士，好把她带回家、交给国王。」

===Original===
Then spake the aged Wâ-te: “A shop I cannot keep;
Not often doth my money in coffers idly sleep;
My lot I’ve shared with fighters, and that I still am doing;
Therein I am not skilful, that I to ladies gew-gaws should be showing.

===Chinese===
年迈的瓦特说道：「店铺我可是开不来；
我的钱财也不常在钱箱里闲睡；
我一生与战士同甘苦，如今依然如此；
向淑媛们兜售小玩意儿，这我可不在行。」

===Original===
“But since my nephew Horant on me this task has laid,
He knows full well that Hagen will never yield the maid:
He prides himself on owning the strength of six and twenty;
If he shall learn of our wooing, our hope to leave his land will be but scanty.

===Chinese===
「但既然我的外甥霍兰特把这差事加在我头上，
他自然深知哈根绝不肯交出那少女：
他自负身兼二十六人的膂力；
他一旦得知我们的求亲，我们想活着离开他的国土，希望便渺茫。

===Original===
“Good king, now let us hasten, but bid that first our hull
With a deck of deal be covered; let it, below, be full
Of knights both strong and doughty, who shall help be giving,
If ever the wild King Hagen forbids that we shall leave his kingdom, living.

===Chinese===
「好王上，让我们从速着手，但请先吩咐给我们的船身
铺上杉木甲板；让甲板之下满载
强悍勇猛的骑士，好随时出手相助，
倘若暴烈的哈根王胆敢不许我们活着离开他的国度。」

===Original===
“Of these brave knights a hundred, with outfits good for war,
Unto the land of Ireland we in our ships must bear;
There shall my nephew Horant in his shop be seated,
Keeping two hundred near him; thus shall the ladies’ coming be awaited.

===Chinese===
「这一百名骁勇的骑士，连同齐整的战备，
我们须用船载往爱尔兰；
我的外甥霍兰特将在那里坐镇店铺，
身边守着两百人；如此静候淑媛们光临。」

===Original===
“Your men must also build us barges strong and wide,
To carry food and horses, and to sail our ships beside:
Enough for a year or longer we must take to feed us;
And we will say to Hagen that to leave our land King Hettel did forbid us,

===Chinese===
「您的人还须为我们造些坚固宽敞的驳船，
装运粮草与马匹，并随同我们的海船出航：
我们要备足一年或更久的口粮；
我们将对哈根说，黑特尔王不许我们离开自己的国土，

===Original===
“And that our lord and master great wrong to us hath wrought.
Then with our gifts so costly we often shall be brought
To Hagen and to Hilda, where they their court are keeping;
Our gifts shall make us welcome, and kindness from the king shall we be reaping.

===Chinese===
「还说我们的君上与主上，加诸我们深冤大屈。
随后，凭着我们那些厚礼，我们便会常常
被引到哈根与希尔达驻跸的宫廷；
礼物会让我们备受欢迎，我们也必将收获国王的恩遇。」

===Original===
“We then the tale will tell him, we wretched outlaws are;
And thus at once the pity of Hagen we shall share.
To us, poor homeless wanderers, shelter will be granted,
And in his land King Hagen thenceforth will see that nought by us is wanted.”

===Chinese===
「到时我们再向他诉说，我们是落难的亡命人；
如此便能立刻赢得哈根的怜悯。
我们这些无家可归的流浪者，会被赐予栖身之所，
而哈根王此后也会留意，让我们在他的国中一无所缺。」

===Original===
Hettel asked his warriors: “My friends, I pray you tell,
Since you to go are willing, how soon you hence will sail?”
“So soon as comes the summer, and May with gladsome weather,”
They said, “we shall be ready, and, riding again to court, will we come hither.

===Chinese===
黑特尔问他的武士们：「朋友们，请告诉我，
你们既已愿意前往，何时扬帆启程？」
「等到夏日来临，五月带来怡人的天气，」
他们答道，「我们便一切就绪，再策马前来宫廷聚齐。

===Original===
“Meanwhile must men be making whatever we shall need﻿—
Sails and also rudders, well-made, and that with speed,
Barges wide, and galleys, to bear us to our haven;
So the swell of the waters shall stir us not, nor make us sick or craven.”

===Chinese===
「这期间须让匠人赶制我们所需的一切——
船帆与船舵，务求精良，而且要快，
还有宽敞的驳船与战船，载我们驶向港湾；
这样水波的起伏便撼不动我们，不致让我们晕眩或胆寒。」

===Original===
King Hettel said: “Ride quickly, now, to your land and home.
For horses and for clothing no cost to you shall come;
For you and all your followers such outfit shall be ready,
That you no shame shall suffer, whenever you are seen by any lady.”

===Chinese===
黑特尔王说：「即刻启程吧，回你们的封地去。
马匹与衣装的花销，一概不用你们破费；
为你和你的全部随从，行装都会置办妥帖，
无论何时被哪位淑媛瞧见，你们都不会蒙羞。」

===Original===
When he his leave had taken, Wâ-te to Sturmland rode;
Horant and with him Fru-te followed in hurried mood,
Back to the land of Denmark, where they held the lordship.
To help their master Hettel they thought could never be to them a hardship.

===Chinese===
辞别之后，瓦特策马回了施图尔姆兰；
霍兰特与弗鲁特随后也行色匆匆，
返回丹麦的封地，他们在那里执掌统治。
在他们看来，相助主上黑特尔，永远不会是苦差。

===Original===
Then, in his home, King Hettel let his will be known;
Of shipwrights and of workmen idle was not one.
While the ships were building to do their best they hastened;
The beams that met together, were with bands of silver strongly fastened.

===Chinese===
于是在自己的家邦，黑特尔王颁下旨意；
造船的工匠与杂役，没有一个闲着。
造船期间，人人奋力赶工；
相接的船梁，都用银箍牢牢加固。

===Original===
All the spars and mast-trees, they were strong and good;
Red gold, and brightly shining, was laid on the rudder-wood,
And like to fire was glowing: wealth their master blesses.
When time it was for leaving, the men their tasks had done, and won high praises.

===Chinese===
所有的桅桁与桅木，全都坚固精良；
赤金灿灿生辉，敷在舵木之上，
灼灼如火光闪耀：财富佑护着它们的主上。
到了启程之日，工匠们早已完工，赢得了高度的赞誉。

===Original===
The ropes that held the anchors came from a far-off strand,
Brought from the shores of Araby; never on sea or land,
Before that day or after, had any man seen better:
So might the men of the Hegelings easier make their way o’er the deep sea-water.

===Chinese===
系锚的缆绳来自遥远的滩岸，
产自阿拉比（Araby）的海滨；无论海上还是陆上，
在那日之前或之后，谁也不曾见过更好的：
黑格林人凭此便可更轻易地横渡深深的海水。

===Original===
They who the sails were making worked late, and early rose;
For the king had bid them hurry. For making these they chose
Silken stuff from Abalie, as good as could be brought them.
Truly far from idle were, in those days, the busy hands that wrought them.

===Chinese===
缝制船帆的人们晚睡早起；
因为国王命他们加紧赶制。选来做帆的
是阿巴利（Abalie）来的丝绸，好得无以复加。
那些日子里，缝帆的巧手确实一刻不得清闲。

===Original===
Can any one believe it? They had the anchors made
Of purest beaten silver. The heart of the king was led
Strongly now to wooing; no rest would he be knowing,
Nor of his men was sparing, until the day when they should thence be going.

===Chinese===
有谁能相信？他们连船锚
都用最纯的锻银铸成。国王的心
如今被求亲之念牢牢牵引；他不得安宁，
也不肯让部属歇息，直到启程的那一天。

===Original===
Well-framed, with heavy planking, now the ships were seen,
Sound ’gainst war and weather. Then word was sent to the men,
That to seek the lovely lady they must soon be faring.
This was told to no one but those who the trust of the king were rightly sharing.

===Chinese===
如今只见船已造好，骨架严整、板材厚重，
足以抵御战火风浪。随后传令给众武士，
说他们不久便要启程，去求娶那位可爱的少女。
这消息只告知那些真正堪受国王信任的人。

===Original===
Wâ-te to meet King Hettel from Sturmland held his course;
With silver gear and housing, heavily went his horse.
To court went, too, his followers, four hundred men undaunted;
And now the doughty Hettel brave knights enough, for guests, no longer wanted.

===Chinese===
瓦特从施图尔姆兰启程，前来与黑特尔王会合；
马披银质辔具与马衣，步履沉重。
他的部下也随行进宫，四百名无畏的汉子；
如今豪雄的黑特尔，款客的勇武士已绰绰有余。

===Original===
Morunc, the brave and daring, from Friesland thither went,
And with him brought two hundred. Word to the king was sent
That now, with helms and breastplates, they were thither riding;
In haste came Irold also; thus gladly Hettel’s kinsmen did his bidding.

===Chinese===
勇武果敢的莫伦克从弗里西亚赶来，
带来两百人马。有人传话给国王，
说他们正顶盔贯甲，骑马前来；
伊罗尔德也匆匆赶到；黑特尔的亲族们便如此欣然听命。

===Original===
Thither rode from Denmark Horant young and brave;
Hettel to do his errand did trusty liegemen have;
A thousand men or over might he for this be sending;
Only a prince so mighty of such a task had ever made an ending.

===Chinese===
年轻勇敢的霍兰特也从丹麦驰来；
为办好这桩差事，黑特尔麾下不乏忠实的封臣；
他满可以为此派出千人犹不止；
唯有这般雄强的君主，才能了结这样一桩重任。

===Original===
Irold, too, of Ortland, was ready now to go:
E’en though on him King Hettel should never clothes bestow,
Yet, for himself and his followers, he had of these so many,
That wheresoe’er they were going, they never need to beg for aught from any.

===Chinese===
奥特兰的伊罗尔德也已整装待发：
纵使黑特尔王一件衣裳也不赏他，
他为自己和随从们备下的衣装也绰绰有余，
无论去到哪里，都不必向任何人乞求分毫。

===Original===
The king, as well beseemed him, greeted all the band;
First, his liegeman Irold he kindly took by the hand;
Then he turned to Wâ-te, to where he found him seated:
At last, his hardy warriors, ready to leave the land, his word awaited.

===Chinese===
国王不失体统地问候了全体人马；
他先亲切地握住封臣伊罗尔德的手；
随后又走向瓦特，到他落座之处：
最后，他那些剽悍的武士整装待发，只候他一声号令。

===Original===
To all it now was bidden that they should give good heed,
And everything make ready that knights could ever need.
Now were seen by the warriors the ships so fair and stately;
To woo the lovely Hilda the king in all things showed his forethought greatly.

===Chinese===
他吩咐众人务必用心，
把骑士可能用到的一切都备办齐全。
武士们如今看到了那些雄伟漂亮的船；
为求娶可爱的希尔达，国王在方方面面都深谋远虑。

===Original===
Two new and well-made galleys they had upon the flood,
With two broad ships of burden; both were strong and good.
A ship of state went with them; than this had ne’er a better,
By any friend or foeman, on the shores of any land, been seen upon the water.

===Chinese===
水面上泊着两艘崭新的精工战船，
外加两艘宽阔的货船；全都坚固精良。
随行的还有一艘御用座船；无论友邦还是敌国，
在任何陆地的水滨，都不曾有人见过比这更好的船。

===Original===
To start they now are willing; already on the ships
Were the clothes and horses loaded. Then from Wâ-te’s lips
Came kindly words to Hettel; he begged him to feel easy,
Till they should again be coming, for to do his bidding they would all be busy.

===Chinese===
众人如今整装欲发；衣装与马匹
早已装载上船。瓦特于是
向黑特尔说了些宽慰的话；他请国王宽心，
只等他们归来；在那之前，众人都会忙着办他的差事。

===Original===
The king to him said mournfully: “I give into your care
The knights, untaught and youthful, who such risks will dare,
With you upon this errand: most earnestly I pray you
That, for your honor, daily you teach these youths with care, and make them to obey you.”

===Chinese===
国王忧郁地对他说：「我把那些骑士托付给你，
他们年少未经战阵，却要随你担此奇险：
我恳切地祈求你，看在你的荣誉份上，
天天悉心管教这些年轻人，让他们听从于你。」

===Original===
Him thus Wâ-te answered: “To that give not a thought;
Keep a brave heart, I beg you, that here at home, in nought
You fail of being steadfast, where’er your honor reaches:
Watch well, too, o’er our holdings: these youths shall learn from me what wisdom teaches.”

===Chinese===
瓦特如此答他：「这大可不必挂心；
我只求你鼓起勇气，在国内无论何处，
凡关涉你荣誉之事，都不可失了坚定：
也请照看好我们的领地：这些年轻人自会从我这里领受智慧的教诲。」

===Original===
The good and trusty Fru-te the wealth of the king did guard﻿—
The gold and costly jewels, and of many things a hoard.
The king was free in spending whatsoe’er was wanted;
If Fru-te aught did ask for, thirty-fold to him he gladly granted.

===Chinese===
忠良的弗鲁特掌管着国王的财富——
黄金与贵重的珠宝，还有许多物资的囤积。
凡有所需，国王挥金从不吝惜；
弗鲁特但凡开口索求，他都乐赐三十倍的酬报。

===Original===
A hundred men were chosen, and now within the ship,
Wherein to woo the maiden his friends must cross the deep,
All craftily were hidden, to help them, if ’twere needful.
Gifts both rich and worthy the king to give these faithful men was heedful.

===Chinese===
一百人被挑选出来，此刻藏进船中；
他的朋友们将乘这艘船渡海去求娶少女，
这百人巧作隐蔽，以备不时之需。
国王也不忘馈赠这些忠仆以丰厚而贵重的礼物。

===Original===
With these, among the followers, every rank was seen;
Of knights and squires also, thirty hundred men,
Who, for toil and struggle, from far-off lands came riding.
Then said the king to his lieges: “May God in heaven to you give careful guiding.”

===Chinese===
连同这些人，部众里各色等级一应俱全；
连骑士与扈从在内，共计三千人，
他们为着辛劳与征战，从远方策马而来。
国王对封臣们说：「愿天上的上帝保佑你们一路平安。」

===Original===
To him thus Horant answered: “From fear now be you free;
When you shall see us coming, you then with us will see
A maid so fair and lovely, you well may wish to greet her.”
This the king heard gladly, but far was the day when he at last should meet her.

===Chinese===
霍兰特如此答他：「您尽管放心；
等您看到我们归来，随我们同来的
将是一位如此美丽可爱的少女，您定会满怀欣喜迎接她。」
国王欣然听着，只是距他终能见到她的那天，还很遥远。

===Original===
They took their leave with kisses, the king and many a guest;
For these the king was feeling wearisome unrest.
While they for him are toiling, each hour he fear must borrow;
He forsooth was downcast, and nought could cheer him, in his mood of sorrow.

===Chinese===
国王与众宾客亲吻作别；
为了这些人，国王心头萦绕着难熬的不安。
他们在为他操劳，他却时时为忧惧所袭；
他委实郁郁寡欢，愁绪之中，什么也不能宽解他。

===Original===
This was for his welfare, that a wind from out the north
Now their sails was swelling, and briskly helped them forth.
The ships were wafted evenly, as they from land were turning;
But hardships they had known not the youths, upon their way, erelong were learning.

===Chinese===
这倒是他的福气：一阵北风
此时鼓起他们的船帆，轻快地送他们前行。
船只离岸转向时，被风平稳地推送着；
但那些年轻人未曾尝过的苦楚，不久便在途中领教了。

===Original===
The truth we cannot tell you, nor can it e’en be guessed,
For nights full six and thirty what lodgings gave them rest,
While upon the water. The youths they with them carried,
Bound by oaths of fealty, swore again to keep them, where’er they tarried.

===Chinese===
真相我们无从奉告，甚至无从猜测，
整整三十六个夜晚，漂在水上的他们
靠什么宿处安身。他们随船携带的年轻人，
曾受忠诚之誓约束，此时又重新起誓：无论身在何处都恪守不渝。

===Original===
However willing were they to sail on the tossing sea,
Yet sometimes it befell them in great unrest to be.
Ease they took but seldom, as the waves would spare it;
But he who ploughs the waters pain must often feel, and yet must bear it.

===Chinese===
无论他们多么情愿在颠簸的海上航行，
有时仍难免陷入极大的惶惶不安。
风浪不肯容情，他们便难得安歇；
但凡犁波耕浪之人，就必须常受苦楚，且必须承受。

===Original===
After the waves had borne them full a thousand miles,
They came to Hagen’s castle, where, as was said erewhiles,
He, the master of Ballian, shamefully had lorded:
This was a wicked falsehood, the deeds were never done as the tale was worded.

===Chinese===
海浪载着他们漂过足足一千里之遥，
他们来到哈根的城堡；前文早已述及，
说他、巴利安（Ballian）之主，曾在此肆行苛政：
那是恶毒的谎言，那些行径从未如传言所说的那般发生。

===Original===
When now the men from Hegeling over the sea had gone,
And neared wild Hagen’s castle, their coming soon was known;
Much the folk there wondered from what far kingdom sailing
The waves had borne them thither; how finely they were clad all men were telling.

===Chinese===
当黑格林的人们渡海而来，
行近暴烈的哈根的城堡，他们的到来很快便传开了；
当地人纷纷称奇，不知风浪从哪个遥远的国度
把他们送来；人人都在议论他们衣着何等华美。

===Original===
First the ship with an anchor was fastened on the strand;
To furl the sails then quickly each gave a ready hand.
It was not long thereafter before the news was bruited,
Throughout King Hagen’s castle, that ships, with unknown men, in his harbor floated.

===Chinese===
头一条船先在岸边抛锚泊定；
众人随即齐动手，迅速收拢船帆。
此后不久，消息便传遍
哈根王的城堡：载着陌生男儿的船只，正泊在他的港湾。

===Original===
Now on the shore they landed, and did their goods unlade;
Whatever could be wanted on the sands, for sale, they spread,
And all that any asked for. In wealth they were not lacking;
But though their men had silver, ’twas little that they bought, or for themselves were taking.

===Chinese===
于是他们登岸，卸下货物；
凡是可能有人想要的，都摊在沙滩上出售，
别人要什么就给什么。他们的财物不乏；
只是部下虽带着银钱，买进或自留的却很少。

===Original===
Clothed in the garb of tradesmen, on the shore did stand
Sixty men or over, well-dight, a goodly band.
Fru-te, the lord of Denmark, was busy as their leader;
His clothing was far better than there was worn by any other trader.

===Chinese===
身着商贾装束，立在岸上的
有六十人犹不止，装束齐整，一队体面人马。
丹麦贵胄弗鲁特作为首领忙前忙后；
他的衣着远胜过任何别的商贩。

===Original===
The worthy lord and master over Ballian town,
When he heard of their coming, and the riches they did own,
Rode down with many followers to where those crafty sellers
He found, himself awaiting. Kind was the mien of all who there were dwellers.

===Chinese===
巴利安城那位可敬的城主，
听闻他们到来，又闻他们家资豪富，
便率大批随从驰往岸边，果见那些精明的商贩
正恭候他驾临。当地居民个个面色和善。

===Original===
First the master asked them: “Whence their way they had made,
And over the sea come thither?” To him then Fru-te said:
“God have you in his keeping; we from afar are sailing;
Tradesmen truly are we; our masters rich, near by, in ships are dwelling.”

===Chinese===
城主先问他们：「从何处启程，
又渡海来到这里？」弗鲁特对他说：
「愿上帝庇佑您；我们从远方航海而来；
我们确是商贾；我们富有的主家，就在近处的船上安身。」

===Original===
“Let peace with us be plighted,” old Wâ-te then began;
But from the master’s grimness, the truth to see was plain,
That, where he was the ruler, stern and harsh was his bearing.
Straightway then to Hagen they led the guests, who with their tale were faring.

===Chinese===
「请与我们缔结和平，」老瓦特开口道；
但从城主的冷面看来，真相一望便知：
在他辖治之地，他作风严酷苛厉。
众人随即把带着说辞的宾客引去见哈根。

===Original===
Hagen said, as he met them: “Safeguard to you I give;
My peace I pledge you willingly. He shall no longer live,
But hang upon the gallows, who these guests shall harry:
Let them not be fearful; them shall nothing harm while in my land they tarry.”

===Chinese===
哈根迎见他们时说道：「我赐你们平安；
我甘愿向你们担保我的和平。谁若胆敢
骚扰这些客人，谁就别想活命，
必被吊上绞架：让他们不必害怕；他们滞留我的国土期间，无人能加害。」

===Original===
Rich and costly jewels they to Hagen gave,
In worth, of marks a thousand. From them he nought did crave,
Nor even so much as a penny; but what for sale they offered
He begged of them to show him, such as to knights and ladies might be proffered.

===Chinese===
他们向哈根献上珍稀贵重的珠宝，
价值达一千马克。他没有向他们索取分文，
连一个便士也不要；只请他们把出售的货色
拿给他瞧瞧，凡适合进献骑士与淑媛的皆可。

===Original===
For all he thanked them warmly; he said: “If I should live
Not more than three days longer, for all that now they give
My guests shall be rewarded. If my liegeman do not heed me,
And these for aught be lacking, all shall then for this with right upbraid me.”

===Chinese===
他热忱地答谢了这一切；他说：「哪怕我
只能再活三天，如今他们所赠的一切，
我的客人们也必得回报。倘若我的臣仆不遵我命，
让客人们短缺了什么，众人就有权为此责难我。」

===Original===
Now the gifts they gave him the king with his men did share;
Among them there were necklaces, fit for ladies fair,
With finger-rings and arm-bands, as well as ribbons dainty,
And head-gear, to bedeck them: these the king to many gave in plenty.

===Chinese===
国王把他们的赠礼分赐给部众；
其中有配得上佳人的项链，
还有指环与臂环，以及雅致的缎带，
和点缀她们的首饰：这些国王都慷慨地分赠给了许多人。

===Original===
His wife and lovely daughter now most rightly thought
That never to their kingdom had gifts for them been brought,
That were so rare and costly, by sellers or by traders.
Horant and Wâ-te also in sending gifts to court were now the leaders.

===Chinese===
他的妻子与爱女这时理所当然地认为，
从不曾有献给她们王国的礼物
如此珍稀贵重，无论出自小贩还是客商。
而在往宫廷送礼一事上，霍兰特与瓦特此刻正领头操办。

===Original===
Sixty silken garments, the best that e’er were sold,
Up to the shore were carried, and forty wrought with gold.
They would have prized but lightly cloths from Baghdad even;
Of linen suits a hundred, the best they had, now to the king were given.

===Chinese===
六十件丝袍，堪称售出的品中至精，
被搬上岸来，另有四十件织金的。
便是巴格达来的锦缎，相形之下也不足贵；
一百套亚麻衣裳，他们所有的最好的，此刻都献给了国王。

===Original===
Beside the handsome clothing, made of silken stuff,
Of richest inner garments they also gave enough;
There might perhaps be forty, or more, if reckoned fully;
Could ever man buy praises, they by their costly gifts had gained them truly.

===Chinese===
除了那些丝料裁成的华服，
最贵重的贴身衣衫他们也给了许多；
细算起来约莫有四十件，或许更多；
倘若世人的赞誉可以买来，他们确已凭厚礼真正购得了。

===Original===
Twelve Castilian horses, all saddled, were brought, I trow;
Also many breastplates, and well-made helmets, too,
Men were bidden to carry; twelve bucklers likewise bore they,
Rimmed with golden edges. Kind were Hagen’s guests; free givers were they.

===Chinese===
我确知牵来了十二匹卡斯蒂利亚骏马，全都备好了鞍；
还有许多胸甲，以及打造精良的头盔，
都吩咐人搬抬；他们还带来十二面小圆盾，
盾缘镶着黄金。哈根的客人们如此厚道；他们是出手豪阔的施赠者。

===Original===
Then, too, with gifts came riding Horant the brave and bold;
Irold the strong came with him; this to the king was told:
’Twas said to him, moreover, that those now thither faring
Of lands were the lords and owners. This might well be seen by the gifts they were bearing.

===Chinese===
随后，勇武豪迈的霍兰特也带着礼物骑马而来；
强健的伊罗尔德与他同行；有人禀告国王：
还说如今来此的这些人，
都是封疆的君主与领主。从他们所携的礼物便足见分晓。

===Original===
After these came riding four and twenty men
Whom they were thither leading, well-bred were they, I ween;
Such also was their clothing, they seemed as if well fitted,
And now in truth were coming, that very day to be by Hagen knighted.

===Chinese===
在他们之后，又有二十四人骑马而来，
那是被他们带来的，依我看个个教养良好；
他们的衣着也如此考究，仿佛早有备而来，
而如今他们确是为此而来：就在当天，要由哈根册封为骑士。

===Original===
Then unto King Hagen one of his friends thus spake:
“The gifts the men now bring you ’tis best you deign to take:
Never must you leave them unthanked for all their treasure.”
Hagen lacked not riches, but yet his thanks he gave them without measure.

===Chinese===
这时哈根王的一位友人进言：
「这些人如今带来的礼物，您最好还是收下：
万不可受了他们如许财宝，却不道一声谢。」
哈根并不缺财富，但他对他们的谢意却给了个十足。

===Original===
He said: “I thank you kindly, as I of right should do.”
Then he bade that his stewards to see the gifts should go;
And also that the clothing, piece by piece, be shown them.
Glad were they to see them, and wondered greatly as they gazed upon them.

===Chinese===
他说：「我由衷感谢你们，这也是我份所应当。」
随后他吩咐管家们去验看送来的礼物；
还吩咐把那些衣裳一件一件指给他们看。
管家们看得很欢喜，端详之际惊叹不已。

===Original===
Then said one of the stewards: “Hear now the truth I tell:
Chests there are of silver, and filled with gold as well,
With many costly jewels, rich and kingly even:
Marks fully twenty thousand the goods are worth, which they to you have given.”

===Chinese===
这时一位管家说道：「且听我道出实情：
这里有银箱，还满盛着黄金，
更有许多贵重珠宝，富贵直逼王家气派：
他们献给您的这些财物，足足值两万马克。」

===Original===
Then the king thus answered: “Blessings on my guests!
I now will share with others the riches in these chests.”
Then to his knights was given whate’er of these they wanted;
To every one among them all that he might wish by the king was granted.

===Chinese===
国王于是答道：「福哉我的客人！
我这就要把这些箱中的财富分给众人。」
他的骑士们随即各取所需；
他们中每一个人心里的愿望，都由国王成全了。

===Original===
The king now seated near him both the two young men﻿—
Irold and also Horant; he began to ask them then,
“Whence to his kingdom sailing, they to come had striven?
Gifts so rich and worthy have ne’er before by guests to me been given.”

===Chinese===
国王把两位年轻人唤到近旁坐下——
伊罗尔德与霍兰特；他开口问他们：
「你们扬帆渡海，为何执意来到我的国度？
如此丰厚贵重的礼物，此前从没有客人送过我。」

===Original===
Then spake the knightly Horant: “This shall you know full well;
My lord, now hear us kindly while we our sorrows tell.
Outlawed wanderers are we, and from our homes were driven;
A king most rich and mighty, to wreak his anger, woe to us hath given.”

===Chinese===
骑士霍兰特答道：「这您一定会知晓；
主上，请仁慈地听我们诉说愁苦。
我们是被放逐的流亡者，被逐出了家园；
一位豪富而强横的君王，为泄其怒，降祸于我们。」

===Original===
Then spake again wild Hagen: “What may be his name,
From whose rich kingdom driven, outcasts you became?
You of wealth are owners, and, if not by his wits forsaken,
To keep such worthy lieges within his land he would some pains have taken.”

===Chinese===
暴烈的哈根又问：「那他叫什么名字？
你们被逐出的是哪片富庶的国土，才沦为流浪者？
你们自身家资丰厚，他若不是昏聩失察，
定会费些心力，把你们这样可敬的封臣留在国中。」

===Original===
He asked “Who them had outlawed, and what name he bore?
Of what misdoings guilty, had they to this far shore
Made their flight in sorrow, to ask the help of strangers?”
To him then answered Horant: “To you will we make known our woes and dangers.

===Chinese===
他问：「是谁放逐了他们？那人是何姓名？
他们犯了什么罪过，才悲悲切切
逃到这遥远的海岸，向陌生人求援？」
霍兰特答他：「我们愿向您和盘托出我们的忧患与凶险。

===Original===
“He bears the name of ‘Hettel, Lord of the Hegeling land’;
Brave and mighty is he, and sways with a heavy hand.
We of all our happiness have been robbed and plundered;
Of right are we embittered, since from our land and home we now are sundered.”

===Chinese===
「他名叫『黑特尔，黑格林之地的大君』；
他骁勇而强悍，以铁腕施治。
我们的一切幸福，都被他劫掠一空；
我们如今背井离乡，心中怨愤，实属应当。」

===Original===
To him spoke Hagen kindly: “This to your good shall turn;
I will in full repay you the losses that you mourn.
If I make myself a beggar, by thus so freely giving,
Yet from the king of the Hegelings you need not ask for help while I am living.

===Chinese===
哈根好言对他说：「这事会转为你们的福分；
你们哀叹的损失，我必尽数补偿。
纵使我如此慷慨，散尽家财沦为乞丐，
只要我一息尚存，你们就不必向黑格林人的国王乞求帮助。」

===Original===
“If you, good knights,” he added, “here with me will stay,
With you will I share right gladly the lands I own to-day;
Such guerdon by King Hettel ne’er to you was given.
The wealth from you he has taken, that give I you, and more by tenfold even.”

===Chinese===
「你们这些好骑士，」他又说，「若肯留在我这里，
我今日所拥有的领地，甘愿与你们共享；
这样的酬赏，黑特尔王从不曾给过你们。
他从你们手里夺走的财富，我如数奉还，还要再加十倍。」

===Original===
“To stay with you we are ready,” then said Horant the Dane,
“But we fear that when King Hettel shall learn that we were seen
Within the Irish borders, he will find a way to reach us;
And I am ever dreading that we can nowhere live, and this he’ll teach us.”

===Chinese===
「留下来我们愿意，」丹麦人霍兰特说，
「只是我们担心，一旦黑特尔王得知有人看见我们
出现在爱尔兰边境之内，他会设法找到我们；
我总在害怕：天下之大，我们将无处容身——他会教我们明白这一点。」

===Original===
Then to the band of wanderers the lordly Hagen said:
“Do what now I bid you, and a home for you is made.
Never will King Hettel dare for your harm to seek you
Within my land and kingdom; it were a wrong to me from hence to take you.”

===Chinese===
威严的哈根对流人们说道：
「照我吩咐的去做，你们便有了家。
黑特尔王绝不敢为加害你们，
而闯入我的国土与王国；若从我这儿把你们夺走，那便是对我的亏负。」

===Original===
He bade they should be sheltered, at once, within his town;
Then to his men and lieges he made his wishes known,
That now unto the wanderers all honor should be granted.
The water-weary sailors soon found the rest that they so long had wanted.

===Chinese===
他吩咐即刻在城中安顿他们栖身；
随后向臣仆与封臣们宣明心意，
要对这些流亡者礼敬有加。
饱经风浪的水手们，很快便寻得了渴求已久的安歇。

===Original===
Then the townsmen freely did the king’s behest;
To do it they were ready: houses, the very best,
Forty, or even over, were empty left, to be taken
By the Danish sailors; their homes, by the king’s good lieges were willingly forsaken.

===Chinese===
城中居民欣然奉行王命；
他们都乐于照办：最好的房屋，
四十座犹不止，都腾了出来，留给
丹麦的水手们居住；国王的忠良臣民甘愿让出自己的家。

===Original===
Up on the beach were carried the wares, full many a pile,
That in the ships lay hidden. Their owners thought, the while,
That they would rather struggle with storms upon the water,
Than to seek their luck and welfare in wooing Hilda, Hagen’s lovely daughter.

===Chinese===
藏在船中的货物，一堆堆被搬上滩头。
它们的主人们此刻心想，
宁愿在海面上与风暴搏斗，
也不愿为求娶哈根的爱女希尔达，去赌自己的运气与前程。

===Original===
Hagen bade his followers: “Now ask these guests of mine
If they will deign most kindly to eat my bread and wine,
Till they, within my kingdom, on lands they hold are living.”
The Danish Fru-te answered: “To take your food would shame to us be giving.”

===Chinese===
哈根吩咐部下：「去问问我的这些客人，
可否赏光用我的面包与酒，
直到他们在我的国中得到封地安身。」
丹麦人弗鲁特答道：「领受您的饮食，反倒会让我们蒙羞。」

===Original===
“If erst the great King Hettel had been to us so good,
That he both gold and silver would give to us for food,
We in our houses had them, and might of them be wasteful;
We e’en could stay our hunger, and feed thereon, if this to us were tasteful.”

===Chinese===
「想当年，倘若伟大的黑特尔王待我们如此之厚，
肯用金银来换我们的饮食，
那些钱财如今还在我们自家屋里，任凭我们挥霍；
我们甚至可以守着它们充饥，只要觉得合口味。」

===Original===
’Twas bidden then by Fru-te that his booth should be set up.
To see for sale such riches men ne’er again could hope.
Never within their borders did any trader offer
Fine goods at such a bargain; they easily were sold before the day was over.

===Chinese===
弗鲁特随后吩咐，搭起他的货棚。
人们再也别想见到如此豪富的售货场面。
在这片国土之内，从没有商贩
以这般贱价出售精美的货物；不到天黑，货便卖了个精光。

===Original===
All could buy who wished them, gold and jewels rare.
The king, by greatest kindness, was to his guests made dear.
If any, without buying, still these treasures wanted,
The traders were so friendly that they, as gifts, the goods to many granted.

===Chinese===
谁想要黄金与珍宝，都可以来买。
国王以极盛的仁慈，赢得了客人们的爱戴。
若有人不曾付钱、却仍想要这些珍宝，
商贩们也如此和气，把货物当礼物送给了许多人。

===Original===
Whate’er of Wâ-te or Fru-te was said by any one﻿—
Of all the deeds of kindness that here by them were done﻿—
The tale might not be trusted, how they for these were ready;
They strove to gain high praises, and this at court was told to many a lady.

===Chinese===
无论谁说起瓦特或弗鲁特——
说起他们在此做下的所有善举——
那些传闻都令人难以尽信，他们办起这些事来多么有求必应；
他们极力博取崇高的赞誉，而这些事也传到了宫中许多淑媛的耳里。

===Original===
Of the poor nor man nor woman for clothes was seen to lack;
To those in need among them they gave their pledges back,
And from debt they freed them. To the princess, morn and even,
Oft by her faithful steward the tale of these guests from far was truly given.

===Chinese===
穷人当中无论男女，无人缺衣少穿；
他们把典当之物还给了穷困的人，
还免除了他们的债务。而公主那里，晨昏之间，
她忠心的管家常把远客们的事迹如实禀报。

===Original===
To the king she made her prayer: “Dearest father mine,
Ask that these guests so worthy to ride to court will deign.
They say that one among them hath charms beyond all measure;
Should he to your bidding listen, the sight of him ofttimes would give me pleasure.”

===Chinese===
她向父亲恳求：「最亲爱的父王，
请您邀请那些高贵的客人屈尊来宫廷一叙。
听说他们中间有一人，风姿超群绝伦；
他若肯听从您的召唤，常常见到他，必让我欢悦。」

===Original===
To her the king thus answered: “That shall quickly be;
His well-bred ways and bearing I soon will let you see.”
But still the great King Hagen never yet had known him;
Long the ladies waited till Wâ-te came, and they could look upon him.

===Chinese===
国王这样答她：「这事很快就能办到；
他那良好的教养与仪态，我即刻便让你们见识。」
只是伟大的哈根王此前从未与他谋面；
淑媛们等了很久，才盼到瓦特到来，得以一睹其人。

===Original===
Word to the guests was carried; to them ’twas kindly said,
That if it e’er should happen that they of aught had need,
They should to the king betake them, and his food be sharing.
To Fru-te this was pleasing, for wise he was, not less than he was daring.

===Chinese===
话传到了客人那里；他们好意被告知，
倘若日后有什么短缺，
尽可去见国王，共享他的饮食。
弗鲁特听了正中下怀，因为他胆略兼备，智谋亦毫不逊色。

===Original===
Those who came from Denmark, when at court, took care
Ever to be blameless for the clothes that they should wear:
’Twas so with the men of Wâ-te, from Sturmland thither faring,
And than himself no sword-knight in any land could show a finer bearing.

===Chinese===
从丹麦来的人一入宫廷，便处处留意，
务使身上的衣着无可指摘：
从施图尔姆兰远道而来的瓦特的部众也是如此；
而放眼任何国度，也没有哪位剑骑士比他本人更有风采。

===Original===
Those who came with Morunc wore mantles over all,
With robes from far Kampalia. Fiery red, as a coal,
Gold and gems that sparkled on their clothes were shining.
Irold, the daring champion, came not alone, young Hilda bent on winning.

===Chinese===
随莫伦克而来的人，人人外披斗篷，
内着遥远的坎帕利亚（Kampalia）来的长袍。袍色火红如炭，
金子与宝石在他们衣上灼灼闪光。
勇敢的斗士伊罗尔德也非独行而来，他一心要赢得年轻的希尔达。

===Original===
Thither came brave Horant; all others he out-vied
In rich and costly clothing. With mantles long and wide,
Gay in hue and gaudy, his men were decked out brightly:
Those brave men from Denmark proudly came, and had a look most knightly.

===Chinese===
英勇的霍兰特也来了；论衣饰的华贵，
他压倒了所有旁人。他的部下身披又长又宽的斗篷，
色彩明艳夺目，个个装束得光彩照人：
这些丹麦勇士昂首而来，尽显骑士气派。

===Original===
Though Hagen’s birth was kingly, and lordly was his mood,
He yet went forth to meet them. His daughter, fair and good,
Rose up before old Wâ-te from where she now was seated.
Such was Wâ-te’s bearing as if with smiles his friends he never greeted.

===Chinese===
哈根虽出身高贵，性情倨傲，
却也亲自出迎。他美丽贤淑的女儿，
在老瓦特面前从座位上起身。
瓦特那副神气，仿佛他从不用笑脸迎接朋友。

===Original===
She said, in way most seemly: “Welcome to you I give;
Both I and the king, my father, must from your looks believe
That you are weary warriors, and sorely have been fighting.
Goodwill the king will show you, and soon his faith to you will he be plighting.”

===Chinese===
她以极其得体的方式说道：「我向诸位致以欢迎；
我和父王从你们的风尘之色推断，
你们必是劳顿的武士，刚经历过恶战。
国王必向诸位示以善意，并很快向你们立下信义。」

===Original===
To her they all bent lowly; their ways, they were well-bred.
The king then bade to be seated, as hosts are wont to bid.
Of drink to them was given, wine the best and rarest;
Better ne’er was tasted in the home of any lord, albeit the fairest.

===Chinese===
他们齐向她躬身行礼；他们举止有度，教养良好。
国王随即请众人入座，一如主人的惯例。
席间奉上的酒饮，是最上等的珍酿；
任凭哪家君侯的宅邸再华美，也不曾尝过比这更好的。

===Original===
In talk and fun and merriment seated were they all.
Soon the queenly maiden was seen to leave the hall:
But first she begged her father the kindness now to show her
To bid the knights so worthy to come to her, for pastime, to her bower.

===Chinese===
众人谈笑风生，欢坐一堂。
不久，那位王后般的少女离席而去：
但行前她恳请父亲施恩于她，
邀那些高贵的骑士到她的闺阁中来，共享欢愉。

===Original===
Her wish the king then granted, (so to us, ’tis said);
His young and lovely daughter at this was truly glad.
Soon fair clothes and jewels the maidens all were wearing;
And earnestly were watching the many knights from far, to see their bearing.

===Chinese===
国王随即应允了她的请求（世人这般传说）；
他年轻可爱的女儿为此满心欢喜。
不多时，少女们个个穿戴起华服与珠宝，
饶有兴味地注视着远来的众多骑士，品评他们的风采。

===Original===
When now the elder Hilda sat by her daughter fair,
Each one of her lovely maidens demeaned herself with care;
So that all who saw her high in breeding thought her,
And nothing else could say of her, but that she was indeed a king’s fair daughter.

===Chinese===
当年长的希尔达在爱女身旁坐下，
她那些可爱的侍女个个举止端庄；
见到她们的人都赞其出身高贵，
只道她们果真是国王的美丽女儿，再无别的评说。

===Original===
Now bade they that old Wâ-te should to the maids be brought;
Though he was gray and aged, none the less they thought,
To guard against his wooing, they must as children meet him.
Then to the aged Wâ-te stepped forth the youthful queen, right glad to greet him.

===Chinese===
随后她们吩咐，把老瓦特带到姑娘们跟前；
他虽已须发斑白，姑娘们仍认为，
为防他动求亲之念，须以孩童之姿相见。
于是年轻的王后迎向年迈的瓦特，欢欢喜喜地问候他。

===Original===
She was the first to do so, but wished she might be spared
When she now must kiss him: broad and gray was his beard,
And the hair of the aged Wâ-te with golden strings was braided.
He and the Danish Fru-te the queen’s behest to seat them slowly heeded.

===Chinese===
她第一个上前见礼，可临到要亲吻他时，
却巴不得能免了这一遭：他的胡须又宽又灰，
老瓦特的头发里还编着一缕缕金线。
他与丹麦人弗鲁特遵王后之命，缓缓落座。

===Original===
Both the well-clad heroes before their seats now stood;
Well they knew fine breeding, and made their teaching good.
In many a bitter struggle, in their manhood early,
They gained a name as warriors; and men to them gave praises for it fairly.

===Chinese===
两位衣冠楚楚的豪杰在座位前伫立；
他们深谙上流礼数，且学以致用。
早在盛年之初，历经多少恶战，
他们便赢得了武人的名号；人们对此称誉，实至名归。

===Original===
Queen Hilda and her daughter, in lively, merry mood,
Began to ask of Wâ-te, whether he thought it good,
Thus with lovely ladies to sit in ease and pleasure,
Or if to him ’twere better his strength in stormy fight with foes to measure?

===Chinese===
王后希尔达与她的女儿兴致盎然，
笑语盈盈地问瓦特，他觉得哪种更好：
是这样陪着可爱的淑媛们安坐享乐，
还是到暴烈的战阵中与仇敌较量膂力？

===Original===
The aged Wâ-te answered: “To me the last seems best;
Altho’ among fair ladies glad am I to rest,
Never am I happier than when with knights most daring,
Wherever that may happen, upon the stormy field the fight I am sharing.”

===Chinese===
年迈的瓦特答道：「依我看，还是后者最好；
虽然在美人们中间安歇也令我欣然，
但与最豪勇的骑士们共处，才最让我快意，
无论身在何处，只要鏖战正酣的战场上有我一份厮杀。」

===Original===
At this the gay young maiden broke into laughter loud;
Well she saw, with ladies, his stern, uneasy mood.
With this in the halls yet longer were the maidens merry;
Queen Hilda and her daughter to talk with Morunc’s knights were never weary.

===Chinese===
听了这话，活泼的少女朗声大笑；
她看得分明：置身脂粉丛中，他神色严峻、坐立难安。
有了这桩趣事，厅堂里的姑娘们愈发欢乐；
王后希尔达母女与莫伦克的骑士们谈兴不衰。

===Original===
She asked about old Wâ-te: “Say, by what name is he known?
Has he any liegemen? Doth he lands and castles own?
Has he a wife and children in the land whence he is roving?
There, as I am thinking, at his home and hearth, there must be little loving.”

===Chinese===
她问起老瓦特：「说说看，他叫什么名字？
他有封臣吗？可有领地与城堡？
在他游历而来的那片国土上，他有妻儿吗？
照我想来，在他自家的炉灶边，怕是少有温情。」

===Original===
Then answered one of the warriors: “Both children and a wife
In his home and land await him. His riches and his life
He risketh for his duty; a hero brave he has shown him.
A bold and daring champion, throughout his life, both friend and foe have known him.”

===Chinese===
一位武士答道：「他的家园故土之中，
妻儿正等候着他。他为尽职责，
不惜钱财与性命；他已证明自己是豪勇的英雄。
一生之中，无论友与敌，都识得他是无畏的斗士。」

===Original===
Irold the tale was telling about this fearless knight,
That never worthier liegeman, or bolder man in fight,
A king need e’er be seeking, his lands and castles over:
Though mildly now he bears him, there ne’er was found a stronger or a braver.

===Chinese===
伊罗尔德也在称道这位无畏的骑士，
说君王为自己的领地城堡，
再不必寻访比他更贤良的封臣、比他更勇猛的战士：
他此刻虽然温文尔雅，天下却再寻不出更强更勇之人。

===Original===
The queen then said to Wâ-te: “Give heed to what I say;
Since in his Danish kingdom Hettel forbids your stay,
I here, within my borders, a home will gladly give you;
There lives no lord so mighty that he would ever dare from hence to drive you.”

===Chinese===
王后又对瓦特说：「请听我一言；
既然黑特尔不许您留居他的丹麦王国，
我便甘愿在我的疆界之内赐您一处家园；
世上再强大的君王，也不敢从这里把您驱逐。」

===Original===
Then to the queen he answered: “I too, myself, own land;
There give I clothes and horses, at will, with open hand.
To wait on you as liegeman, would make me sorry-hearted;
And from my lands and castles, more than a year, I never can be parted.”

===Chinese===
瓦特答王后道：「我自己也拥有领地；
在那里，我随意慷慨地赠人衣马。
若以封臣之身侍奉您，反倒会令我愁闷；
而离开我的领地城堡，我从来不能超过一年。」

===Original===
At last they all were going: then begged the lovely queen,
That when at court they waited, they always might be seen
Seated among the ladies; no shame by this were done them:
Then said to her brave Irold, that in their home this seat was ever shown them.

===Chinese===
最后众人将要告退：美丽的王后恳请他们，
今后在宫廷侍奉时，务必常来
坐在淑媛们中间；这于他们并无羞辱：
勇敢的伊罗尔德答她说，在他们家乡，这样的座席向来为他们设着。

===Original===
To load with gifts these wanderers the king was ever bent.
But in a mood so haughty had they been thither sent,
To no man were they willing to be for a mark beholden.
Hagen, the king, was lordly, and took it ill that their pride should them embolden.

===Chinese===
国王总想给这些流亡者压上厚礼。
但他们此行奉命时心气那般倨傲，
哪怕一枚马克的恩情，他们也不愿受人之惠。
哈根王生性高傲，见他们竟敢如此骄矜，心中不悦。

===Original===
To the king they now betook them; many were they who came;
There they found, for pastime, for each some merry game:
Draughts were many playing, or spear and shield were trying;
For these they cared but little, but ever were in Hagen’s praises vying.

===Chinese===
于是他们去见国王；来的人为数众多；
宫中为每个人备下了消遣的欢戏：
许多人在下棋，也有人试演枪盾；
他们于此却兴致寥寥，只顾争相夸赞哈根。

===Original===
As happens oft in Ireland, with every kind of fun
Forthwith the men made merry. In this old Wâ-te won
A friend for himself in Hagen; but to win the ladies’ praises,
Horant, the knight from Denmark, his time in lightsome frolic with them passes.

===Chinese===
爱尔兰素来如此，形形色色的玩乐
应有尽有，人们随即欢腾起来。老瓦特借此
与哈根结成了好友；而为了博取淑媛们的称誉，
丹麦骑士霍兰特把光阴消磨在与她们的轻快嬉戏里。

===Original===
Fru-te and also Wâ-te were knights full brave and bold;
When standing near each other, both alike looked old.
Their locks were gray and hoary, and with gold were twisted;
But where the bold were needed, to show their bravery earnestly they listed.

===Chinese===
弗鲁特与瓦特，都是胆气豪壮的骑士；
两人并肩而立时，都显出一副老态。
他们的鬓发灰白，间或编捻着金丝；
可一旦需要勇士，他们便热切地一显身手。

===Original===
The followers of King Hagen wore their shields at court,
With clubs as well as bucklers; there they strove in sport,
In the sword-play slashing; thrusts of spears they parried;
Well themselves they shielded. The youthful knights in games were never wearied.

===Chinese===
哈根王的部众在宫廷里携着盾牌，
还有棍棒与小圆盾；他们在竞技中争胜，
剑刃翻飞，又格开来袭的枪刺；
他们自护得滴水不漏。年轻的骑士们对游戏永不厌倦。

===Original===
Then asked the brave King Hagen of Wâ-te and his men,
“If, where they erst were living, such fights were ever seen,
Or such heavy onslaughts, as his good knights were dealing,
Here in his Irish kingdom?” A smile of scorn o’er Wâ-te’s face was stealing.

===Chinese===
勇武的哈根王问起瓦特与他的部众：
「在他们昔日的家园，可曾见过这样的比武，
或如此猛烈的攻势，像他的好骑士们
在我这爱尔兰王国里施展的？」一丝轻蔑悄然爬上瓦特的面庞。

===Original===
Then quoth the knight from Sturmland: “The like I never saw;
If any here could teach it, from here would I not withdraw
Till a year was fully ended, and I had learned it rightly.
Whoe’er should be my master, for his care and pains would I not reward him lightly.”

===Chinese===
施图尔姆兰的骑士答道：「这样的把戏我从未见过；
这里若有人能教我，我绝不离开，
直到整整一年期满，把它真正学通。
谁若做我的师父，他的辛劳与心血，我绝不薄报。」

===Original===
The king to him then answered: “For the love to you I bear,
I will bid my best of masters teach you his art with care,
Till the three strokes are easy, that, in field-storms raging,
Men give to one another; by this will you be helped when battle waging.”

===Chinese===
国王对他说：「看在我对您的情分，
我将命我最好的师父悉心传授您他的技艺，
直到您把那三式招法练得纯熟——鏖战的风暴中，
战士们以此相互馈赠；临阵厮杀时，它必能助您。」

===Original===
Then came a fencing-master, and began his craft to show
To Wâ-te, the daring fighter; in him he found a foe
Who fear for his life soon gave him. Wâ-te his onset parried,
With all the skill of a fencer. The face of Fru-te the Dane a smile now carried.

===Chinese===
随即来了位剑术师父，向
那大胆的斗士瓦特卖弄起本领；却发觉这对手
很快就让他为自己的性命担惊。瓦特格开他的攻势，
剑技娴熟至极。丹麦人弗鲁特的脸上不觉浮起微笑。

===Original===
To save himself, the teacher gave a spring as wide
As doth an untamed leopard. Wâ-te his weapon plied,
And in his hand it clattered, until the fire-sparks glistened
Upon his foeman’s buckler; he well might thank the youth who to him had listened.

===Chinese===
师父为保性命，纵身一跃，跳得
像未驯的豹子那样远。瓦特挥动兵刃，
剑在他手中铿锵作响，直到火星
在对手的小圆盾上闪烁；他真该好好谢谢那位听了他指教的年轻人。

===Original===
Then said the king, wild Hagen: “Give me the sword in hand.
I will take a little pastime with him of the Sturmisch land;
I will be his teacher, and he my four strokes be learning.
He for this will thank me.” Soon was the king high praise from Wâ-te earning.

===Chinese===
随后暴烈的哈根王说：「把剑递到我手里。
我要同这位施图尔姆兰来的客人消遣一番；
我来做他的师父，他来学我的四式招法。
他为此会感激我的。」不多时，国王便从瓦特那里赚得了盛赞。

===Original===
To him old Wâ-te answered: “A pledge I now must hear
That I from you, great Hagen, no guile soe’er may fear;
Should I by you be wounded, with ladies’ scorn shall I redden.”
In the fight was Wâ-te nimble; such quickness to believe should none be bidden.

===Chinese===
老瓦特答他：「我得先听一句保证，
伟大的哈根，让我不必惧怕你有任何欺诈；
我若被你所伤，定会惹得淑媛们讥笑满面。」
交起手来，瓦特身手矫捷；这般迅疾，谁不信也得信。

===Original===
The simple, untaught fencer smote Hagen many a blow;
Till, like a wet brand steaming, was the king before his foe.
The learner outdid his teacher: well his strength he boasted.
The host laid strokes unnumbered upon the guest, who in his skill had trusted.

===Chinese===
那位质朴无文、未受指教的剑客，给了哈根连绵的击打，
直打得国王在对手面前，如同湿柴般蒸腾出汗气。
学生胜过了师父：他的膂力尽可自夸。
主人把数不清的剑击，加在那位自信于技艺的客人身上。

===Original===
Many looked on gladly to see the strength of both.
To own the skill of Wâ-te the king was nothing loth;
He might have shown his anger, and brought no shame upon him.
Great was the strength of Wâ-te, but yet ’twas seen that Hagen had outdone him.

===Chinese===
许多人乐于旁观，看二人较量膂力。
国王也毫不吝于承认瓦特的技艺；
他本可以动怒，那也无损他的体面。
瓦特固然力大无穷，但明眼人看得出，哈根更胜一筹。

===Original===
To the king then spake old Wâ-te: “Let each no favor show,
While we together struggle. Well have I learned from you
Your four strokes to be plying; my thanks be you now sharing.”
Such thanks he later showed him as doth a fighting Frank or Saxon daring.

===Chinese===
老瓦特对国王说：「我们再比一场，
谁也不许留情。您那四式招法我已学好，
现在就请您领受我的谢意。」
他日后回敬的那份谢意，凶悍如同善战的法兰克人与撒克逊人。

===Original===
No more a truce was thought of by Wâ-te and the king;
With strokes that loud were crashing, the hall began to ring.
Harder blows than ever they gave, as now they battled;
All their thrusts were sudden; the knobs upon their swords snapped off and rattled.

===Chinese===
瓦特与国王之间，再没人想起停歇；
剑击铿然震响，大厅里回声四起。
二人愈战愈酣，出手比先前更重；
招招突袭；剑柄上的圆铆纷纷崩落，叮当乱响。

===Original===
The two sat down to rest them; then Hagen said to his guest:
“You fain would be a learner, but you in truth are the best
That ever I was teaching the skill that the foeman dazes.
Wherever you are fighting, you in the field will win most worthy praises.”

===Chinese===
二人坐下来歇息；哈根对客人说：
「你一心要做学生，可你其实是我教过的
令仇敌目眩神摇的剑技中最出色的一个。
无论你在何处征战，都必在战场上赢得无上的赞誉。」

===Original===
Then to the king spake Irold: “My lord, the strife is done
That you so well were waging; such fights have we seen won,
In the land of our king and master. Oft, at home, we freely
Try our skill with weapons; knights and squires there meet in matches daily.”

===Chinese===
伊罗尔德对国王说：「主上，这场较量
您打得如此精彩，如今胜负已分；这样的比武，
在我们君王的国土上，我们也见过胜出。在我们家乡，
人们常随意以兵刃切磋；骑士与扈从天天聚场较艺。」

===Original===
Then again spoke Hagen: “Did I this understand,
I never a fighting weapon had taken in my hand.
No youth have I ever met with who was so quick at learning.”
When to these words they listened, the face of many a one to smiles was turning.

===Chinese===
哈根又说道：「早知如此，
我决不会把兵刃握到手里。
这样一点就透的年轻人，我还从未遇见过。」
众人听了这话，许多人脸上都绽开了笑容。

===Original===
Now by the king ’twas granted to his guests to pass the day
As they might all be choosing. Glad of this were they,
The men from out the Northland. When the hours grew weary,
They vied huge stones in hurling; or else in shooting arrows made them merry.

===Chinese===
国王随即恩准他的客人们，
随心所欲地消磨这一天。北方来的人们
听了都很欢喜。等到时辰渐生倦意，
他们便比赛投掷巨石，或是射箭取乐。

## Tale VI How Sweetly Horant Sang / 第六诗章 霍兰特唱得何等甜美

===Original===
Horant delights the court with his singing, and he with his friend Morunc are introduced to the bower of Hilda. He tells her of Hettel’s suit, which she, charmed by the singing of Horant, receives with favor. They invite her to visit their ship, with her father and mother, and the invitation is accepted.

===Chinese===
霍兰特（Horant）以歌声愉悦了宫廷，随后他与友人莫伦克（Morunc）一同被引见至希尔达（Hilda）的闺阁。他向她转达黑特尔（Hettel）的求亲；为霍兰特的歌声所倾倒的希尔达，欣然垂青。他们邀请她偕同父母前往观看他们的船，邀约终获应允。

===Original===
It came to pass one evening,
 good luck did so befall,
 That Horant, the knight of Daneland,
 sang before them all.

===Chinese===
一天傍晚事有凑巧，
好运恰好降临：
丹麦兰（Daneland）的骑士霍兰特
当着众人放声歌吟；

===Original===
 His singing was so wondrous
 that all who listened near him
 Found his song well-pleasing;
 the little birds all hushed their notes to hear him.

===Chinese===
他的歌声如此奇妙，
凡在近旁侧耳的人
都觉得那歌悦耳动听；
连群鸟也收住音符，静听他的声音。

===Original===
King Hagen heard him gladly,
 and with him all his men:
 The song of the Danish Horant
 friends for him did gain.

===Chinese===
哈根（Hagen）国王听得欢喜，
他麾下的众人亦然：
丹麦人霍兰特的歌声
为他赢得了诸多伙伴；

===Original===
 Likewise the queenly mother
 hearkened with ear befitting,
 As it sounded thro’ the opening
 where she upon the leaded roof was sitting.

===Chinese===
王后般的母亲同样
以相宜的姿态侧耳倾听，
歌声穿过窗洞传来，
她正坐在铅皮铺就的屋顶。

===Original===
Then spake the fair young Hilda:
 “What is it that I hear?
 Just now a song the sweetest
 was thrilling on mine ear,

===Chinese===
随后年轻美丽的希尔达说道：
「我听见的是什么声音？
方才有一支至为甜美的歌，
正颤动着我的耳畔，

===Original===
 That e’er from any singer
 I heard until this hour.
 Would to God in heaven
 my chamberlain to raise such notes had power!”

===Chinese===
在我迄今听过的歌者当中，
从未闻此妙音。
但愿上天的上帝，
赐我内侍唱出这般歌声的本领！」

===Original===
Then she bade them bring her
 him who so sweetly sung;
 Soon as the knight came forward,
 thanks were on her tongue.

===Chinese===
于是她吩咐把那位
唱得如此甜美的人带来；
骑士刚一上前，
道谢的话已到她唇边；

===Original===
 For her with song the evening
 blissfully was ended;
 By Lady Hilda’s women
 the minstrel-knight was carefully befriended.

===Chinese===
为她而歌的这个夜晚
幸福地落下了帷幕；
在希尔达小姐的侍女们那里，
这位歌者骑士受到了殷勤照拂。

===Original===
Then spake the lovely Hilda:
 “Once more you must let us hear
 The songs that you this evening
 have made to us so dear.

===Chinese===
随后可爱的希尔达说道：
「你得再让我们听一听
今晚你的那些歌，
它们已让我们如此倾心。

===Original===
 Truly it were blissful
 every day, at even,
 To hear from you such singing;
 for this would great reward to you be given.”

===Chinese===
倘若每天到了黄昏，
都能听你这样歌唱，
那真是无上的福分；
你也必将得到丰厚的报偿。」

===Original===
“Since you your thanks, fair lady,
 have thus on me bestowed,
 Every day will I gladly
 sing you a song as good;

===Chinese===
「既然你这位美丽的淑女
把谢意如此赐予我，
每天我都乐意
