为你唱一支同样好的歌；

===Original===
 And whoso listens rightly
 shall find his pains departed,
 His cares shall all be lessened,
 and he henceforth will feel himself light-hearted.”

===Chinese===
凡用心聆听的人，
都会觉得愁苦消散，
烦恼尽皆减轻，
从此自觉心情轻快舒坦。」

===Original===
When he his word had given,
 forthwith he left the queen.
 Great reward in Ireland
 did his singing win;

===Chinese===
他许下这番诺言，
随即离开了王后。
在爱尔兰，他的歌声
为他赢得了丰厚的酬庸；

===Original===
 Never in his birthland
 had such to him been meted.
 Thus did the knight from Denmark
 give his help to Hettel, as him befitted.

===Chinese===
在他的出生之邦，
他从未受过这般礼遇。
这位来自丹麦的骑士
就这样为黑特尔效力，恰如其分。

===Original===
Soon as the night was ended,
 with the early dawn of day,
 Horant raised his carol;
 the birds soon stopped their lay,

===Chinese===
黑夜刚刚过去，
趁着破晓的晨光，
霍兰特唱起了颂歌；
鸟儿们随即停了歌唱，

===Original===
 And to his song they listened,
 while in hedges hidden.
 The folk who yet were sleeping
 rested no more, by his sweet tones upbidden.

===Chinese===
它们藏身树篱之间，
静听他的歌声。
尚在酣睡的人们
也被甜美的曲调唤醒，再难安枕。

===Original===
Horant’s song rose softly,
 higher and yet more sweet;
 King Hagen also heard it,
 while near his wife was his seat.

===Chinese===
霍兰特的歌声轻柔升起，
越来越高，也越发甜美；
哈根国王也听见了，
他的座位就在王后近旁。

===Original===
 From out their inner chamber
 drawn to the roof, they waited;
 Their guest of this had warning;
 and Hilda the young gave ear, where she was seated.

===Chinese===
他们从内室走出，
被引上屋顶等候；
客人对此早有知会；
年轻的希尔达也在坐处侧耳谛听。

===Original===
The daughter of wild Hagen
 with her maids around her heard
 From where they sat and listened;
 and now each little bird

===Chinese===
暴烈的哈根的女儿
在一群侍女的环绕中，
坐在原处侧耳倾听；
这时每一只小鸟

===Original===
 Wholly forgot his singing,
 and in the courtyard lighted;
 The warriors hearkened also,
 and well the song of the Danish minstrel greeted.

===Chinese===
全然忘了自己的啼鸣，
纷纷飞落庭院；
武士们也在倾听，
对丹麦歌者的歌喉交口称羡。

===Original===
Thanks to him were given
 by women and by men;
 “But,” said the Danish Fru-te,
 “would that I ne’er again

===Chinese===
妇人和男子都
向他致了谢忱；
「可是，」丹麦人弗鲁特（Fru-te）说道，
「但愿我永远不再

===Original===
 Such songs might hear him singing.
 Whom would he be pleasing?
 To whom is my witless nephew
 such worthless morning-hymns so bent on raising?”

===Chinese===
听他唱这样的歌。
他这是要取悦何人？
我这愚顽的侄子，
究竟执意为谁唱起这等无谓的晨歌？」

===Original===
Then spake King Hagen’s liegemen:
 “My lord, let him be heard;
 There’s none so sick is lying
 but would in truth be cheered,

===Chinese===
随后哈根国王的封臣进言：
「主公，且让他唱来一听；
纵然病榻上的人病得再重，
听了也必得宽慰欢欣，

===Original===
 If to the songs he listened
 which fall from him so sweetly.”
 Said Hagen: “Would to Heaven
 such skill to sing were mine; ’twould glad me greatly.”

===Chinese===
只要听一听那些
自他唇边流出的甜美歌声。」
哈根说：「但愿上天
也赐我这般歌技；那将令我欢欣莫名。」

===Original===
When the knightly minstrel
 three songs to the end had sung,
 No one there who heard him
 thought they were too long,

===Chinese===
当这位歌者骑士
唱完了三支歌，
在场凡听过的人，
没有一个嫌歌太长，

===Original===
 The turn of a hand, not longer,
 they had thought it lasted,
 E’en if they had listened
 while for a thousand miles a horseman hasted.

===Chinese===
只当那不过
是一转手的工夫，
哪怕听歌的当儿，
骑手已疾驰了千里长路。

===Original===
When his song he ended,
 and to leave his seat was seen,
 The youthful, queenly maiden
 more blithe had never been,

===Chinese===
当他的歌声停歇，
他起身将要离席，
那位年轻、王后般的少女
从没有这般欢喜，

===Original===
 Nor decked, at early morning,
 in gayer clothes or better;
 Forthwith the high-born lady
 sent to beg her father now to meet her.

===Chinese===
清晨也从没有穿过
比这更鲜丽更好的衣裳；
高贵的淑女随即
派人去请父亲前来相见。

===Original===
Then came her father quickly,
 and on the maiden looked,
 While, in a mood of sadness,
 her father’s chin she stroked;

===Chinese===
父亲随即赶来，
目光落在少女身上；
她带着一缕愁绪，
轻抚着父亲的下巴；

===Original===
 With her hand she coaxed him,
 to make her word the stronger,
 And said: “My dearest father,
 bid that he at court may sing yet longer.”

===Chinese===
她用手温言哄他，
好教她的话更有分量，
说道：「我最亲爱的父亲，
吩咐他在宫中再唱一场。」

===Original===
He answered: “Best loved daughter,
 if again, at the hour of eve,
 His songs he deigns to sing you,
 a thousand pounds I’ll give.

===Chinese===
他答道：「最疼爱的女儿，
倘若黄昏时分，
他肯再为你唱那些歌，
我便赏他一千镑。

===Original===
 But now a mien so lofty
 these guests of ours are wearing,
 To us ’tis not so pleasant
 here, at court, to give his songs a hearing.”

===Chinese===
可如今我们的这些宾客
一个个神态如此倨傲，
在这宫廷里听他唱歌，
我们心里可不太痛快。」

===Original===
However much she pressed him,
 would the king no longer stay;
 Then strove again young Horant,
 and never on any day,

===Chinese===
任凭她怎样恳求，
国王也不肯再多留；
年轻的霍兰特再度卖力，
而无论在哪一天，

===Original===
 Had his knightly song been better.
 Sick and well together
 All lost their wits in hearing,
 and none could leave who to listen once came hither.

===Chinese===
他的骑士之歌都不曾这般美妙。
病人与健者齐聚，
听得人人神魂颠倒；
但凡来听过一回的，没有一个走得开。

===Original===
The wild beasts in the forest
 let their pasture grow;
 The little worms that creeping
 through grass are wont to go,

===Chinese===
林中的野兽
任凭牧草疯长；
那些惯于爬行
穿草而过的小虫，

===Original===
 The fishes, too, that ever
 amidst the waves were swimming,
 All now stopped to listen;
 the singer’s heart with pride was overbrimming.

===Chinese===
还有永远在波涛中
游动的鱼群，
此刻全都停下倾听；
歌者的心涨满了自豪。

===Original===
Whatever he might sing to them,
 to no one seemed it long;
 Ill vied with his song the choral
 which by priests is sung.

===Chinese===
无论他唱些什么，
谁都不觉其长；
就连祭司们所唱的圣咏，
也难与他的歌较量。

===Original===
 Even the bells no longer
 rang as of yore so sweetly;
 Every one who heard him
 was moved by Horant’s song, and saddened greatly.

===Chinese===
连钟声也不再
像往日那般清甜；
凡听过他歌唱的人，
都为霍兰特的歌所动，怅然满怀。

===Original===
Then begged the lovely maiden
 that he to her be brought;
 Without her father’s knowledge,
 she slyly this besought.

===Chinese===
于是可爱的少女恳求
把歌者带到她跟前；
她背着父亲，
狡黠地暗自相求。

===Original===
 From her mother, Hilda, also
 must the tale be hidden
 That unto her, in her bower,
 unknown to all, the minstrel had been bidden.

===Chinese===
这事也得瞒过她的母亲希尔达，
这段情由要隐而不宣：
歌者曾瞒过众人，
被邀进她的闺阁相见。

===Original===
It was a yielding chamberlain
 who did the wages gain,
 That, for his help, she gave him;
 red gold it was, I ween.

===Chinese===
是一位好说话的内侍
赚得了那笔酬金，
那是她为他的相助所赐；
据我所知，是赤红的黄金。

===Original===
 Glittering and heavy,
 with armlets twelve, full-weighted.
 ’Twas thus within her bower
 the maid, at eventide, the singer greeted.

===Chinese===
金光灿灿，沉甸压手，
另有十二只足重的臂钏。
就这样，黄昏时分，
少女在闺阁中迎见了歌者。

===Original===
By hidden ways he did it;
 Horant was glad indeed
 That such goodwill and kindness,
 at court, had been his meed.

===Chinese===
他抄幽僻的小径前去；
霍兰特着实欢喜，
这般好意与恩遇，
竟在宫廷落到了他手里。

===Original===
 To win her love for his master
 from far had he been faring;
 To his tuneful skill he owed it
 that she such friendly will to him was bearing.

===Chinese===
为替主人赢得她的爱，
他早已远道而来；
多亏这一副歌喉，
她才对他这般厚待。

===Original===
She bade her faithful chamberlain
 to stand before the house;
 That so there might be no one
 who could the threshold cross

===Chinese===
她吩咐忠心的内侍
守在闺阁门首；
这样便不会有一个人
能跨过那道门槛，

===Original===
 Until the songs were ended,
 soon heard with praises truthful.
 None went into her bower
 but Horant only and Morunc the youthful.

===Chinese===
直到歌声唱罢，
随即赢得真心的称许。
进她闺阁的只有
霍兰特和年轻的莫伦克二人而已。

===Original===
She bade the bard be seated:
 “Now sing to me once more,”
 Thus spake the high-born maiden,
 “those songs I heard before.

===Chinese===
她请歌者落座：
「现在再为我唱一回，」
高贵的少女这样说道，
「就唱我先前听过的那些歌。

===Original===
 For this I feel sore craving;
 than aught beside ’tis sweeter
 Unto your lays to listen;
 than any gem or pastime ’tis far better.”

===Chinese===
为此我心渴慕难耐；
听你唱起歌吟，
比什么都更甜美，
远胜任何宝石与消遣。」

===Original===
“If I might dare to sing to you,
 most fair and lovely maid,
 And never need be fearful
 for this to lose my head,

===Chinese===
「倘若我敢为您歌唱，
最美丽可爱的少女，
又不必为此提心吊胆，
担心落得身首异处，

===Original===
 Thro’ your father’s anger,
 never will I falter
 In any wise to serve you,
 if in my master’s land you’ll seek a shelter.”

===Chinese===
那么纵使您父亲震怒，
我也决不踌躇，
总要竭尽所能为您效命，
只要您肯往我国主的国土避居。」

===Original===
He then began a ditty
 of a mermaid of Amilé,
 Which never man nor Christian
 had learned to sing or say,

===Chinese===
他随即唱起一支小调，
唱阿米莱（Amilé）的美人鱼，
从来没有哪个男子或基督徒
学会把它唱出或讲述，

===Original===
 Although he may have heard it
 on some wild, unknown water.
 In this the good knight, Horant,
 gave honor meet at court to Hagen’s daughter.

===Chinese===
尽管他或许曾在
某片蛮荒无名的泽国听过。
好骑士霍兰特凭这一曲，
在宫中向哈根之女致以应得的荣宠。

===Original===
At last, when he the love-song
 had sung unto the end,
 Then said the lovely maiden:
 “Thanks I give, my friend.”

===Chinese===
最后，当他把那支情歌
一直唱到收梢，
可爱的少女说道：
「多谢了，我的朋友。」

===Original===
 She drew a ring from her finger,
 nought of gold were fairer,
 And said: “I give it gladly;
 be this of my goodwill to you the bearer.”

===Chinese===
她从指上褪下一枚戒指，
再没有黄金比它更美，
说道：「我欣然相赠；
愿它替我捎去对你的一片好意。」

===Original===
Now her word she pledged him,
 and with it gave her hand:
 “Should she of a crown be wearer,
 and ever sway the land,

===Chinese===
接着她向他许下信誓，
并把手交到他手中：
「倘若她戴上王冠，
得以统辖这方国土，

===Original===
 That ne’er by the hand of any
 need he be further driven
 Than unto her in her castle;
 there to live in honor would leave be given.”

===Chinese===
任凭何人之手，也永远
不能把他驱逐得更远，
至多退到她城堡中的她跟前；
在那里，他可获准体面地安居。」

===Original===
Of all she pressed upon him
 nothing would he take
 Unless indeed a girdle.
 He said: “Let no man speak,

===Chinese===
她执意赠他的那些礼物，
他一概不肯收受，
只除了那条腰带。
他说：「且别让任何人开口，

===Original===
 And say that I the maiden
 e’er for myself was wooing;
 I will to my master bring her,
 and for this his heart shall be with bliss o’erflowing.”

===Chinese===
说我曾为我自己
向这位少女求过爱；
我要把她带给我的主上，
为此他的心将溢满欢快。」

===Original===
She asked: “Who is thy master?
 By name how is he known?
 Have e’er his liegemen crowned him?
 And any lands doth he own?

===Chinese===
她问：「你的主上是谁？
名号如何称呼？
他的封臣可曾为他加冕？
他拥有何方国土？

===Original===
 For love of thee, most truly,
 goodwill I bear him ever.”
 The knight from Denmark answered:
 “A king so rich and mighty saw I never.”

===Chinese===
看在你的份上，我真心
对他永怀好意。」
来自丹麦的骑士答道：
「这般富强之王，我生平未见。」

===Original===
He said: “To none betray us,
 most fair and lovely maid;
 To thee will I tell most gladly
 what our master said,

===Chinese===
他说：「别向任何人泄露我们，
最美丽可爱的少女；
我极愿告诉你
我们主上的话语，

===Original===
 When from his land we started,
 hither to come at his bidding;
 For thy dear sake, fair lady,
 unto thy father’s land and castle speeding.”

===Chinese===
那是我们离开他的国土、
奉命来此之时所说：
为了亲爱的你，美丽的淑女，
我们兼程奔向你父亲的国土与城堡。」

===Original===
She said: “Then tell me freely
 the errand on which you’re sent
 By him you call your master;
 if my will that way is bent

===Chinese===
她说：「那就坦然告诉我，
你们奉那位你们称作主上的人
差遣前来，
负的是何等使命；倘若我心意如此，

===Original===
 I shall let you know it truly,
 before we yet are parted.”
 But Horant feared wild Hagen,
 and began at court to feel himself faint-hearted.

===Chinese===
在我们分手之前，
我会真真切切地告知你。」
但霍兰特畏惧暴烈的哈根，
在宫廷里渐觉胆怯心虚。

===Original===
To the lady thus he answered:
 “To you he sends this word﻿—
 That his heart for you is longing;
 his love alone is stirred.

===Chinese===
他这样回答那位淑女：
「他托我向你捎话——
他的心为你思慕渴望；
唯你的爱才拨动他的心肠。

===Original===
 For him, I beg, fair lady,
 let now your kindness waken;
 He from other women
 has for your sake his love and longing taken.”

===Chinese===
美丽的小姐，我求你，
快把你的怜悯唤醒；
为了你的缘故，他已把
对其他女子的爱恋尽行抛却。」

===Original===
She said: “May God reward him;
 such love for me he shows.
 If he in birth is my fellow,
 I fain would be his spouse,

===Chinese===
她说：「愿上帝报答他；
他对我怀着这般深情。
倘若他门第与我相当，
我情愿做他的妻室，

===Original===
 If you will deign to sing to me
 every morn and even.”
 He said: “That will I gladly;
 to this no care by you need e’er be given.”

===Chinese===
只要你肯在每天清晨与黄昏
俯身为我歌唱。」
他说：「我乐于应承；
此事你永远不必挂心。」

===Original===
Quoth he to the queenly Hilda:
 “Most fair and high-born maid,
 There daily live with my master,
 and long at court have staid,

===Chinese===
他对王后般的希尔达说：
「最美丽高贵的少女，
同我主上朝夕相处、
久居宫廷的还有一班人，

===Original===
 Twelve minstrels who, before me,
 earn much higher praises;
 But, though sweet their singing,
 my lord, the king, in song still better pleases.”

===Chinese===
那是十二位歌者，他们赢得的
赞誉远在我之上；
但纵使他们的歌喉甜美，
我主上国王唱来犹更动听。」

===Original===
She said: “If your loving master
 in song so skilful be,
 Of longing for him, truly,
 I never can be free;

===Chinese===
她说：「倘若你那位多情的主上
竟有这般歌技，
说实话，我对他的思慕，
从此再也无法止息；

===Original===
 My best of thanks I give him
 for the love he now is showing,
 And, dared I leave my father,
 gladly from here would I with you be going.”

===Chinese===
他如今表露的这份爱意，
请代我致以最诚挚的谢忱；
只要我敢于离开父亲，
便乐意随你们从此地登程。」

===Original===
Then spake the knightly Morunc:
 “Lady, with us there are
 Warriors full seven hundred:
 our weal or woe they share,

===Chinese===
随后骑士莫伦克开口：
「小姐，我们那里
足有七百名武士：
我们的祸福与他们共当，

===Original===
 And each for this is ready;
 if once in our hands we have you,
 Know you nor fear nor sorrow
 lest we to meet wild Hagen’s wrath should leave you.”

===Chinese===
人人皆愿为此效命；
一旦你到了我们手中，
便无须恐惧忧愁，
莫怕我们撇下你去承受暴烈哈根的怒火。」

===Original===
He said: “From Hagen’s kingdom
 we wish forthwith to go;
 Therefore beg your father
 the kindness to us to show,

===Chinese===
他说：「我们想要即刻
离开哈根的王国；
因此请恳求你的父亲，
对我们施以恩泽，

===Original===
 Youthful, high-born maiden,
 that he and your queenly mother
 Will deign our bark to look on;
 and you must also come, e’en if no other.”

===Chinese===
年少高贵的少女，
请他与王后般的令堂
俯允一观我们的船；
纵使别无他人随行，你也务必到场。」

===Original===
“That will I do most gladly,
 if my father’s leave you have;
 Of him and those about him
 this boon you now must crave,

===Chinese===
「这事我万分乐意，
只要你们求得我父亲允准；
你们须向他和他的近侍
恳请这一恩典，

===Original===
 That I and my maidens also
 may ride to the shore some morning.
 If he shall grant your wishes,
 three days before, of the time you must give us warning.”

===Chinese===
让我和我的侍女们
也能在某个清晨骑马去到海边。
倘若他允准所请，
务须提前三日把日子向我等通传。」

===Original===
The first of all the chamberlains
 was wont, and had a right,
 Often to be with the maidens.
 Just then, this very knight

===Chinese===
内侍中的首席
素有惯例，也有权柄，
常到少女们跟前走动。
恰在此时，这位骑士

===Original===
 There had come for pastime,
 and to give to them his greeting;
 There found he Horant and Morunc;
 well might they fear some harm was their lives awaiting.

===Chinese===
又来此消遣，
向她们问安致意；
他当场撞见了霍兰特与莫伦克；
两人大可担心性命堪虞。

===Original===
He said to Lady Hilda:
 “Who are they sitting here?”
 From the lord so hot and hasty
 was never such wrath to fear.

===Chinese===
他对希尔达小姐说道：
「坐在这里的是什么人？」
这位急躁火爆的爵爷，
从未叫人见过这般骇人的怒容。

===Original===
 He said: “Whoe’er allowed you
 to come into this bower?
 Whoso in this hath helped you
 ne’er showed you falser friendship to this hour.”

===Chinese===
他说：「是谁准许你们
进到这间闺阁？
谁在这事上帮了你们，
谁就是至今待你们最虚伪的朋友。」

===Original===
She said: “Now soothe your anger:
 in peace pray let them live.
 If to yourself great evil
 you do not wish to give,

===Chinese===
她说：「且请息怒：
求你让他们平安活着。
倘若你不想
给自家招来大祸，

===Original===
 You must unseen by any,
 them to their rooms be bringing;
 It else hath helped but little
 that his knightly songs the minstrel here was singing.”

===Chinese===
你就得避人耳目，
把他们送回各自的房中；
否则那歌者在此唱的
骑士歌谣，就全然无用了。」

===Original===
“Is this the knight,” he asked her,
 “they say so well can sing?
 E’en such a minstrel know I:
 never hath any king

===Chinese===
「这位骑士，」他问她，
「就是人们说极善歌唱的那位？
这样一位歌者我正好认得：
从未有哪个国王

===Original===
 Had a braver fighter.
 My father and his mother
 Were children of one father;
 worthier knight than he there’s not another.”

===Chinese===
有过比他更勇的战士。
我父亲和他的母亲
同是一位父亲所生；
再没有比他更高贵的骑士了。」

===Original===
The maid began to ask him:
 “Tell me, then, his name.”
 He said: “Men call him Horant;
 from the Danish land he came.

===Chinese===
少女开始问他：
「那就请告诉我他的名字。」
他说：「人们唤他霍兰特；
他从丹麦之地而来。

===Original===
 Although no crown he weareth,
 he yet for one is fitted:
 We now know not each other,
 but once at Hettel’s court our love we plighted.”

===Chinese===
虽然他不曾戴上王冠，
却自有王者之器：
如今我们彼此素不相识，
但从前在黑特尔的宫廷曾结下情谊。」

===Original===
When Morunc, too, was telling
 that erst, in his fatherland,
 He also had been outlawed,
 his heart was sorely pained.

===Chinese===
当莫伦克也讲起
自己当初在故国
同样遭到放逐，
他的心痛苦万分。

===Original===
 His eyes with tears were welling,
 and now were overflowing;
 Then the queenly lady
 kindly looked on him, her sorrow showing.

===Chinese===
他眼中泪水涌起，
此刻夺眶而落；
王后般的贵妇
慈和地望着他，面露哀色。

===Original===
Then saw the chamberlain also
 how that his eyes were wet.
 He said: “Most worthy lady,
 these friends whom here we meet

===Chinese===
内侍也看到了
他的双眼湿润。
他说：「最尊贵的夫人，
我们在此遇见的这两位朋友，

===Original===
 I know to be my kinsmen;
 help now that all goes rightly
 With both these worthy champions:
 most careful will I be to keep them fitly.”

===Chinese===
我认出是我的亲族；
请帮着让诸事顺遂，
护持这两位可敬的勇士；
我必尽心竭力，妥善照拂。」

===Original===
Much for them he sorrowed,
 and felt heart-pain, forsooth;
 “Durst I before my ladies,
 I would kiss upon the mouth

===Chinese===
他为他们深深忧伤，
确然感到了心痛；
「若不是碍着诸位夫人，
我真想一一亲吻

===Original===
 Each of these knights so worthy.
 The days indeed are many
 Since tidings of King Hettel
 I could from a Hegeling ask, or learn from any.”

===Chinese===
这两位可敬骑士的嘴唇。
已经有许多日子，
我不曾从哪位黑格林人（Hegeling）
或旁人口中，问得黑特尔国王的消息。」

===Original===
Then spake the maiden further:
 “Since these thy kinsmen be,
 Now so much the dearer
 are they as guests to me.

===Chinese===
随后少女又说道：
「既然他们是你的亲族，
那他们作为宾客，
如今便越发为我所重。

===Original===
 Known unto my father
 thou should’st quickly make them;
 They will not then so hastily
 to their homes afar across the sea betake them.”

===Chinese===
你应当赶快让他们
与我父亲相识；
那样他们便不会
匆匆渡海，回到远方的家园。」

===Original===
A busy talk began they,
 those two young heroes brave;
 Morunc unto the chamberlain
 his mind most freely gave.

===Chinese===
两位年轻勇武的英雄
就此恳切交谈；
莫伦克向这位内侍
毫无保留地吐露了心怀。

===Original===
 He said for Lady Hilda
 they came within those borders;
 And that their master Hettel
 to bring her back had sent them, as her warders.

===Chinese===
他说他们正是为希尔达小姐
才进入这片疆土；
他们的主上黑特尔
遣他们前来，要如护卫般将她带回。

===Original===
Then said to them the chamberlain:
 “A twofold care I feel,
 As liegeman of my master,
 and to help you, too, as well.

===Chinese===
随后内侍对他们说：
「我心怀双重的忧虑：
既为我主上封臣之责，
也为给你们帮上一把。

===Original===
 How could I turn his anger,
 if he knew you now were seeking
 To win his maiden daughter?
 Never from here could you your way be taking.”

===Chinese===
倘若他知道你们如今
正求娶他年少的女儿，
我怎能平息他的怒火？
那你们休想从这里脱身。」

===Original===
Then spake the knightly Horant:
 “Hear well what now I say;
 In four days’ time to Hagen,
 we will come, and him will pray

===Chinese===
随后骑士霍兰特说道：
「请听我如今所言；
四天之后我们将
觐见哈根，向他恳求，

===Original===
 That we may leave his kingdom,
 if such may be his pleasure.
 The king will then make ready
 gifts for us of clothes, as well as treasure.

===Chinese===
求他允准我们离开王国，
倘若这正合他的心意。
国王那时自会备下
衣装与财宝，作为赠礼。」

===Original===
“We will ask for nothing further,
 (help you here must lend,)
 But that Hagen shall be willing,
 as well beseems a friend,

===Chinese===
「此外我们别无所求，
（只须你从旁相助，）
只求哈根肯念
友人的情分，

===Original===
 To come to the shore to see us,
 my lady with him riding﻿—
 His wife, the high-born Hilda;
 there to see the ship in which we’re biding.

===Chinese===
肯到海边来看望我们，
我家小姐与他同骑——
还有他高贵的妻子希尔达；
来看看我们栖身的那条船。」

===Original===
“Might we in this be lucky,
 our toil we well shall spend;
 And, with a happy outcome,
 our sorrows have an end.

===Chinese===
「倘若我们这般走运，
辛苦便不会虚掷；
一旦功成圆满，
忧愁便告终结。

===Original===
 If only to the seashore
 he will ride with his daughter,
 We well shall be rewarded
 at home by our master Hettel, for whom we sought her.”

===Chinese===
只要他肯带着女儿
一同骑马到海边，
我们在家乡必得犒赏——
来自我们为她求娶的主上黑特尔。」

===Original===
Then from out the castle
 they were led by the crafty man,
 So that the kingly Hagen
 mistrusted not their plan.

===Chinese===
随后他们被引出城堡，
引路的是那机敏之人，
君王哈根
对他们的图谋毫无觉察。

===Original===
 When, for their floating shelter,
 they the courtyard quitted,
 All they had done for their master
 should not, I ween, by him at home be slighted.

===Chinese===
当他们离开庭院，
回到那浮动的栖身之所，
我料想，他们为主上所做的一切，
在家中决不会被他轻慢。

===Original===
They told the aged Wâ-te
 what yet to none was known:
 They said the high-born maiden
 her love did freely own

===Chinese===
他们告诉年迈的瓦特（Wâ-te）
一件尚无人知晓的事：
他们说，那位高贵的少女
已坦然吐露了她的爱，

===Original===
 Unto their master, Hettel,
 for whom they now had sought her;
 They talked with wise old Wâ-te
 how best to bring her home across the water.

===Chinese===
爱的正是他们的主上黑特尔，
他们正是为他前来求娶；
他们同睿智的老瓦特商议，
如何载她渡海，安然归家。

===Original===
Then spake the aged Wâ-te:
 “Were she once outside the gate,
 And I the lovely maiden
 there might only meet,

===Chinese===
随后年迈的瓦特说道：
「只要她一出城门，
而我又能在那里
迎见那位可爱的少女，

===Original===
 However hard the struggle
 that there we had with the foeman,
 To cross her father’s threshold
 none again should see that lovely woman.”

===Chinese===
无论我们与来敌
那一战多么艰险，
世人再不能见她
跨回她父亲的门槛。」

===Original===
Their plot, well-laid and crafty,
 to no one did they break,
 But slyly made them ready
 their homeward way to take.

===Chinese===
他们那周密狡黠的图谋
不曾向任何人泄露，
只暗自整装，
预备踏上归途。

===Original===
 This they told the warriors
 on board their ship there lying;
 Not loth were they to hear it,
 for now to sail the weary men were sighing.

===Chinese===
他们把这告诉了
船上待命的武士；
众人听罢正中下怀，
困乏的汉子们早已盼着扬帆。

===Original===
They quickly brought together
 such goods as they did own;
 Then, in stillness whispered,
 their hidden thought made known.

===Chinese===
他们迅速收拾起
各自所有的财物；
随后在静默中低语，
把深藏的图谋倾吐。

===Original===
 Later, throughout Ireland,
 it was mourned, with bitter wailing;
 Though woe it brought to Hagen,
 the Hegeling’s greatness would it soon be telling.

===Chinese===
后来，整个爱尔兰
都为这事恸哭悲号；
它虽给哈根带来灾殃，
却即将彰显黑格林人的强盛。

===Original===
Upon the fourth day’s morning
 to court they bravely rode,
 With new and well-cut clothing;
 none better ever showed.

===Chinese===
第四天的清晨，
他们意气昂扬地骑向宫廷，
一身剪裁合体的新衣；
从未见过更体面的排场。

===Original===
 Then the guests there gathered
 were their wishes speaking;
 Of the king and all his liegemen
 they asked that they their leave might now be taking.

===Chinese===
随即齐聚的宾客们
各自陈明心愿；
他们向国王和全体封臣
请求此刻告辞启程。

===Original===
Then spake to them King Hagen:
 “Why will you leave my land?
 So far as I was able,
 I have striven for this end﻿—

===Chinese===
随后哈根国王对他们说：
「你们为何要离开我的国土？
凡是我力所能及，
我一直力求做到这步——

===Original===
 That you within my kingdom
 should meet with kindness only;
 Now would you hence be sailing,
 leaving me here, to lead a life all lonely.”

===Chinese===
让你们在我的王国里
只遇上善待与恩慈；
如今你们却要扬帆而去，
留我在此度孤单的日子。」

===Original===
To him old Wâ-te answered:
 “The Hegeling king, our lord,
 Has sent to call us homeward;
 he will not hear a word

===Chinese===
老瓦特回答他道：
「黑格林人之王、我们的主上
已传召我们回乡；
他不肯再听任何话语，

===Original===
 Of aught but our forgiveness.
 Then, too, for us are mourning
 Those we left behind us;
 we therefore soon must back on our way be turning.”

===Chinese===
除了宽恕我们这一桩。
再说，我们留在身后的那些人
也正为我们哀伤；
所以我们很快就得掉转航向。」

===Original===
Then said to him wild Hagen:
 “Your loss my heart doth break;
 Horses and fine clothing
 deign, for my love, to take,

===Chinese===
随后暴烈的哈根对他说：
「你们一去我心欲碎；
看在我的情分，
请收下骏马和华衣，

===Original===
 With gold and costly jewels.
 Right well it doth beseem me
 For all your gifts to pay you;
 in this shall no one ever dare to blame me.”

===Chinese===
连同黄金和贵重的珍宝。
回报你们的厚礼，
于我正相宜；
在这事上，没人敢对我非议。」

===Original===
Then said the hoary Wâ-te:
 “Too rich am I to-day
 That I the gold you give us
 should wish to take away.

===Chinese===
随后白发的瓦特说道：
「如今我已太过富有，
您赠我们的这些黄金，
我不愿再带走。

===Original===
 Our master, whose forgiveness
 our friends have lately won us,
 The rich and mighty Hettel,
 in such a deed would truly never own us.

===Chinese===
我们的朋友新近为我们
赢得了主上的宽恕，
富有而强大的黑特尔，
决不会认下做这等事的我们。」

===Original===
“One thing we have yet further,
 my lord, to ask of thee;
 (If you this kindness show us,
 a worthy boon, ’twill be.)

===Chinese===
「我们还有一件事，
主公啊，要向你求恳；
（您若赐下这份恩典，
那便是莫大的恩惠。）

===Original===
 It is that you shall witness
 how well we can be feasting;
 Of food for hearty eaters
 we have in store what might three years be lasting.

===Chinese===
那就是请您亲自见证
我们宴饮的本事；
我们备下的口粮，
足供大肚汉吃上三年不止。」

===Original===
“To all who ask we give it,
 for hence we sail o’er the deep;
 May God long give you honor,
 yourself may He ever keep.

===Chinese===
「谁来讨要我们都给，
因为我们就要驶向深海；
愿上帝长久赐您荣耀，
愿他永远将您护佑。

===Original===
 We now betake us homeward,
 we here may bide no longer;
 Now may you and your kinsfolk
 ride with us to our ship; no guard were stronger.

===Chinese===
我们此刻便启程回乡，
在这里再不能久留；
请您携您的亲眷，
随我们同去船上一游；再没有比这更强的护卫。」

===Original===
“If but your lovely daughter,
 and with her my lady, your wife,
 Shall look upon our riches,
 glad will it make our life,

===Chinese===
「只要您可爱的女儿，
连同夫人——您的妻子，
肯来看一看我们的财宝，
我们的生活便会欢欣，

===Original===
 And dear to us forever.
 If this to us be granted,
 Great and good King Hagen,
 from you no other gifts shall e’er be wanted.”

===Chinese===
你们也将永远为我们所珍爱。
伟大而仁德的哈根王啊，
只要此事蒙您恩准，
我们再也不需您别的馈赠。」

===Original===
Then to his guests he answered,
 with seemly, well-bred mien:
 “Since you are now so earnest,
 at early morn shall be seen

===Chinese===
随后他以得体优雅的仪态
答复他的宾客：
「既然你们这般恳切，
明日清晨你们将见到

===Original===
 A hundred mares made ready,
 saddled for woman or maiden;
 I, too, will ride down with them;
 right glad am I that to see your ship I’m bidden.”

===Chinese===
一百匹鞍辔齐备的母马，
供夫人小姐们乘骑；
我自己也将随同前往；
承蒙相邀一观贵船，我欢喜不尽。」

===Original===
Then for the night they left him,
 and rode away to the shore.
 Then up on the beach was carried
 of wine a goodly store,

===Chinese===
当夜他们辞别了他，
骑马返回海滩。
随后一批美酒被搬运上岸，
在沙滩上堆放，

===Original===
 That in the bark was lying;
 for food they were not lacking.
 By this the ship was lightened;
 wisely had Fru-te of Denmark his plans been making.

===Chinese===
那些酒原本存载于大船；
食物也不欠缺。
船身借此减了负载；
丹麦人弗鲁特早已把筹划安排得明白。

## Tale VII How the Maidens Came to See the Ship, and Were Carried to Hettel’s Kingdom / 第七诗章 少女们如何来看船并被载往黑特尔的王国

===Original===
Hilda, with her father and mother and her maidens, rides down to the shore; she and the maidens go on board the ship. While Hagen’s attention is occupied, the vessel sets sail, and they arrive in Hettel’s land.

===Chinese===
希尔达（Hilda）偕父母及众侍女骑马前往海边；她与侍女们登上了大船。趁哈根（Hagen）注意力被引开之际，船只扬帆起航，一行人抵达了黑特尔（Hettel）的国土。

===Original===
Early on the morrow,
 after the mass was said,
 To don their richest clothing
 strove each wife and maid:

===Chinese===
次日一大清早，
弥撒方才念完，
夫人和少女们个个
争相穿上自己最华贵的衣裳：

===Original===
 A throng of these King Hagen
 to the sandy shore was leading;
 And with them riding gayly
 a thousand stalwart Irish knights were speeding.

===Chinese===
哈根国王率领着
这样一群人马，前往沙滩海岸；
与他们一道欢快并辔驰骋的，
还有一千名剽悍的爱尔兰骑士。

===Original===
Within the town of Ballian
 the guests had heard the mass.
 Of all the woe and sorrow,
 that soon would come to pass,

===Chinese===
在巴利安（Ballian）城中，
宾客们已听完弥撒。
至于不久便要临头的
那些灾殃与悲哀，

===Original===
 Hagen as yet knew nothing:
 little honor was left him
 By his guests’ withdrawal;
 this of his fair and well-born child bereft him.

===Chinese===
哈根尚一无所知：
宾客们的抽身离去
没给他留下多少体面；
这一去还夺走了他美丽高贵的女儿。

===Original===
When now they all had ridden
 to the ships upon the strand,
 Queen Hilda and her ladies
 were lifted down on the sand.

===Chinese===
当他们一行全数骑抵
滩头的大船近旁，
王后希尔达与众贵妇
被搀扶下来，立足沙滩。

===Original===
 The young and lovely maidens
 to see the ships were taken:
 The traders’ booths were open,
 and the goods did wonder great in the queen awaken.

===Chinese===
年轻可爱的少女们
被领着前去观看大船：
商贩的货棚一齐敞开，
满目货色令王后惊叹连连。

===Original===
Many fair-wrought jewels
 lay in sight in the shops,
 Such as men prize highly;
 King Hagen to see them stops,

===Chinese===
许多打造精美的珍宝
在铺中一览无遗，
件件皆世人珍爱之物；
哈根国王驻足观赏，

===Original===
 And many with him also:
 soon as the goods were shown them,
 The maidens, too, must see them,
 and rings and bands of gold were pressed upon them.

===Chinese===
随他而来的众人也围拢过去：
货物一经亮出，
少女们也须前来见识，
金戒指与黄金镯钏更被纷纷塞到她们手上。

===Original===
To see the sights King Hagen
 into a boat had gone:
 Not all the booths were open,
 nor all the goods were shown,

===Chinese===
为游赏各处景致，
哈根国王登上一条小船：
这时货棚尚未尽数开张，
货品也未统统展出，

===Original===
 When Wâ-te’s men heaved anchor
 up from the sea-sands deftly,
 And Hilda with her maidens
 was borne away from the land of her fathers swiftly.

===Chinese===
便在此时，瓦特（Wâ-te）的部下
麻利地从海沙中起了锚，
希尔达连同她的侍女们
就此被飞快载离了她父亲的国土。

===Original===
For no one’s hate and anger
 Wâ-te greatly cares;
 Little he recks what happens
 to the shops of costly wares:

===Chinese===
任凭他人怀恨动怒，
瓦特都全不在意；
那些摆满贵重货物的铺子
落得什么下场，他也毫不挂心：

===Original===
 Hilda, the queenly mother,
 was sundered from her daughter;
 The men, in the ship long hidden,
 upsprang and sorrow made for Hagen on the water.

===Chinese===
王后般的母亲希尔达
就此与女儿两相分离；
潜藏船中已久的武士
纷纷跃出，在水上给哈根平添悲戚。

===Original===
Then the sails were hoisted,
 and ’twas seen that they were set:
 From the ship they threw the foemen,
 who thoroughly were wet,

===Chinese===
随后船帆升挂起来，
眼看帆已张满：
他们把船上的敌手掷入水中，
那些人个个浑身湿透，

===Original===
 Like sea-birds on the water,
 when near the sands they flutter.
 For her daughter dear-belovèd
 sorrow and anger the queen aloud did utter.

===Chinese===
好似海上的水鸟，
在沙滩近旁扑翅挣扎。
为了她挚爱的女儿，
王后放声倾吐悲伤与怒火。

===Original===
When the weaponed fighters
 by Hagen there were seen,
 Then, in truth, how scornful
 and wrathful was his mien!

===Chinese===
当那些持械的战士
被哈根看在眼里，
说实话，他那时的面色
何其轻蔑，何其震怒！

===Original===
 “Now bring to me my long-spear,
 to feel it I will teach them;
 They all shall die full quickly
 when my strong right arm with that shall reach them!”

===Chinese===
「快把我的长矛取来，
我要教他们领教领教它的滋味；
等我强壮的右臂挥矛及身，
他们个个都得立刻毙命！」

===Original===
Boldly then spoke Morunc:
 “Be not so much in haste!
 Though now you think to fight us,
 and to rush on us so fast

===Chinese===
莫伦克（Morunc）朗声答道：
「且莫这般性急！
纵然你此刻想同我们厮杀，
想这般迅猛地朝我们扑来，

===Original===
 With a thousand well-armed foemen,
 we yet will overthrow them,
 And fling them into the water;
 a damp, cold lodging we will quickly show them!”

===Chinese===
纵然领着一千名披坚执锐的敌兵，
我们仍要把他们尽数掀翻，
把他们掷进水里；
让他们快快领受一处又湿又冷的居所！」

===Original===
Still, brave Hagen’s followers
 the fight would not give o’er;
 The water shone and glistened
 with the armor that they wore;

===Chinese===
然而哈根麾下勇武的部众
仍不肯就此罢战；
他们身披的甲胄
映得水面熠熠生辉；

===Original===
 Then they drew their long-swords,
 spears were thickly flying;
 But oars were dipped full quickly,
 and fast the boats away from the shore were hieing.

===Chinese===
随即他们拔出长剑，
长矛密集横飞；
而船桨急忙入水，
船只飞也似逃离了海岸。

===Original===
The bold and daring Wâ-te
 from the sands had given a bound
 Into a well-manned row-boat;
 loud did his mail resound,

===Chinese===
剽悍无畏的瓦特
从沙滩上一跃而起，
跳进一条满员的桨艇；
他一身锁甲铿然作响，

===Original===
 As he, with fifty warriors,
 after Hilda hasted:
 Hagen’s careless followers
 now must rouse themselves, no time they wasted.

===Chinese===
他带着五十名武士，
兼程追赶希尔达：
哈根那些疏于防范的部下
此刻只得奋起直追，半刻也不敢耽搁。

===Original===
Onward came King Hagen;
 his fighting-gear he wore,
 And a heavy sword, the sharpest,
 he proudly with him bore;

===Chinese===
哈根国王赶了上来；
他披挂着整套战装，
还昂然带着一柄沉重的剑，
是天下最锋利的兵刃；

===Original===
 But now the aged Wâ-te
 almost too long had waited;
 Wild and grim was Hagen,
 and high his spear he raised ’gainst his foe belated.

===Chinese===
可年迈的瓦特
这一回险些等得太久；
哈根狂暴而狰狞，
高擎长矛，直指他那迟误的敌手。

===Original===
Loudly then he shouted,
 and bade his men make haste;
 None of all his followers
 would he allow to rest,

===Chinese===
随后他高声呼喝，
催令部下火速赶路；
他麾下所有从人，
他一个也不许歇息，

===Original===
 Hoping these guests, now fleeing,
 who had been such traitors,
 Might be with speed o’ertaken,
 and either should be slain, or bound in fetters.

===Chinese===
只盼那些此刻逃遁的宾客——
那伙背信弃义之徒——
能被飞速追上，
或就地处决，或锁进镣铐。

===Original===
The king had now about him
 fighters many and brave,
 But yet he could not follow
 across the wild sea-wave;

===Chinese===
国王身边此时
聚集了众多勇猛的战士，
但他终究无法
横渡狂涛巨浪去追赶；

===Original===
 His ships were all unready,
 and many of them leaking,
 When now he would be sailing;
 of Hagen’s blame for this were all soon speaking.

===Chinese===
他的船只全未备齐，
许多还在渗水，
偏偏他此刻就要出海；
不久人人都在议论哈根在这事上的过失。

===Original===
On the gravelly seashore standing,
 no other way he knew
 But that more ships be builded
 for him and his liegemen true,

===Chinese===
他伫立在砾石的海滩上，
除此之外别无良策，
只能下令增造船只，
供他和忠诚的封臣们乘用，

===Original===
 And workmen called together,
 who must therein be speedy:
 All came who now were able,
 and these he found to be both skilled and ready.

===Chinese===
并召集起工匠，
勒令他们火速赶造：
凡能出力的都赶来了，
他发现这些人个个手艺精熟、干劲十足。

===Original===
Upon the seventh morning,
 there left the Irish land
 The men sent forth by Hettel
 to ask for Hilda’s hand,

===Chinese===
第七天的清晨，
离开爱尔兰的，
正是黑特尔先前派出的那些人，
他们曾前去向希尔达求婚，

===Original===
 And bring to him the lady.
 They were a thousand barely;
 Hagen brought against them
 thirty hundred men, if reckoned fairly.

===Chinese===
如今要把那位淑女带回他身边。
他们的人数将将一千；
哈根调来与之相抗的，
据实一算，足有三千人马。

===Original===
The daring knights of Denmark
 sent men home before,
 To carry word to Hettel
 that Hagen’s child they bore,

===Chinese===
胆气逼人的丹麦骑士们
先行遣人回国报信，
给黑特尔捎去音讯：
他们已带着哈根的女儿，

===Original===
 And to his land would bring her,
 with honor him befitting.
 Though now they little thought it,
 still harder work erelong must they be meeting.

===Chinese===
要以配得上他的荣光
把她带回他的国土。
虽然此刻他们还很少想到，
不久便有更险恶的苦战要相逢。

===Original===
To them their master, Hettel,
 in happy mood then spoke:
 “My sorrows now are over.
 Great toils my liegemen took

===Chinese===
他们的主上黑特尔
满怀欣悦地开口说道：
「我的忧愁如今已到头。
我的封臣们为我吃了大苦，

===Original===
 For me in Hagen’s kingdom,
 and now have brought me gladness;
 Since they on their errand left me,
 fear for their doom has filled my heart with sadness.

===Chinese===
在哈根的王国里历尽劳瘁，
如今却给我带来了欢欣；
自从他们奉使命离我而去，
对他们厄运的忧惧便郁积我心。

===Original===
“Dear friends, if with your tidings
 you have not me betrayed,
 And do not tell me falsely
 that you have seen the maid

===Chinese===
「亲爱的朋友们，倘若你们的音讯
并非诓骗于我，
倘若你们并非虚言相告，
说已亲眼见到那位少女

===Original===
 Near to my land and kingdom,
 and in my friends’ safe-keeping,
 For your tale will I reward you,
 and gladly will your praise be ever speaking.”

===Chinese===
已来到我的国土疆界近旁，
安处于我友朋的守护之中，
那我必为这消息犒赏你们，
也乐于永远把你们称颂。」

===Original===
They said: “No lie we tell you,
 that we the maid have seen;
 But when we miles had measured,
 the daughter of the queen

===Chinese===
他们答道：「我们没有一句谎言，
我们确已见到那位少女；
只是当我们量过了迢迢长路，
王后的女儿

===Original===
 Sadly said, for our welfare
 she feared, and was heavy-hearted,
 Lest the king, her father,
 to follow with his ships e’en then had started.”

===Chinese===
忧伤地说，她为
我们的安危担忧，心怀沉重，
唯恐她的父亲国王
彼时已率船队随后追发。」

===Original===
For the tidings, Hettel gave them
 a hundred marks in worth;
 For all his knights there gathered,
 men at once brought forth

===Chinese===
为了这消息，黑特尔赏给他们
价值一百马克的财物；
为款待齐聚一堂的众骑士，
人们随即搬出

===Original===
 Swords as well as helmets,
 and shields for them were bidden:
 Thus from Hettel’s castle
 they went, as if to court, to bring the maiden.

===Chinese===
宝剑连同头盔，
还为他们订制了盾牌：
就这样，他们出了黑特尔的城堡，
如赴宫廷盛典一般，前去迎迓少女。

===Original===
All the men he was able
 Hettel for this now sought;
 Greatly was he hoping,
 and much thereof he thought,

===Chinese===
凡是他能罗致到的壮士，
黑特尔此番都去延请；
他热切地盼望着，
也为这事思虑万端，

===Original===
 So great a host to muster,
 and these so well outfitted,
 That never to king’s fair daughter
 so fine a welcome might again be meted.

===Chinese===
要集结起这么一支大军，
且装束配备得这般精良，
好让国王的美丽女儿
从此再无第二回如此盛美的迎迓。

===Original===
In haste were all then bidden
 who ought with him to go;
 They still made ready slowly,
 till gifts he should bestow

===Chinese===
凡是应当随他同行的，
都得了火急的传召；
众人却仍从容不迫地整备，
只等他把赏赐颁下——

===Original===
 Of all things that they needed;
 they for this were waiting.
 At length by him were gathered
 a thousand men or more, for Hilda’s greeting.

===Chinese===
赏赐他们所需的一切；
众人正为此等候。
到头来随他聚齐的，
足有一千余人，前去迎候希尔达。

===Original===
Gay were they in clothing﻿—
 ’gainst this could none say nay﻿—
 Poor as well as wealthy
 were shining in war-array:

===Chinese===
他们的衣装何等鲜丽——
这一点无人能够否认——
无论贫者还是富者，
个个在戎装里神采焕发：

===Original===
 To bring the lovely ladies
 to their new home and dwelling
 Were Hettel’s lieges earnest;
 with lofty hopes of this their hearts were swelling.

===Chinese===
为把可爱的淑女们
迎往她们的新家新居，
黑特尔的封臣们个个恳切；
崇高的期望在他们胸中鼓荡。

===Original===
Soon as they left the castle,
 shouts the land did fill,
 As they their way were making
 thro’ lowland and o’er hill;

===Chinese===
大军方才出了城堡，
欢呼之声便充盈大地，
一路行进之间，
穿过低谷，越过山丘；

===Original===
 Men saw upon the pathways
 crowds still thronging nearer:
 Hettel hastened forward,
 to see the maid, than every other dearer.

===Chinese===
人们只见大小道上，
人群仍旧蜂拥近前：
黑特尔催马疾进，
去看那比谁都更亲爱的少女。

===Original===
At last the aged Wâ-te,
 the knight from the Sturmisch land,
 Had reached the Waalisch marches
 and stepped upon the sand.

===Chinese===
终于，年迈的瓦特，
那位施图尔姆兰（Sturmisch）之地的骑士，
已抵达瓦尔河（Waalisch）畔的边地，
双足踏上了沙滩。

===Original===
 There on the shore were gathered
 the sailors, water-weary;
 Shelter they sought for Hilda,
 and in a friendly land were glad to tarry.

===Chinese===
岸边聚拢着
被水路磨乏了的水手；
他们为希尔达觅得安身之所，
在这片友好的土地上欣然驻留。

===Original===
Stakes for tents were driven
 near to the broad sea-flood
 By the followers of Wâ-te;
 they were in happy mood.

===Chinese===
瓦特的部众
在浩渺潮水近旁
打下营帐的桩木；
人人心情欢畅。

===Original===
 Erelong the news was bruited,
 and soon to them was given,
 That Hettel, king of the Hegelings,
 had left his home, and now was near them even;

===Chinese===
不多时消息传扬开来，
他们随即得到通报：
黑格林人（Hegeling）之王黑特尔
已离开家园，此刻已近在他们身旁；

===Original===
And that he with many liegemen
 was riding down to the shore,
 To meet his well-belovèd.
 Now hoped the maids the more

===Chinese===
又听说他正带着众多封臣
骑马向海边而来，
要与他的心上人相会。
侍女们于是愈发盼望，

===Original===
 That she with greatest honor
 should, as her birth befitted,
 Be brought into his kingdom.
 No more the thought of strife their hearts affrighted.

===Chinese===
盼她能享与她出身相称的
至高荣耀，
被迎进他的王国。
争战的忧思再不使她们心惊。

===Original===
The guests for nothing wanted,
 they had both wine and food;
 Those who were living near them
 freely on them bestowed

===Chinese===
宾客们无所匮乏，
美酒与饮食俱足；
住在左近的人们
都慷慨地向他们馈赠

===Original===
 The best that they were able;
 the wants of all they heeded;
 Whate’er they had they gave them,
 and left them not to lack for aught they needed.

===Chinese===
各自所能拿出的上品；
众人所需无不照应；
凡自家有的都拿了出来，
不让他们短缺任何所需之物。

===Original===
Hettel now drew nearer
 to those who had reached his land;
 And with him, gathered hastily,
 the strong and goodly band,

===Chinese===
黑特尔这时渐渐行近
那些已然抵达他国土的人；
随他匆匆聚合的，
还有一支雄壮齐整的队伍，

===Original===
 Drawn from his father’s kingdom.
 They came bedecked so gaily,
 And in such glittering armor,
 the guests looked on full glad, and praised them freely.

===Chinese===
是从他自己父亲的王国抽调而来。
他们装束得那般鲜丽，
甲胄那般辉煌，
宾客们看得满心欢喜，交口称赏。

===Original===
Then the men of the Hegelings
 came down upon the plain,
 And soon the rushing riders
 a tilting-match began;

===Chinese===
随后黑格林人的部众
下到平原旷野，
疾驰的骑手们不多时
便开始了一场马上比武；

===Original===
 All with youthful boldness
 for knightly prizes striving:
 Then came the Danish Fru-te,
 and with him Wâ-te, wise as any living.

===Chinese===
人人怀着青春的豪勇，
争夺骑士的彩赏：
随后丹麦人弗鲁特（Fru-te）到了，
同来的还有智谋冠绝当世的瓦特。

===Original===
They were seen from afar by Hettel;
 happy in heart was he.
 His horse he set a-prancing;
 right glad was he to see

===Chinese===
黑特尔远远望见了他们；
他心中欢喜不尽，
策得座下骏马踏着欢腾的碎步；
他何其高兴地看到

===Original===
 Two of his bravest liegemen,
 sent by him o’er the water,
 With fighters bold to Ireland,
 in hopes to win for him wild Hagen’s daughter.

===Chinese===
他麾下最勇的两员封臣——
他曾遣他们渡过大海，
带着剽悍的战士远赴爱尔兰，
盼着为他赢得暴烈的哈根的女儿。

===Original===
On him, too, looked they gladly,
 their worthy king, so good;
 Each day they spent there with him
 found them in happy mood.

===Chinese===
他们也欣喜地望着他，
望着他们可敬的仁德君王；
在那里伴他度过的每一天，
他们都心旷神怡。

===Original===
 Wâ-te with all his fellows,
 while far away they were living,
 Had known much bitter hardship:
 for this would Hettel now reward be giving.

===Chinese===
瓦特和他全体部属，
在远居异邦的日子里，
曾备尝辛酸苦楚：
为此黑特尔如今要大加酬赏。

===Original===
As he met his friendly liegemen,
 King Hettel wore a smile;
 Then said he to them kindly:
 “Much have I feared erewhile

===Chinese===
与友爱的封臣们相见时，
黑特尔国王面带笑容；
随后他温言对他们说道：
「先前我着实忧惧万分，

===Original===
 For you, my faithful helpers,
 and a heavy heart was bearing,
 Lest in Hagen’s castles
 my men were held, and all were bondage sharing.”

===Chinese===
为你们这些忠实的帮手，
心头压着重重的愁苦，
唯恐我的人被押在哈根的城堡，
尽皆沦为阶下之囚。」

===Original===
Then for love he kissed them,
 both those gray old men;
 His eyes had never rested
 on so glad a sight as then,

===Chinese===
随后他出于慈爱，亲吻了
那两位白发苍苍的老臣；
他的双眼从未停留于
如那时一般欢悦的景象，

===Original===
 Nor on a fairer pasture
 had fed, with longing fonder.
 I ween that never to Hettel
 was aught so full of bliss and sudden wonder.

===Chinese===
也从未怀着更深的渴慕，
饱览过比那更美的原野。
我敢说，黑特尔生平
从未遇过这般充满至福与骤喜之事。

===Original===
Then spake the aged Wâ-te:
 No harm to us was done;
 But yet a sway so mighty
 I ne’er before have known,

===Chinese===
随后年迈的瓦特说道：
「我们没有受到伤害；
但这般强横的威势，
我此前从未见识，

===Original===
 As this that wild King Hagen
 over his lands now wieldeth:
 His followers bear them proudly,
 and he himself in strength to no one yieldeth.

===Chinese===
像这暴烈的哈根国王
如今在其国土上行使的这样：
他的部众个个倨傲自矜，
他本人的膂力更不输于任何人。」

===Original===
“It was a day as happy
 as ever could be thought,
 When we to you sent tidings
 that we had Hilda brought,

===Chinese===
「那是人们所能想象的
最快活的一天，
当我们给你送去了音讯，
说我们已把希尔达带到——

===Original===
 The loveliest of maidens
 (no falsehood have I spoken,
 Believe the tale I tell you)
 that ever in this world my eyes did look on.”

===Chinese===
她是少女中最可爱的一位
（我没有半句虚言，
请相信我讲述的事），
是我这双眼在这世上从未见过的。」

===Original===
The high-born knight then added:
 “Belike with greatest speed
 Will come these daring foemen;
 for this should you take heed

===Chinese===
那位高贵的骑士接着说：
「这伙胆大妄为的敌人
多半正兼程疾进；
为此你们应当提防，

===Original===
 Lest the angry Hagen
 soon shall overtake us
 Here within your marches;
 if so, his hatred bitter woe will make us.”

===Chinese===
只怕愤怒的哈根
不久便要追上我们，
就在你们的境内；
真到那时，他的仇恨会让我们大吃苦头。」

===Original===
Then Wâ-te and Sir Fru-te
 down to the shore did bring
 Many worthy followers,
 knights of Hettel, the king,

===Chinese===
随后瓦特与弗鲁特爵士
把众多可敬的随从、
黑特尔国王的骑士们
一路带到海边，

===Original===
 There to see fair Hilda,
 and there must they await her.
 Upon their shining bucklers
 many a spear-shaft crashed in battle later.

===Chinese===
让他们在那里一睹美丽的希尔达，
并在那里恭候她的到来。
日后鏖战之中，
多少矛杆在他们闪亮的圆盾上撞碎。

===Original===
Now came the fair young maiden,
 under a comely hat;
 Then all the men of the Hegelings
 who on their horses sat

===Chinese===
这时美丽的年轻少女来了，
头上戴着一顶雅致的帽子；
黑格林人的全体部众——
那些跨坐马上的汉子——

===Original===
 By the side of the king, their master,
 upon the grass alighted.
 With merry hearts then gladly
 the well-bred throng their love and friendship plighted.

===Chinese===
在他们的主上国王身侧，
纷纷下马，踏上草地。
这群教养良好的众人
随即怀着欢愉之心，欣然缔下爱戴与情谊。

===Original===
Irold, he of Ortland,
 and Morunc of the Frisian land,
 Both of those brave champions,
 one on either hand,

===Chinese===
奥特兰（Ortland）的伊罗尔德（Irold），
与弗里西亚（Frisian）之地的莫伦克，
那两位勇武健将，
一左一右分侍两旁，

===Original===
 Came with lovely Hilda,
 and Hettel soon were meeting;
 Worthy was she of praises.
 Now thought the maid to give the king her greeting.

===Chinese===
伴着可爱的希尔达前来，
不多时便与黑特尔相会；
她当得起万千称颂。
此刻少女心想，要向国王问安致意。

===Original===
With her there came young maidens,
 twenty or even more,
 All clad in fair white linen﻿—
 whiter none e’er wore﻿—

===Chinese===
随她前来的年轻少女，
有二十名乃至更多，
个个身着素美的白麻衣——
再没人穿过更白的衣裳——

===Original===
 Or best of silken clothing,
 that could be found by any:
 Proud were they to wear them,
 and, gaily decked, they there were seen by many.

===Chinese===
或是世人所能觅得的
最上等的丝衣：
她们身穿这些衣裳，满心骄傲，
盛装丽服，受众人瞻仰。

===Original===
The king, both good and stately,
 then began to greet
 With well-bred, seemly bearing,
 the maid he thought was meet

===Chinese===
那国王善良而威仪堂皇，
随即开始致意，
以教养有素的得体风仪，
迎向那位他认定堪当

===Original===
 To wear the crown hereafter.
 He gazed on her with yearning;
 Her in his arms he folded,
 and fondly kissed the maid, her face upturning.

===Chinese===
他日王冠的少女。
他满含渴慕地凝望她；
将她拥入怀中，
深情地亲吻她仰起的脸庞。

===Original===
Then one by one he welcomed
 all the maidens fair;
 But one there was among them
 so lofty in her air

===Chinese===
随后他一一欢迎
所有美丽的少女；
但其中有一位，
气度那般高华，

===Original===
 She might of birth be kingly:
 in nought her kin were lacking.
 She was one of the maidens
 who with the griffin long her home was making.

===Chinese===
论出身她堪称王族：
她的家世无一可挑剔。
她正是那群少女之一，
曾长久随狮鹫（griffin）以异乡为家。

===Original===
She bore the name of Hildeburg:
 from Hilda, Hagen’s wife,
 She ever had won the honor
 befitting her worthy life;

===Chinese===
她名叫希尔德堡（Hildeburg）：
在哈根之妻希尔达跟前，
她素来赢得的荣宠，
与她高洁的人品相称；

===Original===
 Born in the land of Portugal,
 thence had she been taken.
 She now saw many strangers:
 a longing sad for her friends did this awaken.

===Chinese===
她生在葡萄牙之地，
自幼便从那里被人带走。
如今眼前尽是陌生的面孔：
这勾起了她对亲友的忧伤思念。

===Original===
Hettel to all the maidens
 gave a welcome free,
 Yet was their lot no brighter;
 for when they thought to see

===Chinese===
黑特尔向全体少女
致以诚挚的欢迎，
可她们的命运并未更明亮；
因为正当她们以为

===Original===
 An end of all their sadness,
 upon the coming morrow,
 Soon as the day was dawning,
 there came to them again as great a sorrow.

===Chinese===
明天到来之际，
一切悲伤便告终结，
谁知天色刚明，
一场同样深重的哀愁又向她们袭来。

===Original===
Her throng of high-born followers
 were greeted on every side;
 Near to Hagen’s daughter
 on a flowery meadow wide,

===Chinese===
她那一群出身高贵的侍从
在四面八方受人款接；
在哈根女儿的近旁，
一片繁花铺展的宽阔草地上，

===Original===
 Under silken awnings,
 many there were seated.
 But Hagen was now too near them;
 to them from him must many ills be meted.

===Chinese===
丝质的凉篷底下，
许多人在那里安坐。
可哈根此刻已逼得太近；
他们势必要从他手中领受重重祸殃。

## Tale VIII How Hagen Followed His Daughter / 第八诗章 哈根如何追赶他的女儿

===Original===
Hagen and his warriors follow in their ships, in order to rescue Hilda, and reach the Hegeling land: a battle takes place on the seashore. At Hilda’s entreaty, peace is made, and Hagen consents to her marriage with Hettel. Hagen returns home, leaving Hilda and her maidens in Hettel’s kingdom.

===Chinese===
哈根（Hagen）率麾下武士乘船追赶，欲救回希尔达（Hilda），抵达了黑格林人（Hegelings）的土地：海滨爆发一场大战。经希尔达恳求，双方缔结和平，哈根允准她与黑特尔（Hettel）的婚事。哈根返回故乡，把希尔达和她的侍女们留在了黑特尔的王国。

===Original===
When the day was dawning,
 there was seen full well,
 And known by Horant of Daneland,
 a cross upon a sail,

===Chinese===
天色破晓时分，
一切看得真切，
丹麦兰（Daneland）的霍兰特（Horant）
认出一面帆上画着十字，

===Original===
 With other emblems blazoned,
 that pilgrims did betoken.
 For such a band of pilgrims
 in Wâ-te’s heart was little love bespoken.

===Chinese===
帆上还描着别的纹章，
标示那是朝圣的旅人；
只是对这样一伙「朝圣者」，
瓦特（Wâ-te）心中可没有多少好感；

===Original===
Loudly Morunc shouted
 to Irold brave and true:
 “Now ask our lord, King Hettel,
 what he thinks to do?

===Chinese===
莫伦克（Morunc）高声呼喊，
唤那忠勇的伊罗尔德（Irold）：
「快去请问我们的主上黑特尔国王，
他打算怎么办？

===Original===
 A sail with the arms of Hagen
 comes to our shore too nearly:
 Too long have we been sleeping,
 and well to be rid of this will cost us dearly.”

===Chinese===
一面扬着哈根纹章的帆
已驶得太近我们的海岸：
我们沉睡得太久了，
想全身而退，只怕代价高昂。」

===Original===
To Hettel the tale was carried
 that the father of his bride,
 Hither from Ireland sailing,
 with ships broad-built and wide

===Chinese===
这消息传到了黑特尔那里：
他新娘的父亲
正从爱尔兰（Ireland）扬帆驶来，
带着船身宽阔的巨舟，

===Original===
 As well as many a galley,
 now their shore was nearing.
 From Wâ-te and from Fru-te
 their wisest thoughts the king was bent on hearing.

===Chinese===
还有许多加莱战船（galley），
此刻正逼近他们的海岸。
国王一心要听取
瓦特与弗鲁特（Fru-te）最明智的筹划。

===Original===
Both those knights of Denmark
 could hardly this believe,
 Had not their eyes beheld it,
 that Hagen, with followers brave,

===Chinese===
丹麦（Denmark）来的两位骑士
几乎不敢相信，
若非亲眼所见：
哈根竟带着勇猛的部众，

===Original===
 Seeking his daughter Hilda,
 to the river Waal was steering.
 The men who came from Ortland
 lay happy on the beach, no danger fearing.

===Chinese===
为寻他的女儿希尔达，
径直朝瓦尔河（Waal）驶来。
奥特兰（Ortland）来的将士们
却还安卧沙滩，毫不忧惧危险。

===Original===
The fair and noble Hilda
 soon heard the wondrous tale,
 Whereat the kindly maiden
 did loudly thus bewail:

===Chinese===
美丽高贵的希尔达
很快听说了这惊人的消息，
善良的少女闻言
不禁放声这样悲叹：

===Original===
 “My father, if he comes hither,
 soon will make such slaughter,
 That none e’er knew the sorrow
 that will be felt by many a wife and daughter.”

===Chinese===
「我的父亲若来到此地，
转眼便要大开杀戒，
那样的悲苦无人曾经领教，
多少妻子和女儿都将哀恸身受。」

===Original===
“We ’gainst that can guard us,”
 answered the knight Irold:
 “However he may bluster,
 I would not take of gold

===Chinese===
「这我们有法子防备，」
骑士伊罗尔德答道：
「任凭他怎样咆哮，
纵使拿黄金

===Original===
 A mountain’s weight in barter,
 that day when foes are mated,
 Could I see my uncle Wâ-te
 near wild Hagen come, with anger heated.”

===Chinese===
一座山的分量来交换，
那日仇敌短兵相接，
我也不肯错过亲见我叔父瓦特
挟怒火逼近暴烈的哈根。」

===Original===
Then the lovely maidens
 began to wail and mourn.
 The ship was tossed and rolling,
 now by the west wind borne,

===Chinese===
随后可爱的少女们
开始哀哭悲叹。
船身颠簸摇晃，
此时正借西风行驶，

===Original===
 With warriors filled and crowded,
 near to Waal, the river.
 They there, in heavy fighting,
 soon found a blood-stained resting-place forever.

===Chinese===
船上载满挤满武士，
已驶近瓦尔河。
他们将在那里鏖战，
很快寻得一处血染的永久安息之地。

===Original===
Wâ-te bade that Hilda
 on board a ship should stay.
 To guard the queenly maiden,
 while near the shore it lay,

===Chinese===
瓦特吩咐希尔达
留在船中不要上岸。
为守护这王后般的少女，
当船停泊近岸之时，

===Original===
 On every side all hastily
 men their shields were bearing:
 To keep a watch o’er the ladies,
 there were on board a hundred warriors daring.

===Chinese===
四面八方众人急忙
纷纷举起盾牌：
为看护这些贵女，
船上留驻着一百名勇武的战士。

===Original===
Ready now for battle
 were all who to the strand
 Had brought the lovely Hilda
 from her Irish fatherland,

===Chinese===
此刻严阵以待的，
是所有把美丽的希尔达
从她爱尔兰父邦
送到这片滩头的将士，

===Original===
 Whence they the maid had stolen,
 to her father Hagen’s sorrow.
 Many, sound and healthy,
 must sorely fear for their lives before the morrow.

===Chinese===
他们正是从那里劫走了少女，
令她的父亲哈根悲恸。
许多康健无恙的人，
不到明日便要为性命深深恐惧。

===Original===
Hettel was soon heard shouting
 and calling aloud to his men:
 “Be on your guard, brave fighters!
 Who never gold did gain,

===Chinese===
不久便听得黑特尔高喊，
向他的部众大声传令：
「严加戒备，勇敢的战士！
凡从未赢得过黄金的人，

===Original===
 To him it shall be measured,
 in handfuls, without weighing.
 Let this be not forgotten﻿—
 that now your Irish foes you may be slaying.”

===Chinese===
黄金必满把给他量足，
无须称量。
切莫忘记这一点——
眼下正是诛杀爱尔兰仇敌之时。」

===Original===
Bearing then their weapons,
 down they rushed to the sand;
 Stirred with warlike bustle
 was all the Waalisch strand.

===Chinese===
众人执起兵器，
直冲下沙滩；
整片瓦尔河滩
都激荡着临战的骚动。

===Original===
 Thither to King Hettel
 flocked his champions daring;
 Friends as well as foemen
 soon towards the selfsame spot were faring.

===Chinese===
黑特尔国王麾下
悍勇的干将纷纷聚拢；
友人也好，仇敌也好，
很快都朝着同一处进发。

===Original===
Now had Hagen also
 reached the sandy shore,
 And men at him were hurling
 the spears they bravely bore:

===Chinese===
此时哈根也
抵达了沙滩，
众人朝他掷出
他们奋力擎着的长矛：

===Original===
 Those upon the seashore
 well their lives then guarded
 From the stormy Irish onset;
 but wounds yet all the more their bravery rewarded.

===Chinese===
岸上的人们
竭力护卫着性命，
挡开爱尔兰人狂暴的冲击；
但创伤仍愈发成了他们英勇的报偿。

===Original===
How seldom would a father
 have wished to send his child
 Where sparks of fire, all-glowing,
 were struck by foemen wild

===Chinese===
做父亲的何曾愿意
把自己的孩子送往
火星灼灼迸溅之地——
那是狂野的仇敌

===Original===
 Forth from hardened helmets,
 in sight of many a maiden!
 To have sailed with these roving fighters
 did now at last the lovely Hilda sadden.

===Chinese===
从坚硬的头盔上击出，
就当着众多少女的眼前！
与这伙流窜的武士同船而行，
终于令美丽的希尔达黯然神伤。

===Original===
By turns they smote each other
 with heavy spears and long:
 Altho’ themselves they guarded
 beneath their bucklers strong,

===Chinese===
他们轮番互击，
挥着沉重修长的矛：
虽然各自护住了身体，
躲在坚固的圆盾之下，

===Original===
 Yet wounded thro’ their hauberks,
 they were gashed and bloody;
 And soon with flowing life-blood
 the waters’ depths were deeply stained and ruddy.

===Chinese===
仍被刺穿锁子甲，
个个皮开肉绽，血染征衣；
不久滔滔的鲜血
便把深水染得一片殷红。

===Original===
Then to his trusty liegemen
 Hagen called aloud:
 The sea gave back his shouting﻿—
 truly his strength was good﻿—

===Chinese===
于是哈根向他忠诚的封臣
放声高呼：
大海回应着他的呐喊——
他的膂力着实惊人——

===Original===
 He bade them help to land him,
 their wounds by them unheeded;
 Glad were they to do it:
 thereby were spears in many hearts imbedded.

===Chinese===
他命众人助他登岸，
全不顾各自的创伤；
众人欣然从命：
顷刻间多少颗心头扎进了长矛。

===Original===
Hagen now drew nearer,
 not far was he from the sand;
 His sword it clattered loudly;
 Hettel, near at hand,

===Chinese===
哈根又逼近了些，
离沙滩已不相远；
他的剑铮铮作响；
黑特尔就在近旁，

===Original===
 Was standing by the water,
 on the seashore waiting:
 There, with daring followers,
 deeds he did that praise should aye be meeting.

===Chinese===
正立在水边，
在海滨等候：
他在那里同悍勇的部众
立下了永受称颂的功业。

===Original===
Hagen, wild with anger,
 leaped into the wave,
 And to the shore he waded.
 Then on that warrior brave

===Chinese===
哈根怒不可遏，
纵身跃入波浪，
涉水抢上岸来。
于是那勇武的战士身上

===Original===
 Came a shower of lances;
 like snowflakes falling thickly,
 Fast they fell around him,
 shot by the Hegeling foemen, thronging quickly.

===Chinese===
迎来一阵矛雨；
恰似雪片纷纷，
密密落在他四周，
都是黑格林人仇敌簇拥疾射而来。

===Original===
Then from the clash of sword-blades
 a mighty noise arose.
 Those who would slay wild Hagen
 soon beneath his blows

===Chinese===
剑刃相击之间
掀起震天的巨响。
那些想诛杀暴烈的哈根的人
很快便在他的打击下

===Original===
 Were seen to reel and stagger.
 Hettel, the noble fighter,
 Drew near to Hilda’s father;
 at this the maiden wept, with tears most bitter.

===Chinese===
一个个踉跄歪斜。
高贵的战士黑特尔
逼近希尔达的父亲；
少女见状而泣，泪珠至为辛酸。

===Original===
It was indeed a wonder,
 as we the tale have heard,
 So strong and brave was Hagen,
 that Hettel, the Hegeling lord,

===Chinese===
这确是一桩奇事，
如传闻中所述：
哈根是那样刚勇，
而黑特尔，黑格林人的君主，

===Original===
 Before him held his footing.
 As soon as, wildly fighting,
 They had reached each other,
 their helmets rang beneath the heavy smiting.

===Chinese===
竟能在他面前站住脚。
一旦两人疯狂缠斗、
扑到彼此近前，
头盔便在重击之下铿然鸣响。

===Original===
But not so quickly ended
 was yet the stormy fight.
 Soon was Hettel wounded
 by brave King Hagen’s might:

===Chinese===
然而这场狂暴的战斗
并未就此了结。
黑特尔随即负伤，
伤在勇武的哈根国王之手：

===Original===
 Wâ-te the old of Sturmland,
 with his kin, to Hettel hasted,
 With Irold, too, and Morunc﻿—
 knights as good as foemen’s lands e’er wasted.

===Chinese===
施图尔姆兰（Sturmland）的老瓦特
偕族人急忙赶赴黑特尔身旁，
伊罗尔德与莫伦克也同去——
个个都是蹂躏仇敌疆土的好汉。

===Original===
Now came the brave old Fru-te
 and Wâ-te with his throng:
 Knights there were a thousand﻿—
 the press of them was strong.

===Chinese===
勇武的老弗鲁特也到了，
瓦特带着他的一队人马：
骑士足足有一千名——
挤得人马汹汹。

===Original===
 Hettel’s Hegeling kinsmen,
 well their weapons plying,
 Wounded many foemen;
 on every side stretched low, the men were lying.

===Chinese===
黑特尔的黑格林族亲
奋力挥舞着兵器，
创伤了许多仇敌；
四面八方，横陈着倒卧的身躯。

===Original===
After bravest fighting,
 now had reached the land
 The followers of Hagen;
 then crowded to the sand,

===Chinese===
经过最勇烈的鏖战，
此时已经登岸的
是哈根的部众；
接着涌向沙滩的，

===Original===
 After his friends so faithful,
 a host from Ireland’s borders.
 Soon were helmets shattered:
 grimly they fought to win the maids from their warders.

===Chinese===
是追随其忠友而来、
出自爱尔兰边地的大军。
顷刻间头盔迸裂：
他们狠斗着要从看守者手中夺下众少女。

===Original===
Hagen saw then near him
 Hettel, the youthful knight:
 Many strong and stalwart
 were shorn of strength outright,

===Chinese===
哈根这时望见
年轻的骑士黑特尔近在咫尺：
多少魁梧健壮的好汉
被斩落得气力全无，

===Original===
 Both by those from Daneland
 and the Hegeling lieges:
 Now to meet wild Hagen
 every one old Wâ-te loud beseeches.

===Chinese===
有丹麦兰人下的手，
也有黑格林封臣下的手：
此刻要与暴烈的哈根交手，
人人高声恳求老瓦特。

===Original===
Then, by his strength, King Hagen
 broke thro’ the crowd a path,
 And with his sword hewed boldly;
 well he wreaked his wrath,

===Chinese===
于是哈根国王倚仗神力，
从人丛中劈开一条路，
挥剑猛砍，
把满腔怒火尽情宣泄，

===Original===
 Because his much-loved daughter
 from him by craft was taken;
 Coats of mail lay fallen:
 the wrongs of Hagen hate in him did waken.

===Chinese===
只因他至爱的女儿
被人用诡计劫走；
锁子甲纷纷倒落：
哈根所受的欺凌唤醒了他胸中的仇恨。

===Original===
He might not quench his anger
 with the sword alone;
 By the thrust of his heavy long-spear
 soon were overthrown

===Chinese===
单凭一柄剑
压不住他的怒火；
他沉重的长矛一刺，
顷刻间放翻了

===Original===
 Many a knight most daring:
 never the tale was given
 By these unto their kinsmen,
 of how in the stormy fight their luck had thriven.

===Chinese===
多少无比悍勇的骑士：
他们再不能把这段故事
带回讲给亲族——
说自己在这场恶战中何等走运。

===Original===
Now came Wâ-te quickly,
 the knight well born and good;
 Soon of his well-loved kinsmen
 he saw the flowing blood,

===Chinese===
这时瓦特飞驰赶到，
那出身名门、品行端方的骑士；
他很快望见亲爱的族人
鲜血汩汩涌流，

===Original===
 Under the slash of broadswords,
 out of their armor dripping:
 Of those who would have helped him,
 five hundred wounded men in death were sleeping.

===Chinese===
伤在阔剑劈砍之下，
自甲胄缝隙滴洒：
那些本要赶来助他一臂之力的人中，
五百名伤者已沉沉睡入死亡。

===Original===
Everywhere were gathered
 friends as well as foes,
 All in uproar minged;
 a mighty din arose.

===Chinese===
四下里聚拢着
友人也好，仇敌也好，
全都乱作一团；
掀起一片巨大的喧嚣。

===Original===
 Wâ-te and wild Hagen
 rushed on each other madly,
 Whoe’er could shun their pathway
 of all the risk he had fled was thinking gladly.

===Chinese===
瓦特与暴烈的哈根
疯狂地扑向对方，
凡能避开他们所经之路的人，
无不庆幸躲过了全部凶险。

===Original===
Hagen laid on Wâ-te
 many a heavy blow﻿—
 Well his strength he wielded.
 Their helmets were aglow

===Chinese===
哈根朝瓦特
挥出一记记重击——
他的膂力施展得淋漓尽致。
两人的头盔烧得通红，

===Original===
 With fiery sparks outflashing﻿—
 like to brands they glittered;
 Each cleft the other’s helmet,
 and ever still, each other’s blows they bettered.

===Chinese===
迸溅着灼灼的火星——
亮得如同火把；
两人各劈开对方的头盔，
且仍旧一击狠过一击。

===Original===
The ground beneath was trembling
 with aged Wâ-te’s stroke:
 Scarcely could the maidens
 of his onslaught shun the shock.

===Chinese===
脚下的大地
随老瓦特的挥击震颤：
少女们几乎
躲不开他冲杀的震波。

===Original===
 Now the wounds of Hettel
 his faithful friends were binding;
 He then began to ask them
 where his cousin Wâ-te he could be finding.

===Chinese===
此时黑特尔的创伤
正由忠诚的友人们包扎；
他开口询问他们，
到哪里才能找到他的族亲瓦特。

===Original===
With Hagen, “of kings the Devil,”
 he found old Wâ-te soon:
 The skill of him of Sturmland
 to guard himself was shown:

===Chinese===
与「诸王中的魔王」哈根
恶斗之处，他很快寻到老瓦特：
施图尔姆兰人的护身本领
在此显露无遗：

===Original===
 Brave were both these warriors,
 and oft the tale was spoken
 How Wâ-te the bold and Hagen
 in hardest strife had each his anger wroken.

===Chinese===
两位战士都勇猛，
这故事常被人说起：
勇敢的瓦特与哈根
如何在最酷烈的争斗中各泄其愤。

===Original===
Hagen’s spear was broken
 erelong on Wâ-te’s shield:
 Well in the fight he bore it,
 and strength enough did wield.

===Chinese===
哈根的长矛
不久便在瓦特的盾上折断：
他在战局中稳稳撑持，
膂力丝毫不衰。

===Original===
 Ne’er on the field of warfare
 did blows of men fall thicker,
 Even of bravest warriors;
 Wâ-te scorned to flinch, or seem the worker.

===Chinese===
战阵之上，
人的重击从未落得这般密集，
纵然出自最勇的战士；
瓦特耻于退缩，也不屑显出怯意。

===Original===
Hagen cleft the head-piece
 of Hettel’s brave old man,
 The trusty, daring Wâ-te,
 till blood from his helmet ran,

===Chinese===
哈根劈开了
黑特尔那位勇武老臣的头盔，
那忠诚剽悍的瓦特，
鲜血顺着他的头盔流淌，

===Original===
 From out his wounds fast flowing.
 Now the wind blew colder,
 For eventide was nearing;
 the struggling throng in fight but grew the bolder.

===Chinese===
从伤口里汩汩涌出。
此时风刮得更冷，
因为暮色正在逼近；
缠斗的人群反而愈发剽悍。

===Original===
Wâ-te gave back in anger
 each grim and deadly blow,
 Making the blood, like tear-drops,
 on Hagen’s breast to flow;

===Chinese===
瓦特怒气冲冲，回敬了
每一记狠辣致命的打击，
打得哈根胸前的血
如泪珠般淌落；

===Original===
 Strokes he gave his foeman,
 until the sword-blade glittered
 On the bosses of his helmet;
 daylight before his darkened eyesight flittered.

===Chinese===
他一记记击打仇敌，
直到剑刃在
对方头盔的凸饰上寒光闪闪；
天光在哈根昏黑的眼前明灭闪烁。

===Original===
Wounded, too, was Irold,
 Ortland’s champion brave.
 Though many there lay dying
 from the wounds that Hagen gave,

===Chinese===
伊罗尔德也负了伤，
他是奥特兰勇猛的干城。
虽然许多人横卧垂死，
伤在哈根之手，

===Original===
 Yet the blows of Wâ-te
 still did Hagen batter.
 Sorely wept the maidens
 when of so many swords they heard the clatter.

===Chinese===
但瓦特的打击
仍将哈根狠狠捶擂。
少女们痛哭不已，
当她们听见那么多剑刃铿锵作响。

===Original===
Now, in fear and sorrow,
 Hilda, the maiden fair,
 Cried unto King Hettel,
 and begged of him to spare

===Chinese===
此时希尔达又惊又痛，
这位美丽的少女
向黑特尔国王呼告，
恳求他保全

===Original===
 Her father from old Wâ-te,
 the fight so grimly waging.
 He called for his standard-bearer,
 and bade him lead his men where the strife was raging.

===Chinese===
她的父亲，莫让他毁于
正杀得惨烈的老瓦特之手。
他唤来掌旗官，
命他带队奔赴鏖战最烈处。

===Original===
Then the kingly Hettel
 right well and bravely fought;
 Soon he found old Wâ-te,
 to whom no joy it brought:

===Chinese===
尊贵的黑特尔
战得漂亮而英勇；
他很快寻见老瓦特，
这于瓦特并无半分欢喜：

===Original===
 Then Hettel called to Hagen:
 “Let hatred hence be driven;
 So shall it raise your honor,
 if now our friends no more to death be given.”

===Chinese===
黑特尔于是向哈根喊话：
「快把仇恨驱散；
若我们的友人从此不再被交与死亡，
那只会增添您的荣光。」

===Original===
Hagen shouted loudly﻿—
 fell indeed was his mood﻿—
 “Who bids that we be parted?”
 Then cried the warrior good:

===Chinese===
哈根厉声高喝——
他怒气着实骇人——
「是谁下令要我们住手？」
那良善的战士答道：

===Original===
 “I bid it, I, King Hettel,
 the Hegelings’ lord and master,
 Who for the Lady Hilda
 sent my friends so far, from you to wrest her.”

===Chinese===
「是我下的令，我，黑特尔国王，
黑格林人的君与主，
正是为着希尔达小姐，
我才遣友远行，将她从您手中夺来。」

===Original===
Then spake the lordly Hagen:
 “Since first to me ’twas told
 How you to win my daughter
 showed yourself so bold,

===Chinese===
于是尊贵的哈根说道：
「自从我初次听闻
你们为求娶我女儿
竟有那般大胆，

===Original===
 This to your name with warriors
 shame has ne’er been doing;
 Clever was the cunning
 to which your winning of my child is owing.”

===Chinese===
这在武士们耳中
从未玷污你的名声；
那场赚得我女儿的
诡计，确实高明。」

===Original===
Hettel then sprang nearer,
 as oft by one is done,
 Who thinks to stop the fighting.
 Grim was the mood yet shown

===Chinese===
黑特尔随即抢步上前，
如人们常做的那样，
想要拦下这场厮杀。
可那勇武的老瓦特

===Original===
 By the bold and aged Wâ-te;
 but he and Hagen yielded:
 Then with all his followers
 Hagen stepped back, nor longer his weapon wielded.

===Chinese===
心头怒气仍未消解；
但他与哈根终归让步：
于是哈根偕全部部众
退了下去，不再挥动兵器。

===Original===
Now the lordly Hettel
 his helmet laid aside;
 A truce was loudly called for
 by all, both far and wide;

===Chinese===
此时尊贵的黑特尔
摘下了头盔；
四面八方众人
齐声高呼停战；

===Original===
 ’Twas said by Hilda’s father
 there was an end of fighting:
 For many a day, the maidens
 had heard no tale their ears so much delighting.

===Chinese===
希尔达的父亲宣布
战斗就此终结：
多少时日以来，少女们
未曾听过如此悦耳的消息。

===Original===
The men took off the armor
 which they in fight had worn,
 And now at last they rested.
 Many then must mourn

===Chinese===
众人卸下
战斗中披挂的甲胄，
终于得以歇息。
许多人还要哀恸，

===Original===
 For wounds, in warfare given,
 whence the blood was welling;
 But many lay there also
 who never more on thoughts of war were dwelling.

===Chinese===
为战阵所致的创伤，
血正从那里涌出；
但还有许多人躺在那里，
再也不会萦心于战争。

===Original===
Then stepped forth King Hettel
 and near to Hagen stood,
 And thus he spake to the warrior:
 “Since I well have wooed

===Chinese===
黑特尔国王随即上前，
立在哈根近旁，
对这位战士如此说道：
「我既已郑重求娶

===Original===
 Your lovely daughter Hilda,
 ’tis fit that you allow her
 To wear the crown beside me:
 my many well-bred knights will fealty show her.”

===Chinese===
你可爱的女儿希尔达，
你理应允准她
在我身侧佩戴王冠：
我麾下众多贤良的骑士将向她效忠。」

===Original===
Then Hettel sent for Wâ-te,
 of whom he was in need;
 For many years now ended,
 of him it had been said

===Chinese===
黑特尔随后派人去请瓦特，
他正用得着他；
许多年头如今早已过去，
人们曾这样说起他：

===Original===
 That he from some wild woman
 had learned a leech’s cunning:
 Wâ-te, forsooth, was skilful
 to heal deep wounds and stanch the life-blood running.

===Chinese===
说他曾从某个野蛮的女人
学得医师的绝技：
瓦特确实精于此道，
能愈深创、止住奔涌的生命之血。

===Original===
Wâ-te laid by his weapons;
 his wounds he first had bound.
 Herbs that were good for healing
 by him were quickly found;

===Chinese===
瓦特搁下兵器；
先包扎好自己的伤口。
疗伤有效的药草
被他迅速找齐；

===Original===
 He had a box full costly,
 that in it held a plaster.
 Now the fair Queen Hilda
 besought his help, and at his feet she cast her.

===Chinese===
他有一只华贵的药匣，
匣中盛着膏药。
美丽的希尔达王后
恳求他援手，拜倒在他脚下。

===Original===
She said, “My dear friend Wâ-te,
 my father heal, I pray;
 For this, whate’er you ask me,
 I ne’er will say you nay;

===Chinese===
她说：「亲爱的朋友瓦特，
求你医治我的父亲；
作为报答，无论你求什么，
我绝不对你推拒；

===Original===
 And help his warriors also,
 who in the dust lie bleeding,
 And show your skill to his liegemen
 who stood by him, when he their help was needing.

===Chinese===
也请救助他的武士们，
他们正卧于尘土中流血，
对他的封臣们也请施展出你的妙术，
他们曾在他需要时坚守在侧。」

===Original===
“Nor must you be forgetful
 of those of the Hegeling land,
 Who were friends to Hettel;
 wet with their blood is the sand

===Chinese===
「还有黑格林之地的那些人，
你也不得忘记；
他们是黑特尔的朋友；
他们如今横卧的沙滩，

===Original===
 On which they now are lying,
 as if a rain were falling:
 Sorrowful tales of their fighting
 for me there ne’er can be an end of telling.”

===Chinese===
已被他们的血浸湿，
仿佛落过一场雨；
他们苦战的悲壮故事，
我将永远诉说不尽。」

===Original===
Then spake the aged Wâ-te:
 “Their wounds I cannot heal﻿—
 In that I will not meddle,
 until as friends they feel

===Chinese===
老瓦特于是说道：
「这些伤我无法医治——
这事我不愿插手，
除非他们彼此

===Original===
 Each unto the other﻿—
 Hagen brave and knightly,
 And Hettel, my lord and master;
 till then shall I withhold my skill most rightly.”

===Chinese===
真感到情谊——
勇武的骑士哈根，
与我的主上黑特尔；
在那之前，我扣下医术理所当然。」

===Original===
The high-born maiden answered:
 “This I may not dare
 To ask of the king, my father;
 his tears I did not spare,

===Chinese===
高贵的少女答道：
「这个我不敢
去恳求我的父王；
我已让他洒过泪水，

===Original===
 And now have not the boldness
 to bring to him my greeting;
 Both he and all his kinsmen
 I fear would now my love with scorn be meeting.”

===Chinese===
如今再没有胆量
向他问安致意；
只怕他与他的族亲
都会冷眼轻贱我的爱意。」

===Original===
Then ’twas asked of Hagen:
 “My lord, may this now be,
 That it would not stir your anger
 your daughter here to see,

===Chinese===
于是有人去问哈根：
「陛下，此事可否成全：
让您在此见您的女儿
而不激起您的怒气，

===Original===
 The youthful, queenly Hilda?
 If you for this are willing,
 She will come most gladly,
 and soon your many wounds will help in healing.”

===Chinese===
见年轻而王后般的希尔达？
您若愿意，
她将欣然前来，
您的诸多创伤也会好得更快。」

===Original===
“Gladly will I see her,
 whatever she has done;
 To me will she be welcome:
 why should I her disown,

===Chinese===
「我乐意见她，
无论她做过什么；
她来了我必欢喜相迎：
我为何要摈弃她，

===Original===
 Here in a land of foemen,
 nor take her greeting kindly?
 To me and to my daughter,
 King Hettel must atone for deeds unfriendly.”

===Chinese===
在这仇敌的国度，
不亲切地受她问安？
至于我和我的女儿，
黑特尔国王须为这不友好的行径作出补偿。」

===Original===
Horant, the knight from Daneland,
 led her by the hand,
 And with him went brave Fru-te,
 to where the king did stand;

===Chinese===
丹麦兰来的骑士霍兰特
携着她的手，
勇武的弗鲁特也同行，
走到国王伫立之处；

===Original===
 One maiden only with them
 looked on Hagen wounded.
 For friends did Hilda sorrow,
 though Hettel’s love for her was all unbounded.

===Chinese===
只有一名侍女随行，
望着负伤的哈根。
希尔达为友人们悲伤，
尽管黑特尔对她的爱全然无边。

===Original===
On Hildeburg and Hilda
 when Hagen now did look,
 Then, from his seat upspringing,
 thus he quickly spoke:

===Chinese===
哈根望着
希尔德堡（Hildeburg）与希尔达，
随即从座上一跃而起，
急急这样说道：

===Original===
 “Welcome be thou, my daughter,
 Hilda, most noble lady!
 I cannot leave unspoken
 the greeting warm which I to give am ready.”

===Chinese===
「欢迎你，我的女儿，
希尔达，最尊贵的小姐！
我不能不说出
我正要献上的滚烫问候。」

===Original===
His daughter he allowed not
 the care of his wounds to take;
 While Wâ-te these was binding
 he bade the maids step back﻿—

===Chinese===
他不许女儿
沾手照料他的伤口；
瓦特包扎之时，
他命少女们退后——

===Original===
 The youthful high-born ladies.
 Wâ-te’s wish was the stronger
 To heal her father quickly,
 that so his daughter there might weep no longer.

===Chinese===
这些年轻高贵的小姐。
瓦特的心愿愈发急切，
要尽快治好她的父亲，
好让她不再在那里哭泣。

===Original===
Healed with plants and herbage
 and many a far-sought weed,
 From all his pain did Hagen
 feel himself now freed;

===Chinese===
以草木与药草、
许多远觅来的草叶医治，
哈根顿觉
自己解脱了一切疼痛；

===Original===
 They eased his hurts with plaster,
 and when again the maiden
 Turned to see her father,
 she found him well, with aches no longer laden.

===Chinese===
众人再以膏药敷他的伤，
待少女回身
再看她的父亲时，
他已痊愈，不再被疼痛压身。

===Original===
Wâ-te, the healing-master,
 made haste﻿—no time he lost;
 He hoped to gain such riches
 among this wounded host,

===Chinese===
医术大师瓦特
加紧施为——分秒不误；
他指望从这群伤者身上
赚得那样一笔财物，

===Original===
 That scarce could they by camels
 be carried to his dwelling.
 A skill so great and wondrous
 never, that I have heard, have men been telling.

===Chinese===
多到骆驼
都难以驮回家去。
如此伟大奇妙的医术，
就我所闻，还从未有人说起。

===Original===
First he healed King Hettel,
 the lord of the Hegeling land;
 Then all he saw there wounded
 he helped by his skilful hand.

===Chinese===
他先治好黑特尔国王，
黑格林之地的君主；
随后把所有见到的伤者
都以妙手救治。

===Original===
 Those in the care of others
 still with pain did sicken;
 But they, when nursed by Wâ-te,
 were turned to life, though they by death were stricken.

===Chinese===
旁人照看的那些
仍在苦痛中煎熬；
但经瓦特看护的人，
纵已濒死也重获新生。

===Original===
There would they no longer
 let the maidens stay.
 Hagen said to Hilda:
 “Elsewhere must we to-day

===Chinese===
他们不愿再让
少女们留在那里。
哈根对希尔达说：
「今天我们必须

===Original===
 Find us rest and shelter;
 while others must not idly
 Leave the dead thus lying,
 who burial scarce can wait, here scattered widely.”

===Chinese===
另觅歇宿安身之处；
旁人也断不可懈怠，
任死者这样横陈——
他们散落四野，葬事刻不容缓。」

===Original===
Hettel begged King Hagen
 with him to his home to go;
 Though loath, to this he yielded,
 as soon as he came to know

===Chinese===
黑特尔恳请哈根国王
随他回自己的家去；
哈根虽不情愿，还是应允了，
待他得知

===Original===
 That he, the king of the Hegelings,
 of many lands was owner:
 Hagen then with his daughter
 went with him to his home, and there had honor.

===Chinese===
这位黑格林人的君王
坐拥着众多疆土：
哈根便带着女儿
随他回家，在那里备受尊荣。

===Original===
The youthful knights were singing,
 as they left the field.
 Happy then were the living;
 but, never to be healed,

===Chinese===
年轻的骑士们一路歌唱，
离开那片战场。
活着的人何其快乐；
但永无痊愈之望的，

===Original===
 They behind were leaving
 three hundred dead and dying,
 The rich and poor together,
 slashed with the sword, and pitifully lying.

===Chinese===
是他们抛在身后的
三百个死者和垂死之人，
富者贫者同卧一处，
身被剑创，凄然枕藉。

===Original===
Then the war-worn fighters
 through the land went home;
 All who there were dwelling
 were blithe to see them come:

===Chinese===
那些久历战阵的战士
取道乡野归家；
当地的居人
见他们归来无不欢欣：

===Original===
 But the kinsmen of the warriors
 who in death lay sleeping
 Were slow their hearts to gladden;
 they for kindred slain long time were weeping.

===Chinese===
但那些长眠于死亡的
武士们的亲族，
心头迟迟难以欢悦，
为被戕的亲人久久哀哭。

===Original===
Hettel and Hilda with him
 took their homeward way.
 Many, bereft of fathers,
 sorely wept that day,

===Chinese===
黑特尔携希尔达
踏上归途。
许多人失去了父亲，
那天悲泣不止，

===Original===
 Whose after life was happy.
 The mighty Hettel later
 Crowned the fair young Hilda;
 by this the Hegeling name became the greater.

===Chinese===
而他们日后的岁月却是幸福的。
威严的黑特尔后来
为年轻美丽的希尔达加冕；
黑格林人的声名由此愈加显赫。

===Original===
Hettel now had thriven﻿—
 his suit he well did gain.
 Old and young together
 with swords at court were seen,

===Chinese===
黑特尔如今心愿得遂——
他的求婚终获成功。
老老少少齐聚一堂，
佩剑立于宫廷，

===Original===
 As were the guests of Hagen
 who from the ships came kindly.
 The wedding of his daughter
 was highly praised by Hagen, now grown friendly.

===Chinese===
哈根的宾客们也一样，
他们乘船而来，和善有礼。
他女儿的婚礼
深得已然释怀的哈根称许。

===Original===
Then with what great honor
 to the bridal seat was led
 That high-born, lovely lady!
 Moreover, it is said

===Chinese===
她被何等尊荣地
引上了婚仪的宝座，
那位出身高贵、可爱的淑女！
而且，据说

===Original===
 That full five hundred liegemen
 then at court were knighted.
 Fru-te the wise from Denmark
 to guard King Hettel’s wealth was thought well fitted.

===Chinese===
足足五百名封臣
在宫廷受封为骑士。
丹麦来的智者弗鲁特
被认为最宜替黑特尔国王掌管财富。

===Original===
The riches of King Hettel
 by Hagen now were seen;
 The tale had erst been told him
 by many of Hettel’s kin,

===Chinese===
黑特尔国王的财富
此刻尽收哈根眼底；
这话先前早有许多
黑特尔的族亲对他讲起，

===Original===
 That over seven princedoms
 well his sway had thriven.
 All the poor there with them
 were home in gladness sent, and lodgings given.

===Chinese===
说他的权势遍及
七个侯国，治理兴盛。
所有的贫民都随他们
被欢欢喜喜送归家去，还得了居所。

===Original===
Hettel gave rich clothing
 to Ireland’s warriors brave;
 Bright-red gold and silver,
 and horses, too, he gave.

===Chinese===
黑特尔把华服
赠给爱尔兰的勇武战士，
又赠了赤金白银，
还有骏马。

===Original===
 The whole they scarce could carry,
 as they homeward wended:
 Thus good friends he won him,
 and this for Hilda in highest praises ended.

===Chinese===
礼物多得他们几乎拿不动，
在归乡路上：
他由此赢得了好友，
这一切终化作对希尔达的至高颂扬。

===Original===
Upon the twelfth day’s morning
 they left King Hettel’s land.
 The horses bred in Denmark
 led they out on the sand;

===Chinese===
第十二天的清晨，
他们告别了黑特尔国王的国度。
丹麦育出的骏马
被牵上沙滩；

===Original===
 Each his mane, thick hanging,
 down to his hoofs was shaking.
 The guests from afar were happy
 that they King Hettel’s friendship had been making.

===Chinese===
匹匹垂着厚重的鬃毛，
直披到蹄，迎风抖动。
远来的宾客满心欢喜，
庆幸结下了黑特尔国王的友谊。

===Original===
Grooms and also stewards
 with Hagen then did ride,
 With cup-bearers and carvers.
 Ne’er, in his greatest pride,

===Chinese===
马夫与管家们
随哈根一同骑行，
还有司酒官与切肉官。
纵在他最鼎盛的尊荣时，

===Original===
 In his home and kingdom,
 had he been served so truly.
 The crown was worn by Hilda,
 and Hagen’s heart with bliss was brimming fully.

===Chinese===
在他自己的家邦，
也从未受过如此周全的侍奉。
王冠已戴在希尔达头上，
哈根的心中洋溢着圆满的欢喜。

===Original===
Food as well as lodgings
 they found upon their road;
 On Hagen and his followers
 all men their care bestowed:

===Chinese===
一路上饮食与歇宿
处处皆有供应；
凡是哈根及其随从所至，
人人倾心照拂：

===Original===
 So to their homes most gladly
 they the tale did carry
 Of how the friends of Hettel
 in showing them all kindness ne’er were weary.

===Chinese===
于是他们回到家中，
满心欢喜地把这故事传讲：
说黑特尔的朋友们
待他们仁至义尽，从不厌倦。

===Original===
Hagen greeted Hildeburg,
 and clasped her in his arms;
 He said, “Watch over Hilda
 for the love your bosom warms.

===Chinese===
哈根问候希尔德堡，
把她拥进怀中；
他说：「看顾好希尔达，
凭你胸中温热的爱。

===Original===
 So great a throng of followers
 at times a woman dazes;
 Care for her so kindly
 that of your worth all men shall speak with praises.”

===Chinese===
这么一大群仆从，
有时会让女子晕眩；
你要殷勤照料她，
让世人称道你的贤德。」

===Original===
“My lord, that will I gladly:
 to you has much been told
 Of the woes that with her mother
 I bore in days of old;

===Chinese===
「陛下，我甘愿照办：
您想必已听人讲过许多，
当年我随她母亲
一同承受的苦难；

===Original===
 And I for years my friendship
 for her did never loosen;
 Her for miles I followed
 ere for a lover you by her were chosen.”

===Chinese===
多年来我对她的情谊
从未松懈；
在她择定情郎之前，
我曾追随她跋涉数里。」

===Original===
Hagen bade the others
 their way to court to take;
 Never then could the maidens
 an end of weeping make:

===Chinese===
哈根吩咐其余的人
取道前往宫廷；
少女们当时
怎么也哭个不尽：

===Original===
 Now by the hand he took them,
 and to Hettel they were given;
 He asked for them his kindness,
 since from their homes they sadly had been riven.

===Chinese===
他于是携起她们的手，
把她们托付给黑特尔；
他请黑特尔善待她们，
因为她们是被悲哀地夺离家园的。

===Original===
Then said he to his daughter:
 “So well the crown now wear,
 That neither I nor your mother
 the tale shall ever hear

===Chinese===
随后他对女儿说：
「要把王冠戴得端端正正，
让我和你母亲
永远不致听闻

===Original===
 That men ill-will do bear you.
 High your lot has raised you,
 And you of blame were worthy,
 if when men spoke your name they never praised you.”

===Chinese===
有人对你心怀恶意。
你的命运已将你高高托起，
你也就难辞其咎，
若世人道及你的名字却不加称颂。」

===Original===
Low bowed to the king wild Hagen,
 and kissed his child again.
 Neither by him nor his followers
 ever more was seen

===Chinese===
暴烈的哈根向国王深深躬身，
又一次亲吻他的孩子。
从此他和他的部众
再也见不到

===Original===
 The kingdom of the Hegeling:
 too far away was their dwelling.
 Back to his home in Ballian,
 in his trusty ships, King Hagen soon was sailing.

===Chinese===
黑格林人的王国：
他们的家乡太过遥远。
哈根国王乘着可靠的船队，
不久便扬帆驶回巴利安（Ballian）的家园。

===Original===
When he had reached his castle,
 and sat with the queen alone,
 The mother of fair Hilda,
 Hagen was free to own

===Chinese===
当他回到自己的城堡，
与王后单独对坐，
她正是美丽希尔达的母亲，
哈根坦然承认

===Original===
 That none to win his daughter
 more fitly could have pleaded;
 And if he had yet others,
 he fain to the Hegeling land would send them to be wedded.

===Chinese===
论求娶他女儿，
再没有人能说得更加得体；
倘若他还有别的女儿，
他也乐意送去黑格林之地婚配。

===Original===
Hilda for this gave praises
 to her master, Christ the Lord:
 “That I of my dear daughter
 such happy news have heard

===Chinese===
希尔达为此称谢
她的主上、基督吾主（Christ the Lord）：
「我能听到爱女
如此美满的音讯，

===Original===
 Fills my heart with gladness,
 and with bliss o’erflowing.
 How fares it with her followers,
 and Hildeburg, who long her love was showing?”

===Chinese===
我的心充满欢欣，
幸福满溢而出。
她的侍从们可都安好？
还有久施情谊的希尔德堡呢？」

===Original===
Then spake the kingly Hagen:
 “Now in their land and home
 All of them are happy;
 great hath our child become;

===Chinese===
尊贵的哈根于是说道：
「如今在那片土地和家园里，
他们个个幸福；
我们的孩子已成大器；

===Original===
 Ne’er, with us, were her maidens
 clothed in such fine dresses.
 There we now must leave them:
 for her were many breastplates hacked to pieces.”

===Chinese===
她的侍女们从前在我们这里，
从未穿过这样华美的衣裳。
如今我们只得把她们留在那里：
为了她，多少胸甲被剁成了碎片。」

## Tale IX How Gudrun Was Sought by Siegfried / 第九诗章 西格弗里德如何求娶古德伦

===Original===
Wâ-te, Horant, and the other vassals of Hettel go back to their homes. Hilda bears to Hettel a son, Ortwin, and a daughter, Gudrun, who is afterwards sought by Siegfried, king of Moorland. He is rejected by her father, Hettel, and retires in anger.

===Chinese===
瓦特（Wâ-te）、霍兰特（Horant）与黑特尔（Hettel）的其余封臣各自返回家园。希尔达（Hilda）为黑特尔诞下一子奥特温（Ortwin）、一女古德伦（Gudrun）；古德伦后来受到摩尔兰（Moorland）之王西格弗里德（Siegfried）的求娶。她的父亲黑特尔拒婚，西格弗里德含怒而去。

===Original===
We speak no more of Hagen.
 A word may now be told
 About King Hettel’s kinsmen:
 they who land did hold

===Chinese===
哈根（Hagen）其人，不再多讲。
如今有另一段话要传扬：
说的是黑特尔国王的亲族，
他们都各自领有封疆；

===Original===
 Ever owed him fealty
 for these and for their castles;
 To court they all came often
 when Hettel and Hilda sent to call their vassals.

===Chinese===
他们凭这些领地和城堡，
永远向他效忠；
每当黑特尔与希尔达宣召封臣，
他们全都应召入宫；

===Original===
Wâ-te went to Sturmland,
 Morunc to Nifland rode;
 Horant, prince of Denmark,
 led his warriors good

===Chinese===
瓦特去了施图尔姆兰（Sturmland），
莫伦克（Morunc）骑往尼夫兰（Nifland）；
丹麦的君侯霍兰特，
统率他麾下的勇武士卒；

===Original===
 To Givers, by the seashore,
 where as lord they held him;
 There their homes they guarded,
 and many, far and wide, their master called him.

===Chinese===
来到海边的吉弗斯（Givers），
众人尊他为一方之主；
他们在故乡守护家园，
远远近近，许多人都称他主上。

===Original===
With mighty sway in Ortland
 Irold had his seat;
 Its lands he held of Hettel;
 so, as a vassal meet,

===Chinese===
伊罗尔德（Irold）雄踞奥特兰（Ortland），
权势赫赫，坐镇一方；
那片国土他受封于黑特尔；
因此，身为一名合宜的封臣，

===Original===
 Near and far to serve him,
 his duty was the greater:
 The king was brave and worthy;
 and ne’er for a lord of lands was known a better.

===Chinese===
他远近都须效力，
职责也就愈发重大：
国王勇武而贤明；
世人从未见过更好的封疆领主。

===Original===
If ever in any kingdom
 Hettel heard them speak
 Of a fair and well-born maiden,
 her he sought to take

===Chinese===
无论在哪一个王国，
黑特尔只要听人说起
哪位出身高贵的美丽少女，
便要把她接来

===Original===
 Into his home and castle,
 as handmaid to his lady:
 Whatever Hilda wished for,
 to help wild Hagen’s child they all were ready.

===Chinese===
接进自己的家园城堡，
做他夫人的侍女：
凡希尔达心之所欲，
众人都乐于为暴烈的哈根之女效劳。

===Original===
The king, with his wife beside him,
 was happy on the throne;
 Their life was ever blissful.
 To all in the land ’twas known

===Chinese===
国王偕着王后在侧，
在王座上安乐承平；
他们的日子美满无比，
全国上下无人不晓——

===Original===
 That better far and dearer
 than all on earth he thought her.
 Never by all his kinsmen
 a lovelier could be found, where’er they sought her.

===Chinese===
他看她远比世间一切
都更美好、更可珍重。
纵使亲族们寻遍四方，
也再寻不出比她更可爱的人影。

===Original===
Within seven years thereafter
 Hettel, in stormy fight,
 Thrice to his foes gave battle.
 They who, day and night,

===Chinese===
此后七年之间，
黑特尔在激烈鏖战中，
三度与仇敌兵戎相见。
那些昼夜不休、

===Original===
 To wrong his name and honor
 did their utmost gladly,
 Now by the knightly Hettel
 found themselves brought low and chastened sadly.

===Chinese===
蓄意败坏他名声与荣誉、
还甘愿竭尽全力的仇敌，
如今被骑士黑特尔
杀得一败涂地，惨遭惩儆。

===Original===
His castles he did strengthen,
 and peace he gave to his land,
 As well a king befitteth:
 such were the deeds of his hand,

===Chinese===
他加固自己的城堡，
赐给国土以安宁，
正合一位君王的本分：
这便是他亲手立下的功业，

===Original===
 That never in any kingdom,
 when his name was spoken,
 Was it said he was faint-hearted.
 The praise of all did well his worth betoken.

===Chinese===
以致无论在哪个王国，
每当人们说起他的名字，
谁也不曾道他怯懦。
众口的赞誉正昭示着他的价值。

===Original===
While, with name so worthy,
 Hettel held the throne,
 Wâ-te, the man of wisdom,
 never left undone

===Chinese===
当声名这般显赫的
黑特尔君临宝座之时，
智者瓦特
从不曾疏懒

===Original===
 His duty to his master,
 to see him three times yearly;
 Truly he was faithful,
 far and near, to the lord he held so dearly.

===Chinese===
他对主上应尽的职分——
每年三次前去觐见；
他实在忠贞不贰，
远近一心，深爱着他所尊奉的主上。

===Original===
Horant, the lord from Denmark,
 to court not seldom rode;
 Costly gems and clothing
 on the maids he there bestowed,

===Chinese===
丹麦的贵人霍兰特，
也不时骑马来到宫廷；
他把贵重的宝石与衣裳，
赏给宫廷里的少女们，

===Original===
 With gold and silken raiment,
 meet for women’s wearing:
 He from Daneland brought them,
