 and to all who wished was he of gifts unsparing.

===Chinese===
还有黄金与丝绸衣装，
皆是女子合宜的穿戴：
那是他从丹麦兰（Daneland）带来的，
凡有所愿，他馈赠从无吝色。

===Original===
The service true and steady
 that the liegemen of the king
 Gave to the lordly Hettel
 honor to him did bring.

===Chinese===
国王的封臣们
忠诚不移的效命，
献给了尊贵的黑特尔，
也为他赢来了令名。

===Original===
 Praised was he for knighthood
 more than any other:
 This Hilda also furthered,
 a queen herself, and child of a queenly mother.

===Chinese===
论骑士之德，他享有的赞誉
无人能出其右：
这也亏希尔达从旁成全——
她自己身为王后，又是王后般的母亲所生。

===Original===
Hilda, Hagen’s daughter,
 children two did bear
 Unto her lord, King Hettel:
 to bring them up with care

===Chinese===
哈根的女儿希尔达
为她的夫君黑特尔国王
诞下了一双儿女：
要把他们悉心抚育，

===Original===
 His faithful friends were bidden.
 Soon among his vassals
 Were the tidings bruited
 that an heir no more was lacking for his lands and castles.

===Chinese===
他忠信的朋友们奉命照拂。
不久，封臣中间
便传扬开一个消息：
他的国土城堡后继有人。

===Original===
One became a warrior,
 Ortwin was his name;
 To Wâ-te he was trusted.
 It was the teacher’s aim

===Chinese===
一个长成了武士，
名唤奥特温；
他被托付给瓦特调教。
那为师者的心愿，

===Original===
 That he from early boyhood
 should his thoughts be turning
 To all things good and worthy;
 to be a trusty knight he thus was learning.

===Chinese===
是要他打从少年时起，
便把心思转向
一切良善高贵之事；
他就这样学着做一名可倚仗的骑士。

===Original===
The very comely daughter
 of Hilda and the king
 Was called Gudrun the lovely:
 from the land of the Hegeling

===Chinese===
希尔达与国王所生的
那位十分姣美的女儿，
人称美人古德伦：
她自黑格林人（Hegeling）的国度

===Original===
 To Denmark she was carried,
 to be in her kinsmen’s wardship.
 Thus they helped King Hettel,
 and this they never felt to be a hardship.

===Chinese===
被接到丹麦之地，
交由亲族们抚育监护。
亲族们就这样帮扶黑特尔国王，
也从不觉得这是辛苦负担。

===Original===
When the maid grew older,
 her shape became so fair
 That neither man nor woman
 to praise her could forbear:

===Chinese===
待少女年岁渐长，
身姿出落得那般姣好，
无论男子还是女子，
都忍不住交口称道：

===Original===
 Far from the maiden’s birthplace,
 all her worth were telling.
 Gudrun her kinsfolk called her,
 in the Danish land where now she had her dwelling.

===Chinese===
在远离少女出生之地的地方，
人们也传颂着她的种种美好。
亲族们都唤她古德伦，
在这丹麦之地，她如今的栖身之乡。

===Original===
That age she now was reaching
 when, had she been a man,
 A sword she might have wielded.
 Many a prince was fain

===Chinese===
她已快到这样的年纪：
倘若生为男儿，
本已能够挥剑上阵。
多少位王子热切心愿，

===Original===
 To wed the lovely maiden,
 and sought her love and favor;
 But many came a-wooing
 who soon their hopes must lose, and win her never.

===Chinese===
要娶这位可爱的少女，
纷纷谋求她的爱情与青睐；
然而多少前来求亲的人，
希望转瞬落空，终究一无所获。

===Original===
However fair was Hilda,
 Hettel’s lovely wife,
 Yet was Gudrun more lovely,
 and fair beyond belief;

===Chinese===
希尔达纵然美丽，
是黑特尔娇爱的妻子，
古德伦却更加美丽，
美得叫人难以置信；

===Original===
 More fair than the early Hilda,
 erst to Ireland carried.
 Above all other women
 Gudrun was praised, ere yet the maid was married.

===Chinese===
比早年被携往爱尔兰的
先前的希尔达还要美丽。
在天下一切女子之上，
古德伦备受称颂，那时她尚未许嫁。

===Original===
Her father scorned to give her
 to the king of Alzabé;
 When he heard he could not win her
 to him ’twas a sorry day.

===Chinese===
她父亲不屑把她
许给阿尔扎比（Alzabé）之王；
待他听说娶她无望，
那一天于他好不凄惶。

===Original===
 He held himself most highly
 for all his kingly graces,
 And thought there could be no one
 whose deeds, like his, were worthy of men’s praises.

===Chinese===
他自视极高，
恃着君王的一切风采，
自忖普天之下再无一人，
功业如他一般堪受世人赞戴。

===Original===
Both brave he was and daring,
 and from the Moorland came:
 He was known afar and widely,
 Siegfried was his name;

===Chinese===
他既勇猛又剽悍，
从摩尔兰而来：
他声名传扬四方，
西格弗里德便是他的名号；

===Original===
 A king was he full mighty
 over vassals seven.
 He sued for Hilda’s daughter,
 such tales of her lofty worth to him were given.

===Chinese===
他是一位强大的君王，
统辖七方封臣。
他向希尔达的女儿求亲，
只因听得了她高贵令德的种种传闻。

===Original===
He, with his faithful liegemen
 from far Icaria’s strand,
 Won many costly prizes
 there in Hettel’s land:

===Chinese===
他偕同忠心的封臣，
自遥远的伊卡利亚（Icaria）海滨而来，
在黑特尔的国土上，
赢得了许多贵重的彩赏：

===Original===
 His strong and doughty warriors,
 in sight of ladies seated
 Before King Hettel’s castle,
 in games of knighthood often there were mated.

===Chinese===
他那些强壮剽悍的武士，
当着端坐的贵妇们、
在黑特尔国王的城堡前，
常在骑士比武中捉对交锋。

===Original===
When Hilda and her daughter
 passed the hall within,
 Before the house of Wigaleis
 there rose a mighty din

===Chinese===
当希尔达母女二人
穿行殿堂之内，
维加莱斯（Wigaleis）殿前
骤然腾起一片喧声——

===Original===
 From warriors of the Moorland,
 who, all boldly dashing,
 Rode in the sight of the women;
 oft of spears and shields was heard the clashing.

===Chinese===
那是摩尔兰的武士们，
个个奋勇驰突，
在贵妇们眼前策马来往；
不时可闻矛与盾铿然相击之声。

===Original===
Never could knight in tilting
 better in this behave.
 A friendly will she bore him,
 and oft kind words she gave,

===Chinese===
比武场上再没有骑士
能比这更见身手。
她对他怀有好感，
也常赐他温存的话语，

===Original===
 Though he was brown to look on,
 and in hue was dusky even.
 He for her love was yearning,
 yet for a wife she ne’er to him was given.

===Chinese===
尽管他望之肤色棕褐，
颜色甚至近于黝暗。
他渴慕着她的爱，
她却从未被许配给他为妻。

===Original===
This pained him beyond measure,
 and truly he was wroth
 That he from far had ridden,
 yet gave she not her troth.

===Chinese===
这使他痛苦万分，
他实在怒不可遏：
自己远道驰马而来，
她却终不肯许以身心的信誓。

===Original===
 To burn the land of Hettel
 then did he threaten madly:
 His followers from Moorland,
 when now his hopes were lost, were mourning sadly.

===Chinese===
于是他狂言恫吓，
要焚烧黑特尔的国土：
他那些摩尔兰的随从，
眼看希望断绝，无不黯然神伤。

===Original===
From him was the maid withholden
 by Hettel’s lofty pride;
 And now their loving friendship
 was ended on either side.

===Chinese===
只因黑特尔倨傲自矜，
少女才未许配于他；
从此这段彼此倾心的情谊，
双方便断绝净尽。

===Original===
 Then swore the Moor that never
 he his hate would slacken,
 And that the grudge he bore him,
 whate’er befell, should never be forsaken.

===Chinese===
那摩尔人于是立下狠誓，
他的仇恨永不松懈，
他心头怀着的怨毒，
无论日后如何，永不容抛却。

===Original===
Then from the land of the Hegeling
 rode they all away.
 When many years were ended,
 there came at last a day

===Chinese===
随后他们一行人马，
离开了黑格林人的国土。
待到许多年过去，
终于有一天到来——

===Original===
 When by a knight most worthy
 was bitter sorrow tasted;
 Then the foes of Herwic
 did him the worst they could, nor in it rested.

===Chinese===
一位极其高贵的骑士
尝尽了苦涩的悲恸；
那时赫尔维克（Herwic）的仇敌们
对他无所不用其极，犹不肯罢休。

## Tale X How Hartmut Sent to Woo Gudrun / 第十诗章 哈特穆特如何遣使向古德伦求亲

===Original===
Hartmut, a Norman prince, son of Ludwig and Gerlind, hears of the beauty of Gudrun, and resolves to woo her. Messengers are dispatched with letters, but receive a forbidding answer.

===Chinese===
诺曼底（Normandy）王子哈特穆特（Hartmut）——路德维希（Ludwig）与格尔林德（Gerlind）之子——听闻古德伦（Gudrun）的美貌，决意求娶她。使者们奉书信启程，得到的却是回绝的答复。

===Original===
Now in the land of Normandy
 the tale was widely told,
 That never fairer maiden
 did any man behold

===Chinese===
如今在诺曼底的国土上，
这故事流传得广远：
说世人从未有谁目睹过
比她更美的少女容颜——

===Original===
 Than was King Hettel’s daughter,
 Gudrun, the high-born lady.
 A king, whose name was Hartmut,
 to her then turned his love, to woo her ready.

===Chinese===
她便是黑特尔（Hettel）国王的女儿、
出身尊贵的古德伦。
一位名唤哈特穆特的君王，
当时便向她转投爱意，求娶之心已备。

===Original===
Gerlind, Hartmut’s mother,
 her wish to him made known,
 That he should woo the maiden;
 her word he followed soon.

===Chinese===
格尔林德是哈特穆特的母亲，
她向儿子道明了心愿：
他应当去求娶那位少女；
不多时他便依从了她的话。

===Original===
 First they sent for his father,
 when they of this had spoken;
 He bore the name of Ludwig,
 and in Norman lands he wore the kingly token.

===Chinese===
他们把此事商议停当，
先派人去请他的父亲；
父亲名唤路德维希，
在诺曼底的疆土上佩戴着王权的徽记。

===Original===
Then the aged father
 rode to see his son.
 Of the end that he was seeking
 had Ludwig knowledge won;

===Chinese===
于是这位年迈的父亲
骑马前来看望儿子。
路德维希已经知晓
儿子谋求的是怎样一番结局；

===Original===
 But when to him he hearkened,
 and learned his wishes wholly,
 Evil he foreboded,
 yet still the youth’s fond hopes upheld he fully.

===Chinese===
但当他侧耳倾听，
把他的心愿尽数明了，
便预感到凶险，
却仍全然支持这少年痴心的期冀。

===Original===
“Who tells you,” said King Ludwig,
 “she is so very fair?
 Though she all lands were owning,
 the home is not so near,

===Chinese===
「谁告诉你们的，」路德维希国王说道，
「说她就那般美丽？
纵使她坐拥天下所有的疆土，
那少女栖身的家园也并不近便，

===Original===
 Wherein the maid is dwelling,
 that we should go a-wooing;
 If we sent our men before us
 to ask her love, their task they would soon be ruing.”

===Chinese===
容不得我们前去求亲；
倘若我们先派人马
去恳求她的爱情，
他们转瞬便要为这趟差事叫苦不迭。」

===Original===
Then did Hartmut answer:
 “For me ’tis not too far;
 Whene’er the lord of a kingdom
 no pain or toil doth spare

===Chinese===
哈特穆特于是回答：
「在我并不嫌路途遥远；
无论哪一国的君主，
只要不辞劳苦、不畏艰辛，

===Original===
 To win a wife and riches,
 he gains a life-long blessing.
 My wish, I pray you, follow;
 let men be sent, that they my suit be pressing.”

===Chinese===
求得妻室与财富，
便是赢来终身的福分。
我求您依从我的心愿；
且派人前去，好将我的求亲殷殷促成。」

===Original===
Then spake his mother, Gerlind,
 of Normandy the queen:
 “Letters must now be written;
 let clothes, the best e’er seen,

===Chinese===
随后他的母亲格尔林德开口，
她是诺曼底的王后：
「书信须得即刻写下；
再置办世人所见最华美的衣裳，

===Original===
 With gold, to those be given
 upon your errand speeding;
 They, too, must learn the roadways
 that towards the home of fair Gudrun are leading.”

===Chinese===
连同黄金，一并赐予
那些登程奉命的使者；
他们还须认清条条道路，
那通向美人古德伦家园的方向。」

===Original===
Then spake again King Ludwig:
 “Know you not full well
 That Hilda, the maiden’s mother,
 did erst in Ireland dwell?

===Chinese===
路德维希国王又开口道：
「你们岂不是全然知晓，
那位少女的母亲希尔达（Hilda）
早年居住在爱尔兰？

===Original===
 And know you not what happened
 to many a one who sought her?
 Her kin are proud and lofty,
 and now will scorn the love we shall have brought her.”

===Chinese===
你们岂不晓得，当年多少人前来向她求亲，
落得怎样的下场？
她的亲族倨傲而显贵，
如今更要把我们送上门的情爱轻贱。」

===Original===
Then young Hartmut answered:
 “Though with a warlike band
 I afar must seek her,
 over sea and land,

===Chinese===
年轻的哈特穆特于是回答：
「纵然要我率领一支雄师，
去远方寻访她，
跨过大海与陆地，

===Original===
 That shall I do most willingly:
 my heart to her is given,
 And never will I rest me
 till I for Hilda’s daughter happily have striven.”

===Chinese===
我也万般甘愿：
我的心已许给了她，
我永不歇息，
直到为希尔达的女儿求得美满的结局。」

===Original===
“Gladly will I help you,”
 King Ludwig then did say:
 “Let this now make you happy;
 erelong, upon the way

===Chinese===
「我很乐意帮你，」
路德维希国王当时说道：
「且让此事使你宽怀；
不久之后，在路途之上，

===Original===
 I’ll send twelve sumpter-horses
 bearing silver treasure;
 That when they hear our errand,
 our wealth and worth they may more rightly measure.”

===Chinese===
我将派出十二匹驮马，
满载白银的珍宝；
待他们听罢我们的来意，
自会将我们的富贵与分量掂量得更准。」

===Original===
By Hartmut then were chosen
 sixty men, to send
 To woo the fair young maiden,
 and help to him to lend;

===Chinese===
随后哈特穆特选定
六十人出使，
去求娶那位美丽的少女，
也好多为他出一份力；

===Original===
 With food and clothing also
 well were they outfitted,
 And on the road well guided:
 Ludwig was wise, and was in this foresighted.

===Chinese===
他们还领到充足的食物
与衣物，装备周全，
一路之上亦有妥善向导：
路德维希深有智谋，于此早有先见。

===Original===
When everything was ready
 that soon the men would need,
 Then were letters written,
 sealed, and given with speed,

===Chinese===
待到使者们行将启程所需的
一切都已置办齐备，
书信便一一写就、钤印，
火速交到他们手中，

===Original===
 Both by brave young Hartmut
 and his queenly mother.
 Then from home they started;
 so proud a throng there never was another.

===Chinese===
既有英武的少年哈特穆特的手笔，
也有他王后般的母亲的。
随后他们自家中启程；
这般气派的仪从，世上再无其二。

===Original===
Fast they rode and steadily
 for many a day and night,
 Until the land they sought for
 came at last in sight,

===Chinese===
他们快马加鞭，稳稳疾驰，
过了不知多少个日夜，
直到他们寻觅的国土
终于映入眼帘，

===Original===
 And they might tell the errand
 they were thither bringing.
 Long was Hartmut waiting,
 while love and care were in his heart upspringing.

===Chinese===
他们这才好去禀明
此行带来的使命。
哈特穆特久候多时，
爱与牵挂在他心头一齐萌生。

===Original===
Over land and rivers
 they took their toilsome way,
 As far as in days a hundred
 a pasturing herd may stray,

===Chinese===
他们跋涉过陆地与河流，
一路备尝艰辛，
其程途之遥，
足抵放牧的畜群百日游荡之远，

===Original===
 Until the land of the Hegelings
 lay before them stretching.
 Their steeds were worn and weary
 ere they gave the letters they were fetching.

===Chinese===
直到黑格林人（Hegeling）的国土
铺展在他们眼前。
骏马早已困乏疲惫，
他们方才呈上携来的书信。

===Original===
At last they far had ridden,
 and to the sea had come,
 Upon the shores of Denmark:
 sadly they long did roam,

===Chinese===
他们终于骑行了老远，
来到大海之滨，
在丹麦的海岸上，
凄凄惶惶地徘徊许久，

===Original===
 Before they reached the kingdom,
 and its lord did know them;
 Now they begged for guidance,
 and men were bid the nearest way to show them.

===Chinese===
方才抵达那处王国，
为其君主所识得；
这时他们恳请人引路，
众人受命为他们指示最近的路途。

===Original===
The news was given to Horant,
 the knight well-bred and bold;
 Now asked the errand-bearers,
 and the truth to them was told,

===Chinese===
消息报到了霍兰特（Horant）那里，
那位教养有素、英武大胆的骑士；
使者们随即探问来意，
真相也向他们和盘托出，

===Original===
 About King Hettel and Hilda,
 and all they had been hearing.
 They saw the men of Hettel
 coming in throngs, their shields and weapons bearing.

===Chinese===
说的是黑特尔国王与希尔达，
以及他们一路听闻的一切。
他们望见黑特尔的部众
成群而来，携着盾牌与兵刃。

===Original===
Horant, lord of Daneland,
 then to his liegemen spake,
 And bade for the errand-bearers
 a safeguard now to make,

===Chinese===
丹麦兰（Daneland）之主霍兰特
随即向封臣们发话，
吩咐他们即刻为使者们
布置护行的卫队，

===Original===
 And that the men of Hartmut
 should be by them well guided
 To the court of his lord, King Hettel;
 they grudged no toil, and well his bidding heeded.

===Chinese===
并让哈特穆特的人马
由他们妥善引领，
去到他主上黑特尔国王的宫廷；
众封臣不辞辛劳，谨遵他的号令。

===Original===
When thro’ the Hegeling kingdom
 the heralds took their way,
 So lordly was their bearing,
 that often men did say:

===Chinese===
当信使们取道穿越
黑格林人的王国，
他们的气派何等尊贵，
以致人们常常议论道：

===Original===
 “These folk are rich and mighty,
 whatever they are seeking.”
 The news to the king was carried,
 and soon to him all men the tale were speaking.

===Chinese===
「这伙人富贵而有威势，
不论他们所求为何。」
消息呈到了国王面前，
很快人人都在向他讲述这段传闻。

===Original===
To all the guests from Normandy
 were lodgings given there;
 The king now bade his liegemen
 to wait on them with care.

===Chinese===
所有来自诺曼底的宾客
都在那里得了居所；
国王吩咐封臣们
殷勤地侍奉照料。

===Original===
 He knew not yet their errand,
 and why to him they had ridden;
 But on the twelfth day, early,
 young Hartmut’s men before the king were bidden.

===Chinese===
他尚不知晓他们的来意，
也不知他们为何前来；
直到第十二天清早，
少年哈特穆特的使者们才蒙召来到国王面前。

===Original===
An earl there was among them;
 how well his breeding showed!
 Upon their clothing also
 were praises high bestowed;

===Chinese===
他们中间有一位伯爵；
他的教养何等出众！
他们的衣装也
备受众口称扬；

===Original===
 They rode the best of horses
 on which men e’er were seated,
 And before the king they gathered,
 in fairest guise, that well they might be greeted.

===Chinese===
他们骑乘的骏马，
是世人从未坐过的好马，
他们齐聚在国王面前，
仪容俊美，好教国王以礼相迎。

===Original===
The king gave kindly welcome,
 as also did his men,
 Until their wooing errand
 was unto him made plain:

===Chinese===
国王赐以亲切的欢迎，
他的臣下也是如此，
直到他们求亲的来意
向他明白道出：

===Original===
 Then were they ill-treated,
 and knew the king’s hard feeling.
 I ween the mighty Hettel
 to grant young Hartmut’s wish would ne’er be willing.

===Chinese===
随后他们便备受冷遇，
尝到了国王的严词厉色。
我料想威严的黑特尔
断然不会俯允少年哈特穆特的心愿。

===Original===
One who in that was skilful
 to the king the letters read;
 But he was greatly angered
 that they to court were led

===Chinese===
一位精于此道的人
向国王诵读了书信；
国王却勃然大怒，
恼恨他们竟被引到宫廷——

===Original===
 By the good and upright Horant,
 a knight so brave and noble;
 And, had they not his friendship,
 they had not left the king without more trouble.

===Chinese===
引路的是善良正直的霍兰特，
那样一位勇武高贵的骑士；
若非顾念他的情谊，
他们岂能不添更多麻烦就离开国王。

===Original===
Then spake to them King Hettel:
 “No good to you ’twill bring
 That you were sent a-wooing
 by Hartmut, your lord and king.

===Chinese===
黑特尔国王向他们说道：
「哈特穆特——你们的主上与国王——
派你们前来求亲，
这于你们没有半点好处。

===Original===
 To pay for this full dearly
 you may well be fearing;
 Your kingly master’s wishes
 both I and Lady Hilda are wroth at hearing.”

===Chinese===
你们大可心惊胆战，
只怕要为这事付出高昂的代价；
你们那君王的心愿，
我与希尔达夫人听了俱是怒不可遏。」

===Original===
One among them answered:
 “Hartmut makes it known
 That much he loves the maiden;
 and if to wear the crown

===Chinese===
他们中间一人答道：
「哈特穆特要向诸位言明，
他何等深爱那位少女；
倘若她肯屈尊戴上王冠，

===Original===
 In Normandy she deigneth,
 before his friends there living,
 That he, a knight all spotless,
 will rightly earn the love she shall be giving.”

===Chinese===
在诺曼底当着他的友朋临朝而治，
那么他——一名全无瑕疵的骑士，
必将堂堂正正地赢得
她将要赐予的爱。」

===Original===
Then quoth the Lady Hilda:
 “How can she be his wife?
 A hundred and three of his castles
 his father held in fief,

===Chinese===
希尔达夫人随即说道：
「她怎能做他的妻子？
他父亲名下以封邑领有的城堡，
足有一百零三座，

===Original===
 Within the land of Cardigan,
 from Hagen, my noble sire;
 It ill becomes my kinsmen
 to be King Ludwig’s vassals, or owe him hire.

===Chinese===
尽在卡迪根（Cardigan）之地，
受封于我尊贵的父亲哈根（Hagen）；
我的亲族若屈居路德维希国王封臣之列、向他负有贡役，
那实在有失体面。

===Original===
“Ludwig dwelt in Scotland,
 and there it erst befell
 That a brother of King Otto
 did wrong to Ludwig deal:

===Chinese===
「路德维希昔年住在苏格兰，
那时节曾有这样一桩旧事：
奥托（Otto）国王的一位兄弟
对路德维希横加欺凌：

===Original===
 Both were Hagen’s vassals,
 and of him their lands had taken;
 And thus my father’s friendship
 for him was lost, and hate instead did waken.

===Chinese===
两人本都是哈根的封臣，
都从他手中领受封地；
我父亲对路德维希的情谊
就此断绝，反倒生出仇隙。

===Original===
“Say you now to Hartmut
 she ne’er his wife shall be.
 Your lord is not so worthy
 that he to boast is free,

===Chinese===
「你们这就去告诉哈特穆特，
她永不能做他的妻子。
你们的君主并不那样尊贵，
还配不上夸口

===Original===
 That he doth love my daughter,
 and she doth not disdain him;
 Bid him elsewhere be looking,
 if he be fain a queen for his land to gain him.”

===Chinese===
说他爱我的女儿、
而我的女儿又不曾轻贱于他；
教他往别处张望罢，
若他还想为他的国土觅得一位王后。」

===Original===
The heralds’ hearts were heavy;
 ’twas not for their good name
 That they, for miles full many,
 in sorrow and in shame,

===Chinese===
信使们心头沉重；
这实在有损他们的令名——
他们要含着悲苦与羞惭，
跋涉漫漫多少里路，

===Original===
 Back to their homes in Normandy
 this news must carry sadly.
 Hartmut, as well as Ludwig,
 was vexed that they herein were foiled so badly.

===Chinese===
把这消息凄凄惨惨地
捎回诺曼底的家园。
哈特穆特与路德维希一样，
恼恨此事竟败得这般凄惶。

===Original===
Forthwith to them said Hartmut:
 “Tell me now the truth,
 The grand-daughter of Hagen
 have you seen, forsooth?

===Chinese===
哈特穆特随即向他们说道：
「现在对我说句实话，
哈根的孙女，
你们可曾当真亲眼见过？

===Original===
 Is the maid, Gudrun, as lovely
 as men have here been saying?
 May God bring shame to Hettel,
 that he my suit with such ill-will is paying!”

===Chinese===
那位少女古德伦，
可真如这里的人所说的那般可爱？
愿上帝降罪于黑特尔，
他竟以这般恶意回报我的求亲！」

===Original===
Then the earl thus answered:
 “This can I truly say﻿—
 Whoe’er shall see the maiden
 must feel her charms and sway;

===Chinese===
伯爵于是回答：
「这话我可以真切地说——
谁只要亲眼见到那位少女，
必会折服于她的魅力与风华；

===Original===
 Above all maids and women,
 her worth is past the telling.”
 Then quoth the kingly Hartmut:
 “To live without her ne’er shall I be willing.”

===Chinese===
在一切少女与妇人之上，
她的美好言语难以述尽。」
尊贵的哈特穆特随即说道：
「没有她而活着，我永不甘愿。」

===Original===
Whereon his mother, Gerlind,
 sadly thus did say,
 With tears her lot bewailing:
 “My son, oh, lack-a-day!

===Chinese===
于是他的母亲格尔林德
凄然这般说道，
涕泪涟涟，哀叹自身的命途：
「我的儿啊，苦哉！

===Original===
 Alas that e’er the heralds
 to win the maiden started!
 If we at home had kept them,
 e’en to this day had I been still light-hearted.”

===Chinese===
唉，悔不该当初遣出信使，
教他们去赢取那位少女！
倘若我们把他们留在家中，
我到今日也还是个无忧无虑之人。」

## Tale XI How Herwic Sent to Seek Gudrun and How Hartmut Came Himself / 第十一诗章 赫尔维克如何遣使求娶古德伦、哈特穆特如何亲自前来

===Original===
Herwic, king of Sealand, sends suitors for the hand of Gudrun, who are also rejected. Hartmut of Normandy comes to woo her in person, and is kindly received by her; she, however, begs him, if he values his life, to leave the court. He returns to Normandy, resolved to win her by force.

===Chinese===
西兰（Sealand）之王赫尔维克（Herwic）遣使向古德伦（Gudrun）求亲，也同样遭到回绝。诺曼底（Normandy）的哈特穆特（Hartmut）亲自前来向她求婚，受到她亲切的接待；然而她恳求他，倘若还珍重性命，就离开这座宫廷。他返回诺曼底，决意以武力赢得她。

===Original===
Hartmut left his wooing
 to wait for many a year.
 Soon a tale was bruited
 (’twas true what men did hear)

===Chinese===
哈特穆特把他的求亲
搁置下来，等待了许多年。
不久一桩传闻流布开来
（世人所闻句句是真）——

===Original===
 Of one whose name was Herwic,
 a king as yet but youthful;
 Often his worth was spoken,
 and men yet speak of him with praises truthful.

===Chinese===
说的是有一人名叫赫尔维克，
尚是一位年轻的君王；
他的贤德常有人称说，
至今人们仍以真切的赞词把他传扬。

===Original===
He began his wooing,
 trusting the lovely maid
 Would take him for her lover;
 long his hopes he fed,

===Chinese===
他开始了自己的求婚，
指望那可爱的少女
肯把他当作自己的爱人；
他的希望滋长了许久，

===Original===
 And much he toiled to win her,
 both with love and riches:
 But though the maid was willing,
 her father, Hettel, he in vain beseeches.

===Chinese===
还多方辛劳要赢得她，
又凭爱情、又凭财货：
无奈少女虽然情愿，
她父亲黑特尔（Hettel），他苦求也是徒然。

===Original===
Though Herwic long was striving,
 and men to seek her rode,
 Yet was his wooing slighted;
 for this his wrath he showed.

===Chinese===
赫尔维克虽长久力争，
还遣人骑马前去向她求聘，
他的求婚仍遭了轻慢；
为此他把心头怒意显露。

===Original===
 The heart of proud young Herwic
 by heavy care was fettered;
 Freely his love he gave her,
 and thought a life with her could not be bettered.

===Chinese===
高傲的青年赫尔维克，
一颗心被沉重的忧愁缚住；
他把自己的爱全然付与她，
认定与她共度的生涯再无更善。

===Original===
There came at length a morning
 when it to them befell
 That in the Hegeling kingdom
 both knights and maids as well,

===Chinese===
终于有一天早晨来到，
事情便在那时落到他们头上——
在黑格林人（Hegeling）的王国里，
武士们连同众位少女，

===Original===
 With many lovely ladies,
 his coming never fearing,
 Before them saw bold Hartmut;
 Hettel could not believe he’d be so daring.

===Chinese===
偕着许多美丽的贵妇，
全不惧怕他的来临，
竟在眼前望见勇悍的哈特穆特；
黑特尔简直不能相信他竟这般大胆。

===Original===
From this did endless evil
 soon come upon the land:
 These guests high-born and worthy
 were yet an unknown band;

===Chinese===
由此无穷的祸患
不久便降临这片国土：
这批宾客虽出身高贵、可敬可重，
却终究是一队来历不明的人；

===Original===
 Hartmut and his kinsmen
 their host’s goodwill were sharing,
 And he the hope still harbored
 that the maid would yet the crown with him be wearing.

===Chinese===
哈特穆特和他的亲族
共享着主人殷勤的款待；
他心里仍怀着一个指望：
少女终有一日会与他同戴王冠。

===Original===
Now before Queen Hilda
 by ladies he was seen
 To stand with lofty breeding,
 and with a stately mien.

===Chinese===
且说在希尔达（Hilda）王后跟前，
贵妇们望着他
仪止高贵地侍立，
一派庄严的气度风采。

===Original===
 There the proud young Hartmut
 wore a look so knightly,
 That he the love of ladies
 well might ask, and ’twould be granted rightly.

===Chinese===
那高傲的青年哈特穆特，
眉宇间一派骑士气概，
他若向贵妇们求取爱情，
本是合宜之事，必获允准。

===Original===
Well-grown was he in body,
 fair he was and bold,
 Kind as well as lordly.
 Why I ne’er was told

===Chinese===
他身量长得挺拔，
容貌俊美，性情勇毅，
既仁厚而又威严。
只是其中缘由，我从未听闻——

===Original===
 Had Hettel and Queen Hilda
 from him withheld their daughter,
 When he had thought to woo her;
 wroth was he to be scorned when now he sought her.

===Chinese===
黑特尔与希尔达王后
为何在他有意求娶之时
不肯把女儿许配给他；
如今亲来求婚遭拒，他不由怒从心起。

===Original===
Of her his heart had longed for
 he now had gained the sight;
 There oft were stolen glances
 between Gudrun and the knight.

===Chinese===
他心中渴慕已久的她，
如今总算得以相见；
古德伦与这位骑士之间，
不时偷递着脉脉的目光。

===Original===
 He made it known to the maiden,
 by speech from others hidden,
 That he was young King Hartmut,
 and from the Norman land had lately ridden.

===Chinese===
他向少女表明了身份，
言语瞒过旁人的耳目，
说他就是年轻的哈特穆特国王，
新近方从诺曼底之地驰马而来。

===Original===
Then she told her wooer
 the pain to her it gave;
 And though she wished he ever
 a happy life might have,

===Chinese===
于是她向这位求婚者
诉说这情形令她何等痛心；
虽然她愿他从今往后
一生幸福安乐，

===Original===
 Yet from her father’s kingdom
 she begged him now to hasten,
 For in the land of Hettel
 was his life at risk, and this would never lessen.

===Chinese===
却恳求他此刻快快离开
她父亲的国境，
因为在黑特尔的国土上
他性命堪虞，且这险情永不会消减。

===Original===
She looked on him so kindly
 that now her heart was warned
 That he should stay no longer,
 for here his suit was spurned.

===Chinese===
她望向他的神色那般亲切，
以至她心中已然警醒：
他不该再在此逗留，
因为他的求婚在此已遭摒弃。

===Original===
 Friendly was she to Hartmut,
 who her love so wanted,
 But his hopes she little heeded,
 and while he wooed, not much to him she granted.

===Chinese===
她对哈特穆特和颜悦色，
而他那样渴求她的爱；
但她对他的指望并不在意，
他求爱之时，她未肯多赐允诺。

===Original===
At last her well-bred lover
 from Hettel’s land must go;
 He bore upon his shoulders
 a heavy load of woe:

===Chinese===
她那位彬彬有礼的爱慕者，
终于只得离开黑特尔的国土；
他的双肩之上
负着一副沉重的哀伤：

===Original===
 To wreak his wrath on Hettel
 would he now be choosing,
 Yet feared he, if he harmed him,
 that he the maiden’s love would then be losing.

===Chinese===
他此刻一心要拣的，
是把怒火向黑特尔发泄，
却又惧怕一旦加害于他，
便会就此失去少女的爱。

===Original===
’Twas thus the daring Hartmut
 the Hegeling kingdom left;
 Much he felt of sadness,
 though not of hope bereft.

===Chinese===
大胆的哈特穆特就这样
离开了黑格林人的王国；
他心头满怀愁苦，
希望却并未断绝。

===Original===
 He knew not yet the ending
 of his wooing of the maiden;
 For the sake of her, thereafter,
 were helmets cleft, and many sorrow-laden.

===Chinese===
他还不知这场向少女
求亲的结局会怎样；
为了她的缘故，从今以后，
多少头盔遭劈裂，多少人悲苦载身。

===Original===
When he had reached his kingdom,
 and home again did turn,
 Where dwelt his father and mother,
 Hartmut, grim and stern,

===Chinese===
待他回到自己的王国，
重新踏上故乡的家园，
那里住着他的父亲母亲；
哈特穆特面色阴沉、神情严厉，

===Original===
 For war with Hettel longing,
 began to make him ready.
 Gerlind, the old she-devil,
 at all times spurred him on with hatred steady.

===Chinese===
他渴盼与黑特尔开战，
便开始整备起来。
格尔林德（Gerlind），那老女魔，
时时刻刻以不熄的仇恨煽动着他。

## Tale XII How Herwic Made War on Hettel, and How Gudrun Was Betrothed to Him / 第十二诗章 赫尔维克如何向黑特尔兴兵，古德伦如何与他订婚

===Original===
Herwic, being forbidden by Hettel to woo his daughter, invades his kingdom, and, after a battle, Gudrun, with Hettel’s consent, is betrothed to him.

===Chinese===
赫尔维克（Herwic）被黑特尔（Hettel）禁止向他的女儿求亲，便兴兵侵入他的王国；经过一场战斗，古德伦（Gudrun）征得黑特尔同意，与赫尔维克订了婚。

===Original===
What more befell young Hartmut
 we now forbear to say.
 Upon the brave King Herwic
 a weight of sorrow lay,

===Chinese===
年轻的哈特穆特（Hartmut）后来的遭际，
我们暂且按下不讲。
英勇的赫尔维克国王身上，
也压着一副沉重的忧伤，

===Original===
 As great as that of Hartmut,
 for love of the high-born lady.
 He, with all his kinsmen,
 to woo Gudrun, as best they might, made ready.

===Chinese===
其重不亚于哈特穆特所背负的，
都为着那位出身高贵的少女。
他偕同全体亲族，
竭尽所能，准备向古德伦求亲。

===Original===
Near her he was dwelling,
 and there he held his land.
 A thousand times though daily
 he should send to ask her hand,

===Chinese===
他就在少女的近旁安居，
在那里统领自己的国土。
纵使他每日遣使求婚，
来上这么一千个回数，

===Original===
 Ever would his wooing
 be met with scorn and flouting;
 But though he now was thwarted,
 later on her, as his wife, he was fondly doting.

===Chinese===
他的求亲也总是
遭到讥嘲与冷落；
然而虽则此刻受挫，
他却愈发痴心不改，一心要她做自己的妻子。

===Original===
The king forbade him longer
 to woo Gudrun, his child;
 Then sent he word in anger
 that never would he yield:

===Chinese===
国王不许他再
向女儿古德伦求亲；
他便愤然传话回去：
自己绝不肯罢休：

===Original===
 Hettel should see him coming,
 with men and shields, a-wooing;
 And this to him and Hilda
 would evil bring, that they would long be ruing.

===Chinese===
黑特尔将要看见他
携着人马盾牌前来求婚；
这会给黑特尔和希尔达（Hilda）
带来祸殃，教他们长久懊悔。

===Original===
Whose rede it was I know not,
 but thrice a thousand men,
 Showing thus their friendship,
 were soon with Herwic seen.

===Chinese===
这是谁人献策我不得而知，
总之三千名武士，
以此显明了他们的情谊，
不久便随侍在赫尔维克身边。

===Original===
 By them against the Hegelings
 harm erelong was plotted
 For the sake of the lovely maiden
 he fondly hoped would be to him allotted.

===Chinese===
他们不久便合谋，
要加害黑格林人（Hegeling），
只为了那位可爱的少女——
他痴心盼着她会归他所有。

===Original===
Those who came from Sturmland
 the tale would not believe,
 To those from Denmark also
 none the tidings gave;

===Chinese===
那些从施图尔姆兰（Sturmland）来的人
不愿相信这传闻，
也无人把这消息
传给丹麦来的人们；

===Original===
 But Irold, lord of Ortland,
 soon the word was hearing
 That now the daring Herwic
 for warlike ends to Hettel’s home was faring.

===Chinese===
唯有奥特兰（Ortland）之主伊罗尔德（Irold）
很快听得了风声：
胆气逼人的赫尔维克
正为兴兵之事直奔黑特尔的家园。

===Original===
When ’twas known to Hettel
 that Herwic, fearing naught,
 E’en now the land was nearing,
 and followers with him brought,

===Chinese===
当黑特尔得知
赫尔维克无所忌惮、
此刻已逼近国土，
还携着大队人马随行，

===Original===
 Then asked he of his kinsmen,
 and of the queen, his lady:
 “What say you to the tidings?
 I hear that guests to our home have come already.”

===Chinese===
他便询问众位亲族，
也询问王后、他的夫人：
「你们怎么看这消息？
我听说贵客已经来到我们家门。」

===Original===
She said: “What can I answer,
 but that ’tis well and right,
 When one such deeds is doing
 as befit a worthy knight,

===Chinese===
她答道：「我能答什么呢，
只有说这很正当：
一个人行出这般事业，
正合高贵骑士的本分，

===Original===
 Though good or ill it bring us,
 praise should they be earning.
 Can aught amiss befall him?
 Herwic is wise, and aye for honor yearning.”

===Chinese===
哪怕带给我们的是福是祸，
他也该赢得称誉。
他又会有什么差池？
赫尔维克明智，永远渴慕着荣誉。」

===Original===
His queenly wife said further:
 “Yet must we beware,
 That he may bring no burden
 unto our kinsmen here.

===Chinese===
他那王后般的夫人接着说：
「但我们仍须提防，
别让他把重负
带给我们此地的亲族。

===Original===
 This have many told me﻿—
 ’tis for the sake of your daughter
 That he with many warriors
 has come into your borders, o’er the water.”

===Chinese===
这话已有许多人告诉我——
他是为了你的女儿，
才带着众多武士
渡海来到你的边疆。」

===Original===
Hettel with his kinsmen
 had waited a little too long:
 The wrath of young King Herwic
 now had waxen strong.

===Chinese===
黑特尔偕众亲族
未免等候得久了些：
年轻的赫尔维克国王
怒火此刻已炽盛难遏。

===Original===
 In the cool of the early morning,
 he, with followers daring,
 Reached King Hettel’s castle,
 and later with his men the strife was sharing.

===Chinese===
清晨凉爽的时分，
他偕着剽悍的部众，
直抵黑特尔国王的城堡，
随后便与部下一同投身战事。

===Original===
While yet the men were sleeping
 within King Hettel’s halls,
 The watchman from the castle
 down to them loudly calls:

===Chinese===
黑特尔国王的殿中
将士们尚在酣睡，
城堡上的守望人
向他们高声呼喊：

===Original===
 “Up from your rest now, quickly!
 Arm yourselves and listen!
 Foes from abroad are coming!
 E’en now, on their way, I see the helmets glisten.”

===Chinese===
「快从榻上起来，快！
披甲执兵，侧耳听！
外邦的仇敌来了！
这便可见头盔在来路上闪闪生光。」

===Original===
From off their beds upsprang they,
 no longer dared they lie;
 Whoe’er there was among them,
 in rank or low or high,

===Chinese===
众人从床榻上一跃而起，
再不敢躺卧不动；
无论其中是谁，
位分是低是高，

===Original===
 Must bear a heavy burden,
 for life and honor caring.
 Thus the young King Herwic
 strove for a wife, the storm of warfare daring.

===Chinese===
都得挑起沉重的担子，
为性命与荣誉而挂心。
年轻的赫尔维克国王
就这样冒着战争风暴，为娶妻力争。

===Original===
Hettel and Queen Hilda
 had now to the window come:
 Men they saw with Herwic,
 brought from a far-off home

===Chinese===
黑特尔与王后希尔达
此时已来到窗前：
他们看见随赫尔维克来的人马，
皆来自遥远的家乡，

===Original===
 Among the hills of Galeis,
 where they had their dwelling;
 These the mighty Morunc
 in Waleis knew, and oft of them was telling.

===Chinese===
就在加莱斯（Galeis）的群山之中，
那是他们安居的地方；
强健的莫伦克（Morunc）
在瓦莱斯（Waleis）便识得他们，还时常谈起。

===Original===
The foes were seen by Hettel,
 thronging towards the gate.
 Well Gudrun’s brave father
 must fear to meet their hate,

===Chinese===
黑特尔眼见仇敌
蜂拥冲向城门。
古德伦勇敢的父亲
也不免惧怕他们的凶焰，

===Original===
 As they were rushing onward,
 though high his heart was swelling:
 Much they roused his anger,
 but them his burghers helped erelong in quelling.

===Chinese===
眼见他们汹涌冲来，
他胸中怒气高涨：
这些人大大激起了他的愤怒，
但他的堡民不久便助他把他们制服。

===Original===
Armed to guard the castle
 were a hundred men or more;
 Hettel himself fought boldly,
 goodwill for this he bore.

===Chinese===
披坚执锐护卫城堡的
有上百名武士；
黑特尔亲自奋勇力战，
对此他满心甘愿。

===Original===
 His lieges all were doughty,
 but yet they could not save him;
 Hard were the blows for Hettel,
 that in the fight the brave young Herwic gave him.

===Chinese===
他的封臣个个剽悍，
却仍旧护不住他；
黑特尔挨的那些打击何其沉重，
都是年轻勇敢的赫尔维克在酣战中给的。

===Original===
Upon his foeman’s helmet
 whizzing blasts, fire-hot,
 Were struck by the daring Herwic.
 The many blows he smote

===Chinese===
在仇敌的头盔上，
呼啸的火风灼灼而生，
那是剽悍的赫尔维克劈出来的。
他劈出的击打又多又重，

===Original===
 Gudrun now saw with wonder,
 her eyes upon him feeding:
 He seemed a knight most worthy,
 and love she felt, e’en though her heart was bleeding.

===Chinese===
古德伦此刻惊奇地望着，
双眼在他身上流连不去：
他看来是那样高贵的骑士，
她心中生出了爱，纵然心在滴血。

===Original===
Hettel bore his weapon
 grimly ’gainst his foe;
 Of strength no less than riches
 he had, in truth, enow:

===Chinese===
黑特尔挥动兵刃，
狠狠迎向他的仇敌；
论力气与财富，
他实在都不匮乏：

===Original===
 But soon he did unwisely,
 he pressed on him too nearly,
 And those within the castle
 saw the fight between them all too clearly.

===Chinese===
但他随即行得不慎，
逼得对方太近，
城堡里的人们
把两人的拼斗看得一清二楚。

===Original===
The sore-beleaguered dwellers
 the gates would gladly shut;
 But now their losses told them
 that this would nothing boot:

===Chinese===
被围困得苦不堪言的守城者
巴不得关闭城门；
可损失告诉他们，
这样做毫无益处：

===Original===
 Friends as well as foemen
 near the gates were thronging,
 And great was the hope of Herwic
 to win the maid for whom his heart was longing.

===Chinese===
朋友与仇敌
都拥在城门近旁，
赫尔维克怀着莫大的指望，
要赢得他心底渴念的姑娘。

===Original===
Hettel then and Herwic
 against each other dashed,
 In sight of all their followers;
 flames shot out and flashed

===Chinese===
黑特尔与赫尔维克
就此对撞交锋，
在双方部众眼前；只见道道火光迸射，
灼灼闪耀；

===Original===
 On the bosses of the bucklers
 which they both were wearing:
 But little while it lasted,
 ere knowledge of each other they were sharing.

===Chinese===
就在两人各自佩戴的圆盾
盾心之上明灭不定：
但这一幕没有持续多久，
他们便已彼此相认。

===Original===
When Hettel saw in Herwic
 a warrior so proud,
 And one so truly daring,
 he cried to all aloud:

===Chinese===
当黑特尔在赫尔维克身上
看见这样豪雄的武士，
这样无所畏惧的英杰，
他便向众人大声喊道：

===Original===
 “Should any here forbid me
 that I with him be friendly,
 He knows the knight but little;
 deadly wounds he hews, in mood unkindly.”

===Chinese===
「此处若有谁不许我
与他结成友朋，
那他实在不识这位骑士：
他劈出的是致命重伤，出手决不容情。」

===Original===
Gudrun, the lovely maiden,
 looked on, and heard the din.
 Luck is round and rolling,
 like a ball, I ween;

===Chinese===
可爱的少女古德伦
望着，听着这片喧嚣。
命运是圆的，滚滚而转，
恰似一只圆球，我这么想；

===Original===
 And since to end the fighting
 to her it was not given,
 She hoped that, when ’twas over,
 her father and his foe would find their strength was even.

===Chinese===
既然要叫这场战斗停下来
并非她力所能及，
她便但愿战罢之时，
父亲与他的对手势均力敌、不分胜负。

===Original===
She then began to call to him,
 from out the palace hall:
 “Hettel, my noble father,
 behold how blood doth fall,

===Chinese===
她于是从宫殿大厅里
向父亲高声呼唤：
「黑特尔，我尊贵的父亲，
看哪，鲜血正在洒落，

===Original===
 From out the hauberks flowing!
 Everywhere about us
 The walls therewith are spattered!
 A neighbor ill is Herwic, and harm hath wrought us.

===Chinese===
从铠甲之中涌流而出！
我们四下的
墙壁都被血花溅污了！
赫尔维克真是个恶邻，给我们招来了祸殃。

===Original===
“If you would grant my wishes,
 you now will be at peace;
 Give rest to heart from anger,
 and let your fighting cease,

===Chinese===
「你若肯俯允我的心愿，
此刻就该罢战言和；
让心从愤怒中安歇下来，
且收住手，莫再厮杀，

===Original===
 Till I can ask of Herwic,
 and he to us be telling,
 About his land and kingdom,
 and where his nearest kinsmen have their dwelling.”

===Chinese===
等我好去问一问赫尔维克，
让他对我们讲讲
他的国土与王国，
还有他至近的亲族住在何方。」

===Original===
Then said the proud young Herwic:
 “Not yet may peace begin,
 Unless without my weapons
 I your love may win.

===Chinese===
于是高傲的年轻赫尔维克说道：
「此刻还谈不上言和，
除非我不凭兵刃
也能赢得你的爱。

===Original===
 If rest a while be granted,
 the knowledge you are seeking
 I then will give you freely,
 and of my kinsmen will to you be speaking.”

===Chinese===
若蒙暂且休战，
你们想要知晓的事，
我便甘愿相告，
也会向你们说起我的亲族。」

===Original===
Now, for love of the maiden,
 the strife did they forego.
 Then shook they off their armor,
 each battle-weary foe,

===Chinese===
为了对少女的爱，
两人便就此罢斗。
他们卸下甲胄，
这两个久战疲惫的仇敌，

===Original===
 And bathed in running waters,
 from rusty stains to free them.
 They soon were cheered and rested,
 and none could grudge in happy mood to see them.

===Chinese===
又在流动的活水里沐浴，
洗去锈渍斑斑。
他们很快便重振精神、得享安歇，
人人见了都心怀欣悦，没有谁还存怨怼。

===Original===
A hundred knights with Herwic
 went from the field to find
 Gudrun, the Hegeling maiden,
 still wavering in her mind.

===Chinese===
百名骑士随同赫尔维克
离了战场去寻访
黑格林少女古德伦，
她心中尚在踌躇不定。

===Original===
 She, with other ladies,
 gave him welcome kindly;
 But the worthy, high-born Herwic
 hardly dared to think their wishes friendly.

===Chinese===
她同其余的贵妇们
亲切地迎接了他；
但高贵出身的赫尔维克
几乎不敢把她们的善意信以为真。

===Original===
The fair and comely maiden
 showed the guests their seats;
 The bravery of Herwic
 erelong with love she meets:

===Chinese===
那美丽娟好的少女
为宾客们安排座席；
赫尔维克的英勇，
不久便赢得了她的爱意：

===Original===
 His high and noble breeding
 earned him kindest greeting.
 ’Twas thought Gudrun and Hilda
 should grant his suit, without a longer waiting.

===Chinese===
他高贵的教养
为他博得最亲切的礼遇。
众人都认为古德伦与希尔达
该即刻允婚，不必再作迁延。

===Original===
To the ladies then spake Herwic:
 “I oft have heard it said
 That you of me speak lightly,
 and think me lowly bred:

===Chinese===
赫尔维克于是对贵妇们说：
「我时常听人讲起，
你们把我谈论得轻贱，
当我出身寒微：

===Original===
 Your scorn may bring you sorrow,
 after all my striving;
 The rich may from the poorest
 a blessing gain, the while with them they’re living.”

===Chinese===
在我这番苦斗之后，
你们的轻蔑或许会带来悲伤；
纵然富贵，也能从最贫贱的人
得着福分，只要与之共同生活。」

===Original===
She said: “Where is the maiden
 who could behold with scorn
 A knight who strove so bravely,
 or from his love could turn?

===Chinese===
她答道：「哪里有哪位少女
会带着轻蔑去看
一位如此奋勇力争的骑士，
或是背弃他的爱情？

===Original===
 Believe me,” said the maiden,
 “I do not hold you lightly;
 Never maid more kindly
 has looked on you, or prized your worth more rightly.

===Chinese===
请相信我，」少女说道，
「我并非看轻了你；
世上再没有哪位少女
比我更温情地看你、更真切地珍重你的价值。

===Original===
“If now my friends and kindred
 leave for this will give,
 Even as you wish it,
 with you I will gladly live.”

===Chinese===
「倘若我的朋友与亲族
肯在这件事上应允，
正如你所心愿的那样，
我便甘愿与你相守度日。」

===Original===
 Then with fondest glances
 he her eye was seeking:
 In her heart she bore him,
 and owned the truth to all, no falsehood speaking.

===Chinese===
于是他怀着最深情的目光
去寻觅她的眼睛：
她心中早已有了他，
便向众人坦承实情，没有半句虚言。

===Original===
The brave and happy Herwic
 begged that he might dare
 To woo the fair young maiden.
 Now to grant his prayer

===Chinese===
勇敢而欢喜的赫尔维克
恳求让他得以
向这位年轻貌美的少女求婚。
此刻要应允他的祈请，

===Original===
 Were Hettel and Hilda ready;
 but first must they be knowing
 Whether Gudrun, their daughter,
 was glad or sorry for the kingly Herwic’s wooing.

===Chinese===
黑特尔与希尔达都已情愿；
但他们先要知道
女儿古德伦
对君王赫尔维克的求亲是喜还是忧。

===Original===
Herwic was quick in learning
 how kindly was her mood:
 And now the brave young warrior
 before the maiden stood,

===Chinese===
赫尔维克很快便探知
她的心意何等温存：
于是这位年轻勇武的战士
来到少女跟前站定，

===Original===
 In shape as fair and comely
 as if the hand of a master
 On a white wall had drawn him:
 while there he stood her love but grew the faster.

===Chinese===
身姿俊美娟秀，
仿佛丹青妙手
在白壁上把他描画出来：
他立在那里，她的爱意愈发滋长难抑。

===Original===
“If you your love will give me,”
 he said, “most lovely maid,
 Then shall my truest worship
 to you be ever paid;

===Chinese===
「你若肯把爱赐给我，」
他说，「最可爱的少女，
我最诚挚的敬慕
将永远向你奉献；

===Original===
 Throughout my lands and castles
 to you there shall be given
 My kinsmen’s faithful service,
 and ne’er shall I repent that thus I’ve striven.”

===Chinese===
在我全部的国土与城堡之中，
都将赐予你
我亲族的忠诚效力，
我也永不懊悔自己曾这般力争。」

===Original===
She said: “I give you freely
 the love for which you pray;
 By all your toils and daring
 you well have earned to-day

===Chinese===
她说道：「我甘愿把你
祈求的爱赐给你；
凭你这一切的劳苦与英勇，
你今日已然赢得——

===Original===
 That you and all my kindred
 foes shall be no longer.
 Now none can make me sorrow,
 and every day our bliss shall grow the stronger.”

===Chinese===
你与我的全体亲族
从此不再是仇敌。
如今再没有人能令我悲伤，
我们的幸福将一日强似一日。」

===Original===
Then they sent for Hettel:
 thus ended was the fight.
 Soon came he to his daughter;
 and many a faithful knight

===Chinese===
随后他们请来黑特尔：
这场战斗就此终结。
他很快来到女儿跟前；
多少忠诚的骑士

===Original===
 Followed the king, their master,
 who unto him had ridden
 From all the Hegeling kingdom.
 Thus to the strife a long farewell was bidden.

===Chinese===
追随着他们的主上国王，
从黑格林王国的
四面八方驰马赶来。
就此向争战长久地道了别。

===Original===
Now when Hettel’s kinsmen
 their wish for this did speak,
 Then asked he of his daughter
 if she would gladly take

===Chinese===
待黑特尔的亲族们
说出了这桩心愿，
他便问女儿
是否甘愿接受

===Original===
 Herwic, the knight so noble,
 who in his heart had set her.
 Then said the lovely maiden:
 “There’s not another I could love the better.”

===Chinese===
那位高贵的骑士赫尔维克——
他已把她放在心上。
可爱的少女便答道：
「再没有别人能让我爱得更深。」

===Original===
They then betrothed the maiden
 at once to the knightly king,
 Who in his land would crown her.
 This did gladness bring

===Chinese===
众人当即把少女
许配给这位骑士君王，
他要在自己的国土上为她加冕。
这件事带来了欢喜，

===Original===
 To him, and sorrow likewise:
 ere many years were ended,
 And she to him was wedded,
 good knights in stormy fight their lives defended.

===Chinese===
也给他带来了悲伤：
还没有过完多少个年头、
待她与他完婚之前，
忠勇的骑士们已在鏖战中拼死卫护。

===Original===
To take the maiden with him
 Herwic now was fain;
 But this her mother grudged him:
 thereby much woe and pain

===Chinese===
赫尔维克此刻一心盼着
携少女同归；
她母亲却以此相拒：
由此许多悲伤与苦痛

===Original===
 Came upon him later
 from foes as yet unheeded.
 The king was told by Hilda
 that longer time ere she be crowned was needed.

===Chinese===
日后落到他的身上，
出自那时还未被放在心上的仇敌。
希尔达告诉国王：
她加冕之前还须更长的时日。

===Original===
They thought it best for Herwic
 to leave the maiden there,
 While he with other women
 might pass the time elsewhere,

===Chinese===
众人认为对赫尔维克最妥当的，
是把少女留在原处，
他则可与其他女子
在他乡消磨时光，

===Original===
 And wait to wed the lady
 until a year were ended.
 This learned the men of Alzabie:
 to wait so long for her young Herwic ill befriended.

===Chinese===
静候一年期满，
再与这位贵女成婚。
这消息被阿尔扎比（Alzabie）之人探得：
叫年轻的赫尔维克苦候这么久，实在有亏于他。

## Tale XIII How Siegfried Made War Against Herwic / 第十三诗章 西格弗里德如何向赫尔维克兴兵

===Original===
Siegfried, a rival suitor for Gudrun, invades the land of Herwic, who sends to Hettel for his assistance: Hettel with many of his vassals comes to his help. Siegfried is driven into a castle, and there besieged.

===Chinese===
西格弗里德（Siegfried）作为古德伦（Gudrun）的另一位求婚者，兴兵侵入赫尔维克（Herwic）的国土；赫尔维克遣使向黑特尔（Hettel）求援，黑特尔偕众多封臣前来相助。西格弗里德被逼入一座城堡，在那里遭到围困。

===Original===
Siegfried, king of Moorland,
 called for all his men;
 Ships were soon made ready,
 wherever they were seen;

===Chinese===
摩尔兰（Moorland）国王西格弗里德，
传令召集他全部的人马；
船舶很快就整治齐备，
放眼所至处处可见；

===Original===
 Then with food and weapons
 to load them it was bidden,
 For war against King Herwic:
 from all but faithful friends his thoughts were hidden.

===Chinese===
随后又吩咐下来，
以粮草兵械把船装满，
为的是向赫尔维克国王开战：
除忠实友人之外，他的图谋瞒住了所有人。

===Original===
A score of wide, strong barges
 bade he to be made.
 I ween they liked it little
 to whom the king now said

===Chinese===
他吩咐打造
二十艘宽阔坚固的战船。
我料想国王此刻点名的人，
心里多半不太乐意，

===Original===
 That forthwith unto Sealand
 to fight must they be faring;
 And he would thither hasten
 as soon as, winter o’er, springtide was nearing.

===Chinese===
他们须即刻开赴
西兰（Sealand）投入战斗；
而他自己也要尽快赶去，
只等冬尽春回、春潮将临。

===Original===
Eighty thousand warriors
 soon to him had come;
 Of fighting men in Alzabie
 none were left at home.

===Chinese===
八万名武士
不久便聚到他麾下；
阿尔扎比（Alzabie）的战士，
没有一个留在家中。

===Original===
 Then swore the Moorland princes
 for war to make them ready;
 Some of these still lingered,
 others to follow with the king were speedy.

===Chinese===
于是摩尔兰的众王侯
宣誓备战；
其中一些尚自逗留，
另一些已火速随王启程。

===Original===
Then against the Sealands
 the threat of war he made.
 This roused the wrath of Herwic,
 who well might him upbraid;

===Chinese===
随后他对西兰人
发出了战争的威胁。
这激起了赫尔维克的怒火，
他满有理由痛责此人：

===Original===
 To earn the hate of Siegfried
 wrong had he done him never.
 His marches and his castles
 he bade his men to guard, now more than ever.

===Chinese===
要说招西格弗里德的怨恨，
他可从不曾亏负过他。
他命部下守卫
自己的边疆与城堡，比往日更甚。

===Original===
Then he said in sorrow
 to friends who came in haste
 That foes would burn his castles,
 and his lands lay waste:

===Chinese===
他忧容满面地
对匆匆赶来的友人们说，
仇敌要烧他的城堡，
使他的国土化作荒芜：

===Original===
 All he could give his liegemen,
 that he held but lightly.
 They took their wages gladly;
 that war would bring them riches, hoped they rightly.

===Chinese===
凡是他能赏给封臣的东西，
他都看得甚轻。
部众们欢欢喜喜领了饷银；
他们指望战争带来财货，这指望确有道理。

===Original===
About the gladsome May-time,
 there went across the sea
 Warriors out of Alzabie,
 and eke from Abakie.

===Chinese===
正当欢乐的五月时节，
有一支人马渡海而来，
战士们出自阿尔扎比，
也有阿巴基（Abakie）的儿郎。

===Original===
 Onward came they proudly,
 as though the world’s end seeking;
 Many now trod boldly
 who in the dust their rest would soon be taking.

===Chinese===
他们一路昂然挺进，
仿佛要去寻访世界的尽头；
多少人此刻迈步豪雄，
不久便将在尘土中长眠不起。

===Original===
Into the land of Herwic
 they cast the burning brand.
 Then all whom he could gather,
 and all his friends at hand,

===Chinese===
他们把熊熊的火把
掷进赫尔维克的国土。
于是凡他所能纠集的人马，
连同在侧的所有友人，

===Original===
 Rode to the field with Herwic.
 Thro’ war-storms grimly driven,
 They with their lives must bargain
 for gold and gems and silver to them given.

===Chinese===
都随赫尔维克驰赴战场。
他们在酷烈的战暴中被驱迫，
只得拿性命作抵押，
去换取发到手中的黄金、宝石与白银。

===Original===
To him, the king of Sealand,
 great ill erelong was wrought.
 A stalwart foeman was he:
 Aha, how well he fought!

===Chinese===
这位西兰之王，
不久便遭了大难。
他本是剽悍的对手：
啊哈，他打得多漂亮！

===Original===
 He made the land the richer
 with the dead there lying:
 The old in fight grew youthful:
 the strong were slain, who recked not yet of dying.

===Chinese===
他以遍地的陈尸
使那方土地更见丰饶：
老者在酣战中焕发青春；
强者被戮，尚不把死亡放在心上。

===Original===
Long the fighting lasted,
 till thickly lay the dead:
 Then to the brave King Herwic
 came at last the need

===Chinese===
战斗持续了很久，
直到死者枕藉遍地：
这时英勇的赫尔维克国王
终于面临那样的急需——

===Original===
 To flee into his marches,
 for life he there was turning;
 All his lands lay smoking:
 of this to Gudrun, his lady, sent he warning.

===Chinese===
退避到自己的边疆深处，
好在那里保全性命；
他全部的国土都在冒烟：
他为此给贵女古德伦送去了警讯。

===Original===
Now to the land of Hettel
 men at his bidding went:
 Many tears and bitter
 they shed when they were sent

===Chinese===
于是奉他之命，
有人前往黑特尔的国土：
临行时他们洒下了
多少辛酸的眼泪，

===Original===
 To find the great King Hettel,
 and the tale to him to carry.
 They were not long in showing
 unto the king their plight so hard and dreary.

===Chinese===
去寻尊大的黑特尔国王，
把消息传到他耳中。
他们没用多久便向国王
诉尽了处境的凄苦与艰辛。

===Original===
Though sad in mood he found them,
 a welcome kind he gave,
 Such as far-off wanderers
 and homeless friends should have.

===Chinese===
黑特尔见他们满怀愁绪，
仍给了他们亲切的接待，
正如远来的游子
与无家可归的友人所应得的。

===Original===
 He asked if from their homesteads
 they were hither driven,
 When foes their lands had wasted,
 and all their marches had to flames been given.

===Chinese===
他问他们可是
被人从家园赶到此地，
仇敌糟践了他们的土地，
把他们的边疆尽数付之一炬。

===Original===
Then to him they answered:
 “In sorrow did we leave:
 The faithful men of Herwic,
 from early morn till eve,

===Chinese===
于是他们回答他：
「我们是含着悲伤离乡的：
赫尔维克忠勇的部众，
从清晨直到黄昏，

===Original===
 Sell their lives full dearly,
 and well his gifts are earning;
 They fight for name and honor:
 for this at home are many women mourning.”

===Chinese===
都在把性命高价抛售，
他的犒赏他们受之无愧；
他们为名声与荣誉而战：
为此家乡有多少妇人正在哀恸。」

===Original===
Then to them said Hettel:
 “To my daughter make it known;
 Whatever she shall wish for
 at once shall that be done.

===Chinese===
于是黑特尔对他们说：
「去把这话说给我女儿听；
凡她心中所愿的，
立刻便照办不误。

===Original===
 If she for vengeance calleth
 for the wrongs he wrought you,
 We then will help you gladly,
 and pay him back the ill that he has brought you.”

===Chinese===
她若为你们所受的荼毒
呼唤复仇，
我们便甘愿出力相助，
把他加给你们的祸害如数奉还。」

===Original===
Before they yet had spoken
 unto the fair young maid,
 Already of her sorrow
 her friends had taken heed.

===Chinese===
他们还未及向那
美丽年轻的少女开口，
她的忧伤，
友们早已看在眼里。

===Original===
 The lady had been longing
 to see the heralds hourly;
 Them in haste she sent for,
 the loss of land and honor, mourning sorely.

===Chinese===
这位贵女时时刻刻
盼着见到那些信使；
她急忙派人去请，
为国土与荣誉的沦丧悲恸不已。

===Original===
When they came before her,
 they found the queenly maid
 Sitting sad, and weeping﻿—
 faithful love she had;

===Chinese===
当他们来到她跟前，
只见那王后般的少女
黯然坐着，垂泪——
她怀着一片忠贞的爱；

===Original===
 She asked them of her lover,
 and how they leave had taken,
 And if he still was living
 when they of late had land and home forsaken.

===Chinese===
她向他们问起她的爱人，
问他们如何向他辞行，
问他们不久前抛下
土地与家园时，他是否还活在人间。

===Original===
Then answered one among them:
 “We left him sound and well;
 But since the day we saw him
 we know not what befell,

===Chinese===
他们中间一人答道：
「我们离开他时他安然无恙；
可自我们见他那日起，
便不知他遭了什么变故，

===Original===
 Or how the men of Moorland
 may his home have wasted:
 Mischief they had done him,
 neither from fire and plunder had they rested.

===Chinese===
也不知摩尔兰的人马
可曾蹂躏他的家园：
他们已对他下了毒手，
纵火劫掠，不曾有片刻歇手。

===Original===
“Listen, high-born maiden!
 my master’s bidding heed:
 He and all his warriors
 are now in sorest need.

===Chinese===
「请听，高贵的少女！
要体察我主上的心意：
他和他全体的武士
此刻已陷入极度的危境。

===Original===
 To lose both life and honor
 they are fearing daily;
 And now my lord, King Herwic,
 sends to beg your men to his help to rally.”

===Chinese===
他们日日担心
性命与荣誉双双不保；
如今我的主上赫尔维克国王，
恳求贵方人马前去驰援。」

===Original===
Gudrun, the lovely maiden,
 then from her seat upstood;
 The wrongs that had been done her
 she to her father showed:

===Chinese===
可爱的少女古德伦
随即从座上起身；
把降临到自己身上的横祸
一一呈给父亲看：

===Original===
 She said her men were slaughtered,
 and her castles wasted,
 And told her father, Hettel,
 that to ride to Herwic’s help she would he had hasted.

===Chinese===
她说她的部众遭屠戮，
她的城堡被夷为废墟，
又恳请父亲黑特尔
即刻发兵驰援赫尔维克。

===Original===
Then in her arms she pressed him,
 her eyes with weeping wet:
 “Help, O dearest father!
 My woes are all too great,

===Chinese===
接着她把父亲搂在怀里，
双眼已被泪水浸湿：
「救命啊，最亲爱的父亲！
我的苦难实在深重，

===Original===
 Unless your many liegemen,
 with ready hand, are willing
 To help my good friend Herwic:
 none else can end the strife, my sorrow healing.”

===Chinese===
除非您那众多的封臣
肯慨然出手，
来救助我的好友赫尔维克：
否则再无人能平息这场争战、医好我的悲伤。」

===Original===
“That will I leave to no one,”
 the king did freely say;
 “I will haste to help King Herwic,
 and wait not many a day.

===Chinese===
「这事我不推给旁人，」
国王慨然说道；
「我要尽快去救助赫尔维克国王，
一天也不多等。

===Original===
 As well as I am able,
 I will end your sorrow:
 I will call for the aged Wâ-te
 and many other friends, before the morrow.

===Chinese===
我要竭尽所能，
终结你的悲伤：
天明之前，我就去请年迈的瓦特（Wâ-te）
与许多别的友人。

===Original===
“He will bring from Sturmland
 all the men of his lands;
 And when ’tis known by Morunc
 how ill with us it stands,

===Chinese===
「他会从施图尔姆兰（Sturmland）
带来他全境的人马；
待莫伦克（Morunc）得知
我们的处境何等艰险，

===Original===
 Fighters full a thousand
 to bring will he be speedy.
 Our foes shall find out quickly,
 that under helmets we to march are ready.

===Chinese===
他也会火速
带来足足一千名战士。
我们的仇敌很快便会发现：
我们已顶盔贯甲、整装待发。

===Original===
“Horant, too, from Denmark
 shall bring upon the way
 Of men full thrice a thousand:
 nor will Irold stay;

===Chinese===
「霍兰特（Horant）也会从丹麦
一路带来
足足三千名人马；
伊罗尔德（Irold）也不会滞留；

===Original===
 But he will raise his banner,
 and hasten to the slaughter.
 Then, too, thy brother Ortwin
 will come, and all will earn the blessing of my daughter.”

===Chinese===
他要高擎自己的大旗，
疾驰赶赴这场杀伐。
还有你的兄弟奥特温（Ortwin）
也将前来——众人都要赢得我女儿的祝福。」

===Original===
The heralds soon went riding
 whom the maid did send.
 Her friends far off were living,
 but all who help would lend

===Chinese===
少女派遣的信使们
不久便策马登程。
她的友人们住得遥远，
但凡愿出力相助者，

===Original===
 To heal the maiden’s sorrow
 would honor great be earning;
 Knights would she warmly welcome:
 for this erelong the more to her were turning.

===Chinese===
只要能医好少女的悲伤，
都将赢得莫大的荣誉；
骑士们将受她热忱欢迎：
为此不久人们愈发归心于她。

===Original===
Hilda, the maiden’s mother,
 unto her daughter spake:
 “Whoe’er is quick to help you,
 and now his shield shall take

===Chinese===
少女的母亲希尔达（Hilda）
对女儿说道：
「凡乐于帮你、
此刻便要拿起盾牌

===Original===
 To follow with your warriors
 when they to war are faring,
 Whate’er we gain by fighting
 he shall, in truth, henceforth with us be sharing.”

===Chinese===
随你的人马一同
出征作战的人，
无论我们凭战斗赢得什么，
今后都必与我们共享。」

===Original===
Then the chests were opened;
 men to court soon bore
 Whate’er therein was lying,
 of fighting-gear a store,

===Chinese===
于是箱柜尽数打开；
人们把里面的存物
很快搬进宫来，
那是满满一批战具，

===Original===
 Fast with steel well studded;
 then the knights were laden
 With armor white as silver:
 this made glad the heart of the queenly maiden.

===Chinese===
件件牢牢镶着钢钉；
随后骑士们披挂上
白得如银的甲胄：
这让王后般少女的心欢悦不已。

===Original===
To full a thousand warriors
 were given clothes and steeds;
 Out of stalls men brought them,
 as oft the horse one leads,

===Chinese===
整整一千名武士
领得了衣装与战马；
人们把马从厩中牵出，
一如平常牵马那样，

===Original===
 When, along the highways,
 men to the fight go riding.
 Of all the king’s good horses
 they left but very few at rest abiding.

===Chinese===
当将士们沿着大道
策马奔赴战场之时。
国王的全部良驹，
几乎没剩几匹安卧厩中。

===Original===
When from his queenly lady
 the king his leave did take,
 Both Hilda and her daughter
 began to weep for his sake;

===Chinese===
当国王向他王后般的夫人
辞行的时候，
希尔达和她的女儿
都为他落起泪来；

===Original===
 But on the knights forth riding
 gladly they were gazing,
 And said: “May God in heaven
 so help the fight that men may you be praising.”

===Chinese===
但她们仍欣然
目送骑士们驰骋而去，
说道：「愿天上的上帝
如此扶助这场战斗，教人们称颂你们。」

===Original===
After they all were gathered
 without the castle gate,
 Youths were there heard singing,
 hoping for plunder great.

===Chinese===
待众人齐聚
城堡大门之外，
那里响起了少年们的歌声，
他们盼着大发横财。

===Original===
 Each thought, by hardest fighting,
 to win himself much riches;
 But far must they yet be riding,
 for long the way to their master’s foemen stretches.

===Chinese===
人人都在想，凭一番死斗，
为自己赢得大量财货；
只是他们还须赶远路，
主上的仇敌相距迢迢。

===Original===
On the third morning early
 came, at break of day,
 The very aged Wâ-te
 with a thousand to the fray;

===Chinese===
第三天一大早，
天刚破晓，
年迈已极的瓦特
便率一千人马赶来赴战；

===Original===
 And from the Danish kingdom,
 as the seventh day was dawning,
 Came Horant with four thousand,
 to whom the fair Gudrun had sent her warning.

===Chinese===
到第七日破晓时分，
霍兰特又从丹麦王国
带来四千人马——
可爱的古德伦早已向他们传过警讯。

===Original===
From out the Waalisch marches
 Morunc thither rode;
 He ever fought for the ladies,
 for the love to them he owed.

===Chinese===
莫伦克也从瓦尔河（Waalisch）边的疆土
策马赶到；
他素来为贵妇们而战，
为的是报答他对她们的敬爱。

===Original===
 Twenty thousand warriors
 he brought﻿—for nought he tarried:
 These were all well-weaponed,
 and happily rode, while help to the king they carried.

===Chinese===
他带来两万名武士——
一路不曾稍有耽搁：
这些人个个兵器齐整，
欢然策马，把援手送到王上那里。

===Original===
The queenly maiden’s brother,
 Ortwin, the youthful knight,
 Brought across the water,
 to help her in the fight,

===Chinese===
王后般少女的兄弟、
年轻的骑士奥特温，
也渡海而来，
为在战斗中援助她

===Original===
 Forty hundred warriors,
 or even a number greater:
 Were it known to the men of Alzabie,
 well might they have feared to meet him later.

===Chinese===
带来了四千名武士，
说不定数目还不止于此：
这事若让阿尔扎比的人知晓，
日后再遇上他，怕不胆寒。

===Original===
Before they yet could help him,
 to Herwic and his men
 The strife had now gone badly,
 his luck began to wane:

===Chinese===
援军还未来得及赶到，
赫尔维克和他的部众
这边战事已然不利，
他的运道开始衰微：

===Original===
 To him and all his followers
 was evil sore betiding;
 Altho’ they struggled bravely,
 his foes too near his castle gate were riding.

===Chinese===
灾祸沉沉地压向
他和全体随从；
虽然他们奋勇抗争，
仇敌已逼近他城堡的门下。

===Original===
Great mishaps to Herwic
 from Siegfried’s kin arose;
 For now the gates of the castle
 were shattered by their blows.

===Chinese===
西格弗里德的亲族
给赫尔维克酿成大祸；
如今连城堡的大门
也被他们击得粉碎。

===Original===
 False friends had made it easy,
 and boasts too loudly spoken:
 If e’er to such one trusteth,
 it worketh him no good, and his hopes are broken.

===Chinese===
是虚伪的朋友成全了此事，
还有那吹得太过的大话：
谁若信靠这种人，
必无好处，指望尽成泡影。

===Original===
Now ’twas told to Herwic,
 men fast for help had gone.
 The foes from fight ne’er rested,
 by anger driven on;

===Chinese===
这时有人禀报赫尔维克，
求援的人已兼程而去。
仇敌们被怒火驱赶，
战斗片刻不停；

===Original===
 From early morn to even,
 they oft to the strife were bidden:
 But now the friends of Herwic
 on every side drew near, nor long lay hidden.

===Chinese===
从清晨到夜晚，
他们一次次被遣上战场：
可此刻赫尔维克的友军
已从四面逼近，不再久藏。

===Original===
When this the men of Karadie
 did learn, they well might fear
 That now two kings against them
 in the fight should share:

===Chinese===
卡拉迪（Karadie）的人马
闻讯，实在该心惊：
如今有两位君王
要同他们交锋：

===Original===
 For them it was unlucky
 that Hettel now was leading
 His many fighters thither;
 he from afar had come, to Herwic speeding.

===Chinese===
对他们来说真是不幸，
黑特尔此刻正统领
大队战士开来；
他自远方兼程，直趋赫尔维克。

===Original===
Friends were they to each other;
 so both would meet the foe.
 These, the men from Moorland,
 bold themselves did show:

===Chinese===
两位君王彼此交好；
便同心迎击仇敌。
这些摩尔兰来的人，
自己也好生剽悍：

===Original===
 One saw by all their bearing
 they would from none be flying;
 Those who with them struggled
 by hardest toil must their reward be buying.

===Chinese===
从他们的做派便看得出，
他们绝不临阵脱逃；
与他们角力的人，
须历经苦斗才能挣得报偿。

===Original===
Wâ-te, the very daring,
 with all his knights had come;
 Gudrun, the lovely lady,
 had called him from his home

===Chinese===
剽悍绝伦的瓦特
偕全体骑士赶到；
可爱的贵女古德伦
把他从家乡召唤而来，

===Original===
 To help her lover, Herwic,
 and a host had ridden hither:
 Whate’er might now befall them,
 later full happy rode they thence together.

===Chinese===
为的是援助她的爱人赫尔维克，
一彪人马驰到此地：
无论眼下遭遇什么，
日后他们都将欢然同归。

===Original===
Although their foes were heathen,
 from out the Moorish land,
 They might not back be driven:
 one well might understand

===Chinese===
虽说他们的仇敌是异教之众，
来自摩尔人的国土，
却休想把他们击退：
人们尽可以明白，

===Original===
 That in any earthly kingdom
 they were the best and boldest.
 To all who came to meet them
 they gave a sorry welcome and a shelter coldest.

===Chinese===
在世间任何王国里，
他们都算得上最精最强。
凡来与他们照面的人，
得到的只是可悲的接待与最冷酷的栖身之处。

===Original===
Herwic, king of Sealand,
 his loss would now make good
 Upon his foes from Alzabie.
 For this must flow the blood

===Chinese===
西兰之王赫尔维克
如今要向阿尔扎比来的仇敌
讨还自己蒙受的损失。
为此必得血流

===Original===
 On either side of many;
 to friends and kin were given
 Wounds full fast and heavy:
 to bear his own was hard for Hettel even.

===Chinese===
许多人的鲜血流在两边；
朋友与亲族们领受
又急又重的创伤：
连黑特尔也难以承受自己那一份。

===Original===
When they had come together
 of whom I spoke before,
 Bringing all their followers,
 gladness they knew no more;

===Chinese===
当我先前讲到的那些人
齐聚到一处，
带着他们所有的部众，
从此再不知欢乐为何物；

===Original===
 On them were ever resting
 heavy care and sorrow
 For what the night might bring them.
 They thought: “How shall we live to see the morrow?”

===Chinese===
沉重的忧惧与悲哀
始终压在他们心头，
不知长夜会带来什么。
他们心想：「我们怎能活着见到明天？」

===Original===
Thrice with the Moorish foemen
 they strove on the stormy field,
 While peace was given the castle,
 as knights are wont to yield.

===Chinese===
他们同摩尔人的敌军
在风暴般的战场三度鏖战，
其间城堡获赐休战，
一如骑士相争时惯有的停让。

===Original===
 Again with sword and spear-shaft
 they the strife would settle:
 Peace not yet they wished for,
 but wounds the more they got in hard-fought battle.

===Chinese===
他们又凭刀剑与矛杆
来一决这场纷争：
和平尚非他们所愿，
苦战中他们只是平添创伤。

===Original===
Nor Herwic’s men nor Siegfried’s
 yet would leave the fight;
 They to the last had struggled,
 and many a bravest knight

===Chinese===
赫尔维克的人与西格弗里德的人
都不肯离开战斗；
他们拼杀到了最后，
多少最英勇的骑士

===Original===
 Upon the field lay wounded,
 or in death was sleeping.
 This was told to the women,
 who now began a wild, unmeasured weeping.

===Chinese===
带着重伤横卧战场，
或已沉沉睡入死亡。
这消息传到妇人们耳中，
她们顿时放声恸哭，不能自抑。

===Original===
How well the daring Wâ-te
 in battle-storm did fight!
 Strong was he and skilful,
 and oft the aged knight

===Chinese===
剽悍的瓦特
在战暴中打得多出色！
他既强壮又娴熟，
这位年迈的骑士屡屡

===Original===
 Gave to the foe heart-sorrow,
 by all the ill he wrought him:
 Ever to fight with his warriors,
 by the side of the boldest and best, his wishes taught him.

===Chinese===
以他造下的种种祸害，
教仇敌痛彻心底：
他的心愿始终教他与战士们
并肩奋战于最勇最强者之侧。

===Original===
Horant, too, from Denmark,
 brave was he enough!
 Beneath his hand were shattered
 helmets strong and tough;

===Chinese===
还有从丹麦来的霍兰特，
他何其英勇！
在他手下被击碎的，
是又坚又韧的头盔；

===Original===
 Ne’er by him ’twas forgotten
 to wear his armor shining;
 Ill he wrought to many,
 and oft the ranks of his foemen he was thinning.

===Chinese===
他从不忘穿起
自己灿亮的甲胄；
他给许多人降下灾殃，
又一再削薄仇敌的行列。

===Original===
The quick and fearless Morunc
 boldly stretched his hand
 Ofttimes beyond his buckler,
 and oft the fight he gained.

===Chinese===
敏捷而无畏的莫伦克
大胆探出手臂，
屡屡伸过自己的圆盾之外，
又屡屡赢得战斗。

===Original===
 To shun the king of Moorland
 ne’er would he be seeking;
 Upon that king, so mighty,
 he the wrath of Herwic now was wreaking.

===Chinese===
要避开摩尔兰国王，
这他从不曾想过；
在那位强横的君王身上，
他此刻正发泄着赫尔维克的怒火。

===Original===
The great and doughty Hettel,
 when that his daughter fair
 Had sent to beg her father
 in Herwic’s fight to share,

===Chinese===
尊大而勇武的黑特尔，
当他的爱女
遣人恳求父亲
加入赫尔维克的战事，

===Original===
 That peace at last might follow,
 fought for him not idly:
 If life were dear to any,
 ’twere best to shun King Hettel’s borders widely.

===Chinese===
好教和平终得降临，
便为他奋战不懈：
谁若爱惜性命，
最好远远避开黑特尔国王的疆界。

===Original===
Bravely strove King Herwic
 on the field and at the gate;
 None than he fought better.
 His head was often wet,

===Chinese===
赫尔维克国王奋勇
厮杀于旷野与城门；
无人比他战得更好。
他的头颅常常湿透，

===Original===
 Beneath his armor dripping,
 with sweat that fast was oozing.
 In death were many deafened;
 they who would crush him must their lives be losing.

===Chinese===
甲胄之下涔涔滴落
急涌而出的汗水。
许多人就此坠入死寂；
要压垮他的人必得赔上性命。

===Original===
Wigaleis, the faithful,
 great ill to many wrought.
 Sir Fru-te, too, from Daneland,
 with knightly prowess fought:

===Chinese===
忠诚的维加莱斯（Wigaleis）
给许多人降下大祸；
弗鲁特（Fru-te）爵士也自丹麦兰（Daneland）而来，
凭骑士的悍勇作战：

===Original===
 The thanks of all his fellows
 he should of right be sharing;
 He strove where the fight was stormy,
 and none e’er knew an aged knight so daring.

===Chinese===
他理当分享
全体同袍的称谢；
他专向战事最烈处冲杀，
这般胆色的老骑士，世人从未见过。

===Original===
The lord who came from Ortland,
 Ortwin, brave and young,
 Showed the hand of a warrior;
 it was on many a tongue,

===Chinese===
从奥特兰（Ortland）来的那位领主——
年轻英武的奥特温——
显出了真正战士的身手；
众口都在传说，

===Original===
 That never man in warfare
 bore himself more boldly:
 Wounds he gave the deepest,
 and this by none was ever told of coldly.

===Chinese===
从没有人在战阵中
比他更英勇豪迈：
他给出的创伤最深，
这事从来无人淡然讲起。

===Original===
For twelve long days of fighting,
 earnestly they strove.
 The men led on by Hettel
 oft their spear-shafts drove

===Chinese===
整整十二天的鏖战，
他们拼杀得无比惨烈。
黑特尔统率的人马
一次次挺矛直刺，

===Original===
 Thro’ their foes’ light bucklers,
 as close they met together:
 The fighters proud from Moorland
 sorely rued the day that brought them thither.

===Chinese===
矛尖穿透仇敌轻薄的圆盾，
当两军紧紧相逼之际：
摩尔兰来的骄横战士
深深懊悔把他们带到此地的日子。

===Original===
Upon the thirteenth morning,
 ere early mass was said,
 With sorry heart spake Siegfried:
 “How many here lie dead

===Chinese===
到了第十三天清晨，
早弥撒还未诵念，
西格弗里德满怀忧伤地说道：
「这里躺倒了多少

===Original===
 Of all our bravest warriors!
 In his lofty wooing
 The king of Sealand also
 here to himself has evil great been doing.”

===Chinese===
我们最英勇的战士！
那西兰国王在他攀高的求亲里，
也在这里
给自己造下了大大的祸殃。」

===Original===
Then to the men of Karadie
 made he known his will,
 To a stronghold to betake them,
 there their wounds to heal:

===Chinese===
随后他向卡拉迪的人马
道出了自己的心意：
要退守一处要塞，
在那里将养创伤：

===Original===
 They, with those from Alzabie,
 were earnest to go thither;
 Right glad were these far-riders
 that all in death might not be found together.

===Chinese===
他们与阿尔扎比来的人
都急切地想退往那里；
这些远来的骑士好生欢喜：
总算不至于全数死在一处。

===Original===
Then to a sheltering castle
 to turn they all began,
 Where onward, fast beside it,
 a wide, deep river ran.

===Chinese===
于是众人开始
折向一座可以栖身的城堡，
就在它的近旁，
奔涌着一条宽阔幽深的大河。

===Original===
 While they were thither riding,
 fleeing away from danger,
 They were still seen fighting
 with those who ne’er would yield their homes to a stranger.

===Chinese===
在避危逃奔、
驰向那里的途中，
他们仍在与追兵交战——
那些人绝不容外乡人占去自己的家园。

===Original===
Now against King Hettel
 the king of Moorland rode:
 Well might one believe it,
 his former warlike mood

===Chinese===
这时摩尔兰国王
纵马直取黑特尔：
人们尽可以相信，
他先前的战意

===Original===
 Was but a slight beginning;
 he soon a foe was meeting
 Who many of his kinsmen
 with deep and deadly wounds of late was greeting.

===Chinese===
不过是小小的开端；
他随即迎面遇上一个对手，
那人新近曾把深而致命的创伤
送给他许多亲族。

===Original===
Hettel, he of the Hegelings,
 and Siegfried, the Moorland king,
 There unto the struggle
 all their strength did bring;

===Chinese===
黑格林人（Hegeling）的黑特尔
与摩尔兰王西格弗里德，
在那里把全身的力气
都投入了这场搏斗；

===Original===
 Shields were hacked to pieces
 by the swords they wielded:
 The mighty lord of Moorland
 to the castle fled, nor to him of Daneland yielded.

===Chinese===
他们挥剑把盾牌
砍得粉碎：
摩尔兰强横的君王
逃进了城堡，也不向丹麦兰之主屈服。

===Original===
Camps by the men from Denmark
 for themselves were made:
 Then the beleaguered warriors﻿—
 it cannot be gainsaid﻿—

===Chinese===
丹麦来的人马
为自己扎下营盘：
这时被围的战士——
这是无可否认的——

===Original===
 E’er many days were over,
 with care were burdened sadly;
 However good their shelter,
 all would then have been at home more gladly.

===Chinese===
没过多少天，
便被忧愁压得神情惨淡；
无论庇身之处多么安稳，
众人还是宁愿回到家中。

===Original===
Thus the boastful fighters
 were by the foeman’s hand
 Fast held within the stronghold;
 nor was their knightly band

===Chinese===
就这样，那些夸口的战士
被仇敌之手
牢牢困在要塞之中；
他们那支骑士的队伍

===Original===
 Now able to give battle,
 although for this yet longing.
 Their castle well they guarded,
 as best they might, wherein they now were thronging.

===Chinese===
再也无法出城接战，
尽管心犹未甘。
他们竭尽所能守卫城堡，
众人此刻正麇集于其内。

## Tale XIV How Hettel Sent Tidings from Herwic’s Land / 第十四诗章 黑特尔如何从赫尔维克的国土传来音讯

===Original===
Hettel sends to announce to Hilda, his wife, and to Gudrun, his daughter, his success in the fight with Siegfried. Hartmut, the Norman, takes advantage of the absence of both Hettel and Herwic, and invades the Hegeling kingdom.

===Chinese===
黑特尔（Hettel）派人向妻子希尔达（Hilda）与女儿古德伦（Gudrun）传告，他已在与西格弗里德（Siegfried）的战斗中取胜。诺曼底（Normandy）人哈特穆特（Hartmut）趁黑特尔与赫尔维克（Herwic）双双外出之机，入侵黑格林人（Hegeling）的王国。

===Original===
Hettel then sent tidings,
 to still their fears at home.
 To the fair and high-born ladies
 men with news did come,

===Chinese===
黑特尔于是遣人传送音讯，
以平家中的忧惧。
带着消息的人们，
来到了美丽高贵的贵妇们跟前，

===Original===
 That unto the old and youthful,
 throughout the stormy fighting,
 Good luck had aye befallen;
 and now, with hope must they for them be waiting.

===Chinese===
消息说，老老少少
在这场风暴般的战斗中，
好运始终相伴；
如今她们须怀着希望，等候他们归来。

===Original===
He bade his men to tell them
 how Siegfried was besieged,
 While he with all his followers
 war against him waged,

===Chinese===
他吩咐部众告知她们，
西格弗里德如何被围困，
他又如何亲率全体部属，
与之兵戎相见，

===Original===
 To help the lord of Sealand,
 loved by Gudrun, his daughter;
 That all, as they were able,
 daily fought for her, and for him who sought her.

===Chinese===
去援助西兰（Sealand）之主——
古德伦、他女儿的心上人；
还说众人各尽所能，
日日为她、也为求娶她的他而战。

===Original===
Hettel’s queen, fair Hilda,
 the hope began to have
 That luck would follow Herwic
 and all his warriors brave;

===Chinese===
黑特尔的王后、美丽的希尔达
心中开始生出指望：
好运将会眷顾赫尔维克
和他全体勇敢的武士；

===Original===
 And, as their worth befitted,
 all might well be speeding.
 Then said Gudrun: “God grant it,
 that they our friends may back in health be leading.”

===Chinese===
而凭着他们相称的德行，
诸事也定会顺遂。
古德伦便说道：「愿上帝垂允，
让他们引领我们的亲朋康健归来。」

===Original===
By Wâ-te’s men from Sturmland,
 the foes from Alzabie
 And all who came from Moorland
 were kept away from the sea;

===Chinese===
借着瓦特（Wâ-te）麾下来自施图尔姆兰（Sturmland）的人马，
阿尔扎比（Alzabie）来的仇敌，
连同所有来自摩尔兰（Moorland）的人，
都被隔绝在海岸之外；

===Original===
 Sadly must they tarry
 within the sheltering castle:
 In Wâ-te and in Fru-te
 foes they had with whom they ill could wrestle.

===Chinese===
他们只得凄凄然困守
在那栖身的城堡里：
瓦特与弗鲁特（Fru-te）
正是他们难以角力的对手。

===Original===
Loudly swore King Hettel
 the castle ne’er to leave;
 That he and all his followers
 still to the end would strive,

===Chinese===
黑特尔国王朗声起誓，
绝不撤离那座城堡；
他必与全体部属
奋战到最后一刻，

===Original===
 Till those to him had yielded
 who now the Moor befriended.
 Unwise had been their inroad,
 and this for them one day in sorrow ended.

===Chinese===
直到如今与那摩尔人为伍的人
尽数向他屈服。
他们此番犯境本不智，
终有一日要以悲伤收场。

===Original===
Meanwhile the spies of Hartmut,
 whom he had thither sent,
 Though little good they looked for,
 from the Norman border went;

===Chinese===
与此同时，哈特穆特
先前派往那边的探子，
虽不指望探得什么好处，
也从诺曼底的边界出发了；

===Original===
 Ever to learn what happened
 they a watch were keeping,
 And from the stormy warfare
 they hoped that Hettel might no gain be reaping.

===Chinese===
他们时刻留意守望，
只求探知那边的事态，
并指望黑特尔
在这场风暴战事中一无所获。

===Original===
Now they saw that Siegfried,
 the Moorland king high-born,
 Was kept within the castle,
 besieged both eve and morn;

===Chinese===
此刻他们看见，出身尊贵的
摩尔兰之王西格弗里德
被困在城堡之中，
朝朝暮暮都遭围困；

===Original===
 Thence could he sally never,
 and this he knew with sorrow;
 His lands so far were lying,
 he little help from them could hope to borrow.

===Chinese===
他从此再难突围而出，
深知此点，心中悲苦；
他的国土又远在天边，
指望不上那里的多少援手。

===Original===
The Norman errand-bearers,
 sent forth their watch to make
 By Ludwig and young Hartmut,
 to them now hastened back:

===Chinese===
路德维希（Ludwig）与年轻的哈特穆特
先前派去守望的
那些诺曼底使者，
此刻匆匆返回他们身边：

===Original===
 The happy news they carried,
 and soon at home were giving,
 That Hettel, the king, and Herwic
 were busy now, in warfare ever striving.

===Chinese===
他们带回喜讯，
很快便在家中禀报：
黑特尔国王与赫尔维克
正陷于战事，无休无止地相争。

===Original===
To them the lord of Normandy
 thanks for the tidings gave,
 And asked them: “Can you tell us
 how long those foemen brave,

===Chinese===
诺曼底之主
谢过他们带来的音讯，
又问他们：「你们可否告知，
那些勇敢的仇敌

===Original===
 The men from the land of Karadie,
 will in Sealand tarry,
 Fighting ’gainst its warriors,
 till they, their wrongs avenged, of war are weary?”

===Chinese===
——卡拉迪（Karadie）之地来的人——
还将在西兰滞留多久，
同那方的武士厮杀，
直到冤仇得报、对战争厌倦为止？」

===Original===
One of them made answer:
 “The truth you now may hear:
 There they yet must linger
 more than another year.

===Chinese===
其中一人答道：
「实情你们如今便可听闻：
他们还要在那里滞留，
不止一个年头。

===Original===
 Never from their stronghold
 will the Hegelings free them;
 They there so well are guarded,
 that on their homeward way none e’er shall see them.”

===Chinese===
黑格林人绝不会
把他们从那堡垒中放走；
他们被看守得那样严紧，
归途上永远无人能再见到他们。」

===Original===
Then the knight of Normandy,
 the daring Hartmut, spake:
 “This frees my heart from sorrow,
 and hope in me doth wake!

===Chinese===
于是诺曼底的骑士、
胆气逼人的哈特穆特说道：
「这话解了我心头的愁苦，
唤起了我胸中的希望！

===Original===
 If they are now beleaguered,
 then are we well befriended;
 We must to Hegeling hasten,
 ere Hettel’s fight with Siegfried shall be ended.”

===Chinese===
他们既已被围困，
我们便有了得力的臂助；
我们必须急赴黑格林人的地界，
趁黑特尔与西格弗里德之战尚未了结。」

===Original===
Ludwig and young Hartmut
 had both the selfsame mind﻿—
 Had they ten thousand fighters
 whom they at once could find,

===Chinese===
路德维希与年轻的哈特穆特
抱着同一个心思﻿——
他们若有万名武士
能即刻召集到手，

===Original===
 Gudrun they might lay hold on,
 and to their home might carry,
 Before her father, Hettel,
 came back again from the land where he did tarry.

===Chinese===
便能将古德伦掳到手，
载回自己的家园，
赶在她父亲黑特尔
从滞留之地归来之前。

===Original===
Hartmut’s mother, Gerlind,
 earnestly gave thought
 To wreak her wrath on Hettel,
 that he to harm be brought,

===Chinese===
哈特穆特之母格尔林德（Gerlind）
处心积虑，
一心要向黑特尔泄愤，
叫他遭殃罹祸，

===Original===
 Because her dear son Hartmut
 he shamefully had slighted.
 She wished the aged Wâ-te
 and Fru-te might be hanged, for the help they plighted.

===Chinese===
只因他可耻地慢待了
她亲爱的儿子哈特穆特。
她但愿年迈的瓦特
连同弗鲁特都被绞死，只因他们倾力相助。

===Original===
Then spake the old she-devil:
 “Good knights, your hire behold!
 If you will now ride thither,
 my silver and my gold,

===Chinese===
随后那老女魔开口道：
「好骑士们，看你们的酬赏！
你们若肯即刻骑马前往，
我的白银与黄金，

===Original===
 That will I give you freely﻿—
 but women shall not share it.
 I care not if Hettel and Hilda
 shall rue their wrong, and ne’er again will dare it.”

===Chinese===
我都甘愿相赠﻿——
只是女子不得分享。
黑特尔与希尔达是否痛悔、
是否再不敢重犯，我全不在意。」

===Original===
Quoth Ludwig, Hartmut’s father:
 “We from our Norman land
 Forthwith must make an inroad:
 soon will I have at hand

===Chinese===
哈特穆特之父路德维希说道：
「我们必须即刻从诺曼底
我们的国土兴兵犯境：
我很快便会拥有

===Original===
 Twenty thousand fighters
 whom I for war will gather;
 With these it will be easy
 to seize Gudrun, and bear her from her father.”

===Chinese===
两万名武士，
由我征召来投入战争；
有这些人手，掳走古德伦、
把她从她父亲身边载走，易如反掌。」

===Original===
Then spake the youthful Hartmut:
 “Might ever this betide,
 That Hilda’s lovely daughter
 I here should see my bride,

===Chinese===
年轻的哈特穆特随即说道：
「但愿此事终能成真，
希尔达的可爱女儿
能在此地成为我的新娘，

===Original===
 I would not take in barter
 for that a princedom fairest;
 Then might we here together
 pass our lives, each one to the other dearest.”

===Chinese===
纵拿最美的公国来交换，
我也决计不肯；
那时我们便可在此地相守，
彼此视若至珍，共度此生。」

===Original===
Busily his followers,
 hour by hour, gave thought
 How they could do his wishes.
 A host King Ludwig brought

===Chinese===
他的部众时时刻刻
忙碌地筹算着，
如何遂他的心愿。
路德维希国王调集起一支大军，

===Original===
 To lead against the Hegelings;
 well were they outfitted.
 How should Hilda know it,
 that soon thereby her welfare would be blighted?

===Chinese===
用来进讨黑格林人；
他们装备得甚是精良。
希尔达哪里知道，
她的福祉将由此很快被摧折？

===Original===
The wife of Ludwig also
 helped them as she could.
 For this she plotted ever,
 that fair Gudrun be wooed,

===Chinese===
路德维希的妻子
也竭尽所能从旁相助。
她为此百般图谋，
要让美丽的古德伦被求娶到手，

===Original===
 And, as the bride of Hartmut,
 to Normandy be carried;
 She did her best most busily
 that the maid one day should to her son be married.

===Chinese===
并作为哈特穆特的新娘，
被载往诺曼底；
她忙不迭地竭尽全力，
只盼那少女终有一日嫁给她儿子。

===Original===
Ludwig said to Hartmut,
 his well belovèd son:
 “Think well, O knight most worthy,
 no toil we now must shun,

===Chinese===
路德维希对他深爱的
儿子哈特穆特说：
「深思啊，最高贵的骑士，
如今我们什么劳苦都不可避，

===Original===
 Until our foes are mastered
 and from their lands are driven.
 Reward the guests who help us;
 to our men at home by me shall gifts be given.”

===Chinese===
直到我们的仇敌被制伏、
被逐出他们的土地。
要厚赏相助的宾客；
家中部下，也将由我颁赐礼物。」

===Original===
These they soon were sharing,
 all and every one.
 Never yet in Suabia
 gifts so rich were known,

===Chinese===
这些赏赐很快便分发下去，
人人有份，无一遗漏。
士瓦本（Suabia）从未见识过
如此丰厚的馈赠，

===Original===
 Of steeds for war or burden,
 saddles, and shields fair shining;
 I ween they were gladly given:
 Ludwig ne’er before such thanks was winning.

===Chinese===
有战马与驮马，
有马鞍，有明光锃亮的盾牌；
我想人们都欢喜领受：
路德维希从未赢得过这许多谢意。

===Original===
Quickly all made ready
 to start upon their way.
 Sailors were found by Ludwig;
 skilful men were they,

===Chinese===
众人迅速整备，
准备登程上路。
路德维希寻到了水手；
他们个个技艺精熟，

===Original===
 Who the deep sea-pathways
 knew, and well could follow;
 Hard must they be toiling
 to win their wages high upon the billow.

===Chinese===
深识大海的航道，
能够稳稳循行；
他们须苦力劳作，
才挣得波涛之上的高额酬劳。

===Original===
Now, in seemly measure,
 fit were they to go.
 Throughout the lands and highways
 soon the news did grow

===Chinese===
如今诸事皆有章法，
众人已整装待发。
消息很快传遍
各地与大道，

===Original===
 That Ludwig and young Hartmut
 home and land were leaving.
 They yet would see much sorrow,
 when they erelong their Hegeling foe were braving.

===Chinese===
说路德维希与年轻的哈特穆特
即将背井离乡。
待他们不久直面黑格林仇敌之时，
还有无尽的悲伤等着他们。

===Original===
When to the shore they had ridden,
 ships were floating there,
 That workmen well had builded,
 the knights away to bear;

===Chinese===
当他们骑马来到岸边，
船只已浮在水上，
那是工匠们精心造就的，
用来载走这些骑士；

===Original===
 Gerlind’s gold and riches
 had made them strong and steady.
 Nor Wâ-te the old nor Fru-te
 of this knew aught, nor were for their coming ready.

===Chinese===
格尔林德的黄金与财富
把船造得坚固沉稳。
老瓦特与弗鲁特
对此一无所知，也未做半分防备。

===Original===
With three and twenty thousand
 they sailed the waters o’er.
 Now for Gudrun young Hartmut
 a weight of sorrow bore:

===Chinese===
他们带着两万三千人
渡过了海面。
年轻的哈特穆特如今
为古德伦背负着沉重的忧伤：

===Original===
 This, before his followers,
 to hide he was not earnest;
 He hoped to meet King Hettel,
 and him to overcome in strife the sternest.

===Chinese===
在部众面前，
他并不着意遮掩；
他盼着与黑特尔国王相逢，
在最酷烈的鏖战中将他制服。

===Original===
As yet they knew not fully
 how they his land could reach.
 To the sons of many a mother
 the raid did sorrow teach.

===Chinese===
他们自己尚且不甚明了，
该怎样抵达那片国土。
这场劫掠给多少母亲的儿子
带去了悲伤的教训。

===Original===
 Near to the shores of Ortland
 the rolling billows bore them,
 Before ’twas known to Hettel:
 now Hilda’s castle rose in sight before them.

===Chinese===
翻滚的波涛把他们送到
奥特兰（Ortland）海岸近旁，
黑特尔尚在懵懂不知：
希尔达的城堡已在他们眼前耸立。

===Original===
The warriors led by Hartmut
 were still twelve miles away;
 Yet had they come already
 over the wide, deep sea,

===Chinese===
哈特穆特统领的武士
尚在十二英里之外；
他们却已然渡过
宽广深邃的大海，

===Original===
 Unto the land of the Hegelings,
 and to its shores so nearly
 That castles, towers, and palaces
 in Hilda’s town they all could see most clearly.

===Chinese===
来到黑格林人的土地，
离那海岸近得
希尔达城中的城堡、塔楼与宫殿，
他们全都望得清清楚楚。

===Original===
Ludwig, king of Normandy,
 bade that on the sand
 They now should drop the anchors;
 he then gave word to land

===Chinese===
诺曼底国王路德维希
下令就在沙滩上
抛下船锚；
随后他传令登陆，

===Original===
 To all his men together,
 and bade them do it quickly:
 They now had come so near them,
 they feared the Hegeling bands would gather thickly.

===Chinese===
向全体部众下令，
并要他们动作迅捷：
此刻他们离得这样近，
生怕黑格林人的队伍密集聚拢。

===Original===
Then bore they up the weapons,
 with shields and helmets good,
 That they had with them carried
 over the heaving flood:

===Chinese===
随后他们扛起了兵器，
还有精良的盾牌与头盔，
都是他们随身一路
载过起伏波涛而来的：

===Original===
 They to fight made ready;
 yet they at first bethought them
 To send through the land their runners,
 to learn if friendly helpers might be brought them.

===Chinese===
他们摆开战阵；
但起初他们又商议，
要先遣急使驰遍全境，
探明能否求得友善的援手。

## Tale XV How Hartmut Carried Away Gudrun / 第十五诗章 哈特穆特如何掳走古德伦

===Original===
Hartmut sends messengers again to Gudrun to ask her hand, and threatens to take her by force if she still rejects his suit. She answers that she is betrothed to Herwic, and refuses him. Hartmut and Ludwig bring an army and capture the Hegeling castle, and carry away Gudrun with her handmaidens.

===Chinese===
哈特穆特（Hartmut）再次遣使向古德伦（Gudrun）求婚，并威胁说，她若再回绝这门亲事，便要动武把她夺走。古德伦回答自己已与赫尔维克（Herwic）订了婚，拒绝了他。哈特穆特与路德维希（Ludwig）率大军攻破黑格林人（Hegeling）的城堡，把古德伦连同她的侍女们一并掳走。

===Original===
Now at Hartmut’s bidding
 heralds quickly rode
 To where the queenly Hilda
 and her daughter dear abode.

===Chinese===
奉哈特穆特之命，
信使们策马疾驰，
奔向王后般的希尔达（Hilda）
与她爱女安居之地。

===Original===
 To them his word they carried,
 that if to wed the maiden
 They should think him worthy,
 her and her mother both it well might gladden.

===Chinese===
他们向他母女传话：
说若肯将少女许嫁、
又看得上他这个人，
母女二人尽可欢喜。

===Original===
If she her love would give him,
 as he had asked before﻿—
 Ofttimes his heart was heavy
 for the love to her he bore﻿—

===Chinese===
若她肯把爱恋赐他，
一如他先前所求——
他常因对她怀着深情，
心头沉重难休——

===Original===
 That he would ever serve her
 so long as he was living,
 And many lands wide-reaching,
 held of his father, would to her be giving.

===Chinese===
他将终生侍奉她，
只要一息尚存，
还要把父亲治下
许多辽阔的国土向她奉赠。

===Original===
But if she would not love him,
 she then would earn his hate;
 He asked of her that kindly
 she his love would meet,

===Chinese===
可她若不肯爱他，
便要招他的怨恨；
他恳求她发善心，
回应他的爱情，

===Original===
 So that he to his fatherland
 his lovely bride might carry
 Without a fight or struggle.
 To hope for this brave Hartmut ne’er was weary.

===Chinese===
好让他把美丽的新娘
携回自己的故土，
无须刀兵相争。
怀着这指望，勇敢的哈特穆特从不厌倦。

===Original===
Did she gainsay his wooing,
 Hartmut sent this word:
 “I will not be bought with silver,
 albeit a heavy hoard,

===Chinese===
她若回绝这门求亲，
哈特穆特便传下话来：
「纵使拿成堆的白银重宝，
也买不动我罢手，

===Original===
 To leave in peace her kingdom;
 she yet shall give me heeding.
 I will show Gudrun, fair maiden,
 brave knights enough, to be for her eyes fine feeding!

===Chinese===
安然离开她的王国；
她终究须俯就于我。
我要让古德伦、那美丽的少女，
看个够英勇的骑士，好饱一饱她的眼福！

===Original===
“Further, good errand-bearers,
 this say to her from me:
 I ne’er will leave her borders
 to sail on the wide, deep sea;

===Chinese===
「再者，好使者们，
替我把这话传给她：
我绝不离开她的国土，
去航行于宽广的深海；

===Original===
 Better will I think it
 to be hewn in pieces even,
 Unless the Hegeling maiden
 will follow me hence, to me in wedlock given.

===Chinese===
若非黑格林人的少女
肯随我同去、嫁我为妻，
我宁可被剁成碎片，
也觉得称心快意。

===Original===
“But, should she scorn me wholly,
 and never my bride will be,
 Then me, with my daring fighters,
 riding here she will see.

===Chinese===
「但她若全然鄙弃我，
永不肯做我的新娘，
她就会看见我
偕同剽悍的武士策马而至。

===Original===
 Before the Hegeling castle
 I will then leave lying
 Twenty thousand warriors,
 on both sides of the roadway, dead or dying.

===Chinese===
在黑格林人的城堡前，
我将留下两万名武士，
横陈在大道两旁，
死的死，垂死的垂死。

===Original===
“Since by the craft of Wigaleis
 King Hettel has been led,
 And by the aged Wâ-te,
 hither our way we’ve made

===Chinese===
「只因维加莱斯（Wigaleis）的巧计，
黑特尔（Hettel）国王被调开，
又因年迈的瓦特（Wâ-te），
我们才一路来到了

===Original===
 Into the Hegeling kingdom,
 time and toil thus spending;
 For this shall many be fatherless,
 and glad shall I be of the whole to make an ending.”

===Chinese===
黑格林人的王国，
不惜时日与辛劳；
为这，多少孩子将失去父亲，
我也乐得把这一切做个了结。」

===Original===
Those sent forth by Hartmut
 fast on their way did ride,
 For he bade them wait no longer.
 They came to a castle wide,

===Chinese===
哈特穆特派出的人
一路纵马飞驰，
因他吩咐不得耽搁。
他们来到一座宽阔的城堡，

===Original===
 By name ycleped Matelan;
 therein was Hilda dwelling,
 And with her was her daughter,
 the maid about whose charms all men were telling.

===Chinese===
名字唤作马特兰（Matelan）；
希尔达住在里面，
与她同住的是她的女儿——
那位少女的妍姿，人人都在称道。

===Original===
With them sent Hartmut also
 two earls of wealth and name,
 Who with him out of Normandy
 over the waters came.

===Chinese===
哈特穆特又随行使者
派出两位富贵闻名的伯爵，
就是当初随他离开诺曼底、
渡过重洋而来的那两位。

===Original===
