 He bade them see Queen Hilda,
 and kindly to bespeak her;
 To pledge to her his friendship,
 and say that his goodwill would ne’er forsake her.

===Chinese===
他命他们觐见希尔达王后，
以好言向她致意；
向她申明他的友谊，
说他的善意永不离弃她。

===Original===
Of her they must ask her daughter,
 for him who in his mind
 So high had ever set her,
 above all womankind:

===Chinese===
他们还须向她求取她的女儿，
为了那个在心里
始终把她捧得极高、
高过世间一切女子的男子：

===Original===
 In worthy love he wooed her,
 and she would rank be taking
 That for aye would make her happy;
 to do her will she ne’er would find him lacking.

===Chinese===
他以高尚的爱情向她求亲，
她将得到的位分，
会使她永远欢欣；
凡她的心愿，他永不会亏欠分毫。

===Original===
To the maiden’s waiting-women
 the news was quickly told,
 That from out the land of Normandy
 a band of wooers bold

===Chinese===
少女的贴身侍女们
很快听得了这风声：
说有一队大胆的求婚人
已从诺曼底之地

===Original===
 Hither rode to Matelan,
 and for Gudrun were suing:
 Hilda hushed the tidings,
 for now Gudrun in fright the tale was ruing.

===Chinese===
策马来到马特兰，
为古德伦求婚说项；
希尔达把消息按下，
只因古德伦闻言惊惶，正自忧伤。

===Original===
Queen Hilda’s faithful warders
 opened soon the gate;
 Those who had ridden thither
 need no longer wait;

===Chinese===
希尔达王后忠实的守卫
很快开了城门；
骑马而来的众人
不必再久候；

===Original===
 They to come in were bidden.
 The gate was thrown wide open,
 And the men sent there by Hartmut
 into Matelan rode: no ill to them did happen.

===Chinese===
他们奉请入内。
大门敞然洞开，
哈特穆特遣来的人马
驰入马特兰，未遭任何损伤。

===Original===
They quickly told their wishes,
 to see King Hettel’s wife.
 It was not yet allowed them;
 they who should guard her life,

===Chinese===
他们急急道明来意，
求见黑特尔国王的王后。
一时还未获准许；
那些负责护卫她性命、

===Original===
 And to the king must answer,
 at first had this forbidden:
 They never left uncared for
 Hilda the queen, and eke Gudrun the maiden.

===Chinese===
又须向国王交账的人，
起初挡了这一请：
他们从不曾撇下王后希尔达
与少女古德伦，任她们无人照看。

===Original===
At last the men of Hartmut
 into the hall were led.
 To them the queenly Hilda
 kindly greeting made,

===Chinese===
哈特穆特的人终于
被引进了大厅。
王后般的希尔达
向他们殷勤见礼，

===Original===
 As did Gudrun the lady,
 with fair and lofty bearing;
 But she, the high-born maiden,
 love for Herwic in her heart was wearing.

===Chinese===
古德伦贵女也同样还礼，
仪态娴雅而高贵；
只是这位出身高贵的少女，
心中怀着对赫尔维克的爱。

===Original===
Altho’ they felt unfriendly,
 yet drink they gave to the men
 Ere yet they told their errand;
 freely then the queen

===Chinese===
尽管她们心怀芥蒂，
还是在来使道明来意之前
先以酒饮相待；
随后王后慨然

===Original===
 Bade them to be seated
 before herself and her daughter.
 She begged them then to tell her:
 “What boon to seek had brought them o’er the water?”

===Chinese===
请他们落座，
就在她母女面前。
接着她请他们言明：
「为求什么恩典，使他们渡海而来？」

===Original===
All the men of Hartmut
 before their seats yet stood,
 As well-bred men beseemeth,
 and errand-bearers should.

===Chinese===
哈特穆特的众使者
仍在座前侍立，
正如有教养者当行的，
也是使者应守的仪节。

===Original===
 Then they told the ladies
 what they would there be doing﻿—
 That for their master, Hartmut,
 they for the fair Gudrun had come a-wooing.

===Chinese===
然后他们向贵妇们
道出了此行的所为——
说他们奉主人哈特穆特之命，
前来向美丽的古德伦求婚。

===Original===
The high-born maiden answered:
 “Of this I nought will hear﻿—
 That with the young King Hartmut
 I the crown should share,

===Chinese===
高贵的少女答道：
「这事我全然不听——
说我该同年轻的哈特穆特国王
共戴王冠、

===Original===
 Before our friendly kinsmen,
 and troth to him be plighted:
 The name of the knight is Herwic
 whose love shall never by myself be slighted.

===Chinese===
当着我们友好的亲族，
对他许下婚誓：
那骑士的名字是赫尔维克，
我对他的爱情永不轻慢。

===Original===
“To him I am betrothed;
 me he chose for a wife,
 And him for myself I have taken.
 Ever, throughout his life,

===Chinese===
「我已同他订下婚约；
是他选中我为妻，
我也把他选作我的夫君。
在他有生之年，

===Original===
 All of good I wish him
 that can henceforth befall him:
 Ne’er, till my days are ended,
 will I ask the love of another, or my lord will call him.”

===Chinese===
凡此后能降临他身的
好事，我尽都为他祝愿：
直到我的日子完结，
再不会求他人的爱，或唤别人作夫君。」

===Original===
One of them then answered:
 “This warning Hartmut gives:
 If nay shall be your answer,
 before three days, if he lives,

===Chinese===
使者中一人答道：
「哈特穆特有此警告：
倘若你们回以拒绝，
三日之内，只要他还活着，

===Original===
 Against great Matelan castle
 you shall see him leading
 All his knightly followers.”
 Smiles at this were the maiden’s face o’erspreading.

===Chinese===
你们就会看见他统率
麾下全体骑士，
兵临宏伟的马特兰城堡。」
闻言，少女面上漾开了笑意。

===Original===
Their leave they would be taking,
 and hasten on their way,
 Those two great earls so haughty;
 but Hilda bade them stay.

===Chinese===
他们正要请求告辞，
匆匆踏上归程，
那两位倨傲已极的伯爵；
希尔达却唤他们留下。

===Original===
 Altho’ she ne’er had known them,
 of gifts she was not chary;
 But yet they would not take them,
 for crafty men were they, and in truth were wary.

===Chinese===
虽与他们素不相识，
她馈赠起来毫不吝惜；
可他们不肯收受，
这两人诡诈多端，实实心存戒惧。

===Original===
At those sent there by Hartmut
 Hettel’s followers sneered,
 And said, their scorn and anger
 they very little feared:

===Chinese===
黑特尔的部众对着
哈特穆特遣来的使者冷笑，
说这些人对于他们的
轻蔑与怒火全不在乎：

===Original===
 If to drink the wine of Hettel
 they were, in truth, unwilling,
 Then this warning gave they:
 that they their cup with blood would soon be filling.

===Chinese===
若他们当真不愿
畅饮黑特尔的美酒，
黑特尔部众便警告：
他们的杯中顷刻将斟满鲜血。

===Original===
When they had heard this answer,
 back to the shore they went
 Whence they had been by Hartmut
 upon their errand sent.

===Chinese===
听得了这样的答复，
他们便折返海岸——
正是哈特穆特当初
遣他们出使之地。

===Original===
 He then ran forth to meet them,
 to ask how they were treated,
 And what had them befallen,
 and how his courtship by Gudrun was greeted.

===Chinese===
哈特穆特奔上前来相迎，
问他们受了怎样的接待，
又遭逢了什么，
他的求婚得了古德伦怎样的对待。

===Original===
Then one of them thus answered:
 “This to us they said:
 The high and queenly maiden
 a lover long has had,

===Chinese===
使者中一人这样回禀：
「他们对我们说：
那位高贵而王后般的少女，
早已有了一位恋人，

===Original===
 For whom, beyond all others,
 love in her heart she is feeling:
 If you will not taste their wine-cup,
 they soon will fill to you, your life-blood spilling.”

===Chinese===
在她心里，此人胜过
世间一切的人：
你们若不肯尝他们的酒，
他们便要向你们满斟一盏，泼洒你们的性命之血。」

===Original===
“Ah, woe is me!” said Hartmut,
 when he this answer heard;
 “My heart is full of anger,
 with shame I hear your word!

===Chinese===
「唉，我真是不幸！」哈特穆特说，
当他听得这一答复；
「我心中满是愤怒，
你们的话叫我羞惭难当！

===Original===
 Never men more friendly
 shall I need, till I am dying,
 Than those who now will help me.”
 Straightway his men upsprang, on the shore then lying.

===Chinese===
直到我死之日，
也再不需要比眼前
肯助我的人更亲善的帮手。」
卧在岸边的部众当即跃起。

===Original===
Ludwig now and Hartmut,
 with their men, set out for war;
 Their banners high uplifted
 in pride and wrath they bore.

===Chinese===
路德维希与哈特穆特
于是率众出征；
他们把旌旗高高擎起，
挟着骄矜与怒火向前。

===Original===
 These from Matelan castle
 were seen afar to shimmer:
 “Cheer up!” then said the maiden;
 “Herwic and Hettel come! their weapons glimmer!”

===Chinese===
这些旌旗远远望去，
已在马特兰城堡外闪耀：
「高兴起来吧！」少女说道；
「是赫尔维克和黑特尔来了！兵器正闪光！」

===Original===
But Hilda saw the standard
 bore not King Hettel’s mark:
 “Ah, woe shall now betide us
 before this day grows dark!

===Chinese===
但希尔达望见那面大旗
未带着黑特尔国王的标记：
「唉，不等今日入夜，
灾祸就要临到我们头上！

===Original===
 To seek Gudrun are coming
 foemen grim and daring;
 Many a well-made helmet
 their blows shall hew before the night is nearing.”

===Chinese===
前来寻夺古德伦的，
是凶悍大胆的仇敌；
不等夜色将临，
多少精良的头盔要被他们的刀剑劈碎。」

===Original===
Then her friendly Hegelings
 thus to Hilda spake:
 “If those led on by Hartmut
 to-day an onslaught make,

===Chinese===
于是她亲善的黑格林人
这样对希尔达说：
「倘若哈特穆特率领的人
今日发动强攻，

===Original===
 Wounds we then must deal them,
 and show we are the stronger.”
 Queen Hilda then gave bidding
 to shut the castle gates, and wait no longer.

===Chinese===
我们必以创伤还施，
叫他们知道谁更强大。」
希尔达王后随即传令：
关紧城堡大门，不再等待。

===Original===
But the men of brave King Hettel
 followed not her hest;
 They who the castle guarded
 thought to fight their best.

===Chinese===
可勇敢的黑特尔国王的部众
没有依从她的吩咐；
守卫城堡的将士
只想倾力一战。

===Original===
 They bade that now their banners
 to the shafts be fastened;
 King Hettel’s daring followers,
 to slay his foes, from out the castle hastened.

===Chinese===
他们吩咐把军旗
牢牢系上旗杆；
黑特尔国王剽悍的部众
冲出城堡，要诛杀他的仇敌。

===Original===
The bars that should be lowered,
 to keep the foemen out,
 Were left, in over-boldness,
 and the gates not fully shut,

===Chinese===
本该放下的门闩——
用来把仇敌挡在外面——
因过分自负而未放，
大门也没有关严，

===Original===
 Since from Hartmut’s foreguard
 they little harm foreboded.
 But when they pressed in boldly,
 then came the rest, who ever on them crowded.

===Chinese===
因为对哈特穆特的前锋，
他们没料到多少凶险。
可当前锋悍然抢入，
后队便蜂拥而至，向他们重重压来。

===Original===
A thousand men or over
 stood before the gate;
 These, their swords upbearing,
 the fight did there await.

===Chinese===
千名开外的武士
伫立在大门前；
他们高擎着剑，
就在那里候战。

===Original===
 A thousand more with Hartmut
 now came thronging thickly;
 They then from their steeds alighted,
 and back to the rear they sent their horses quickly.

===Chinese===
另有一千人随哈特穆特
麇集涌至；
他们翻身下马，
又急急把马匹遣往后方。

===Original===
Spears in hand they carried,
 with points full keen to cut.
 Who could shun their onset?
 With heavy wounds they smote

===Chinese===
他们手中擎着长矛，
矛锋犀利无俦。
谁能避开他们的冲杀？
他们挥出沉重的创伤，

===Original===
 Those who the castle guarded,
 in their pride o’erweening.
 Just at the hour came Ludwig,
 with his Norman knights, as the fight was now beginning.

===Chinese===
落在守城将士的身上，
那些人正自负过甚。
恰在此时路德维希赶到，
带着他的诺曼骑士，此刻战事方兴。

===Original===
Much the women sorrowed
 as Ludwig nearer rode:
 The banners o’er them floating
 well and proudly showed

===Chinese===
妇人们满怀悲戚，
眼看路德维希逼近：
飘扬在他们头顶的旌旗，
分明而傲然地显出

===Original===
 The fearless foe oncoming;
 beneath each standard flocking,
 Three thousand now came boldly,
 though sad on their homeward way they might yet be looking.

===Chinese===
无畏之敌正节节进逼；
每面大旗之下都麇集着人马，
三千之众悍然杀到，
纵使回望归途，或将满是凄惶。

===Original===
Before the walls beleaguered
 the guards were a busy band:
 Never hardier fighters
 were seen in any land

===Chinese===
在被围的城墙前，
守军人人奋力当先：
再没有更顽强的斗士
曾在任何国度被人看见，

===Original===
 Than were the faithful warders
 in Hettel’s castle dwelling;
 Their blows they were thickly dealing,
 and Hartmut’s men their strength were quickly feeling.

===Chinese===
胜过黑特尔城堡中
这些忠实的守卫；
他们的刀剑密密挥落，
哈特穆特的人很快尝到了厉害。

===Original===
Ludwig, Hartmut’s father,
 the Norman king, was seen
 From hardened rims of bucklers
 to strike a fiery sheen:

===Chinese===
哈特穆特之父路德维希，
那位诺曼国王，只见他
从淬硬的盾沿上
击出灼灼火星：

===Original===
 Truly, great was the bravery
 that now his heart was swelling;
 His friends and followers also,
 in the bloody game, were bold beyond all telling.

===Chinese===
诚然，此刻鼓荡他胸怀的
是何等豪勇；
他的友伴与部众
在这血腥的戏局中，也勇猛得难以言传。

===Original===
When they who the castle guarded
 hoped for rest and peace,
 Then their daring foemen
 did nearer to them press,

===Chinese===
每当守城的将士
指望喘息与安宁，
那些剽悍的仇敌
便又向他们进逼，

===Original===
 Led by him of Normandy:
 the youthful Hartmut’s father
 Grudged no toil to help him;
 and this from that day’s fight one well might gather.

===Chinese===
领头的正是诺曼底之主：
年轻的哈特穆特之父，
助子成事不惜辛劳；
从当日的战况里，这一点尽可看清。

===Original===
Now the trustful warders
 began in truth to mourn,
 That they, ’gainst Hilda’s bidding,
 had their care forborne﻿—

===Chinese===
如今这些轻信的守卫
当真开始哀叹，
悔不该违背希尔达之命，
把警戒抛在一边——

===Original===
 The hest of the wife of Hettel,
 the high and worthy lady.
 For this their shields were shattered,
 and many a life was lost, in fight too ready.

===Chinese===
违背了黑特尔之妻、
那位高贵可敬的夫人的嘱咐。
他们的盾牌因此击碎，
多少性命断送，只因恋战过急。

===Original===
Ludwig now and Hartmut
 on the field had met,
 And, holding speech together,
 learned that, striving yet,

===Chinese===
路德维希与哈特穆特
在战场上相见，
两人聚谈之间，
得知守军虽仍在苦撑，

===Original===
 Queen Hilda’s men were seeking
 the castle gates to fasten;
 Then, with shields before them,
 to bear their flags within they all did hasten.

===Chinese===
希尔达王后的人正设法
把城堡大门闩紧；
于是他们举盾当前，
急忙把军旗运入城中。

===Original===
Rocks were hurled from the castle,
 and many spears were thrown,
 But the foe it hurt but little,
 and his daring lessened none.

===Chinese===
城头掷下巨石，
掷出无数长矛，
可仇敌所伤无几，
胆气丝毫不减。

===Original===
 Little thought was given
 to the dead around them lying:
 With heavy stones down beaten,
 many bold besiegers there fell dying.

===Chinese===
谁也无暇顾及
横陈在四下的死者：
沉重的石块砸落，
多少悍勇的围攻者倒地殒命。

===Original===
When Hartmut and King Ludwig
 came within the gate,
 Many, badly wounded,
 from them their death-stroke met.

===Chinese===
哈特穆特与路德维希国王
闯进城门之时，
许多身负重伤的人
死在他们的剑下。

===Original===
 For this the lovely maiden
 began to sorrow sorely;
 Now in Hettel’s castle
 the woe they wrought was growing greater hourly.

===Chinese===
为此那可爱的少女
开始深自悲恸；
黑特尔的城堡里，
他们造下的苦难与时俱增。

===Original===
Then the king of Normandy
 was glad enough, I ween,
 When to the halls of Hettel
 he could lead his men,

===Chinese===
我想，那位诺曼底国王
可算是称心遂意，
当他能率领部众
开进黑特尔的殿堂，

===Original===
 Bearing well their weapons:
 soon his banner fluttered
 Over the roof of the castle.
 Hilda at this her sorrow loudly uttered.

===Chinese===
兵刃个个在手：
不多时他的大旗
便在城堡的屋顶上飘扬。
希尔达见了，放声悲号。

===Original===
Greatly do I wonder
 what might these guests befall,
 Had now the grim old Wâ-te
 been there, and seen it all,

===Chinese===
我深深纳罕，
这班贵客会遭逢什么，
倘若狰狞的老瓦特
当时在场，目睹了这一切，

===Original===
 The while the men of Hartmut,
 with Ludwig, brave and daring,
 Thro’ the halls were rushing,
 and from her home the fair Gudrun were tearing.

===Chinese===
其时哈特穆特的人
正随剽悍的路德维希
冲杀穿过一座座厅堂，
把美丽的古德伦从家中硬生生夺去。

===Original===
Both Wâ-te and King Hettel,
 if to them that day
 A warning had been given,
 would stoutly have barred the way;

===Chinese===
瓦特与黑特尔国王二人，
倘若那一天
有人给他们报了信，
必会坚决拦住去路；

===Original===
 They their foemen’s helmets
 with swords would so have riven
 That back to their homes in Normandy,
 without Gudrun, would they have soon been driven.

===Chinese===
他们定会用利剑
把仇敌的头盔劈烂，
教他们早早被赶回诺曼底的家园，
身边再没有古德伦。

===Original===
Now within the castle
 were all in saddest mood;
 So men to-day might sorrow.
 Whate’er the foemen would,

===Chinese===
此刻城堡之中
一片愁惨气象；
今日之人见了亦当悲恸。
仇敌无论想要什么，

===Original===
 There did they lay hands on,
 and took from out the dwelling.
 Rich grew Hartmut’s followers﻿—
 you well may trust that I the truth am telling.

===Chinese===
他们伸手便夺，
把财物掳出宅邸。
哈特穆特的部众个个暴富——
请相信，我说的句句是实。

===Original===
Then came the bold young Hartmut
 where he Gudrun could see,
 And said: “Most worthy lady,
 you erst looked down on me;

===Chinese===
随后胆大的年轻哈特穆特
来到古德伦跟前，
说道：「最尊贵的夫人，
你从前可把我小看；

===Original===
 But now both I and my followers
 think of your kin so little,
 We will not seize and hold them,
 but slay and hang them, so the strife to settle.”

===Chinese===
可如今我与我的人
都不把你的亲族放在心上，
我们不要活捉拘押，
只管杀的杀、吊的吊，就此了结这场纷争。」

===Original===
Then said the maiden only:
 “Alas! O father mine,
 Had you of this been knowing,
 that I, a child of thine,

===Chinese===
少女只答了这一句：
「唉，我的父亲啊，
你若早知道有此一日，
知道你的孩子、

===Original===
 One day from out your kingdom
 would thus by foes be stolen,
 Never to me, poor maiden,
 such woe and sorry shame had here befallen.”

===Chinese===
有一天会这样被仇敌
从你的国土里劫走，
我这可怜的少女，
便不会在这里遭受这般悲辱。」

===Original===
Then was the gold and clothing
 borne out by the robber band:
 Forth they took Queen Hilda,
 led by her snow-white hand.

===Chinese===
接着黄金与衣裳
被那伙强盗搬运一空：
他们又把希尔达王后
拽着她雪白的手带走。

===Original===
 Matelan’s goodly castle
 they would have burned up gladly;
 For what became of the dwellers
 the Normans never cared, nor thought of sadly.

===Chinese===
马特兰华美的城堡，
他们本乐得付之一炬；
至于居民们落得什么下场，
诺曼人全不挂心，也毫无怜惜。

===Original===
But Hartmut now had bidden
 that it should not be burned,
 To leave the land he hastened,
 and home again he turned,

===Chinese===
但哈特穆特已有吩咐，
不许焚毁城堡；
他匆匆离开这片国土，
转身踏上归程，

===Original===
 Before ’twas known to Hettel,
 who with his men was lying
 Within the Waalisch marches,
 and there against his foe his strength was trying.

===Chinese===
趁黑特尔尚未知晓——
他正率部驻扎
在瓦尔河（Waalisch）边地，
在那里与强敌较劲。

===Original===
“Leave your stolen booty!”
 to his men young Hartmut said;
 “At home my father’s riches
 will I give to you instead:

===Chinese===
「放下你们抢来的财物！」
年轻的哈特穆特对部众说；
「回到家乡，我父亲的财宝
我会如数补给你们：

===Original===
 Thus o’er the watery pathway
 our sail will be the lighter.”
 To Gudrun the hand of Ludwig
 brought a heavy wrong, and woe full bitter.

===Chinese===
这样渡过水路时，
我们的船帆会更轻快。」
可路德维希的手加在古德伦身上的，
是沉重的欺辱与彻骨的悲苦。

===Original===
They overthrew the castle,
 the town with fire they burned;
 From it the best was taken;
 with wealth they homeward turned:

===Chinese===
他们把城堡夷平，
用烈火焚了城镇；
其中最贵重的尽被夺走；
他们携着财货掉头回程：

===Original===
 Two and sixty women
 thence with them they carried,
 And many lovely maidens.
 With heartfelt woe was queenly Hilda wearied.

===Chinese===
六十二名妇人
被他们一并带走，
还有许多可爱的少女。
王后般的希尔达怀着锥心的悲痛，心力交瘁。

===Original===
How were they filled with sadness
 to leave the wine behind!
 Now did the queenly mother
 a seat in the window find,

===Chinese===
把美酒抛在身后，
他们是何等怏怏！
这时王后般的母亲
在窗边寻了个座位，

===Original===
 And looked upon her daughter,
 from home in sorrow turning.
 Many a stately lady
 the Normans left in tears, and bitterly mourning.

===Chinese===
望着自己的女儿
满怀离愁背井而去。
多少雍容的贵妇
被诺曼人撇下，涕泪涟涟，哀恸不已。

===Original===
Weeping now and wailing
 was heard on either hand;
 No one there was happy,
 when from the fatherland

===Chinese===
啼泣与哀号之声
在两侧处处可闻；
没有一人欢欣，
当着故土之上，

===Original===
 The foe with Hilda’s daughter
 and with her maidens hasted.
 Many, now but children,
 for this, when men, to work them woe ne’er rested.

===Chinese===
仇敌挟着希尔达的女儿
和她的侍女们匆匆而去。
许多如今尚是孩童的人，
只待长成男子汉，便要为此向他们降下灾祸，永不止息。

===Original===
Those who were seized by Hartmut
 down to the shore he took;
 All their lands were wasted;
 their homes went up in smoke.

===Chinese===
哈特穆特掳来的众人，
全被他押到岸边；
他们的田土尽遭蹂躏，
家园化为烟尘。

===Original===
 Now his hopes and wishes
 happily were granted:
 Both Gudrun and Hildeburg
 he with him carried off﻿—the prize he wanted.

===Chinese===
如今他的指望与心愿
都欣然得偿：
古德伦与希尔德堡（Hildeburg）
都被他掳走——正是他志在必得的战利品。

===Original===
Well he knew that Hettel
 was many a league away,
 And war was grimly waging;
 no more would Hartmut stay.

===Chinese===
他深知黑特尔
远在数十里格之外，
正鏖战于酷烈的战场；
哈特穆特再不作停留。

===Original===
 Yet from the Hegeling kingdom
 no whit too fast he speeded,
 For word was sent by Hilda
 to Hettel and his friends, that much their help was needed.

===Chinese===
但他撤出黑格林人的王国，
也不算快了一分，
因为希尔达已遣人传话
给黑特尔与他的友伴：急需他们驰援。

===Original===
How mournful were the tidings
 before the king she laid!﻿—
 That in his home and castle
 his knights were lying dead,

===Chinese===
她呈到国王面前的，
是何等哀痛的音讯！——
说他的家园与城堡中，
骑士们横尸在地，

===Original===
 Or else were left by Hartmut
 now with death-wounds bleeding;
 That foes had seized his daughter,
 and with her many maids were homeward speeding.

===Chinese===
或者被哈特穆特撇下，
带着致命伤流血；
说仇敌掳走了他的女儿，
正携着她与众多少女疾驶回乡。

===Original===
She said: “Now tell King Hettel
 that I am here alone;
 Evil hath me o’ertaken, and now,
 with pride o’ergrown,

===Chinese===
她说：「快去告诉黑特尔国王，
我孤身在此；
灾祸已将我笼罩，而如今，
那骄横不可一世的

===Original===
 Our mighty foeman, Ludwig,
 back to his land is faring;
 A thousand men or better
 lie at our gates, and the pains of death are bearing.”

===Chinese===
强敌路德维希，
正班师回他的国土；
上千的人横在我们门前，
正忍受着死亡的剧痛。」

===Original===
Quickly then went Hartmut,
 and, ere three days were o’er,
 On board his keels was ready;
 these the plunder bore,

===Chinese===
哈特穆特随即启程，
不出三日，
便已整装在船上待发；
船中满载着掳获，

===Original===
 As much as they could carry,
 whate’er his men had stolen.
 The men of brave King Hettel
 were dazed and stunned by all that had befallen.

===Chinese===
能装载的尽量装载，
凡部众劫来的一概上船。
勇敢的黑特尔国王的部下
对这一切变故惊愕失措。

===Original===
What further did betide them,
 who in truth can tell?
 Loud on the ear it sounded,
 as they shifted the flapping sail,

===Chinese===
此后又遭逢了什么，
有谁能确言？
只听得声响灌耳，
当他们变换鼓涨的帆篷，

===Original===
 And away from the Hegeling kingdom,
 unto an isle forsaken,
 They their barks were turning;
 the name of Wulpensand﻿—or shore of the wolves﻿—it had taken.

===Chinese===
驶离黑格林人的王国，
朝一座荒岛进发；
那岛的名字
唤作武尔彭沙洲（Wulpensand）——又名狼之滨。

## Tale XVI How Hilda Sent to Hettel and Herwic to Ask Their Help Against Hartmut / 第十六诗章 希尔达如何遣人向黑特尔与赫尔维克求援抗击哈特穆特

===Original===
Hettel and Herwic, being informed of their losses by Hilda, make peace with Siegfried, and release him on condition that he will unite with them in war against Hartmut. They take possession of the ships of a pilgrim band, and pursue Hartmut.

===Chinese===
黑特尔（Hettel）与赫尔维克（Herwic）从希尔达（Hilda）处得知自家的损失，便与西格弗里德（Siegfried）议和，放他离去，条件是他须同他们联手对哈特穆特（Hartmut）作战。他们夺取了一队朝圣的旅人所乘的船只，随后追击哈特穆特。

===Original===
The fair and queenly Hilda,
 with all her will and mind,
 Gave her thoughts now wholly
 trusty men to find

===Chinese===
美丽而王后般的希尔达，
一心一意，
此刻把全部心思
都用在寻访可靠之士，

===Original===
 To bear the tale to Hettel.
 Her heart indeed was riven
 By the wrongful deeds of Hartmut,
 and food for tears he to her eyes had given.

===Chinese===
好把音讯带给黑特尔。
她的心确已被撕裂：
哈特穆特横行不义，
给她双眼赐下了流泪的滋养。

===Original===
To Herwic and her husband
 she bade that it be said
 That foes had seized her daughter,
 that many knights lay dead;

===Chinese===
她吩咐传话，
说与赫尔维克和她的夫君：
仇敌已劫走她的女儿，
多少骑士已陈尸殒命；

===Original===
 And she was left in wretchedness,
 lonely and forsaken;
 That all her gold and jewels
 the Normans on their way had with them taken.

===Chinese===
她只落得凄凉悲苦，
孤零零遭人撇弃；
她的黄金与珠宝，
尽被诺曼底人顺手掳去。

===Original===
Quickly rode the heralds
 and through the land they went:
 The queen in greatest sorrow
 these on their way had sent.

===Chinese===
信使们策马疾驰，
一路穿行国土：
这班人是悲恸至深的王后
打发上路的使者。

===Original===
 Upon the seventh morning,
 they came where they were greeted
 With the sight of beleaguering Hegelings
 who before their Moorland foes were seated.

===Chinese===
第七日的清晨，
他们来到一处，迎接他们的是这般景象：
围城的黑格林人，
正面对摩尔兰（Moorland）的仇敌安营对峙。

===Original===
Oft in knightly matches
 strove they every day,
 And one might also hear them
 at many a game and play,

===Chinese===
他们天天都在
骑士比武中较量；
人们还能听见他们
嬉游于种种玩乐竞技，

===Original===
 That they might not be weary
 who the siege were keeping;
 Some at a mark were shooting,
 and others strove in running and in leaping.

===Chinese===
好教围城的人们
不致倦怠生烦；
有的对着箭靶射击，
有的赛跑、又比纵跳。

===Original===
When by the Danish Horant
 errand-bearers were seen
 Who to the land were coming,
 thither sent by the queen,

===Chinese===
当丹麦的霍兰特（Horant）
望见使者们的身影，
他们正朝这片土地赶来——
是王后遣他们至此，

===Original===
 Then said he unto Hettel:
 “With news for us they’re riding;
 May God in kindness grant it,
 no ill to those at home is now betiding!”

===Chinese===
他便对黑特尔说道：
「他们正携我们的音信疾驰而来；
愿上帝仁慈垂顾，
家中如今并无灾祸临头！」

===Original===
The king himself went forward,
 and met them where they stood.
 He said, with seemly bearing,
 to them in their sorry mood:

===Chinese===
国王亲自上前，
在他们驻足之处相迎。
他仪态雍容，
向愁容满面的来使开言：

===Original===
 “Brave knights, I give you welcome
 here to this far-off border.
 How fares it with Queen Hilda?
 Who sent you here? and who is left to guard her?”

===Chinese===
「勇敢的骑士们，欢迎你们
来到这遥远的边疆。
希尔达王后可还安好？
是谁差你们前来？又是谁留守她的身旁？」

===Original===
Said one: “Your lady sent us;
 to you for help she turns:
 Wasted are your castles;
 your lands the foeman burns.

===Chinese===
其中一人说道：「你们的夫人差我们前来；
她向你们求取援助：
你们的城堡已夷为废墟，
你们的国土正被敌人焚烧。

===Original===
 Gudrun from thence is carried;
 her maidens, too, are taken:
 Never can your kingdom
 from all these woes and ills again awaken.

===Chinese===
古德伦（Gudrun）已从那里被掳走；
她的侍女们也一并被擒：
你们的王国
再也无力从这重重灾殃中苏醒。

===Original===
“This must I say, moreover,
 we are in straitest need;
 Now of your men and kindred
 a thousand there lie dead;

===Chinese===
「此外我还须直言：
我们已困厄至极；
你们的部众与亲族，
已有千人陈尸在地；

===Original===
 And into far-off kingdoms
 have foes your riches carried;
 Your hoard of wealth is scattered:
 it shames good knights that thus your lands are harried.”

===Chinese===
你们的财富已被仇敌
掠往遥远的国度；
你们的珍宝积蓄四散：
国土横遭蹂躏，实令好骑士汗颜。」

===Original===
The king then bade them tell him
 who these deeds had done.
 One among them answered,
 and their names to him made known:

===Chinese===
国王便命他们说出
这些事是何人所为。
其中一人作答，
向他道出了那几个名字：

===Original===
 “Ludwig was one, the Norman;
 with many knights he fought us;
 Hartmut, his son, was the other:
 ’twas they the inroad made, and havoc wrought us.”

===Chinese===
「一个是诺曼底人路德维希（Ludwig），
他率众多骑士同我们交战；
另一个是他的儿子哈特穆特：
闯进来肆虐、降祸我们的正是这班人。」

===Original===
Then King Hettel answered:
 “To Hartmut I would not give,
 For his bride, Gudrun my daughter;
 for this he now doth strive

===Chinese===
于是黑特尔王答道：
「我决不肯把古德伦、
我的女儿，交给哈特穆特
做新娘；他如今却为此力争，

===Original===
 To waste with war my kingdom.
 I know his lands are holden
 Of Hagen, her mother’s father;
 to woo her should his rank not him embolden.

===Chinese===
妄图借战火蹂躏我的王国。
我知道他的领地
隶属她外祖父哈根（Hagen）治下；
论门第，他不配胆敢向她求亲。

===Original===
“To our beleaguered foemen
 we nought of this must tell,
 And to our friends but whisper
 the ills that us befell;

===Chinese===
「这等事，对被我们围困的仇敌
半个字也不许透露，
对自家亲友也只能低声
诉说我们所遇的灾殃；

===Original===
 We then must call our kinsmen
 hither to be hasting.
 Worse could never happen
 unto good knights at home, from warfare resting.”

===Chinese===
我们还须召请亲族，
催他们火速赶来。
对留守家园、息兵休战的好骑士，
再没有比这更惨的祸灾。」

===Original===
Herwic then was bidden
 to Hettel forthwith to go:
 Hettel’s friends and kindred
 and his men were sent for, too.

===Chinese===
赫尔维克随即奉命，
即刻前往黑特尔处：
黑特尔的朋友与亲族，
连同他的部下也一并被召来。

===Original===
 When now these knights so worthy
 their way to him had taken,
 They found their king and master
 dark in mind, and of every hope forsaken.

===Chinese===
当这些尊贵的骑士
来到他的面前，
只见他们的君王主人
心绪阴沉，万念俱灰。

===Original===
Then said the lord of the Hegelings:
 “To you I make my moan;
 And, trusting in your friendship,
 my sorrows must I own:

===Chinese===
于是黑格林人之主开言：
「我要向你们倾吐苦衷；
既信赖你们的情谊，
我的伤痛也须如实相告：

===Original===
 The queen, my Lady Hilda,
 has sent to give us warning,
 That the men of the Hegeling kingdom
 are ill bestead, and bitterly are mourning.

===Chinese===
王后、我的夫人希尔达
已遣人来示警：
说黑格林王国的臣民
深陷困厄，正悲恸失声。

===Original===
“My lands with fire are wasted,
 and my castle broken down;
 Ill our walls were guarded
 while we from home were gone:

===Chinese===
「我的国土遭战火焚毁，
我的城堡已破败坍塌；
我们不在家的日子里，
城墙守御得太不像话：

===Original===
 Foes have seized my daughter;
 my kin in death are sleeping;
 My trusty men are slaughtered
 to whom I left my land and name in keeping.”

===Chinese===
仇敌掳走了我的女儿；
我的亲族长眠不起；
我托付国土名声的忠勇部众，
也已横遭屠戮。」

===Original===
Herwic now was weeping,
 in his eyes the tear-drops stood;
 Wet were the eyes of Hettel,
 and fast they overflowed:

===Chinese===
赫尔维克此刻落泪，
泪珠在他眼中盈盈欲坠；
黑特尔的双眼也湿了，
泪水滚滚难止：

===Original===
 So it was with others,
 at seeing them thus weeping;
 Every one was sorrowful
 who, near the king, his faith to him was keeping.

===Chinese===
旁人见他们这般哭泣，
也同样悲从中来；
凡在王驾之前
矢忠相随的人，无不伤怀。

===Original===
Then said the aged Wâ-te:
 “Further of this say nought.
 For all the woe and losses
 these friends to us have brought,

===Chinese===
于是年迈的瓦特（Wâ-te）说道：
「这些事不必再提。
这班朋友带给我们的
种种祸殃与损失，

===Original===
 Soon will we repay them,
 and we shall yet be gladdened;
 Ludwig’s kin and Hartmut’s
 shall at our hands for this erelong be saddened.”

===Chinese===
我们很快便要偿还；
届时我们且自欢欣：
路德维希和哈特穆特的亲族
不久便要因我们之手悲苦难禁。」

===Original===
Hettel asked in wonder:
 “How can that be done?”
 To him old Wâ-te answered:
 “ ’Tis best that peace be won

===Chinese===
黑特尔诧异地问道：
「这怎么能办到？」
老瓦特回答他说：
「眼下最好是先把和局促成——

===Original===
 Now with the king of Moorland,
 with whom we yet are warring;
 Our men, who here besiege him,
 to seek for fair Gudrun we may then be sparing.”

===Chinese===
即刻同摩尔兰之王讲和，
我们还在同他交战；
届时围城的这班人马，
便可抽去寻访美丽的古德伦。」

===Original===
Wise was the aged Wâ-te,
 the words he spake were meet:
 “To-morrow morning early,
 let us with Siegfried treat;

===Chinese===
年迈的瓦特见识高明，
出言句句妥帖：
「明天一大早，
我们就去与西格弗里德议和；

===Original===
 And we ought so to bear us
 that he shall well be knowing
 That, should we not allow it,
 he with his men can ne’er be homeward going.”

===Chinese===
我们的行事还须教他
心里看得清清楚楚：
若非我们放行，
他和他的人马休想回乡一步。」

===Original===
Then said the daring Herwic:
 “Wâ-te has spoken right;
 To-day must you be thinking
 how, with the morrow’s light,

===Chinese===
于是刚勇的赫尔维克说道：
「瓦特说得对；
今天你们就该筹划，
待明日天光一亮，

===Original===
 You all before the foeman
 may show a warlike bearing:
 It gives me pain that women
 should make us leave our siege, and hence be faring.”

===Chinese===
你们众人如何在仇敌面前
摆出威武的阵仗：
叫我痛心的是，竟为了妇人
教我们撤了围城、奔赴远方。」

===Original===
Then they got together
 horses and clothes with speed;
 Unto the words of Wâ-te
 they readily gave heed.

===Chinese===
于是他们火速
备齐了鞍马与行装；
对瓦特的一番言语，
他们欣然遵行。

===Original===
 When the day was dawning,
 they again were striving
 ’Gainst those from Abakia.
 Great praise for this were all to them soon giving.

===Chinese===
天色破晓时分，
他们又奋力接战，
对垒阿巴基（Abakia）来的兵马。
众人很快为此对他们交口称赞。

===Original===
On every side, with banners,
 they to the field did throng;
 Many, sound in body,
 there were slain erelong:

===Chinese===
旌旗招展，四面八方，
他们涌向战阵；
多少体魄健壮的好汉，
转眼便殒命沙场：

===Original===
 Wâ-te’s men from Sturmland
 “Nearer! Nearer!” shouted;
 But those they would o’ermaster
 were quicker yet in fight, and nought it booted.

===Chinese===
瓦特麾下施图尔姆兰（Sturmland）的部众
高声呐喊：「逼近！逼近！」
可他们想压倒的对手
出手愈加快捷，喊杀全然无用。

===Original===
Soon the knightly Irold,
 over the edge of his shield,
 Called out, “Men of Moorland,
 to peace with us will you yield?

===Chinese===
不多时，骑士伊罗尔德（Irold）
越过盾沿
高声喊道：「摩尔兰的众将士，
可愿俯就与我们言和？

===Original===
 King Hettel bids us ask you,
 will you this be choosing?
 Your lands so far are lying,
 that you your goods and men will else be losing.”

===Chinese===
黑特尔王命我们动问，
你们可愿作此抉择？
你们的国土远隔重洋，
再打下去人马辎重终将折损。」

===Original===
Siegfried, lord of Moorland,
 answered to him thus:
 “Would you for peace have pledges,
 then win the fight o’er us;

===Chinese===
摩尔兰之主西格弗里德
如此回答他：
「你们若要和平的凭据，
那就先在战斗中赢过我们；

===Original===
 With no one will I bargain
 for aught my name may lessen:
 If you think to overcome us,
 you will the more by this your losses hasten.”

===Chinese===
我决不与任何人交易，
接受任何有损我声名的条件：
你们若自恃能胜过我们，
只会加速折损自己的人马。」

===Original===
Then spake the knightly Fru-te:
 “If help to us you’ll give,
 And pledge your word to do it,
 your stronghold you may leave

===Chinese===
于是骑士弗鲁特（Fru-te）说道：
「你若肯向我们施以援手，
并立誓践约，
便可撤出你的堡垒，

===Original===
 And go from my master’s kingdom,
 without more bloody fighting.”
 The Moors from Karadie
 on this stretched forth the hand, their faith thus plighting.

===Chinese===
离开我主上的国土，
不必再多一场血战。」
卡拉迪（Karadie）来的摩尔人
闻言伸出了手，就此立誓为凭。

===Original===
There came to strife a stand-still,
 this I for truth may say.
 The glad and happy warriors
 met that selfsame day;

===Chinese===
干戈就此止息，
我此言句句属实。
那班欢欣鼓舞的武士
当天便相聚一处；

===Original===
 Those who erst were foemen
 their help to each other granted.
 They both had quenched their hatred;
 to fight the Normans now was all they wanted.

===Chinese===
昔日的仇敌
彼此伸出了援手。
双方的仇恨俱已消解；
如今一心只想同诺曼底人交手。

===Original===
Then to Siegfried of Moorland
 at once King Hettel told
 All the heavy tidings
 that he in his breast did hold;

===Chinese===
于是黑特尔王当即
向摩尔兰的西格弗里德
倾吐了他胸中
郁结的全部沉重音讯；

===Original===
 He pledged to him his friendship,
 so long as he was living,
 If Hartmut’s foul misdoing
 now to repay, his help he would be giving.

===Chinese===
他向他许下情谊，
终生不渝——
只要他肯出手相助，
向哈特穆特的卑劣行径复仇。

===Original===
To him the lord of Alzabie,
 the Moorish Siegfried said:
 “Knew we where to find them,
 they should our coming dread.”

===Chinese===
阿尔扎比（Alzabie）之主、
摩尔人西格弗里德对他说：
「只要知道上哪儿寻他们，
管叫他们畏惧我们的到来。」

===Original===
 The aged Wâ-te answered:
 “I can show you nearly
 Their path across the water:
 and we perhaps on the sea may meet them early.”

===Chinese===
年迈的瓦特答道：
「我能大致给你们指明
他们渡海的航线：
或许我们还能在海上及早截住他们。」

===Original===
Then to them all spake Hettel:
 “Where can ships be sought?
 And, if I wish to harm them,
 how bring my wish about?

===Chinese===
于是黑特尔对众人说：
「哪里能寻得船只？
我若想加害他们，
这心愿又如何落实？

===Original===
 I might at home make ready
 within their lands to seek them,
 And there, when I had found them,
 my anger for my wrongs should quick o’ertake them.”

===Chinese===
我可在国内整备人马，
前去他们的国土搜寻；
一旦寻见了他们，
我雪恨的怒火便会迅速降临。」

===Original===
To him then said old Wâ-te:
 “In this I can help you still;
 God is ever mighty
 to do whate’er he will.

===Chinese===
于是老瓦特对他说：
「这事我还能助你一臂；
上帝永远大能，
凡他愿意的，无不成就。

===Original===
 I know within these borders
 now are lying near us
 Well-made ships full seventy;
 filled with food, these barks from the sands will bear us.

===Chinese===
我知道就在这方边境之内，
离我们不远的地方，
泊着整整七十条坚固好船；
满载粮秣，能载我们离开沙岸。

===Original===
“In them have wandering pilgrims
 sailed the waters o’er:
 Their ships, whatever happens,
 we must seize upon the shore;

===Chinese===
「驾着这些船漂洋过海的，
是云游四方的朝圣的旅人：
无论如何，他们的船，
我们都必须在岸边夺下；

===Original===
 The pilgrims must be willing
 that on the sand we leave them,
 Until our Norman foemen
 make good our wrongs, or we again shall brave them.”

===Chinese===
朝圣的旅人们只得甘心
被我们留在沙滩，
直到诺曼底仇敌偿还我们的损失，
否则我们再与他们决战。」

===Original===
At once old Wâ-te started,
 no longer would he wait;
 A hundred knights went with him,
 the others lingered yet.

===Chinese===
老瓦特当即动身，
再不肯多耽搁；
一百名骑士随他同往，
其余的人暂且滞留。

===Original===
 He said he came for buying;
 what could the pilgrims sell him?
 For this men died thereafter,
 and, for himself, but sorry luck befell him.

===Chinese===
他口称是来做买卖的；
朝圣的旅人又能卖他什么？
为此后来死了不少人，
他本人也落得时运不济。

===Original===
On the shore he found the pilgrims﻿—
 this I know is true﻿—
 Fully thirty hundred,
 I ween, and better, too.

===Chinese===
他在岸边找到了朝圣的旅人——
这事我知道确凿为真——
足足三千之众，
我想，只多不少。

===Original===
 To fight were they unready,
 and could not rouse them quickly:
 Nearer came King Hettel,
 and with him led his men, now crowding thickly.

===Chinese===
他们全无应战的准备，
也未能迅速奋起：
黑特尔王步步进逼，
率领人马，黑压压涌来。

===Original===
Their goods the pilgrims guarded,
 yet Wâ-te sent on shore
 All that he had no need for,
 of silver and clothes a store;

===Chinese===
朝圣的旅人守着自家财物，
可瓦特仍把
他用不着的东西卸到岸上：
一批白银与衣装；

===Original===
 But the food was left on shipboard,
 so old Wâ-te chooses:
 He said he should come hereafter,
 and would reward them well for all their losses.

===Chinese===
粮食却留在了船上，
老瓦特自有盘算：
他说日后还会前来，
把他们的一切损失厚加偿还。

===Original===
Sadly mourned the pilgrims,
 for sorest was their need;
 But for all they said old Wâ-te
 cared not a crust of bread:

===Chinese===
朝圣的旅人哀哀悲叹，
他们的困苦深重已极；
可任凭他们如何陈说，老瓦特
连一块面包皮也不放在眼里：

===Original===
 The bold, unyielding warrior,
 stern and never smiling,
 Said: “Both ships and flatboats
 they to leave to him must now be willing.”

===Chinese===
这大胆不屈的武夫，
冷峻严酷、从不露笑，
说道：「大船连同平底船，
他们如今只得甘愿留给他。」

===Original===
Hettel recked but little
 if ever they sailed again
 Over the sea with their crosses:
 then he took of their men

===Chinese===
他们日后可还能
携着十字架重渡沧海——
黑特尔全不挂心：
于是他带走他们的人，

===Original===
 Five hundred at least, or over,
 the best they had among them;
 Of these to the Hegeling kingdom
 few came back, from the death that overhung them.

===Chinese===
至少五百，只多不少，
尽是他们中间的精粹；
这些人得以重返黑格林王国的
寥寥无几——死亡笼罩着他们。

===Original===
I know not whether Hettel
 atoned for his evil deed
 Done to these poor pilgrims,
 that made their hearts to bleed,

===Chinese===
我不知黑特尔后来
可曾为自己的恶行
向这些可怜的朝圣的旅人赎补——
那恶行害得他们心头泣血，

===Original===
 And, in a far-off kingdom,
 rent their band, to their sorrow.
 I ween the God in heaven
 saw the wrong, and his anger showed on the morrow.

===Chinese===
又在遥远的国度
生生扯散他们的旅队，令他们悲苦。
我想，天上的上帝
已看见这冤情，翌日便昭示了震怒。

===Original===
King Hettel and his followers
 met with a kindly breeze,
 And now their way were taking
 quickly across the seas;

===Chinese===
黑特尔王与他的部众
遇上了一阵和畅的顺风，
此刻他们正取道
迅速横渡重洋；

===Original===
 Seeking for their foemen,
 they sailed far over the water,
 Wherever they might find them,
 longing to show their wrath, and bent on slaughter.

===Chinese===
一路搜寻仇敌，
他们在水面上航行了很远，
无论在哪里撞见对方，
都渴望一泄怒火，存心大开杀戒。

## Tale XVII How Hettel Came to the Wulpensand in Search of His Daughter / 第十七诗章 黑特尔如何来到武尔彭沙洲寻找他的女儿

===Original===
Ludwig and Hartmut, on their way to Normandy with Gudrun, stop at a desert island, called Wulpensand; Hettel and Herwic, in pursuit, reach the island, and a battle ensues.

===Chinese===
路德维希（Ludwig）与哈特穆特（Hartmut）携古德伦（Gudrun）在返回诺曼底的途中，泊留在一片名叫武尔彭沙洲（Wulpensand）的荒岛上；黑特尔（Hettel）与赫尔维克（Herwic）衔尾追来，抵达该岛，一场大战随即展开。

===Original===
Ludwig, king of the Normans,
 and Hartmut, too, his son,
 Now, with all their followers,
 far away had gone,

===Chinese===
诺曼人之王路德维希，
连同他的儿子哈特穆特，
此刻已偕全部人马
远走他方，

===Original===
 And on a lone, wild seashore,
 after their toil, were resting.
 Though many there were gathered,
 yet little happiness they then were tasting.

===Chinese===
在一处孤寂荒凉的海滨，
劳顿之余就地歇息。
尽管聚集了众多人马，
彼时却尝不到几分欢愉。

===Original===
’Twas on a broad, low island,
 hight the Wulpensand,
 That now the brave King Ludwig,
 and they of the Norman land,

===Chinese===
那是一座开阔低平的岛屿，
名唤武尔彭沙洲；
勇敢的路德维希国王
与诺曼底来的部众，

===Original===
 Shelter for men and horses
 had found unto their liking;
 But a doom to them most woeful
 erelong must come, instead of the rest they were seeking.

===Chinese===
就在那里为人员马匹
觅得了中意的栖身之处；
但一桩对他们至惨的厄运
不久必将降临，取代他们所寻的安歇。

===Original===
The very high-born maidens,
 torn from the Hegeling land,
 Had been led out, and wandered
 along the barren sand;

===Chinese===
那些出身高贵的少女，
被从黑格林人（Hegeling）的国土掳离，
给带了出来，沿着贫瘠的沙地
徘徊游荡；

===Original===
 So far as ’twas allowed them
 to show their feelings freely,
 They who had been stolen
 in sadness wept before the foeman daily.

===Chinese===
只消她们获准
得以自由流露心怀，
这些遭劫夺的少女
便日日在仇敌跟前哀伤垂泪。

===Original===
Fires upon the seashore
 were seen on every side;
 The men from far-off Normandy
 were thinking there to abide.

===Chinese===
海岸的每一面
都望得见生起的营火；
远自诺曼底而来的人马
打算就在此地驻留。

===Original===
 Gladly with the maidens
 would they seven days have rested,
 And there have made them lodgings;
 but every hope of this erelong was blasted.

===Chinese===
他们本愿伴着众少女
在此安歇七日，
就地筑起居所；
但这指望不久便尽成泡影。

===Original===
While on this isle forsaken
 Hartmut now must stay,
 Loth were he and his followers
 the hope to put away,

===Chinese===
而哈特穆特如今
必须留在这座被弃的荒岛，
他与部众都老大不情愿
舍掉那份指望，

===Original===
 Which till now they fostered,
 that they for rest might tarry
 Throughout a week in the shelter
 whither they the maidens fair did carry.

===Chinese===
那指望他们培育至今：
盼能在这处藏身之地
歇息整整一周，
他们正是携着姣好的少女们来到那里。

===Original===
It was from far-off Matelan
 that Ludwig and his band
 The fair Gudrun had taken
 unto this lonely strand;

===Chinese===
正是从遥远的马特兰（Matelan），
路德维希和他的一伙
把美丽的古德伦
掳到了这片孤寂的海滨；

===Original===
 Nor felt they now uneasy
 lest to their hidden dwelling
 Wâ-te them should follow,
 and never harm from him were they foretelling.

===Chinese===
他们此刻也毫不忧惧
瓦特（Wâ-te）会寻踪而至
他们藏匿的居处，
从不曾料想他会带来伤损。

===Original===
Now saw King Ludwig’s sailors,
 tossing on the wave,
 A ship with sails the richest.
 To the king they warning gave;

===Chinese===
这时路德维希国王的水手们
望见波涛颠簸之中
一艘帆篷最为华美的船，
便向国王发出警报；

===Original===
 But when ’twas seen by Hartmut,
 and others with him standing,
 That on the sails were crosses,
 they said these must be pilgrims, bent on landing.

===Chinese===
可待哈特穆特
和侍立在他身旁的人望见
帆上绘着十字，
便说这必是执意登岸的朝圣的旅人。

===Original===
On the waters floating
 three good ships were seen,
 With new and well-made flatboats;
 they bore across the main

===Chinese===
水面上漂浮着
望见三艘上好的船，
带着崭新的精工平底艇；
它们载人横渡了大海，

===Original===
 Those who on their clothing
 never yet wore crosses,
 Their love to God thus showing.
 The Normans must from them meet heavy losses.

===Chinese===
船中之人从不曾
在衣裳上佩缀十字，
如此显明对上帝的爱。
诺曼人注定要吃他们的大亏。

===Original===
As they the shore were nearing,
 one on the ships might see
 Helmets brightly shining.
 No more from care were free

===Chinese===
当船只渐近海岸，
船上的人便望得见
头盔熠熠生辉。
从此再难得安枕无忧的，

===Original===
 King Ludwig and his kinsmen,
 and harm their fears foreboded:
 “Look there!” then shouted Hartmut;
 “with grimmest foes of mine these ships are loaded.”

===Chinese===
是路德维希国王与他的亲族，
他们的疑惧预感着祸殃：
「看那儿！」哈特穆特随即高喊；
「这些船上载满了我最狰狞的仇敌。」

===Original===
The ships were turned so quickly
 that now men loudly heard
 Rudders strained and cracking,
 held by those who steered.

===Chinese===
船身调转得那般迅疾，
人们高声听得见
掌舵者手中紧握的船舵
绷紧迸裂之声。

===Original===
 Both the young and aged,
 who on the sea-sands rested,
 Were indeed bewildered
 when to spring on shore the foeman hasted.

===Chinese===
在沙滩上歇息的
老少人等，
见仇敌奔跃抢登岸来，
果然尽都惊惶失措。

===Original===
Ludwig and young Hartmut
 their shields in hand now bore.
 For them it had been easier
 to reach their homes once more

===Chinese===
路德维希与年轻的哈特穆特
此刻手中已擎起盾牌。
对他们来说，本可以
更轻易地重返家园，

===Original===
 If they had not too freely
 their rest on the island taken:
 They had falsely reckoned
 that Hettel had now no friends, and was all forsaken.

===Chinese===
若不是他们在岛上
歇息得太过放任：
他们错打了算盘，
以为黑特尔如今再无友援、已尽遭背弃。

===Original===
Ludwig called out loudly
 to all his trusty men,
 (He thought it child’s play only
 that he before had seen,)

===Chinese===
路德维希放声高呼，
招呼全体心腹之士，
（他只当先前所见
不过是儿戏一场，）

===Original===
 “Now with worthy foemen
 must I, at length, be striving!
 He shall be the richer
 who ’neath my flag his help to me is giving.”

===Chinese===
「如今我终于
要同配得上的敌手较量！
凡在我旗下
出力助我者，必得厚赏。」

===Original===
Soon was Hartmut’s banner
 raised upon the shore.
 The ships had now come nearer;
 with spears the Normans bore

===Chinese===
不久，哈特穆特的战旗
便在岸上升起。
船只此刻靠得更近；
诺曼人挺着长矛，

===Original===
 To reach the foe were easy
 from where they now were waiting:
 I ween the aged Wâ-te
 was ready with his shield, the foeman meeting.

===Chinese===
从他们此刻候敌之处
扑向敌阵本非难事：
我料那年迈的瓦特
早已挺盾而立，专候来敌。

===Original===
Ne’er before so grimly
 did champions guard their land.
 Boldly the Hegeling warriors
 nearer pressed to the strand;

===Chinese===
壮士们守卫疆土，
从未这般凶悍。
黑格林人的武士
奋勇迫近海滩；

===Original===
 Soon they met the Normans
 with sword and spear, undaunted;
 Blows they freely bartered:
 such bargains cheaply given no more they wanted.

===Chinese===
不多时他们便与诺曼人
刀矛相接，全无惧色；
打击你来我往，出手慷慨：
这等贱售的买卖，他们再不想做。

===Original===
Everywhere the Hegelings
 sprang upon the shore.
 After a wind from the hill-tops
 was never seen before

===Chinese===
黑格林人
跃上了各处岸滩。
任凭山巅劲风过后，
也从未见过

===Original===
 Snow so thickly whirling
 as spears from hands that threw them:
 Though they had done it gladly
 idle it were to shun the strokes that slew them.

===Chinese===
雪花旋舞得这般稠密，
恰似脱手纷飞的长矛：
纵然乐得闪避，
要躲开那些致命的劈击也是枉然。

===Original===
Thick fly the spears on both sides:
 the time but slowly goes,
 Till they on the beach are standing.
 Quickly on his foes

===Chinese===
长矛在两侧密集纷飞：
时光只是缓缓流逝，
直到他们站定滩头。
迅即向仇敌扑去的，

===Original===
 Sprang the aged Wâ-te,
 just as they were nearing;
 His mood was of the grimmest,
 and soon they saw what mind he now was bearing.

===Chinese===
正是年迈的瓦特，
正当敌军逼近之时；
他杀性狰狞至极，
众人很快便看清了他此刻的心肠。

===Original===
Ludwig, king of the Normans,
 then at Wâ-te ran,
 And hurled a spear well sharpened
 against the brave old man.

===Chinese===
诺曼人之王路德维希
径直朝瓦特冲去，
掷出一杆磨得锋利的长矛，
直取那勇敢的老人。

===Original===
 The shaft, in splinters shattered,
 high thro’ air went crashing,
 For Ludwig drove it bravely;
 soon to the fight came Wâ-te’s kinsmen dashing.

===Chinese===
矛杆迸碎成屑，
高高地呼啸着掠过半空，
只因路德维希掷得骁勇；
不多时瓦特的亲族也飞驰赴援。

===Original===
With a heavy stroke, old Wâ-te
 Ludwig’s helmet cut;
 The edge of the sword he wielded
 the head of his foeman smote,

===Chinese===
老瓦特挥起一记重击，
劈开了路德维希的头盔；
他掌中长剑的锋刃
斫在仇敌的头顶，

===Original===
 Who beneath his breastplate
 a shirt of silk was wearing;
 (In Abalie ’twas woven;)
 were it not for this, his end he must be nearing.

===Chinese===
那人胸甲之下
正穿着一袭丝绸衬衣；
（那是在阿巴利（Abalie）织成的；）
若非有它，他的死期便已在眼前。

===Original===
Hardly from him could Ludwig
 with life and limb go free;
 The spot he would fain be leaving,
 for Wâ-te was ill to see

===Chinese===
路德维希好不容易
才从对方手里保得性命肢体；
他巴不得逃离那处地方，
只因瓦特怒起来模样骇人，

===Original===
 When he was roused to anger,
 and to win the day was trying:
 Struck by his hand were many,
 who, brave in warfare, now on the field lay dying.

===Chinese===
每当他被激得大怒、
正奋力要赢下此战之时：
他亲手击倒的不可胜数——
纵是善战的勇士，此刻也横卧沙场垂死。

===Original===
Irold and young Hartmut
 each on the other sprang:
 On either side their weapons
 on the foeman’s helmet rang;

===Chinese===
伊罗尔德（Irold）与年轻的哈特穆特
彼此扑击：
两人各自的兵器
在仇敌的头盔上铿然震响；

===Original===
 Throughout the throng of fighters,
 all could hear it loudly;
 For bold in war was Irold,
 and Hartmut, too, was brave, and bore him proudly.

===Chinese===
响声贯满酣战的人群，
人人都听得真切；
伊罗尔德临阵骁猛，
哈特穆特亦勇敢，气概轩昂。

===Original===
Herwic from the Sealands,
 a warrior strong and good,
 Could not reach the landing,
 but leaped into the flood,

===Chinese===
西兰（Sealand）来的赫尔维克，
一位刚强良善的勇士，
赶不及凑上登陆之处，
便纵身跃入洪流，

===Original===
 And in the waves was standing,
 up to his shoulders hidden.
 Soon to his cost was he learning
 how hard a task it is to win a maiden.

===Chinese===
在浪涛中屹立，
深没至肩。
他很快便自付代价地领教了：
赢得一位少女是何等艰难之事。

===Original===
They the shore who guarded
 their foemen thought to drown
 While in the waters struggling.
 Shafts at them were thrown,

===Chinese===
那些把守海岸的人
打算趁仇敌
在水中挣扎时把他们淹毙。
矛杆朝他们掷去，

===Original===
 And many on them broken;
 but they, their foes now seeking,
 Soon the sands were treading,
 and many a knight his wrath on them was wreaking.

===Chinese===
许多矛杆在他们身上断折；
但他们既已寻上仇敌，
不多时便踏住了沙地，
许多骑士把满腔怒火发泄在他们身上。

===Original===
Ere they had reached the shoreland,
 one saw the watery flood
 Dyed by the killed and wounded,
 in hue as red as blood;

===Chinese===
不等他们抵达滩头，
人们便望见那片水泽
被死伤者染就，
色如鲜血一般赤红；

===Original===
 Everywhere, so widely
 the reddened waves were flowing,
 One could not shoot beyond them,
 how far soe’er he might his spear be throwing.

===Chinese===
四面八方，赤染的波涛
漫流得那样宽广，
任凭把长矛掷出多远，
谁也无法投越它们。

===Original===
Heavier toil and losses
 heroes never found,
 And never so many warriors
 lay trampled on the ground:

===Chinese===
这般沉重的苦斗与折损，
英雄们从未遭遇，
也从未有这么多武士
被践踏在地：

===Original===
 Enough were they for a kingdom
 who lay, unwounded, dying.
 The Normans who o’erthrew them,
 on all sides too, I ween, in death were lying.

===Chinese===
那些不带创伤、
奄奄待毙者，足以充盈一国。
而击倒他们的诺曼人，
我料想，也同样倒毙在四面八方。

===Original===
It was to save his daughter
 that there King Hettel fought,
 And all his kinsmen with him.
 On every side were wrought,

===Chinese===
黑特尔国王在那里鏖战，
正是为了救回女儿，
全体亲族随他一同出战。
四面八方都酿成了

===Original===
 By him and those who helped him,
 havoc and bitter sorrow.
 Dead on the Wulpensand
 were many bodies found before the morrow.

===Chinese===
由他和他麾下的援手
酿成的浩劫与惨痛悲怆。
天明之前，武尔彭沙洲上
已寻获了众多的尸身。

===Original===
Unto their lords all faithful,
 they strove upon the sand﻿—
 Alike the men of Normandy
 and they of the Hegeling land.

===Chinese===
他们对各自的主上
无不忠贞，在沙洲上拼死力战——
无论是诺曼底的人马，
还是黑格林国土的部众。

===Original===
 Warriors brave from Denmark
 fought with matchless daring;
 He ne’er should wait their onset
 who much for his welfare or his life was caring.

===Chinese===
丹麦来的勇武战士
厮杀得胆气无双；
但凡珍重自身性命福祉者，
绝不肯坐待他们掩杀过来。

===Original===
Morunc and with him Ortwin
 boldly held their ground,
 And for themselves won honor;
 nowhere could be found

===Chinese===
莫伦克（Morunc）与奥特温（Ortwin）
并肩牢牢守住阵地，
为自己赢得了荣誉；
普天之下再找不出

===Original===
 Men who greater slaughter
 wrought, with hearts undaunted:
 The heroes twain, with their followers,
 gave full many wounds, with spears well planted.

===Chinese===
杀伐更重、而心魂
毫无畏怯的战士：
这对英雄偕同部众，
长矛扎得结实，赠出的创伤无数。

===Original===
Proudly the men from Moorland,
 as I have heard it said,
 When from their ships they landed,
 the way to the foemen led.

===Chinese===
摩尔兰（Moorland）来的战士意气轩昂，
如我听闻的那般，
他们一下船登岸，
便径直向仇敌杀去。

===Original===
 Hettel hoped, in his struggle,
 help from them to be gaining,
 For they were daring fighters:
 one saw the blood beneath their helmets raining.

===Chinese===
黑特尔指望在这场苦斗中
得到他们的援助，
因为他们是敢死的勇士：
人们看得见血从头盔下如雨倾落。

===Original===
How could he who led them
 have braver or bolder been?
 That day he dimmed with life-blood
 many breastplates’ sheen;

===Chinese===
统领他们的那人，
又怎能更勇更悍？
那一天，他以性命之血染晦了
许多副胸甲的光泽；

===Original===
 Siegfried it was, unyielding
 in storm of battle ever.
 How could the Danish Fru-te,
 or even Wâ-te the old, have shown them braver?

===Chinese===
那正是西格弗里德（Siegfried），
在大战风暴中永不退让。
丹麦的弗鲁特（Fru-te）、
就连老瓦特，又怎比他们更英勇？

===Original===
Thickly hurled were lances,
 hither and thither thrown:
 Ortwin, with his followers,
 in hopeful mood came on;

===Chinese===
长矛密密地掷出，
四处横飞：
奥特温偕其部众，
怀昂扬之志推进；

===Original===
 Helmets that day he shattered,
 blows upon them dealing.
 Gudrun was bitterly weeping:
 her women, too, were deepest sorrow feeling.

===Chinese===
那一天他击碎了头盔，
劈击连连落在盔上。
古德伦悲恸哭泣：
她的侍女们也沉浸在至深的哀伤里。

===Original===
The strife, on both sides, lasted
 throughout the livelong day;
 Longing to reach each other,
 they crowded to the fray.

===Chinese===
这场鏖战，双方
缠斗了整整一日；
彼此渴望交手，
人马涌向战团。

===Original===
 There to knights and warriors
 must the fight go badly,
 Where the friends of Hettel
 to win his daughter back were striving gladly.

===Chinese===
在那里，骑士与武士
战况必然凶险，
因为黑特尔的朋友们
正欣然奋力，要夺回他的女儿。

===Original===
The evening sun sank lower;
 and for King Hettel now
 His losses grew the greater.
 King Ludwig’s men, I trow,

===Chinese===
夕阳愈沉愈低；
此刻黑特尔国王的
损失愈见惨重。
路德维希国王的人马，我确知，

===Original===
 Did their best in fighting,
 but could not flee the slaughter;
 Their foes they wounded deeply,
 and guarded thus Gudrun from those who sought her.

===Chinese===
个个竭力死战，
却无法从屠戮中脱身；
他们把仇敌创得深重，
就这样守住了古德伦，不落进寻她之人手中。

===Original===
The strife began at morning;
 by night alone ’twas stopped,
 And steadily had lasted;
 they ne’er their weapons dropped.

===Chinese===
这场争斗自清晨打响；
唯独入夜方才止息，
始终鏖战不息；
他们从不曾撒手兵器。

===Original===
 The old and young together
 gained no shame in fighting.
 Now the brave King Hettel
 forward pressed, the king of the Normans meeting.

===Chinese===
老将与少壮一同
上阵，未尝蒙羞。
此刻勇敢的黑特尔国王
奋然进逼，直取那诺曼人之王。

## Tale XVIII How Ludwig Slew Hettel, and Stole Away in the Night / 第十八诗章 路德维希如何诛杀黑特尔，并趁夜潜逃

===Original===
Ludwig, king of the Normans, slays Hettel, and his army steals away with the maidens in the night. The Hegelings discover their departure, bury the dead, and erect a hospital and cloister in their memory.

===Chinese===
诺曼人之王路德维希（Ludwig）诛杀了黑特尔（Hettel），他的大军趁夜携少女们悄然遁走。黑格林人（Hegelings）发觉他们已去，埋葬死者，并建起一座济贫院与修道院以资纪念。

===Original===
High in hand their weapons
 Hettel and Ludwig bore﻿—
 Well had they been sharpened.
 Soon each knew the more

===Chinese===
黑特尔与路德维希
把兵刃高高擎起——
刀锋磨得锐利，
不多时各自便看清

===Original===
 Who was now his foeman,
 such strength they both were showing.
 Ludwig slew King Hettel;
 and out of this our mournful tale is growing.

===Chinese===
对面的敌手是何等人物，
两人都使出浑身解数。
路德维希诛杀了黑特尔国王；
我们这支哀歌，便由此生发。

===Original===
When the lord of Matelan
 upon the field lay slain,
 Soon ’twas told to his daughter:
 loudly then began

===Chinese===
马特兰（Matelan）之主
阵亡于沙场之上，
死讯不久便传到他女儿耳中：
古德伦（Gudrun）随即放声

===Original===
 Gudrun to mourn her father,
 so did many a maiden;
 Not one could stop her wailing:
 friends and foes alike were sorrow-laden.

===Chinese===
哀悼起自己的父亲，
众多少女也一同悲泣：
没有一人止得住她的哭号：
友与仇，无人不心怀哀恸。

===Original===
Soon as the grim old Wâ-te
 the death of the king did know,
 He cried and roared in anger.
 Like to the evening glow,

===Chinese===
狰狞的老瓦特（Wâ-te）
一得知国王的死讯，
登时狂呼怒吼。
恰似晚霞烧红了天，

===Original===
 Now were helmets blazing,
 beneath the strokes quick given
 By him and all his followers,
 who by their loss were unto madness driven.

===Chinese===
顶顶头盔此刻熊熊生光，
尽在他与全体部众
疾风般挥落的击打之下——
他们都因这一殇而狂乱若痴。

===Original===
However hard their fighting,
 how could it bring them good?
 Drenched was all the island
 with many knights’ hot blood.

===Chinese===
他们打得再苦再狠，
又怎能讨得好处？
整座海岛都浸透了
无数骑士滚烫的热血。

===Original===
 Not yet the Hegeling warriors
 to think of peace were ready;
 Away from the Wulpensand
 they only wished to bring Gudrun, their lady.

===Chinese===
黑格林人的武士
尚无意念及言和；
他们一心只想把女主人古德伦
带离这武尔彭沙洲（Wulpensand）。

===Original===
In stormy fight the Waal men
 bewreked the death of the king;
 To many a fighting Ortlander
 and hard-pressed Hegeling

===Chinese===
苦战中，瓦尔河（Waal）一带的人
为国王之死复了仇；
对多少浴血奋战的奥特兰（Ortland）人
和苦苦支撑的黑格林人，

===Original===
 Those who came from Denmark
 of friendship gave a token:
 Soon these knights so daring
 found in their hands their trusty weapons broken.

===Chinese===
从丹麦来的人
献上了友情的凭证：
不多时，这些剽悍的骑士
便见手中惯用的兵器纷纷折断。

===Original===
Now to avenge his father
 Ortwin bravely strove:
 Faithful to him did Horant
 and all his followers prove.

===Chinese===
如今奥特温（Ortwin）
奋勇要为父亲复仇：
霍兰特（Horant）与他全体部众
都证明对他忠心耿耿。

===Original===
 Night the field had darkened,
 the light of day was failing;
 Then were given to many
 wounds from which the life-blood fast was welling.

===Chinese===
夜幕罩暗了战场，
白昼的光辉正在消逝；
多少人于是身受重创，
创口里生命之血奔涌不止。

===Original===
Soon, in the dark, on Horant
 a Danish follower sprang;
 The sword that he was holding
 loud on the armor rang:

===Chinese===
黑暗里，一名丹麦随从
猛然扑向霍兰特；
只见他手中挥舞的剑
当啷一声击在甲胄上：

===Original===
 Thinking he was a foeman,
 Horant at once upon him
 Wrought most bitter sorrow:
 a deadly wound by that warrior brave was done him.

===Chinese===
霍兰特只当来的是仇敌，
当下便向他痛下杀手，
铸成至悲的悔恨：
一记致命的创伤，落在了这勇敢武士的身上。

===Original===
When Horant saw that his kinsman
 beneath his blow lay dead,
 Then he bade that his banner
 be borne with his own o’erhead.

===Chinese===
待霍兰特看清那亲族
已倒毙在自己剑下，
便传令把他的军旗
与自己的旗一同高擎。

===Original===
 The voice of him who was dying
 told whose life he had taken
 With his hand so rashly;
 sorely he mourned the friend who never would waken.

===Chinese===
那垂死之人开口的声音，
道出了他一时鲁莽
断送的是谁的性命；
他为此痛悼那再不会醒来的朋友。

===Original===
Loudly called out Herwic:
 “Murder here is done!
 Since we can see no longer,
 and daylight now is gone,

===Chinese===
赫尔维克（Herwic）高声呼喊：
「这里在自相残杀！
既然我们再看不见彼此，
天光又已散尽，

===Original===
 We all shall kill each other,
 friends and foes together.
 If this shall last till morning,
 two may be left to fight, but not another.”

===Chinese===
我们终会互相杀死，
朋友与仇敌搅作一处。
倘若厮杀延续到天明，
至多剩得两人还能相斗，再无第三人。」

===Original===
Where’er they saw old Wâ-te
 on the stormy fighting-ground,
 No one there was willing
 near him to be found;

===Chinese===
在鏖战酣烈的原野上，
人们但见老瓦特所在之处，
谁也不肯
靠近他半步；

===Original===
 No welcome, in his madness,
 was he to any giving:
 Many a foe he wounded,
 and laid on the spot that he would ne’er be leaving.

===Chinese===
他在狂怒之中
对谁也不留情面：
多少仇敌被他杀伤，
击倒在地，教他们永远离不开那片地方。

===Original===
’Twas well the foes were sundered
 until the break of day;
 On either side the foemen
 near each other lay,

===Chinese===
幸而敌对两军
就此分开，直到破晓；
两侧的仇敌
彼此相隔不远地卧着，

===Original===
 Wounded to death or slaughtered.
 Fast the light was waning,
 Not yet the moon was risen,
 and the Hegeling foe the field were nowhere gaining.

===Chinese===
或伤重垂死，或已遭屠戮。
天光迅速黯淡，
月亮还没有升起，
黑格林人的仇敌在战场上寸步未得。

===Original===
The warriors grim, unwillingly,
 to the strife now put a stop;
 The hands of all were weary
 ere they gave the struggle up:

===Chinese===
这些剽悍的武士，老大不情愿，
这才把厮杀止住；
到众人撒手放弃苦斗时，
一双手早已困乏不堪：

===Original===
 But, when the fight was over,
 they near each other loitered.
 Wherever fires were burning,
 for each the other’s shields and helmets glittered.

===Chinese===
可战斗一停，
他们仍在彼此近旁逗留。
但凡有火堆燃起之处，
各人都望见对方盾牌与头盔的闪光。

===Original===
Ludwig then and Hartmut,
 lords of the Norman land,
 Talked aside together.
 Then to his faithful band

===Chinese===
随后路德维希与哈特穆特（Hartmut），
这两位诺曼底之主，
避到一旁密谈。
接着，对着他的亲信部众，

===Original===
 Spake the elder warrior:
 “Why be longer staying
 So near the brave old Wâ-te,
 who all of us is madly bent on slaying?”

===Chinese===
年长的那位武士开言：
「何必再滞留在此，
紧挨着剽悍的老瓦特——
他正发了疯，定要把我们斩尽？」

===Original===
The wily king then bade them:
 “Lie low, and be not seen,
 With your heads upon your bucklers:
 you then must make a din;

===Chinese===
狡黠的国王随即吩咐：
「卧倒，别让人看见，
把头伏在圆盾上，
然后你们只管喧闹作声；

===Original===
 And so the men of the Hegelings
 my plan will not be knowing﻿—
 That, if I now can do it,
 I with you all may hence unseen be going.”

===Chinese===
这样，黑格林人
就识不破我的计谋——
只要此刻办得到，
我就带你们大家悄然离去，无人得见。」

===Original===
Ludwig’s men and kinsmen
 did as he had said:
 They upon their sackbuts
 and trumpets loudly played,

===Chinese===
路德维希的部众与亲族
依言而行：
他们在弯号（sackbut）与铜号上
高声吹奏起来，

===Original===
 As if they, by their prowess,
 the land had gained them wholly.
 Ludwig now to his followers
 showed his crafty plot and cunning fully.

===Chinese===
仿佛凭着他们的骁勇，
已把这地方全然赢到手里。
路德维希就此向部众
亮出了他诡计的全貌。

===Original===
Then were heard, on all sides,
 mingled shouts and cries;
 But wailing from the maidens
 was not allowed to rise:

===Chinese===
于是四面八方
响起混杂的呐喊与喧哗；
但少女们的哀泣
却不许扬起：

===Original===
 All who would not stop it
 were threatened death by drowning﻿—
 To be sunk beneath the waters﻿—
 if they were sobbing heard, or loudly moaning.

===Chinese===
凡不肯止住哭号的，
都被以溺死相威胁——
要把她沉入水底——
只要让人听见抽噎或高声呜咽。

===Original===
Whate’er was owned by the Normans
 now to the ships was ta’en;
 The dead were there left lying,
 e’en where they were slain.

===Chinese===
诺曼人所有的财物
这时都搬上了船；
死者被弃在原地，
就倒在各自丧命之处。

===Original===
 Friends were lost to many
 who, seeking, could not find them:
 So few there were still living,
 that many an empty ship was left behind them.

===Chinese===
许多人失去了同伴，
四下寻觅也找不见：
活着的人所剩无几，
以至于身后抛下许多空船。

===Original===
Thus unbeknown and slyly,
 sailed away o’er the main
 The men of the land of Normandy;
 great was the women’s pain

===Chinese===
就这样无人察觉、鬼鬼祟祟，
诺曼底之地的人
扬帆渡过大海；
妇人们何其伤痛——

===Original===
 From kinsfolk to be sundered,
 and yet to hush their weeping.
 Of this the men knew nothing
 who now upon the Wulpensand were sleeping.

===Chinese===
既要与亲族生生离散，
还得强自忍住啼泣。
此刻酣睡在武尔彭沙洲上的人
对此浑然不知。

===Original===
Before the day was dawning,
 well were on their way
 They whom the Danish warriors
 had thought that morn to slay.

===Chinese===
天色尚未破晓，
有一伙人早已上路——
正是丹麦武士们
本以为那天清晨可加诛戮的人。

===Original===
 Then Wâ-te bade that loudly
 his war-horn should be sounded;
 He was in haste to follow,
 and hoped erelong to fell them, deeply wounded.

===Chinese===
瓦特于是传令，
叫把战号大声吹响；
他急欲随后追赶，
指望不久便将他们重创砍翻。

===Original===
On foot and on their horses,
 the men of the Hegeling land
 All were seen together,
 flocking o’er the sand,

===Chinese===
黑格林之地的人，
或徒步或骑马，
只见他们聚作一处，
黑压压涌过沙地，

===Original===
 To fight the fleeing Normans;
 never in this they rested.
 Ludwig with his followers
 already far upon their way had hasted.

===Chinese===
去追击奔逃的诺曼人；
在这上头他们片刻不停。
而路德维希带着部众
早已遁出老远，一路疾行。

===Original===
Many ships lay empty,
 and clothing there was found;
 All about the Wulpensand
 ’twas scattered o’er the ground;

===Chinese===
许多船只空空如也，
还寻见不少衣物；
武尔彭沙洲上下，
衣物抛撒了一地；

===Original===
 Many weapons also
 were seen, with none to bear them.
 They had overslept their going,
 and never to harm their foes could they come near them.

===Chinese===
还有许多兵器
散见各处，无人执掌。
他们一觉睡过了敌人的离去，
从此再不能近身伤敌分毫。

===Original===
When this was told to Wâ-te,
 with anger he was torn:
 How for the death of Hettel
 he bitterly did mourn!

===Chinese===
这消息一报给瓦特，
他就被怒火撕扯：
他为黑特尔之死
悲恸得何等锥心！

===Original===
 And that on Ludwig’s body
 his wrath he was not wreaking!
 Helmets there lay shattered;
 for this must many a woman’s heart be aching.

===Chinese===
又恨这一腔怒火
没能发泄到路德维希身上！
那里头盔碎裂满地；
为此，多少妇人的心必在隐隐作痛。

===Original===
How gloomily and sadly
 now, in angry mood,
 Ortwin was bewailing
 the loss of his warriors good!

===Chinese===
奥特温此时何等阴郁哀伤，
在愤懑之中
悲悼他失去的
那些勇武的部下！

===Original===
 He said: “Rouse up, my fighters!
 we may perhaps o’ertake them
 Before they leave these waters;
 not far from shore we yet in flight may check them.”

===Chinese===
他说：「奋起吧，我的战士们！
我们或许还能追上他们，
赶在他们驶离这片海域之前；
离岸不远，我们尚能把这伙逃敌截住。」

===Original===
Willingly old Wâ-te
 would his bidding do:
 Fru-te the winds was watching,
 to learn which way they blew.

===Chinese===
老瓦特满口应承，
甘愿听从他的号令：
弗鲁特（Fru-te）观测着风，
看它朝哪一边吹。

===Original===
 Then said he to his kinsmen:
 “What helps it though we hasten?
 Mark what now I tell you:
 the thirty miles they’ve gained we ne’er can lessen.

===Chinese===
接着他对亲族们说道：
「纵使我们疾追，又有何益？
记住我此刻告诉你们的话：
他们已抢得的三十英里，我们再也缩短不了。

===Original===
“Moreover, we of fighters
 have not here enough
 That we in aught can harm them,
 e’en should we now set off:

===Chinese===
「再者，说到战士，
我们在此的人手不够，
纵然立即启程，
也伤不得他们分毫：

===Original===
 Scorn me not,” said Fru-te,
 “and to my words give heeding;
 What more to say is needful?
 Your foes you cannot reach, howe’er you’re speeding.

===Chinese===
莫要轻看我，」弗鲁特说，
「且把我的话听进心里：
还有什么多余可讲？
任你们追赶得再快，也够不着你们的仇敌。

===Original===
“Bid that now the wounded
 upon the ships be laid;
 Then on the field of battle
 let search for the dead be made,

===Chinese===
「传令：即刻把伤者
抬放到船上；
再到战场之上
把死者搜寻一番，

===Original===
 And bid that they be buried
 upon this strand forsaken,
 So friends may rest together;
 this good at least from them should not be taken.”

===Chinese===
并吩咐把他们安葬
在这片荒寂的海滨，
好让朋友们同眠一处；
至少这一件善举，不该从他们身上夺去。」

===Original===
All, standing there together,
 wringing their hands were seen.
 For this one sorrow only,
 would their lot have hapless been﻿—

===Chinese===
只见众人伫立一处，
个个绞扭着双手。
倘若只有这一桩悲恸，
他们的命途也已算不幸——

===Original===
 To lose the youthful maiden,
 Hilda’s lovely daughter.
 How, when they saw her mother,
 if home they went, could news so sad be brought her?

===Chinese===
痛失那如花的少女、
希尔达（Hilda）的爱女。
倘若回到家中、面见她的母亲，
这般凄惨的噩耗叫人如何开口？

===Original===
Then to them said Morunc:
 “Would there were nothing more,
 Beyond our own sad losses,
 for which our hearts are sore!

===Chinese===
这时莫伦克（Morunc）开口：
「但愿除了我们自家的
惨痛死丧之外，
再没有别的伤心事！

===Original===
 Small reward will be given
 for the news we home shall carry,
 That Hettel dead is lying:
 far from Hilda fain would I longer tarry.”

===Chinese===
我们回家要捎的音讯——
说黑特尔已横死在此——
断然换不来什么酬谢：
我倒情愿远远躲开希尔达，在此多留些时日。」

===Original===
Then went the warriors searching
 for the dead upon the sand.
 Those they knew were Christians
 who lay upon the strand,

===Chinese===
于是武士们在沙地上
四处搜寻死者。
凡他们认得是基督徒、
又倒在海滩上的，

===Original===
 As the Sturmisch Wâ-te bade them,
 were all together carried;
 Then both the old and the younger
 chose a spot whereon the dead were buried.

===Chinese===
都遵施图尔姆兰（Sturmisch）人瓦特的吩咐，
一并抬放到一处；
随后老少众人
择定了一块地，把死者安葬。

===Original===
Then said the knight, young Ortwin:
 “Let us bury them here;
 And thought must we be taking
 to build a church full near,

===Chinese===
年轻的骑士奥特温说道：
「就把他们安葬在这里；
我们还须费心
在近旁兴建一座教堂，

===Original===
 That they be not forgotten,
 while this their end is showing.
 For it shall all their kinsmen
 give of their wealth, each one his share bestowing.”

===Chinese===
好教他们不致被遗忘，
只要这终局还在向人昭示。
他们的亲族还应
各捐资财，人人献上一份。」

===Original===
Then spake the Sturmisch Wâ-te:
 “In this thou well hast said;
 We now should sell the horses
 and the clothing of the dead,

===Chinese===
施图尔姆兰人瓦特接着说：
「这话你算说对了；
我们该把死者的马匹
连同他们的衣裳变卖，

===Original===
 Who on the shore are lying;
 so, since their life is ended,
 Shall many poor and needy,
 with the wealth they left, be holpen and befriended.”

===Chinese===
他们正陈尸海滨；
既然性命已了，
就让许多贫穷匮乏的人
靠他们留下的财物，得蒙救济与眷顾。」

===Original===
Then asked the warrior Irold,
if foes who there lay dead
 Should also now be buried,
 or if wolves should on them feed,

===Chinese===
这时武士伊罗尔德（Irold）问道：
那些横尸此地的仇敌，
如今是否也该一体安葬，
还是任凭豺狼撕咬、

===Original===
 And hungry ravens tear them,
 that round their bodies hovered?
 Then to the wise they listened;
 none of the dead were left on the field uncovered.

===Chinese===
任凭盘旋在尸身周遭的
饿鸦去啄食？
众人听从了智者的意见：
死者无一被弃置荒野、暴骨无掩。

===Original===
When now the fight was over,
 and all were free from care,
 Hettel, their king, they buried,
 who for his daughter dear,

===Chinese===
此刻战事已了，
众人都卸下重负；
他们安葬了国王黑特尔——
他为着亲爱的女儿，

===Original===
 Upon this barren seashore,
 e’en unto death had striven.
 To others who had fallen,
 whate’er their land and name, was burial given.

===Chinese===
在这片荒瘠的海滨
直厮杀到生命最后一息。
对其他阵亡的人，
无论来自何方何名，都一体安葬。

===Original===
First, the men from Moorland
 each by himself they laid;
 The same was done for the Hegelings
 found among the dead;

===Chinese===
来自摩尔兰（Moorland）的人，
最先被一一分开安放；
对在死者中寻获的黑格林人，
也照此一一安置；

===Original===
 Unto the Normans, also,
 gave they graves allotted:
 Alone was each one buried,
 if Christian he were or heathen, it nothing booted.

===Chinese===
对诺曼人，同样
划定了各自的墓穴：
人人都是独自下葬，
是基督徒还是异教徒，全不相干。

===Original===
Until six days were over,
 busy were they, at their best,
 And never time were finding
 (for the warriors took no rest)

===Chinese===
直到六天期满，
他们都竭尽全力地忙碌，
始终寻不出空暇——
（因为武士们不肯歇息）——

===Original===
 To ask for dead and dying
 the grace of God in heaven,
 For sins of which they were guilty;
 that they for their misdeeds should be forgiven.

===Chinese===
去为死者与垂死之人
祈求天上上帝的恩宠，
赦免他们身负的罪愆，
好教他们的过恶得蒙宽宥。

===Original===
Saying mass and singing
 were later heard on the strand.
 Never was God so worshipped,
 in any other land,

===Chinese===
后来，海滨之上
响起了诵弥撒与唱圣咏之声。
无论在何方国土，
上帝从未受如此崇奉，

===Original===
 For the dead in stormy fighting.
 Wherever men were lying
 With their death-wounds smitten,
 holy priests they brought to shrive the dying.

===Chinese===
这都是为了风暴之战中的亡者。
但凡有人带着致命的创伤
倒卧之处，
他们都请来圣洁的教士，为垂死者行临终告解。

===Original===
Many there did tarry
 to care for the churchly men.
 A deed of gift was written,
 wherein it could be seen

===Chinese===
许多人留在那里，
照料这些教会中人。
一份捐赠文书写定，
从中可以看清：

===Original===
 How of land to the brothers
 three hundred hides was given.
 Far and wide ’twas bruited,
 that well a godly house was builded, and had thriven.

===Chinese===
赠予修士弟兄的土地，
足有三百海得。
于是风声远播四方：
一座敬神的殿宇已然建成，且日渐兴旺。

===Original===
All who there were mourning
 the loss of friends and kin
 Gave of their wealth a tithing,
 women as well as men,

===Chinese===
所有在那里哀悼
亲友沦亡的人，
无论女男，都捐出
自己财货的什一，

===Original===
 For weal of the souls of any
 whose bodies there lay buried.
 The cloister soon was wealthy,
 by the yield of three hundred hides, through toil unwearied.

===Chinese===
愿凡尸身葬在那里的人，
灵魂都得享福泽。
修道院很快富足起来——
凭三百海得的出产，加上不倦的辛劳。

===Original===
Now may God in his keeping
 have those who there lie dead,
 And the holy men there dwelling.
 Those then homeward sped

===Chinese===
愿上帝把长眠于此的死者、
连同居住其间的圣洁之人，
都收进他的庇护。
随后，那些人便启程还乡——

===Original===
 Who still upon the Wulpensand
 were left among the living;
 After all their sorrows,
 they reached their fatherland, no more in warfare striving.

===Chinese===
他们仍在武尔彭沙洲上、
幸存于世间；
历尽重重悲怆之后，
他们终于抵达故国，从此不再兴兵攻战。

## Tale XIX How the Hegelings Went Home to Their Own Land / 第十九诗章 黑格林人如何归返故土

===Original===
The Hegelings return, and announce to Queen Hilda the death of her husband and their ill-success. They decide to wait till the young knights become old enough for war, and then to make a second campaign against the Normans.

===Chinese===
黑格林人（Hegelings）归来了，向王后希尔达（Hilda）禀报她丈夫的死讯与他们此番出师的失利。他们决意等到年轻的骑士们长成堪战之龄，再向诺曼人发动第二场征讨。

===Original===
The kinsmen of King Hettel
 upon the sands had left
 Many in death’s fast keeping;
 never knights bereft

===Chinese===
黑特尔（Hettel）国王的亲族
把众多同伴留在那片沙洲上，
交由死亡紧紧收执；
从来没有一支骑士

===Original===
 Their homeward way had taken,
 hearts so sorry bringing.
 Thereafter lovely women
 for this, with weeping eyes, their hands were wringing.

===Chinese===
在归乡的路上
怀着这般沉痛的心。
此后，美丽的妇人们
为此泪眼涟涟，绞扭着双手。

===Original===
Ortwin, the knight of Ortland,
 who to the fight had come,
 After such shame and losses,
 back to fair Hilda’s home

===Chinese===
奥特兰（Ortland）的骑士奥特温（Ortwin）
曾赶赴那场鏖战，
蒙受这般耻辱与折损之后，
要回到美丽的希尔达的家园，

===Original===
 Feared to bring these tidings,
 his mother dear to sadden.
 She there was waiting daily,
 hoping her men would bring Gudrun the maiden.

===Chinese===
实在不敢捎回这些音讯，
唯恐教亲爱的母亲伤怀。
她正在那里日日守望，
盼她的儿郎们带回少女古德伦（Gudrun）。

===Original===
Wâ-te, fearing sorely,
 rode to Hilda’s land;
 The others dared not tell her
 of the loss on the Wulpensand.

===Chinese===
瓦特（Wâ-te）满心忧惧，
策马去往希尔达的国度；
其余的人都不敢
向她禀报武尔彭沙洲（Wulpensand）上的折损。

===Original===
 Ill in the storm of fighting,
 his strength her men had warded;
 Not lightly her forgiveness
 he hoped to gain, who thus her lord had guarded.

===Chinese===
在那风暴般的鏖战中，
他曾以勇力护佑她的儿郎；
可这般守护她夫君的人，
不敢指望轻易求得她的宽谅。

===Original===
When the word was spoken
 that Wâ-te near had come,
 At once were men faint-hearted.
 Erewhiles when he came home,

===Chinese===
瓦特已然临近的消息
一经传报，
众人登时心头发怵。
往日他每次还家，

===Original===
 Back from the war-field riding,
 it was with war-horns braying.
 This he did at all times;
 but now they all were still, and nought were saying.

===Chinese===
从战场上纵马而归，
总伴着战号的喧鸣。
他向来回回如此；
可此刻众人一片沉寂，默然无语。

===Original===
“Woe’s me!” said Lady Hilda,
 “what sorrows must we fear?
 The men of the aged Wâ-te
 shattered shields now bear;

===Chinese===
「我真是命苦啊！」希尔达夫人说道，
「还有什么悲伤在等着我们？
年迈的瓦特的部众
如今扛着的是碎裂的盾牌；

===Original===
 Slowly step the horses,
 with armor heavy-loaded.
 Some evil has befallen.
 Oh! say what harm to the king is now forboded?”

===Chinese===
马匹举步迟缓，
驮着沉重的甲胄。
必是大祸临头了。
啊！快说，国王眼下遭的是什么凶兆？」

===Original===
When thus the queen had spoken,
 but little time had passed
 Ere to the aged Wâ-te
 crowds came up in haste,

===Chinese===
王后这话音方落，
没过多久，
便有一群群人急匆匆
涌到年迈的瓦特近前，

===Original===
 Who of friends and kinsfolk
 tidings now were seeking.
 Soon a tale he told them
 with which the hearts of all were well-nigh breaking.

===Chinese===
个个打探着
亲友们的音讯。
他随即讲出一段实情，
听得众人的心几欲碎裂。

===Original===
Thus spake the Sturmisch Wâ-te:
 “Your loss I may not hide,
 Nor falsehood will I tell you;
 all in the fight have died.”

===Chinese===
施图尔姆兰（Sturmisch）人瓦特这样开言：
「你们的损失我无法隐瞒，
我也不会对你们说谎：
他们全都殁于那场战斗。」

===Original===
 The young and old together
 at this with fear were stricken.
 Ne’er was a throng more wretched;
 no other woes could one to theirs e’er liken.

===Chinese===
老老少少听了这话，
无不惊惶失措。
从没有哪群人这般凄惨；
再没有别的苦难能与他们相比。

===Original===
“Alas! my bitter sorrow!”
 said King Hettel’s wife.
 “From me my lord is sundered,
 who there laid down his life,

===Chinese===
「唉，我这锥心的悲痛！」
黑特尔国王的妻子说道。
「我的夫君与我天人永隔，
他在那边捐躯殒命，

===Original===
 The great and mighty Hettel!
 My pride, how is it fallen!
 Lost are child and husband!
 Gudrun I ne’er shall see, from me forever stolen.”

===Chinese===
那伟大而强毅的黑特尔！
我的荣光，就这样陨落了！
女儿与丈夫都已失去！
古德伦我再不得见，她永远被人从我身边夺走。」

===Original===
Then both knights and maidens
 with sharpest woe were torn;
 Their sorrow knew no healing.
 Loudly the queen forlorn

===Chinese===
骑士与少女们
个个被至痛撕扯；
他们的悲伤无从疗慰。
那孤苦无依的王后，

===Original===
 Was heard, throughout the palace,
 for her husband mourning.
 “Ah, wretched me,” cried Hilda,
 “that now to Hartmut’s side the luck is turning!”

===Chinese===
恸悼夫君的声音，
响彻整座宫阙。
「啊，我这苦命的人，」希尔达哭喊，
「如今时运竟转向哈特穆特（Hartmut）那一边！」

===Original===
Then spake the brave old Wâ-te:
 “My lady, end your moan:
 Home are they coming never,
 but when to men are grown

===Chinese===
随后剽悍的老瓦特开言：
「我的夫人，请止住哀恸：
他们永远回不来了，
但待到我们王国里的少年

===Original===
 The youths within our kingdom,
 sad days will have an ending;
 To Ludwig and to Hartmut
 the like we’ll do, our wrath upon them spending.”

===Chinese===
个个长成堂堂男儿，
悲惨的岁月自会了结；
路德维希（Ludwig）与哈特穆特，
我们也要照样回敬，把满腔怒火倾泻到他们头上。」

===Original===
Then quoth the weeping lady:
 “Alas, that I must live!
 Whatever I am owning
 I would most gladly give

===Chinese===
啼泣的夫人接着说道：
「唉，我竟还要苟活于世！
凡我名下所有的财物，
我都甘愿悉数奉送，

===Original===
 Could e’er my wrongs be righted.
 If but this were granted,
 That I, poor God-forsaken,
 might see Gudrun again, naught else were wanted.”

===Chinese===
只要我的冤屈终得昭雪。
但愿上天肯开恩，
让我这被上帝抛弃的可怜人
再见古德伦一面，别无他求。」

===Original===
Old Wâ-te spake to Hilda:
 “Lady, weep no more.
 ’Tis best that we be sending,
 before twelve days are o’er,

===Chinese===
老瓦特对希尔达说道：
「夫人，请莫再哭泣。
我们最好即刻遣人，
赶在十二天期满之前，

===Original===
 To gather all your warriors,
 who will help you gladly
 To plan a raid on the foeman;
 so with the Norman will it yet go badly.”

===Chinese===
把您的武士们尽数召集起来，
他们都乐意为您效命，
共谋一场对仇敌的突袭；
到时候，诺曼人的日子就要不好过了。」

===Original===
He said: “My Lady Hilda,
 list to what befell:
 Erewhile I took from pilgrims
 nine ships, and then set sail:

===Chinese===
他说：「我的夫人希尔达，
且听我说一件往事：
先前我曾从朝圣的旅人手里
夺下九条船，随即扬帆出海：

===Original===
 These should again be given
 to those we ill have treated;
 That when new strifes we’re waging,
 a better luck to us may then be meted.”

===Chinese===
这些船理当归还
给那些受了我们亏待的人；
这样，待我们再启新的战端，
更好的运道才会降到我们头上。」

===Original===
The weeping Hilda answered:
 “ ’Tis best that this be done;
 Ever is it fitting
 that men for misdeeds atone.

===Chinese===
涕泣的希尔达答道：
「此事理当照办；
人为自己的过恶赎罪，
从来都是分所应当。

===Original===
 To steal the goods of pilgrims
 is a sin not lightly shriven:
 For every mark we’ve taken,
 to them three marks of silver shall be given.”

===Chinese===
偷盗朝圣的旅人的财物，
是桩不容轻饶的罪愆：
凡我们取走的每一马克，
都要赔给他们三马克白银。」

===Original===
The ships were brought to the pilgrims,
 as the queen did say;
 Not one there was among them,
 when they sailed away,

===Chinese===
那些船被送还到朝圣的旅人手中，
一如王后的吩咐；
待他们扬帆离去之时，
没有一个人

===Original===
 Who left a curse behind him.
 For wrongs they found a healing;
 And for Hilda, Hagen’s daughter,
 they harbored, when they left, no bitter feeling.

===Chinese===
留下半句咒诅。
他们所受的委屈得到了抚偿；
对哈根（Hagen）之女希尔达，
众人临行时已不再心怀怨怼。

===Original===
Upon the morrow early,
 thither to come was seen
 Herwic, the lord of Sealand;
 soon he found the queen

===Chinese===
次日一早，
有人望见西兰（Sealand）之主
赫尔维克（Herwic）赶到了那里；
很快他便寻见王后——

===Original===
 Weeping for her husband,
 who in death was lying.
 She gave the knight a welcome,
 with hands she ever wrung, and deeply sighing.

===Chinese===
她正为亡故的丈夫
涕泪滂沱。
她接待了这位骑士，
只是不住绞扭双手，深深叹息。

===Original===
Seeing the lady weeping,
 then, too, to weep began
 The young and lordly Herwic;
 soon spake that well-born man:

===Chinese===
眼见夫人恸哭，
年轻而尊贵的赫尔维克
也不禁潸然泪下；
这位高贵的男子随即开言：

===Original===
 “Their lives not all have given,
 who help to you are owing,
 And who would gladly grant it;
 though many by their death their love were showing.

===Chinese===
「并非所有亏欠您援助的人
都已献出性命，
他们依然甘愿为您效力，
尽管许多人已用一死表明了忠爱。

===Original===
“My arm shall never falter,
 nor heart from care be free,
 Till Hartmut feels my anger,
 who stole the maid from me,

===Chinese===
「我的手臂决不迟疑，
我的心也片刻不得安宁，
直到哈特穆特领教我的愤怒——
正是他偷走了我心中的少女，

===Original===
 And dared from home to tear her,
 death to many dealing:
 Soon will I ride to his borders;
 then will I seize and hold his lands and dwelling.”

===Chinese===
正是他斗胆将她从家中劫夺，
害得多少人丧命：
不久我便要挥师直抵他的边境；
那时我要夺下并占据他的国土与家园。」

===Original===
His men, though filled with sorrow,
 rode towards the town,
 Flocking to Matelan castle.
 The queen her hope made known

===Chinese===
他的部众虽满怀悲怆，
仍策马向城中进发，
成群涌向马特兰（Matelan）城堡。
王后道出了她的期望：

===Original===
 That, whatsoe’er might happen,
 their fealty would not weaken;
 And, though the worst befell them,
 that she by them would never be forsaken.

===Chinese===
无论日后发生什么，
他们的忠义都不会动摇；
纵使大难临到他们头上，
她也永远不会被他们抛弃。

===Original===
To her the men from Friesland
 and those from Sturmland went,
 And from the Danish kingdom
 were warriors likewise sent;

===Chinese===
弗里西亚（Friesland）来的人
与从施图尔姆兰（Sturmland）来的人
都投到她的身边，
丹麦王国也同样遣来了武士；

===Original===
 The knights of Morunc also,
 from the land of Waleis riding,
 Thither came with the Hegelings,
 to where the fair Queen Hilda was abiding.

===Chinese===
还有莫伦克（Morunc）的骑士们，
他们从瓦莱斯（Waleis）之地驰来，
与黑格林人一同赶到
美丽的王后希尔达驻留的地方。

===Original===
Forthwith there came from Ortland,
 Ortwin, Hilda’s son;
 Then mourned they, as was fitting,
 his father dead and gone.

===Chinese===
随即从奥特兰又来了
奥特温，希尔达之子；
众人便依礼哀悼
他那已然亡故的父亲。

===Original===
 Soon were all the warriors
 aside with their ladies speaking,
 And talking of the inroad
 the fighters strong one day would thence be making.

===Chinese===
不多时，武士们
都退到一旁与女眷们交谈，
议论着日后
剽壮的战士们将自那里发动的征伐。

===Original===
Then said the aged Wâ-te:
 “This can never be
 Till those who now are children
 fully-grown we see,

===Chinese===
这时年迈的瓦特说道：
「此事断然行不通，
除非我们亲眼见到如今
还是孩童的那辈人长大成人，

===Original===
 And worthy to be swordsmen.
 Then, their fathers mourning,
 And of their kinsmen mindful,
 gladly will they with us to war be turning.”

===Chinese===
且堪当执剑的战士。
到那时，他们哀悼着父辈，
惦念着自己的亲族，
自会欣然随我们投身战事。」

===Original===
Queen Hilda then made answer:
 “To wait for this were long;
 Meanwhile Gudrun, my daughter,
 held by foemen strong,

===Chinese===
王后希尔达随即答道：
「等这一天未免太久；
这期间我的女儿古德伦
落在强敌的手中，

===Original===
 Must in a far-off kingdom
 be kept in bondage bitter;
 And I, poor queen and mother,
 shall know no bliss, and my heart will ne’er grow lighter.”

===Chinese===
必得在遥远的国度里
受尽苦役的煎熬；
而我，可怜的王后与母亲，
将不知幸福为何物，这颗心永难宽解。」

===Original===
Then said the Danish Fru-te:
 “The maid we cannot free
 Until once more your kingdom
 shall full of warriors be.

===Chinese===
这时丹麦的弗鲁特（Fru-te）说道：
「眼下我们还救不出那位少女，
非得等到您的王国
重新布满武士不可。

===Original===
 Then, for the struggle ready,
 we hence shall ride, unfearing;
 And so upon our foemen
 shall work the greatest ill with blows unsparing.”

===Chinese===
到那时，我们整装待发，
无所畏惧地自此驰征；
定要叫我们的仇敌
尝尽最大的苦头，挨下毫不留情的痛击。」

===Original===
To this Queen Hilda answered:
 “That day may God soon give;
 But I, unhappy woman,
 a weary life must live.

===Chinese===
王后希尔达对此答道：
「愿上帝早日赐下那一天；
只是我，不幸的妇人，
还得熬过这倦怠的人生。

===Original===
 Whoe’er of me is mindful,
 and of Gudrun, poor maiden,
 Him will I trust most fully,
 knowing his heart for us with care is laden.”

===Chinese===
凡还惦念着我、
也惦念着可怜的古德伦的人，
我都要全然信任他，
因为我知道，他心中为我们悬着忧思。」

===Original===
They now their leave were taking;
 to them the lady spake:
 “May he be blest and happy
 who thought for me shall take.

===Chinese===
这时众人前来辞行；
夫人对他们说道：
「愿那肯为我
费心筹划的人蒙福而安乐。

===Original===
 ’Tis right that you, brave warriors,
 to fight for me are ready;
 Meanwhile for the coming inroad
 do all you can, and therein be you speedy.”

===Chinese===
你们这些勇武的战士
甘愿为我而战，本是义所当为；
在此期间，为那场将至的征伐，
请各尽所能，并务求从速。」

===Original===
Wisely then spake Wâ-te,
 the warrior old and good:
 “Lady, we should be felling
 trees in the western wood.

===Chinese===
随后瓦特明智地开言，
这位年高德劭的武士说道：
「夫人，我们该去采伐
西边林子里的树木。

===Original===
 Since we to fight have chosen,
 our hopes upon it staking,
 The men of every princedom
 should forty well-built ships for us be making.”

===Chinese===
既然我们决意一战，
把希望都押在这上头，
各侯国的属民就该
为我们造出四十条坚固的好船。」

===Original===
“I too will bid,” quoth Hilda,
 “that near the deep sea-flood
 Twenty ships be builded,
 strong, and firm, and good;

===Chinese===
「我也要传令，」希尔达说，
「在深深的海潮近旁
再造起二十条船，
条条坚固、牢实、精良；

===Original===
 And have them fully ready
 —my hest shall well be heeded﻿—
 To bear my friends and kindred
 to where they for the fight will soon be needed.”

===Chinese===
并把它们一一备办齐全
——我的号令必得遵行——
好载我的朋友与亲族
前往即将需要他们赴战的地方。」

===Original===
Siegfried, lord of Moorland,
 while their leave they took,
 With kind and seemly bearing,
 thus to the women spoke:

===Chinese===
摩尔兰（Moorland）之主西格弗里德（Siegfried）
趁众人辞行之际，
带着和善而得体的仪态，
对妇人们这样说道：

===Original===
 “You have to tell me only
 when our time to wait is ending;
 To sail shall I be ready,
 nor need you then for me be further sending.”

===Chinese===
「你们只需捎话给我，
说我们等候的时日已到；
我自会整装待发，
届时再不必专程遣人来请。」

===Original===
Then to the sorrowing women,
 before they spread the sail,
 The friendly guests, now leaving,
 bade a kind farewell.

===Chinese===
随后，在扬帆启程之前，
那些友好的宾客
向悲伤的妇人们
道了一声亲切的珍重。

===Original===
 The hearts of knights and maidens
 deep in woe were sinking;
 Yet warlike deeds they plotted
 of which their Norman foes were never thinking.

===Chinese===
骑士与少女的心
都沉入深深的哀愁；
然而他们已在筹谋武勋——
那是他们诺曼仇敌从未想到的。

===Original===
When they at length had ridden
 back again to their land,
 Sadly they mourned their losses:
 then to the Wulpensand,

===Chinese===
当他们终于骑马
返回了自己的国土，
仍在凄然哀悼所受的折损；
随后希尔达又传令，向武尔彭沙洲

===Original===
 For the sake of the dead, did Hilda
 bid that food be taken
 To the priests for them there praying.
 The queen was wise, the dead were not forsaken.

===Chinese===
为着亡者的缘故，
送去食物，
供给在那里为他们祈祷的教士。
王后真是贤明，死者并未被抛忘。

===Original===
There she bade to be builded
 a minster fair and wide;
 A house for the sick, and a cloister
 built they at its side,

===Chinese===
她又吩咐在那里兴建
一座宽广华美的大教堂；
他们还在教堂旁边
造起一座病院和一座修道院，

===Original===
 Near where the slain were buried.
 In many a land one heareth
 Its name, and of those there fallen:
 ‘The church of Wulpensand’ is the name it beareth.

===Chinese===
就坐落在阵亡者安葬之地的近旁。
多少地方的人们
都听闻它和那些捐躯者的名声：
『武尔彭沙洲教堂』便是它承载的名号。

## Tale XX How Hartmut Went Home to Normandy / 第二十诗章 哈特穆特如何回到诺曼底家中

===Original===
The Normans reach their land with Gudrun and her maidens; she refuses to marry Hartmut, and is placed in the care of Gerlind, his mother, who treats her harshly and obliges her to perform menial service. Ortrun, the sister of Hartmut, shows kindness to Gudrun.

===Chinese===
诺曼人携古德伦（Gudrun）与众少女抵达他们的国土；她拒绝嫁给哈特穆特（Hartmut），被交由他的母亲格尔林德（Gerlind）照管——格尔林德待她苛虐，逼她做卑贱的杂役。哈特穆特的妹妹奥特龙（Ortrun）则对古德伦善待有加。

===Original===
No further will we tell you
 of how with these it fared,
 Or how the cloister-brothers
 their life together shared.

===Chinese===
那些人此后的境况，
我们不再向诸位细讲，
也不说修道院的弟兄
如何相守着共度岁月。

===Original===
 Now to the tale of Hartmut
 we ask you all to listen;
 How he with many maidens,
 high-born and fair, unto his land did hasten.

===Chinese===
如今哈特穆特的故事，
请诸位都来侧耳倾听：
他如何携着众多少女，
出身高贵、容貌姣好，急急赶回他的国土。

===Original===
After the fight was ended,
 as I have told before,
 For many there was sorrow
 for the bitter wounds they bore:
