===Chinese===
战斗既已结束，
如我先前所述，
许多人满怀哀伤，
为他们身受的苦痛创伤：

===Original===
 Many who had fallen
 on the stormy field lay dying;
 Children bereft of fathers
 bewailed them soon with tears they ne’er were drying.

===Chinese===
许多倒在风暴战场上的人
正奄奄待毙；
失去父亲的孩童
很快便为他们啼哭，泪水永擦不干。

===Original===
With heavy hearts the Normans
 were wafted o’er the flood;
 Every night and morning
 many a warrior good

===Chinese===
诺曼人怀着沉重的心
被风送过洪流；
每个夜晚与清晨，
多少勇武的战士

===Original===
 Felt ashamed and sorry,
 thus from the sands to be driven;
 So felt the old and the youthful,
 although in all things else they well had thriven.

===Chinese===
都感到羞惭悲苦——
竟这样被人逐出沙洲；
老少人人心同此感，
尽管在其他诸事上他们都顺遂兴旺。

===Original===
They came to the Norman borders,
 unto King Ludwig’s land.
 It was a day of gladness
 to all the sailing band,

===Chinese===
他们来到诺曼底的疆界，
来到路德维希（Ludwig）国王的国土。
对全体航行的队伍来说，
这是个欢欣的日子，

===Original===
 To see at last their homesteads
 and thither to be steering.
 Then said one among them:
 “These are Hartmut’s towns that we are nearing.”

===Chinese===
终于望见自家的田庄，
船头正向那里驶去。
人群中有一人说道：
「我们正在驶近的，是哈特穆特的城镇。」

===Original===
Helped by kindly breezes,
 soon they reached the shore.
 Now the men of Normandy
 happy hearts all bore,

===Chinese===
承着和煦的微风，
他们不久便抵岸。
此刻诺曼底的人人
都怀着欢畅的心情，

===Original===
 When to their wives and children
 they again were coming;
 Long had they been fearing
 that they must die, while they afar were roaming.

===Chinese===
因为他们即将重见
自己的妻儿；
他们长久以来担惊受怕，
只恐客死他乡，在漂泊中丧命。

===Original===
When now the glad King Ludwig
 did on his castles look,
 Thus the lordly Norman
 to Gudrun, the maiden, spoke:

===Chinese===
欣喜的路德维希国王
这时眺望自己的城堡，
那位倨傲的诺曼人
便对古德伦少女如此说道：

===Original===
 “See you that palace, Lady?
 In bliss you may there be living;
 If you to us are kindly,
 our richest lands will we to you be giving.”

===Chinese===
「夫人，你可望见那座宫殿？
你尽可以在那里安享荣华；
你若善待我们，
我们最丰饶的土地都愿奉送于你。」

===Original===
Then the high-born maiden
 thus made her sorrow known:
 “To whom should I feel kindly,
 when kindness none have shown?

===Chinese===
于是那高贵的少女
道出了自己的悲伤：
「我该对谁怀有好意，
当无人曾善待于我？

===Original===
 From that, alas! I’m sundered,
 and in my hopes am thwarted;
 Nothing I know but hardship,
 and all my weary days I spend sad-hearted.”

===Chinese===
唉，我已与它无缘，
种种指望皆成泡影；
我尝到的唯有苦楚，
倦怠的日子里，我总是心怀悲戚。」

===Original===
Then answered her King Ludwig:
 “Throw off this sorry mood,
 And give your love to Hartmut,
 a knight both brave and good.

===Chinese===
路德维希国王回答她：
「抛却这哀愁的心绪吧，
把你的爱给了哈特穆特，
他是位既勇敢又良善的骑士。

===Original===
 Whatever we are owning
 to give you we are willing;
 With one who is so worthy
 blest may you live, and lofty rank be filling.”

===Chinese===
我们所拥有的一切，
都甘愿赠予你；
与这样一位配得上的人相守，
愿你福乐绵长，尊位崇高。」

===Original===
Then spake Hilda’s daughter:
 “Why leave me not in peace?
 Rather than wed with Hartmut
 death would I dread far less.

===Chinese===
希尔达（Hilda）之女开口道：
「为何不放我安宁？
与其嫁给哈特穆特，
我倒远不那么畏惧死亡。

===Original===
 That he should be my lover
 by birth he is not fitted;
 To lose my life were better
 than take his love and as his bride be greeted.”

===Chinese===
论出身，他配不上
做我的爱侣；
宁可丢掉性命，
也胜过接受他的爱、被他当作新娘迎娶。」

===Original===
When this was heard by Ludwig,
 filled with wrath was he;
 Quick by the hair he seized her,
 and flung her into the sea.

===Chinese===
路德维希听罢此言，
登时怒火中烧；
他一把揪住她的头发，
将她掷进了大海。

===Original===
 Straightway the daring Hartmut
 his ready help then gave her;
 He sprang at once to the maiden,
 and from the whirling waves his arm did save her.

===Chinese===
果敢的哈特穆特
立即向她施以援手；
他纵身扑向少女，
用臂膀将她从漩涡般的浪涛中救起。

===Original===
Just as the maid was sinking
 Hartmut reached her side;
 Had not her lover helped her
 drowned were she in the tide.

===Chinese===
正当少女渐渐下沉，
哈特穆特赶到了她身旁；
若非她的爱人相救，
她早已葬身潮水之中。

===Original===
 Her yellow locks well grasping,
 then from out the water
 With his hands he drew her:
 else nought from death had spared Queen Hilda’s daughter.

===Chinese===
他紧紧攥住她金黄的长发，
随即从水里
用双手将她拉出：
否则，再无什么能从死神手中救下王后希尔达之女。

===Original===
Back to the ship did Hartmut
 bring the maiden fair;﻿—
 Rough ways to lovely women
 Ludwig did not spare.

===Chinese===
哈特穆特把那美丽的少女
带回了船上——
对娇美的女子行粗暴之事，
路德维希毫不知避忌。

===Original===
 Dragged from out the water,
 she in her smock was seated;
 How full was she of sadness!
 Never before had the maiden thus been treated.

===Chinese===
刚从水里被拖出来，
她只穿着贴身衬衣坐在那里；
她是何等悲恸！
少女从不曾遭受这般对待。

===Original===
Then all her friends together
 wept for the lovely maid,
 None could there be happy;
 for what could be more sad

===Chinese===
她的同伴们齐聚一处，
为这可爱的少女落泪；
无人能有半点欢欣；
还有什么比这更令人悲伤——

===Original===
 Than to see the king’s own daughter
 handled thus so roughly?
 The thought to them was rising:
 “To us they now will bear themselves more gruffly.”

===Chinese===
眼看国王的亲生女儿
遭人这般粗暴摆布？
他们心中升起一个念头：
「往后他们待我们只会更加蛮横。」

===Original===
Then said the knightly Hartmut:
 “Why drown my hoped-for wife,
 Gudrun, the lovely maiden,
 dear to me as life?

===Chinese===
骑士哈特穆特开口说道：
「为何要溺死我期盼的妻子
古德伦，这可爱的少女，
她于我贵如性命？

===Original===
 If any but my father
 so foul a wrong had done her,
 Sore would be my anger,
 and I from him would take both life and honor.”

===Chinese===
倘若是父亲以外的任何人
对她施这等污行，
我必雷霆大怒，
定叫他以性命与名誉抵偿。」

===Original===
To him King Ludwig answered:
 “Ever free from shame
 Have I till age been living,
 and still a worthy name

===Chinese===
路德维希国王答他道：
「我活到这般年纪，
向来行事无愧于心，
至今仍持有一个体面的名声，

===Original===
 And rank among my fellows
 will hold till life is ending.
 Bid now Gudrun, your lady,
 that she no more her scorn on me be spending.”

===Chinese===
在侪辈间的身份地位，
也要保持到生命尽头。
去吩咐你的夫人古德伦，
叫她莫再把轻蔑浪费在我身上。」

===Original===
Now unto Queen Gerlind
 errand-bearers came,
 Who, in mood most happy,
 bore in Hartmut’s name

===Chinese===
这时，使者们前来
觐见格尔林德王后，
他们满心欢喜，
以哈特穆特的名义

===Original===
 Words of love and honor,
 as from her son was fitting.
 He asked a friendly welcome
 for his many knights who on the shore were waiting.

===Chinese===
捎来充满爱意与敬意的言语，
一如她的儿子所当言。
他请求为他的众多骑士
预备友好的迎接——他们正候在岸上。

===Original===
They bore from him the tidings
 that he across the wave
 Had brought the Hegeling maiden,
 to whom his love he gave

===Chinese===
他们从他那里捎来音讯，
说他已渡过波涛，
掳回了黑格林（Hegeling）少女，
他对她早已倾注了爱意——

===Original===
 Ere he had looked upon her,
 and for whom he still was pining.
 When this was heard by Gerlind,
 a happier day on her was never shining.

===Chinese===
早在尚未谋面之时，
至今仍为她苦苦思恋。
格尔林德听罢此讯，
从未有哪个日子让她如此阳光灿烂。

===Original===
Then said he who told it:
 “Lady, you now should ride
 To the sea before the castle,
 where yet the maid doth bide,

===Chinese===
那报讯人接着说道：
「夫人，您现在该骑马
前往城堡外的海边，
那少女还滞留在那里，

===Original===
 And give her, in her sorrow,
 your love and kindly greeting;
 You and your daughter, Ortrun,
 should haste to the shore, the homeless maiden meeting.

===Chinese===
在她悲伤之际，
献上您的爱护与温言问候；
您与您的女儿奥特龙
该速速赶赴岸边，迎接这无家可归的少女。

===Original===
“Likewise, riding with you
 down unto the flood,
 Should go both maids and women,
 and also warriors good.

===Chinese===
「此外，随您一同
策马前往水滨的，
还应有少女与妇人们，
以及勇武的战士。

===Original===
 Her you will find in the harbor
 who from home was riven;
 Both to the maid and her followers
 a welcome kind by you should now be given.”

===Chinese===
您会在港湾里见到她——
那个被人从家中劫离的姑娘；
对这位少女和她的随从们，
您此刻都该赐下亲切的欢迎。」

===Original===
Then Queen Gerlind answered:
 “That will I gladly do;
 ’Twill make me richly happy
 King Hettel’s child to know,

===Chinese===
格尔林德王后答道：
「这事我乐意去办；
能结识黑特尔（Hettel）国王的孩子，
将给我莫大的欢喜，

===Original===
 And to find that, with her maidens,
 she has come to tarry.
 Well I know that Hartmut
 will soon be blest, when he the maid shall marry.”

===Chinese===
得知她带着众少女
前来此地驻留，
我深知哈特穆特
迎娶这少女之日，福分便将降临。」

===Original===
Then she bade that horses,
 with saddle-cloths, be brought.
 Ortrun, the youthful princess,
 was happy in the thought

===Chinese===
她随即吩咐备马，
连鞍垫一并牵来。
年轻的公主奥特龙
想到很快便能在

===Original===
 Soon in her father’s kingdom
 to see Gudrun, the maiden,
 If this might truly happen;
 for the speech of all was with her praises laden.

===Chinese===
父亲的国土上见到古德伦少女，
心中好不喜欢——
但愿这事真能成真；
因为众人言谈里，对她满口称赞。

===Original===
Then out of chests were taken
 of all the clothes the best
 They knew therein were lying,
 to be worn to meet the guest.

===Chinese===
于是人们从箱柜里取出
他们知道存放在内的
所有衣裳中最华美的，
预备穿去迎客。

===Original===
 Soon the knights of Hartmut
 to don the clothes were bidden;
 Erelong a throng of followers,
 gaily bedight, from Gerlind’s halls had ridden.

===Chinese===
不久，哈特穆特的骑士们
便受命穿上这些衣裳；
随即一大队随从盛装华服，
从格尔林德的殿宇策马而出。

===Original===
Upon the third day early,
 women as well as men,
 All who there had gathered
 before Gerlind, their queen,

===Chinese===
第三天一大早，
男人们连同女人们，
所有聚集在他们的王后
格尔林德面前的人，

===Original===
 To give the maidens welcome,
 were ready and outfitted;
 Out of the gates they crowded,
 and on their steeds not long in the courtyard waited.

===Chinese===
为了迎接众少女，
都已整装齐备；
他们涌出大门，
在庭院里登马，不曾久候。

===Original===
The Normans now with the women
 had into the harbor come:
 The booty they unloaded
 that they would carry home.

===Chinese===
此刻诺曼人偕同妇人们
已来到港湾之中：
他们卸下战利品——
那是要运回家去的财物。

===Original===
 All unto their birthland
 back had come right gladly;
 Gudrun and her band of maidens,
 alone of all, demeaned themselves but sadly.

===Chinese===
众人重返故土，
个个欢天喜地；
唯独古德伦和她的一班少女，
举止间唯有凄惶。

===Original===
Now the brave Sir Hartmut
 led her forth by the hand,
 If she had deemed it fitting,
 this she had not deigned;

===Chinese===
勇武的哈特穆特爵士
此时执她的手引她前行；
她若觉得这是体面事，
断不肯受这份抬举；

===Original===
 Yet the poor child, in sorrow,
 took his love but coldly,
 Altho’ he showed it warmly,
 and worship more had done freely and boldly.

===Chinese===
然而这可怜的孩子满腹愁苦，
对他的爱意只冷淡领受，
尽管他表现得炽热，
还曾自由不羁地对她大献殷勤。

===Original===
With her went sixty maidens
 who over the sea had come:
 One saw, as he beheld them,
 how that all from their home

===Chinese===
随她同行的有六十名少女，
都是渡海而来；
旁人一望便知，她们全都
背井离乡，

===Original===
 Came with proudest bearing.
 They erst high rank had taken,
 In other lands and kingdoms;
 their hearts were heavy now, of bliss forsaken.

===Chinese===
却仍怀着最骄傲的仪态。
她们从前在别的邦国与王国
曾身居高位；
如今福乐尽失，心头沉重。

===Original===
The sister of young Hartmut
 between two barons rode;
 Now to Hilda’s daughter
 a welcome warm she showed:

===Chinese===
年轻的哈特穆特之妹
骑行在两位男爵之间；
这时她向希尔达之女
献上热忱的欢迎：

===Original===
 Ortrun, Ludwig’s daughter,
 her eyes now wet with weeping,
 Kissed the homeless maiden,
 while she her fair white hands in her own was keeping.

===Chinese===
奥特龙，路德维希之女，
双眼噙满泪水，
亲吻这无家可归的少女，
把她莹白的手久久握在自己手中。

===Original===
Then the wife of Ludwig
 to kiss her, too, was fain,
 But to the youthful maiden
 the thought was full of pain.

===Chinese===
随后路德维希的妻子
也想要与她亲吻，
可在这年轻的少女看来，
这念头只令人痛苦。

===Original===
 Thus she spake to Gerlind:
 “Why come you here to meet me?
 Loath am I to kiss you,
 and neither can I bear that you should greet me.

===Chinese===
她便对格尔林德说道：
「您为何前来迎我？
我不愿与您亲吻，
也受不得您对我的问候。

===Original===
“ ’Twas by your own ill-doing
 that I, poor wretched maid,
 Have known no home nor dwelling;
 heart-sorrow long I’ve had;

===Chinese===
「正是因您自己的恶行，
我这可怜不幸的少女
才落得无家无室；
心中的悲苦我饱尝已久；

===Original===
 My lot, alas! is shameful,
 and will, I fear, grow harder.”
 Then Ortrun strove to soothe her,
 and did her best that with love Gudrun should reward her.

===Chinese===
唉，我的命运何其屈辱，
而且恐怕还要更苦。」
于是奥特龙竭力宽慰她，
一心盼古德伦以爱回报她。

===Original===
One by one she greeted
 the maids on every side.
 Now rose a wondrous shouting;
 men flocked from far and wide:

===Chinese===
她逐一问候了
四下里的众少女。
这时响起一片惊叹的欢呼；
人群从四面八方涌来：

===Original===
 Upon the pebbly sea-beach
 stakes for tents were driven;
 With silken ropes were they fastened;
 to Hartmut and his men was shelter in them given.

===Chinese===
在多卵石的海滩上，
帐篷的木桩被夯入土中；
又用丝绳把它们系牢；
哈特穆特和他的人便在帐中栖身。

===Original===
To bear the goods from the seaside
 the folk were all astir.
 Gudrun, fair maiden, sorrowed,
 and pain it gave to her

===Chinese===
为把货物从海边搬走，
人人都忙碌起来。
美丽的少女古德伦却满怀忧愁，
眼看四周

===Original===
 To see that all around her
 the Normans were so many;
 Unless it were to Ortrun,
 she never showed a friendly mood to any.

===Chinese===
诺曼人何其众多，
她心中便阵阵作痛；
若非对着奥特龙，
她对谁都从未露过好脸色。

===Original===
The maidens on the seashore
 must all the day abide.
 With tears their eyes were flowing,
 whatever others did;

===Chinese===
众少女只得在海滨
挨过一整日。
任凭旁人做什么，
她们的眼中泪流不止；

===Original===
 Dry were they but seldom,
 their cheeks were pale with sorrow:
 Hartmut tried to soothe them,
 but their sadness lasted yet through many a morrow.

===Chinese===
泪眼鲜有干时，
双颊因哀愁而苍白：
哈特穆特设法宽慰她们，
可她们的悲伤仍绵延了许多时日。

===Original===
To hold Gudrun in honor
 was Ortrun ever stern,
 And, e’en if others wronged her,
 with love to her did turn:

===Chinese===
维护古德伦的尊荣，
奥特龙向来坚定不移，
纵使旁人慢待她，
她也总以爱意向她相待：

===Original===
 She in her father’s kingdom
 strove to make her merry,
 But, far from friends and kindred,
 often the poor young girl was sad and weary.

===Chinese===
她在父亲的国土上
千方百计使她欢愉，
可这可怜的姑娘远离亲友，
常常悲伤又疲惫。

===Original===
To the Normans home was welcome,
 as indeed was right;
 They boasted much of the booty,
 both churl as well as knight,

===Chinese===
诺曼人重返家园，自是可喜，
这也合乎情理；
他们对战利品大肆夸耀，
无论庶民还是骑士，

===Original===
 Brought from the Hegeling kingdom,
 as they home were turning.
 What welcome glad all gave them
 who ne’er to see them hoped, albeit yearning!

===Chinese===
夸那从黑格林人的王国
缴获而来的财货，就在他们还乡之时。
那些从未敢指望再见他们的人——尽管日夜渴念——
给了他们何等欢畅的迎接！

===Original===
Soon as Hartmut’s warriors
 from all their toil were free,
 And they were fully rested
 from off the stormy sea,

===Chinese===
哈特穆特的武士们
一从辛劳中脱身，
又在风暴的海上颠簸之后
充分休整过来，

===Original===
 They quickly left each other,
 for their homes in many places:
 While some their hands were wringing,
 smiles were seen to brighten others’ faces.

===Chinese===
便很快彼此作别，
各赴散居多处的家园：
有人绞扭着双手，
也有人脸上绽开了笑意。

===Original===
Then did Hartmut also
 turn away from the shore,
 And to a stately palace
 the fair Gudrun he bore.

===Chinese===
随后哈特穆特也
转身离开海滨，
把美丽的古德伦
带往一座堂皇的宫殿。

===Original===
 Henceforth the youthful maiden
 must tarry there far longer
 Than she to stay was minded,
 and there her woe and pain grew ever stronger.

===Chinese===
从此这年轻的少女
只得在那里滞留，
远超她本愿的时日；
她的哀伤与苦痛在那里愈积愈深。

===Original===
When now the high-born maiden
 sat in Hartmut’s hall,
 Where his men should crown her,
 then he bade them all

===Chinese===
当下这高贵的少女
坐进了哈特穆特的殿堂，
他的部众本要为她加冕，
他便吩咐众人

===Original===
 To be forever faithful,
 and their goodwill to show her;
 So would she not forget them,
 but would enrich whoe’er should kindness do her.

===Chinese===
永远效忠于她，
向她表露他们的善意；
这样她便不会忘记他们，
凡善待她的人，她都会使之富贵。

===Original===
Then spake the mother, Gerlind,
 old King Ludwig’s wife:
 “When will Gudrun be ready
 to share young Hartmut’s life,

===Chinese===
这时母亲格尔林德，
老王路德维希之妻，开言道：
「古德伦何时才愿
与年轻的哈特穆特共度人生，

===Original===
 Our youthful prince so noble,
 and in her arms to fold him?
 Of her his rank is worthy,
 and ne’er will she be sorry for her lord to hold him.”

===Chinese===
我们这般高贵的年轻王子，
将她搂入臂弯？
论身份他配得上她，
她拥这位夫君为伴，永不会懊悔。」

===Original===
Gudrun to this had listened,
 the wretched, homeless maid;
 She said: “My Lady Gerlind,
 ’twould make you sad indeed

===Chinese===
古德伦听罢这话，
这不幸的无家少女说道：
「格尔林德夫人，
倘若您必须嫁与

===Original===
 If you must take in wedlock
 one who the lives had wasted
 Of many friends and kinsfolk;
 by toil for him your life were ever blasted.”

===Chinese===
一个断送了许多亲友性命的人，
您也定会黯然神伤；
为他终日操劳，
您的一生都将被毁尽。」

===Original===
“This shall no one hinder,”
 to her then said the queen;
 “Gainsay his will no longer,
 let your love for him be seen,

===Chinese===
「这事谁也拦不得，」
王后对她说道；
「别再违逆他的心意，
把你对他的爱显露出来，

===Original===
 And on my head I pledge you
 that rich shall be your guerdon:
 If to be a queen you spurn not,
 you of my crown shall bear the happy burden.”

===Chinese===
我以自己的头颅向你担保，
你的酬报必丰厚无比：
只要你不鄙弃为王后，
我这顶王冠的荣担，你将欣然担起。」

===Original===
Then said the sorrowing maiden:
 “That will I never wear;
 Of all his wealth and greatness
 you the tale may spare.

===Chinese===
悲戚的少女答道：
「那样的担子我永不承受；
他如何豪富、如何显赫，
您也不必再费口舌。

===Original===
 Your son, the knightly Hartmut,
 my love can ne’er be winning:
 Unwilling here I linger,
 and hence to go I day by day am pining.”

===Chinese===
您的儿子，骑士哈特穆特，
永远赢不去我的爱：
我在此地强留非所愿，
日复一日只盼离去。」

===Original===
Then the youthful Hartmut,
 who of the land was lord,
 Was angry with the maiden
 when he her answer heard.

===Chinese===
年轻的哈特穆特，
这片国土之主，
听罢少女的回答，
不由得怒上心头。

===Original===
 He said: “If, then, to wed her
 the lady granteth never,
 So, also, to the fair one
 shall my goodwill and love be wanting ever.”

===Chinese===
他说：「既然那位夫人
永不肯应允婚事，
那么我这番好意与爱，
也永不施与这美人。」

===Original===
Then the wicked Gerlind
 to Hartmut said, in turn:
 “Ever the young and thoughtless
 from the wise should learn.

===Chinese===
这时歹毒的格尔林德
反过来对哈特穆特说：
「年轻而不知深浅的人，
总该向智者讨教。

===Original===
 Now leave to me this maiden,
 let me for her be caring,
 And I so well shall teach her
 that she will quickly drop her lofty bearing.”

===Chinese===
如今把这少女交给我，
让我来照管她，
我会把她调教得服服帖帖，
叫她很快收起那副高傲的架子。」

===Original===
“That will I grant you gladly,”
 Hartmut answering said;
 “Whate’er from this may follow,
 to you I give the maid,

===Chinese===
「这事我乐于应允，」
哈特穆特答道；
「此后无论生出什么事端，
这少女都交托给你，

===Original===
 To have in your good keeping,
 as suits her rank and honor;
 The maid is sad and homeless;
 lady, ’tis right that kindly care be shown her.”

===Chinese===
由你好好看顾，
合乎她的身份与体面；
这少女悲苦而无家可归；
夫人啊，善待她才是正理。」

===Original===
So Gudrun, the fair one,
 when Hartmut went that day,
 Was left unto his mother,
 and given to her sway:

===Chinese===
就这样，美丽的古德伦
在哈特穆特当日离去后，
被留给他的母亲，
任凭她摆布：

===Original===
 But Hilda’s youthful daughter
 Gerlind’s guidance hated;
 She could not brook her teaching,
 and never her dislike for this abated.

===Chinese===
可希尔达年轻的女儿
憎恶格尔林德的管教；
她忍受不了她的训诫，
这份厌憎从未消减。

===Original===
Then to the lovely maiden
 the old she-devil spake:
 “If you will not live happy,
 then sorrow you must take.

===Chinese===
于是那老女魔
对可爱的少女说道：
「你若不肯享福，
那就得吃苦头。

===Original===
 You have to heat my chamber;
 yourself the fire must kindle;
 See, there is none to help you,
 nor may you hope your toil will ever dwindle.”

===Chinese===
你得给我暖屋子；
火要你亲手去生；
瞧吧，没人会帮你，
也别指望你的劳役会有穷尽。」

===Original===
The high-born maiden answered:
 “That I well can do;
 Whatever you shall bid me,
 in all must I yield to you,

===Chinese===
高贵的少女答道：
「这我完全做得到；
无论您吩咐我什么，
我都必须事事顺从，

===Original===
 Until the God in heaven
 at last my wrongs has righted.
 Never my mother’s daughter
 the fire upon the hearth ere this has lighted.”

===Chinese===
直到天上的上帝
终有一日为我的冤屈主持公道。
我母亲的女儿此前
从不曾亲手点起炉膛之火。」

===Original===
Said Gerlind: “As I’m living,
 to toil must you begin,
 As never queenly daughter
 to do before was seen.

===Chinese===
格尔林德说：「我敢以性命起誓，
你必须开始操劳，
这般苦役，从没有哪位
王后般的女儿做过给人看。

===Original===
 To be so proud and headstrong
 I will make you weary:
 Before to-morrow darkens,
 your maidens you must leave, and ne’er be merry.

===Chinese===
你这般高傲倔强，
我要叫你疲于奔命：
不等明日天黑，
你就得离开你的少女们，从此不得欢愉。

===Original===
“You hold yourself too highly,
 as I have heard it said;
 For this shall work most toilsome
 soon upon you be laid.

===Chinese===
「你把自己抬得太高，
这话我早有耳闻；
为着这个，最苦重的活计
很快就要压到你身上。

===Original===
 This pride and froward bearing
 must be by you forsaken;
 Your lofty mood will I lower,
 and all your hopes will very quickly weaken.”

===Chinese===
这股傲气与忤逆的做派，
你须得统统抛掉；
我要压下你那高傲的心性，
把你的一切指望迅速削弱。」

===Original===
Then went the wicked Gerlind
 to court, in anger wild;
 She said to her son, young Hartmut:
 “Hettel’s wilful child

===Chinese===
随后歹毒的格尔林德
怒气冲冲走向朝廷；
她对儿子、年轻的哈特穆特说：
「黑特尔那任性的孩子

===Original===
 Scorns both you and your kindred,
 and ever at us is sneering:
 Would we had never seen her,
 if we such talk from her must now be hearing.”

===Chinese===
既轻蔑你又轻蔑你的亲族，
还总对着我们冷嘲热讽：
早知要从她口中听这般话，
当初倒不如从不曾见过她。」

===Original===
Then spake unto his mother
 Hartmut, the knight so brave:
 “Pray treat the maiden kindly,
 howe’er she may behave:

===Chinese===
勇敢的骑士哈特穆特
于是对母亲说道：
「求您善待这少女，
无论她举止如何：

===Original===
 So, for the care you show her,
 my thanks will you be earning.
 Greatly have I wronged her;
 it well may be that she my love is spurning.”

===Chinese===
您对她付出的照料，
必赢得我的感激。
我已大大亏负了她；
她会拒斥我的爱，也情有可原。」

===Original===
Then said to him old Gerlind:
 “Whate’er by us is done,
 In mood she is so stubborn
 that she will yield to none.

===Chinese===
老格尔林德对他说：
「任凭我们做什么，
她心性那般倔强，
绝不肯向任何人低头。

===Original===
 Unless we treat her harshly
 she ne’er, as you would have her,
 Will come to you in wedlock;
 this must we do, or else to herself must leave her.”

===Chinese===
若不用严酷手段待她，
她断不会如你所愿
前来与你成婚；
我们必须如此，否则只能随她去。」

===Original===
Then to her thus answered
 the worthy Norman knight:
 “Good lady, show her kindness
 henceforth in all men’s sight,

===Chinese===
那位正派的诺曼骑士
这样回答她：
「好夫人，从今往后，
当着众人的面善待她吧，

===Original===
 Now for the love you bear me;
 such care I beg you give her
 That from her love and friendship
 the king’s fair daughter may not bar me ever.”

===Chinese===
就当是看在对我的爱；
我求您这般照料她，
好教国王的爱女
永远不把她的爱与情谊拒我于外。」

===Original===
Then his devilish mother,
 with anger brimming o’er,
 To the throng of Hegeling maidens
 quickly went once more.

===Chinese===
于是他魔鬼般的母亲，
满腔怒火沸腾，
又快步走向
黑格林众少女聚集之处。

===Original===
 She said: “Make ready, maidens,
 and to your toil betake you,
 To do what you are bidden;
 the task to each that’s given ne’er forsake you.”

===Chinese===
她说：「准备起来，少女们，
各就去操劳吧，
去做吩咐你们的事；
分派给各人的活计，谁也不许撂下。」

===Original===
The maidens then were sundered,
 and soon from each other torn;
 They saw not one another,
 and long must live forlorn.

===Chinese===
众少女随即被拆散，
很快彼此分离；
她们再也见不着面，
长年过着孤苦的日子。

===Original===
 Those who once so worthily
 lofty rank were taking,
 In winding yarn were busied;
 while they sat at work their hearts were aching.

===Chinese===
那些从前何等荣耀地
身居高位的女子，
如今埋首绕纱；
坐在活计前，心口阵阵作痛。

===Original===
Some her flax were combing,
 others for her must spin;
 Ladies of lofty breeding,
 whose pastime it had been

===Chinese===
有的为她梳理亚麻，
有的为她纺线；
这些教养高贵的淑女，
从前的消遣

===Original===
 On their silken clothing
 to lay, with skill unsparing,
 Gold and gems most costly,
 these for her now heavy toil were bearing.

===Chinese===
是往自己的绸衣上
不惜工本地缀满
黄金与最贵重的宝石；
如今却为她背负沉重的劳役。

===Original===
The first in birth among them
 at the court was kept;
 Water she must carry
 to the room where Ortrun slept:

===Chinese===
她们当中出身最尊贵的
被留在了宫中；
她得提水
送到奥特龙安睡的房间：

===Original===
 To wait upon that lady
 the high-born maid was bidden;
 By name was she called Hergart;
 her lofty birth was nought, she still was chidden.

===Chinese===
这位高贵的少女受命
去侍奉那位女君；
她的名字叫赫加特（Hergart）；
尊贵的出身无济于事，她仍挨责骂。

===Original===
Among them was another,
 brought from Galicia’s strand;
 The griffin her from Portugal
 had borne to a far-off land.

===Chinese===
她们中间还有一个，
来自加利西亚的海滨；
狮鹫（griffin）曾把她从葡萄牙
掳到一片遥远的土地。

===Original===
 She to the Hegeling kingdom
 with Hagen’s child was carried,
 From over Ireland’s borders;
 now with the maids in the Norman land she tarried.

===Chinese===
随后她与哈根（Hagen）的孩子
一同被带到黑格林人的王国，
来自爱尔兰边境之外；
如今随众少女滞留在诺曼底之地。

===Original===
She was a prince’s daughter,
 who castles owned and lands;
 The fire must now be lighted
 by her, with fair white hands,

===Chinese===
她本是君侯之女，
坐拥城堡与领地；
如今生火的活计
却要她用莹白的手去做，

===Original===
 While in the room well heated
 Gerlind’s ladies rested.
 For all the work she was doing
 no thanks on her by them were ever wasted.

===Chinese===
而屋子烧得暖暖和和，
格尔林德的女眷们安享清福。
她做的一切活计，
从无人对她道过半声谢。

===Original===
Now you well may wonder
 to hear her sorry plight.
 For Gerlind’s lowest wenches
 she drudged both day and night;

===Chinese===
诸位听她这悲惨境遇，
尽可为之惊叹。
她为格尔林德最下等的粗婢
日夜操劳服贱役；

===Original===
 Whatever task they set her,
 to do must she be willing.
 It helped her not with the Normans
 that she at home a lofty rank was filling.

===Chinese===
她们派她什么差事，
她都得甘心去干。
她在娘家身居高位这一节，
在诺曼人这里帮不了她分毫。

===Original===
The work was mean and shameful
 that they were made to do
 For seven half years and over,
 —this is all too true﻿—

===Chinese===
她们被迫去做的那等活计，
卑贱而又屈辱，
一做便是七年半还多——
这千真万确，不容置疑——

===Original===
 Until the young Lord Hartmut,
 when three wars were ended,
 Had come again to his kingdom,
 and found the maids at work, and ill-befriended.

===Chinese===
直到年轻的哈特穆特大人
在打完三场战事之后，
重归他的王国，
发现众少女仍在操劳，无人眷顾。

===Original===
To see again his loved one
 Hartmut deeply yearned;
 But when he looked upon her,
 the truth he quickly learned,

===Chinese===
哈特穆特渴念不已，
盼再见他的心上人；
可当他看到她时，
很快便明白了实情——

===Original===
 That she good food and lodging
 of late had seldom tasted:
 For choosing to live rightly,
 ’twas her reward to be with sorrow wasted.

===Chinese===
近来她极少尝到
像样的饭食与安寝；
只因择善自守，
她得到的报偿便是被忧伤耗损。

===Original===
When forth she came to meet him,
 to her young Hartmut said:
 “Gudrun, most lovely maiden,
 what is the life you have led

===Chinese===
她迎上前来相见时，
年轻的哈特穆特对她说：
「古德伦，最可爱的少女，
你过的是怎样的日子

===Original===
 Since I, with all my warriors,
 my lands and home was leaving?”
 She said: “Such tasks they set me,
 ’twas sin for you, and shame to me ’twas giving.”

===Chinese===
自从我带着全体武士
离开我的国土与家园？」
她说：「他们派给我的差事，
于你是罪愆，于我是羞辱。」

===Original===
Then outspoke young Hartmut:
 “Why has this been done,
 Gerlind, my dearest mother?
 Your love she should have known;

===Chinese===
年轻的哈特穆特又开口：
「这是为什么，
格尔林德，我最亲爱的母亲？
她本该领受您的慈爱；

===Original===
 When with you I left her,
 her lot you should have brightened,
 And all her heavy sorrows
 you should for her within my land have lightened.”

===Chinese===
我把她托付给您时，
您本该让她命运增辉，
把她那沉重的哀伤，
在我的国土上替她一一宽解。」

===Original===
His wolfish mother answered:
 “How could I better teach
 King Hettel’s ill-bred daughter?
 ’Twas bootless to beseech,

===Chinese===
他那狼心狗肺的母亲答道：
「对黑特尔这没家教的女儿，
我还能怎么调教？
苦苦恳求也是枉然，

===Original===
 Nor could I ever bend her,
 to make her leave her jeering:
 She scorned both you and your father
 and kindred, too: to this should you give hearing.”

===Chinese===
我也从来折服不了她，
叫她收起讥诮：
她既轻蔑你，也轻蔑你父亲
和你的亲族：这话你该听进耳中。」

===Original===
Then again spake Hartmut:
 “Much wrong we’ve done the maid.
 Slain by us, her kindred
 and many knights lie dead;

===Chinese===
哈特穆特又说道：
「我们已大大亏负了这少女。
她的亲族连同许多骑士，
死于我们之手，横尸于地；

===Original===
 While from the lovely maiden
 her father we have taken,
 Slain by my father, Ludwig,
 and now with thoughtless words her woes we waken.”

===Chinese===
我们还从这可爱的少女身边
夺走了她的父亲——
他死于我父亲路德维希之手，
如今我们又用轻率的话语搅醒她的伤痛。」

===Original===
Then answered him his mother:
 “My son, ’tis truth I say;
 If we Gudrun, proud maiden,
 for thirty years should pray,

===Chinese===
他的母亲回答他：
「我的儿子，我说的是实话：
哪怕我们向古德伦这骄傲的少女
祈求上三十年，

===Original===
 If she with brooms were stricken,
 or with rods were beaten,
 Your wife we ne’er could make her;
 hopeless it is the wayward maid to threaten.”

===Chinese===
哪怕用扫帚打她、
用棍棒抽她，
也绝不能使她成为你的妻子；
威胁这任性少女，全然无望。」

===Original===
She farther said to Hartmut:
 “However, since you bid,
 I’ll gladly treat her better.”
 But still her mind she hid,

===Chinese===
她又对哈特穆特说：
「不过，既然你开了口，
我乐意待她好些。」
可她心底的盘算仍藏着不说，

===Original===
 And Hartmut never knew it;
 erelong Gudrun would find her
 Harsher yet than ever;
 and now the maiden’s wrongs could no one hinder.

===Chinese===
哈特穆特对此一无所知；
不久古德伦就会发现，
她比从前更加狠戾；
而这少女所受的冤屈，已无人能阻挡。

===Original===
Then went again old Gerlind
 to where Gudrun then sat,
 And said to the Hegeling maiden,
 in her wrath and hate:

===Chinese===
随后老格尔林德又走到
古德伦安坐之处，
怀着满腔怒火与怨恨，
对这黑格林少女说道：

===Original===
 “ ’Twere best you now bethink you,
 or else, my fair young maiden,
 You with your flowing tresses
 must wipe the stools and seats, with dust thick laden.

===Chinese===
「你最好放明白些，
否则，我美丽的年轻少女，
你就得用你披垂的长发
去擦拭积满厚尘的凳子与坐榻。

===Original===
“Then the room I sleep in,
 mark what now I say,
 You, to do my bidding,
 must sweep three times a day;

===Chinese===
「还有我安寝的房间，
记住我此刻的话，
你为听我差遣，
每日须打扫三回；

===Original===
 You carefully must warm it,
 and keep the fire well burning.”
 Said she: “That do I gladly,
 rather than take a lover I am spurning.”

===Chinese===
还得细心把它烘暖，
让炉火烧得旺旺的。」
她说：「这我甘愿去做，
总好过接受我所鄙弃的爱侣。」

===Original===
Whatever she was bidden
 the willing maiden did;
 No work of hers she slighted,
 nor should for aught be chid.

===Chinese===
凡吩咐她做的事，
这恭顺的少女无一不从；
她的活计无可挑剔，
不该为任何事受责骂。

===Original===
 For seven years, full-numbered,
 in a land far over the water,
 The maid was toiling wearily,
 and none did hold her as a kingly daughter.

===Chinese===
整整七个年头，
在重洋之外的异乡，
这少女凄然操劳，
无一人把她当王家的女儿看待。

===Original===
The years had long been running,
 and the ninth was coming on,
 When Hartmut to bethink him
 wisely had begun,

===Chinese===
岁月流转已久，
第九个年头正在到来，
此时哈特穆特开始
明智地思量起自己的处境，

===Original===
 That indeed ’twas shameful
 that he no crown was wearing;
 And for himself and his kinsmen
 ’twas right the name of king he now were bearing.

===Chinese===
想到自己头上竟无王冠，
实在有失体面；
为了他自己和他的亲族，
如今他正该称王才是。

===Original===
After heavy fighting,
 Hartmut, with his men,
 Bearing the prize of bravery,
 riding home was seen.

===Chinese===
历经鏖战之后，
人们望见哈特穆特与部众
携着英勇赢来的锦标，
策马还乡。

===Original===
 He hoped the love of the maiden
 would now to him be granted;
 For, more than any other,
 he the fair Gudrun for his true love wanted.

===Chinese===
他指望少女的爱
此刻终能赐予他；
因为胜过世上任何人，
他一心想娶美丽的古德伦为真爱。

===Original===
When he reached his homestead,
 he bade them bring the maid.
 His evil mother, Gerlind,
 allowed her to be clad

===Chinese===
待他回到家园，
便吩咐把少女带来。
他那恶毒的母亲格尔林德
只准她穿得

===Original===
 In meanest clothing only:
 Gudrun but little heeded
 The youthful Hartmut’s wooing;
 steadfast and true, no love from him she needed.

===Chinese===
最寒酸的衣裳：
古德伦全不在意
年轻的哈特穆特的求爱；
她坚贞如一，不需要他的爱。

===Original===
To him his friends then whispered,
 that, whether glad or no
 For this might be his mother,
 he never should forego

===Chinese===
这时他的亲友们私下进言，
说无论他的母亲
为此高兴与否，
他都万不可放弃

===Original===
 To bend the maid to his wishes;
 and must his care be giving
 That so he might with the lady
 for many a happy day in love be living.

===Chinese===
务使这少女屈从他的心意；
他还须费心料理，
好让自己与这位佳人
相守相爱，长享欢愉的岁月。

===Original===
To the ladies’ room he hastened,
 when thus his kinsmen spoke,
 And there he found the maiden;
 her by the hand he took,

===Chinese===
亲族们这番话说罢，
他急忙赶往女眷的居室；
在那里他找到了少女，
执起她的手，

===Original===
 And said to her: “Fair lady,
 love me now, I pray you,
 And sit as queen beside me;
 my knights and men shall worship ever pay you.”

===Chinese===
对她说：「美丽的夫人，
求你此刻就爱我吧，
登上后座坐在我的身旁；
我的骑士与臣仆将永世向你致敬。」

===Original===
Then said the lovely maiden:
 “For this I have no mind;
 For while the fiendish Gerlind
 to me is so unkind,

===Chinese===
可爱的少女答道：
「此非我所愿；
只因那恶魔般的格尔林德
待我如此狠毒，

===Original===
 The love of knights, though worthy,
 I can long for never.
 To her and all her kindred
 henceforth am I a bitter foe forever.”

===Chinese===
骑士的爱纵然可贵，
我也永远无从渴慕。
对她及她全族，
我从此是永世的死敌。」

===Original===
“Sorry am I,” said Hartmut;
 “to you will I make good
 The hate my mother Gerlind
 to you so harshly showed;

===Chinese===
「我很抱歉，」哈特穆特说；
「我母亲格尔林德
对你施与的那番狠戾憎恶，
我必向你补偿；

===Original===
 As for both of us is worthy,
 your wrongs shall now be righted.”
 The high-born maiden answered:
 “I trust you not; your word need ne’er be plighted.”

===Chinese===
以合乎你我二人身份的方式，
你的冤屈如今终得昭雪。」
高贵的少女答道：
「我不信你；你的誓词也不必发。」

===Original===
Then said to her young Hartmut,
 the lord of the Norman land:
 “Gudrun, most lovely maiden,
 you well must understand

===Chinese===
诺曼底之主、年轻的哈特穆特
又对她说：
「古德伦，最可爱的少女，
你须心知肚明：

===Original===
 Mine are these lands and castles:
 to none may you betake you;
 Who is there here would hang me
 if, ’gainst your will, I now my own should make you?”

===Chinese===
这些土地与城堡尽归于我：
你无处可以投奔；
倘若我此刻违背你的意愿强占你，
此地又有谁敢来绞死我？」

===Original===
Then said King Hettel’s daughter:
 “That were a deed of shame:
 Of aught so wrong and hateful
 never did I dream.

===Chinese===
黑特尔国王之女说道：
「那是无耻的行径：
如此不义、如此可憎之事，
我做梦也不曾想过。

===Original===
 It would be said by princes,
 should they the tale be hearing,
 That one of the kin of Hagen
 in Hartmut’s land a harlot’s name is bearing.”

===Chinese===
君王们若听闻这故事，
定会如此评说：
哈根的骨血之一，
在哈特穆特的国土上背着娼妇之名。」

===Original===
Then did Hartmut answer:
 “What care I what they say?
 If only you, fair lady,
 do not say me nay,

===Chinese===
哈特穆特答道：
「旁人说什么，与我何干？
只要你，美丽的夫人，
不拒绝我，

===Original===
 A king my men shall see me,
 and you my seat be sharing.”
 Then said the maid to Hartmut:
 “That I should love you be you never fearing.

===Chinese===
我的部众将见我称王，
而你共享我的宝座。」
少女随即对哈特穆特说：
「你永远不必担心我会爱你。

===Original===
“Well you know, Sir Hartmut,
 how with me it stands;
 And all the wrong and sorrow
 I met with at your hands,

===Chinese===
「你清楚，哈特穆特爵士，
我的处境如何；
你亲手加于我的
种种冤屈与悲苦，

===Original===
 When far from home you carried
 me whom you had stolen,
 And, wounded by your warriors,
 my father’s men erewhile in death had fallen.

===Chinese===
当年你把劫夺的我
带往远离家园的地方，
我父亲的部众被你的武士所创，
早已相继殒命。

===Original===
“Well known to you ’tis also,
 —for this I mourn again﻿—
 How my father, Hettel,
 was by your father slain.

===Chinese===
「你也分明知晓——
为此我再添一恸——
我的父亲黑特尔
如何丧命于你父亲之手。

===Original===
 Were I knight, and not a woman,
 he durst not come before me
 Unless his weapons wearing.
 Why wed the man who from my kindred tore me?”

===Chinese===
我若是骑士而非女流，
他不敢来到我面前，
除非身披兵甲全副武装。
我为何要嫁给劫我离亲之人？」

===Original===
For many years now bygone,
 it ever was the way,
 No man should take a woman,
 and have her in his sway,

===Chinese===
在业已逝去的许多年代里，
通行的规矩向来如此：
男子不可强娶女子，
将她置于自己的掌控，

===Original===
 Unless they both were willing.
 Much praise for this is owing.
 Gudrun, the homeless maiden,
 her father’s loss still mourned, with tears o’erflowing.

===Chinese===
除非两厢情愿。
这一条深堪赞颂。
无家可归的古德伦
仍为丧父而哀恸，泪流不止。

===Original===
Then spake to her in anger
 Hartmut, the youthful knight:
 “Whatever may befall you,
 I reck not for your plight;

===Chinese===
年轻的骑士哈特穆特
怒气冲冲地对她说：
「无论你遭遇什么，
你的苦楚我全不顾惜；

===Original===
 Since now you are not willing
 to wear the crown beside me,
 You’ll have what you are seeking,
 your meed you’ll daily earn, nor need you chide me.”

===Chinese===
既然你不肯
在我身侧戴上王冠，
你自会得你所求，
日日挣得你的报偿，也不必怨我。」

===Original===
“That will I earn most gladly,
 as I have done before,
 Though for the men of Hartmut
 the hardest toil I bore,

===Chinese===
「那报偿我甘愿去挣，
一如我从前所做的，
尽管为哈特穆特的人手，
我做过最苦重的活计，

===Original===
 And for Queen Gerlind’s women.
 If God my wrongs forgetteth,
 To bear them I am willing;
 but heavy is the woe that me besetteth.”

===Chinese===
也为格尔林德王后的女眷们卖过力。
纵使上帝忘了我的冤屈，
我也甘愿承受；
只是压在我身上的苦难实在太重。」

===Original===
Still they sought to soothe her:
 first to the court they sent
 Young Ortrun, Hartmut’s sister,
 whose looks all kindness meant;

===Chinese===
人们仍设法宽慰她：
他们先把哈特穆特之妹、
年轻的奥特龙遣往宫中，
她的眉眼间尽是善意；

===Original===
 ’Twas hoped that she and her maidens,
 now by friendly dealing,
 Would bring Gudrun, poor lone one,
 to bear towards them all a better feeling.

===Chinese===
指望她和她的随行少女
以友好的相处，
使可怜孤零的古德伦
对他们众人生出好感。

===Original===
Then to his sister Ortrun
 Hartmut freely spake:
 “Wealth I will give you, sister,
 if kindly, for my sake,

===Chinese===
哈特穆特坦诚地
对妹妹奥特龙说：
「妹妹，我愿赐你财富，
只要你肯看在我的份上，好心地

===Original===
 To me you will be helpful,
 and bring Gudrun, fair lady,
 Soon to forget her sorrows;
 nor o’er her woes to brood be ever ready.”

===Chinese===
助我一臂之力，
让美丽的古德伦夫人
快些忘却她的悲伤，
再不终日沉溺于哀愁。」

===Original===
Then spake the youthful Ortrun,
 the Norman maiden fair:
 “To help both her and her maidens
 shall ever be my care,

===Chinese===
年轻的奥特龙，
那诺曼美少女，答道：
「看顾她和她的少女们，
将永远是我的本分，

===Original===
 Till they forget their sorrows:
 I bow my head before her,
 And I and mine will hold her
 even as our kin, and watchful love spread o’er her.”

===Chinese===
直到她们忘却忧愁：
我在她面前俯首致敬，
我与我的家人必将待她
如同亲族，把细致的关爱铺展在她身上。」

===Original===
Gudrun now said to Ortrun:
 “My hearty thanks you win,
 That you, with kindly wishes,
 would see me sit as queen,

===Chinese===
古德伦对奥特龙说：
「你赢得我由衷的感谢，
因为你怀着善意，
愿意见我登上后座，

===Original===
 By the side of Hartmut,
 while with pride I’m gladdened:
 For this my trust I give you,
 but homeless, none the less, my days are saddened.”

===Chinese===
坐在哈特穆特的身旁，
在荣光中欢欣：
为此我赠予你我的信任；
然而我依旧无家可归，我的岁月仍染着哀伤。」

## Tale XXI How Gudrun Must Wash Clothes on the Beach / 第二十一诗章 古德伦如何不得不在海滩上洗衣

===Original===
Gudrun receives better treatment, but, still refusing to marry Hartmut, she is sent to wash clothes on the seashore. Hildeburg, one of her maidens, obtains permission to share her labors, which are continued through many years.

===Chinese===
古德伦（Gudrun）受到了较好的对待，但她依然拒绝嫁给哈特穆特（Hartmut），于是被派往海滨去洗衣。她的侍女之一希尔德堡（Hildeburg）获准分担她的劳役，这苦役一直延续了许多年。

===Original===
Then to Gudrun they offered
 castles strong and lands:
 Of these would she have nothing.
 So, upon the sands,

===Chinese===
他们向古德伦许下了
坚牢的城堡与土地：
这些她一概不取。
于是，在那沙滩之上，

===Original===
 She must wash their clothing,
 from early morn till even.
 Great ill this wrought for Ludwig,
 when he with Herwic in the fight had striven.

===Chinese===
她只得替他们洗涤衣裳，
从清早一直洗到黄昏。
这给路德维希（Ludwig）种下大祸，
待他日后与赫尔维克（Herwic）兵戎相见之时。

===Original===
First, Gudrun was bidden
 to leave her seat, that soon
 She, the high-born maiden,
 should go with fair Ortrun;

===Chinese===
起初，他们吩咐古德伦
离开自己的座席，好使
这位出身高贵的少女
不久便去与美丽的奥特龙（Ortrun）作伴；

===Original===
 They bade that she be merry,
 and wine with her be drinking.
 The homeless wanderer answered:
 “To make me queen you never need be thinking.

===Chinese===
他们要她开怀作乐，
陪着一同饮酒。
那无家可归的流亡少女答道：
「要立我为王后，你想也休想。

===Original===
“Well you wot, Lord Hartmut,
 whate’er your wish may be,
 Betrothed am I to another,
 and am no longer free.

===Chinese===
「哈特穆特大人，你深知内情，
无论你的心愿如何，
我已许给另一人，
早不是自由之身。

===Original===
 That I one day shall wed him
 has with an oath been plighted;
 Until by death he’s taken
 I will not wed with any man e’er knighted.”

===Chinese===
我终有一日要嫁给他，
这已用誓言订定；
除非死神将他夺去，
否则受封的骑士，我谁也不嫁。」

===Original===
Then spake the lordly Hartmut:
 “You only waste your breath;
 By nought shall we be sundered
 unless it shall be death.

===Chinese===
尊贵的哈特穆特随即开言：
「你只是白费口舌；
没有什么能将我们分开，
除非是死亡。

===Original===
 In friendship with my sister
 you should now be living;
 Your hardships she will lighten,
 and will, I know, her love to you be giving.”

===Chinese===
你如今该与我的妹妹
和睦相处；
她会减轻你的艰辛，
我知道，她还会把她的爱倾注于你。」

===Original===
Fain to think was Hartmut
 that her unyielding mood
 Might now by this be softened;
 he hoped, whatever good

===Chinese===
哈特穆特一心指望，
她那不屈的心肠
从此或可回软；
他盼着，无论什么福分

===Original===
 Should e’er befall his sister,
 the maiden would be sharing:
 Thus for both he trusted,
 that a happy life erelong would them be cheering.

===Chinese===
只要降临他妹妹身上，
这少女都能同享：
他就这样为两人深信，
幸福的日子不久便会来宽慰她们。

===Original===
Gudrun soon greeted kindly
 many a friend and maid.
 Ortrun sat beside her;
 her hue grew rosy-red

===Chinese===
古德伦不久便亲切地
问候了许多朋友与侍女。
奥特龙坐在她身旁；
她的容颜渐渐透出红晕，

===Original===
 With eating and with drinking,
 ere many days were ended.
 Enough was always ready:
 still the poor girl her mood ne’er wisely mended.

===Chinese===
吃着美食，饮着美酒，
没过多少时日。
充足的饮食总是备办齐整：
可怜的姑娘，心绪却始终不见明智的起色。

===Original===
If Hartmut thought to greet her,
 and spoke in friendly mood,
 How little did it cheer her!
 She o’er her woes did brood,

===Chinese===
哈特穆特若想来问候她，
说几句亲善的话，
这对她是何等微末的慰藉！
她只顾暗自熬着愁苦，

===Original===
 That she and all her maidens
 in a far-off land were bearing.
 Soon, against young Hartmut,
 of harsh and angry words she was not sparing.

===Chinese===
想着她与全体侍女
在遥远的异乡所承受的一切。
不久，对着年轻的哈特穆特，
她便毫不吝惜尖刻忿怒的言辞。

===Original===
So long a time this lasted,
 the king at length was wroth;
 He said: “Gudrun, fair lady,
 as good am I in birth

===Chinese===
这般情形持续得久了，
国王终于动了怒；
他说：「古德伦，美丽的夫人，
论出身之贵，

===Original===
 As is the young King Herwic,
 who now you think is fitter
 Than I to be your lover:
 too much you jeer at me, with words most bitter.

===Chinese===
我不输年轻的赫尔维克国王，
你如今却觉得他
比我更配做你的情郎：
你太会讥诮我了，言辞何其刻毒。

===Original===
“If you would leave your sorrow,
 for both of us ’twere gain.
 It wounds me out of measure
 when any gives you pain,

===Chinese===
「你若肯抛开愁苦，
对我们两人都是得益。
无论谁给你气受，
都教我伤痛无以复加，

===Original===
 Or seeks your heart to burden,
 or in your wish to cross you:
 Though now you are unfriendly,
 to be my queen I yet would gladly choose you.”

===Chinese===
无论谁想压上你的心头，
或要拂逆你的心愿：
尽管你如今待我不亲善，
我仍甘愿选你做我的王后。」

===Original===
Then young Hartmut left her,
 and straight his men he sought.
 He bade them to be watchful
 of ills that threatened aught,

===Chinese===
年轻的哈特穆特随即离开她，
径直去寻他的部众。
他吩咐他们时刻戒备，
防备种种不测之祸，

===Original===
 And well to guard his kingdom;
 for he the while bethought him,
 So sorely was he hated,
 ’twas much to fear some harm would yet be wrought him.

===Chinese===
并用心守卫他的王国；
因为他心中暗自思量，
自己遭人恨之入骨，
只怕日后再有祸事临头。

===Original===
The cross and wicked Gerlind
 for her hard tasks did set;
 She on a seat but seldom
 any rest did get.

===Chinese===
乖戾狠毒的格尔林德（Gerlind）
给她派下了苦役；
她难得在座席之上
得到片刻安歇。

===Original===
 Erst ’mong princes’ daughters
 men were wont to greet her,
 As for her was rightful;
 now with the scorned and lowly they must meet her.

===Chinese===
往日在诸侯的女儿们中间，
人们惯常向她致敬，
这本是她分内的礼遇；
如今人们只得在受尽轻贱的卑贱者中间与她相见。

===Original===
To her, in mood unfriendly,
 the old she-wolf then spake:
 “Now Queen Hilda’s daughter
 I a drudge will make;

===Chinese===
她怀着满腔恶意，
那老母狼随即开言：
「如今我要把王后希尔达（Hilda）的女儿
使唤成一个做苦工的婢仆；

===Original===
 Although her evil feelings
 seem so strong and steady,
 We yet shall see her toiling
 as ne’er before to do has she been ready.”

===Chinese===
尽管她的恶念
显得如此顽固执拗，
我们且看她去苦役里操劳——
从前她可从不曾甘愿动手。」

===Original===
Then said the high-born maiden:
 “To work with all my might,
 With hand and heart, I’m willing;
 in this, both day and night,

===Chinese===
这位出身高贵的少女说道：
「竭尽我的全力去劳作，
凭双手与真心，我都甘愿；
在这上头，无论白昼黑夜，

===Original===
 Will I be always busy,
 and every hour be striving;
 Since ill-luck begrudges
 that I among my friends should now be living.”

===Chinese===
我都愿时时操劳，
每个时辰都奋力而为；
只因厄运见不得
我如今还活在亲友中间。」

===Original===
The wicked Gerlind answered:
 “Now daily to the beach
 You my clothes must carry,
 there on the sands to bleach.

===Chinese===
狠毒的格尔林德答道：
「从今你要天天把我的衣裳
背到海滩上去，
摊在沙地上漂晒。

===Original===
 You must for me and my maidens
 be washing and be drying;
 And that no one find you idle,
 your work with care you ever must be plying.”

===Chinese===
你要替我和我的侍女们
又洗又晾；
为了教谁也见不得你偷闲，
你的活计须得时时用心赶做。」

===Original===
Then spake the high-born maiden:
 “Wife of a mighty king,
 If they will only teach me
 the way to wash and wring,

===Chinese===
这位出身高贵的少女说道：
「强大的国王之妻啊，
只要她们肯教我
洗濯拧绞的门道，

===Original===
 And how to cleanse your clothing,
 to do it I am willing.
 Bliss no more I look for;
 still greater woe my heart must yet be filling.

===Chinese===
教我怎样洗净您的衣裳，
我都甘愿去做。
福祉我已不再指望；
更大的悲苦还须灌满我的心。

===Original===
“Bid them now to teach me,
 and I will gladly learn;
 So high I do not hold me
 that I the task should spurn.

===Chinese===
「吩咐她们这就来教我，
我必欣然学习；
我并不把自己
抬举到不屑做这活计。

===Original===
 Thus shall I be earning
 the food I here am eating;
 Nought I say against it.”
 The poor Gudrun her lot was wisely meeting.

===Chinese===
这样，我便挣得了
在此地吃下的饮食；
对此我没有半句怨言。」
可怜的古德伦就这样明智地安顿了自己的命运。

===Original===
Then by a washerwoman
 clothes to the sands were brought,
 And how to wash and dry them
 the maiden now was taught.

===Chinese===
随后由一名洗衣妇
把衣裳送到沙滩上，
怎样洗、怎样晾晒，
这少女如今都受教了一番。

===Original===
 Much at first she sorrowed,
 and by the work was flurried,
 Yet was she spared by no one.
 So was the fair Gudrun by Gerlind worried.

===Chinese===
起初她满心悲苦，
被这活计弄得慌乱无措，
然而无人肯放过她。
美丽的古德伦就这样受着格尔林德的煎熬。

===Original===
Before King Ludwig’s castle,
 she gained a skilful hand;
 For knights who there were dwelling
 within the Norman land,

===Chinese===
在路德维希国王的城堡前，
她练出了一双巧手；
因为对于栖身
诺曼底之地的骑士们，

===Original===
 None could be more helpful,
 their clothing better washing.
 Loudly mourned her maidens
 to see her toiling where the waves were dashing.

===Chinese===
无人比她更堪效力，
洗衣裳洗得无人更精。
她的侍女们放声哀恸，
眼见她在波浪拍击之处操劳。

===Original===
One there was among them
 who was also a great king’s child;
 The wailing of the others
 was to hers a whisper mild.

===Chinese===
她们中间有一人，
同样是一位伟大国王之女；
其余人的哀哭
比之她的恸哭，只算轻柔的低语。

===Original===
 This work so mean and lowly
 went to their hearts too nearly,
 As they saw the high-born lady
 drudging on the shore, both late and early.

===Chinese===
这等卑贱低微的活计，
直刺她们的心底，
眼见这位出身高贵的女主人
起早贪黑地在海滩上苦熬。

===Original===
Then with love true-hearted
 Hildeburg made moan:
 “Well we all must rue it﻿—
 to God may this be known﻿—

===Chinese===
于是赤诚相爱的希尔德堡
悲声叹惋：
「我们全都悔不当初——
愿上帝知晓这一切——

===Original===
 Who in this Norman kingdom
 erst with Gudrun were landing;
 No rest ought we to hope for
 while on the sea-beach washing she is standing.”

===Chinese===
我们当初随古德伦
登上这诺曼底王国的土地；
只要她还立在海滩上洗衣，
我们便休想指望安歇。」

===Original===
This was heard by Gerlind,
 who in anger spoke:
 “If on the toils of your lady
 with such ill-will you look,

===Chinese===
这话被格尔林德听去，
她怒气冲冲地说：
「你若怀着这等恶意
眼看你们女主人服苦役，

===Original===
 The work shall you be doing,
 and her place be filling.”
 “That would I do right gladly,”
 said Hildeburg, “if only you were willing.

===Chinese===
这活计就由你来承担，
顶了她的位置。」
「这事我千肯万肯，」
希尔德堡说，「只消您肯点头。

===Original===
“For the love of God Almighty,
 Gerlind, my lady queen,
 Let not this great king’s daughter
 toiling alone be seen:

===Chinese===
「看在全能上帝的份上，
格尔林德，我的王后夫人，
别让这位伟大国王的女儿
孤零零地在劳苦中受人观看：

===Original===
 A crown, too, wore my father,
 yet work would I be doing;
 Let me with her stand washing,
 whatever good or ill we may be knowing.

===Chinese===
我的父亲也曾头戴王冠，
我却甘愿来做这活计；
容我与她一同站着洗衣，
无论我们日后遭逢的是福是祸。

===Original===
“It fills my heart with sorrow,
 I feel her woes my own.
 Once the greatest honor
 to her by God was shown:

===Chinese===
「这使我心中充满悲伤，
我把她的苦难当作自己的苦难。
上帝曾一度赐给她
至高无上的荣光：

===Original===
 Her forefathers and kindred
 were kings, and none were higher;
 Though now her work is lowly,
 to toil with the maiden I shall never tire.”

===Chinese===
她的列祖与亲族
皆为君王，无人更为尊贵；
纵然如今她的活计卑贱，
与这少女一同操劳，我永不倦怠。」

===Original===
Then said the wicked Gerlind:
 “This oft will bring you pain;
 However hard the winter,
 still in snow and rain

===Chinese===
狠毒的格尔林德说道：
「这常会给你带来苦痛；
任凭寒冬何等严酷，
顶着风雪、冒着冷雨，

===Original===
 My clothes must you be washing,
 altho’ cold winds are blowing;
 So will you be wishing
 that you the warmth of heated rooms were knowing.”

===Chinese===
你都得替我洗衣，
哪怕寒风呼啸；
到那时你就会渴望
领受一番暖房里的热气了。」

===Original===
Unwillingly she waited
 until the night drew near;
 From this Gudrun the high-born
 gained at last some cheer.

===Chinese===
她不情愿地苦挨着，
直到夜色临近；
出身高贵的古德伦
由此终于得了些许宽慰。

===Original===
 Then into her bedroom
 went Hildeburg in sorrow;
 There they wept together
 for the work that they must do upon the morrow.

===Chinese===
随后希尔德堡满怀哀伤，
走进她的卧房；
两人在那里相对而泣，
为着明日不得不做的活计。

===Original===
Then the Lady Hildeburg
 said to her in tears:
 “The woes that you are bearing
 my heart with you now shares;

===Chinese===
于是希尔德堡夫人
噙着泪对她说：
「你所承受的苦难，
我的心如今与你共担；

===Original===
 I begged the old she-devil
 no more alone to leave you
 Upon the sea-sands washing;
 with you I’ll bear the burden, and my help will give you.”

===Chinese===
我已恳求那老女魔，
莫再让你独自一人
留在海滩上洗衣；
我要与你共挑重担，把我的助力给你。」

===Original===
The homeless maiden answered:
 “May Christ your love reward,
 That you with so much sorrow
 of all my woes have heard.

===Chinese===
那无家可归的少女答道：
「愿基督报偿你的爱心，
你怀着这许多哀伤，
听遍了我全部的苦情。

===Original===
 If we may wash together,
 the days will be the brighter,
 And time will seem far shorter,
 and on our hearts the shame will weigh the lighter.”

===Chinese===
倘若我们能一同洗衣，
日子会变得明亮些，
光阴也会显得短些，
压在我们心头的羞辱也会轻些。」

===Original===
Soon as her wish was granted,
 down to the sandy shore
 The clothing then she carried,
 gladness to know no more.

===Chinese===
她的心愿一经获准，
便把衣裳背起，
走到下面的沙滩上，
从此再不知欢欣为何物。

===Original===
 There must they wash in sorrow,
 whatever was the weather;
 Whate’er was done by others,
 yet still these two must wash and toil together.

===Chinese===
无论天气如何，
她们都得在那里含悲洗衣；
旁人做下什么都不相干，
这两人却始终要一同洗濯、一同操劳。

===Original===
When her throng of handmaidens
 had time from work to spare,
 Bitter was their weeping,
 to see her standing there

===Chinese===
每当她的一大群侍女
从劳作中得了空闲，
眼见她站在那里，
她们便哀哀痛哭，

===Original===
 Upon the sea-sands washing.
 Loud were their moans and many,
 Nor did their sorrow lessen;
 greater woe was never known by any.

===Chinese===
在海滩上洗着衣裳。
她们的悲叹又多又响；
她们的哀伤从不稍减；
世人从未见过比这更深的苦难。

===Original===
Long the toiling lasted﻿—
 that is true enough;
 There must they be working
 full five years and a half.

===Chinese===
这苦役延续了许久——
这话千真万确；
她们在那里操劳了
足足五年半的光景。

===Original===
 Clothes for Hartmut’s followers
 they must wash and whiten:
 Ne’er were maidens sadder;
 their toils before the castle nought could lighten.

===Chinese===
哈特穆特部众的衣裳，
都得由她们洗濯漂白：
再没有比这更悲伤的少女；
城堡前的这番辛劳，无物能使之减轻。

## Tale XXII How Hilda Made War to Bring Back Her Daughter / 第二十二诗章 希尔达如何兴兵夺回她的女儿

===Original===
Hilda summons her vassals to make an expedition against the Normans. After visiting the graves of their kinsmen on the Wulpensand, they pass in safety the magnetic rocks at Givers, and arrive on the coast of Normandy.

===Chinese===
希尔达（Hilda）召集封臣，出征诺曼人。众人在武尔彭沙洲（Wulpensand）凭吊过亲人的坟茔之后，平安驶过吉弗斯（Givers）的磁岩，抵达诺曼底（Normandy）海岸。

===Original===
We now will speak no longer
 of the toil the maidens bore
 For knights as well as ladies.
 Queen Hilda evermore

===Chinese===
如今我们不再多讲
少女们承受的苦役，
无论对骑士还是贵妇。
王后希尔达却始终

===Original===
 Her thoughts to this had given
 how to win back her daughter,
 Out of the Norman kingdom,
 whither from home the daring Hartmut brought her.

===Chinese===
把心思全倾在这上头：
如何夺回她的女儿，
救出那诺曼人的王国——
胆大妄为的哈特穆特（Hartmut）把她从家中掳到了那里。

===Original===
First were workmen bidden,
 near to the deep sea-flood,
 Of ships to build her seven,
 strong, well made, and good;

===Chinese===
首先她召集工匠，
在深深的海潮近旁，
为她造起七艘战船，
坚固精良，尽善尽美；

===Original===
 With two-and-twenty barges,
 broad, with both ends rounded.
 Whate’er for them was needed
 was quickly brought, and everything abounded.

===Chinese===
外加二十二艘驳船，
船身宽阔，两端浑圆。
凡是所需的物事
都迅速办齐，样样充盈。

===Original===
Forty galleys also
 lay upon the sea;
 On these her eyes were feeding.
 Longing great had she

===Chinese===
还有四十艘桨帆战船
停卧在大海之上；
她望着它们目不转睛。
她怀着炽烈的渴望，

===Original===
 To see the throng of fighters
 who should soon be sailing.
 She their food made ready;
 for this the knights her praise were loudly telling.

===Chinese===
盼见那一群武士
不日扬帆出海。
她亲手替他们备办饮食；
骑士们为此齐声颂扬她。

===Original===
The time was drawing nearer,
 when now to cross the sea
 No more should they be waiting,
 who wished the maids to free,

===Chinese===
时辰渐渐临近，
此刻渡海之事
再不容他们等待——
那些立誓要解救少女的人，

===Original===
 That in a far-off kingdom
 in hardest toil were living.
 Now Hilda sent for her liegemen;
 to those who called them clothes she first was giving.

===Chinese===
少女们正在遥远的国度
受着最严酷的苦役。
希尔达随即召来她的封臣；
又先把衣裳发给前去传召的人们。

===Original===
The day that she had chosen
 was at the Christmas-tide,
 When they must seek the foemen
 by whom King Hettel died.

===Chinese===
她择定的日子
正当圣诞时节，
那时他们要去寻那班仇敌——
正是害死黑特尔（Hettel）国王的凶手。

===Original===
 Forthwith to friends and kinsmen
 Hilda gave her bidding,
 That they to bring her daughter
 back from the Norman land must then be speeding.

===Chinese===
希尔达当即向亲友
颁下她的号令，
命他们速速成行，
去把她的女儿从诺曼底（Normandy）之地接回。

===Original===
Trusty men were bidden
 by Hilda first to go
 To Herwic and his followers,
 that one and all should know

===Chinese===
希尔达先遣心腹
即刻登程，
去见赫尔维克（Herwic）和他的部众，
好教他们人人知晓

===Original===
 Of the inroad on the Normans
 that she had sworn and plotted.
 To many Hegeling children
 this erelong an orphan’s life allotted.

===Chinese===
她已立誓筹谋，
要对诺曼人大举进犯。
不久，许多黑格林人（Hegeling）的孩儿
便由此被派定了孤儿的生涯。

===Original===
The men sent out by Hilda
 to Herwic rode in haste:
 For what they then were coming
 the king full quickly guessed;

===Chinese===
希尔达遣出的使者
策马疾驰去见赫尔维克：
他们此来为了何事，
国王一猜便中；

===Original===
 Then went he forth to meet them,
 soon as he saw them nearing;
 Gladly them he greeted,
 and soon from them Queen Hilda’s wish was hearing.

===Chinese===
他一望见他们临近，
便出门相迎；
他欣然向他们致意，
随即听明了希尔达王后的心愿。

===Original===
“Well you know, Lord Herwic,
 our woe and plight forlorn,
 And how the Hegeling warriors
 to help the queen have sworn.

===Chinese===
「赫尔维克大人，你深知
我们的哀恸与孤绝的苦境，
也知黑格林的武士们
已立誓鼎助王后。

===Original===
 Yourself Queen Hilda trusteth
 more than any other;
 To none Gudrun is dearer﻿—
 the homeless maid, long sundered from her mother.”

===Chinese===
希尔达王后对您信重，
胜过世上任何人；
再没有人比您更疼古德伦（Gudrun）——
这无家可归的少女，与母亲睽违已久。」

===Original===
The well-born knight thus answered:
 “I know in truth too well
 How Hartmut had the boldness
 my fair betrothed to steal,

===Chinese===
那位出身高贵的骑士答道：
「我实在知道得太清楚，
哈特穆特是何等胆大包天，
竟劫走我美丽的未婚妻，

===Original===
 Because his love she slighted,
 and hearkened to my wooing;
 For this Gudrun, my lady,
 her father lost, and still her lot is ruing.

===Chinese===
只因她薄了他的情意，
而俯允了我的求亲；
为此，古德伦，我的夫人，
失去了父亲，至今犹自哀叹命运。

===Original===
“My pledge and hearty greeting
 bear to your lady good;
 No more the Norman Hartmut
 by me shall be allowed

===Chinese===
「请把我的誓言与诚挚的问候
带给你们贤德的王后；
那诺曼人哈特穆特，休想再
得到我的容许，

===Original===
 To hold so long in bondage
 my own betrothèd maiden:
 For me, of all, ’tis fittest
 to bring the lady home, our lives to gladden.

===Chinese===
把我订下婚约的少女
这样久久拘为奴婢：
在众人之中唯我最宜
把这位夫人接回家来，为我们添欢。

===Original===
“To Hilda and her kinsmen
 this answer you may say:
 When Christmas time is over,
 on the sixth-and-twentieth day,

===Chinese===
「请向希尔达和她的亲族
转达这样的答复：
待圣诞时节一过，
到那第二十六日，

===Original===
 I will ride to the Hegelings,
 three thousand fighters taking.”
 Then the men of Hilda
 waited no more, but home their way were making.

===Chinese===
我便驰往黑格林人那里，
带上三千武士。」
希尔达的使者们
不再耽搁，当即踏上归程。

===Original===
Now Herwic made him ready,
 and to the strife gave thought,
 With many faithful liegemen
 who oft had bravely fought.

===Chinese===
于是赫尔维克整备停当，
一心思谋争战，
身边聚着许多忠实的封臣，
都曾屡屡奋勇作战。

===Original===
 Those who to go were willing
 he for war outfitted;
 Though wintry was the weather,
 they to take the field no longer waited.

===Chinese===
凡愿随征的人，
他都披挂武装；
纵然天时正值隆冬，
他们上阵也不再迟延。

===Original===
Of help the widowed Hilda
 sorely felt the need:
 Soon to her friends in Denmark
 she sent her men with speed,

===Chinese===
孀居的希尔达深感
援手之急需：
她随即火速遣人
去往丹麦（Denmark）的友人们那里，

===Original===
 To tell the knights and warriors
 no more at home to tarry;
 For they to the Norman kingdom
 must ride, to free Gudrun from bondage dreary.

===Chinese===
传告骑士与武士们，
不可再滞留家中；
因为他们必须驰赴
诺曼人的王国，救古德伦脱离凄苦的奴役。

===Original===
They bore to the youthful Horant
 this errand from the queen:
 That he and all his kinsmen
 were to her lord of kin,

===Chinese===
他们向年轻的霍兰特（Horant）
传了王后的这桩使命：
说他与他全体的亲族
于她夫君皆为亲属，

===Original===
 And the sorrows of her daughter
 should by them be heeded;
 For death to her were better
 than ever that her child to Hartmut should be wedded.

===Chinese===
她女儿的苦难
理当他们挂怀；
因为对她而言，宁可一死，
也强似女儿终有一日嫁给哈特穆特。

===Original===
Then sent the knight this answer:
 “Unto Queen Hilda say﻿—
 Though yet ’twill cost to women
 many a bitter day,

===Chinese===
那位骑士随即回话：
「请转告希尔达王后——
尽管此事还将教妇人们
捱过许多辛酸的日子，

===Original===
 I still, with all my followers,
 will help be gladly giving;
 For this will be heard the weeping
 of many a mother’s child, in the land now living.

===Chinese===
我仍愿偕我全体的部众，
欣然出力相助；
因为今世还活着的人里，
多少母亲的孩子将为此哀哭。

===Original===
“I bid you now, moreover,
 to say unto the queen﻿—
 Ere many days are ended,
 in her land will I be seen;

===Chinese===
「此外我还要你们
转告王后——
用不了多少时日，
我便会现身在她的国土；

===Original===
 Tell her that my wishes
 all to war are bending,
 And soon ten thousand warriors
 from out the Danish land will I be sending.”

===Chinese===
告诉她，我的心愿
全都倾注在战事上，
不久我将从丹麦之地
遣出一万名武士。」

===Original===
The men sent there by Hilda
 of Horant took their leave:
 They sped to the Waalisch marches,
 and found Morunc the brave

===Chinese===
希尔达遣到那里的使者
向霍兰特辞行：
他们又疾赴瓦尔河（Waalisch）边地，
寻见勇武的莫伦克（Morunc）

===Original===
 With all his men about him,
 a margrave rich and daring.
 He gladly saw them coming,
 and of a loving welcome was not sparing.

===Chinese===
他部下的兵众环侍四周，
是一位豪富而胆壮的边地侯爵（margrave）。
他欣然看着他们到来，
挚切的欢迎毫不吝惜。

===Original===
Then spake the knightly Irold:
 “Since now by me ’tis known
 That into the Hegeling kingdom,
 before seven weeks are gone,

===Chinese===
骑士伊罗尔德（Irold）随即开言：
「既然如今我已得知，
七周未尽之前，就要
驰进黑格林人的王国，

===Original===
 I with all my followers
 am bidden to be riding,
 For this will I be ready,
 whatever luck be there for us betiding.”

===Chinese===
我便要偕全体部众
奉召骑行；
为此我必整装以待，
无论何种命运在前头等着我们。」

===Original===
The news was spread by Morunc,
 within the Holstein land,
 That Hilda now was sending
 for all her friends at hand;

===Chinese===
莫伦克又把消息
传遍荷尔斯泰因（Holstein）之地：
希尔达如今正遣人
召集她四近的亲友；

===Original===
 He said that all good warriors
 must the field be taking.
 To the Danish knight, brave Fru-te,
 they also gave the word, his help bespeaking.

===Chinese===
他说凡是好武士
都必须披挂上阵。
他们又向丹麦骑士、勇武的弗鲁特（Fru-te）
传话，请他出力相助。

===Original===
The worthy knight, then answering,
 his ready will did show:
 “Back to her home will we bring her.
 Thirteen years ago,

===Chinese===
那可敬的骑士当即作答，
表明他慨然效命的心意：
「我们定要把她接回家去。
十三年前，

===Original===
 We swore the land of the Normans
 should with war be wasted;
 ’Twas then the friends of Hartmut
 stole the maid Gudrun, and homeward hasted.”

===Chinese===
我们立誓：定要叫
诺曼人的国土遭兵燹摧残；
正是那时，哈特穆特的同党
劫走了少女古德伦，匆匆奔回了家。」

===Original===
Wâ-te, the knight from Sturmland,
 to this at once gave thought,
 How he might also help her.
 Altho’ he yet knew nought

===Chinese===
施图尔姆兰（Sturmland）的骑士瓦特（Wâ-te）
闻讯立刻动了心思，
盘算自己如何也能相助。
尽管他还不曾听闻

===Original===
 Of the word that Hilda sent him,
 yet he at once bestirred him;
 Of his knights a goodly number
 then in haste he called, who gladly heard him.

===Chinese===
希尔达捎给他的口信，
他却当即振作起来；
他麾下为数可观的骑士
被他匆匆召集，个个欣然听命。

===Original===
All of them were busy
 with care for the coming war;
 Wâ-te the old from Sturmland
 brought from near and far

===Chinese===
众人全都忙着
为将临的战事操劳；
施图尔姆兰的老瓦特
从远近四方招来了

===Original===
 Full a thousand kinsmen,
 for the fight well fitted;
 With these he hoped that Hartmut
 would soon be overcome and be outwitted.

===Chinese===
足足一千名亲族，
个个堪当鏖战；
他指望凭这班人马，
教哈特穆特速速折戟、中计成擒。

===Original===
The sad and homeless women
 in toil and pain were kept
 By the cross and evil Gerlind:
 but fewer wrongs were heaped

===Chinese===
那些悲苦无家可归的妇人，
被乖戾狠毒的格尔林德（Gerlind）
困在苦役与伤痛之中：
只是堆在赫加特（Hergart）夫人

===Original===
 Upon the Lady Hergart;
 (this name to her was given:)
 She loved the king’s high cup-bearer,
 and greatly hoped to be a princess even.

===Chinese===
身上的欺凌要少些；
（这名字便是这样加诸她身的：）
她爱慕国王的高阶司酒官，
满心盼望自己有朝一日也能做王妃。

===Original===
For this fair Hilda’s daughter
 often sorely wept;
 And Hergart, too, yet later
 woe and sorrow reaped,

===Chinese===
为此，美丽的希尔达之女
常常哀哀哭泣；
而赫加特后来也
收获了哀伤与愁苦，

===Original===
 Because she ne’er with others
 would their toils be sharing.
 Whate’er to her might happen,
 Gudrun for all her ills was little caring.

===Chinese===
因为她从不肯与旁人
一同分担苦役。
无论她自己会遭逢什么，
古德伦对自己身受的苦难全不放在心上。

===Original===
Of the Hegelings none were idle,
 as you before have heard:
 Though many for all their toiling
 would find but scant reward,

===Chinese===
黑格林人中无人闲坐，
一如你们此前所闻：
尽管许多人纵然操劳，
所得的报偿也微薄，

===Original===
 Yet all within the kingdom their
 ready help were lending.
 Now the knights were thinking
 for the brother of Gudrun ’twere best they should be sending.

===Chinese===
王国之内的所有人
却仍踊跃效力。
此刻骑士们商议，
最好还是派人去请古德伦的弟弟。

===Original===
Riders then went swiftly
 into the land of the North,
 And found in an open meadow
 the youth of kingly birth,

===Chinese===
骑手们随即疾驰而去，
奔往北方的国土，
在一片开阔的草地上
寻到了那位王家血胤的少年，

===Original===
 Where by the edge of a river
 many birds were flocking:
 There with his trusty falconer
 he showed his skill, and spent his time in hawking.

===Chinese===
那里在一条河的近旁，
群鸟正纷纷翔集：
他与亲信的驯鹰官在那里
炫示身手，以放鹰行猎消磨光阴。

===Original===
As soon as, riding quickly,
 these by him were seen,
 He said: “Those men now coming
 are sent to us by the queen;

===Chinese===
他远远望见那些人
策马疾驰而来，
便说道：「如今来的那些人，
是王后差来见我们的；

===Original===
 They come to give her bidding,
 proudly hither hasting;
 My mother thinketh wrongly
 that we the war forget, and time are wasting.”

===Chinese===
他们奉她的旨意，
意气昂扬地疾驰到此；
我母亲错怪了我们，
只当忘了战事、虚掷光阴。」

===Original===
He set his hawk a-flying,
 and thence at once he rode.
 Very soon thereafter
 darkened was his mood;

===Chinese===
他把猎鹰放上天去，
随即策马离了那地方。
此后不久，
他的心绪便阴沉下来；

===Original===
 For when the men he greeted,
 and they their tale were telling,
 He learned that the queen, his mother,
 ever in tears her loss was aye bewailing.

===Chinese===
因为待他问候过来使，
听他们讲明来意，
他才得知王后，他的母亲，
还在终日以泪哀悼着自己的失丧。

===Original===
She to the youthful warrior
 sent her greeting kind:
 In her wretched lot, she asked him
 what might be his mind;

===Chinese===
她向这位年轻的武士
致以亲切的问候：
在悲惨的命运里，她问他
心中作何打算；

===Original===
 And asked how many followers
 he could to the war be leading;
 For from the Hegeling kingdom
 they all to the Norman land must soon be speeding.

===Chinese===
又问他能率领
多少部众赴战；
因为从黑格林人的王国，
他们全都要速速开赴诺曼底之地。

===Original===
Then Ortwin sent this answer:
 “Me dost thou rightly bid;
 I from hence will hasten,
 and bring from far and wide

===Chinese===
奥特温（Ortwin）随即回了话：
「你这样吩咐我，正合其宜；
我即刻从此地兼程赶路，
从四面八方召集

===Original===
 Twenty thousand fighters﻿—
 men both brave and daring;
 These my steps will follow
 even to death, their lives and homes forswearing.”

===Chinese===
两万名战士——
个个勇武刚胆；
他们将追随我的脚步
直至赴死，不惜舍却性命与家园。」

===Original===
Now from every border
 many warriors went
 Riding to Hilda’s kingdom,
 for whom the queen had sent;

===Chinese===
于是从每一处边陲，
许多武士启程，
驰往希尔达的王国——
他们正是王后所召之人；

===Original===
 They vied with one another,
 to win her praises striving.
 Not less than sixty thousand
 together came, their help for Hilda giving.

===Chinese===
他们你争我赶，
力求博得她的嘉许。
足有不下六万人
济济而至，为希尔达效力。

===Original===
On the river Waal Sir Morunc
 had upon the wave
 Of broad-built ships full sixty,
 strong to bear the brave

===Chinese===
在瓦尔河（Waal）上，莫伦克爵士
备下整整六十艘
宽体巨船浮波而列，
坚牢堪载勇士，

===Original===
 Who with the Hegelings sailing
 would o’er the sea be carried,
 To free Gudrun, the maiden,
 who sadly now among the Normans tarried.

===Chinese===
载他们随黑格林人扬帆，
渡过大海，
去解救少女古德伦——
她此刻正凄凄地滞留在诺曼人中间。

===Original===
From out the Northland also
 finest ships were brought,
 With horses and with clothing,
 as good as could be sought:

===Chinese===
从北方的国土
也运来了上好的船只，
连同战马与衣装，
精良得无可更求：

===Original===
 Decked were all the helmets,
 the weapons glittered brightly,
 Ready for the onset
 bravely they came, in armor fair and knightly.

===Chinese===
头盔顶顶装点得华美，
兵刃闪烁生光，
只待冲锋陷阵，
他们便披挂堂堂骑士甲胄，英武而来。

===Original===
Now by their shields men reckoned
 how many there might be
 Who to the Norman kingdom
 would go the maid to free,

===Chinese===
人们按着盾牌清点
人数究竟几何——
那些要开赴诺曼人的王国
解救少女的战士，

===Original===
 And to the great Queen Hilda
 their help to give were ready;
 They numbered seventy thousand;
 gifts to all were given by the queenly lady.

===Chinese===
并甘愿向伟大的希尔达王后
献上援手的人；
计点得七万之众；
这位王后般的贵妇向众人颁了赏。

===Original===
On all who there were gathered,
 or to court who later came,
 The queen, though ever mournful,
 yet let her kindness beam:

===Chinese===
凡在那里聚集的人，
或是日后赶来宫廷的人，
王后虽然长怀哀恸，
仍让慈惠之光焕发：

===Original===
 She gave them hearty welcome,
 and every one she greeted;
 Wondrous was the clothing
 that to the chosen knights Queen Hilda meted.

===Chinese===
她向他们殷勤相迎，
逐一问候了每个人；
希尔达王后颁发给
拣选的骑士们的衣装，华美得出奇。

===Original===
The many ships of Hilda
 were stored with all things well,
 And early on the morrow
 were ready thence to sail;

===Chinese===
希尔达的众多船只
都装载得妥妥帖帖，
翌日一早
便整备待发；

===Original===
 Seemly was the outfit
 for her worthy guests who waited:
 They chose not to be going,
 while aught they lacked to meet the foeman hated.

===Chinese===
为候在那里的贵客
置办的行装体面周全：
但凡还缺一件对敌之物，
他们便不肯启程。

===Original===
They put on board the weapons,
 as was the queen’s behest,
 And with them many helmets
 of beaten steel the best.

===Chinese===
他们遵王后之命
把兵刃装上船，
又随载了许多头盔，
都是锻打精钢中的上品。

===Original===
 Hauberks white were given,
 besides the ones in wearing,
 For warriors full five hundred;
 these she bade them take, to war now faring.

===Chinese===
还发下了雪白的锁子甲，
在正穿着的之外，
足够整整五百名武士；
她吩咐他们带上，就此出征。

===Original===
Their anchor-ropes well twisted
 of strongest silk were made:
 Their sails both rich and showy
 to the winds were spread;

===Chinese===
他们的锚缆搓绞得
极其坚韧，以最坚的丝制成：
船帆又华贵又炫目，
迎着风张展开来；

===Original===
 These to the shores of the Norman
 the Hegelings would carry,
 Who back to Lady Hilda
 would gladly bring Gudrun, of waiting weary.

===Chinese===
黑格林人要驾着它们
驶往诺曼人的海滨，
好欣然把古德伦接回
希尔达夫人身边——等待早已叫人望眼欲穿。

===Original===
The anchors for the sailors
 were not of iron made,
 But of bell-metal moulded;
 (so have we heard it said:)

===Chinese===
船上众水手用的锚
并非用铁铸成，
而是以铸钟青铜浇铸；
（我们是这般听说的：）

===Original===
 They with Spanish brasses
 all were bound and strengthened,
 That loadstones should not hold them,
 and so the sailors’ way by this be lengthened.

===Chinese===
又全用西班牙的铜箍
缠束加固，
免得磁石（loadstone）将它们吸住，
平白把水手的航程拖长。

===Original===
To Wâ-te and his followers
 the Lady Hilda gave
 Many clasps and arm-bands.
 This roused the strong and brave

===Chinese===
希尔达夫人向瓦特
和他的部众
颁赐了许多扣饰与臂环。
这激起了刚强勇武之士

===Original===
 To meet their death from foemen,
 for the Hegelings fighting,
 When they from Hartmut’s castle
 strove to wrest the maid, in bondage sitting.

===Chinese===
迎着仇敌赴死的决心，
为黑格林人奋战，
只待从哈特穆特的城堡
夺回那被拘为奴的少女。

===Original===
Freely then and earnestly
 Queen Hilda spoke her thought
 Unto the men from Daneland:
 “When you have bravely fought

===Chinese===
希尔达王后随即恳切而坦率地
向丹麦兰（Daneland）来的人们
吐露了心声：
「待你们英勇地打完

===Original===
 On the stormy field of warfare,
 I will reward you fitly.
 Still my banner follow;
 that will show the way, and lead you rightly.”

===Chinese===
那风雨如晦的战阵，
我必酬赏得宜。
只管紧随我的旌旗；
它会指引正路，领你们走得分毫不差。」

===Original===
They asked of her, who held it;
 to this then answered she:
 “He bears the name of Horant;
 a Danish lord is he.

===Chinese===
他们问她，是谁执掌旌旗；
她这样作答：
「执旗者名叫霍兰特；
是丹麦的一位领主。

===Original===
 His mother, Hettel’s sister,
 she it was who bore him;
 Let him by you be trusted;
 forsake him not in fight with foes before him.

===Chinese===
他的母亲，黑特尔的妹妹，
正是生他之人；
你们只管信他；
临阵当敌之时，切莫将他抛下。

===Original===
“Never, my hardy warriors,
 must you forget my son,
 Young Ortwin, dear-belovèd,
 to manhood nearly grown.

===Chinese===
「我剽悍的武士们，
你们万万不可忘了我儿，
年轻的奥特温，我的心肝，
已快要长成堂堂男子。

===Original===
 Of life the youth has numbered
 twenty years already;
 If any risk should threaten,
 to guard him well then let your help be speedy.”

===Chinese===
这少年已在人生中
数满了二十个年头；
倘若有何凶险相逼，
你们务须火速护卫周全。」

===Original===
To this they pledged them gladly,
 and all together said,
 So long as they were with him
 nought had he to dread;

===Chinese===
他们欣然立下承诺，
齐声说道，
只要他们在他身旁，
他便一无所惧；

===Original===
 If he their lead would follow,
 those from whom he parted
 Again unharmed would see him.
 At this young Ortwin showed himself light-hearted.

===Chinese===
他若肯听他们指引，
那些与他分别的人
必能再见他安然无恙。
年轻的奥特温闻言，露出了轻快的神色。

===Original===
Soon the ships were laden
 with goods of every kind,
 And now to tell his wonder
 none fit words could find.

===Chinese===
船只很快装满了
形形色色的物资，
而今要述说此番奇观，
谁也觅不得贴切的言语。

===Original===
 They asked good Hilda’s blessing
 on the work now undertaken;
 The queen then begged of Heaven
 that they by Christ should never be forsaken.

===Chinese===
他们请仁厚的希尔达
为着手的大事降福；
王后则向苍天祈祝，
愿基督永不将他们弃绝。

===Original===
Many youths went with them
 whose fathers erst were slain;
 Now bereft, these brave ones
 to right their wrongs were fain.

===Chinese===
随行的还有许多青年，
父亲当年惨遭杀戮；
如今这些失怙的勇士
急欲一雪沉冤。

===Original===
 The women of the Hegelings
 were mourning all and weeping,
 Beseeching God in Heaven
 to bring them back their sons in his holy keeping.

===Chinese===
黑格林人的妇人们
人人哀恸涕泣，
恳求天上的上帝，
以神圣的护佑把儿子们带回来。

===Original===
But all this pain and sorrow
 the warriors might not bear;
 They sternly bade the women
 their bitter wails to spare;

===Chinese===
可这般哀痛悲声，
武士们实难承受；
他们厉声叫妇人们
收住辛酸的恸哭；

===Original===
 Then on their way they started
 in gladness, shouting loudly,
 And as they went on shipboard
 all were heard to sing, and set forth proudly.

===Chinese===
随即他们欣然登程，
高声呼喊喧腾，
待到上得船来，
只闻人人放歌，意气昂扬地进发。

===Original===
After these daring sailors
 had cast off from the land,
 Many sorrowing women
 did at the windows stand:

===Chinese===
待这些剽悍的水手
驶离陆地之后，
许多满怀哀愁的妇人
伫立在一扇扇窗前：

===Original===
 From Matelan’s lofty castle,
 never the watch forsaking,
 Their eyes the sea-path followed,
 as from the land the men their way were taking.

===Chinese===
在马特兰（Matelan）高耸的城堡上，
望楼的守候从未间断，
她们的目光追随着海路，
望着男人们离岸远去。

===Original===
A friendly wind was blowing,
 and loudly cracked the mast;
 They the sails stretched tightly,
 and left the land at last.

===Chinese===
顺风阵阵吹来，
桅杆被风压得高声作响；
他们把船帆绷得紧挺，
终于离了陆地。

===Original===
 The son of many a mother
 went, for honor seeking;
 Though this awaited many,
 yet to gain it they must toil be taking.

===Chinese===
多少母亲的儿子
为求荣名而去；
虽然荣名在望，
要赢得它却须吃尽劳苦。

===Original===
I cannot tell you fully
 of all that them befell,
 Save that the lord of Karadie,
 who in that land did dwell,

===Chinese===
我无法向你们细数
他们遭遇的一切，
只知道卡拉迪（Karadie）之主——
他就居于那片地方——

===Original===
 With fighters came to help them,
 the foeman never fearing;
 He from home brought with him
 ten thousand knights, all men of strength and daring.

===Chinese===
率领武士前来相助，
对仇敌毫无惧色；
他从家中带来
一万名骑士，个个雄健胆豪。

===Original===
Where foes upon the Wulpensand
 had met in deadly fray,
 These knights from many a kingdom,
 now, at this later day,

===Chinese===
从前仇敌们在武尔彭沙洲
性命相搏的地方，
来自许多国度的这些骑士，
而今在后来的这个时日，

===Original===
 Chose the spot for meeting;
 and here they came together:
 A church had here been builded,
 and old and young alike had their gifts brought hither.

===Chinese===
择定了会师之地；
他们在此相聚：
这里曾建起一座教堂，
老老少少都曾把礼物携到此处。

===Original===
Now within its harbor,
 to seek their fathers’ graves,
 Out of the ships here gathered
 went many of Hilda’s braves.

===Chinese===
而今在那港汊之内，
为寻他们父亲的坟茔，
希尔达麾下的许多勇士
从群集的船上登了岸。

===Original===
 Bitter was their sorrow,
 and anger keen did waken;
 Hard would it be for any
 who erst in fight the lives of their friends had taken.

===Chinese===
他们的悲恸何其锥心，
郁怒熊熊燃起；
谁若当年在战阵上
害过他们朋友的性命，往后便难讨得好去。

===Original===
Unto the lord of Moorland
 they hearty welcome gave.
 Four and twenty broad-boats
 he brought with warriors brave;

===Chinese===
他们向摩尔兰（Moorland）之主
致以热诚的欢迎。
他带来了二十四艘
满载勇武士卒的宽底船；

===Original===
 Food therein was laden
 that might for all have lasted
 Till twenty years were ended:
 to war with the Normans now they gladly hasted.

===Chinese===
船上装载的粮食
足够众人支用
直到二十年满：
此刻他们欣然兼程，去与诺曼人交战。

===Original===
When they to sail were ready,
 they left the sheltering shore
 To make their way o’er the waters;
 but heavy toil they bore

===Chinese===
待到扬帆欲发，
他们离了蔽护的海岸，
取道水上进发；
却吃尽了苦头，

===Original===
 Upon the wild sea-billows
 before their sail was ended.
 What helped it that their leaders,
 Fru-te the Dane and Wâ-te, them befriended?

===Chinese===
在狂野的海涛之上，
直熬到航程告终。
纵有首领丹麦人弗鲁特
与瓦特照应，又济得何事？

===Original===
A wind from the south was blowing,
 and drove them out to sea.
 The crew of warlike shipmates
 from fear no more were free;

===Chinese===
一阵南风吹起，
把他们刮向大海。
满船剽悍的同舟伙伴
再也摆脱不掉恐惧；

===Original===
 They could not find the bottom,
 altho’ they should be casting
 Lengths of rope a thousand;
 many sailors wept, their lot foretasting.

===Chinese===
他们探不到海底，
纵使抛下
千寻长绳也无济于事；
许多水手哭了，预尝着命运的苦涩。

===Original===
Before the mount at Givers
 soon lay Queen Hilda’s host;
 However good their anchors,
 upon that gloomy coast,

===Chinese===
不久，在吉弗斯的山前，
泊下了希尔达王后的大军；
无论他们的锚何等精良，
在那阴沉的海岸上，

===Original===
 Drawn by loadstones thither,
 they a long time rested.
 Their masts so tough and hardy
 soon before their eyes were bent and twisted.

===Chinese===
都被磁石吸到那里，
久久滞留难动。
他们坚韧刚硬的桅杆
转眼便当着他们的眼弯折扭曲。

===Original===
When now the hopeless sailors
 were weeping o’er their lot,
 Thus spoke the aged Wâ-te:
 “Anchors again throw out,

===Chinese===
当绝望的水手们
正为自己的命运哭泣，
年迈的瓦特这样说道：
「再抛一次锚，

===Original===
 The strongest and the heaviest,
 into the sea unsounded.
 I’ve heard of many wonders
 I would rather see, than here on the rocks be grounded.

===Chinese===
挑最坚固最沉重的，
抛进莫测深浅的海里。
我听闻过许多奇事，
宁可见识那些，也不愿在此触礁搁浅。

===Original===
“Since, astray long sailing,
 our lady’s ships here lie,
 And we so far are driven
 across the darkling sea,

===Chinese===
「既然长久迷航，
我们夫人的船泊在此地，
我们又被吹打得
远远漂过幽暗的海面，

===Original===
 I now will tell a sea-tale,
 that stirred my childish wonder,
 Of how, near the mount at Givers,
 a kingdom erst was built by a mighty founder.

===Chinese===
我这就讲一个海上奇谈，
它曾搅动我孩童时的惊叹：
说的是在吉弗斯山近旁，
昔日如何由一位雄主奠立了一个王国。

===Original===
“Men there in wealth are living;
 so rich is all their land
 That under the flowing rivers
 silver is the sand;

===Chinese===
「那里的人家活在富足里；
他们的国土富饶至极，
流淌的河床底下，
河沙尽是白银；

===Original===
 With this they make their castles,
 and the stones are golden
 With which their walls are builded.
 In all the kingdom none in want are holden.

===Chinese===
他们用这白银筑造城堡，
砌墙所用的
石头也是黄金。
举国之内无一人受穷。

===Original===
“ ’Twas told to me, moreover,
 (by God are wonders wrought,)
 If one who by the loadstone
 unto this mount is brought,

===Chinese===
「此外还有人告诉过我，
（奇迹本出自上帝，）
谁若被磁石
引到这座山前，

===Original===
 Here will only tarry
 till the wind from the land is blowing,
 He with all his kindred
 may be forever rich when homeward going.

===Chinese===
只须稍作逗留，
等到陆地吹来的风起，
他便能偕全族归家，
从此富贵终身。

===Original===
“Let us our food be eating
 until our luck shall turn,”
 Said then the aged Wâ-te;
 “before we hence are borne,

===Chinese===
「且让我们吃着饮食，
静候时来运转，」
年迈的瓦特说道；
「在我们被带离此地之前，

===Original===
 Our ships that here are lying
 shall with ore be loaded:
 When this we home shall carry,
 wealth shall we have that no one e’er foreboded.”

===Chinese===
泊在这里的船要装上
金银矿砂：
待我们载回家去，
那份富贵将远出世人意料。」

===Original===
Then spake the Danish Fru-te:
 “A still, unruffled sea
 Shall never keep in idleness
 the men now here with me:

===Chinese===
丹麦人弗鲁特随即开言：
「一片死寂无波的海
也休想叫我身边
这些人闲散度日：

===Original===
 A thousand times I swear to you,
 no gold would I be seeking,
 But rather away from this mountain,
 with friendly winds, would I my way be taking.”

===Chinese===
我向你们立上千重的誓，
我不贪求什么黄金，
只想远远离开这座山，
乘着顺风踏上归程。」

===Original===
The Christian men among them
 raised to Heaven a prayer;
 But yet the ships ne’er yielded,
 strongly fastened there:

===Chinese===
他们中间的基督徒
向苍天献上祷告；
然而船只仍纹丝不动，
死死钉在那里：

===Original===
 For four long days or over
 all their hopes were thwarted;
 Sorely feared the Hegelings
 that they from thence could nevermore be started.

===Chinese===
足足四天，或犹不止，
一切指望尽遭摧折；
黑格林人深深忧惧，
再也无法从那里脱身。

===Original===
The clouds now lifted higher,
 as the mighty God had willed;
 Then no more they sorrowed,
 for soon the waves were stilled,

===Chinese===
云层此时向高处退散，
一如全能的上帝所愿；
他们不再哀愁，
因为波涛很快平息，

===Original===
 And from out the darkness
 the sun was shining brightly.
 A wind from the west was blowing,
 and now the woes were o’er of the wanderers knightly.

===Chinese===
太阳从幽暗之中
放射出明亮的光辉。
西风吹拂，
骑士远行者们的苦难至此告终。

===Original===
For miles full six and twenty,
 past Givers’ craggy shore,
 The ships at last were wafted.
 By this they saw yet more

===Chinese===
沿着吉弗斯嶙峋的海岸，
足足二十六英里，
船队终被风送了过去。
由此他们更看得分明——

===Original===
 The work of God and his goodness,
 in all the help then given.
 Wâ-te with his followers
 had been too near the rocks of loadstone driven.

===Chinese===
上帝的作为与他的恩慈，
在那时一切的护佑之中。
瓦特和他的部众
曾被逼得太近磁石的岩礁。

===Original===
To smoothly flowing waters
 they now were come at last:
 Their sins were not rewarded,
 and all their woes were past,

===Chinese===
他们终于来到
平缓流淌的水域：
他们的罪愆未遭清算，
一切苦难都成过往，

===Original===
 While fear from them was taken,
 since God was not unwilling.
 The ships that bore the warriors
 straight to the Norman land at length were sailing.

===Chinese===
恐惧也从他们身上卸去，
因为上帝并非不情愿。
载着武士的船队
终于径直朝诺曼底之地破浪而行。

===Original===
But soon among the sailors
 arose again a wail;
 For now the ships were groaning,
 and soon began to reel,

===Chinese===
可是不久，水手中间
又起了一阵哀号；
因为此刻船身呻吟起来，
随即开始倾侧摇晃，

===Original===
 Tossed among the breakers
 that overwhelmed them nearly:
 Then said the brave knight Ortwin:
 “We now indeed must buy our honors dearly.”

===Chinese===
在碎浪之间颠簸，
险些被整个吞没；
勇武的骑士奥特温说道：
「如今这荣名，我们实在得花大价钱来买了。」

===Original===
Outspake then one of the sailors:
 “Alas! and well-a-day!
 I would we were at Givers,
 and dead near its mountain lay!

===Chinese===
水手中有一人开口道：
「唉！苦哉！
但愿我们还在吉弗斯，
死在那山的近旁倒也甘心！

===Original===
 If one is by God forgotten,
 by whom is he befriended?
 My brave and hardy warriors,
 the roar of the blustering sea is not yet ended.”

===Chinese===
人若被上帝忘却，
还有谁来眷顾他？
我剽悍的勇士们，
怒海的咆哮还没有到头。」

===Original===
Then cried the knight, Sir Horant,
 he of the Danish land:
 “Be of good heart, brave fellows;
 I well can understand

===Chinese===
丹麦之地的骑士霍兰特爵士
随即高呼：
「振作起来，勇敢的伙伴们；
我心里很明白，

===Original===
 This wind no harm will do us;
 from out the west ’tis blowing.”
 This cheered the lord of Karadie,
 on him and on his men fresh hope bestowing.

===Chinese===
这风伤不了我们；
它是从西面吹来的。」
这话安了卡拉迪之主的心，
也给他和他的人马注入了新的希望。

===Original===
Horant, the daring warrior,
 up to the topmast climbed,
 And the widely stretching billows
 swept, with eyes undimmed,

===Chinese===
霍兰特这位胆气过人的武士
攀上桅顶，
以不昏花的目光
扫视浩瀚铺展的波涛，

===Original===
 Keeping for land an outlook.
 They soon his call were hearing:
 “Wait you now, unfearing;
 I see that we the Norman land are nearing!”

===Chinese===
瞭望着陆地。众人随即听见
他的呼喊：
「且宽心，莫惊惶；
我看见我们正驶近诺曼人的国土！」

===Original===
The word to all was given,
 that they should lower sail:
 Searching the waters over,
 they saw far off a hill,

===Chinese===
号令传遍众人，
命他们落下船帆：
他们搜索了整片海面，
望见远处一座山丘，

===Original===
 Lofty, and thickly wooded,
 with groves and leafage shaded;
 Then old Wâ-te bade them
 thither to bend their way, and this they heeded.

===Chinese===
高峻而林木蓊郁，
荫蔽在丛树与繁叶之下；
于是老瓦特吩咐他们
把船驶往那里，众人奉命而行。

## Tale XXIII How Hilda’s Warriors Landed in Sight of Hartmut’s Kingdom / 第二十三诗章 希尔达的武士们如何在望见哈特穆特王国之处登陆

===Original===
Herwic and Ortwin, the brother of Gudrun, agree to go forward in advance to procure intelligence in regard to her and her maidens.

===Chinese===
赫尔维克（Herwic）与古德伦（Gudrun）的弟弟奥特温（Ortwin）商定先行上路，去打探有关她和她那些侍女的消息。

===Original===
Before the hill they landed,
 in sight of the leafy grove;
 Wary to be, and daring,
 them did it now behoove.

===Chinese===
他们在山丘之前登了岸，
望得见那枝叶扶疏的树丛；
如今他们须得
既谨慎戒备，又果敢无畏。

===Original===
 First they dropped their anchors,
 deep the waters under;
 In a lonely spot were they hidden,
 where none could see, nor at their coming wonder.

===Chinese===
他们先抛下了船锚，
深深没入水底；
他们藏身在一处荒僻之地，
无人看得见，也无人对他们的到来诧异。

===Original===
Then from the ships, to rest them,
 they stepped upon the beach.
 Hey! what they had longed for
 was now within their reach!

===Chinese===
随后，为了歇息歇乏，
他们走下船来，踏上海滩。
嘿！他们久已渴盼的东西，
如今已近在咫尺！

===Original===
 A stream of pure, cold water,
 through the fir-trees flowing,
 Ran down the wooded hillside,
 upon the wave-worn knights new life bestowing.

===Chinese===
一道纯净清冽的溪水，
在冷杉林间流淌，
沿着林木葱郁的山坡流下，
为这些被浪涛磨折的骑士重新注入生命。

===Original===
While the weary warriors
 were resting and asleep,
 Irold soon had clambered,
 there his watch to keep,

===Chinese===
当疲惫的武士们
歇息酣睡之际，
伊罗尔德（Irold）早已攀了上去，
在那里守望，

===Original===
 Into a tree high-branching.
 He then began to ponder
 Which way they should be taking;
 and, lo! the Norman land he saw with wonder.

===Chinese===
攀上一棵高枝入云的大树。
他随即开始思量
他们该取哪条路径；
看哪！他惊奇地望见了诺曼底的大地。

===Original===
“Now, my youths, be merry!”
 thus cried the youthful knight.
 “My cares indeed are lightened,
 for now I have in sight

===Chinese===
「现在，我的儿郎们，欢畅起来吧！」
那年轻的骑士如此高呼。
「我的心事实已轻了，
因为此刻我已望在眼中

===Original===
 Seven lofty palaces,
 with roomy halls wide-spreading;
 Before to-morrow’s midday,
 the land of Normandy shall we be treading.”

===Chinese===
七座高耸的宫殿，
殿宇宽阔铺展；
明日晌午之前，
我们便会踏上诺曼底的国土。」

===Original===
Then said the wise old Wâ-te:
 “Up to the sands now bear
 All your shields and weapons,
 whate’er in fight you wear.

===Chinese===
这时睿智的老瓦特（Wâ-te）说道：
「现在把你们的盾牌与兵器
统统搬到沙滩上来，
凡临战穿戴之物，一件不落。

===Original===
 Let every one be busy,
 and let the youths be hastened;
 At once lead out the horses;
 helmets and breastplates must with straps be fastened.

===Chinese===
让每个人都忙碌起来，
催着儿郎们加紧行动；
立刻把战马牵出；
头盔与胸甲都要用皮带扣牢。

===Original===
“And now, if any outfits
 are not good to wear,
 Nor meet for you in fighting,
 to that I’ll give my care.

===Chinese===
「还有，若有哪套装束
不够完好耐穿，
或不配你们上阵厮杀，
这事我自会照管。

===Original===
 The queen, my lady Hilda,
 has sent with us already
