 Full five hundred breastplates;
 these will we give to any who are needy.”

===Chinese===
王后，我的女主人希尔达（Hilda），
早已随我们送来了
整整五百副胸甲；
谁有缺用，我们便发给谁。」

===Original===
Quickly were the horses
 forth on the sea-beach led;
 And all the showy horse-cloths,
 that should on them be spread,

===Chinese===
战马很快
就被牵到了海滩上；
那些华彩夺目的马衣，
本该披搭在它们身上的，

===Original===
 Were by the men unfolded,
 and laid on steeds in waiting,
 To see which best beseemed them;
 and each then took the one he deemed most fitting.

===Chinese===
都被人们一一展开，
披在侍立一旁的战马身上，
看看哪一件最为相称；
随后各人便取了他觉得最合宜的一件。

===Original===
In leaping, and in galloping
 up and down the shore,
 They rode, and watched the horses;
 many, strong before,

===Chinese===
他们骑着马在滩头上下驰骋，
时而腾跃，时而飞奔，
一路检视着这些战马；
有许多匹从前神骏有力，

===Original===
 Now were dull and sluggish,
 nor longer quick at running;
 Too long had they been standing,
 and Wâ-te had them killed, as not worth owning.

===Chinese===
如今却呆滞懒怠，
奔跑再不复轻捷；
只因站立得太久，
瓦特便把它们宰了，当作不值得留养。

===Original===
Fires by the men were lighted;
 and good and hearty food,
 The best that could be met with
 so near the shore and flood,

===Chinese===
人们生起了火堆；
丰盛喷香的吃食，
是在这近海傍水之处
所能觅得的上品，

===Original===
 By the tired and hungry wanderers
 soon was cooked and eaten.
 They had not hoped beforehand
 that rest like this their toilsome life would sweeten.

===Chinese===
很快被又倦又饥的远行者们
烹好吃尽了。
他们事先何曾指望，
这样的安歇会给劳苦的生涯添上甘甜。

===Original===
Throughout the night they rested,
 till dawn of the coming day.
 To Ortwin Wâ-te and Fru-te
 each his mind did say;

===Chinese===
他们安歇了一整夜，
直到来日破晓。
瓦特与弗鲁特（Fru-te）
各自向奥特温吐露心声；

===Original===
 Talking aside on the seashore,
 many a threat was spoken
 Against their Norman foemen,
 who into the Hegeling castle erst had broken.

===Chinese===
他们在海岸旁私下谈论，
说出了许多狠话，
冲着他们的诺曼仇敌——
那些昔日攻破黑格林人（Hegeling）城堡的家伙。

===Original===
“Men must we now be sending,”
 to them young Ortwin said,
 “Who shall tidings bring us,
 if they be not yet dead,

===Chinese===
「我们如今必须派人出去，」
年轻的奥特温对他们说，
「他们好替我们带回音讯——
倘若她们还没有丧命——

===Original===
 About my long-lost sister
 and many a homeless maiden;
 For when on them I’m thinking,
 my heart is heavy, oft with sorrow laden.”

===Chinese===
关于我那失散已久的姐姐
和许多无家可归的少女；
因为每逢念及她们，
我的心便沉重，常常载满忧愁。」

===Original===
Together they bethought them,
 whom they hence should send,
 By whom the news they wished for
 might with truth be gained,

===Chinese===
他们一同合计，
该从这里派谁前去，
借谁之手能真真切切
探得他们渴望的消息，

===Original===
 And who could tell them rightly
 where to find the maiden;
 By them, too, must the errand
 on which they came, from foes be wisely hidden.

===Chinese===
又有谁能确切告知
到哪里去寻那位少女；
况且他们此行的差事，
也须托这些人从仇敌眼中巧妙遮掩。

===Original===
Then spake the youthful Ortwin,
 who from Ortland came,
 A faithful knight as any:
 “Myself for the search I name;

===Chinese===
这时年轻的奥特温开言，
他来自奥特兰（Ortland），
是个不逊于任何人的忠贞骑士：
「这趟寻访，我自荐承当；

===Original===
 The maid, Gudrun, is my sister,
 child of my father and mother;
 Of all, however worthy,
 am I more fit to go than any other.”

===Chinese===
那少女古德伦是我的姐姐，
是我父亲母亲的孩子；
纵使众人个个贵重，
却无人比我更该前去。」

===Original===
Then spake the kingly Herwic:
 “I too will go with thee;
 To live or die I am ready,
 seeking the maid to free.

===Chinese===
这时王者般的赫尔维克开言：
「我也要与你同去；
或生或死，我都甘愿，
只为寻得那少女，还她自由。

===Original===
 To you she is a sister,
 but to me for a wife they gave her;
 To her am I ever faithful,
 nor for a day uncared-for will I leave her.”

===Chinese===
对你们，她是姐姐；
可人们把她给了我做妻子；
我对她永远忠贞，
绝不肯有一日撇下她不闻不问。」

===Original===
Then quoth Wâ-te angrily:
 “ ’Tis childish thus to speak,
 Brave and chosen warriors:
 such risks you should not seek,

===Chinese===
这时瓦特怒冲冲地说：
「这样讲话，未免孩子气，
英勇的、精选出来的武士啊：
这样的凶险，你们不该去寻，

===Original===
 And this for truth I tell you.
 Spurn you not my warning;
 Should you be found by Hartmut,
 you’ll on his gallows hang, your rashness mourning.”

===Chinese===
我这话句句属实。
莫要轻慢我的警告；
你们若被哈特穆特（Hartmut）察觉，
就要吊上他的绞架，为鲁莽悲叹。」

===Original===
To him King Herwic answered:
 “Though good or ill betide,
 Friends should aye be friendly,
 standing side by side.

===Chinese===
赫尔维克国王回答他：
「无论临到的是福是祸，
朋友总该永远待之以友，
彼此并肩而立。

===Original===
 I and my friend, young Ortwin,
 will ne’er the task give over,
 Whatever shall befall us,
 and search will make till we Gudrun recover.”

===Chinese===
我和我的朋友，年轻的奥特温，
绝不把这使命中途撂下，
无论我们遭逢什么，
定要寻到古德伦方肯罢休。」

===Original===
When now upon this errand
 both were bent to go,
 They sent for friends and kinsfolk,
 and did their wishes show.

===Chinese===
如今两人既打定主意
要承担这趟差事，
他们便召来亲友，
把自己的心愿一一晓谕。

===Original===
 They bade them to be faithful,
 and said the oaths then taken
 Must never be forgotten,
 and they who went must never be forsaken.

===Chinese===
他们嘱托众人要忠心不移，
并说此刻立下的誓言
永不容忘却，
而去的人也永不容抛弃。

===Original===
“Of your pledges I remind you,”
 the youthful Ortwin said:
 “If we, by foemen taken,
 should be in bondage led,

===Chinese===
「你们的誓约，我且提醒你们，」
年轻的奥特温说道：
「我们若被仇敌擒住，
被押去囚为俘虏，

===Original===
 You with gold must free us,
 and so our bonds must loosen;
 Lands must you sell and castles,
 nor ever sorrow feel that thus you’ve chosen.

===Chinese===
你们须用黄金赎救我们，
如此解开我们的缚索；
该卖地的卖地，该卖城的卖城，
永远莫为这抉择心怀悲伤。

===Original===
“And, warriors brave, now hearken
 to what we more will say;
 If foes our life begrudge us,
 and us in fight shall slay,

===Chinese===
「还有，勇敢的武士们，且听
我们要再叮嘱的话；
倘若仇敌忌恨我们的性命，
在阵前将我们杀害，

===Original===
 Be not our death forgotten,
 let it on them be wroken:
 Your swords in Hartmut’s kingdom
 must make your daring there be loudly spoken.

===Chinese===
莫把我们的死忘怀，
要向他们复仇雪恨：
你们的剑在哈特穆特的王国里，
要让你们的胆气在那里高声传扬。

===Original===
“This we further bid you,
 my good and well-born knights:
 E’en though, with toil the hardest,
 every warrior fights,

===Chinese===
「这事我们还要相托，
我的好骑士们，出身高贵的骑士：
哪怕苦斗何等艰辛，
每个武士都拼力鏖战，

===Original===
 Let not those homeless maidens
 be by you forsaken;
 Until the strife is settled,
 let not their hope and trust in you be shaken.”

===Chinese===
也莫抛下那些无家可归的少女；
直到这场争战见个分晓，
莫让她们对你们的期望与信托
有半分动摇。」

===Original===
Their faith then freely pledging,
 each gave to the king his hand;
 And all the best among them
 swore that home and land

===Chinese===
众人随即慷慨立誓表忠，
各自向国王伸手为凭；
他们中间最出众的众人
都立誓说：家园与国土，

===Original===
 They nevermore would look on,
 but still afar would tarry,
 Until again to their homesteads
 they from the Norman land the maids should carry.

===Chinese===
他们永不再回望一眼，
只甘愿长滞远方，
直到把众少女从诺曼底之地
重新载回她们的家舍。

===Original===
All of them were faithful,
 but yet were weeping sore;
 They feared the hate of Ludwig,
 and ills for them in store.

===Chinese===
他们个个忠心不贰，
却仍恸哭不已；
他们畏惧路德维希（Ludwig）的仇恨，
也畏惧正等着他们俩的祸殃。

===Original===
 That they could send no others
 they were deeply mourning;
 And all were sadly thinking,
 “No one now can death from them be turning.”

===Chinese===
他们深深哀恸，
再派不出旁人前去；
众人皆黯然思忖：
「如今再无人能把死神从他们身旁引开。」

===Original===
All day they talked together;
 it now was near its end:
 The sun, that low was sinking,
 thro’ clouds its beams did send:

===Chinese===
他们整日相与商谈；
这一天已近尽头：
低低西沉的太阳，
把光芒透过云层洒下：

===Original===
 Erelong it sank o’er Gulstred,
 and there at last was hidden.
 Ortwin and Herwic tarried,
 that night to go, by the waning light forbidden.

===Chinese===
不多时它便沉落在古尔斯特雷德（Gulstred）之后，
终于在那里隐没。
奥特温与赫尔维克滞留未行——
微弱的天光已不容他们连夜赶路。

## Tale XXIV How Their Coming Was Made Known to Gudrun / 第二十四诗章 他们的到来如何传报给古德伦

===Original===
While Gudrun and Hildeburg are washing on the seashore, a swan announces to them the approach of Queen Hilda’s army for their rescue, and tells them that two knights will come the next morning to inform them still further of this.

===Chinese===
古德伦（Gudrun）与希尔德堡（Hildeburg）正在海滨洗衣时，一只天鹅向她们宣告，希尔达（Hilda）王后为营救她们而来的大军已经临近；天鹅还告诉她们，次日清晨将有两位骑士前来，进一步告知详情。

===Original===
Of them we speak no longer;
 we now will let you hear
 Yet more about the maidens:
 how hope their lot did cheer

===Chinese===
关于他们，我们不再多讲；
如今我们要让你们再听听
这些少女们此后的遭遇：
希望如何宽慰了她们凄苦的命运——

===Original===
 Who on a far-off seashore
 must wearily toil at washing:
 Gudrun and Hildeburg
 must wash all day on the sands where waves were dashing.

===Chinese===
她们在遥远的海滨，
困顿地操劳洗衣：
古德伦与希尔德堡
整日在浪花飞溅的沙滩上洗濯不停。

===Original===
’Twas the time of springtide fasting,
 and at the noon of day.
 To them a swan came floating;
 thereat Gudrun ’gan say:

===Chinese===
那时正值春日斋期，
时辰是一天的正午。
一只天鹅向她们游来；
古德伦见了便开口说道：

===Original===
 “O bird so fair and lovely,
 such pain for me thou art feeling,
 That now thou hither speedest
 from a far-off land, across the water sailing.”

===Chinese===
「啊，鸟儿，你这般美丽可爱，
你为我怀着这般深切的悲苦，
以致如今匆匆飞临此地，
从遥远的国土，一路渡海而来。」

===Original===
Then to her in answer
 spake the friendly swan,
 Although a God-sent angel,
 in speech most like a man:

===Chinese===
那和善的天鹅随即
开口回答她，
它虽是上帝差来的天使，
言谈却极似人的声音：

===Original===
 “Words from God I bring you;
 if you for this be seeking,
 Tidings I give of your kindred;
 of these, most high-born maid, would I be speaking.”

===Chinese===
「我从上帝那里给你们带来话语；
你们若寻求的正是这个，
我就把你们亲人的音讯相告；
出身高贵的少女，我正要向你们讲说。」

===Original===
When the lovely maiden
 his speech so wondrous heard,
 Scarce could she believe it,
 that thus an untamed bird,

===Chinese===
当那可爱的少女
听了他这般奇妙的言语，
她几乎不能相信，
一只未经驯养的鸟儿

===Original===
 Now, within her hearing,
 in tones like these had spoken.
 While to him she listened,
 it seemed that his words from the mouth of a man had broken.

===Chinese===
如今竟会在她耳畔，
用这样的声调开口言谈。
她一边听他讲述，
只觉他的话语像是从人的口中吐出。

===Original===
Then said the bird-like angel:
 “Hopeful you now may be,
 Homeless, sorrowing maiden;
 gladness shall come to thee.

===Chinese===
那鸟形的天使随即说道：
「你如今可以存着指望了，
无家可归的忧伤少女；
欢乐终将降临于你。

===Original===
 If you would hear of your birthland,
 listen while I tell you;
 From there I bring you tidings,
 for God hath sent me, of your woes to heal you.”

===Chinese===
你若愿意听听故国的消息，
就请听我诉说；
我从那里给你带来了音讯，
上帝差我来，是要医治你的伤悲。」

===Original===
At this, Gudrun, the fair one,
 upon the sands down fell;
 Crossing her arms, the maiden
 her lowly prayers did tell.

===Chinese===
听了这话，美丽的古德伦
扑倒在沙滩之上；
这少女交叠起双臂，
低声作了谦卑的祈祷。

===Original===
 Then she said to Hildeburg:
 “God hath us in his keeping,
 And help to us has granted;
 we now no more shall sorrow know, nor weeping.”

===Chinese===
随后她对希尔德堡说道：
「上帝已把我们置于他的护佑之中，
又已赐给我们援手；
从今我们再不必尝哀伤与哭泣。」

===Original===
To the bird then said the maiden:
 “Christ has sent thee here
 To us, poor homeless maidens,
 our heavy hearts to cheer;

===Chinese===
那少女随即对鸟儿说道：
「是基督差你来到
我们这些可怜的、无家可归的少女这里，
来宽慰我们沉重的心；

===Original===
 Good and trusted harbinger,
 tidings tell yet other:
 Is now Queen Hilda living?
 Of poor Gudrun is she the much-loved mother.”

===Chinese===
善良而可靠的前驱啊，
请再讲些别的音讯：
希尔达王后如今还在世吗？
她可是可怜的古德伦深爱的母亲。」

===Original===
The Heaven-sent bird thus answered:
 “This can I say to thee;
 Hilda, thy queenly mother,
 in health did I lately see.

===Chinese===
那上天差来的鸟儿这样答道：
「这话我能告诉你：
希尔达，你那王后般的母亲，
我新近见她身体康健。

===Original===
 To search for thee already
 her warriors she has banded;
 Such throngs no kin or widow,
 seeking for friends, on foeman’s shore e’er landed.”

===Chinese===
为寻访你的下落，
她早已将她的武士集结成军；
这般浩大的人群，寻亲问友的族人寡妇，
从不曾在仇敌的海岸登临。」

===Original===
Then spake the high-born maiden:
 “Good tidings thou dost bear:
 Be thou with me not weary,
 still more I fain would hear.

===Chinese===
那出身高贵的少女随即开言：
「你带来的确是好消息：
请你莫要对我生厌，
我还想再多听一些。

===Original===
 Lives yet my brother Ortwin,
 as king in Ortland dwelling,
 And Herwic, my betrothèd?
 ’Twould gladden me could’st thou this news be telling.”

===Chinese===
我的兄弟奥特温（Ortwin）还在世吗？
还以国王之尊安居在奥特兰（Ortland）吗？
还有我的未婚夫赫尔维克（Herwic）？
你若能说出这消息，该教我多么欢喜。」

===Original===
The bird-like angel answered:
 “That can I gladly tell;
 Herwic and young King Ortwin
 are both alive and well.

===Chinese===
那鸟形的天使答道：
「这个我乐于奉告：
赫尔维克与年轻的国王奥特温
两人都安然无恙。

===Original===
 Upon the swelling billows,
 that rose and sank unending,
 I saw those knightly sailors;
 each with even stroke to his oar was bending.”

===Chinese===
在那汹涌的波涛之上，
波涛无休无止地起伏，
我望见了那些骑士般的水手；
个个以齐整的桨势俯身划桨。」

===Original===
She said: “This tell me also,
 if ’tis known to thee,
 Whether Morunc and Irold
 are now upon the sea,

===Chinese===
她说：「这个也请告诉我，
如果你知晓实情，
莫伦克（Morunc）与伊罗尔德（Irold）
如今可正在海上，

===Original===
 And hither come to seek me;
 the truth I fain would gather.
 Gladly I would see them,
 for they are kin to Hettel, who was my father.”

===Chinese===
可是来此寻我？
我一心想探得真相。
我巴不得见到他们，
因为他们是我父亲黑特尔（Hettel）的亲族。」

===Original===
To her the bird thus answered:
 “That can I tell you, too;
 Morunc, and with him Irold,
 I saw, in search of you.

===Chinese===
那鸟儿向她这样回答：
「这个我也能告诉你：
莫伦克，还有他身边的伊罗尔德，
我都见到了，他们正在寻你。

===Original===
 They to this land are coming;
 their help will soon be given
 To fight for you, fair lady,
 and many a helmet will by them be riven.”

===Chinese===
他们正向这片国土赶来；
他们的援助不久便要
用来为美丽的夫人征战，
多少头盔都将被他们劈碎。」

===Original===
Then spake the winged angel:
 “I bid you now farewell,
 And leave you in God’s keeping,
 for work awaits me still.

===Chinese===
那生翼的天使随即说道：
「我如今要向你们道别，
把你们交托上帝护佑，
因为我还有差事待办。

===Original===
 I overstay my errand
 to linger here, yet speaking.”
 Then from their sight he faded,
 and left the maidens’ hearts well-nigh to breaking.

===Chinese===
我逗留在此，已误了使命，
却仍在这里絮絮言说。」
说罢，他从她们眼前消隐，
只教少女们的心几乎为之碎裂。

===Original===
Then said Hilda’s daughter:
 “My sorrows none can know;
 Much that I wished to ask thee,
 now must I forego.

===Chinese===
于是希尔达的女儿说道：
「我的愁苦无人能够知晓；
我还有许多话想问你，
如今却只得舍弃。

===Original===
 For the sake of Christ, I beg thee,
 ere thou alone dost leave me,
 Poor and wretched maiden,
 that freedom from my woes thou yet wilt give me.”

===Chinese===
看在基督的份上，我恳求你，
在你独自撇下我而去之前，
怜惜我这可怜悲惨的少女，
求你终于赐我脱离这重重苦难。」

===Original===
Before her eyes he floated,
 and once again he spake:
 “Ere yet we two are parted,
 and hence my way I take,

===Chinese===
他在她眼前浮荡，
再度开口说道：
「在我们两人分手、
我由此上路之前，

===Original===
 If I in aught can help you,
 of that I will not weary,
 And, since through Christ you ask it,
 to tell you of your kin will longer tarry.”

===Chinese===
但凡有什么我能相助之处，
我绝不惮烦，
既然你是凭着基督开口求告，
我就再盘桓片刻，说说你的亲族。」

===Original===
She said: “I fain were hearing,
 if thou the truth hast learned,
 If Horant, lord of Denmark,
 his way has hither turned,

===Chinese===
她说：「我很想听听，
你可曾探得实情，
丹麦之主霍兰特（Horant）
可曾把航向转向此地，

===Original===
 And with him leads his kinsmen?
 They leave me here forsaken.
 Knowing him brave and daring,
 I would my lonely lot his care might waken.”

===Chinese===
可率领着他的亲族同来？
他们竟把我孤零零抛在此地。
我素知他英勇而无畏，
但愿我这孤苦的命运能唤起他的挂怀。」

===Original===
“From Denmark sailing hither,
 Horant, your kinsman, comes;
 He to war is leading
 his followers from their homes.

===Chinese===
「正从丹麦扬帆驶来，
你的亲族霍兰特就要抵达；
他正率领着他的部众
辞家离乡，前去征战。

===Original===
 The banner of Queen Hilda
 aloft in his hand he is bearing;
 ’Tis thus the Hegeling warriors
 now the Norman Hartmut’s land are nearing.”

===Chinese===
希尔达王后的旌旗，
他正高擎在手；
黑格林人（Hegeling）的武士们
就这样日渐逼近诺曼人哈特穆特（Hartmut）的国土。」

===Original===
Gudrun then asked him further:
 “This would I also hear:
 Lives Wâ-te still of Sturmland?
 If so, no more I fear.

===Chinese===
古德伦又进而问他：
「这事我也想听个明白：
施图尔姆兰（Sturmland）的瓦特（Wâ-te）还健在吗？
若还健在，我便再无所惧。

===Original===
 We all might then be happy,
 if thou could’st this be telling﻿—
 That under the flag of my mother
 he and the aged Fru-te are hither sailing.”

===Chinese===
那样我们大家便都能欢喜，
只要你能如此相告——
在我母亲的旗帜之下，
他与年迈的弗鲁特（Fru-te）正扬帆驶来。」

===Original===
To her the angel answered:
 “Hither comes in haste
 Wâ-te the old from Sturmland.
 He in his hand holds fast

===Chinese===
那天使回答她道：
「正兼程赶来的是
施图尔姆兰年迈的瓦特。
他的手中牢牢握定

===Original===
 The strong and guiding rudder,
 and Fru-te’s ship is steering.
 Truer friends or better
 you ne’er need wish their swords for you were bearing.”

===Chinese===
那坚固的领航之舵，
正为弗鲁特的船掌舵。
再忠实、再好的朋友，
你也不必指望他们更能为你仗剑。」

===Original===
Once more the bird was ready
 upon his way to go;
 Then said the wretched maiden:
 “I still am full of woe;

===Chinese===
那鸟儿又一次准备停当，
就要登程上路；
那不幸的少女说道：
「我心里仍盛满悲苦；

===Original===
 And now to know am longing﻿—
 if life such bliss can lend me﻿—
 When I, poor homeless maiden,
 shall see my mother’s knights, whom she doth send me.”

===Chinese===
如今我渴望知道——
倘若此生还能赐我这样的福气——
我这可怜的、无家可归的少女，
何时才得见母亲派来给我的骑士。」

===Original===
The angel answered quickly:
 “Your happiness is near;
 To-morrow morning early,
 will two brave knights be here.

===Chinese===
那天使迅即答道：
「你们的幸福已然临近；
明日清晨一大早，
就有两位勇武的骑士到此。

===Original===
 Both are true and upright,
 and falsehood ne’er will tell you;
 Whatever news they bring you
 you well may trust, and never will it fail you.”

===Chinese===
两人都忠实而正直，
绝不会对你们说谎；
无论他们带来什么消息，
你们尽可信赖，那绝不会有误。」

===Original===
At last the heavenly angel
 hence in truth must go:
 From him the homeless maidens
 sought no more to know.

===Chinese===
那天上的天使终于
当真不得不从此离去：
无家可归的少女们
便不再向他探问什么。

===Original===
 In mind they ever wavered,
 ’twixt hope and fear still tossing;
 Where their helpers lingered
 they could not know, yet trust were never losing.

===Chinese===
她们的心绪始终摇曳，
在希望与恐惧之间翻腾；
援助她们的义士滞留何处，
她们无从知晓，却从未失去信赖。

===Original===
Lazily and slowly
 they washed the livelong day;
 Of knights sent there by Hilda,
 who now were on their way

===Chinese===
她们懒懒地、缓缓地
洗濯了整整一天；
说到希尔达派出的骑士——
他们此刻正在途中，

===Original===
 From over the Hegeling border,
 busily they chatted:
 Gudrun’s good, faithful kinsmen
 were by the long-lost maids uneasily awaited.

===Chinese===
正从黑格林人的边境赶来——
她们絮絮谈说不休：
古德伦善良而忠实的亲族，
正被这久失音讯的少女们焦灼地盼望着。

===Original===
Each day must have its ending;
 to the castle now must go
 The weary, homesick maidens.
 They there must harshness know

===Chinese===
每一天终须有个尽头；
那疲乏思乡的少女们
如今只得回城堡去。
她们要在那里领受苛虐，

===Original===
 From evil-minded Gerlind,
 who their lives still harrowed;
 A day went by but seldom
 that she scolded them not, nor still their bondage narrowed.

===Chinese===
施虐的是心怀恶毒的格尔林德（Gerlind），
她仍在一日日作践她们的生活；
几乎没有哪一天
她不斥骂她们，不把她们的枷锁收紧。

===Original===
Thus she spoke to the maidens:
 “Who gave the word to you
 That you might wash so slowly
 my clothes and linen, too?

===Chinese===
她就这样对少女们说道：
「是谁给了你们许可，
让你们这样慢条斯理地洗濯
我的衣裳，还有衬布？

===Original===
 All the things I gave you
 must be quickly whitened;
 ’Twere best that you be careful,
 you else shall weep, and for your lives be frightened.”

===Chinese===
我交给你们的一切物件，
都必须快快漂白；
你们最好仔细当心，
不然就要啼哭，就要为性命胆战。」

===Original===
Then answered her young Hildeburg:
 “Our work we ever mind;
 Truly you ought, fair lady,
 to be to us more kind.

===Chinese===
年轻的希尔德堡回答她道：
「我们的活计时刻放在心上；
说真的，美丽的夫人，
您本该待我们更宽厚些。

===Original===
 We oft are almost freezing,
 with water o’er us splashing;
 If only the winds were warmer,
 we might for you far better then be washing.”

===Chinese===
我们常常冻得几乎僵住，
水花不住泼溅在周身；
只要风势暖和些，
我们便能为您把衣裳洗得更好。」

===Original===
Grimly answered Gerlind,
 and roughly them did twit:
 “Whatever be the weather,
 my work you may not slight.

===Chinese===
格尔林德声色俱厉地作答，
粗暴地讥斥她们：
「无论天气如何，
我的活计你们不得怠慢。

===Original===
 Early must you be washing,
 nor rest till night be knowing;
 To-morrow morn, at daybreak,
 you from my room must down to the beach be going.

===Chinese===
你们必须一早动手洗衣，
不到入夜不得停歇；
明日清晨天一亮，
你们就得从我房中下海滩去。

===Original===
“I ween you know already
 that Holytide is near;
 Palm-Sunday soon is coming,
 and guests will then be here:

===Chinese===
「我想你们早已知晓
圣节就快要来临；
棕枝主日转眼即到，
宾客们届时将齐聚于此：

===Original===
 If to ill-washed clothing
 my knights shall then be treated,
 Never in kingly castle
 to those who washed have woes like yours been meted.”

===Chinese===
倘若我的骑士们到时
穿的是洗得不洁的衣裳，
在王者的城堡里，
从未有洗衣之人受过你们这等责罚。」

===Original===
Then the maidens left her;
 they laid aside, all wet,
 The clothing they were wearing﻿—
 they better care should get.

===Chinese===
随后少女们离开了她；
她们把身上穿着的、
湿淋淋的衣裳脱在一旁——
本该得到更好的照料才是。

===Original===
 All they had known of kindness
 for them no longer lasted,
 And soon for this they sorrowed,
 for bread and water now was all they tasted.

===Chinese===
她们从前所知的一切善待，
从此不再为她们留存，
不久她们便为此悲苦：
如今她们尝到的只有面包和清水。

===Original===
Now the downcast maidens
 for sleep had sought their bed;
 But this was not the softest,
 and each one, in her need,

===Chinese===
如今这垂头丧气的少女们
上床去寻睡眠；
但这床铺绝非柔软，
每个人出于无奈，

===Original===
 A dirty shirt was wearing.
 Thus was Gerlind showing
 Her care and kindness for them,
 on benches hard a pillow ne’er bestowing.

===Chinese===
身上都穿着污秽的衬衣。
格尔林德就是这样
向她们施与关怀和慈爱：
硬板凳上，从不肯赏一个枕头。

===Original===
Never Gudrun, poor maiden,
 on a harder bed had lain;
 All were tired with watching
 till day should dawn again.

===Chinese===
可怜的少女古德伦，
从未睡过比这更硬的床；
人人都在守望中困乏，
只待天色再度破晓。

===Original===
 They had but broken slumber;
 I ween, they oft bethought them
 How soon the knights were coming,
 of whom the angel-bird the news had brought them.

===Chinese===
她们的睡眠支离破碎；
我想，她们心里常常思量，
那些骑士多久便要到来——
天使鸟儿早已把消息带给她们。

===Original===
Soon as the morning lightened,
 Hildeburg the good,
 Erst from Galicia stolen,
 at the window gazing stood;

===Chinese===
晨光刚刚透亮，
善良的希尔德堡——
她当初从加利西亚（Galicia）被掳来——
便伫立窗前凝望；

===Original===
 All night she slept but little,
 but on her bed lay tossing.
 She saw that snow had fallen,
 and hope the heart-sick maid was well-nigh losing.

===Chinese===
她整夜几乎未曾合眼，
只在床上辗转反侧。
她看见天上降下了雪，
这心碎的少女几乎失去了希望。

===Original===
Then spake the hapless maiden:
 “To wash we now must go.
 Should God not change the weather,
 and we, in storm and snow,

===Chinese===
那不幸的少女随即说道：
「如今我们必须去洗衣了。
倘若上帝不把天气改变，
我们就得顶着风暴与冰雪，

===Original===
 To-day must stand a-washing,
 before the evening cometh
 We, all chilled and barefoot,
 shall dead be found, while us the cold benumbeth.”

===Chinese===
今天站着洗衣一整天，
不等黄昏降临，
我们这些冻透的赤脚之人，
便要被寒冷麻僵，被人发现死在那里。」

===Original===
By hope they yet were gladdened,
 e’en as they well might be,
 That those sent out by Hilda
 they ere night should see.

===Chinese===
希望仍教她们心怀欢欣，
这本也合乎情理，
她们想着入夜之前，
便能见到希尔达遣出的人。

===Original===
 When the lovely maidens
 upon this thought were dwelling,
 It made them now more happy,
 and lighter was the pain their hearts were feeling.

===Chinese===
每当这些可爱的少女
在这念头上萦回不去，
这念头便教她们更觉快乐，
心头的苦痛也随之轻了几分。

===Original===
Then said Hilda’s daughter:
 “My friend, you should beseech
 The stern, ill-minded Gerlind,
 that on the pebbly beach

===Chinese===
于是希尔达的女儿说道：
「我的朋友，你该去恳求
那严苛恶毒的格尔林德，
让她允我们在那砾石的海滩上

===Original===
 Shoes she will allow us;
 she may herself be learning
 That if we go there barefoot
 we soon shall freeze, and there our death be earning.”

===Chinese===
穿一双鞋履；
她自己总该会明白，
我们若赤脚去到那里，
很快就要冻僵，在那里断送性命。」

===Original===
The maidens then went seeking
 King Ludwig and his queen.
 He, in sleep held fondly,
 in Gerlind’s arms was seen;

===Chinese===
于是少女们前去寻找
路德维希（Ludwig）国王与他的王后。
只见他在睡梦中
被格尔林德亲昵地搂在臂弯里；

===Original===
 Both were sunk in slumber,
 and the maids, their anger fearing,
 Dared not them to waken:
 erelong Gudrun yet greater woe was bearing.

===Chinese===
两人都沉入了酣梦，
少女们畏惧他们的怒火，
不敢将他们唤醒：
不久，古德伦便承受起更大的苦难。

===Original===
The weeping of the maidens
 by the sleeping queen was heard,
 Who quick began to chide
 them with many a surly word:

===Chinese===
少女们的哭泣
惊动了沉睡的王后，
她当即开口斥责，
用许多粗暴的言语数落她们：

===Original===
 “Why, you heedless maidens,
 are you not to the seashore going,
 There to wash my clothing,
 and rinse them with clean water o’er them flowing?”

===Chinese===
「怎么，你们这些不上心的丫头，
还不快到海滨去，
在那里洗我的衣裳，
用淌过的清水把它们漂净？」

===Original===
Then said Gudrun, in sorrow:
 “I know not where to go,
 For in the night has fallen
 a deep and heavy snow.

===Chinese===
古德伦悲苦地说道：
「我不知该往何处去，
因为夜里已降下
一场又深又厚的大雪。

===Original===
 That we by death be stricken
 unless you now are willing,
 Do not send us washing;
 to stand without our shoes will us be killing.”

===Chinese===
除非您现在肯开恩，
我们都要被死亡击倒；
请别派我们去洗衣；
赤着脚立在那里，会要了我们的命。」

===Original===
To her the she-wolf answered;
 “That I do not fear;
 Now to the shore betake you,
 or weal or woe to bear.

===Chinese===
那母狼回答她道：
「这个我可不怕；
这就给我到岸边去，
是福是祸，都由你们承当。

===Original===
 If you be slow in washing,
 my wrath may you be dreading;
 E’en if you die, what care I?”
 At this the hopeless maids more tears were shedding.

===Chinese===
你们若洗衣洗得迟慢，
就得提防我的怒火；
哪怕你们死了，与我何干？」
绝望的少女们闻言，泪水流得更凶了。

===Original===
Taking then the clothing,
 they went to the water’s brink:
 “Of this,” said Gudrun,
 “God willing, I will make you think.”

===Chinese===
于是她们拿起衣裳，
走到水边：
「这件事，」古德伦说，
「只要上帝允许，我定要教你们记得。」

===Original===
 Then, in the cold, barefooted,
 through the snow they waded;
 The very high-born maidens,
 forsaken in their woe, were worn and faded.

===Chinese===
随后，冒着严寒，赤着双足，
她们蹚过了积雪；
这出身高贵的少女们，
在悲苦中被弃不顾，容色枯槁凋败。

===Original===
Down to the beach they plodded,
 as was their wont before,
 Bearing the clothing with them
 to the bleak and sandy shore.

===Chinese===
她们一步一步挨下海滩，
一如往常的旧路，
随身背着衣裳，
走向那荒凉多沙的海岸。

===Original===
 They once more were standing,
 over the washing stooping;
 Ever they were thinking
 of their sorry plight, and sadly were they hoping.

===Chinese===
她们又一次站立在那里，
俯身对着待洗的衣裳；
心里始终想着
自己凄凉的处境，黯然怀着指望。

===Original===
Often now, and earnestly,
 over the watery waste,
 While they toiled and sorrowed,
 longing looks they cast;

===Chinese===
她们如今一次又一次，殷切地
望向那茫茫的水面，
一面操劳一面哀伤，
投去渴望的目光；

===Original===
 Still of those now dreaming
 sent by the queen to free them,
 Who o’er the sea were sailing.
 The high-born maidens hoped erelong to see them.

===Chinese===
心里仍梦想着那些
奉王后之命来解救她们、
正渡海而来的人。
出身高贵的少女们只盼早日与他们相见。

## Tale XXV How Herwic and Ortwin Found Gudrun / 第二十五诗章 赫尔维克与奥特温如何找到古德伦

===Original===
Herwic and Ortwin, coming in a boat, see the maidens washing on the shore, recognize them, and make themselves known. Thinking it dishonorable to take the maids away without winning them in fight, the knights return to bring their army. The maidens go back to Gerlind, and, feeling sure of her speedy rescue, Gudrun pretends to agree to Hartmut’s suit, and is restored to favor.

===Chinese===
赫尔维克（Herwic）与奥特温（Ortwin）乘船而来，看见少女们正在岸边洗衣，认出了她们，并表明了自己的身份。两位骑士认为，不经战斗赢得便将少女们带走有损荣誉，于是回去调集大军。少女们回到格尔林德（Gerlind）处；古德伦（Gudrun）确信自己很快就会获救，便假意应承哈特穆特（Hartmut）的求婚，重新得回了恩宠。

===Original===
After they long had waited,
 now saw these washers lone
 Two in a boat fast nearing;
 others were there none.

===Chinese===
她们久久守望之后，
孤身的洗衣少女们如今望见
一叶小船正飞快驶近，载着两人；
此外再无别人。

===Original===
 Then said the maiden, Hildeburg,
 unto Gudrun, the lady:
 “These two are sailing hither;
 perhaps the friends sent here are come already.”

===Chinese===
少女希尔德堡（Hildeburg）随即
对古德伦贵女说道：
「这两个人正朝这里驶来；
说不定派来相援的亲友已经到了。」

===Original===
She, full of sorrow, answered:
 “Ah, woe is me, poor maid!
 Although, in truth I’m happy,
 I yet am also sad.

===Chinese===
她满怀忧伤地答道：
「唉，我真是不幸，苦命的少女！
虽说心里实在欢喜，
却也依然悲伤。

===Original===
 If at the seaside washing
 Queen Hilda’s men shall see us,
 Standing thus barefooted,
 we from the shame of this can never free us.

===Chinese===
倘若希尔达（Hilda）王后的部下
看见我们在这海滨操劳洗衣，
赤着双脚站在这里，
这份耻辱我们永远洗刷不掉。

===Original===
“A poor, unhappy woman,
 I know not what to do:
 Hildeburg, my dearest,
 your mind now let me know;

===Chinese===
「我这贫苦不幸的女子，
不知该如何是好：
希尔德堡，我最亲爱的，
现在让我知道你的心意；

===Original===
 To hide me were it better,
 or shall I stay to shame me
 When they shall find me toiling?
 Rather would I that they a drudge should name me.”

===Chinese===
我是躲起来为好，
还是留下来受辱，
任他们瞧见我操这贱役？
我宁愿被他们唤作苦役的婢女。」

===Original===
Then said the maiden Hildeburg:
 “E’en how it stands you see;
 A thing that is so weighty
 you should not leave to me,

===Chinese===
少女希尔德堡说道：
「眼下的情形你也看得明白；
这样重大的一件事，
你不该推给我来定夺，

===Original===
 Whate’er you think the better,
 your choice will I be sharing;
 With you I’ll stay forever,
 both good, and ill together with you bearing.”

===Chinese===
无论你怎样想更妥当，
我都愿与你共同担当；
我要永远陪伴着你，
祸福与共，一同承受。」

===Original===
Then from the water turning,
 both fled away in haste;
 But now the boat of the sailors
 had neared the land so fast,

===Chinese===
她们随即转身离开水边，
两人慌忙奔逃；
可这时水手们的小船
已飞快逼近陆地，

===Original===
 They saw the lovely washers,
 away from the seashore hieing,
 And at once bethought them
 that they for shame away from the clothes were flying.

===Chinese===
他们望见可爱的洗衣女们
正匆匆离开海滨，
立刻想到
她们是因羞惭才丢下衣裳逃开。

===Original===
They called unto the maidens,
 as they sprang upon the beach:
 “Whither so fast are you fleeing,
 fair washers, we beseech?

===Chinese===
他们跃上沙滩，
向少女们高声叫喊：
「美丽的洗衣姑娘，
你们这样急匆匆，是要逃往何处？

===Original===
 We are far-off wanderers,
 as well our looks are showing;
 Your linen may be stolen,
 if you leave it here, and from us in haste are going.”

===Chinese===
我们是远方的游子，
这从相貌上也看得分明；
你们若丢下亚麻衣裳匆匆离去，
它们可能会被人偷走。」

===Original===
They kept their way still swiftly,
 as if they heard it not:
 But yet the boisterous shouting
 had reached their ears, I wot.

===Chinese===
她们依旧快步赶路，
仿佛什么也没有听见；
但那喧腾的呼喊
分明已传进她们耳中，我可以作证。

===Original===
 The bold and knightly Herwic
 too roughly bade them hear him,
 For he not yet mistrusted
 ’twas his betrothed that now he saw so near him.

===Chinese===
英武的骑士赫尔维克
过于粗重地喝令她们驻足聆听，
因为他尚未疑心，
近在眼前的正是自己的未婚妻。

===Original===
Cried Herwic, lord of Sealand:
 “Maidens fair and young,
 Tell us now, we pray you,
 to whom these clothes belong.

===Chinese===
西兰（Sealand）之王赫尔维克喊道：
「年轻美丽的少女们，
我们恳请你们现在告诉我等，
这些衣裳是替谁家洗的。

===Original===
 We ask you in all honor,
 by the faith to maidens owing,
 Most fair and lovely ladies,
 that back to the shore you will again be going.”

===Chinese===
我们怀着十足的敬意，
凭对少女应守的信义相问，
最美丽可爱的贵女，
恳请你们重新回到岸边来。」

===Original===
Gudrun, the maid, then answered:
 “It were a shame, forsooth,
 Since to the trust of woman
 you give your pledge in truth,

===Chinese===
少女古德伦答道：
「那确乎是耻辱，
既然你们以真诚
把承诺托付给女子的信义，

===Original===
 Were I of this unworthy,
 nor faith in you were showing:
 To the shore we back will hasten,
 although my eyes with tears are overflowing.”

===Chinese===
倘若我当之有愧，
不对你们报以信任：
我们仍会赶回岸边，
尽管我双眼泪水盈眶。」

===Original===
They, in their smocks, came nearer;
 both with the sea were wet.
 Before that time, the maidens
 were always clean and neat;

===Chinese===
她们身着单薄的罩衣走近；
两人都被海水浸湿。
在那之前，这两位少女
一向干净整洁；

===Original===
 Now the wretched drudges
 with cold and frost were quaking;
 Little of late had they eaten,
 and with the March-like winds were chilled and shaking.

===Chinese===
如今这两个可怜的苦役女
在寒霜中瑟瑟发抖；
她们近来食不果腹，
又被三月般的寒风冻得颤栗不止。

===Original===
The time had come already
 for snows to melt away,
 And, with each other vying,
 the little birds, each day,

===Chinese===
时节已然到来，
积雪该当消融，
小鸟们彼此争胜，
日复一日地

===Original===
 Again their songs would warble,
 as soon as March was ended;
 But in the snow, and ice-cold,
 the maids were found forlorn, and unbefriended.

===Chinese===
重新啭鸣欢唱，只待三月过去；
然而在冰雪严寒之中，
少女们却被寻见孤苦伶仃，
举目无亲。

===Original===
Stiff were their locks and frosted,
 when they now drew near;
 However well and carefully
 they had smoothed their hair,

===Chinese===
她们的长发冻得僵硬结霜，
当她们此刻走近；
无论先前怎样细心
把头发梳理平整，

===Original===
 It now was tossed and tumbled
 by the wind so wildly blowing:
 Hard bestead were the maidens,
 toiling there, whether it rained or was snowing.

===Chinese===
此刻也已凌乱蓬松，
被狂风吹得四散纷飞：
少女们处境艰难，
在那里操劳，无论降雨还是落雪。

===Original===
The ice was loose and broken,
 floating everywhere
 Upon the sea before them.
 The maids were filled with care;

===Chinese===
浮冰松散破碎，
在她们面前的海上
四处漂流。
少女们满怀忧惧；

===Original===
 Pale were now their bodies,
 e’en as the snow around them,
 By their scanty clothes scarce hidden.
 Sad was the lot in which the knights had found them.

===Chinese===
她们的身躯如今苍白，
恰似周遭的积雪，
单薄的衣衫几乎遮掩不住。
骑士们所见她们的处境何其悲惨。

===Original===
Then the high-born Herwic
 a kind “Good-morning” bade
 To the sad and homeless maidens;
 of this sore need they had,

===Chinese===
出身高贵的赫尔维克
向忧伤无家的少女们
道了一声亲切的「早安」；
这样的温情她们正深切匮乏，

===Original===
 For oft their keeper, Gerlind,
 had them with harshness taunted.
 To hear “Good-morning,” “Good-evening,”
 was now to the maids but very seldom granted.

===Chinese===
因为她们的监看人格尔林德
常常苛酷地奚落她们。
如今能听到一声「早安」「晚安」，
对少女们已是难得的恩赐。

===Original===
Then said the youthful Ortwin:
 “I beg you say to me
 To whom belongs this clothing,
 that on the sands I see?

===Chinese===
年轻的奥特温说道：
「我恳请你们告诉我，
这沙滩上堆着的衣裳
究竟是属于谁的？

===Original===
 For whom are you here washing?
 You both are so comely showing,
 Who can this shame have done you?
 May God bring low the man such outrage doing!

===Chinese===
你们在这里替谁洗衣？
你们两位这般容色秀美，
是谁忍心加给你们这样的羞辱？
愿上帝贬抑那行此暴行之人！

===Original===
“So fair are you and lovely,
 you well might wear the crown;
 If all that is your birthright
 you now could call your own,

===Chinese===
「以你们这般姣好可爱，
尽可以头戴王冠；
倘若生来应有的一切
如今都能归你们所有，

===Original===
 You would, in truth, be worthy
 to be with ladies seated.
 Has he for whom you are toiling
 more such washers fair so foully treated?”

===Chinese===
你们实在配得上
与贵妇同席而坐。
你们为之操劳的那个人，
可还这样糟践过别的美丽洗衣女？」

===Original===
To him the lovely maiden
 in greatest sorrow spoke:
 “Many he hath beside us
 who fairer still do look.

===Chinese===
那可爱的少女
怀着极大的悲伤对他说：
「除了我们，他还占有许多
容貌更为姣好的人。

===Original===
 All that you list now ask us;
 yet, with eye unsleeping,
 One from the leads doth watch us,
 who ne’er will forgive the talk with you we’re keeping.”

===Chinese===
你们想问什么尽管问；
只是有一双不眠的眼睛，
有人正从屋顶瞭望处看守我们，
他绝不会饶恕我们与你们的这番交谈。」

===Original===
“Be not at this uneasy,
 but deign our gold to take,
 And with it these four arm-bands.
 These your reward we make,

===Chinese===
「不必为此不安，
但请赏光收下我们的黄金，
连同这四只臂环。
这是我们给你们的酬谢，

===Original===
 If you, most lovely ladies,
 of speech will not be wary;
 To you we give them gladly,
 if of the truth we seek you be not chary.”

===Chinese===
只要你们，最可爱的贵女，
不对言谈多加提防；
我们乐意把它们送给你们，
只求你们对我们探问的实情不要吝惜。」

===Original===
“God leave to you your arm-bands,
 albeit you we thank;
 Nought for hire may you give us,”
 quoth the lady high in rank.

===Chinese===
「愿上帝把臂环留给你们，
尽管我们心怀感激；
但你们不能拿钱雇动我们，」
身份尊贵的贵妇说道。

===Original===
 “Ask what you will, but quickly,
 for we must hence be going;
 If we were seen here with you,
 nothing but sorrow should we then be knowing.”

===Chinese===
「你们想问什么就快问，
我们必须赶快离开；
倘若被人看见同你们在一起，
我们到时只剩愁苦。」

===Original===
“We beg you first to tell us
 who this land doth own?
 Whose are the castles also?
 By what name is he known

===Chinese===
「我们恳请你们先说说，
这片国土归谁所有？
那些城堡又是谁的？
那人身负何名，

===Original===
 Who leaves you without clothing,
 low tasks upon you laying?
 He may of his worth be boastful;
 that he doeth well no man may now be saying.”

===Chinese===
他竟让你们衣衫单薄，
把下贱的活计压在你们身上？
他尽可以夸耀自己的身价；
可谁也不能说他行事端正。」

===Original===
To him Gudrun thus answered:
 “Hartmut is one of the lords
 To whom these lands owe fealty.
 His castles well he guards,

===Chinese===
古德伦这样回答他：
「哈特穆特是这里的领主之一，
这片土地都向他称臣纳贡。
他把城堡守卫得严严实实，

===Original===
 With Ludwig, king of the Normans,
 who is Hartmut’s father:
 And many knightly vassals,
 to keep their lands from foes, they round them gather.”

===Chinese===
还有诺曼人之王路德维希（Ludwig），
他是哈特穆特的父亲：
众多骑士封臣环卫着他们，
为他们抵御仇敌、守护疆土。」

===Original===
“Gladly would we see them,”
 said Ortwin, the friendly knight;
 “Happy were I, fair lady,
 if we could learn aright

===Chinese===
「我们很乐意见到他们，」
和善的骑士奥特温说道；
「美丽的贵女，倘若我们
能打听个确切，

===Original===
 Where, within their kingdom,
 we might those kings be meeting,
 We bring to them an errand;
 as henchmen of a king, we bear his greeting.”

===Chinese===
知道在这片国土上的何处
可以拜见那些君王，
我们身负一桩使命；
身为国王的侍从，我们捎来他的问候。」

===Original===
Gudrun, the high-born lady,
 thus to the warrior spake:
 “This very morning early,
 ere yet they were awake,

===Chinese===
出身高贵的贵妇古德伦
对武士这样说道：
「就在今天清晨，
趁他们还未醒来，

===Original===
 I left them in their castle;
 in their beds they slumbered.
 I know not if thence they have ridden:
 their men, I think, full forty hundred numbered.”

===Chinese===
我离开了他们的城堡；
他们还在床上酣睡。
不知他们可曾骑马外出：
我想他们的部众足有四千之数。」

===Original===
Again King Herwic asked her:
 “To us yet further tell,
 Why is it such brave princes
 in fear like this should dwell,

===Chinese===
赫尔维克王又问她：
「再跟我们讲讲，
为何这般英武的君王
要在如此的惶恐中度日，

===Original===
 That they so many warriors
 always should be needing?
 Had I that band of fighters,
 to gain a kingdom I would them be leading.”

===Chinese===
竟要时时刻刻
豢养这么多的武士？
倘若我拥有那支劲旅，
我定要带着他们去夺取一个王国。」

===Original===
To him Gudrun thus answered:
 “Of that we nothing know;
 And where their lands are lying,
 that neither can we show:

===Chinese===
古德伦这样回答他：
「这个我们一无所知；
他们的疆土坐落何处，
我们也说不上来：

===Original===
 But from the Hegeling kingdom,
 although it is not near them,
 They fear that harm awaits them
 from foes who soon may come, who hatred bear them.”

===Chinese===
只是他们惧怕黑格林（Hegeling）王国——
尽管它离他们遥远——
会有怀恨的仇敌很快前来，
降下他们担忧的灾祸。」

===Original===
Trembling, cold, and shivering,
 the maids before them stood;
 Then the knightly Herwic
 spake, in kindly mood:

===Chinese===
少女们又冷又颤，
哆嗦着立在他们面前；
骑士赫尔维克
于是和颜悦色地开言：

===Original===
 “I would, most lovely ladies,
 if we might be so daring,
 And if no shame it gave you,
 that on the shore our cloaks you would be wearing.”

===Chinese===
「美丽的贵女，倘若我们
可以这样冒昧，
倘若这不致使你们蒙羞，
我真愿你们在岸边披上我们的斗篷。」

===Original===
Hilda’s daughter answered:
 “May God your kindness bless;
 We cannot take your mantles,
 but we thank you none the less.

===Chinese===
希尔达之女答道：
「愿上帝赐福你们的善意；
我们不能接受你们的披风，
但仍要感谢你们。

===Original===
 No eye shall ever see me
 manly clothing wearing.”
 If only the maidens knew it,
 much greater ills would they yet be often bearing.

===Chinese===
任何人的眼睛都休想看见我
身穿男人的衣裳。」
少女们若是知道内情，
往后还不知要受多少更大的苦楚。

===Original===
Oft the eyes of Herwic
 did on the maiden rest;
 To him she seemed most comely,
 and her bearing was the best.

===Chinese===
赫尔维克的目光
频频落在那少女身上；
在他眼里她秀美绝伦，
仪态也最为出众。

===Original===
 For all her heavy sorrows
 sighs in his heart were wakened;
 And to one erst thought of kindly,
 from him long taken, he the maiden likened.

===Chinese===
为她深重的苦难，
他心中唤醒了声声叹息；
他觉得这少女酷似
他昔日挂念、久已离散的那个人。

===Original===
Then spake again young Ortwin,
 who was of Ortland king:
 “Can either of you ladies
 tidings whatever bring

===Chinese===
年轻的奥特温又开口了，
他正是奥特兰（Ortland）之王：
「两位贵女可有什么音讯，
无论什么都能相告，

===Original===
 Of a band of homeless maidens
 who to this land were carried?
 Gudrun was one among them,
 and gladly would we learn where she has tarried.”

===Chinese===
关于一群无家可归的少女，
她们被掳到了这片国土？
古德伦也在她们中间，
我们很想知道她如今滞留何方。」

===Original===
To him the maiden answered:
 “To me is that well known;
 A maiden throng came hither
 in days now long bygone:

===Chinese===
那少女回答他：
「这事我知道得很清楚；
曾有一大群少女
在久远的往日被带到这里：

===Original===
 They to this far-off kingdom
 by fighters bold were taken;
 And full of heavy sorrow
 came these maids forlorn, of hope forsaken.

===Chinese===
她们被剽悍的武士
掳到这遥远的国度；
这些孤苦的少女满怀沉痛，
心中的希望尽已断绝。

===Original===
“The maid whom you are seeking
 I know,” she said, “full well;
 I here have seen her toiling,
 this for a truth I tell.”

===Chinese===
「你们要寻找的那位少女，
我认识，」她说道，「千真万确；
我在这里亲眼见过她操劳，
这话句句属实。」

===Original===
 She was herself the maiden
 who was by Hartmut stolen,
 Gudrun, Queen Hilda’s daughter,
 and all she told had erst herself befallen.

===Chinese===
她自己正是那个
被哈特穆特掳走的少女，
希尔达王后之女古德伦，
她所说的一切都是亲身遭遇。

===Original===
Then spake the knightly Herwic:
 “Ortwin, list to me:
 If fair Gudrun, your sister,
 yet alive may be,

===Chinese===
骑士赫尔维克说道：
「奥特温，听我说：
倘若你美丽的姐姐古德伦
还活在人间，

===Original===
 In any land whatever,
 for us on earth still watching,
 This must be that lady;
 ne’er have I seen two maids so nearly matching.”

===Chinese===
无论在世上什么地方，
还在人间企望着我们，
这一位必是她无疑；
我从未见过如此相像的两位少女。」

===Original===
To him then said young Ortwin:
 “The maid in truth is fair,
 But to my long-lost sister
 no likeness doth she bear.

===Chinese===
年轻的奥特温对他说：
「这少女确实美丽，
但与我失散多年的姐姐
并无相像之处。

===Original===
 The days are not forgotten
 when we were young together;
 Should I rove the whole world over,
 so fair as she I ne’er could find another.”

===Chinese===
我们年幼相伴的岁月
至今记忆犹新；
纵然我走遍整个世界，
也再找不到像她这样美丽的人。」

===Original===
When now Gudrun, who listened,
 heard the name of the man,
 That his friend did call him Ortwin,
 she looked at him again:

===Chinese===
这时一旁倾听的古德伦
听见了那人的名字，
他的同伴唤他奥特温，
便又把目光投向他：

===Original===
 For she indeed were happy
 if she were thus befriended,
 And found in him a brother,
 for then her cares were o’er and her sorrows ended.

===Chinese===
因为倘若真有这般际遇，
她确实会喜出望外，
在他身上认出一位兄长，
那她的烦忧便会终止，愁苦也将了结。

===Original===
“However they may call you,
 a worthy knight are you:
 A man in all things like you
 in days of yore I knew;

===Chinese===
「不论人们如何称呼你，
你都是位可敬的骑士：
像你这样处处相似的一个人，
我往日曾经识得；

===Original===
 The name of Herwic bore he,
 in Sealand was his dwelling.
 If that brave knight were living,
 to loose us from our bonds he were not failing.

===Chinese===
他名叫赫尔维克，
在西兰安身立业。
倘若那位英勇的骑士还在人世，
他必定会解救我们脱离枷锁。

===Original===
“I am one of the maidens
 whom Hartmut’s warriors stole,
 And bore across the waters,
 in thraldom sorrowful.

===Chinese===
「我正是那些少女中的一个，
被哈特穆特的武士掳走，
又被载过重洋，
在奴役中悲苦度日。

===Original===
 Gudrun you here are seeking,
 but need not thus have hasted;
 The queenly Hegeling maiden
 at last is dead, with toil and hardship wasted.”

===Chinese===
你们在此寻找古德伦，
却本不必这般急切赶来；
那王后般的黑格林少女
终究死了，被苦役和磨难耗尽了生命。」

===Original===
The eyes of Ortwin glistened,
 filling fast with tears;
 Nor was it without weeping
 that now King Herwic hears

===Chinese===
奥特温的双眼闪闪发亮，
很快噙满泪水；
赫尔维克王听闻
这消息时，也不免潸然落泪——

===Original===
 The tidings to them given﻿—
 that fair Gudrun, their lady,
 From them by death was taken;
 at this their heavy hearts to break were ready.

===Chinese===
这噩耗他们已然听悉——
美丽的贵女古德伦
已被死亡从他们身边夺去；
两颗沉重的心闻言几乎碎裂。

===Original===
When both, before her weeping,
 were seen by the homeless maid,
 With eyes upon them fastened,
 thus to them she said:

===Chinese===
当这位无家可归的少女
看见两人在她面前垂泪，
便目不转睛地望着他们，
这样对他们说道：

===Original===
 “It seems to me most likely,
 by the mood that you are wearing,
 That to Gudrun, the maiden,
 you worthy knights are love and kinship bearing.”

===Chinese===
「从你们脸上流露的神情
看来十有八九，
你们两位高贵的骑士
对古德伦少女怀着爱意与亲谊。」

===Original===
To her young Herwic answered:
 “Yes, for the maid, forsooth,
 I shall pine till life be ended;
 to me she gave her troth,

===Chinese===
年轻的赫尔维克答道：
「是的，为了那位少女，
我确将憔悴至死；
她曾与我立下婚约，

===Original===
 And to me, in wedlock plighted,
 with faithful oaths was given:
 Since then, by the craft of Ludwig,
 her have I lost, by him from her birthland riven.”

===Chinese===
并以忠贞的誓约
许配给我为妻：
自那以后，由于路德维希的奸计，
我失去了她，她被他生生夺离了故土。」

===Original===
Then said the sorrowing maiden:
 “Your words would me mislead,
 For men have often told me
 that Herwic long is dead.

===Chinese===
忧伤的少女说道：
「你们的话怕是要把我误导，
因为人们常常告诉我，
赫尔维克早已死去。

===Original===
 No bliss on earth were greater,
 that God to me were granting,
 Could I learn that he is living;
 a friend to lead me hence were then not wanting.”

===Chinese===
倘若我能得知他还活着，
上帝赐给我的恩惠
再没有比这更大的了；
那时便不愁没有朋友带我离开这里。」

===Original===
Then said the knightly Herwic:
 “Upon my hand now look;
 Know you this ring I am wearing?
 Mine is the name you spoke;

===Chinese===
骑士赫尔维克说道：
「现在看看我的手；
你可认得我戴着的这枚戒指？
你方才说出的名字正是我的名字；

===Original===
 With this were we betrothèd:
 to Gudrun I am faithful ever,
 And if you were my loved one,
 I would lead you hence, and would forsake you never.”

===Chinese===
我们就是用它订的婚：
我对古德伦永远忠诚，
倘若你就是我的爱人，
我定带你离开此地，永不将你背弃。」

===Original===
Upon his hand then looking,
 a ring there met her sight,
 Set with a stone from Abalie,
 in gold that glittered bright;

===Chinese===
她朝他的手上望去，
一枚戒指映入眼帘，
镶着一颗阿巴利（Abalie）的宝石，
金环灿灿生辉；

===Original===
 Never her eyes had rested
 on one more rich or fairer.
 Gudrun, the queenly maiden,
 of this same ring had whilom been the wearer.

===Chinese===
她的眼睛从未见过
比这更华美珍奇的戒指。
王后般的少女古德伦，
从前正是这枚戒指的佩戴者。

===Original===
The happy maiden, smiling,
 with words her bliss did show:
 “Of this I once was owner,
 and well the ring I know.

===Chinese===
幸福的少女微笑着，
用言语道出心中的欢喜：
「这戒指我曾拥有过，
我对它再熟悉不过。

===Original===
 Look upon this I am wearing;
 ’twas the gift of my early lover,
 While I, a gladsome maiden,
 still dwelt at home, nor stepped its borders over.”

===Chinese===
看看我手上戴的这枚；
那是我早年情郎的赠礼，
那时我还是个快乐的少女，
安居家中，从未跨出家门一步。」

===Original===
He, on her hand now gazing,
 upon the ring did look;
 Then unto the maiden
 the knightly Herwic spoke:

===Chinese===
他凝望着她的手，
端详那枚戒指；
随后骑士赫尔维克
对少女说道：

===Original===
 “That a queenly mother bore thee,
 I see by many a token;
 After my heavy sorrows,
 a blessed sight upon my eyes has broken.”

===Chinese===
「从种种迹象我看得出，
生你的是一位王后般的母亲；
在我深重的忧患之后，
一幅蒙福的景象终于映入眼帘。」

===Original===
Then in his arms he folded
 the fair and high-born maid:
 For all they told each other
 they were both glad and sad.

===Chinese===
于是他把这位美丽、
出身高贵的少女拥入怀中：
他们彼此诉说的一切，
让两人既欢喜又心酸。

===Original===
 He kissed the maiden fondly,
 how oft I cannot reckon;
 So, too, he greeted Hildeburg,
 showing his love to both the maids forsaken.

===Chinese===
他深情地亲吻少女，
次数多得我无法计数；
他也同样问候了希尔德堡，
对这两个被弃的少女都表露了爱怜。

===Original===
Then the youthful Ortwin
 begged the maid to say
 Whether to do her task-work
 there was no other way

===Chinese===
随后年轻的奥特温
恳请少女告知，
要完成她那些活计，
是否别无他法，

===Original===
 Than, standing by the seaside,
 all day to wash the clothing?
 At this she greatly sorrowed,
 and felt for her work the deepest shame and loathing.

===Chinese===
只能伫立在海边
整日浆洗衣裳？
听闻此问她悲从中来，
对这份苦役感到了最深的羞耻与厌憎。

===Original===
“Tell me now, fair sister,
 where may your children be
 Whom you have borne to Hartmut,
 in his land across the sea,

===Chinese===
「告诉我，美丽的姐姐，
你的孩子们都在哪里，
你为哈特穆特生下的、
远在海那边他国土上的孩子，

===Original===
 That all alone on the seashore
 to wash they thus allow you?
 If here a queen they call you,
 the name you bear but little good can do you.”

===Chinese===
他们竟容你独自一人
在海滨洗衣？
纵然他们在此称你为王后，
这名号也带给你半点好处。」

===Original===
Shedding tears, she answered:
 “How should I have a child?
 No love could Hartmut kindle,
 that I to him should yield;

===Chinese===
她含泪答道：
「我怎么会有孩子？
哈特穆特点燃不了爱火，
让我向他屈身相就；

===Original===
 And well do all men know it
 who near him here are dwelling.
 Because I would not love him
 I now must toil, and woe my heart is swelling.”

===Chinese===
住在他近旁的人
对此都一清二楚。
只因我不肯爱他，
如今才受这苦役，我心中悲苦填膺。」

===Original===
Then spake the knightly Herwic:
 “We now can truly say
 That we good luck have met with,
 on our errand far away;

===Chinese===
骑士赫尔维克说道：
「如今我们当真可以说，
我们此番远行的差遣
交上了好运气；

===Original===
 And nought could have befallen
 that for us were better.
 It behooves us now to hasten
 to free the maid from the ills that here beset her.”

===Chinese===
再没有比这更顺遂的遭际了。
我们眼下必须抓紧，
把少女从这围困她的祸患中
解救出来。」

===Original===
Then said the knight, young Ortwin:
 “That may never be.
 Had I a hundred sisters,
 I would sooner let them die

===Chinese===
骑士年轻的奥特温说道：
「那万万不可。
即便我有一百个姐妹，
我也宁可让她们死去，

===Original===
 Than here, in another’s kingdom,
 to hide a deed of plunder;
 Stealing those from our foemen
 whom they by stormy fight from us did sunder.”

===Chinese===
也不愿在别人的国土上
藏匿这桩劫掠的勾当，
从仇敌那里偷回
他们凭血战从我们身边夺走的人。」

===Original===
Then spake the lord of Sealand:
 “This do I greatly fear,
 Should our search be known to any,
 or if they find us here,

===Chinese===
西兰之王说道：
「我深深惧怕的是，
倘若我们的寻访被人察觉，
或是他们在这里撞见我们，

===Original===
 They then may take the maiden,
 and her far hence may carry,
 And never shall we see her:
 ’twere best to hide the deed, nor longer tarry.”

===Chinese===
他们便会把少女掳走，
带到遥远的地方，
我们将永远见不到她：
不如隐匿此行踪迹，不要再耽搁。」

===Original===
Him did Ortwin answer:
 “How can we leave in need
 Her faithful band of maidens?
 So long a stay they’ve made

===Chinese===
奥特温回答他：
「我们怎能弃之不顾，
让她那群忠心的侍女
陷于困厄？她们已经滞留

===Original===
 Here in this land of foemen
 that well may they be weary:
 Gudrun, my worthy sister,
 should ne’er forsake her maids, in bondage dreary.”

===Chinese===
在这仇敌的国土上
这么久，想必要厌倦了：
我高贵的姐姐古德伦
决不该抛弃囹圄中的侍女们。」

===Original===
To him then spake brave Herwic:
 “Is this in truth your mind?
 Ne’er shall my well-belovèd
 be left by me behind;

===Chinese===
英勇的赫尔维克对他说：
「这果真是你的心意？
我心爱的人儿，
我绝不会把她丢在身后；

===Original===
 To take the ladies with us,
 e’en as we can, ’tis better.”
 Him did Ortwin answer:
 “Here to be hacked with the sword for me were fitter.”

===Chinese===
能带女士们同走，
就尽量带上，这样更好。」
奥特温回答他：
「依我看，留在此地挨剑砍更相宜。」

===Original===
Then said the downcast maiden:
 “What have I done to thee,
 My dearest brother Ortwin?
 Never as yet in me

===Chinese===
沮丧的少女说道：
「我究竟哪里对不住你，
我最亲爱的兄长奥特温？
在我身上可从未见过

===Original===
 Was seen such ill-behavior
 that I for that was chidden.
 For what great sin I know not
 am I, my lord, to make atonement bidden?”

===Chinese===
什么失德的行止，
曾招来这般责备。
不知为了何等大罪，
我的君上，我竟被勒令赎罪？」

===Original===
“I do not thus, dear sister,
 for want of love to thee;
 Thereby your band of maidens
 I shall the better free.

===Chinese===
「我这样做，亲爱的姐姐，
并非出于对你的爱有欠缺；
如此反而能让我
更顺利地救出你那一群侍女。

===Original===
 Only as fits my honor,
 hence will I ever take you;
 Herwic for your lover
 you yet shall have, and ne’er will he forsake you.”

===Chinese===
我只是要按合乎我荣誉的方式，
堂堂正正地带你离开；
你终将重新得到赫尔维克作情郎，
他永远也不会把你抛弃。」

===Original===
Gudrun was heavy-hearted
 as they went on board the boat;
 She said: “Woe worth my wanderings!
 my sorrow endeth not.

===Chinese===
古德伦心情沉重，
当他们登上小船之际；
她说道：「我这漂泊的命运何其不幸！
我的忧伤没有尽头。

===Original===
 He whom once I trusted,
 must hope in him be shaken
 That he will break my bondage?
 My bliss is yet far off, and my faith mistaken.”

===Chinese===
我曾深信的那个人，
我对他的指望难道
竟要动摇——指望他斩断我的枷锁？
我的幸福还遥遥无期，我的信赖怕是错付了。」

===Original===
In haste the daring warriors
 turned from the shore away.
 Gudrun, the maid, heart-broken,
 to Herwic called to stay:

===Chinese===
大胆的武士们匆匆
调转船头离开海岸。
心碎的少女古德伦
呼喊赫尔维克留下：

===Original===
 “Of me you once thought highly,
 but now you hold me lightly:
 To whom, in my woe, do you leave me?
 Bereft of kin, to whom can I trust me rightly?”

===Chinese===
「你从前高看我，
如今却轻贱我：
在我的苦难中，你要把我丢给谁？
举目无亲，我又能信托何人？」

===Original===
“I do not hold you lightly;
 you are of maids the best.
 My coming, queenly lady,
 hide within your breast;

===Chinese===
「我并没有轻贱你；
你是少女中最出众的。
王后般的贵女，请把我来过的事
深藏在你心中；

===Original===
 Again, ere morning lightens,
 these shores will I be treading﻿—
 For this my troth I pledge you﻿—
 eighty thousand followers with me leading.”

===Chinese===
天色未明，我便会再度
踏上这片海岸——
我以我的誓约向你担保——
率领八万部众同来。」

===Original===
As fast as they were able
 they hastened then away;
 Never friends were sundered
 more sadly than that day

===Chinese===
他们尽其所能，
急匆匆扬长而去；
亲友之间的离别，
从未有比那天更令人心伤——

===Original===
 Were these from one another;
 (the truth to you I’m telling.)
 As far as their eyes could follow,
 the maidens watched the boat away fast sailing.

===Chinese===
他们就这样彼此分离；
（我向你们说的是实情。）
少女们目送小船飞快驶远，
一直望到目光的尽头。

===Original===
Gudrun, the queenly maiden,
 her washing now forgot;
 Betwixt her bliss and sorrow,
 her toil she heeded not.

===Chinese===
王后般的少女古德伦
此刻忘却了洗衣；
处在欢喜与忧愁之间，
她全然顾不上劳作。

===Original===
 The harsh and wicked Gerlind,
 the idle women spying
 Standing by the seashore,
 in anger stormed, that her clothes unwashed were lying.

===Chinese===
冷酷恶毒的格尔林德
窥见那两个闲散的女子
伫立在海滨，
不禁勃然大怒——她的衣裳竟搁着未洗。

===Original===
Then said the maiden Hildeburg,
 from Ireland, o’er the sea:
 “Why do you let the clothing
 here uncared for be?

===Chinese===
来自海外爱尔兰的
少女希尔德堡说道：
「你为何任凭这些衣裳
丢在这里无人照管？

===Original===
 The clothes of Ludwig’s followers
 still unwashed are waiting.
 If this be known to Gerlind,
 yet harder blows from her shall we be getting.”

===Chinese===
路德维希随从们的衣裳
还等着未洗。
这事若让格尔林德知道了，
我们还要挨更狠的毒打。」

===Original===
Queen Hilda’s daughter answered:
 “Too proud I am, I ween,
 That for the wicked Gerlind
 I e’er should wash again.

===Chinese===
希尔达王后之女答道：
「我想我心气太高，
再也不肯为恶毒的格尔林德
洗什么衣裳。

===Original===
 Henceforth a toil so lowly
 in scorn shall I be holding,
 For two young kings have kissed me,
 they in kindness me in their arms enfolding.”

===Chinese===
从今往后，这样下贱的苦活
我要加以鄙夷，
因为两位年轻的君王亲吻了我，
还亲切地把我拥入他们怀中。」

===Original===
Then Hildeburg made answer:
 “Scorn not that I should teach
 Or that I now would show you
 how best the clothes to bleach:

===Chinese===
希尔德堡随即答道：
「别嫌我多嘴教训你，
也别怪我此刻指点你
怎样把衣裳漂洗得最白：

===Original===
 We must not leave them yellow,
 but carefully must whiten;
 Else do I greatly fear me
 our backs with blows and stripes will well be beaten.”

===Chinese===
总不能任它们泛黄，
得仔细把它们洗白；
否则我真担心，
我们的背脊又要挨一顿好打。」

===Original===
Then said old Hagen’s grandchild:
 “At last my lot is bright,
 With hope and gladness beaming.
 If they my back shall smite

===Chinese===
老哈根（Hagen）的孙女说道：
「我的命运终于放亮，
闪耀着希望与欢欣。
他们纵然抽打我的背，

===Original===
 With rods, from now till morning,
 I trow it will not kill me;
 But soon shall those who wronged us
 know themselves the ills they chose to deal me.

===Chinese===
用鞭条从现在抽到天明，
我相信也抽不死我；
倒是那些亏待我们的人，
很快就要自尝他们存心加害我的恶果。

===Original===
“These clothes I should be washing
 down to the tide I’ll bear,
 And fling them into the water,”
 said the maiden fair;

===Chinese===
「这些本该由我来洗的衣裳，
我要把它们带到潮水边，
一股脑儿抛进海里，」
美丽的少女说道；

===Original===
 “Their freedom I will give them,
 even as ’tis fitting
 That I, a queen, should do it;
 hence they may float away, no hindrance meeting.”

===Chinese===
「我要还它们自由，
正如我身为王后
理当行事的模样；
让它们漂走，一路畅通无阻。」

===Original===
Whate’er was said by Hildeburg,
 Gudrun the clothes then took,
 That Gerlind her had given;
 her task she would not brook,

===Chinese===
无论希尔德堡如何劝说，
古德伦还是抱起了衣裳——
那是格尔林德交给她的；
这差事她再不愿忍受，

===Original===
 But far into the billows
 she threw them, strongly hurling:
 I know not if ever she found them;
 they soon were lost to sight, in the waters swirling.

===Chinese===
而是奋力扬手，
把它们远远抛进巨浪：
我不知她后来可曾寻见；
衣裳转眼便消失在漩涡翻卷的水中。

===Original===
The night was drawing nearer,
 and the light began to wane;
 To the castle, heavy-laden,
 went Hildeburg again.

===Chinese===
夜幕渐渐逼近，
天光开始暗淡；
希尔德堡又肩负重担，
向城堡走去。

===Original===
 Seven robes of finest linen
 she bore, with other clothing;
 Gudrun, young Ortwin’s sister,
 with Hildeburg went also, bearing nothing.

===Chinese===
她抱着七件上好的亚麻长袍，
外加别的衣裳；
年轻的奥特温之姊古德伦，
也随希尔德堡同去，两手空空。

===Original===
When they had reached the castle,
 the time was very late.
 Before King Ludwig’s palace,
 standing at the gate,

===Chinese===
她们回到城堡时，
夜色已十分深沉。
在路德维希王的宫前，
立在大门旁边，

===Original===
 They saw the wicked Gerlind,
 watching there to meet them:
 Soon as she saw the washers,
 with words of bitter scorn she ’gan to greet them.

===Chinese===
她们望见恶毒的格尔林德
正守在那里等着她们：
她一见到洗衣的女仆，
便开口用刻毒的话羞辱她们。

===Original===
Thus she spake in anger:
 “What does this gadding mean?
 Stripes upon your bodies
 you both have earned, I ween,

===Chinese===
她怒气冲冲地骂道：
「这般闲逛游荡是什么意思？
我看你们两个都挣下了
一顿好鞭子，

===Original===
 Thus upon the seashore,
 in the evening light, to wander;
 For me it were unseemly
 into my room to take you, after loitering yonder.”

===Chinese===
竟这样在暮色里
海滨之畔信步徜徉；
你们在外头磨蹭了这半天，
我再让你们进我的屋子就不成体统了。」

===Original===
She said: “Now tell me quickly,
 think you this is meet?
 You spurn the greatest princes,
 and show them nought but hate,

===Chinese===
她说：「现在快说给我听，
你们可觉得这样像话？
你们对最尊贵的君王不屑一顾，
对他们只显得满怀憎恨，

===Original===
 But linger yet, at nightfall,
 with low-born varlets flirting.
 Would you be thought of highly,
 know you that this your own good name is hurting.”

===Chinese===
入夜却同下贱的汉子们
厮混调笑。
你们若还想让人高看，
可知这是在糟践自己的名声。」

===Original===
The well-born maiden answered:
 “Why speak of me so ill?
 Never have I, poor maiden,
 had the thought or will

===Chinese===
出身高贵的少女答道：
「为何把我说得这样难听？
我这可怜的少女，可从未起过
那样的念头或心思，

===Original===
 With any man to tattle,
 however dear I held him,
 Unless it were a kinsman;
 a talk with him I rightfully might yield him.”

===Chinese===
同任何男人私语调笑，
无论我把他看得多亲，
除非他是我的亲族；
同他交谈才是我名正言顺可以应允的。」

===Original===
“Say you I chide you wrongly?
 Hush, you idle jade!
 For this, to-night, I tell you,
 a reckoning shall be made.

===Chinese===
「你说我责骂得冤枉？
住口，你这懒贱婢！
今夜我要告诉你，
这笔账定要好好清算。

===Original===
 To be so bold and shameless
 you then will dare no longer;
 Before with you I’ve ended,
 your back shall feel that I than you am stronger.”

===Chinese===
看你往后还敢不敢
这般放肆无耻；
不等同你算了结，
你的背脊就会知道我比你厉害。」

===Original===
“In that will I gainsay you,”
 said then the maiden proud;
 “Again with rods to beat me
 you ne’er shall be allowed.

===Chinese===
「这一点我可要驳你，」
高傲的少女说道；
「想再用鞭条抽我，
你们再也不能得逞。

===Original===
 You and all your kindred
 in birth are far below me;
 You may yet for this be sorry,
 if treatment so unseemly you shall show me.”

===Chinese===
论出身，你和你的满门亲眷
都远远配不上我；
倘若你如此无礼地待我，
日后你有得后悔。」

===Original===
Then spake the wolfish Gerlind:
 “Where is my clothing left,
 That, folded in your apron,
 you thus your hands have wrapt;

===Chinese===
恶狼般的格尔林德说道：
「我的衣裳哪里去了，
怎么被你们裹在围裙里，
这样抱在手中？

===Original===
 Bearing yourself so idly,
 now from toil thus turning?
 If I live a little longer,
 another kind of work shall you be learning.”

===Chinese===
摆出这般悠闲的做派，
如今竟想撂下活计？
只要我再活些时日，
定叫你尝尝另一种苦工的滋味。」

===Original===
King Hagen’s grandchild answered:
 “Down by the deep sea-flood
 I left your clothing lying.
 It was too great a load;

===Chinese===
哈根王的孙女答道：
「在深深的潮水边，
我把你的衣裳撂下了。
那担子实在太重；

===Original===
 I found the weight too heavy,
 alone to the house to carry.
 If never again you see them,
 but little I care, the while with you I tarry.”

===Chinese===
我一个人实在扛不动，
没法带回府里。
哪怕你再也见不着它们，
只要我还待在你这儿，就毫不在乎。」

===Original===
Then quoth the old she-devil:
 “All this shall help you not;
 Before I sleep this evening,
 bitter shall be your lot!”

===Chinese===
老女魔随即说道：
「这一切都帮不了你；
今夜我上床之前，
定叫你尝尝苦头！」

===Original===
 Then were tied, at her bidding,
 rods from hedges broken;
 Gerlind would not give over
 the training hard ’gainst which the maid had spoken.

===Chinese===
于是应她的命令，
人们折下树篱的枝条捆作鞭条；
格尔林德不肯罢休，
定要施那少女刚才抗议过的严酷管教。

===Original===
Then strongly to a bedstead
 she bade them bind the maid,
 And alone in a room to leave her,
 where not a friend she had:

===Chinese===
随后她又命人
把少女牢牢绑在床架上，
再把她独自留在房中，
那里她举目无亲：

===Original===
 There should she be beaten,
 till skin from bone was falling.
 When this was known to her women,
 they all began to weep, and loud were wailing.

===Chinese===
他们要在那里抽打她，
直到皮开肉绽。
这事传到侍女们耳中，
众人无不失声痛哭，哀号不已。

===Original===
Then spake Gudrun, with cunning:
 “Now list to what I say:
 If I with rods am beaten
 thus shamefully to-day,

===Chinese===
古德伦随即机敏地说道：
「现在听我讲：
倘若我今日就这样
受了奇耻、挨了鞭条，

===Original===
 Should e’er an eye behold me
 with kings and princes seated,
 And I a crown be wearing,
 to you a fit reward shall then be meted.

===Chinese===
日后倘若有眼睛看见我
与君王贵胄同席而坐，
头上还戴着王冠，
你们那时必会得到相称的报偿。

===Original===
“Henceforth for me such teaching
 ’twere best you let alone;
 Sooner the king I’ve slighted
 shall have me for his own:

===Chinese===
「往后再这样的管教，
你们最好还是作罢；
我瞧不起的那位君王
说不定早晚会把我弄到手：

===Original===
 Then as queen of Normandy
 here will I be dwelling;
 And when I here am mighty,
 what I will do may no one now be telling.”

===Chinese===
那时我将以诺曼底王后之尊
在这里安居；
待我大权在握，
谁也说不准我会做出什么来。」

===Original===
“Be this your will,” said Gerlind,
 “angry no more I’ll be:
 E’en if a thousand garments
 you thus had lost for me,

===Chinese===
「就依你的意思吧，」格尔林德说，
「我不会再动怒：
纵然你为我
弄丢了一千件衣裳，

===Original===
 I would, in truth, forgive it;
 well you will have thriven
 If to my son, young Hartmut,
 the Norman prince, your love at last be given.”

===Chinese===
我也真心愿意原谅；
只要你最终把爱
给了我儿年轻的哈特穆特、
那位诺曼人王子，你就是大大的造化了。」

===Original===
Then said the lovely maiden:
 “I now would take some rest;
 This care and heavy sorrow
 my strength doth sorely waste.

===Chinese===
可爱的少女说道：
「我现在想歇息片刻；
这份操劳与沉重的悲伤
把我的气力耗损得太厉害。

===Original===
 Send for the young King Hartmut,
 bid him be hither speeding,
 And say, whate’er he wishes,
 that I henceforth will always do his bidding.”

===Chinese===
请去唤年轻的哈特穆特王，
叫他速速到这里来，
并告诉他，无论他想要什么，
从今往后我都一概依从他。」

===Original===
Those who heard them talking,
 straightway to Hartmut ran,
 And to the youthful warrior
 told the tale again.

===Chinese===
听到她们谈话的人
立刻跑去见哈特穆特，
把这桩事一五一十
讲给了年轻的武士听。

===Original===
 Some of his father’s liegemen
 there with him were seated,
 When word to him was given
 in haste to seek Gudrun, who for him waited.

===Chinese===
他父亲手下的几名封臣
正陪坐在他身边，
这时有人传话给他，
要他赶快去见正在等他的古德伦。

===Original===
Then said the one who told him:
 “Give me now my fee;
 Queen Hilda’s lovely daughter
 will grant her love to thee.

===Chinese===
报信的人开口道：
「现在给我赏钱吧；
希尔达王后美丽的女儿
愿把她的爱许给你。

===Original===
 She bids you now to hasten
 at once to her in her bower;
 No longer are you hated,
 for better thoughts she harbors than of yore.”

===Chinese===
她命你现在就火速
赶到她的闺阁相见；
你不再被她憎恨了，
她心中的念头比从前好多了。」

===Original===
The high-born knight then answered:
 “To lie you have no need.
 If true indeed were your tidings,
 well should you be feed;

===Chinese===
出身高贵的骑士答道：
「你们用不着撒谎。
倘若这消息属实，
你们理当得到厚赏；

===Original===
 By me would three great castles
 and a hide of land be given,
 With sixty golden arm-bands;
 while bliss thenceforth my days should long enliven.”

===Chinese===
我要赏赐三座雄伟的城堡
和一海得土地，
外加六十只黄金臂环；
让欣喜从此长驻，为我余生的岁月添彩。」

===Original===
Then said to him another:
 “This tale, I know, is true;
 The fee should I be sharing.
 At court they wish for you;

===Chinese===
又有一个人对他说：
「这消息我知道是真的；
赏钱也该有我一份。
宫里的人都盼着你去；

===Original===
 Gudrun, the maid, has said it.
 To love you she is ready;
 And if in truth you wish it,
 she in your land will be your queen and lady.”

===Chinese===
这是少女古德伦亲口说的。
她已准备好爱你；
只要你真心愿意，
她将在你的国土上做你的王后和主母。」

===Original===
To those who told the tidings
 his thanks young Hartmut gave;
 From off his seat, o’er-gladdened,
 upsprang the warrior brave.

===Chinese===
年轻的哈特穆特向
报信的人们道了谢；
这位勇武的战士欣喜若狂，
从座位上一跃而起。

===Original===
 He thought that, in His kindness,
 God this boon had done him,
 And, with a heart now happy,
 he sought the maiden’s bower who love had shown him.

===Chinese===
他以为这是上帝
出于慈恩赐给他的福分，
于是怀着欢畅的心，
去寻那对他示爱的少女的闺阁。

===Original===
In garments wet there standing,
 was seen the high-born maid;
 With eyes still dim with weeping,
 greeting to him she said.

===Chinese===
只见出身高贵的少女
立在那里，衣衫犹湿；
她双眼还噙着泪，
向他道了问候。

===Original===
 Forward she came to meet him;
 and now so near was standing
 That he, in fondness turning,
 her in his arms would clasp, towards her bending.

===Chinese===
她迎上前来；
这时站得那样近，
他情不自禁侧身俯就，
想把她搂进自己的臂弯。

===Original===
She said: “Not so, King Hartmut,
 this you may not do;
 For men in truth would wonder
 if they should look on you.

===Chinese===
她说：「万万不可，哈特穆特王，
这您做不得；
人们真会惊诧不已，
倘若他们看见您这副模样。

===Original===
 Nought am I but a washer;
 in scorn would they be holding
 You, a king so mighty,
 if in your arms Gudrun you should be folding.”

===Chinese===
我不过是个洗衣婢；
他们会讥笑您——
笑您这样威严的君王，
竟把古德伦搂在怀里。」

===Original===
“This will I, Sir Hartmut,
 freely to you allow,
 When, by my crown, your kinsmen
 me as a queen shall know.

===Chinese===
「到那时，哈特穆特爵爷，
我自会欣然应允您，
等您的亲族凭着我的王冠
认我为王后。

===Original===
 No longer shall I scorn you,
 when I that name am bearing:
 For both will this be fitting;
 me in your arms to take you may then be daring.”

===Chinese===
待我戴上那名号之后，
便再不会轻慢您：
那时于你我二人皆合宜；
您再把我拥入怀中，也不算唐突。」

===Original===
Then, with all good-breeding,
 he farther off withdrew,
 And thus Gudrun he answered:
 “Maiden fair and true,

===Chinese===
随后他极知礼数地
退开了一段距离，
这样回答古德伦：
「美丽而真诚的少女，

===Original===
 Since now you deign to love me,
 richly will I reward you;
 Myself and all my kinsmen,
 whate’er you bid, will kindness show toward you.”

===Chinese===
既然承你垂青爱我，
我必重重报答你；
我和我的全体亲族，
但凡你所吩咐，都会善待于你。」

===Original===
Then said to him the maiden:
 “Such bliss I never knew.
 If, after my weary toiling,
 I aught may ask of you,

===Chinese===
少女对他说：
「这样的福分我从未体尝。
在这困顿的操劳之后，
倘若我可以向你求点什么，

===Original===
 This first of all I wish for,
 that I, poor wretched lady,
 This night, before I slumber,
 may have for me a restful bath made ready.

===Chinese===
我头一件想要的，
便是我这可怜愁苦的女子
今夜安寝之前，
能有一场安适的沐浴为我备好。

===Original===
“And list to me yet further:
 another boon I crave;
 ’Tis that my friendly maidens
 I now with me may have.

===Chinese===
「再听我往下说：
我还求另一桩恩典；
便是我那些可亲的侍女，
现在能让她们回到我身边。

===Original===
 Among Queen Gerlind’s women
 you will find them, sad and weary;
 But in their room no longer
 those toiling ones away from me must tarry.”

===Chinese===
她们就在格尔林德王后的女仆群里，
个个悲苦疲惫；
只是别再让那些操劳的人儿
留居别室，与我分离。」

===Original===
“Your wish I grant you freely,”
 the young King Hartmut said.
 Then from the room of the women
 the many maids were led;

===Chinese===
「你的愿望我欣然应允，」
年轻的哈特穆特王说道。
随后从女仆们的房中
领出了众多的少女；

===Original===
 With hair unkempt and streaming,
 and scanty clothing wearing,
 They to court betook them:
 for them the wicked Gerlind nought was caring.

===Chinese===
她们蓬头散发，
衣衫单薄，
一同来到宫中：
恶毒的格尔林德对她们毫不挂心。

===Original===
Of these came three and sixty;
 on them did Hartmut look.
 Then Gudrun, the high-born,
 with lofty breeding spoke:

===Chinese===
她们来了六十三人；
哈特穆特目光落在她们身上。
出身高贵的古德伦
随即以尊贵的气度开言：

===Original===
 “Behold, my lord, these maidens!
 Is it your worth befitting
 That they are thus uncared for?”
 He said: “No more shall they the like be meeting.”

===Chinese===
「君上，请看这些少女！
这样慢待她们，
可还配得上您的尊贵？」
他说：「她们再不会遭遇这样的对待了。」

===Original===
Then spake the high-born lady:
 “Hartmut, for love of me,
 I beg that these my maidens,
 whom here in shame you see,

===Chinese===
出身高贵的贵妇又说道：
「哈特穆特，看在我的情分上，
我恳请你让这些我的侍女——
你亲眼见在此受辱的这些人，

===Original===
 May have a bath made ready.
 Let now my word be heeded;
 You ought yourself to see them
 decked in the comely clothes they long have needed.”

===Chinese===
也能备好一场沐浴。
请现在就依我的话行事；
您还该亲自去看她们
穿上早已缺欠的体面衣裳。」

===Original===
To her then answered Hartmut,
 of knights a worthy one:
 “Gudrun, belovèd lady,
 if clothes the maids have none

===Chinese===
哈特穆特回答她，
他不愧为骑士中的楷模：
「古德伦，我心爱的贵女，
倘若少女们没有衣裳

===Original===
 Erst by them brought hither,
 when they their home were leaving,
 To them yet other clothing,
 the best in all the world, will I be giving.

===Chinese===
可穿——那都是她们当初
离家时随身带来的——
我便要另外赐予她们衣裳，
举世无双的最好衣裳。

===Original===
“Gladly would I see them,
 with you, more fitly clad.”
 Then by those in waiting
 baths were ready made.

===Chinese===
「我很乐意看到她们
同你一道穿得更为体面。」
随后侍从们
便把浴汤备办停当。

===Original===
 Among the kin of Hartmut
 chamberlains many were there;
 To help Gudrun they hastened,
 thinking that later she their hopes would further.

===Chinese===
哈特穆特的亲族中
有许多内侍在场；
他们赶忙去侍候古德伦，
心想她日后定能成全自己的指望。

===Original===
Gudrun and all her maidens
 were by the bath made glad;
 Then the best of clothing
 that any ever had

===Chinese===
古德伦和她的全体侍女
都因这场沐浴而欢畅；
随后，任何人从未拥有过的
最好的衣裳

===Original===
 To all the homeless women
 alike was freely given.
 The lowliest one among them
 might gain the love of a king, if she had striven.

===Chinese===
都一一慷慨地分给了
这些无家可归的女子。
她们中最低微的一个，
只要稍加用心，也能博得一位君王的爱。

===Original===
When they their bath had taken,
 wine to them was brought;
 In all the land of Normandy
 none better need be sought;

===Chinese===
她们沐浴完毕后，
有人端来了美酒；
在整个诺曼底的国土上，
再好的酒也不必另寻；

===Original===
 And soon the weary maidens
 the best of mead were drinking.
 To Hartmut thanks were given;
 to gain such praises how could he e’er be thinking!

===Chinese===
不久疲惫的少女们
又畅饮起上好的蜂蜜酒。
众人都向哈特穆特道谢；
能赢得这样的赞誉，他何曾想到！

===Original===
Soon the lovely maiden
 was seated in the hall.
 Gerlind bade her daughter
 then, with her maidens all,

===Chinese===
不久，那可爱的少女
便在厅堂上坐定。
格尔林德随即吩咐自己的女儿，
带着她全体侍女，

===Original===
 To don their clothing quickly,
 the finest and most fitting,
 If they Queen Hilda’s daughter
 wished to see, among her maidens sitting.

===Chinese===
赶快穿上最华美合体的衣裳，
倘若她们想去看望
希尔达王后之女——
她正坐在侍女们中间。

===Original===
At once the well-born Ortrun
 clothed her in her best;
 To seek Gudrun then straightway
 gladly did she haste.

===Chinese===
出身高贵的奥特龙（Ortrun）
立刻穿上自己最好的衣裳；
随后她欣然
匆匆前去寻访古德伦。

===Original===
 The grandchild of wild Hagen
 quickly went to meet her;
 When they saw each other,
 the happiness of both was never greater.

===Chinese===
暴烈的哈根的孙女
也快步迎上前去；
两人相见之时，
心中的欢喜再没有比这更盛的了。

===Original===
Each one kissed the other,
 ’neath a band of gold on her head;
 The hue of both was brighter
 for the golden light they shed.

===Chinese===
她们彼此亲吻，
在各自头上束发的金环之下；
两人容色愈发皎洁，
映着金饰流溢的光辉。

===Original===
 Each in her way was happy;
 Ortrun’s eyes were beaming,
 To see the high-born washer
 in finest clothes now clad, so comely seeming.

===Chinese===
两人各有各的欢喜；
奥特龙双眸放光，
望着那出身高贵的洗衣女
如今盛装在身，如此秀美动人。

===Original===
The poor Gudrun was blithesome,
 as we have said before,
 That soon her friendly kinsmen
 she would see once more.

===Chinese===
可怜的古德伦满心欢喜，
正如我们先前所说，
因为不久她就能再见
她那些亲爱的亲族。

===Original===
 The maidens sat together,
 with playful talk now gladdened;
 Whoever looked upon them
 might gain a happy heart, however saddened.

===Chinese===
少女们相聚而坐，
戏语玩笑令她们愉悦；
无论谁望上她们一眼，
纵有千般愁绪也会心生欢喜。

===Original===
“ ’Tis well for me,” said Ortrun,
 “that I have lived till now,
 When as the wife of Hartmut
 you here yourself will show.

===Chinese===
「我真有福，」奥特龙说，
「能活到今天，
见你将以哈特穆特之妻的身份
在此展现风采。

===Original===
 To one who loves my brother
 gladly will I give her
 The crown of my mother, Gerlind,
 that I of right should wear did I outlive her.”

===Chinese===
对爱我兄长的人，
我乐意奉上
我母亲格尔林德的王冠——
倘若我比她长寿，那本该由我佩戴。」

===Original===
“Ortrun, may God reward you,”
 thus the maiden spake;
 “Whatever you shall bid me,
 that will I do for your sake.

===Chinese===
「奥特龙，愿上帝报答你，」
少女这样说道；
「无论你吩咐我什么，
看在你的份上我都照办。

===Original===
 You have bewept so often
 the sorrows I was bearing,
 From you will I ne’er be sundered,
 and every day shall you my love be sharing.”

===Chinese===
你曾屡屡为
我承受的苦难落泪，
我永远不与你分离，
我的友爱你要日日分享。」

===Original===
Then with maiden wiliness
 spake the fair Gudrun:
 “Now you ought, Sir Hartmut,
 to send out runners soon,

===Chinese===
随后美丽的古德伦
带着少女的机心说道：
「哈特穆特爵爷，您现在
该尽快派出传令的信使，

===Original===
 Through all the Norman kingdom,
 to give to friends your bidding,
 As many as will hear it,
 to come to your palace now, to see our wedding.

===Chinese===
派往诺曼人王国的四方，
把您的意旨传告亲友，
凡愿听从的人，
都即刻请到宫中来，观礼我们的婚典。

===Original===
“When peace is in your borders,
 this to you I say,
 Before your host of warriors
 I will wear the crown one day.

===Chinese===
「待您边境安宁之时，
我要向你进一言，
总有一天，我会在您
大军将士的面前戴上王冠。

===Original===
 How many he has who woos me
 thus shall I be knowing;
 Then before your liegemen
 myself and all my kin will I be showing.”

===Chinese===
这样我便能知道，
向我求婚的人拥有多少力量；
那时我还要在您的封臣面前，
展现我自己和我的全体亲族。」

===Original===
The maid in truth was crafty;
 from the castle on that day
 A hundred men or over
 did Hartmut send away.

===Chinese===
这少女确实施了巧计；
就在那一天，哈特穆特
从城堡派出了
一百名上下的信使。

===Original===
 So, when the Hegeling fighters
 should for him be seeking,
 Fewer foes should meet them:
 for this was Gudrun their going thence bespeaking.

===Chinese===
如此一来，等黑格林武士
前来寻他之时，
迎战他们的敌人便少了：
因为这正是古德伦安排他们此行的用意。

===Original===
Then spake the old Queen Gerlind:
 “Now, fair daughter mine,
 You two must leave each other;
 when another morn shall shine,

===Chinese===
年迈的格尔林德王后说道：
「好了，我美丽的女儿，
你们两人该分开安歇了；
待明晨天光再亮，

===Original===
 Then may you be together,
 with none your bliss forbidding.”
 She left Gudrun, low bowing,
 and begged that God would her in his ways be leading.

===Chinese===
你们再相聚不迟，
无人禁止你们的欢好。」
她深深躬身告退，
祈求上帝引领古德伦行在他的道路上。

===Original===
Then did Hartmut leave her.
 All hearkened to her word;
 They gave to the maiden cup-bearers,
 and carvers at the board:

===Chinese===
随后哈特穆特也离开了她。
众人无不听从她的话；
他们为少女配备了司酒官，
还有席前侍膳的切肉官：

===Original===
 The high-born lady’s wishes
 they bade should well be heeded;
 Nor food nor drink she wanted:
 busy were they to bring her all she needed.

===Chinese===
出身高贵贵妇的心愿，
他们都奉命妥善照应；
她想要什么饮食：
众人忙不迭地把她所需的一切奉上。

===Original===
Then spake one lovely maiden
 among the Hegeling band:
 “When we on this are thinking,
 how from our fatherland

===Chinese===
黑格林一族的少女中
有一位可爱的开口道：
「每当想起
我们是怎样被人从故国

===Original===
 Our foes have brought us hither,
 to live unblest forever,
 We still are bowed with sorrow;
 when in our homes, such woe we thought of never.”

===Chinese===
掳到这异乡，注定永世不得福佑，
我们仍被哀愁压弯了腰；
在家园时，
我们何曾想到这样的祸殃。」

===Original===
She then began a-weeping,
 where sat her lady fair.
 When this was seen by others
 who stood beside her there,

===Chinese===
她在美丽的女主人身旁
坐着落起泪来。
站在她身边的
其他侍女见状，

===Original===
 They felt yet greater sorrow
 their heavy hearts now filling.
 All then wept together;
 but they saw their mistress, fair Gudrun, was smiling.

===Chinese===
只觉更深的悲哀
涌满沉重的心。
众人于是一同哭泣；
却见她们的女主人、美丽的古德伦正在微笑。

===Original===
They thought that now forever
 they far from home must stay:
 But their lady ne’er was thinking
 to bide so long away;

===Chinese===
她们以为从今往后
要永远流落他乡：
但她们的女主人从未想过
要在外羁留这么久；

===Original===
 They would, ere four days later,
 their freedom all be knowing.
 The time had come already
 to whisper to Gerlind that they would soon be going.

===Chinese===
用不了四天，
她们就将尽知自由的滋味。
时机已然成熟，
可以向格尔林德暗示她们不久便要离去。

===Original===
Beyond her wont a little
 to laugh had the maid begun;
 For fourteen years now bygone
 she never bliss had known.

===Chinese===
少女近来偶尔
露出了超乎往常的笑容；
已然逝去的这十四年里，
她从未尝过欢愉的滋味。

===Original===
 Of her glee the bad she-devil
 quickly now was hearing;
 She gave the hint to Ludwig,
 for care she felt, and anger past all bearing.

===Chinese===
她的欢欣很快
传进了那恶毒老女魔的耳中；
她把风声透露给路德维希，
心中忧惧，怒不可遏。

===Original===
She went at once to Hartmut,
 and said: “Oh, son of mine,
 List to the truth I tell you!
 throughout this land of thine,

===Chinese===
她立刻去见哈特穆特，
说道：「啊，我的儿，
听我告诉你实情！在你这整片国土上，
凡居住其间的人

===Original===
 All within it dwelling
 shall see both strife and toiling.
 Why it is I know not,
 the fair young queen, Gudrun, is now so smiling.

===Chinese===
都将目睹争斗与操劳。
缘何如此我不得而知，
反正美丽的年轻王后古德伦
如今正笑意盈盈。

===Original===
“I know not how it happened,
 or how the news she heard,
 But men sent out by her kinsmen
 hither to come have dared.

===Chinese===
「我不知事情怎么会这样，
也不知她从哪里听到的风声，
可她亲族派来的人
竟敢潜到这地方来。

===Original===
 Therefore, knightly Hartmut,
 some way must you be choosing,
 Lest, thro’ the friends she looks for,
 your worthy name and life you may be losing.”

===Chinese===
所以，骑士的哈特穆特，
你得速想办法应对，
免得因她盼望的那些亲友，
赔上你的英名和性命。」

===Original===
He said: “Be not so fearful.
 I grudge it not to the maid
 That she, with all her women,
 should for a time be glad.

===Chinese===
他说：「不必这样惊惶。
我并不嫉妒那少女
能同她全体侍女
欢乐一时。

===Original===
 All her nearest kinsmen
 far from me are dwelling;
 What harm can they be doing?
 I need not guard ’gainst ills they may be dealing.”

===Chinese===
她最近的亲族
都住得离我遥远；
他们能有什么作为？
我用不着提防他们可能使的坏。」

===Original===
Gudrun, now over-wearied,
 some of her maidens sent
 To see if her bed were ready,
 for she on sleep was bent;

===Chinese===
古德伦这时疲惫不堪，
便派了几名侍女
去看她的床铺可曾备好，
她一心想要安睡；

===Original===
 For a night at least her sorrow
 she could now be leaving.
 Then went with them most kindly
 King Hartmut’s chamberlain, his service giving.

===Chinese===
至少这一夜，她可以
暂且抛开愁苦。
哈特穆特王的内侍
也亲切地随她们同去，殷勤侍奉。

===Original===
Youths of the Norman palace
 before her bore the light;
 On her they ne’er had waited
 until that very night.

===Chinese===
诺曼人宫廷的少年们
在她面前掌灯引路；
直到那天夜里之前，
他们从未侍奉过她。

===Original===
 Thirty beds or over
 now were found made ready;
 Nice were they and cleanly,
 meet for Gudrun and many a well-born lady.

===Chinese===
三十张或更多的床
这时都已铺设停当；
床铺精美洁净，
配得上古德伦和众多出身高贵的贵女。

===Original===
On them were pillows lying
 from far Arabia brought,
 With green, like leaves of clover,
 and other hues, inwrought.

===Chinese===
床上安放着枕头，
是从遥远的阿拉伯运来，
织着三叶草叶般的绿色，
还有种种别的色彩。

===Original===
 Bedspreads on them hanging
 were sewed in strips most fairly;
 And red as fire was shining
 the gold mixed in with silken threads not sparely.

===Chinese===
垂挂的锦被
以条带缝缀得极为精美；
混织其间的黄金灿若火焰，
丝线也毫不吝惜。

===Original===
Beneath the silken bedspreads
 fishes’ skins were laid,
 To make them thicker and warmer.
 The fair and lovely maid,

===Chinese===
丝织锦被的下面
还铺着一层鱼皮，
好让被褥更厚更暖。
美丽可爱的少女，

===Original===
 Thither come from the Hegelings,
 Hartmut would be wooing,
 For he as yet knew nothing
 of the harm to him that her friends would soon be doing.

===Chinese===
这来自黑格林一族的贵女，
哈特穆特还要向她求爱，
因为他尚且一无所知——
她的亲友很快就要降祸于他。

===Original===
Then said the high-born maiden:
 “To sleep you now may go,
 All you that wait on Hartmut;
 we, too, the same will do.

===Chinese===
出身高贵的少女说道：
「你们现在可以去睡了，
所有侍奉哈特穆特的人；
我们也要照样安歇。

===Original===
 I, and my ladies with me,
 one night at least will rest us;
 For, since our coming hither,
 freedom from hardest toil hath never blest us.”

===Chinese===
我和我的贵女们，
至少这一夜要好好休息；
自从来到此地，
脱离苦役的自由从未眷顾过我们。」

===Original===
All who there were gathered
 of Hartmut’s knights and men,
 The wise as well as youthful,
 thence to go were seen;

===Chinese===
在场的哈特穆特的
骑士与部众，无论老成持重还是年少，
一个个相继
离席而去；

===Original===
 They to rest then hasted,
 the ladies’ bower now leaving.
 Wine and mead unstinted
 to the homeless maids were others freely giving.

===Chinese===
他们匆匆离开女眷的闺阁，
各自去安歇。
还有他人把美酒与蜂蜜酒，
慷慨不吝地供给无家的少女们。

===Original===
Then said Hilda’s daughter:
 “Now shut for me the door.”
 They barred the ladies’ bedroom
 with heavy bolts full four:

===Chinese===
希尔达之女说道：
「现在替我关上门。」
他们用四道粗重的门闩，
闩上了贵女们的寝房：

===Original===
 The room was shut so tightly
 that what therein was doing,
 However much one listened,
 outside he nought could hear, nor might be knowing.

===Chinese===
房间封闭得那样严实，
以致里面在做什么，
无论谁在外面怎样侧耳倾听，
也听不见分毫，更无从知晓。

===Original===
Awhile they all were seated,
 merrily drinking wine;
 Then said Gudrun, the queenly:
 “Dearest maidens mine,

===Chinese===
众人坐了一阵，
欢畅地饮着美酒；
随后王后般的古德伦说道：
「我最亲爱的少女们，

===Original===
 You well may now be happy,
 after your heavy sorrow:
 Your friends I soon will show you;
 on gladsome sights your eyes shall feed to-morrow.

===Chinese===
经历了沉重的苦难，
如今你们尽可以欢欣：
我很快就会让你们见到亲友；
明天你们的眼中将尽是欢欣的景象。

===Original===
“Herwic, my betrothèd,
 did I this morning kiss,
 And Ortwin, too, my brother;
 you now may think on this.

===Chinese===
「我的未婚夫赫尔维克，
今天清晨我亲吻了他，
还有我的弟弟奥特温；
这些你们如今可以细想了。

===Original===
 She shall soon be richer,
 and care from her be taken,
 Who shall well be mindful,
 when night is over, me in the morn to waken.

===Chinese===
那个人即将变得更为富有，
愁烦也将从她身上卸去——
她会牢牢记得，
黑夜过后，清早唤我醒来。

===Original===
“You well shall be rewarded.
 To us glad days are nigh:
 And freely will I give you
 castles strong and high,

===Chinese===
「你们都将得到厚报。
欢乐的日子离我们不远了：
我要慷慨地赐给你们
坚固高峻的城堡，

===Original===
 And with them many acres;
 for these shall I be gaining,
 If I the day shall witness
 when, as a queen, I o’er my lands am reigning.”

===Chinese===
还要附上广袤的田亩；
因为这些我都将赢得，
只要我能活到那一天——
以王后之尊君临我的疆土。」

===Original===
They now lay down to slumber,
 with hearts all free from care.
 They knew to them were speeding
 knights full brave to dare,

===Chinese===
她们于是躺下安睡，
心中再无一丝牵挂。
她们知道有一队
慓悍敢战的骑士正兼程赶来，

===Original===
 Who erelong would help them,
 and their woes would lighten.
 To see them they were hoping,
 soon as to-morrow’s sun the day should brighten.

===Chinese===
不久便会来救助她们，
为她们减轻苦难。
她们盼望着与他们相见，
只等明日的太阳照亮天光。

## Tale XXVI How the Hegelings Landed Near Ludwig’s Castle / 第二十六诗章 黑格林人如何在路德维希的城堡附近登陆

===Original===
On their return to their friends, Herwic and Ortwin make known the result of their search. The Hegeling army arrives before the Norman castle, and is discovered by Ludwig and Hartmut.

===Chinese===
赫尔维克（Herwic）与奥特温（Ortwin）回到亲友身边，把此番查访的结果告知众人。黑格林人（Hegeling）的大军兵临诺曼人的城堡之前，被路德维希（Ludwig）与哈特穆特（Hartmut）察觉。

===Original===
We ask you now to listen
 to a tale as yet untold:
 Ortwin still and Herwic
 their way did onward hold

===Chinese===
我们如今请你们倾听
一段尚未讲说的故事：
奥特温与赫尔维克
一路继续赶着行程，

===Original===
 Until they found their followers
 on the seashore standing.
 Then ran these Hegeling liegemen
 to meet them on the sands where they were landing.

===Chinese===
直到他们望见自己的部众
正伫立在海滨；
那些黑格林部众随即奔来，
在两人登陆的沙滩上相迎。

===Original===
Them they gladly welcomed,
 and bade that they make known
 The news that they were bringing,
 and freely all to own.

===Chinese===
部众们欣然迎接了他们，
并请他们把
带来的音讯讲明，
把一切和盘托出。

===Original===
 First they asked of Ortwin,
 if he could them be telling,
 If still Gudrun were living,
 and if in Ludwig’s land she now was dwelling?

===Chinese===
众人先向奥特温发问，
问他可愿相告：
古德伦（Gudrun）是否还在人世，
如今是否就住在路德维希的国土？

===Original===
The knightly Ortwin answered:
 “Of this I may not speak
 To each and all that ask it;
 the truth I will not break

===Chinese===
骑士般的奥特温答道：
「这事我不能
讲给每一个发问的人听；
我决不先行说破真相，

===Original===
 Till all are met together;
 then shall you be hearing
 All that our eyes there greeted,
 when we to come near Hartmut’s walls were daring.”

===Chinese===
直等众人全都聚齐；
到那时你们自会听到
我们的双眼在那里见到了什么，
当我们壮着胆子逼近哈特穆特的城墙。」

===Original===
The word was told to others,
 and soon a mighty band
 Of warriors brave and knightly
 around the two did stand.

===Chinese===
这话传到了其余人耳中，
不久便有一大群
英勇而具骑士风度的武士
围站在两人身旁。

===Original===
 Then to them said Ortwin:
 “Sad is the news I give you,
 And, were my wishes granted,
 gladly I’d spare the tale, for much ’twill grieve you.

===Chinese===
于是奥特温对他们说道：
「我带给你们的音讯甚是悲惨，
倘若能依我的心愿，
我宁可略去这段故事，因为它必将使你们深感哀伤。

===Original===
“List to what has happened,
 for wonders now begin;
