 Gudrun, my long-lost sister,
 I, in truth, have seen,

===Chinese===
「且听听事情的经过，
奇事如今开始了：
古德伦，我那失散已久的妹妹，
我当真已经见到，

===Original===
 And with her also Hildeburg,
 erst in Ireland living.”
 When he the tidings gave them,
 they thought the tale he told not worth believing.

===Chinese===
还有她身边的希尔德堡（Hildeburg），
从前住在爱尔兰。」
他报出这音讯时，
众人只当他讲的故事不足凭信。

===Original===
All then said together:
 “It is not well to jest;
 For her we long have waited,
 and now our time you waste.

===Chinese===
众人于是一齐说道：
「如此取笑可不应当；
我们久久盼望着她，
如今你却在虚掷光阴。

===Original===
 We hoped from Ludwig’s kingdom
 you would bring her sooner;
 To Ortwin and his followers
 belong the shame and blame for wrongs still done her.”

===Chinese===
我们本指望你们会早些把她
从路德维希的王国带回；
奥特温和他的部众啊，
她至今仍受的冤苦，羞责全在你们身上。」

===Original===
“Ask you, then, King Herwic:
 he, too, has seen the maid;
 And he can also tell you
 what wrongs on us are laid.

===Chinese===
「那就去问赫尔维克王吧：
他也见过那位少女；
他还能告诉你们
我们蒙受的是怎样的欺凌。

===Original===
 Could you, my friends, bethink you
 of any shame that’s greater?
 We found Gudrun and Hildeburg
 upon the seashore standing, washing in the water.”

===Chinese===
我的朋友们，你们可还能想出
比这更大的羞辱？
我们发现古德伦和希尔德堡
正伫立在海滨，在水里洗濯衣裳。」

===Original===
Soon were his kindred weeping,
 all who there were seen.
 At this the aged Wâ-te
 right scornful was, I ween:

===Chinese===
他的亲族们很快饮泣起来，
在场的人无不如此。
见此情景，我想年迈的瓦特（Wâ-te）
心中大是不屑：

===Original===
 “Truly for women only
 is such behavior fitting;
 Why you weep you know not.
 This, in a knight, one never should be meeting.

===Chinese===
「说实话，这般举止
只合于妇人之流；
你们连自己为何哭泣都不明白。
在一个骑士身上，谁也不该见到这种情形。

===Original===
“But if you are in earnest,
 to help Gudrun in her need,
 The clothes that she has whitened
 must you in war make red.

===Chinese===
「但你们若真心
要救助危难中的古德伦，
她漂白过的那些衣裳，
就须由你们在战阵中染成殷红。

===Original===
 Erst white hands did wash them
 for men who must be bleeding;
 So you now may help her,
 and soon the maid forlorn be homeward leading.”

===Chinese===
昔日洗濯它们的是雪白的双手，
为的是那些终将流血的男儿；
如今你们便这样去相助她，
不久就能把那孤苦的少女领回家乡。」

===Original===
Then said the Danish Fru-te:
 “How can this be done?
 How can we reach their kingdom
 before our plan is known,

===Chinese===
于是丹麦人弗鲁特（Fru-te）说道：
「这怎样才能做到？
我们怎能抵达他们的王国，
而让我们的图谋不致泄露，

===Original===
 Before the men of Ludwig,
 and Hartmut’s knights, are learning
 That Hilda’s friends are gathered,
 and toward the Norman land at length are turning?”

===Chinese===
不让路德维希的部属
和哈特穆特的骑士们得悉
希尔达（Hilda）的亲友已经聚齐，
终于挥师转向诺曼人的国土？」

===Original===
Then said the aged Wâ-te:
 “Hear what ’tis best to do;
 I trust before his castle
 fitly to meet the foe,

===Chinese===
年迈的瓦特随即说道：
「听听什么是上策；
我自信定能在他城堡之前
堂堂正正地会一会那仇敌，

===Original===
 If I may live to see him
 there before me standing.
 Brave knights, your rest now leaving,
 soon on the Norman shore must you be landing.

===Chinese===
只要我能活着见到他
站在我的面前。
勇敢的骑士们，撇下你们的安歇吧，
不久你们就须在诺曼人的海岸登岸。

===Original===
“The air is fresh and gladsome,
 the sky is broad and bright,
 And, well for us it happens,
 the moon will shine to-night.

===Chinese===
「空气清新而宜人，
天空辽阔又明朗，
况且于我们恰好的是，
今夜将有月光朗照。

===Original===
 From the sandy shore now hasten,
 my warriors bold and daring:
 Before it dawns to-morrow,
 we King Ludwig’s stronghold must be nearing.”

===Chinese===
快从这沙岸启程吧，
我勇猛无畏的武士们：
不等明日天色破晓，
我们就须逼近路德维希王的堡垒。」

===Original===
Then they all were busy,
 when thus old Wâ-te spoke;
 Soon their clothes and horses
 on board the ships they took.

===Chinese===
老瓦特这一番话后，
众人一齐忙活起来；
不多时，他们的衣物与战马
便都装载上了船。

===Original===
 All the night still sailing,
 towards the land they hasted;
 And ere the morrow’s daylight,
 before the castle, on the sands they rested.

===Chinese===
他们整夜扬帆不停，
匆匆向那片国土赶路；
不等次日天色放亮，
已在城堡前的沙滩上歇足。

===Original===
Hushed were all by Wâ-te,
 throughout the warlike band,
 As soon as they to rest them
 lay down upon the sand.

===Chinese===
瓦特喝令全体肃静，
遍及这支雄武之师，
他们刚一卧倒
在沙地上歇息安顿。

===Original===
 To his water-weary followers
 leave for this was granted;
 Their shields about them spreading,
 on them they laid their heads, for sleep they wanted.

===Chinese===
他那些饱经风浪的部众
为此获得了准许；
他们把盾牌四下铺开，
头枕盾牌，因为他们亟需睡眠。

===Original===
“Whoe’er to-morrow morning
 hopes to gain the fight
 Must not,” said the aged Wâ-te,
 “oversleep to-night.

===Chinese===
「谁若指望明日清晨
赢得这场战斗，
万万不可，」年迈的瓦特说道，
「今夜贪睡过头。

===Original===
 For the struggle now before us
 we hardly can be waiting;
 As soon as morning lightens,
 then, good knights, the foe must we be meeting.”

===Chinese===
眼前这场恶斗，
我们几乎等不得了；
晨光一旦透亮，
好骑士们，我们就须与仇敌交锋。」

===Original===
“Further I give you warning:
 whoe’er my horn shall hear
 Along the seashore sounded,
 soon as it meets his ear,

===Chinese===
「我还要告诫你们一声：
凡听到我的号角
在沿岸吹响的人，
号声一入他的耳中，

===Original===
 Let him at once make ready
 the foeman to be meeting.
 When I shall blow at daybreak,
 no longer then may any there be waiting.

===Chinese===
就须立即整装，
准备迎击仇敌。
待我破晓吹响号角，
任何人便不得再有迟延。

===Original===
“When I again shall blow it,
 let each to this give heed;
 Quickly let his saddle
 be laid upon his steed.

===Chinese===
「待我再度吹响号角，
人人都要记在心里；
快把马鞍
备上各自的战马。

===Original===
 Let him then be waiting,
 till I see ’tis daylight fully,
 And the time has come for the onset;
 let none hang back, but meet the struggle truly.”

===Chinese===
然后便备马静候，
直到我看见天光全亮，
出击的时辰已到；
谁也不得逡巡，都须真刀真枪地接战。」

===Original===
To do as Wâ-te bade them
 their word they gladly gave.
 How many a lovely woman
 did he of bliss bereave!

===Chinese===
众人欣然立下诺言，
都愿照瓦特的吩咐去做。
可他从此又教多少可爱的女子
失去了她们的幸福！

===Original===
 For soon their dearest kindred
 unto death were wounded,
 Who now were only waiting
 until the horn in the early morning sounded.

===Chinese===
因为他们最亲爱的亲族
不久便受了致命的重伤，
而此刻他们还只是在静候，
静候清晨号角的吹响。

===Original===
“When you, my friends and kinsmen,
 thrice my horn shall hear,
 Then, seated on your horses,
 must you your weapons wear;

===Chinese===
「当你们，我的亲友们，
第三次听到我的号角，
就须跨坐马背，
披戴上你们的兵刃；

===Original===
 Thus must you, brave warriors,
 wait, your steeds bestriding,
 Till me you see, well-weaponed,
 under the fair Queen Hilda’s banner riding.”

===Chinese===
勇敢的武士们，就这样
跨着战马等候，
直到望见我全身披挂，
驰骋在美丽的希尔达王后的旌旗之下。」

===Original===
Now on the seashore weary
 lay they, one and all;
 Very near were they resting
 to old King Ludwig’s hall.

===Chinese===
如今他们个个疲乏，
全都卧在海滨；
他们歇息的地方，
离老王路德维希的殿堂极近。

===Original===
 Altho’ the night had fallen,
 its towers they saw while waking;
 The brave and fearless warriors
 in stillness lay, no sound or outcry making.

===Chinese===
夜幕虽已降临，
他们醒着时仍望得见那些塔楼；
勇敢无畏的武士们
静静卧着，不发出一声喧嚷。

===Original===
The early star of morning
 now had risen high;
 Then came a lovely maiden
 unto the window nigh.

===Chinese===
清晨的启明星
此刻已升得很高；
随后有一位可爱的少女
来到近旁的窗前。

===Original===
 She there was gazing skyward,
 to see when day was breaking,
 That she might bring the tidings,
 and rich reward from fair Gudrun be seeking.

===Chinese===
她在那里仰望天空，
看天色何时破晓，
好把音讯传报，
向美丽的古德伦求得丰厚的赏报。

===Original===
Ere she long had waited,
 there dawned on the maiden’s sight,
 With its wonted gleam on the waters,
 the early morning light;

===Chinese===
她不曾久等，
破晓的晨光
便带着惯常映在水面的微芒，
映入少女的眼帘；

===Original===
 Then the sheen of helmets
 and many shields there flittered:
 Foes had besieged the castle,
 and all the sands below with weapons glittered.

===Chinese===
随即头盔的光泽
和无数盾牌在那里闪动：
仇敌已将城堡围困，
下方整片沙滩都闪着兵刃的寒光。

===Original===
Back then went the maiden
 to where Gudrun she found:
 “Arouse, my queenly lady,
 wake from your slumber sound!

===Chinese===
那少女随即回身，
走到她寻见古德伦的地方：
「醒来吧，我王后般的女主人，
从酣沉的睡眠中醒来！

===Original===
 The land is held by foemen,
 who will these walls be storming;
 We have not been forgotten
 by those at home; our friends come hither swarming.”

===Chinese===
国土已被仇敌占据，
他们就要猛攻这些城墙；
家里人并未忘记我们；
我们的亲友正蜂拥而来。」

===Original===
Gudrun, the high-born lady,
 quickly sprang from her bed,
 And, hasting to the window,
 to the maid her thanks she said.

===Chinese===
出身高贵的贵妇古德伦
急忙从床上跃起，
她奔到窗前，
向那少女道谢。

===Original===
 “For this good news you give me,
 wealth shall you be earning.”
 After her heavy sorrow,
 now for her friends Gudrun was sorely yearning.

===Chinese===
「你给我带来这好消息，
必能赢得财帛。」
熬过沉重的悲苦之后，
古德伦如今苦苦思念起她的亲友。

===Original===
Rich sails were seen to flutter
 near by upon the sea;
 Then said the high-born maiden:
 “Ah, wellaway! Woe’s me!

===Chinese===
海上近处望见
华美的船帆飘动；
那出身高贵的少女随即说道：
「唉，苦啊！我真是不幸！

===Original===
 Would that I ne’er were living!”
 the wretched one was sighing:
 “Many a doughty warrior
 this day for me shall here in death be lying.”

===Chinese===
但愿我从未活在世上！」
这悲惨的人叹息道：
「多少骁勇的武士，
今日将为我陈尸此地。」

===Original===
While thus she was bewailing,
 nearly all still slept;
 But soon was one heard shouting,
 who guard for Ludwig kept:

===Chinese===
她正这样悲叹时，
众人几乎都还睡着；
但不久便听得一人高声喊叫，
那是为路德维希守望的人：

===Original===
 “Be up, you careless warriors!
 your arms, your arms be taking!
 And you, my king of Normandy!
 I fear that all too late you will be waking.”

===Chinese===
「起来，你们这些疏懒大意的武士！
拿起兵器，快拿起兵器！
还有您，我诺曼底的王！
我怕您醒来已是为时太迟。」

===Original===
This the wicked Gerlind
 heard, as the warder cried;
 Then, while fast he slumbered,
 she left the old king’s side.

===Chinese===
恶毒的格尔林德（Gerlind）
听见了守望者的呼喊；
趁老王睡得正沉，
她便离开了他身旁。

===Original===
 Up to the roof of the castle
 then at once she hastened;
 She thence saw many foemen,
 and on her devilish heart great sorrow fastened.

===Chinese===
她随即匆匆
登上城堡的屋顶；
从那里她望见众多仇敌，
深重的哀愁攫住了她那恶魔般的心。

===Original===
Back again she speeded
 to where she found the king:
 “Awake, my lord, make ready
 for guests who followers bring!

===Chinese===
她又疾速折返，
来到寻见国王的地方：
「醒来吧，我的主上，快做准备，
宾客们已携着随从临门！

===Original===
 Now hem they in your castle,
 and well may they be dreaded:
 That smile of young Gudrun
 will cost your knights a strife as yet unheeded.”

===Chinese===
他们如今把您的城堡团团围住，
实在不可等闲视之：
年轻古德伦的那一笑，
将教您的骑士们付出一场他们尚未放在心上的鏖战。」

===Original===
“Hush!” then answered Ludwig,
 “I will go myself to see;
 We must all be bravely waiting
 for whatsoe’er may be.”

===Chinese===
「嘘！」路德维希随即答道，
「我要亲自去看看；
无论事态如何，
我们都须勇敢地静候。」

===Original===
 Then looked he from his castle,
 to see the foemen thronging;
 His eyes by guests were greeted,
 on whom to look he never might be longing.

===Chinese===
于是他从城堡中望去，
只见仇敌成群涌来；
映入他眼帘的这些宾客，
他可是从来不愿见到。

===Original===
Before his palace waving,
 he saw their banners spread;
 Then said the old King Ludwig:
 “Let some one go with speed

===Chinese===
他望见他们的旌旗
在宫殿之前招展；
老王路德维希随即说道：
「快派一人疾速前去，

===Original===
 And bear this news to Hartmut.
 I for pilgrims take them,
 To sell their wares come hither;
 before my hall a market would they make them.”

===Chinese===
把这音讯带给哈特穆特。
我只当他们是一伙朝圣的旅人，
来此地贩卖货物，
竟想在我殿堂前摆起集市。」

===Original===
Then they wakened Hartmut,
 that he the tale might hear.
 Outspoke that daring warrior:
 “Let none be sad or fear.

===Chinese===
众人随即唤醒哈特穆特，
好让他听闻这段事由。
那位无畏的武士朗声说道：
「谁也不必忧愁或惊恐。

===Original===
 I see full twenty princes
 their blazoned banners bearing;
 I ween these foes are coming
 to wreak the hate they long ’gainst us are wearing.”

===Chinese===
我看足有二十位王侯
高擎着各自的纹章旗帜；
我料想这些仇敌前来，
是要泄一泄他们久积于心的仇恨。」

## Tale XXVII How Ludwig and Hartmut Met the Hegelings / 第二十七诗章 路德维希与哈特穆特如何迎战黑格林人

===Original===
Hartmut names to Ludwig the banners of the coming knights. On learning that the Hegeling army is approaching, Gerlind counsels her son, Hartmut, to await a siege; but he chooses to sally forth, with Ludwig and their followers, and a battle ensues, in which Ludwig overthrows Herwic.

===Chinese===
哈特穆特（Hartmut）向路德维希（Ludwig）一一说出来犯骑士们的旗帜。得知黑格林人（Hegeling）大军正在逼近，格尔林德（Gerlind）劝她的儿子哈特穆特据城待围；他却选择同路德维希率部众出城迎击，于是一场会战就此展开，路德维希在阵中击倒了赫尔维克（Herwic）。

===Original===
Asleep still left he lying
 all his faithful men.
 He and his father Ludwig,
 the twain, to go were seen,

===Chinese===
他仍旧让忠实的部下
一个个躺着酣睡。
他与父亲路德维希两人，
双双被人瞧见走动，

===Original===
 And, gazing from the window,
 they saw the throngs below them.
 Quickly then said Hartmut:
 “Too near our castle-walls methinks they show them.

===Chinese===
两人凭窗眺望，
望见下方的人群。
哈特穆特急忙说道：
「依我看，他们逼得离我们城墙太近了。

===Original===
“I ween they are not pilgrims,
 in truth, my father dear;
 More like it is that Wâ-te
 and all his men draw near.

===Chinese===
「我看他们并非朝圣的旅人，
实话对你说，亲爱的父亲；
倒更像是瓦特（Wâ-te）
正带着他全部人马逼近。

===Original===
 He from Sturmland cometh,
 the lord of Ortland bringing;
 The men I see are like them,
 as I know from the flag that they to the breeze are flinging.

===Chinese===
他从施图尔姆兰（Sturmland）赶来，
还把奥特兰（Ortland）之主一并带来；
我望见的人马正与他们相仿，
这一点我从他们迎风招展的旗帜上就已认出。

===Original===
“I see a brown silk pennon,
 that comes from Karadé;
 Before that flag is lowered,
 many will rue the day.

===Chinese===
「我望见一面褐色绸子的尖旗，
它来自卡拉迪（Karadé）；
不等那面旗降下，
就要有许多人悔恨这一天。

===Original===
 On it a head is blazoned﻿—
 as red as gold it glitters:
 Guests so bold and warlike
 we well can spare; their sight the day embitters.

===Chinese===
旗上绘着一颗头颅——
它闪着金子般的红光：
这样大胆好战的宾客
我们大可不要；他们一露面，这日子就变得苦涩。

===Original===
“The Moorland king is bringing
 full twenty thousand men,
 Knights as strong and daring
 as any I have seen;

===Chinese===
「摩尔兰（Moorland）之王带来了
足足两万名武士，
个个骑士都强健而无畏，
不逊于我平生所见；

===Original===
 To win from us great honor
 methinks they now are craving.
 There comes another banner,
 that o’er yet other knights its folds is waving.

===Chinese===
我看他们此刻正一心渴盼
要从我们身上博取莫大的荣光。
又有一面旗帜出现了，
它的旗幅正招展在另一队骑士头顶。

===Original===
“It is the flag of Horant,
 the knight from the Danish land;
 I see with him Lord Fru-te,
 I know both him and his band.

===Chinese===
「那是霍兰特（Horant）的旗帜，
这位骑士来自丹麦；
我看见他身旁的弗鲁特（Fru-te）大人，
他与他的部众我都识得。

===Original===
 And hither, too, from Waleis,
 many foemen leading,
 Morunc now comes riding;
 he, for the morning’s fight, o’er the sands is speeding.

===Chinese===
还有，从瓦莱斯（Waleis）
率着大队仇敌前来的
莫伦克（Morunc）此刻也骑马赶到；
为赴清晨的战斗，他正疾驰过沙滩。

===Original===
“I see another banner,
 on it a chevron red,
 With sharpened spears within it;
 for this shall many bleed.

===Chinese===
「我又望见一面旗帜，
上面绘着一道红色人字纹，
纹里是削尖的长矛；
为这个，许多人将要流血。

===Original===
 Ortwin it is who bears it,
 from Ortland hither faring:
 Erewhile we slew his father;
 no kindly thought to us he now is bearing.

===Chinese===
执掌这面旗的是奥特温（Ortwin），
他从奥特兰远道而来：
先前我们诛杀了他的父亲；
他如今对我们怀的可不是善意。

===Original===
“There floats another banner,
 whiter than any swan;
 Blazons bright and golden
 you well may see thereon.

===Chinese===
「又有一面旗帜飘扬着，
比任何天鹅还要洁白；
明亮金黄的纹章，
你们在旗上看得真切。

===Original===
 It is our mother Hilda
 who sends it o’er the water;
 The hatred of the Hegelings
 will soon be known by me who stole her daughter.

===Chinese===
那是我们的母亲希尔达（Hilda）
把它差遣过海而来；
黑格林人的仇恨
很快就要让我这抢走她女儿的人领教。

===Original===
“There I see uplifted
 a flag outspreading wide;
 Of sky-blue silk ’tis woven.
 The truth I will not hide;

===Chinese===
「那边我望见一面
高高擎起、幅面铺展的旗帜；
它是用天蓝的绸子织成。
实情我不隐瞒；

===Original===
 Herwic bears this banner,
 he in the Sealands dwelling.
 Sea-leaves are shown upon it;
 he soon on us his wrath will here be telling.

===Chinese===
执掌这面旗的是赫尔维克，
他安居在西兰（Sealand）。
旗上绘着海叶的图样；
不久他就要在这里向我们倾泻怒火。

===Original===
“There Irold, too, is coming﻿—
 this that I say is true﻿—
 From Friesland leading many,
 as well indeed I know,

===Chinese===
「那边伊罗尔德（Irold）也来了——
我说的这话是真的——
他从弗里西亚（Friesland）率来许多人马，
这一点我确实清楚，

===Original===
 With fighting men from Holstein,
 warriors brave and daring.
 A stormy fight is nearing;
 now in our castle all must arms be wearing.”

===Chinese===
还带着荷尔斯泰因（Holstein）的战士，
个个勇武而无畏。
一场风暴般的战斗正在逼近；
如今我们城堡里人人都必须披上兵甲。」

===Original===
Then cried Hartmut loudly:
 “Up, my faithful men!
 If to these guests so warlike,
 who ’neath our walls are seen,

===Chinese===
哈特穆特随即高声喊道：
「起来，我忠诚的部下！
对这般好战的宾客——
他们已现身我们城墙之下——

===Original===
 It may not now be granted
 to ride so boldly near us,
 Then, before the gateway,
 with sword-blows we must greet them, and bravely bear us.”

===Chinese===
如今岂能容许
他们这样大胆地逼近；
那么，就在城堡大门前，
我们必须以剑击迎接他们，并奋勇自持。」

===Original===
Then from their beds upsprang they
 all who yet did lie;
 At once, to bring their war-gear,
 loudly did they cry.

===Chinese===
于是凡还躺卧的
全都从床上一跃而起；
他们当即高声呼喊，
要去取来兵甲。

===Original===
 The call to guard their master
 gladly they were hearing;
 Forty hundred warriors
 showed themselves, their shining armor wearing.

===Chinese===
护卫主上的号令，
他们听得满心欢喜；
四千名武士
显露身形，个个披着闪亮的甲胄。

===Original===
Ludwig and Hartmut with him
 armed themselves for fight:
 To the sad and homeless maidens
 this was a sorry sight;

===Chinese===
路德维希和随侍在侧的哈特穆特
都披挂起来准备战斗：
对那忧伤的、无家可归的少女们，
这是一幅凄惨景象；

===Original===
 These within the castle
 uneasy hearts were keeping;
 They said to one another:
 “Let him who smiled before this day be weeping!”

===Chinese===
她们在城堡之内
各自怀着不安的心；
她们彼此说道：
「今天之前还面带微笑的人，如今该哭泣了！」

===Original===
Quickly came Queen Gerlind,
 old King Ludwig’s wife;
 She said: “What will you, Hartmut?
 Would you lose your life,

===Chinese===
格尔林德王后急急赶来，
她是老王路德维希之妻；
她说：「哈特穆特，你这是要干什么？
你难道要丢掉性命，

===Original===
 With that of all your kinsmen
 who here our lot are sharing?
 The foe will surely slay you,
 if to leave the castle-walls you now be daring.”

===Chinese===
连同在此与我们共命运的
你全体亲族的性命吗？
仇敌定会把你们杀戮，
倘若你此刻斗胆要出城堡的围墙。」

===Original===
The well-born knight then answered:
 “Mother, stay within;
 You may not give your teaching
 to me or to my men.

===Chinese===
那出身高贵的骑士回答道：
「母亲，您且留在堡内；
您不可以把您的教诲
加给我或加给我的部下。

===Original===
 Spare your words for women;
 they mayhap will listen,
 While they sit at sewing,
 making their silks with gold and gems to glisten.

===Chinese===
把您的话留给女人们吧；
她们或许还肯听上一听，
趁她们坐着做针线活，
让绸缎在金线宝石间闪闪生光的时候。

===Original===
“Now, mother, let us see you
 send Gudrun to wash,
 As you did before, with her maidens,
 where the billows dash.

===Chinese===
「母亲，现在倒要请您
照旧派古德伦（Gudrun）去洗衣，
带着她的侍女们，
派到那波涛拍击的地方去。

===Original===
 You weened they all were friendless,
 and had no kindred living;
 You yet may see, ere nightfall,
 what thanks to us our guests will yet be giving.”

===Chinese===
您当初料定她们全都无亲无故，
亲族都不在人世了；
天黑之前您就会看到，
这些宾客要怎样报答我们。」

===Original===
Then spake his devilish mother:
 “I did it for your sake,
 Thinking her will to bridle.
 My bidding kindly take;

===Chinese===
他那魔鬼般的母亲随即说道：
「我这样做都是为了你，
原想煞住她的性子。
我的吩咐请你好生领受；

===Original===
 Strongly built is the castle,
 let now the gates be fastened;
 They then will gain but little
 who on their toilsome way have hither hastened.

===Chinese===
这城堡修造得坚固，
现在且把城门紧紧闩上；
那些一路辛苦、
匆匆赶到此地的人，将所获无几。

===Original===
“Full well you know it, Hartmut,
 you bear the maiden’s hate,
 For you have slain her kinsmen:
 your watch you must not bate.

===Chinese===
「你十分清楚，哈特穆特，
你结下了那少女的仇恨，
因为你诛杀了她的亲族：
你的警戒一刻也不可放松。

===Original===
 It is not friends or kinsfolk
 who at our gates are knocking;
 The proud and warlike Hegelings,
 twenty to one of us, come hither flocking.

===Chinese===
在我们的门前叩门的，
既非朋友也非亲眷；
那些高傲好战的黑格林人，
人数对我们是二十比一，正朝这里蜂拥而来。

===Original===
“Of this bethink you further,
 my well-belovèd son:
 Bread we have in the castle
 and wine for every one;

===Chinese===
「这些你再多想想，
我心爱的儿子：
城堡里有的是面包，
人人都有酒喝；

===Original===
 Food will not be lacking
 if here for a year we are staying;
 But if on the field you are taken,
 our foes will you from bondage ne’er be freeing.”

===Chinese===
即便我们在此坚守上一年，
口粮也不会匮乏；
但你若在战场上被俘，
我们的仇敌决不会把你从囚缚中释放。」

===Original===
Then to him spake further
 old King Ludwig’s wife:
 “Ever guard your honor,
 but do not lose your life.

===Chinese===
老王路德维希的王后
又继续对他说道：
「永远要守住你的名誉，
但不要丢掉性命。

===Original===
 Bid men to shoot with longbows
 at the loop-holes standing;
 So shall wounds be given,
 for which their friends at home will tears be spending.

===Chinese===
吩咐弓手们持长弓
守在箭孔旁施射；
这样便能造成创伤，
教他们的亲友日后在家为此垂泪。

===Original===
“Let slings with ropes be fitted;
 we then will meet the foe
 By hurling rocks upon them:
 knights we have enow.

===Chinese===
「把绳索系上投石器；
我们便要用
投掷巨石来迎击仇敌：
我们的骑士尽够了。

===Original===
 Before with these new-comers
 you your swords are crossing,
 Stones will I and my maidens
 bring in aprons white, on them to be tossing.”

===Chinese===
在你同这些新来的客人
交剑之前，
我和我的女仆们
就会兜着白围裙运来石块，朝他们投掷。」

===Original===
Angrily spake Hartmut:
 “Lady, get you gone!
 Why do you seek to lead me?
 Is not my mind my own?

===Chinese===
哈特穆特怒气冲冲地说道：
「夫人，您退下吧！
您为什么总想辖制我？
难道我自己的心思还不归我自己做主？

===Original===
 Before my foes shall find me
 within my castle hiding,
 Outside I would die far sooner,
 in fight with Hilda’s men, against me riding.”

===Chinese===
与其让我的仇敌发现我
躲在自己的城堡里，
我宁可早早死在外面，
同冲杀过来的希尔达部下决一死战。」

===Original===
Then to him said, weeping,
 old King Ludwig’s wife:
 “I gave to you this warning
 that you might spare your life,

===Chinese===
老王路德维希的王后
噙着泪对他说道：
「我给你这番告诫，
是为了让你保全性命，

===Original===
 And guard yourself the better.
 Whoe’er is seen this morning
 Beneath your banner fighting,
 rich gifts from us shall he be fairly earning.

===Chinese===
也是为了让你把自己
保护得更好。今晨凡是
在你旗下作战的人，
都将从我们这里公平地挣得厚赏。

===Original===
“Now arm ourselves,” cried Gerlind,
 “stand by my son in fight;
 Strike from your foemen’s helmets
 a glowing, fiery light.

===Chinese===
「现在让我们武装起来，」格尔林德喊道，
「要在我儿子身旁作战；
要从你们仇敌的头盔上，
击出灼灼飞腾的火光。

===Original===
 Be always near your master,
 to help him ever striving;
 Fitly these guests to welcome,
 deep be the wounds that you to them are giving.”

===Chinese===
要永远贴近你们的主上，
时刻奋力相援；
要配得上迎接这些宾客，
你们给他们造成的创口要深。」

===Original===
Then to his men said Hartmut:
 “My mother’s words are true;
 If you to me are faithful,
 and strive your best to do,

===Chinese===
哈特穆特随即对部下说道：
「我母亲的话不假；
你们若对我忠实，
又肯竭尽全力去做，

===Original===
 And this day, in the struggle,
 to give your help are ready,
 When fathers shall have fallen,
 a friend I’ll be to sons bereft and needy.”

===Chinese===
在今天这场鏖战里
随时准备出力相援，
那么当父辈们倒下之后，
我必做那些丧父困顿之人的朋友。」

===Original===
A thousand and a hundred
 within King Ludwig’s halls
 Now were all well-weaponed.
 Before from out the walls

===Chinese===
路德维希王殿堂里的
一千一百名武士
此刻都已兵刃齐备。在从城墙里
走出任何一座城门之前，

===Original===
 Went any thro’ the gateways,
 they left the stronghold guarded;
 Still within it posted,
 five hundred warriors brave the castle warded.

===Chinese===
他们都留下了守卫，
把堡垒看守好；
仍有五百名勇武的武士
驻守堡中，把城堡护住。

===Original===
On four gates of the castle
 the bolts were backward thrown:
 Ne’er had they been opened
 to a single spur alone.

===Chinese===
城堡的四座城门，
门闩一一向后拉开：
这样的城门从不曾
只为单独一骑的马刺而开启。

===Original===
 Then with the youthful Hartmut,
 outgoing at his bidding,
 All with helmets fastened,
 went thirty hundred followers boldly riding.

===Chinese===
随后奉年轻的哈特穆特之命，
与他一同出城，
人人系紧了头盔，
三千名部下雄赳赳驰马而出。

===Original===
The hour of strife drew nearer.
 He of the Sturmisch land,
 Wâ-te, his horn was blowing;
 and loud across the sand,

===Chinese===
鏖战的时刻更近了。
施图尔姆兰来的瓦特，
吹响了他的号角；
那洪亮的声音掠过沙滩，

===Original===
 For thirty miles or over,
 men the blast were hearing;
 The fighters of the Hegelings,
 to flock to Hilda’s flag, their arms were wearing.

===Chinese===
三十英里开外甚至更远，
人们都听得见那号声；
黑格林人的战士们
披上兵甲，向希尔达的旗帜聚拢。

===Original===
Once again he blew it:
 at this should all take heed,
 That every knight among them
 then should mount his steed,

===Chinese===
他又吹了一遍：
这一声是要众人留意，
他们之中每一位骑士
都应当即时翻身上马，

===Original===
 And each his men should gather
 to ride as they were bidden.
 A knight so old as Wâ-te,
 and yet so brave, to the fight had never ridden.

===Chinese===
各自集结起自己的人马，
遵命驰骋出征。
像瓦特这样年迈的骑士，
却又这般英勇，还从未有人赴战过。

===Original===
The third time that he blew it,
 he such a blast did make
 That all the land was shaken,
 and the sea a sound gave back;

===Chinese===
到他第三次吹响时，
迸出的号声竟这般强劲，
震得整片大地都在摇晃，
连大海也回应出声响；

===Original===
 Almost from Ludwig’s castle
 the corner-stones were falling:
 To raise Queen Hilda’s banner
 Wâ-te to Horant then was loudly calling.

===Chinese===
路德维希城堡的墙角石
几乎都要被震落下来：
为了升起希尔达王后的旌旗，
瓦特向霍兰特高声呼喊。

===Original===
They feared old Wâ-te sorely,
 none dared to speak aloud;
 A horse was e’en heard neighing.
 Upon the roof now stood

===Chinese===
他们对年迈的瓦特怕得厉害，
谁也不敢高声说话；
连一匹马的嘶鸣都听得见。
这时屋顶上伫立着

===Original===
 Herwic’s well-belovèd,
 and saw the warriors daring,
 Onward proudly riding,
 to wage the fight with Hartmut, nothing fearing.

===Chinese===
赫尔维克心爱的佳人，
她望着那些无畏的武士
昂然向前驰骋，
去与哈特穆特交战，全无惧色。

===Original===
Hartmut rode to meet them;
 he and all his men,
 Bearing well their weapons,
 to leave the gates were seen.

===Chinese===
哈特穆特驰马迎向他们；
他和他全体部下，
携着称手的兵刃，
被人瞧见正涌出城门。

===Original===
 Those from the windows gazing
 saw the helmets glisten
 Of friends as well as foemen.
 Hartmut not alone to the fight did hasten.

===Chinese===
凭窗眺望的人们
望见头盔熠熠生光，
那是朋友，也是仇敌。
并非哈特穆特一人驰赴战阵。

===Original===
To all four sides of the castle
 the foes their banners bore;
 Bright in hue like silver
 was the armor that they wore;

===Chinese===
仇敌们把旌旗
直擎到城堡四面；
他们身披的甲胄
色泽明亮，恰似白银；

===Original===
 The bosses of their bucklers
 were seen to glitter brightly.
 Much was Wâ-te dreaded;
 no lion grim and wild were feared more rightly.

===Chinese===
圆盾上的盾脐
闪着灿亮的光。
瓦特为人惧惮至深；
再狰狞狂野的狮子也没有他这般可畏。

===Original===
The fighters from the Moorland
 were seen apart to ride,
 And heavy shafts were hurling;
 splinters were scattered wide.

===Chinese===
摩尔兰来的战士们
被瞧见在旁侧驰骋，
投出沉重的矛杆；
碎屑迸散四方。

===Original===
 When with the Norman foemen
 soon the fight did thicken,
 Sharply from their weapons
 and from their breastplates fiery sparks were stricken.

===Chinese===
待他们同诺曼人的战斗
不久便酣烈起来，
兵刃与胸甲之上
被猛烈击出四溅的火花。

===Original===
The warriors from Denmark
 near to the castle rode.
 There the mighty Irold
 six thousand fighters good

===Chinese===
丹麦来的战士们
驰近了城堡。
那边威武的伊罗尔德
正把六千名精良的战士

===Original===
 Up to the walls was leading,
 an onslaught to be making:
 Brave and daring were they;
 sore ill from them erelong was Ludwig taking.

===Chinese===
一路带到城墙之下，
要发起一场猛攻：
他们勇猛而无畏；
不久路德维希便在他们手里吃足苦头。

===Original===
Elsewhere, riding boldly,
 Ortwin his followers led,
 No less than eighty hundred;
 sorrow and woe they made

===Chinese===
在另一处，奥特温豪迈地
统领着他的部众，
足足八千之数；
他们给许许多多

===Original===
 For many of the Normans,
 and all the land they harried.
 Gerlind and Ortrun weeping,
 watching the fight from the roof, together tarried.

===Chinese===
诺曼人带来忧伤与苦难，
还蹂躏了整片国土。
格尔林德与奥特龙（Ortrun）泪流满面，
一同守在屋顶上观战。

===Original===
Then came Herwic also,
 betrothed to fair Gudrun;
 Through him full many a woman
 must come to sorrow soon,

===Chinese===
随后赫尔维克也赶到了，
他是美丽古德伦的未婚夫；
因为他，多少妇人
不久便要陷入悲哀，

===Original===
 When, for his heart’s belovèd,
 he to the fight was springing.
 Beneath the heavy weapons
 were heard the clattering helmets loudly ringing.

===Chinese===
当他为了心中的爱人
纵身投入战阵的时候。
沉重的兵刃之下，
只听头盔铿锵作响，声声震耳。

===Original===
Now came the aged Wâ-te,
 with warriors not a few;
 Grim was he and fearless,
 as soon they all well knew.

===Chinese===
年迈的瓦特也到了，
率领的武士不在少数；
他狰狞可怖又毫无惧色，
这一点他们很快便都深知。

===Original===
 His spear not yet he lowered
 as he to the walls came riding:
 Sad was the sight to Gerlind,
 but other were the thoughts Gudrun was hiding.

===Chinese===
他驰到城墙跟前，
长矛仍不肯放低：
这景象教格尔林德悲伤，
古德伦心中藏的却是另一番思绪。

===Original===
Then came the Norman Hartmut,
 riding before his men.
 E’en had he been Kaiser,
 never would he be seen

===Chinese===
随后诺曼人哈特穆特也出现了，
驰马行在他的部众之前。
即便他贵为皇帝，
人们也绝不会见到他

===Original===
 To bear himself more proudly.
 In the sun was seen to glisten
 All his shining armor.
 His boldness on the field not yet did lessen.

===Chinese===
摆出比这更骄矜的气派。
只见他浑身闪亮的甲胄
在太阳底下熠熠生辉。
他在战场上的胆气丝毫未减。

===Original===
When he was seen by Ortwin,
 the lord of Ortland’s throne,
 He said: “Will any tell us,
 to whom this knight is known,

===Chinese===
奥特兰王座的君主奥特温
望见他时，
便说：「有谁能告诉我们，
这位骑士是谁？

===Original===
 Who is the daring fighter
 now against us turning?
 He shows as bold a bearing
 as if to win a kingdom he were yearning.”

===Chinese===
这位无畏的战将
此刻正掉头向我们冲来？
他摆出这般大胆的气派，
仿佛一心渴盼着赢取一个王国。」

===Original===
Then said one among them:
 “ ’Tis Hartmut whom you see;
 There indeed is a warrior!
 a daring knight is he.

===Chinese===
他们中间有一人说道：
「你看见的正是哈特穆特；
这才真是一位战士！
他是位无畏的骑士。

===Original===
 The selfsame foeman is he
 who erstwhile slew your father.
 Where’er the strife is raging,
 a bolder man than he there’s not another.”

===Chinese===
正是这同一个仇敌，
从前诛杀了你的父亲。
无论战事在何处肆虐，
再也找不出比他更大胆的人。」

===Original===
Angrily spake Ortwin:
 “Me for his wrongs he owes,
 And must atone full dearly
 before from here he goes.

===Chinese===
奥特温怒声说道：
「他欠下我对他的冤仇，
必须付出高昂的代价，
才能从这里脱身。

===Original===
 The ills that he has done us
 must he be soon undoing;
 Gerlind cannot help him
 that he from hence may e’er alive be going.”

===Chinese===
他加给我们的种种祸害，
他必须尽快一一清偿；
格尔林德救不了他，
教他活着离开此地。」

===Original===
Down upon young Ortwin
 Hartmut riding bore.
 Altho’ he did not know him,
 deep he plunged his spur;

===Chinese===
哈特穆特驱马
向年轻的奥特温撞去。
虽说他并不认得对方，
却把马刺深深踢下；

===Original===
 His horse sprang forward widely,
 against brave Ortwin driven.
 Both their spears were lowered;
 fire on their armor flashed from spear-strokes given.

===Chinese===
他的战马猛地前跃，
直撞向勇猛的奥特温。
两人的长矛同时压低；
矛击之下，甲胄上进闪出火光。

===Original===
No thrust against the other
 did either leave undone:
 The war-horse then of Ortwin
 was on his haunches thrown;

===Chinese===
两人对着对方，
一记突刺也未留手：
奥特温的战马
顿时被打得后臀坐地；

===Original===
 Soon, too, the steed did stagger
 whereon was Hartmut seated;
 They could not bear the onset
 of kings who rushed together, to madness heated.

===Chinese===
不久，哈特穆特所骑的
战马也摇晃起来；
它们承受不住两位王者
杀得失了心智般的迎面对冲。

===Original===
High upreared the horses;
 a mighty clang arose
 From clash of kingly sword-blades.
 Thanks were due to those

===Chinese===
战马高高人立而起；
王者剑锋相击，
迸出一声巨大的铿锵。
这番光彩该归于那两位——

===Original===
 Who the fight thus opened,
 as knights beseemeth ever.
 Brave were both and fearless;
 to shrink from one another thought they never.

===Chinese===
他们就这样揭开了战端，
恰如骑士本色，永远如此。
两人都勇猛而无畏；
谁也从未想过向对方退缩。

===Original===
On both sides came their followers,
 lowering their spears,
 And bringing death to many;
 each his foeman nears,

===Chinese===
双方的部众纷纷赶到，
压低了长矛，
给许多人带来死亡；
各自逼近自己的仇敌，

===Original===
 And in the shock of the onset
 heavy wounds was giving.
 All of them were faithful,
 and well for a worthy name they now were striving.

===Chinese===
在冲杀的撞击之中
造成沉重的创伤。
他们个个忠实，
此刻都奋力以求无愧的声名。

===Original===
A thousand ’gainst a thousand,
 now the strife began
 Of Hartmut’s men with Wâ-te’s,
 each man against his man.

===Chinese===
千人对一千人，
哈特穆特部众与瓦特部众
之间的厮杀就此开始，
每一人各对各的敌手。

===Original===
 Soon by the lord of Sturmland
 were they so badly treated
 That whoso now came near him
 never a second time with him was mated.

===Chinese===
不久，施图尔姆兰之主
把他们整治得这般惨，
以致凡有人向他逼近，
便再没有第二次与他交手的机会。

===Original===
Now were thickly mingled
 of foes ten thousand men,
 Among King Herwic’s warriors;
 they came in anger keen.

===Chinese===
这时，上万名仇敌
密密地混入了
赫尔维克王的武士中间；
他们是怀着炽盛的怒火来的。

===Original===
 Their mood it was so stubborn
 that rather than be flying
 Far from the field of fighting,
 they on the ground would first in death be lying.

===Chinese===
他们的心气这般倔强，
宁死也不肯
远远逃离战场，
而要先倒在阵地之上。

===Original===
A knight indeed was Herwic;
 what daring deeds he did!
 Earnest was he in fighting,
 that so the lovely maid

===Chinese===
赫尔维克真不愧骑士之名；
他建下了多少无畏的功绩！
他作战这般奋勉，
只求那可爱的少女

===Original===
 Might be to him the kinder.
 But how could he be dreaming
 The boon could e’er befall him,
 that the eyes of fair Gudrun on him were beaming?

===Chinese===
待他能更加温柔。
可他怎能梦想到
这恩福竟会落到头上——
美丽的古德伦的双眼正朝他熠熠生辉？

===Original===
Ludwig, king of the Normans,
 and they of the Danish land,
 Now had met together.
 Ludwig bore in hand

===Chinese===
诺曼人之王路德维希，
与丹麦来的武士们，
此刻已经相遇。
路德维希手里

===Original===
 His strong and heavy weapon;
 lordly was his bearing,
 Yet he with all his followers
 to come too far without the walls was daring.

===Chinese===
提着他那结实沉重的兵器；
他的气派君王一般威严，
然而他竟带着全体部下
斗胆冲出城墙太远的地方。

===Original===
There, with his men from Holstein,
 Fru-te, brave and bold,
 Slew full many a foeman;
 of this could much be told.

===Chinese===
那边，弗鲁特与他那些
荷尔斯泰因的部众，英勇而无畏，
诛杀了多少仇敌；
这其间的故事可讲的实在很多。

===Original===
 Now, too, from the land of Waleis,
 Morunc, many slaying,
 Before King Ludwig’s castle
 made rich the earth with the dead he low was laying.

===Chinese===
这时，从瓦莱斯来的莫伦克
也诛杀多人，
在路德维希王的城堡前，
他放倒的尸骸把大地铺得殷厚。

===Original===
Irold, the youthful champion,
 a knight both true and good,
 Slashed thro’ foemen’s armor,
 shedding their hot life-blood.

===Chinese===
年轻的猛将伊罗尔德，
是位忠实而良善的骑士，
劈开仇敌的甲胄，
洒下他们滚烫的鲜血。

===Original===
 Under Hilda’s banner
 was Wâ-te’s kinsman fighting;
 Many in death grew paler
 as Horant thinned the crowd he fast was smiting.

===Chinese===
在希尔达的旗帜之下，
瓦特的亲族正酣战；
霍兰特连连挥击、杀散人群，
许多人在死亡中渐渐失色。

===Original===
Now the young King Hartmut
 and Ortwin met again.
 Thicker then than snowflakes
 blown by the wind are seen,

===Chinese===
这时年轻的哈特穆特王
又与奥特温相遇。
武士们挥出的剑击，
比风卷的雪花还要密集，

===Original===
 The sword-strokes of the warriors
 upon each other lighted:
 Thus it was that Hartmut
 once more by Ortwin on the field was greeted.

===Chinese===
纷纷落在对方身上：
就这样，哈特穆特
在战场上再度领受了
奥特温迎头的招呼。

===Original===
Gudrun’s young brother, Ortwin,
 was bold and brave enow,
 But Hartmut through his helmet
 smote him a heavy blow;

===Chinese===
古德伦的弟弟奥特温
也算得又大胆又英勇，
可哈特穆特隔着头盔
给了他重重的一击；

===Original===
 Over his shining breastplate
 soon the blood was streaming:
 The followers of Ortwin
 sadly saw the flow, its brightness dimming.

===Chinese===
不久鲜血便涌流过
他闪亮的胸甲：
奥特温的部众
悲伤地望着血涌，它的光泽渐渐黯淡。

===Original===
Great was the crush and uproar;
 hand to hand they fought;
 Many wounds were gaping
 thro’ rings of steel well-wrought;

===Chinese===
挤压与喧嚣鼎沸；
他们短兵相接；
许多伤口豁开，
穿透了精工锻打的钢环；

===Original===
 Many a head had fallen
 beneath the sword-strokes given:
 Death was like a robber,
 that from their kin the dearest friends had riven.

===Chinese===
多少头颅在
挥落的剑击之下滚落：
死亡就像一个强盗，
把最亲爱的朋友从亲族身边夺走。

===Original===
Now saw the Danish Horant
 that Ortwin from his foe
 A bloody wound had taken;
 then Horant bade them show

===Chinese===
这时丹麦的霍兰特
望见奥特温已从仇敌
那里受了一处血伤；
霍兰特便吩咐他们指认，

===Original===
 Who ’twas that thus had wounded
 his master loved so dearly.
 Hartmut at this was laughing,
 for both upon the field had met too nearly.

===Chinese===
究竟是谁伤了他
深爱着的主上。
哈特穆特听了大笑，
因为两人在战场上早已近身相见了。

===Original===
Ortwin himself then answered:
 “ ’Tis Hartmut this has done.”
 Then Hilda’s banner was given
 by Horant to one of his own;

===Chinese===
奥特温亲自回答道：
「这是哈特穆特干的。」
于是霍兰特把希尔达的旗帜
交给了自己的一名随从；

===Original===
 Thinking thus the foeman
 he could harm the better,
 And gain himself much honor:
 now he sought his foe with boldness greater.

===Chinese===
心想这样便能把
仇敌伤得更重，
又为自己赢得莫大的荣誉：
此刻他怀着更盛的胆气寻向对手。

===Original===
Hartmut heard around him
 a loud and stormy din.
 On many of his warriors
 streams of blood were seen

===Chinese===
哈特穆特听得四周
一片狂风暴雨似的喧嚣。
他许多部下的身上
都看得见血流如注，

===Original===
 Fast from wounds out-welling;
 down to their feet ’twas flowing.
 Then cried Hartmut boldly:
 “For this shall you atone, and this be ruing.”

===Chinese===
从伤口里迅猛涌出；
一直淌到脚边。
哈特穆特随即大胆喊道：
「这笔账你们要偿还，还要为此懊悔。」

===Original===
Now he turned him quickly
 where Horant met his sight;
 Then might one be seeing,
 so brave were both in fight,

===Chinese===
他当即迅速转身，
朝霍兰特映入眼帘处望去；
那时人们便能看出，
两人在战斗中都是何等英勇，

===Original===
 How from their ringed armor
 sparks of fire were flying;
 Blunted were the sword-blades
 which they on each other’s helmets fast were plying.

===Chinese===
环甲之上
火星四下飞溅；
他们互相朝对方头盔
疾挥的剑锋都已卷刃。

===Original===
Hartmut wounded Horant,
 even as he had done
 Not long before to Ortwin;
 a ruddy stream full soon

===Chinese===
哈特穆特杀伤了霍兰特，
正如不久之前
他对奥特温做的那样；
一股赤红的血流顷刻

===Original===
 Ran from out his armor,
 at Hartmut’s hand forth welling.
 Strong indeed was his foeman;
 who now to win his lands could hope be feeling?

===Chinese===
从他甲胄里奔涌而出，
自哈特穆特手中汩汩淌出。
他的对手着实强悍；
如今谁还敢指望夺得他的国土？

===Original===
Then in bitter struggle
 many, on either side,
 Saw their bucklers shattered,
 though strong and often tried;

===Chinese===
于是在这场酷烈的鏖战中，
双方都有许多人
眼见自己的圆盾碎裂，
尽管它们坚固又久经战阵；

===Original===
 Beaten were they and broken
 by sword-strokes quickly given
 By each upon the other.
 Well to guard himself had Hartmut striven.

===Chinese===
它们被彼此迅疾挥出的
剑击砸得破碎开岔。
哈特穆特也竭尽了全力，
把自己的身体护牢。

===Original===
Now the friends of Ortwin,
 and those of Horant, too,
 Away from the field did lead them;
 and care did they bestow

===Chinese===
这时，奥特温的友伴，
还有霍兰特的友伴，
把两人搀离了战场；
他们悉心照料，

===Original===
 To bind their wounds wide-gaping;
 no time for this they wasted.
 Then again to the war-field
 the knights both rode; once more to the strife they hasted.

===Chinese===
为他们包扎豁开的创口；
一刻也不曾耽搁。
随后两位骑士又驰回战场，
再度扑向鏖战。

===Original===
We now must leave them fighting
 as bravely as they will.
 Who the day was winning,
 or whom his foe did kill,

===Chinese===
我们此刻只得撇下他们，
任他们怎样奋勇地鏖战。
这一天谁占着上风，
谁的仇敌又死于谁手，

===Original===
 Before King Ludwig’s castle,
 none could yet be saying.
 Grimly strove the Normans;
 their foes, not less, for fame were strength outlaying.

===Chinese===
在路德维希王的城堡前，
还没有人能够说清。
诺曼人拼死苦斗；
他们的对手为荣誉耗下的气力也丝毫不减。

===Original===
Of all that there befell them
 none may ever tell;
 But ’tis not yet forgotten
 that many a knight there fell.

===Chinese===
那里降临到他们身上的
种种遭遇，无人能够尽述；
但有一点至今未被遗忘：
多少骑士倒在了那里。

===Original===
 On every side were sword-blades
 heard together ringing;
 Foemen all were mingled,
 the slow with those who quick in fight were springing.

===Chinese===
四面八方都听得见
剑锋相击的铮鸣；
仇敌全都混作一团，
迟缓的与阵前一跃而前的搅在一处。

===Original===
Wâ-te stood not idle,
 that can I well believe.
 He bade farewell to many,
 nor longer let them live;

===Chinese===
瓦特绝没有袖手闲着，
这一点我深信不疑。
他向许多人道了永别，
再不容他们多活；

===Original===
 Cut down by him in the struggle,
 were they before him lying.
 Fain were Hartmut’s kinsmen
 to wreak their wrath for friends who there were dying.

===Chinese===
在鏖战中被他砍倒的，
一一横陈在他眼前。
哈特穆特的亲族们
一心要为阵亡的友伴泄愤报仇。

===Original===
Now came Herwic nearer,
 so the tale is told,
 And led against King Ludwig
 many a champion bold.

===Chinese===
这时赫尔维克逼近了，
故事是这样传述的：
他统率着许多无畏的猛士
杀向路德维希王。

===Original===
 He saw that aged warrior
 his weapons bravely bearing,
 Where he with all his liegemen,
 a wondrous host of foes beat down, unsparing.

===Chinese===
他望见那员年迈的战将
正威风凛凛地挥动兵刃，
在他与全体封臣鏖战之处，
惊人的一大片仇敌被毫不留情地杀倒。

===Original===
Herwic called out loudly:
 “Can any one now tell
 Who is that fighting graybeard,
 who all his foes doth fell?

===Chinese===
赫尔维克高声喊道：
「有谁能告诉我，
那位鏖战的白发老将是谁？
他把自己所有的仇敌一一击倒？

===Original===
 Deepest wounds for many
 there his hand is hewing,
 With bravery so fearless:
 women in tears will this erelong be ruing.”

===Chinese===
他的手在那里
为多少人劈出最深的创伤，
这般毫无惧色的英勇：
妇人们的泪水不久便要为此抛洒。」

===Original===
When this was heard by Ludwig,
 outspoke that Norman foe:
 “Who in the midst of battle
 seeks my name to know?

===Chinese===
路德维希听了这话，
那诺曼仇敌开言道：
「是谁在战阵正酣之际
探问我的名姓？

===Original===
 I bear the name of Ludwig:
 for Normandy I’m fighting;
 Could I but meet my foemen,
 them indeed would I be sorely smiting.”

===Chinese===
我名路德维希：
我为诺曼底而战；
但教我撞上我的仇敌们，
定叫他们狠狠吃我的痛击。」

===Original===
Then spake to him King Herwic:
 “This thou well dost earn:
 Seeing thou art Ludwig,
 with hate for thee I burn.

===Chinese===
赫尔维克王随即对他说道：
「这正是你应得的：
既然你是路德维希，
我对你便怀着熊熊的仇恨。

===Original===
 For us, upon the sand-drifts,
 many knights thou wast slaying:
 Thou slewest Hettel also;
 a warrior brave was he, beyond all saying.

===Chinese===
为了我们，在那沙洲之上，
你曾屠戮多少骑士：
你还诛杀了黑特尔（Hettel）；
他是位无以言传的英勇战士。

===Original===
“Still further thou hast wronged us,
 before thy day was done:
 For this we still are mourning.
 I for my loss have known

===Chinese===
「你对我们还有更深的亏欠，
在你的末日来到之前：
为此我们至今仍在哀恸。
我为了我的损失，早已尝尽

===Original===
 Heart-heaviness and sorrow:
 thou hast my lady stolen
 From me upon the Wulpensand;
 and many knights for her in death have fallen.

===Chinese===
心头的沉重与悲苦：
你把我的夫人
在武尔彭沙洲（Wulpensand）上从我身边夺走；
多少骑士为她坠入了死亡。

===Original===
“I bear the name of Herwic:
 thou hast taken my hoped-for wife,
 And again to me must give her;
 else to give his life,

===Chinese===
「我名赫尔维克：
你抢走了我盼望中的妻子，
还必须把她还给我；
不然的话，就得有一条命

===Original===
 With that of many a liegeman,
 must one of us be willing.”
 Then King Ludwig answered:
 “Too boldly thou in my land in threats art dealing.

===Chinese===
连同许多封臣的性命，
在我们两人之间舍出。」
路德维希王随即答道：
「你在我的国土上出言威胁，未免太过大胆。

===Original===
“Thy name, and this thy warning,
 thou hast no need to tell;
 There yet are many others
 from whom I took, as well,

===Chinese===
「你的名姓，还有你这番威吓，
都无需你来相告；
此外还有许许多多别的人，
我也照样从他们手里夺过

===Original===
 Their goods and eke their kinsmen.
 To trust my word be ready,
 In this I will not falter;
 thou nevermore may’st hope to kiss thy lady.”

===Chinese===
他们的财物，还有他们的亲族。
你只管信我的话，
在这一点上我绝不反悔；
你再也休想亲吻你的夫人。」

===Original===
When they thus had spoken,
 the kings no more did rest,
 But sprang upon each other.
 If either got the best,

===Chinese===
两人这样说过之后，
两王便再不停歇，
径直扑向对方。
无论哪一边占了上风，

===Original===
 To hold it was not easy;
 youths were forward pushing
 Under both the standards,
 and daring knights to help their lords were rushing.

===Chinese===
要守住它都非易事；
双方旗下都有年轻的勇士
奋力争先，
无畏的骑士们蜂拥上前援救各自的主上。

===Original===
A fearless king was Herwic,
 and long and bravely fought;
 But quickly Hartmut’s father
 the youthful Herwic smote,

===Chinese===
赫尔维克是位无畏的君王，
长久而英勇地厮杀；
但哈特穆特的父亲很快
便把年轻的赫尔维克击中，

===Original===
 Till he began to stagger
 ’neath blows by Ludwig given,
 Who gladly would have slain him,
 or would from out his lands his foe have driven.

===Chinese===
打得他在路德维希的
挥击之下开始踉跄，
路德维希巴不得就此杀了他，
或是把这仇敌逐出自己的国土。

===Original===
If Herwic’s faithful followers
 so near him had not been,
 And given help so quickly,
 never could he, I ween,

===Chinese===
若不是赫尔维克忠实的部下
就在他近旁，
赶来救助得这般迅捷，
我想他绝不可能

===Original===
 Have freed himself from Ludwig,
 or left the field yet living;
 So well that aged warrior
 to make young Herwic dread him now was striving.

===Chinese===
从路德维希手中脱身，
或活着撤离战场；
那员老将就是这样卖力，
务要教年轻的赫尔维克畏惧他。

===Original===
But help to him was granted,
 his life he did not lose;
 And, neither stunned nor wounded,
 he from his fall arose.

===Chinese===
但援助终究赐给了他，
性命得以保全；
他既没有昏厥也没有负伤，
从倒下的地方站起身来。

===Original===
 Then to the roof quick turning,
 his eyes he now was raising,
 To see if, ’mongst the ladies,
 his heart’s beloved had on his fall been gazing.

===Chinese===
随即他急忙转身朝向屋顶，
抬起他仰望的目光，
要看在众位贵妇中间，
他心中的爱人可曾望见他跌倒。

## Tale XXVIII How Herwic Slew Ludwig / 第二十八诗章 赫尔维克如何诛杀路德维希

===Original===
Herwic, feeling mortified that Gudrun from the castle should see his fall, returns to the fight, and slays Ludwig. Gerlind bribes one of her men to put Gudrun to death, but he is prevented by the threats of Hartmut. Hartmut is taken prisoner, and carried on board a ship.

===Chinese===
赫尔维克（Herwic）一想到古德伦（Gudrun）会在城堡上望见他坠马，便深感羞愤，于是重返战阵，诛杀了路德维希（Ludwig）。格尔林德（Gerlind）收买一名手下要去害古德伦的性命，却因哈特穆特（Hartmut）出言威吓而未能得逞。哈特穆特被俘，被押上了一艘船。

===Original===
Now said Herwic sadly:
 “Ah, welaway! Woe’s me!
 If fair Gudrun, my lady,
 my fall did lately see.

===Chinese===
赫尔维克凄然说道：
「唉，苦啊！我何其不幸！
要是美丽的古德伦，我的女郎，
新近望见了我坠马的模样，

===Original===
 Should e’er the hour be coming
 when I shall clasp the maiden,
 And as a wife shall own her,
 with blame and scorn shall I by her be laden.

===Chinese===
将来若真有那么一刻，
让我把这位少女拥入怀中，
娶她为妻、称她为己有，
她带给我的将尽是责难与轻蔑。

===Original===
“Sorely doth it shame me,
 that now the gray old man
 Thus has overthrown me.”
 Forthwith he bade again

===Chinese===
「这深深令我羞愧：
那白发老翁
竟这样把我打翻在地。」
随即他又下令，

===Original===
 His men to raise his banner,
 and ’gainst King Ludwig bear it;
 Then rushed they on the foemen,
 who might not flee the fight, but all must share it.

===Chinese===
命部下重新举起他的大旗，
朝着路德维希王擎去；
于是他们扑向敌阵，
敌人已无从脱逃，只得全体迎战。

===Original===
Ludwig heard behind him
 an uproar loud and din;
 Then he turned him quickly,
 and Herwic sought again.

===Chinese===
路德维希听见身后
响起震天的喧嚣；
他急忙转过身来，
再度寻战赫尔维克。

===Original===
 Soon he heard on helmets
 many sword-blows stricken.
 Those who stood near Ludwig
 well might dread the wrath that both did quicken.

===Chinese===
顷刻他又听见剑锋
在头盔上连连砍响。
站在路德维希近旁的人
无不畏惧两人急速燃起的怒火。

===Original===
They sprang upon each other,
 and fast and well they smote;
 Blows on blows loud sounded
 the stormy field throughout.

===Chinese===
两人扑向对方，
出剑又疾又稳；
一击接一击，响彻
这风暴席卷的战场。

===Original===
 Who can tell how many
 now in death were lying?
 The day was lost to Ludwig,
 who there his strength with Herwic would be trying.

===Chinese===
谁能说得清
此刻有多少人倒卧于死？
这一日路德维希败局已定，
他偏要在此同赫尔维克较量膂力。

===Original===
Soon Gudrun’s betrothèd
 reached over Ludwig’s shield,
 And smote him ’neath his helmet;
 well his sword did he wield.

===Chinese===
古德伦的未婚夫很快
将剑探过路德维希的盾沿，
砍中他头盔之下的脖颈；
他的剑使唤得着实高明。

===Original===
 Him he sorely wounded,
 and strength no more did leave him;
 Grim death he there awaited
 until King Herwic should of life bereave him.

===Chinese===
他把他砍成重伤，
力气再不曾回到他身上；
他只得在那里等候狰狞的死，
等赫尔维克王来夺取他的性命。

===Original===
Then Herwic with his broadsword
 smote the king anew;
 At once the head of Ludwig
 from off his shoulders flew.

===Chinese===
随后赫尔维克挥起宽剑，
朝那国王再度砍去；
路德维希的头颅顿时
从他的肩头飞落。

===Original===
 Well repaid was Herwic
 for his shameful overthrowing;
 The king lay dead before him.
 For this fair eyes must soon be overflowing.

===Chinese===
赫尔维克这一击
把受辱坠马的耻辱尽数偿清；
那国王横死在他面前。
为此，美人的眼睛不久便要泪如泉涌。

===Original===
Ludwig’s faithful followers,
 after their king was slain,
 His banner to the castle
 thought to bear again;

===Chinese===
路德维希忠心的部属，
在他们的王被杀之后，
还想着把他的旗帜
重新带回城堡；

===Original===
 But all too far from the gateway
 they had now been straying:
 From them the flag was taken,
 and death must them erelong with their lord be laying.

===Chinese===
但他们此刻离城门
已经驰突得太远；
旗帜被人夺去，
不多时他们就得随他们的主上一同倒毙。

===Original===
The watchman saw from the castle
 how Ludwig lost his life;
 Then was heard the mourning
 of knights and many a wife:

===Chinese===
城堡上的守望者望见
路德维希如何丢了性命；
随即便听见骑士们
和众多妇人的哀恸：

===Original===
 Their king, so old and mighty,
 they knew in death was lying;
 Gudrun and all her maidens
 stood in the hall in fear, and loud were crying.

===Chinese===
他们知道他们年高势重的王
已卧于死亡之中；
古德伦带着全体侍女
立在厅中，惊惧地放声哭喊。

===Original===
As yet the Norman Hartmut,
 knew nothing of the tale,
 How that the king, his father,
 and kinsmen young as well,

===Chinese===
这时诺曼人哈特穆特
对这消息还一无所知，
不知他的父王
连同年轻的亲族们

===Original===
 With many bravest warriors,
 now in death were sleeping,
 Then he heard from the castle
 the shrieks and wails of those who there were weeping.

===Chinese===
已同许多最英勇的武士
一齐长眠于死亡；
这时他听见城堡里传来
那些哀哭者的尖号与悲泣。

===Original===
Now the knightly Hartmut
 unto his followers said:
 “ ’Tis best we hence withdraw us;
 how many here lie dead

===Chinese===
骑士般的哈特穆特
对他的部众说道：
「我们最好从这里撤身；
躺在这里的死者已不知多少，

===Original===
 Who in stormy fighting
 thought our men to be slaying!
 Now will we seek the castle,
 and there until a better time be staying.”

===Chinese===
他们原想在鏖战之中
把我们的武士杀败！
现在我们要退回城堡，
在那里静待更好的时机。」

===Original===
To him they listened gladly,
 and followed where he rode.
 Great was the work of slaughter
 the field around them showed,

===Chinese===
部众欣然听从他，
跟随他驰马所往。
四周的原野上
展现出何等浩大的杀戮，

===Original===
 Where with grimmest foemen
 they were closely warring;
 Freely had blood been flowing
 beneath the hand of Hartmut and his followers daring.

===Chinese===
在那里他们正与最狰狞的仇敌
短兵相接地苦战；
在哈特穆特与其无畏部众的剑下，
鲜血一直在纵情奔流。

===Original===
“So well,” he said, “have you helped me,
 who my kinsmen are,
 That all my lands and riches
 gladly with you I’ll share.

===Chinese===
「你们助我助得这样好，」他说，「我的亲族们，
我情愿把全部的土地与财货，
都与你们一同分享，
绝无半点舍不得。

===Original===
 We now will ride to my castle,
 and there to rest betake us;
 Men the gates will open,
 and wine for us will pour, and mead will make us.”

===Chinese===
我们这就驰往我的城堡，
到那里去歇息一番；
仆役们会敞开大门，
为我们斟上美酒，调制蜜酒。」

===Original===
Fallen knights full many
 they left on the field behind:
 Were these of the land the owners,
 still with no braver mind

===Chinese===
无数倒伏的骑士
被他们留在身后的原野上：
这些人纵然是这方土地的主人，
心气也不会更勇敢半分

===Original===
 They then had met the onset.
 Those for the gates now striving,
 By Wâ-te and his thousand
 were not allowed to reach the castle living.

===Chinese===
去迎击敌人的猛攻。
此刻那些拼死冲向城门的人，
瓦特（Wâ-te）和他的一千部众
不容他们活着挨近城堡。

===Original===
He with a host of fighters
 near the gates was seen,
 When Hartmut with his followers
 sought to come within;

===Chinese===
他带着一支武士大军
出现在城门近旁，
这时哈特穆特正带着他的部众
想冲进城去；

===Original===
 They in this were baffled,
 and their strength were wasting.
 Those who the castle guarded
 heavy stones from off the wall were casting.

===Chinese===
他们被阻在城外，
气力渐渐耗竭。
守卫城堡的人们
正把巨石从墙上频频投下。

===Original===
They hurled them down so wildly
 on Wâ-te and his men,
 Like hailstones they were falling,
 with not a stop between.

===Chinese===
他们朝瓦特和他的部众
狂暴地掷下石块，
石头如冰雹般坠落，
中间没有一刻停歇。

===Original===
 Wâ-te recked but little
 how many were dead or living,
 Might he the day be gaining;
 to this alone his thoughts he now was giving.

===Chinese===
瓦特全不怎么在意
死了多少、活着多少，
只要能把这一日赢下；
如今他一心所想的只有这件事。

===Original===
Hartmut saw old Wâ-te
 before the castle-gate.
 He said: “Though from our foemen
 our gains this day are great,

===Chinese===
哈特穆特望见老瓦特
立在城堡门前。
他说：「虽然今日从仇敌手里
我们收获颇丰，

===Original===
 Before it shall be ended,
 for this their hate they’ll show us:
 Let now the strong be heedful;
 dead must many lie on the field below us.

===Chinese===
但这场战事一日不了，
他们总要为此向我们泄恨：
让强健者各自当心；
我们脚下的原野上还要横尸无数。

===Original===
“Fear and care it gives me
 that many here are seen
 Whom we must now be meeting.
 Wâ-te with all his men

===Chinese===
「这叫我又是忧又是怕：
此处望见了许多人，
个个都得由我们此刻
迎面去对付。瓦特和他全部人马，

===Original===
 I see before the gateway,
 there with sword-strokes hewing.
 If he of the gate be keeper,
 I look for little kindness he’ll be doing.

===Chinese===
我看见他正在大门口，
挥剑乱劈乱砍。
倘若他把守着这扇大门，
我看他绝不会发什么善心。

===Original===
“See for yourselves, my warriors,
 the gateways and the walls
 By foes on all sides girded;
 knight to knight there calls.

===Chinese===
「武士们，你们亲自看看，
各处城门与城墙
都被仇敌四面围拢；
骑士正对着骑士叫阵。

===Original===
 The roadways all are crowded,
 whichever way we’re turning:
 Gudrun’s good friends and champions
 will spare no toil; to win the day they’re burning.

===Chinese===
无论我们转向哪条道路，
条条都已挤满敌兵：
古德伦的良友与勇士们
不辞劳苦，一心只想赢下今日。

===Original===
“That you may know too truly,
 as I see already well;
 Friends we must lose full many.
 Howe’er it so befell,

===Chinese===
「你们尽可以确信，
这我早已看得分明；
我们要折损许多友人。
然而事情既已到了这般地步，

===Original===
 Before the outer gateway
 already see I waving
 The Moorland foeman’s banner;
 lest they get in, a care must you be having.

===Chinese===
外城门前我已望见
摩尔兰（Moorland）仇敌的旗帜翻飞；
你们务须留神，
别让他们攻进城里。

===Original===
“Near to the second gateway
 I see yet other foes:
 I saw Lord Ortwin’s banner,
 as on the breeze it rose.

===Chinese===
「第二道城门近旁
我又望见别的仇敌：
我看见了奥特温（Ortwin）大人的旗帜
正乘着风扬起。

===Original===
 Gudrun’s young brother is he;
 fair women’s smiles he’s seeking:
 Ere he shall cool his anger,
 beneath his blows will helmets yet be breaking.

===Chinese===
他是古德伦的幼弟；
正追逐美人们的笑靥：
不等他的怒气冷却，
还要有多少头盔碎在他的剑下。

===Original===
“Now see I, too, brave Herwic,
 before the third gate there;
 With him seven thousand followers
 upon the field are near.

===Chinese===
「我还望见英勇的赫尔维克，
就在第三道门前；
他带着七千名部众，
在原野上步步逼近。

===Original===
 He comes in guise most knightly,
 to win his own heart’s lady;
 On him are gazing gladly
 the fair Gudrun, and many maids already.

===Chinese===
他来得一派骑士气度，
要赢取他心上的女郎；
美丽的古德伦
和许多少女已欣然把目光投向他。

===Original===
“To hasten back to my castle,
 the thought too late has come.
 I know not where, with my warriors,
 now to seek a home.

===Chinese===
「想抢时间回我的城堡，
这念头来得太迟。
我已不知该带着我的武士
往何处寻觅一个家。

===Original===
 I see the stern old Wâ-te
 before the fourth gate fighting;
 My many friends in the castle,
 I fear indeed must long for us be waiting.

===Chinese===
我望见严峻的老瓦特
正在第四道门前厮杀；
城堡里我那许多朋友，
我怕他们真要久久把我们盼望。

===Original===
“Fly from here I cannot;
 no wings for this have I;
 Nor in the earth can hide me,
 whatever else I try.

===Chinese===
「从这里逃是逃不脱的；
我没有生就的翅膀；
也钻不进地里藏身，
任凭我再怎么设法。

===Original===
 Neither from the foeman
 to the waves can we be turning:
 Now, in our lot so wretched,
 what best it is to do from me be learning.

===Chinese===
也没法背对仇敌
转身扑向海浪：
如今我们的命运这般凄惨，
怎样做才最好，就从我这里领教吧。

===Original===
“Good knights of mine, now hearken;
 there’s nothing left to do
 But, to the ground alighting,
 their hot life’s-blood to hew

===Chinese===
「我的好骑士们，且听着；
如今别无他策，
只有翻身下马、立足在地，
把他们滚烫的性命之血

===Original===
 From out the ringed armor:
 fear not the word I’ve given.”
 Then, from their saddles leaping,
 their horses back at once from them were driven.

===Chinese===
从环甲之中砍出：
我许下的话绝不会作废。」
于是众人从鞍上一跃而下，
马匹随即被人从他们身边牵开。

===Original===
“Now on, brave knights and warriors!”
 Hartmut called to all;
 “To the castle-gates press nearer,
 whatever may befall.

===Chinese===
「上啊，英勇的骑士与武士们！」
哈特穆特向众人高呼；
「朝城门步步进逼，
无论有什么灾殃降临。

===Original===
 I yet must meet old Wâ-te,
 whether I live or am dying;
 To drive him from the gateway,
 and from the walls, I will at least be trying.”

===Chinese===
我总还要会一会老瓦特，
不论是生是死；
要把他从大门口赶走，
还要把他从城墙上赶走，我至少要奋力一试。」

===Original===
Soon, with swords uplifted,
 rushing on were seen
 The brave and youthful Hartmut,
 and with him all his men.

===Chinese===
顷刻间，只见高举利剑
猛冲而来的
英勇年轻的哈特穆特，
和他身后的全体部众。

===Original===
 He fell upon grim Wâ-te,
 who met his coming gladly;
 Now their sword-blades clattered,
 and many knights lay dead, or wounded badly.

===Chinese===
他直扑狰狞的瓦特，
瓦特欣然迎他来战；
两柄剑锋铿锵乱响，
无数骑士非死即重伤，横陈在地。

===Original===
When Wâ-te saw young Hartmut
 the onslaught on him make,
 While Fru-te bore the banner,
 in wrath old Wâ-te spake:

===Chinese===
瓦特见年轻的哈特穆特
朝自己发起猛攻，
这时弗鲁特（Fru-te）高擎着军旗，
老瓦特怒声发话：

===Original===
 “I hear the swords loud ringing
 of many pressing near us;
 I beg, dear cousin Fru-te,
 let none come out from the gates; from that now spare us.”

===Chinese===
「我听见剑声铿锵，
是许多敌人正向我们逼近；
我求你，好族亲弗鲁特，
别放任何人出城；这一点就饶了我们吧。」

===Original===
Then Wâ-te, wild with anger,
 did on King Hartmut run;
 But he, so brave and daring,
 the onset would not shun.

===Chinese===
瓦特怒不可遏，
径直朝哈特穆特王扑去；
而哈特穆特如此英勇无畏，
绝不肯躲开这一击。

===Original===
 The sun with dust was darkened,
 now from the struggle rising:
 Their strength was unabated;
 still for good name they fought, that both were prizing.

===Chinese===
烈日被尘土遮暗，
此刻正从这场鏖战中升起；
两人的膂力毫不衰减；
仍在为双方各自珍视的荣名而战。

===Original===
What helped it that of Wâ-te
 men said he was as strong
 As six and twenty warriors?
 Though this was on each tongue,

===Chinese===
纵然人们都说瓦特
强悍得一人
抵得过二十六名武士，
这话虽挂在人人嘴上，又有什么用？

===Original===
 Yet still to him young Hartmut
 his knightly skill was showing:
 Howe’er his foe was striving,
 the Norman lord and his men no less were doing.

===Chinese===
年轻的哈特穆特仍向他
展露了自己的骑士本领：
任凭仇敌如何奋力，
这诺曼人君主和他的部众也不遑多让。

===Original===
A knight he was most truly,
 and well indeed he fought;
 Of the dead there lay a mountain
 whom on the field he smote.

===Chinese===
他千真万确是位骑士，
也委实打得漂亮；
被他砍倒在原野上的死者
堆成了一座山冈。

===Original===
 It was, forsooth, a wonder
 that Hartmut had not yielded,
 And died before old Wâ-te:
 grim was the wrath from which himself he shielded.

===Chinese===
说真的，这简直是奇迹：
哈特穆特竟还没有降服，
还没有丧命于老瓦特剑下——
他挡开的那股怒火何其狰狞。

===Original===
Soon heard he, loudly shrieking,
 old King Ludwig’s wife;
 Sorely she was mourning
 the loss of her husband’s life.

===Chinese===
不久他听见老王路德维希的妻子
在高声尖号；
她正为失去
丈夫的性命而凄恻。

===Original===
 She said she would reward him
 who felt his death past bearing,
 And would Gudrun be slaying,
 with all the maids who there her lot were sharing.

===Chinese===
她说，谁若对他的死
痛心得难以忍受，她必重赏；
她还要杀掉古德伦，
连同在此与她同命运的众侍女。

===Original===
Then ran a worthless fellow,
 to whom the fee was dear,
 To where the Hegeling maidens
 sat together near.

===Chinese===
随即窜出一个卑贱的家伙，
那笔赏钱在他眼里金贵无比，
直奔黑格林人（Hegeling）的少女们
围坐的地方而去。

===Original===
 Then the hearts of the women
 with many fears he loaded;
 For the sake of gold to be given,
 to take their lives he now was sharply goaded.

===Chinese===
他往众女的心头
压上重重恐惧；
为了那将要到手的黄金，
他受人狠命催逼，要来取她们的性命。

===Original===
When that Hilda’s daughter
 against her saw him bear
 A sharp and naked weapon,
 she well indeed might fear,

===Chinese===
当希尔达（Hilda）的女儿
看见他朝自己
擎来一柄锋利的赤刃，
她自然满心惊骇，

===Original===
 And mourn that, far from kindred,
 she was thus forsaken.
 Had not young Hartmut seen it,
 the knave her head from her would then have taken.

===Chinese===
也自伤远离亲族，
竟这般遭人弃绝。
若不是年轻的哈特穆特瞧见，
那恶棍当时便会取下她的头颅。

===Original===
She so forgot her breeding
 that now she screamed aloud,
 As if in dread of dying;
 great fear made wild her mood.

===Chinese===
她全然忘了平日的教养，
此刻放声尖叫，
仿佛性命就在顷刻；
巨大的恐惧搅得她心神狂乱。

===Original===
 ’Twas the same with all her maidens,
 there beside her seated,
 From out the window gazing;
 the ladies such behavior ill befitted.

===Chinese===
她身旁陪坐的众侍女
也个个如此，
纷纷朝窗外张望；
这般失态实在不合贵妇的身份。

===Original===
At once the sound of her wailing
 to Hartmut made her known;
 And greatly did he wonder
 what made her scream and moan.

===Chinese===
她的哭号声随即
让哈特穆特认出了她；
他大为惊诧，
是什么引得她又哭又叫。

===Original===
 Soon he saw a ruffian
 whose sword was near to falling,
 As if he meant to kill her.
 Loudly now to him ’gan Hartmut calling:

===Chinese===
转眼他望见一个恶徒，
手中长剑眼看劈落，
像是要取她性命。
哈特穆特当即朝他高声喝道：

===Original===
“Who are you, low-born dastard?
 For what reward or need
 Do you affright these maidens,
 and seek to strike them dead?

===Chinese===
「你是什么东西，出身下贱的懦夫？
为了什么赏钱、什么缘故，
竟敢恐吓这些少女，
还想把她们砍死？

===Original===
 If you shall strike one lady,
 I give you now this warning,
 Your life shall quick be ended;
 your kinsmen too shall hang, this very morning.”

===Chinese===
你若碰一位贵妇一根指头，
我现在就警告你，
你的性命立刻了结；
你的亲族也在今天早晨统统绞死。」

===Original===
Back then sprang the rascal﻿—
 his anger he did fear;
 For now the youthful Hartmut
 held his life not dear,

===Chinese===
那无赖随即缩身退开——
他畏惧他的怒火；
因为年轻的哈特穆特此刻
为助这些无家可归的少女，

===Original===
 When to the homeless maidens
 he his help was giving:
 With care was he o’erladen,
 while from grim death to free them he was striving.

===Chinese===
早把自家性命看得不值一文：
他忧劳集于一身，
一面竭力把她们
从狰狞的死亡手里解救出来。

===Original===
Quickly then came Ortrun,
 she of Norman lands,
 The fair and youthful princess;
 in woe she wrung her hands.

===Chinese===
奥特龙（Ortrun）随即赶来，
她来自诺曼人的国土，
是位美丽年轻的公主；
悲苦中绞着双手。

===Original===
 She to Gudrun came nearer,
 the stately, high-born maiden,
 And, at her feet down-falling,
 bewept her father’s death, with sorrow laden.

===Chinese===
她走近古德伦——
那位雍容而高贵的少女——
拜倒在她脚边，
满怀哀恸，哭悼父亲的死。

===Original===
She said: “Most queenly lady,
 do not your tears forbear,
 For all my many kinsmen
 who death together share.

===Chinese===
她说：「王后般尊贵的夫人，
请不要强忍您的眼泪，
为了我这许多亲族，
他们正一同赴死。

===Original===
 Bethink you, if you also
 a father slain were weeping,
 How you would feel, great princess.
 My father slain I mourn, in death now sleeping.

===Chinese===
请您想想，倘若您
也在为被害的父亲哭泣，
尊贵的公主，您将作何感受。
我哀悼我那被害的父亲，他如今长眠于死亡。

===Original===
“Behold, most high-born maiden,
 my woe and bitter need;
 How almost all my kinsmen
 lie, with my father, dead:

===Chinese===
「请看，最尊贵的少女，
我的悲苦与困厄；
我的亲族几乎尽数
随我的父亲一同倒毙：

===Original===
 And now the knightly Hartmut
 is death from Wâ-te fearing.
 If I should lose my brother,
 bereft of kindred, nought could life be cheering.

===Chinese===
如今骑士般的哈特穆特
正怕要死在瓦特手里。
我若再失去这位兄长，
亲族尽失，人生再无可慰。

===Original===
“Reward the love I’ve shown you,”
 said the Norman maid.
 “Of all that saw your sorrow,
 when none a tear did shed,

===Chinese===
「请报答我对您的爱，」
这位诺曼少女说道。
「当初您身陷忧苦，众人冷眼旁观，
没有一人落泪，

===Original===
 I then alone was friendly,
 and had you in my keeping;
 For all the wrongs they did you,
 I the livelong day for you was weeping.”

===Chinese===
唯有我独自善待您，
把您护在我的照料之中；
他们加于您的种种欺凌，
我为您整日哭泣。」

===Original===
Queen Hilda’s daughter answered:
 “Thou wast indeed my friend;
 But yet this strife so deadly
 I know not how to end.

===Chinese===
希尔达的女儿答道：
「你确实曾是我的朋友；
可这场你死我活的争斗，
我不知怎样才能了结。

===Original===
 Were I indeed a warrior,
 and knightly weapons wearing,
 I’d stop the fighting gladly;
 and none to slay your brother then were daring.”

===Chinese===
我若真是个武士，
披挂着骑士的兵刃，
定会欣然制止这场厮杀；
那时便再无人敢杀你的兄长。」

===Original===
Ortrun was sorely weeping;
 she still the maid besought,
 Until within the window
 Gudrun at length she brought,

===Chinese===
奥特龙哭得凄楚；
仍一再恳求那位少女，
直到终于把古德伦
引到了窗前，

===Original===
 Who with her hand then beckoned,
 and begged that it be told her
 If from the land of her fathers
 knights had come who did in friendship hold her.

===Chinese===
古德伦以手示意，
恳求来人告知她：
可曾有骑士从她父祖的国土前来，
且对她怀着友好的情谊。

===Original===
Then the knightly Herwic
 answer thus did make:
 “Who are you, young maiden,
 who news from us do seek?

===Chinese===
骑士般的赫尔维克
这样作答：
「你是哪一位，年轻的少女，
向我们探问消息？

===Original===
 We are not the Hegelings,
 whom you see so near you;
 We hither come from the Sealands.
 Tell us, maiden, how we now can cheer you?”

===Chinese===
我们不是黑格林人，
不是你眼前望见的这些；
我们是从西兰（Sealands）来的。
告诉我们，少女，如今怎样才能宽慰你？」

===Original===
“This do I beseech you,”
 said the queenly maid:
 “Sore has been the fighting;
 him will I thank, indeed,

===Chinese===
「这件事我恳求你，」
王后般的少女说道：
「这场厮杀何其惨烈；那个人，
我必真心感激，

===Original===
 Who now cuts short the struggle.
 Me will he be cheering
 Who from the hands of Wâ-te
 will Hartmut free in the strife that I am fearing.”

===Chinese===
只要他能就此止息这场争斗。
能宽慰我的人，
正是能在这场我畏惧的恶战中，
把哈特穆特从瓦特手里救出的人。」

===Original===
Then asked the well-bred warrior
 who from the Sealands came:
 “Tell me, worthy maiden,
 what may be your name?”

===Chinese===
随后那位教养有素的武士——
他自西兰而来——问道：
「告诉我，可敬的少女，
你名唤什么？」

===Original===
 She said: “Gudrun they call me,
 of Hagen’s blood I own me;
 Altho’ my birth was lofty,
 of late but little love has here been shown me.”

===Chinese===
她说：「人们唤我古德伦，
我自认是哈根（Hagen）的血脉；
虽然我出身高贵，
近来此地却少有人善待于我。」

===Original===
He said: “If you, fair lady,
 my dear Gudrun can be,
 Then faithfully to help you
 gladness will give to me;

===Chinese===
他说：「美丽的女郎，
你若真是我亲爱的古德伦，
那么忠心相助于你，
便是我莫大的欢欣；

===Original===
 For I, in truth, am Herwic;
 you for my own I have chosen,
 And fain am I to show you
 how you from bonds of sorrow I can loosen.”

===Chinese===
因为我实在是赫尔维克；
我已把你选作我的人，
巴不得让你看看，
我如何能把你从忧苦的束缚中解开。」

===Original===
She said: “If you would help me,
 my good and worthy knight,
 I trust that you will grant me
 that what I ask is right:

===Chinese===
她说：「你若愿意帮我，
我善良可敬的骑士，
我相信你会应允我
我这请求并无不义：

===Original===
 To me these lovely maidens
 their prayers are ever making,
 That from the fight with Wâ-te
 some friendly hand will Hartmut soon be taking.”

===Chinese===
这些可爱的少女们
向我日夜祈求不息，
盼有一只友好的手，
早些把哈特穆特从与瓦特的恶战中领走。」

===Original===
“That will I do right gladly,
 dearest lady mine.”
 Then to his men young Herwic
 called above the din:

===Chinese===
「这事我万分乐意去办，
我最亲爱的女郎。」
于是年轻的赫尔维克
在喧声中向部众高呼：

===Original===
 “Now against old Wâ-te
 let my flag be carried.”
 Herwic then pressed forward,
 and none of all his men behind him tarried.

===Chinese===
「现在把我的大旗
朝着老瓦特擎去。」
赫尔维克随即向前突进，
他的部众无一落后。

===Original===
To do the lady’s bidding
 hard it was for him;
 But Herwic called out loudly
 to Wâ-te old and grim,

===Chinese===
要依从那位女郎的吩咐，
对他来说何其艰难；
但赫尔维克还是高声
呼喊那老迈而狰狞的瓦特，

===Original===
 And said, “My dear friend Wâ-te,
 to grant my wish be ready:
 Let strife be ended quickly:
 this is the prayer of many a lovely lady.”

===Chinese===
说道：「我亲爱的朋友瓦特，
请准备好答应我的心愿：
快让这场争斗结束吧：
这是许多可爱女郎的祈愿。」

===Original===
Then spake in wrath old Wâ-te:
 “Sir Herwic, get you gone!
 Did I mind the will of a woman,
 how should I do my own?

===Chinese===
老瓦特怒声说道：
「赫尔维克爵爷，滚开！
我若事事顺从妇人的心意，
还怎么遂我自己的心意？

===Original===
 If I thought to spare the foeman,
 unasked I’d do it even.
 I will not do your bidding:
 Hartmut to pay for his sins must now be driven.”

===Chinese===
我若想饶那仇敌一命，
不等开口我自会饶他。
我不会听你的吩咐：
哈特穆特如今必须偿还他的罪愆。」

===Original===
Herwic, for love of his lady,
 on both the fighters sprang
 Right fearlessly and boldly;
 loud the sword-blades rang.

===Chinese===
赫尔维克为了他的女郎，
朝那两个酣战的人
全无惧色、大胆地扑去；
剑锋迸出大响。

===Original===
 Wâ-te was wild with anger,
 and bitter pain it gave him
 That, ere the foeman yielded,
 Herwic from his hand should dare to save him.

===Chinese===
瓦特怒不可遏，
心中苦痛万分：
仇敌尚未降服，
赫尔维克竟敢从他手里把人救走。

===Original===
Then he smote King Herwic
 a strong and heavy blow,
 Ere he could part the fighters,
 and quickly laid him low;

===Chinese===
他朝赫尔维克王劈下
又重又狠的一击——
抢在他拆开两个战者之前，
把他一下子打倒在地；

===Original===
 Now rushed the men of Herwic,
 and did from Wâ-te bear him.
 Hartmut was seized and taken,
 though Herwic and his knights had sought to spare him.

===Chinese===
赫尔维克的部众随即涌上，
把他从瓦特手里抢了回来。
哈特穆特遭擒被俘，
尽管赫尔维克和他的骑士们本想饶他性命。

## Tale XXIX How Hartmut Was Taken Prisoner / 第二十九诗章 哈特穆特如何被俘

===Original===
The Hegelings obtain possession of the castle, and spare neither old nor young. Ortrun seeks refuge with Gudrun, and Gerlind also implores her protection, but she is recognized and killed by Wâ-te. The Hegeling army devastate the land: Hartmut and Ortrun are held captive.
===Chinese===
@@@黑格林人（Hegeling）攻占了城堡，老幼一概不留。奥特龙（Ortrun）投奔古德伦（Gudrun）寻求庇护，格尔林德（Gerlind）也哀求她的保护，却被认了出来，死于瓦特（Wâ-te）之手。黑格林大军蹂躏全境：哈特穆特（Hartmut）与奥特龙沦为囚虏。

===Original===
Wâ-te loud was storming;
 then went he towards the hall
 That stood before the gateway.
 On every side did fall
===Chinese===
瓦特高声怒喝，
随即朝那座殿堂走去，
殿堂就矗立在大门之前。
只听得四面八方

===Original===
 The din of sword-blades clashing,
 of groaning and of weeping.
 Hartmut was in bondage;
 ill luck alone his liegemen, too, were reaping.
===Chinese===
剑锋相击的铿锵，
还有呻吟与哭泣。
哈特穆特已沦为囚虏；
他的封臣们同样只落得厄运临头。

===Original===
With him were also taken
 eighty warriors brave;
 The others all were slaughtered.
 Hartmut his life did save,
===Chinese===
与他一同被俘的
还有八十名勇武的武士；
其余的人全遭屠戮。
哈特穆特保住了性命，

===Original===
 But to a ship was carried,
 and fast and long they kept him.
 Not yet was sorrow ended;
 greater ills must they know who now bewept him.
===Chinese===
却被押上了一艘船，
被他们紧紧囚禁了许久。
悲哀还没有到头；
如今为他哭泣的人们，还须领受更大的不幸。

===Original===
Though often from the stronghold
 Wâ-te’s men they drove,
 Both with slings and arrows,
 yet still he grimly strove,
===Chinese===
尽管守军一次次
凭投石与箭矢
把瓦特的部众逐出堡垒，
他却仍旧狰狞地苦战不休，

===Original===
 And won from them the castle.
 The heavy bolts were broken
 That once the gates had fastened;
 at this fair women wept, with fear outspoken.
===Chinese===
终于从他们手里夺下城堡。
那曾经闩牢城门的
粗重门闩都被砸断；
美人们为此哭泣，毫不掩饰心中的恐惧。

===Original===
Horant, the lord of Denmark,
 Queen Hilda’s flag now bore;
 Him followed many warriors,
 he might not wish for more.
===Chinese===
丹麦之主霍兰特（Horant）
此刻擎着希尔达（Hilda）王后的旗帜；
多少武士追随于他，
多到他再无所求。

===Original===
 Up to a palace tower
 that high its walls was rearing,
 Far above all others,
 the Hegeling men the banner soon were bearing.
===Chinese===
他们直上一座宫塔，
塔垣高高拔起，
远远高过其余诸楼，
黑格林人不久便把旗帜插上了那里。

===Original===
As I have told already,
 the castle now was won:
 To those they found within it
 grimmest deeds were done.
===Chinese===
正如我已经讲过的，
城堡如今已被攻下：
对堡内发现的人，
最残酷的手段都使尽了。

===Original===
 Great was the crowd on-pressing,
 for booty to enrich them.
 Then cried the stern old Wâ-te:
 “Where are now the sacks, and youths to fetch them?”
===Chinese===
人群蜂拥向前，
只想掳获财物来充实自己。
严峻的老瓦特随即喊道：
「麻袋都在哪里？还有搬麻袋的年轻人在哪里？」

===Original===
Now was broken open
 many a well-filled room;
 Loud was the din and uproar
 that from within did come:
===Chinese===
多少塞得满满的库房
此刻都被砸开；
从里面传出的
喧嚣嘈杂震耳欲聋：

===Original===
 But all were not like-minded
 who the halls were thronging;
 While wounds were dealt by many,
 others for plunder searched, for riches longing.
===Chinese===
但挤满殿堂的人
心思并不都一样；
有的挥刃连连砍杀，
有的搜刮劫掠，一心渴求财货。

===Original===
They bore so much from the castle,
 as we have heard it told,
 That such a heavy burden
 two ships could never hold:
===Chinese===
据我们听人传讲，
他们从城堡里搬出的东西
竟沉重得两艘船
也万万载不下：

===Original===
 Richest silken clothing,
 silver and gold, were taken,
 To load the ships on the waters;
 though much they took, yet much must be forsaken.
===Chinese===
最华贵的绫罗绸缎、
白银与黄金都被取走，
装上海面上的船只；
虽拿走了许多，还有许多只得割舍。

===Original===
Now within the castle
 joy was all unknown.
 To all the folk there gathered
 the greatest wrongs were done;
===Chinese===
此刻在城堡之内，
欢欣已无人知晓。
对聚在那里的所有人，
最深的暴行都施加了；

===Original===
 Men alike and women
 were slain who there were dwelling:
 To children in their cradles,
 even to them, the foemen death were dealing.
===Chinese===
居住在那里的男人
和女人一样惨遭杀戮：
连摇篮里的孩童，
仇敌们也不放过，同样赐以死亡。

===Original===
Irold then to Wâ-te
 thus his mind made known:
 “Of harm to you these children
 devil-a-bit have done.
===Chinese===
伊罗尔德（Irold）随即向瓦特
这样表明心迹：
「这些孩童对您
没有半分伤害。

===Original===
 They indeed are blameless,
 nor hate to our kin were showing;
 For the love of God, I beg you,
 spare the poor babes, some pity now bestowing.”
===Chinese===
他们实在清白无辜，
也从未对我们的亲族心怀仇怨；
看在上帝份上，我求您，
饶过这些可怜的婴孩，施舍一点怜悯吧。」

===Original===
The aged Wâ-te answered:
 “Thou hast the mind of a child;
 Though now in the cradle wailing,
 say, wouldst thou have willed
===Chinese===
年迈的瓦特答道：
「你怀的是一颗孩童的心；
如今他们还在摇篮里啼哭，
可你说，难道你竟愿意

===Original===
 That I should leave them living?
 As soon as they are older,
 They never can be trusted;
 to trust a Saxon wild would be no bolder.”
===Chinese===
我留下他们的性命？
等他们长大一些，
就再也不能信任了；
信任一个野蛮的撒克逊（Saxon）人，也不会比这更大胆。」

===Original===
Blood throughout the castle
 flowed on every side.
 Those who saw the slaughter,
 how bitterly they cried!
===Chinese===
鲜血在城堡里
四处流淌。
亲眼目睹这场屠杀的人，
哭得何等悲伤！

===Original===
 Now the high-born Ortrun,
 filled with care and sorrow,
 Sought Gudrun, kind maiden:
 she feared yet greater wrongs before the morrow.
===Chinese===
这时出身高贵的奥特龙
满怀忧惧与哀愁，
去寻仁善的少女古德伦：
她唯恐天明之前还要遭受更大的祸殃。

===Original===
Then, her head low bending
 before the lovely maid,
 She said: “Gudrun, my lady,
 have pity on my need,
===Chinese===
于是她在可爱的少女面前
深深地低下头，
说道：「古德伦，我的夫人，
可怜可怜我的危难吧，

===Original===
 And, in my sharpest sorrow,
 leave me not forsaken;
 I trust me to your kindness,
 or else my life will by your friends be taken.”
===Chinese===
在我最痛切的悲哀里，
别把我抛下不管；
我把自己托付给您的仁慈，
不然我的性命就要丧在您朋友们手里了。」

===Original===
“Gladly will I shield you,”
 she answered, “if I can;
 Ever to do you kindness,
 and help you, I am fain.
===Chinese===
「我很乐意庇护你，」
她回答道，「只要我力所能及；
对你行善、
援助你，是我永远情愿的事。

===Original===
 I will gain for you forgiveness;
 no more for life be fearing.
 Your maids and women also
 must stand near me, my care they, too, are sharing.”
===Chinese===
我会为你求来宽恕；
不必再为性命担忧。
你的女仆和妇人们
也须站到我身边来，同受我的照拂。」

===Original===
“This doth make me happy,”
 the youthful Ortrun said.
 With three and thirty maidens,
 she was kindly kept and fed;
===Chinese===
「这真教我欢喜，」
年轻的奥特龙说道。
她与三十三名少女一起，
受到善意的看护与供养；

===Original===
 Warriors two and sixty
 there the ladies guarded:
 If they should gain their freedom,
 their keepers would be slain, and thus rewarded.
===Chinese===
六十二名武士
在那里护卫着这些贵女：
她们若想脱身得自由，
看守者便要被杀，以此领受报偿。

===Original===
The old and wicked Gerlind
 ran to Gudrun in haste;
 As if she were her bondwoman,
 herself at her feet she cast,
===Chinese===
年迈而狠毒的格尔林德
急急奔到古德伦跟前；
她仿佛成了古德伦的女奴一般，
一下子扑倒在她脚边，

===Original===
 Saying: “Most high-born lady,
 thou alone canst save us
 From Wâ-te and his followers;
 else will his wrath, I ween, of life bereave us.”
===Chinese===
说道：「最尊贵的夫人啊，
唯有您能救我们，
救我们脱离瓦特和他的部众；
不然他的怒火，我看，定要夺去我们的性命。」

===Original===
To her said Hilda’s daughter:
 “I hear you asking now
 That I to you be friendly;
 how should I kindness show?
===Chinese===
希尔达的女儿对她说：
「我听你此刻恳求
我对你友好相待；
可我怎能对你施以善意？

===Original===
 Nought that e’er I wished for
 to grant me were you willing:
 To me you showed but hatred;
 and now my heart with hate for you is swelling.”
===Chinese===
凡是我曾经盼望的，
你们一概不肯赐予；
你们对我只有仇恨；
如今我心里对你的恨意正翻涌不息。」

===Original===
That Ortrun then was near him
 Wâ-te became aware:
 He his teeth was gnashing,
 and straight upstood he there;
===Chinese===
奥特龙正在他近旁，
瓦特察觉了这事：
他咬得牙齿格格作响，
霍地挺直身子站起；

===Original===
 Now his eyes were flashing;
 his yard-wide beard was flowing;
 And all were sorely frightened,
 and feared what the Sturmisch lord would next be doing.
===Chinese===
此刻他双目喷出火光；
尺宽的胡须四下披散；
众人都吓得不轻，
唯恐那位施图尔姆兰（Sturmisch）之主接下来还要干出什么。

===Original===
Over him blood was streaming,
 with it his clothes were wet.
 Though Gudrun was glad to see him,
 she had liked it better yet
===Chinese===
鲜血在他周身
不断涌流，衣裳尽被浸湿。
古德伦见到他固然欢喜，
但她更愿

===Original===
 If he, in mood less wrathful,
 had come for her to greet him;
 Such fear they all were feeling,
 I ween that no one there was glad to meet him.
===Chinese===
他怀着不那么暴怒的心绪
前来受她的问候；
众人心里都害怕成那样，
我想那里没有一个人乐意见到他。

===Original===
To meet her friend, old Wâ-te,
 went Gudrun alone;
 Then said Hilda’s daughter,
 with sad and care-fraught tone:
===Chinese===
古德伦独自上前，
去迎接她的老友瓦特；
希尔达的女儿随即开口，
语调里满是忧伤与忧惧：

===Original===
 “Welcome art thou, Wâ-te!
 How glad would be my greeting,
 If now these folk so many
 no evil from thy hand should here be meeting.”
===Chinese===
「欢迎你，瓦特！
我的问候本该多么欢欣，
倘若此刻这许多人们
不会遭你毒手、在此蒙受祸殃。」

===Original===
“I thank you, fair young maiden!
 Are you Queen Hilda’s child?
 Who are these many women,
 whom here you seek to shield?”
===Chinese===
「多谢你，美丽年轻的少女！
你便是希尔达王后的孩子吗？
这许多妇人都是什么人，
你要在此庇护她们？」

===Original===
 “This,” said Gudrun, in answer,
 “is Ortrun, high in breeding;
 I beg you, Wâ-te, spare her:
 her women here your wrath are sorely dreading.
===Chinese===
「这一位，」古德伦回答道，
「是奥特龙，门第高贵；
我求你，瓦特，饶过她：
她的女仆们在此对你的怒火怕得厉害。

===Original===
“Those there are wretched maidens,
 from far across the sea,
 Brought from the Hegeling kingdom
 by Ludwig’s men with me.
===Chinese===
「那边那些是不幸的少女，
来自遥远的海外，
同我一起被路德维希（Ludwig）的人
从黑格林人的王国掳来。

===Original===
 But you are wet and bloody;
 do not come so near us:
 For all your help we thank you,
 nor in our woe do scorn the love you bear us.”
===Chinese===
可你浑身是水又是血；
不要走得离我们这么近：
我们感谢你的一切援手，
身处苦难也绝不轻看你对我们的爱心。」

===Original===
Wâ-te went on further,
 and Herwic soon he found,
 And with him youthful Ortwin,
 as king in Ortland owned.
===Chinese===
瓦特继续向前，
不久便找到了赫尔维克（Herwic），
年轻的奥特温（Ortwin）也在他身旁，
他是奥特兰（Ortland）公认的君王。

===Original===
 Irold was there and Morunc;
 Fru-te had thither hasted:
 None of these were idle;
 many they slew, nor soon from slaughter rested.
===Chinese===
伊罗尔德和莫伦克（Morunc）都在那里；
弗鲁特（Fru-te）也匆匆赶到：
这些人没有一个闲着；
他们诛杀多人，许久不曾停下屠戮。

===Original===
Quickly then came Hergart,
 the lady of a duke,
 And said: “Gudrun, good lady,
 on me with kindness look﻿—
===Chinese===
赫加特（Hergart）随即匆匆赶来，
她已是一位公爵的夫人，
说道：「古德伦，好夫人，
求你垂顾我，待我以仁慈——

===Original===
 On me, a wretched woman.
 Forget not that we ever
 Have been and are your handmaids;
 and let me, lady, lose thy friendship never.”
===Chinese===
顾念我这个不幸的妇人。
别忘了我们从前、如今
一直是你手下的侍女；
夫人啊，千万别让我失去你的情谊。」

===Original===
Gudrun in anger answered:
 “Stand back, come not so near!
 Whatever we poor maidens
 of wrong have had to fear,
===Chinese===
古德伦怒气冲冲地答道：
「退后，别靠这么近！
我们这些可怜的少女
无论遭受怎样的欺凌，

===Original===
 For all you wept but little,
 and cared for it but slightly.
 Not much do I care either
 whether for you it now goes ill or rightly.
===Chinese===
你们可曾为此流过几滴泪，
可曾把这事放在心上。
如今我也不大在乎
你的境遇是凶是吉。

===Original===
“You still among my maidens
 may linger, if you choose.”
 Now the stern old Wâ-te
 looked round among his foes,
===Chinese===
「你若愿意，仍可以
留在我的侍女中间。」
这时严峻的老瓦特
在仇敌中间环视，

===Original===
 To find the wicked Gerlind,
 whom he in wrath was seeking.
 That devilish crone, with her women,
 the kindness of Gudrun was now bespeaking.
===Chinese===
要找出狠毒的格尔林德，
他正满怀怒火寻她。
那魔鬼般的老妪带着她的女仆们，
正向古德伦乞求着善意。

===Original===
Grimly then old Wâ-te
 stood before the hall,
 And said: “Gudrun, my lady,
 send down, with her maidens all,
===Chinese===
老瓦特随即面目狰狞地
站立在大殿之前，
说道：「古德伦，我的夫人，
把那狠毒的老格尔林德连同她全体女仆

===Original===
 The old and wicked Gerlind,
 who made you wash by the water;
 And with her send her kinsmen,
 who in our land so many knights did slaughter.”
===Chinese===
都给我送下来，
就是她逼你在水边洗衣的；
再把她的亲族一并送来，
他们曾在我们的国土上屠戮多少骑士。」

===Original===
The lovely maiden answered:
 “Not one of them is here.”
 Then Wâ-te, in his anger,
 went in and to her came near;
===Chinese===
可爱的少女答道：
「他们一个也不在这里。」
瓦特顿时动了怒，
走进来逼近她跟前；

===Original===
 He said: “Now show me quickly
 the women I am seeking;
 Else shall they, with your maidens,
 all alike in the grave their home be making.”
===Chinese===
他说：「快把我要找的
那些妇人指给我看；
不然她们就要同你的侍女们
一起埋进坟墓里安家。」

===Original===
Wâ-te was sorely angry,
 of this was she aware.
 A wink of her eye then gave him
 a lovely maiden there,
===Chinese===
瓦特怒不可遏，
这她心里明白。
一位可爱的少女
朝他递了个眼色，

===Original===
 And he knew the old she-devil,
 on whom her glance was turning.
 “Tell me,” he said, “Queen Gerlind,
 for other maids to wash are you still yearning?”
===Chinese===
他认出了那老女魔，
少女的目光正落在她身上。
「告诉我，」他说，「格尔林德王后，
你还想叫别的少女替你洗衣吗？」

===Original===
Then by the hand he took her,
 and dragged her thence away;
 The while the wicked Gerlind
 sank down in sore dismay.
===Chinese===
说罢他一把攥住她的手，
把她从那里拖了出去；
狠毒的格尔林德
一路瘫软下去，惶恐万状。

===Original===
 Said Wâ-te, wild to madness:
 “Most lofty queen, I warn you,
 Never again, at your bidding,
 shall my ladies wash for you; they now can scorn you.”
===Chinese===
瓦特狂怒得像失了心智，
说道：「至高无上的王后，我警告你，
再也不会凭你一声吩咐，
就让我家的贵女们为你洗衣了；她们如今尽可以轻贱你。」

===Original===
I ween that when he brought her
 without the palace gate,
 All looked on to witness
 what he would do in his hate.
===Chinese===
我想，当他把她拖出
宫殿大门之外，
众人都围拢来做见证，
看他要怎样泄愤。

===Original===
 Then by the hair he grasped her,
 no one a whit he dreaded,
 His wrath indeed was bitter,
 and at once the evil queen he there beheaded.
===Chinese===
他随即一把揪住她的头发，
对谁都没有半分忌惮，
他的怒火实在酷烈，
手起一刀，当场斩了那邪恶的王后。

===Original===
Loudly shrieked the maidens,
 their fright at this was sore.
 Back again went Wâ-te,
 and said: “Who is there more,
===Chinese===
少女们放声尖叫，
她们被这一幕吓得不轻。
瓦特转身回到殿内，
说道：「还有谁

===Original===
 Who to the queen owns kinship?
 To me you now must show her;
 However high she holds her,
 I yet to earth her head will quickly lower.”
===Chinese===
与这王后沾亲带故？
现在你们必须把她指给我；
不论她自视何等高贵，
我也要很快叫她的头颅点地。」

===Original===
Sobbing then and weeping,
 the child of Hettel said:
 “Let these with me find shelter,
 who now to me have fled,
===Chinese===
黑特尔（Hettel）的孩子
抽噎着、淌着泪说道：
「让这些投奔我的人
在我这里得到庇护吧，

===Original===
 To ask of me forgiveness,
 here my love bespeaking.
 This is the well-born Ortrun,
 who with her Norman maids my help is seeking.”
===Chinese===
她们来求我的宽恕，
在这里向我表明敬爱。
这位是出身名门的奥特龙，
她带着她的诺曼侍女们正寻求我的救助。」

===Original===
Those who were forgiven
 she bade stand further back.
 Then, in mood unfriendly,
 the angry Wâ-te spake:
===Chinese===
得了宽恕的那些人，
她吩咐站到更后面去。
随后怒气未消的瓦特
怀着不善的神色开言道：

===Original===
 “Where shall I find young Hergart,
 now of a lord the lady,
 Who here within this kingdom
 to take the love of the king’s great lord was ready?”
===Chinese===
「我到哪里去找年轻的赫加特？
她如今已是一位领主的夫人，
当初在这王国里，
她可曾甘愿承受国王重臣的爱？」

===Original===
None of them would tell him,
 but he to her came near,
 And said: “Were you the owner
 of all this kingdom here,
===Chinese===
没有一个人肯告诉他，
他却径自走到了她跟前，
说道：「就算你拥有
这里的整个王国，

===Original===
 Who could in you be looking
 to see so proud a bearing?
 Ill have you served your lady,
 here in the land where you her lot were sharing.”
===Chinese===
谁又能从你身上
看出这般骄矜的气派？
你实在亏待了你的女主人，
在这片国土上，你本与她同甘共苦。」

===Original===
Then all cried out together:
 “Let her now go free.”
 But the aged Wâ-te answered:
 “That can never be;
===Chinese===
众人随即齐声呼喊：
「这就放她去吧。」
年迈的瓦特却回答道：
「那绝无可能；

===Original===
 I have the care of the women;
 behold my overseeing!”
 With a stroke he her beheaded,
 while the maids in fright behind Gudrun were fleeing.
===Chinese===
照管这些妇人的是我；
瞧瞧我是怎么管教的！」
他手起一刀，斩下了她的头颅，
吓得众少女纷纷躲到古德伦身后。

===Original===
Now from the bloody struggle
 there was a rest for all.
 Then the brave King Herwic
 came to Ludwig’s hall,
===Chinese===
这场血腥的鏖战
如今人人都得了片刻歇息。
英勇的赫尔维克王
随即来到路德维希的殿堂，

===Original===
 Leading in his warriors,
 with stains of blood upon them.
 Gudrun her welcome gave him;
 her love for him was shown, and kindness done them.
===Chinese===
率领着他的武士们，
个个身上带着斑斑血渍。
古德伦向他致以欢迎；
她对他的爱意尽显，并厚待了众人。

===Original===
Soon the knightly Herwic
 his sword from his side unbound:
 He then shook off his armor
 into his shield on the ground,
===Chinese===
不久，骑士风度的赫尔维克
解下了腰间的佩剑：
随后他把甲胄卸落
在地面的盾上，

===Original===
 And stood before the ladies;
 iron-stained was his body.
 That day, for love of his lady,
 he oft on the field had hewn a pathway bloody.
===Chinese===
站到贵妇们面前；
身上满是铁器的血污。
那一天，为了他的爱人，
他多少次在战场上砍出一条血路。

===Original===
With him came Ortwin also,
 who was of Ortland king.
 When Irold came with Morunc,
 the clothes they off did fling
===Chinese===
奥特温也随他一同前来，
他已是奥特兰的君王。
待伊罗尔德与莫伦克赶到，
两人把穿在甲胄外的

===Original===
 Worn outside their armor,
 for they were over-heated.
 They wished to see the ladies,
 and hoped by them they would be kindly greeted.
===Chinese===
外袍一把扯下抛掉，
因为浑身燥热难当。
他们想见一见贵妇们，
盼着能受她们亲切的迎接。

===Original===
When now the Danish warriors
 were both with slaughter spent,
 They laid aside their weapons,
 and before the ladies went.
===Chinese===
这时那两位丹麦武士
都已杀得精疲力竭，
他们放下兵刃，
径到贵妇们面前。

===Original===
