 Shields no longer bore they,
 their helmets were unfastened;
 A very loving welcome
 to give to both the knights Gudrun then hastened.
===Chinese===
盾牌不再扛在身上，
头盔也都解了下来；
古德伦赶忙上前，
向两位骑士致以最亲切的欢迎。

===Original===
Irold and Morunc with him
 then most lowly bowed
 Before the lovely maiden.
 How well her bearing showed
===Chinese===
伊罗尔德和随行的莫伦克
随即深深躬身，
拜见那可爱的少女。
她的仪态分明显示出，

===Original===
 That to see these guests so lordly
 she was indeed most willing!
 Right glad and happy truly
 the child of the Hegeling Hilda now was feeling.
===Chinese===
见到这般气派的贵客，
她实在满心欢喜！
黑格林人希尔达的孩子
此刻真是说不出的高兴。

===Original===
Alike they all were thinking,
 both lords and all their men:
 “Since now we have the castle﻿—
 the stronghold Kassiane﻿—
===Chinese===
众人的心思都一样，
主公们连同全体部下：
「如今我们已拿下这座城堡——
卡西安（Kassiane）坚垒——

===Original===
 Of the land are we the masters,
 and everything is ours.”
 Soon bade the aged Wâ-te
 that men should burn with fire the palace with its towers.
===Chinese===
这国土便归我们做主，
一切尽归我们所有。」
年迈的瓦特随即吩咐
众人放火焚烧这座带塔楼的宫殿。

===Original===
The Danish Fru-te answered:
 “That may never be;
 In this my queenly lady
 to live must now be free.
===Chinese===
丹麦的弗鲁特答道：
「那万万不可；
我们王后般的贵女
今后还须自由地住在里面。

===Original===
 Bid that from out the castle
 men the dead shall carry;
 Then ’twill be the better
 for all our knights who in the land shall tarry.
===Chinese===
吩咐众人把死者
从城堡里抬走；
这对我们留驻
这片国土的所有骑士都更有益。

===Original===
“Very strong is the castle,
 wide it is and good;
 Bid from the walls now everywhere
 to wash away the blood,
===Chinese===
「这城堡十分坚固，
又宽敞又完好；
吩咐人把城墙各处
的血迹统统洗刷干净，

===Original===
 That for a home the maidens
 may not dislike it wholly:
 Then the land of Hartmut
 we will raid throughout, and see it fully.”
===Chinese===
好教少女们
不致全然嫌弃它作安身之家；
然后我们把哈特穆特的国土
整个劫掠一遍，看个透彻。」

===Original===
They did as Fru-te bade them,
 for wise he was, in truth;
 They bore from out the castle
 many who there, forsooth,
===Chinese===
众人照弗鲁特的吩咐行事，
他实在是个智者；
他们从城堡里
抬出了许多人，

===Original===
 Were sorely slashed and wounded,
 and many who were dying:
 Then to the waves they carried
 those who before the gates in death were lying.
===Chinese===
他们确实被砍得
遍体鳞伤，还有许多奄奄一息：
随后又把那些
横死在城门之前的尸体交付给海浪。

===Original===
They to the sea entrusted
 four thousand of the dead;
 This to them was toilsome,
 but Fru-te thus had bade.
===Chinese===
他们把四千名死者
交付给了大海；
这活计着实辛苦，
但弗鲁特如此吩咐。

===Original===
 The work that they were doing
 not as yet was ended;
 Then in Ludwig’s castle
 the maid Ortrun was held, now ill-befriended.
===Chinese===
他们手头的事情
还没有做完；
这时在路德维希的城堡里，
少女奥特龙仍被看押着，无人照应。

===Original===
Two and sixty warriors
 and thirty maidens fair
 With her were also taken.
 Then said Gudrun: “Forbear!
===Chinese===
六十二名武士
和三十名美丽少女
也同她一起被俘。
古德伦随即说道：「住手！

===Original===
 The maids are in my keeping,
 my word to them I plighted:
 Wâ-te may do as he wishes
 with the knights he seized, until my wrongs are righted.”
===Chinese===
这些少女归我看护，
我已向她们许下诺言：
瓦特尽可以对他擒获的
骑士们随意处置，直到我的冤屈得到昭雪。」

===Original===
Siegfried, king of Moorland,
 found a welcome warm,
 As should to knights be granted
 after the battle-storm.
===Chinese===
摩尔兰（Moorland）之王西格弗里德（Siegfried）
受到了热诚的欢迎，
正如同鏖战风暴过后
骑士们应得的礼遇。

===Original===
 Thanks to that worthy warrior
 were by the ladies given,
 That he from the land of Karadie
 so far had come, and so well for them had striven.
===Chinese===
贵妇们向那位可敬的勇士
道了谢，
谢他从卡拉迪（Karadie）之地
远道而来，又为她们这般尽力奋战。

===Original===
To the care of the Danish Horant
 they their foes did give
 Who in the castle of Kassian
 still were left alive.
===Chinese===
他们把那些在卡西安城堡里
还活着的仇敌
都交给了丹麦的霍兰特看管。
古德伦也被托付给他，

===Original===
 To him was Gudrun entrusted,
 and all her maidens near her:
 To her was he a kinsman;
 they so might hope that he would kindness bear her.
===Chinese===
还有她近旁的全体侍女：
他对她来说是一位亲族；
众人便可以指望
他会善待于她。

===Original===
Him they made the master
 of forty towers strong,
 And six wide, roomy dwellings,
 that stood the shore along.
===Chinese===
他们让他执掌
四十座坚固的塔楼，
还有六座宽敞的宅邸，
它们沿着海岸排开。

===Original===
 Over three rich palaces
 to him was lordship granted,
 And there Gudrun, the maiden,
 with him must stay, and nought she ever wanted.
===Chinese===
三座华美的宫殿
也归他统领，
少女古德伦便须
同他住在那里，想要什么都不缺。

===Original===
To guard their ships on the waters
 others now they bade;
 Then back to castle Kassian
 Hartmut, the knight, was led
===Chinese===
他们又派了旁人
去看守海上的船只；
随后骑士哈特穆特
被押回卡西安城堡，

===Original===
 With many of his kinsmen,
 who in the fight were taken;
 There the Norman ladies,
 seized with the knights, were held, by hope forsaken.
===Chinese===
同行的还有许多亲族，
都是在战阵中被擒的；
那些诺曼贵妇也与骑士们
一同被押在那里，希望尽失。

===Original===
They bade that care be taken
 that none from them might flee;
 A thousand of their brave ones
 must the women oversee:
===Chinese===
他们吩咐严加看管，
一个也不许逃脱；
一千名勇士
负责监督这些妇人：

===Original===
 They, with the men from Denmark,
 kept guard in many places.
 Wâ-te, meanwhile, with Fru-te,
 sought other foes, and shields to hew in pieces.
===Chinese===
他们同丹麦来的人马
在许多地方轮番看守。
这期间，瓦特同弗鲁特
去寻别的仇敌，把一面面盾牌砍碎。

===Original===
Thirty thousand warriors
 with them the war-path shared.
 Fire was thrown on all sides;
 flames now flashed and flared.
===Chinese===
三万名武士
与他们同走征途。
火把四面抛掷；
烈焰阵阵迸闪腾起。

===Original===
 Throughout the land, the dwellings
 everywhere were burning;
 And now the brave young Hartmut,
 sad at heart, his first true woe was learning.
===Chinese===
整片国土上，房屋
处处都在燃烧；
勇敢的年轻哈特穆特
这时才伤了心，初次尝到真正的哀恸。

===Original===
The warriors from Sturmland,
 and they of the Danish land,
 Broke down the well-built castles
 on every hill and strand.
===Chinese===
施图尔姆兰的战士
和丹麦来的战士，
把每座山冈与海滨的
坚固城堡一一捣毁。

===Original===
 They took away more plunder
 than foemen ever carried;
 Many lovely women
 the Hegelings seized, the while the land they harried.
===Chinese===
他们掠走的赃物
比任何敌军搬走的都多；
黑格林人一路蹂躏国土，
还掳走了许多美丽的妇人。

===Original===
Before the friends of Hilda
 came back thro’ the wasted land,
 Six and twenty castles
 fell beneath their hand.
===Chinese===
希尔达的亲友们
穿越满目疮痍的国土归来之前，
已有二十六座城堡
落入了他们手中。

===Original===
 Happy went they homeward;
 proud were they of their raiding;
 Soon of those there taken
 a thousand or more to Hilda they were leading.
===Chinese===
他们欢欢喜喜往回走；
为这一场劫掠颇为自得；
不久便把掳来的
千余名俘虏押去见希尔达。

===Original===
Throughout the Norman kingdom
 was Hilda’s banner seen,
 Waving now unhindered;
 back again her men
===Chinese===
偌大的诺曼人王国里，
处处都望得见希尔达的旗帜，
如今再无阻拦地飘扬；
她的人马又把旗

===Original===
 Bore it down to the sea-sand,
 where they had left their lady.
 Here would they stay no longer;
 to seek their homes they all were glad and ready.
===Chinese===
擎回海边的沙滩，
他们正是把女主人留在那里的。
他们不愿再在此地逗留；
人人都欢欢喜喜，准备踏上归途。

===Original===
Those who still were resting
 within King Hartmut’s halls
 Down to their friends came riding
 from out the castle walls.
===Chinese===
那些还在哈特穆特王
殿堂里歇息的人，
也从城墙之内
驰马下来与伙伴们相会。

===Original===
 Gladly both old and youthful
 now each other greeted;
 Then asked they of Denmark:
 “Youths, what luck in the raid hath you awaited?”
===Chinese===
年老的与年轻的
如今欢欢喜喜互相问候；
随后他们问起丹麦来的人：
「小伙子们，这趟出征你们得了什么彩头？」

===Original===
To them King Ortwin answered:
 “We there have done so well
 That I to those who helped me
 my thanks must ever tell.
===Chinese===
奥特温王回答他们道：
「我们在那里干得漂亮，
凡帮过我的人，
我都须永远称谢。

===Original===
 Our foes are well rewarded,
 though sore has been the fighting,
 For all the wrongs they did us;
 a thousand-fold have we ourselves been righting.”
===Chinese===
我们的仇敌得到了应有的报偿，
虽然这场恶战打得好苦，
但为他们加给我们的
一切欺凌，我们已千倍讨还。」

===Original===
Then spake the aged Wâ-te:
 “Who best can tarry here
 To guard for us this kingdom?
 Bid now Gudrun, the fair,
===Chinese===
年迈的瓦特随即说道：
「谁最适合留在这里，
替我们守卫这片王国？
请美丽的古德伦

===Original===
 Come down again to meet us;
 soon shall we be going
 To Hilda’s land of the Hegelings;
 and what we bring we will to her be showing.”
===Chinese===
现在再下来见我们一面；
我们很快就要动身
回希尔达的黑格林故土；
我们带来的东西，都要一一向她展示。」

===Original===
Then said they all together,
 both the old and young:
 “To Horant and to Morunc
 doth the warder’s task belong;
===Chinese===
于是众人齐声说道，
不分年老与年轻：
「守望者的职责
就归霍兰特与莫伦克；

===Original===
 They, and a thousand with them,
 here in this land must tarry.”
 ’Twas done as they had bidden;
 but those who went did many a hostage carry.
===Chinese===
他们俩连同一千人马
必须留驻这片国土。」
众人之命一一照办；
只是离去的人还带走了许多人质。

===Original===
When to go back to Hegeling
 they now made up their minds,
 Then to their ships they carried
 goods of many kinds,
===Chinese===
当他们下定决心
要回黑格林人的故土去，
便把各色财物搬上船，
当初随身带来的，

===Original===
 All they once brought with them,
 and all they had of plunder.
 Gladly they bore their booty;
 on this their friends at home would look with wonder.
===Chinese===
连同掳来的一切，
全都装了上去。
他们欢欢喜喜载着战利品；
家里的亲友见了，定要啧啧称奇。

===Original===
Hartmut now was bidden
 to leave his father’s hall,
 With all his bravest warriors,
 five hundred men in all;
===Chinese===
哈特穆特此刻奉命
离开他父亲的殿堂，
带着他全部最勇的武士，
一共五百人；

===Original===
 They now were held in bondage
 who had in strife been taken,
 And won from their foes thereafter
 many a weary day, of hope forsaken.
===Chinese===
这些战阵中被擒的人
从此沦为囚徒，
往后还要在仇敌手里
熬过多少失去指望的疲惫岁月。

===Original===
Ortrun took they likewise,
 the fair and high-born maid;
 On her and on her maidens
 a heavy woe they laid:
===Chinese===
他们也带走了奥特龙，
那位美丽而高贵的少女；
她和她的侍女们
从此背上沉重的苦难：

===Original===
 As they away from fatherland
 far from friends were carried,
 They well might know the sorrows
 felt by Gudrun and her maids, who with them tarried.
===Chinese===
她们远离故土、
被押往亲友难及的远方，
也就此懂得了古德伦
和众侍女的愁苦——她们正是同路人。

===Original===
Those whom they had taken
 they bore with them away.
 The castles, overmastered,
 henceforth must own the sway
===Chinese===
掳来的那些人，
都被他们一并带走。
被降服的城堡
从此须听命于

===Original===
 Of Morunc and of Horant:
 when they homeward started,
 They left in the Norman kingdom
 a thousand of their men, all fearless-hearted.
===Chinese===
莫伦克与霍兰特：
大军启程回国之时，
他们在诺曼人王国里
留下了一千名勇士，个个无所畏惧。

===Original===
“Now do I beseech you,”
 to them young Hartmut spake,
 “That in my father’s kingdom
 my freedom I may take;
===Chinese===
「如今我恳求你们，」
年轻的哈特穆特对他们说道，
「容我在我父亲的王国里
恢复我的自由；

===Original===
 If this to me be granted,
 I pledge my life and riches.”
 The aged Wâ-te answered:
 “Now in our hands to keep you wisdom teaches.
===Chinese===
倘若肯把这恩典赐我，
我便以性命和财富作保。」
年迈的瓦特答道：
「留你在我们手里，方是明智之道。

===Original===
“Why it is I know not,
 that ’tis my nephew’s will
 To carry home young Hartmut,
 who him would gladly kill,
===Chinese===
「这是为什么我不知道，
我那侄儿竟执意
要把年轻的哈特穆特带回家去，
此人可巴不得宰了他，

===Original===
 And take from him his riches.
 Even before the morrow,
 Were only my nephew willing,
 I would see that his foe no more in bonds should sorrow.”
===Chinese===
还要夺走他的财富。
只消我那侄儿肯点头，
不等明天到来，
我保管教他的仇人不再在枷锁里受苦。」

===Original===
Then spake the youthful Ortwin:
 “What gain to us would come
 If we should slay our foemen
 here in their land and home?
===Chinese===
年轻的奥特温随即说道：
「若我们在这里、在他们的
国土家园上把仇敌杀掉，
对我们又有什么益处？

===Original===
 Hartmut and his kindred
 may better things be hoping;
 Them will I bring to my mother,
 as well beseems a knight to wrong ne’er stooping.”
===Chinese===
哈特穆特和他的亲族
还可以指望更好的结局；
我要把他们带到我母亲面前，
这也合乎骑士身份，永不屈尊行不义。」

===Original===
All their goods and riches
 down to the ships were brought;
 With gold and gems and clothing,
 and horses they were fraught.
===Chinese===
他们的全部财物
都被搬上了船；
船上载满黄金宝石、
衣料和骏马。

===Original===
 Her whom they had sought for
 they were homeward bringing:
 They who once went mourning
 now on their way were heard in gladness singing.
===Chinese===
他们当年苦苦寻访的她，
此刻正被载着还乡：
当初含哀而去的人们，
如今一路上都唱着欢歌。

## Tale XXX How Gudrun Was Brought Home to Hilda / 第三十诗章 古德伦如何被迎回希尔达家中

===Original===
The Hegeling army returns home, bringing Gudrun and her maidens, with the Norman captives. Hilda welcomes the returning ships, and embraces her daughter, whom she does not at first recognize, after her long absence. By Gudrun’s request, Hilda is reconciled to Hartmut and Ortrun, and Ortwin takes Ortrun for his bride. Hartmut and his men are set free, on their promise not to escape, and Hartmut agrees to wed Hildeburg. Herwic’s sister is sent for, and betrothed to Siegfried.

===Chinese===
黑格林人（Hegeling）大军带着古德伦（Gudrun）和她的众少女，连同诺曼人俘虏一起归返家园。希尔达（Hilda）迎接归来的船队，与久别多年、一时未能认出的女儿相拥。应古德伦的请求，希尔达与哈特穆特（Hartmut）、奥特龙（Ortrun）和解，奥特温（Ortwin）娶奥特龙为妻。哈特穆特和他的部众在承诺不逃走之后获释，哈特穆特同意迎娶希尔德堡（Hildeburg）。赫尔维克（Herwic）的妹妹被接来，与西格弗里德（Siegfried）订了婚。

===Original===
Homeward the men of the Hegelings
 gladly took their way;
 But many whom they carried
 erewhile across the sea

===Chinese===
黑格林人的大军
欣然踏上了归途；
但他们先前渡海
带在身边的许多人，

===Original===
 Now lay dead and wounded;
 these must they be leaving:
 Three thousand men or over
 were mourned by friends, who tears to each were giving.

===Chinese===
如今已死伤枕藉，
这些人他们只得抛下：
三千上下的战士，
亲友们含泪一一为他们哀悼。

===Original===
Now their ships went smoothly,
 the winds for them were good:
 Bearing home their booty
 they came in happy mood.

===Chinese===
如今他们的船行得顺畅，
一路上风对他们也甚是相帮：
载着掳获的战利品归家，
他们个个心情欢畅。

===Original===
 How it was done I know not,
 they sent on men before them
 Unto the Hegeling ladies:
 of what had them befallen they tidings bore them.

===Chinese===
个中情形我不得而知，
他们先遣了人手
赶往黑格林人的女眷那里，
把这场经历的音讯带给她们。

===Original===
With all their speed they hastened﻿—
 that I well can say﻿—
 And reached at last their kingdom﻿—
 I cannot tell the day.

===Chinese===
他们竭尽全力赶路——
这一点我言之凿凿——
终于抵达了他们的王国——
具体是哪一天我却说不上。

===Original===
 Never a tale so happy
 had Lady Hilda gladdened
 As this that now they told her:
 Ludwig was slain, who long her life had saddened.

===Chinese===
从没有一则音讯
曾让希尔达夫人这般欢欣，
像他们此刻讲给她听的这一则：
路德维希（Ludwig）被诛杀了，他曾长久黯淡她的生涯。

===Original===
She asked: “Still lives my daughter,
 and all her maiden band?”
 They answered: “Herwic brings her,
 his own, again to her land.

===Chinese===
她问道：「我的女儿还活着么，
还有她那群少女可都安在？」
他们答道：「赫尔维克正带着她，
带着他的人儿，重返她的故土。

===Original===
 Ne’er to so brave a warrior
 it hath befallen better.
 Ortrun, too, they are bringing,
 and Hartmut, her brother; these in bonds they fetter.”

===Chinese===
如此勇敢的一位武士，
从未得过比这更好的际遇。
他们还带来了奥特龙，
和她的兄弟哈特穆特；这两人被缚在锁链之中。」

===Original===
“A happy tale you bring me,”
 said then the well-bred queen;
 “My life with care and sadness
 by them hath cumbered been.

===Chinese===
「你们给我带来了一则喜讯，」
贤淑的王后随即说道；
「我的生命被忧烦与哀伤
久久地因他们而窒塞。

===Original===
 If e’er my eyes behold them,
 ill shall they be faring:
 Through them have I much sorrow,
 untold and openly, for years been bearing.

===Chinese===
倘若我的双眼再见到他们，
他们必没有好下场：
因着他们，我多年来承受了
说不尽也藏不住的悲伤。

===Original===
“The news that you have brought me
 a rich reward shall gain;
 For you my heart have lightened
 of hopeless woe and pain.

===Chinese===
「你们带来的这则音讯
必得丰厚的酬赏；
因为你们让我的心
卸下了无望的悲苦与哀伤。

===Original===
 Gold I give you freely,
 and this I do most rightly.”
 They said: “Most noble lady,
 to make us rich we need your gold but slightly.

===Chinese===
我要慷慨地赠你们黄金，
这样做也最为合宜。」
他们说道：「最尊贵的夫人，
要使我们富有，您的黄金其实用不着几分。

===Original===
“Of the booty we have gotten
 we’re bringing home so much,
 You need not think us scornful
 if your gold we do not touch:

===Chinese===
「凭我们掳获的战利品，
我们带回家的已足够多，
您不必以为我们是轻慢，
才不去碰您的黄金：

===Original===
 Indeed, our boats are heavy
 with shining gold they’re bearing.
 Over all our riches
 keepers we have, who well for it are caring.”

===Chinese===
说实在的，我们的船已沉重，
载满了灿灿发光的黄金。
我们全部的财货
都有专人看管，照看得十分上心。」

===Original===
Then did Lady Hilda,
 when she the tidings heard
 That guests so dear were coming,
 for food and drink give word;

===Chinese===
于是希尔达夫人，
一听到这音讯——
如此可亲的贵客将临——
便吩咐备下饮食；

===Original===
 For stools and benches, also,
 on which they should be seated.
 She of all was thoughtful,
 that they might feel that they were fitly greeted.

===Chinese===
还有凳子与长凳，
好让他们落座安身。
她对一切都想得周到，
要让来客觉得受到了得体的迎接。

===Original===
Now at Matelan castle
 none were idle found;
 Down on the sandy beaches
 and on the level ground

===Chinese===
此刻在马特兰（Matelan）城堡，
找不出一个闲人；
在沙滩上，
在平坦的场地上，

===Original===
 Workmen quickly gathered,
 who nought of toil abated
 That fair Gudrun and Herwic,
 as them beseemed, should worthily be seated.

===Chinese===
工匠们火速聚集，
没有一人惮于辛劳，
好让美丽的古德伦与赫尔维克
得到与身份相称的体面座席。

===Original===
I cannot tell you truly
 if aught upon the sea
 Of ill had them befallen.
 Six long weeks it must be

===Chinese===
我无法真切地告诉你们，
海上可曾有什么
灾厄降临他们头上。
想必足足有六个星期

===Original===
 Ere Ortwin’s men saw Matelan
 at length before them looming.
 They brought with them their lady,
 and many well-bred maids with her were coming.

===Chinese===
奥特温的部众才终于望见
马特兰在眼前渐渐显现。
他们带回了他们的女主人，
同来的还有许多淑女伴在她身边。

===Original===
When now they reached their homeland,
 this for truth we hear,
 Their search and strife for the lady
 had lasted full a year:

===Chinese===
如今他们抵达了家园，
我们听到的实情是：
他们为这位女子的追寻与争战
已足足延续了一年：

===Original===
 It was upon a May-time
 their foes they home were bringing.
 Their toils were not forgotten,
 but, as they came, the strand with shouts was ringing.

===Chinese===
正是在五月时节，
他们把仇敌带回了家乡。
他们的辛劳没有被遗忘，
归来之时，海滩上一片欢呼震响。

===Original===
Soon as Matelan castle
 now from the ships was seen,
 Of sackbuts and of trumpets
 loud began the din,

===Chinese===
马特兰城堡
刚从船上一望得见，
弯号和小号
便齐声轰然作响，

===Original===
 Of horns as well as fluting,
 and drums that men were beating.
 The ships of the aged Wâ-te
 at last in a harbor good their rest were meeting.

===Chinese===
还有号角与笛声，
以及人们擂动的鼓声。
年迈的瓦特（Wâ-te）的船队
终于在一处良港得到安顿。

===Original===
After these came also
 Ortland’s warriors brave;
 Then Hilda with her ladies
 to them a welcome gave.

===Chinese===
随后驶来的还有
奥特兰（Ortland）勇敢的武士；
希尔达随即带着她的女眷们，
向他们表示欢迎。

===Original===
 Out from Matelan’s castle
 she to the shore went riding;
 Gudrun they saw was coming,
 with well-bred maidens wont to do her bidding.

===Chinese===
她从马特兰城堡
策马来到海岸；
众人望见古德伦来了，
身边跟着惯于听她差遣的淑女们。

===Original===
Alighted from their horses,
 and standing on the sand,
 Were Hilda and all her ladies.
 Then, leading by the hand

===Chinese===
希尔达和她的全体女眷
下了马，
伫立在沙滩上。
这时，伊罗尔德（Irold）牵着手

===Original===
 Gudrun, the lovely maiden,
 came Irold, proud and knightly.
 Though Hilda well had known her,
 yet now she knew her not, nor others rightly.

===Chinese===
领着美丽的少女古德伦，
骄傲而英武地走来。
尽管希尔达本该熟识她，
此刻却认不出她，旁人也一样认不出来。

===Original===
Hilda, among the followers
 a hundred women saw;
 She said: “I know not truly
 which one from me should draw

===Chinese===
希尔达在随行的人群中
看到上百名女子；
她说：「我实在分辨不出
哪一个该从我这里领受

===Original===
 A mother’s loving welcome;
 unknown to me is my daughter:
 I give to all my greeting
 who here with her have come across the water.”

===Chinese===
一位母亲的慈爱欢迎；
我的女儿于我已是陌生：
我向所有随她渡海
来到这里的女子致意。」

===Original===
“This is your long-lost daughter,”
 by Irold she was told;
 Hilda to her stepped nearer.
 Could ever wealth or gold

===Chinese===
「这就是你失散多年的女儿，」
伊罗尔德对她说道；
希尔达朝她走近了几步。
世上可有什么财富或黄金

===Original===
 Outweigh the bliss that filled them,
 as each the other greeted,
 And welcome gave with kisses?
 Now from their hearts had all their sorrow fleeted.

===Chinese===
能胜过此刻充盈她们的至福——
母女彼此相见，
以亲吻相迎？
而今，一切愁苦都从她们心中消散了。

===Original===
To Irold and his kinsmen
 kind greeting Hilda said;
 Then to the aged Wâ-te
 a lowly bow she made.

===Chinese===
希尔达向伊罗尔德和他的亲族
致以亲切的问候；
又向年迈的瓦特
深深地躬身行礼。

===Original===
 “Welcome, knight of Sturmland!
 bravely thou hast striven!
 Who can e’er reward thee,
 unless to thee both land and crown are given?”

===Chinese===
「欢迎你，施图尔姆兰（Sturmland）的骑士！
你奋战得何等英勇！
谁能报答得了你，
除非把国土与王冠一并相赠？」

===Original===
He to the lady answered:
 “To help you all I may,
 For that am I most willing,
 e’en to my latest day.”

===Chinese===
他回答那位夫人道：
「为您效力我无有不能，
对此我心甘情愿，
直到我生命的最后一天。」

===Original===
 Then, for love, she kissed him,
 and Ortwin thus she greeted.
 Now came Herwic also,
 with proud and worthy knights, as him befitted.

===Chinese===
出于慈爱，她亲吻了他，
又这样向奥特温致意。
赫尔维克也来了，
带着高傲而高贵的骑士，正与他的身份相称。

===Original===
Ortrun, the Norman maiden,
 then by the hand he led.
 Gudrun besought her mother
 kindly to meet the maid:

===Chinese===
诺曼人少女奥特龙，
这时被他牵着手领来。
古德伦恳求母亲
亲切地接待这位少女：

===Original===
 “Dear lady, greet with kisses
 this good and high-born maiden;
 Oft in my years of sorrow
 my life with help and kindness she did gladden.”

===Chinese===
「亲爱的母亲，请以亲吻
欢迎这位善良的出身高贵的少女；
在我多年的愁苦岁月里，
她常以援手和善意点亮我的生命。」

===Original===
“To none will I give kisses
 who is to me unknown.
 Who are this maiden’s kinsmen?
 What name doth the lady own,

===Chinese===
「我不会把亲吻给与
任何一个我不认识的人。
这位少女的亲族是谁？
这位夫人姓甚名谁，

===Original===
 That you should bid me kiss her,
 and be so friendly with her?”
 He said: “Her name is Ortrun;
 she from the Norman kingdom cometh hither.”

===Chinese===
竟要我去吻她，
对她这般亲厚？」
他说道：「她的名字是奥特龙；
她从诺曼人王国来到此地。」

===Original===
“Never shall I kiss her;
 how can you ask for this?
 If I should bid them kill her
 I should not do amiss.

===Chinese===
「我绝不吻她；
你怎么能提出这种要求？
就算我吩咐他们杀了她，
我也不算做错。

===Original===
 Truly have her kinsmen
 filled my life with sorrow;
 They fed their eyes upon it,
 and gladness all the while from this did borrow.”

===Chinese===
她的亲族着实
让我的生命盛满悲苦；
他们看在眼里津津有味，
还始终以此为乐。」

===Original===
Gudrun to Hilda answered:
 “Ne’er hath this lovely maid
 The word to any given
 that wrong on you be laid.

===Chinese===
古德伦回答希尔达道：
「这位可爱的少女
从未对任何人说过
要加害于您的话。

===Original===
 Bethink you now, dear mother,
 would blame to me be owing
 Should our men slay her kinsmen?
 To the luckless maid, I beg, your love be showing.”

===Chinese===
请您想想，亲爱的母亲，
倘若我们的武士杀了她的亲族，
这罪责岂不要落在我头上？
我求您把慈爱施与这不幸的少女。」

===Original===
Gudrun in vain besought her,
 until at last, with tears,
 The maid now begged her mother;
 then gave she willing ears,

===Chinese===
古德伦苦苦恳求，终属徒劳，
直到最后，这少女
含泪哀求她的母亲；
她这才愿意倾听，

===Original===
 And said: “I can no longer
 see you sadly weeping:
 If e’er the maiden helped you,
 for this shall she, in my land, her life be keeping.”

===Chinese===
说道：「我不忍再看着
你这样伤心地哭泣：
倘若这位少女曾帮助过你，
凭这一点，她将在我国土上保全性命。」

===Original===
Then the stately Hilda
 kissed King Ludwig’s child,
 And greeted other ladies,
 e’en as Gudrun had willed.

===Chinese===
于是仪态雍容的希尔达
亲吻了路德维希王的女儿，
又向其他女子致意，
全照着古德伦的心愿。

===Original===
 Then came also Hildeburg,
 from far-off lands brought thither,
 Erst with her found washing.
 Now, by the hand, Sir Fru-te led her hither.

===Chinese===
希尔德堡也来了，
她从遥远的国度被带到此处，
先前人们发现她在洗衣；
如今，弗鲁特（Fru-te）大人牵着她来到这里。

===Original===
Then Gudrun said further:
 “Mother, most dear to me,
 Your greeting give to Hildeburg.
 What better can there be

===Chinese===
古德伦又进一步说道：
「母亲，我最亲爱的人，
请把您的问候给与希尔德堡。
世上还有什么

===Original===
 Than true and faithful friendship?
 Gold and jewels even,
 Whate’er the kingdom holdeth,
 to Hildeburg most rightly should be given.”

===Chinese===
比真挚而忠贞的友谊更好？
即便是黄金与宝石，
这王国所拥有的一切，
都理应交给希尔德堡。」

===Original===
Then said to her Queen Hilda:
 “To me it hath been told
 How she both weal and sorrow
 hath borne with you of old.

===Chinese===
于是王后希尔达对她说：
「我已听人讲起
她昔日如何与你
同甘共苦、患难相依。

===Original===
 Never shall I sit happy
 beneath the crown I’m wearing,
 Till I indeed reward her
 for all the ills that she with you was sharing.”

===Chinese===
我戴着头上这顶王冠
也永远不会安享快乐，
直到我真正酬报了她
陪你共历的种种苦难。」

===Original===
At once she kissed the maiden,
 and others, too, as well.
 Then Hilda said to Fru-te:
 “No shame for this I feel﻿—

===Chinese===
她当即亲吻了这位少女，
也亲吻了其他女子。
随后希尔达对弗鲁特说：
「我并不以此为耻——

===Original===
 That I have come to meet you
 and those whom you are leading.
 Good knights, you all are welcome
 into the Hegeling land, now homeward speeding.”

===Chinese===
我亲自前来迎接你
和你率领的众人。
好骑士们，欢迎你们
速速归返黑格林人的国土。」

===Original===
As they with thanks were bowing,
 and she her greeting gave,
 Siegfried, king of Moorland,
 drew nearer on the wave,

===Chinese===
他们正躬身致谢，
她正向他们致意，
摩尔兰（Moorland）之王西格弗里德
乘着波浪驶近，

===Original===
 And with his warriors, shouting,
 up to the beach was springing:
 A merry song from Araby
 were all, as best they might, together singing.

===Chinese===
他的武士们高声呐喊着，
一跃登上海滩：
一首阿拉比（Araby）的欢快歌谣，
众人正各展其能地齐声唱起。

===Original===
Queen Hilda him awaited
 till on the shore he stood.
 Then to the lord of Karadie
 a greeting warm she showed:

===Chinese===
王后希尔达等候着他，
直到他站上岸来。
随后她向卡拉迪（Karadie）之主
致以热忱的问候：

===Original===
 “Sir Siegfried, king of Moorland,
 welcome to you is given;
 It ne’er shall be forgotten
 how you to right my wrongs have ever striven.”

===Chinese===
「西格弗里德爵士，摩尔兰之王，
欢迎你的到来；
你为伸张我的冤屈
始终奋争，这永远不会被遗忘。」

===Original===
“Lady, if I have helped you,
 to do it I was glad.
 Now must I hasten thither
 to where my home I’ve had

===Chinese===
「夫人，倘若我帮过你，
做这件事我满心欢喜。
如今我必须赶回
自少年时代起便安家的地方，

===Original===
 Since early days of boyhood,
 ere I thence had ridden
 To war against King Herwic;
 henceforth to strive with him it is forbidden.”

===Chinese===
直到我骑马离开那里
去与赫尔维克王交战；
从今往后，与他相争
已是禁绝之事。」

===Original===
Then they their ships unloaded,
 and up they bore on the sand
 The many things brought with them
 into Queen Hilda’s land.

===Chinese===
随后他们卸下船只，
把随行带来的众多物件
搬运到沙滩上，
运进王后希尔达的国土。

===Original===
 The night was drawing nearer,
 the air was colder growing;
 The guests no longer waited:
 to seek a shelter they in haste were going.

===Chinese===
夜色渐渐临近，
空气越来越冷；
宾客们不再逗留：
匆匆赶去寻找栖身之所。

===Original===
Then with the guests Queen Hilda
 rode up on to the plain.
 Before great Matelan castle
 huts and tents were seen

===Chinese===
于是王后希尔达陪着宾客们
策马登上平川。
在大马特兰城堡前面，
只见棚屋与帐篷连绵

===Original===
 Bedecked with gold and shining;
 there the guests were seated
 Upon rich seats made ready:
 within the tents were all most kindly treated.

===Chinese===
装点着黄金、熠熠生辉；
宾客们在
备好的华贵座席上就座：
帐篷之中人人受到最亲切的款待。

===Original===
Such wealth, at Hilda’s bidding,
 was brought up to the land,
 That none need leave behind him
 his pledge or bond to stand.

===Chinese===
遵照希尔达的吩咐，
如许财富被搬运上岸，
谁也不必把自己的
抵押或债券留下作保。

===Original===
 Never in giving freely
 could any host be vying
 With this most high-born widow:
 no guest need wine or other cheer be buying.

===Chinese===
论慷慨馈赠，
纵是东道也无人
能与这位出身高贵的寡妇相比：
宾客们无需自购美酒或其他欢饮之物。

===Original===
There the weary rested
 until five days were gone.
 The greatest care and kindness
 unto the guests were shown;

===Chinese===
疲惫的人们在那里歇息，
直到五天过去。
人们对宾客
展现出最大的关怀与善意；

===Original===
 But Hartmut greatly sorrowed﻿—
 no happiness it gave him﻿—
 Until the lovely maiden
 begged Queen Hilda would in freedom leave him.

===Chinese===
唯有哈特穆特深陷愁苦——
这于他毫无欢愉可言——
直到美丽的少女恳求
王后希尔达还他自由之身。

===Original===
Then Ortwin went with his sister
 where Hilda had her seat.
 She said: “My dearest mother,
 never this forget﻿—

===Chinese===
随后奥特温与妹妹一同
来到希尔达的座前。
她说道：「我最亲爱的母亲，
这一点永莫忘记——

===Original===
 We must not reward with evil
 him who a wrong is doing.
 Of your worthy name bethink you;
 you should on Hartmut smile, forgiveness showing.”

===Chinese===
对一个正在作恶的人，
我们也不可以恶相报。
请顾念您尊贵的名声；
您该对哈特穆特展露笑颜，示以宽恕。」

===Original===
She answered: “Dearest daughter,
 you do not ask aright:
 I at the hands of Hartmut
 the greatest ills have met;

===Chinese===
她回答道：「最亲爱的女儿，
你的请求提得不合情理：
我在哈特穆特手中
遭际过最深重的祸殃；

===Original===
 He must atone in bondage
 for all his wrongful dealing.”
 Then at the feet of Hilda
 Gudrun fell down, with sixty maidens, kneeling.

===Chinese===
他必须在枷锁之中
为他的全部恶行赎罪。」
于是古德伦带着六十名少女，
跪倒在希尔达的脚下。

===Original===
Then spake the lady Ortrun:
 “In freedom let him live;
 To you will he be faithful,
 for this my word I give.

===Chinese===
随后奥特龙夫人说道：
「让他自由地活着吧；
他必对您忠心耿耿，
为此我愿以言语作保。

===Original===
 Be to my brother friendly,
 nor of your love be sparing;
 ’Twill be to you an honor
 if he again the kingly crown be wearing.”

===Chinese===
请善待我的兄弟，
不要吝惜您的慈爱；
倘若他能重新戴上王冠，
于您也是一份荣光。」

===Original===
His friends all wept together
 that he in bondage sat,
 Wearing chains so heavy;
 their eyes with tears were wet:

===Chinese===
他的友人们一齐落泪，
只因他身陷枷锁，
戴着沉重的锁链；
他们的双眼泪水盈盈：

===Original===
 Much they pitied Hartmut,
 no more his kingdom swaying.
 On him and on his followers
 fetters fast and strongest now were weighing.

===Chinese===
他们深深怜悯哈特穆特，
他再不能君临他的王国。
在他和他的部众身上，
如今压着最坚固的镣铐。

===Original===
Then spake to them Queen Hilda:
 “Leave your weeping now;
 Their chains will I unloosen;
 they to my court may go:

===Chinese===
于是王后希尔达对他们说：
「别再哭泣了；
我要为他们解开锁链；
他们可以到我的宫廷来：

===Original===
 But not to seek their freedom
 they their word must give me,
 And with an oath must swear it﻿—
 not hence to ride unbidden, nor to leave me.”

===Chinese===
但他们必须向我保证
不图谋脱身逃走，
还须为此立下誓言——
不得擅自骑马离开，也不得弃我而去。」

===Original===
Now the noble bondsmen
 were from chains set free.
 Gudrun then bade these warriors
 to bathe them in the sea;

===Chinese===
如今这些高贵的囚徒
已从锁链中获释。
古德伦随即吩咐武士们
下海沐浴净身；

===Original===
 Then, in finest clothing,
 men to court must lead them.
 Knights were they most worthy;
 and so the more, good luck did ever speed them.

===Chinese===
沐浴之后，人们要让他们
穿上最精美的衣裳，带进宫廷。
他们是最配得上骑士之名的人；
也正因如此，好运总在成全他们。

===Original===
There among the others
 Hartmut now was seen;
 Never a braver warrior
 or better knight had been:

===Chinese===
在那里的人群之中，
如今见到了哈特穆特；
再没有哪位武士比他更勇敢，
也没有比他更好的骑士：

===Original===
 E’en now, amid his sorrows,
 such a mien was he wearing,
 It seemed as if a pencil
 had drawn him there, and a parchment him was bearing.

===Chinese===
即便身处愁苦之中，
他的仪态依然如此，
仿佛有一支画笔
把他描画在那里，立在一幅羊皮纸上。

===Original===
Now on him with kindness
 did all the ladies look,
 While he, their friendship trusting,
 greater boldness took.

===Chinese===
如今众位女眷
都慈和地望着他，
而他信赖她们的友谊，
胆气也渐渐壮了起来。

===Original===
 Ill-will, that erst was borne him,
 none were longer feeling;
 It was by all forgotten
 what wounds they erst had been to each other dealing.

===Chinese===
先前对他怀有的敌意，
如今已无人再觉；
彼此昔日给对方
造成的创伤，已被众人尽皆忘却。

===Original===
Herwic now bethought him
 from the land of the Hegeling
 How he might be going.
 He bade his men to bring

===Chinese===
赫尔维克这时动了
离开黑格林人国土
归去的念头。
他吩咐部下取来

===Original===
 His clothing and his weapons,
 and on the horses load them:
 When this was known to Hilda,
 to let them go no ready will she showed them.

===Chinese===
他的衣物与兵器，
驮到马背上：
希尔达得知此事，
全无放他们离去的意愿。

===Original===
She said: “My good Lord Herwic,
 I beg you longer stay!
 All your love and kindness
 a weight on me doth lay.

===Chinese===
她说：「我的好赫尔维克大人，
我求你再逗留些时日！
你们的深情与厚谊
沉沉地压在我心上。

===Original===
 Not yet with my good wishes
 may you hence be riding;
 Before you yet shall leave me,
 there shall be high times for the guests with me abiding.”

===Chinese===
我还不能带着我的祝福
放你们就此上路；
在你们离开我之前，
还要为与我同留的宾客们办一场盛会。」

===Original===
To her Lord Herwic answered:
 “Lady, you know the way,
 How those who send their kinsmen
 to lands which others sway

===Chinese===
赫尔维克大人回答她道：
「夫人，您是知道的，
人们把自己的亲族
派往他人统治的国度之后，

===Original===
 Again at home to see them
 are always greatly longing:
 With pain our friends are waiting
 until again they see us homeward thronging.”

===Chinese===
心里总盼着能在家里
再见到他们的身影：
我们的朋友正怀着焦灼等待，
只盼再见我们成群结队地归乡。」

===Original===
Then spake again Queen Hilda:
 “Grudge not, I beg, to me
 One happiness and honor,
 for none can greater be;

===Chinese===
王后希尔达又说道：
「我求你，不要吝惜
给我这一份幸福与荣光，
再没有比这更大的了；

===Original===
 Herwic, king most worthy,
 the boon now deign to give me,
 That I, poor lonely woman,
 may see my daughter crowned, ere she shall leave me.”

===Chinese===
至尊至贵的赫尔维克王啊，
现在就请恩准我这一请求，
让我这可怜的孤身妇人
在女儿离开我之前，亲眼见她加冕。」

===Original===
For this was he unwilling;
 but still she begged and bade:
 Thereby those held in bondage
 were soon from sorrow freed.

===Chinese===
起初他并不愿意；
但她再三恳求、坚持：
这样一来，那些被缚的囚徒
很快便脱离了愁苦。

===Original===
 When now at last he told her
 that to do it he was willing,
 Then the Lady Hilda
 was glad in heart, and rest of mind was feeling.

===Chinese===
当他终于告诉她
自己愿意照办时，
希尔达夫人随即
心中欢喜，得到了心境的安宁。

===Original===
Seats were made at her bidding,
 yet more and better still,
 Which many knights with honor,
 near Hilda, soon did fill,

===Chinese===
遵照她的吩咐，
又造起了更多更好的座席，
许多骑士满怀荣耀地
在希尔达近旁落座，

===Original===
 When came the high times merry,
 that now were widely bruited.
 To crown Gudrun, the fair one,
 King Herwic bade, for him it now well suited.

===Chinese===
这时欢腾的盛会来临，
消息早已传遍四方。
为美丽的古德伦加冕，
是赫尔维克王亲自吩咐的，此刻正合他心意。

===Original===
Of those who him had followed
 there went away not one
 Before at Matelan castle
 the high times were begun.

===Chinese===
追随过他的那些人，
没有一个离开，
直到马特兰城堡的
盛会宣告开始。

===Original===
 Then by Lady Hilda
 was clothing kindly given
 To sixty maids or over:
 for praise and honor she had ever striven.

===Chinese===
随后希尔达夫人
亲切地分赠衣裳，
给六十名或更多的少女：
她一生都追求赞誉与荣光。

===Original===
To full a hundred women
 clothing good she gave:
 None of those were slighted,
 but all her care did have,

===Chinese===
她给了足足上百名女子
上好的衣裳：
无人受到冷落，
人人都蒙她关照，

===Original===
 Who from their homes were taken;
 these had clothes the rarest.
 The gifts indeed were wondrous
 that Hilda gave, of queens the best and fairest.

===Chinese===
她们都是被人从家中掳来的；
这些人得的衣裳最为珍稀。
希尔达所赠的礼物
着实令人惊叹，她是王后中最善最美的一位。

===Original===
Irold must guard the treasure;
 to dwell in Hilda’s home
 That knight erelong was bidden,
 and quickly did he come:

===Chinese===
伊罗尔德须看守财宝；
这位骑士不久便受邀
住进希尔达的宅邸，
他来得也十分迅捷：

===Original===
 Wâ-te, he of Sturmland,
 must carve the meat at table;
 They also sent for Fru-te,
 to come to her as soon as he was able.

===Chinese===
施图尔姆兰的瓦特
须在席上执刀切肉；
他们还派人去请弗鲁特，
请他尽早来到她的身边。

===Original===
Her cup-bearer she made him;
 thereon thus spake the knight:
 “That will I be most gladly,
 if now you think it right.

===Chinese===
她任命他为司酒官；
对此骑士这样说道：
「我非常乐意担任此职，
倘若您认为此举正当。

===Original===
 A fief you then will give me,
 with banners twelve to show it;
 Then am I lord in Denmark.”
 Queen Hilda smiled, but never thought to do it.

===Chinese===
那时您还要赐我一块封地，
配十二面旗帜为凭；
那样我就是丹麦的领主了。」
王后希尔达微微一笑，却从未打算照办。

===Original===
To Fru-te thus she answered:
 “That gift is not for thee;
 For still your nephew Horant
 Daneland’s lord must be.

===Chinese===
她这样回答弗鲁特道：
「那份封赏不是给你的；
因为你的外甥霍兰特（Horant）
仍须做丹麦兰（Daneland）之主。

===Original===
 You, in his stead, for friendship,
 must now our cup be filling;
 And, while he is with the Normans,
 kindly to care for him must you be willing.”

===Chinese===
而你，念着情分，要代他
为我们斟满酒盏；
在他与诺曼人相处的日子里，
也须甘愿悉心照拂于他。」

===Original===
The men and maids in waiting
 all to their tasks were set:
 Silken clothes were called for;
 a hoard both rich and great,

===Chinese===
侍立的男侍女侍
人人都领了差事：
人们要来绸衣；
一座丰饶浩大的库藏，

===Original===
 In rooms and chests long treasured,
 Queen Hilda bade them open.
 These were brought by stewards,
 and all the guests to them were freely holpen.

===Chinese===
久贮于内室与箱柜之中，
王后希尔达命人开启。
管事们把衣物搬运出来，
宾客们尽可自由取用，各得其所。

===Original===
Of these the very lowest
 had clothing of the best.
 If others than the Normans
 were bidden to the feast,

===Chinese===
这些人中身份最低微的，
穿的都是最上等的衣裳。
倘若除诺曼人以外还有旁人
也应邀前来赴宴，

===Original===
 Or why they called them thither,
 I have no way of telling:
 Full thirty thousand were they
 whom there they brought, in Norman lands once dwelling.

===Chinese===
或者为何要把他们召来，
我可无从知晓：
他们足有三万之众，
都曾栖居在诺曼人之地。

===Original===
Clothes for all were wanted,
 but where could these be found?
 If e’en the wealth of Araby
 any there had owned,

===Chinese===
人人都要衣裳，
可上哪里去寻这么多？
纵使有人怀揣
阿拉比的泼天财富，

===Original===
 I ween he could no better
 or finer clothes have given
 Than now they shared so freely:
 that this should be, Gudrun her best had striven.

===Chinese===
我相信他也拿不出
比他们如今尽情分享的
更好更精的衣裳：
为促成此事，古德伦已竭尽所能。

===Original===
Soon as this lovely maiden
 by the guests had now her seat,
 She sent for her brother Ortwin,
 and did his coming wait,

===Chinese===
这位美丽的少女
刚在宾客间落座，
便派人去请兄弟奥特温，
一心等候他的到来，

===Original===
 That she the word might give him
 to be fair Ortrun’s lover;
 She, King Ludwig’s daughter,
 beside Gudrun was seated then, as ever.

===Chinese===
好亲口吩咐他
去做美丽的奥特龙的恋人；
她，路德维希王的女儿，
一如既往坐在古德伦身旁。

===Original===
Ortwin, lord of Ortland,
 made haste to his sister’s bower:
 Him welcomed many a maiden
 who sat with her that hour.

===Chinese===
奥特兰之主奥特温
匆匆赶往妹妹的闺阁：
许多少女正陪她坐在那里，
一齐向他表示欢迎。

===Original===
 Then, from her seat arising,
 by the hand she kindly took him;
 And him aside then leading,
 at the further end of the hall she thus bespoke him,

===Chinese===
随后她从座上起身，
亲切地牵起他的手；
把他领到一旁，
在大厅尽头对他这般开言，

===Original===
Saying: “Dearest brother,
 hear what for you is best;
 All that I shall tell you
 comes from a faithful breast.

===Chinese===
「最亲爱的兄弟，
听听什么于你最好；
我要告诉你的一切，
都出自一颗忠诚的心。

===Original===
 If you for bliss are hoping,
 so long as you are living,
 Then for Hartmut’s sister
 you must, as best you may, henceforth be striving.”

===Chinese===
倘若你还指望此生
得到至福，
那么今后你必须尽力
去争取哈特穆特的妹妹。」

===Original===
To her young Ortwin answered:
 “Now think you this is well?
 I and her brother Hartmut
 never as friends can feel;

===Chinese===
年轻的奥特温回答她：
「你以为这样妥当么？
我和她的兄弟哈特穆特
永不可能以朋友相待；

===Original===
 We slew their father Ludwig,
 and, when to me she’s wedded,
 Of him will she be thinking;
 then with her sighs I oft shall be upbraided.”

===Chinese===
我们杀了他们的父亲路德维希，
待她嫁给我之后，
她会时时惦念着他；
那时我将常被她的叹息所责备。」

===Original===
“You such love must show her
 that for him she will not long.
 If now this word I give you,
 ’tis from a love as strong

===Chinese===
「你必须对她施与这样的爱，
让她不再把他牵挂。
我如今对你说这番话，
是出于同样深挚的爱，

===Original===
 As I have had for any,
 or e’er in my life was feeling.
 Should she to you be wedded,
 your bliss with her will be beyond all telling.”

===Chinese===
不逊于我此生
对任何人怀有的情意。
倘若她嫁给你，
你与她的幸福将无可言传。」

===Original===
Then said her knightly brother:
 “If she to you is known,
 And now you think the Hegelings
 will her for a mistress own,

===Chinese===
她那骑士的兄弟随即说道：
「既然她为你所深知，
你又认为黑格林人
肯奉她为主母，

===Original===
 Gladly will I love her﻿—
 a maid of such high-breeding.”
 Him Gudrun then answered:
 “You’ll ne’er a sorry day with her be leading.”

===Chinese===
我便甘愿爱她——
如此教养高贵的一位少女。」
古德伦随即回答他道：
「与她相伴，你决不会过上一个愁苦的日子。」

===Original===
Of this he spoke to others,
 but Hilda’s word was nay;
 He told it unto Herwic,
 to hear what he would say,

===Chinese===
他又把这事讲给旁人听，
但希尔达的答话是否定；
他把这事告诉了赫尔维克，
想听听他的高见，

===Original===
 Who held it right and worthy;
 then to Fru-te speaking,
 That friend would have him woo her,
 “for many knights will she your own be making.

===Chinese===
赫尔维克认为此事正当而相宜；
随后又与弗鲁特谈起，
那位朋友也劝他向她求亲，
「因为她会为你收服许多武士。

===Original===
“Soothed should be the hatred
 that we each other bore;
 Of how it may be ended,
 I now will tell you more;

===Chinese===
「我们彼此怀有的仇怨，
理应加以化解；
至于如何了结它，
我现在再对你说个明白；

===Original===
 Then,” said the Danish Fru-te,
 whose word was ever heeded,
 “Hildeburg, the maiden,
 to young King Hartmut also must be wedded.”

===Chinese===
那么，」那位丹麦人弗鲁特说道，
他的言语素来为人重视，
「少女希尔德堡，
也必须许配给年轻的哈特穆特王。」

===Original===
The wise and upright Herwic
 with faithful words thus spake:
 “I deem it right and fitting
 the maiden him should take;

===Chinese===
睿智而正直的赫尔维克
以恳切的话语这样说道：
「我认为让她
嫁给他是正当而相宜的；

===Original===
 When in the land of Hartmut
 she is queen and lady,
 A thousand lordly castles
 to own her sway will there be glad and ready.”

===Chinese===
当她在哈特穆特的国土上
成为王后与女主，
一千座堂皇的城堡
都将欣然恭顺她的统辖。」

===Original===
Then to the high-born Hildeburg
 Gudrun the fair thus spake,
 With words unheard by others:
 “Care for your weal I’ll take;

===Chinese===
随后美丽的古德伦
对出身高贵的希尔德堡低语，
说的是旁人听不见的话：
「你的福祉我自会挂心；

===Original===
 If I may well reward you,
 my friend and playmate dearest,
 For all the love you’ve shown me,
 soon in the Norman land a crown thou wearest.”

===Chinese===
倘若我能好好酬谢你，
我最亲爱的朋友与游伴，
为了你给我的全部情谊——
不久你将在诺曼人之地戴上王冠。」

===Original===
To her then said fair Hildeburg:
 “For me it were not well
 To give my troth to any
 who ne’er his love did tell,

===Chinese===
美丽的希尔德堡对她说道：
「于我而言这并不妥当：
把终身许给一个
从未吐露过爱意的人，

===Original===
 Nor unto me, in fondness,
 e’er his heart was turning;
 Should we grow old together,
 I fear between us oft there’ll be heart-burning.”

===Chinese===
他的心也从未
在深情中向我倾慕；
倘若我们相守到老，
恐怕彼此间常会郁郁生怨。」

===Original===
Her Gudrun thus answered:
 “Give not a thought to that:
 I soon will send to Hartmut,
 and bid him answer straight

===Chinese===
古德伦回答她道：
「这事你不必挂虑：
我马上就遣人去找哈特穆特，
要他直截了当回话，

===Original===
 Whether he now would like it
 if from his pledge I free him,
 As well as all his followers,
 and send him home, that his friends again may see him.

===Chinese===
看他是否愿意
让我解除他的誓约，
连同他全部部众的誓约，
并送他回乡，让亲友们再见他一面。

===Original===
“If he his thanks shall tell me,
 I then in turn will bid
 That he by deeds shall show it,
 and shall my wishes heed.

===Chinese===
「倘若他向我道谢，
我便回过头来要求
他以行动来表明，
并遵从我的心愿。

===Original===
 I then will freely ask him
 if he will wed a maiden,
 That I and all my kinsmen
 may him with love and friendship ever gladden.”

===Chinese===
那时我会坦然问他
是否愿娶一位少女，
好让我和我全体亲族
永远以爱与情谊使他欢欣。」

===Original===
To her they brought young Hartmut,
 king of the Norman land,
 And with him came old Fru-te.
 Near her, on either hand,

===Chinese===
他们把年轻的哈特穆特
带到她面前，他是诺曼人之地的王，
随他同来的还有年迈的弗鲁特。
在她的左右两侧，

===Original===
 Proud Hildeburg and Ortrun
 within her bower were sitting;
 If the lady’s word they heeded,
 their many woes they both would be forgetting.

===Chinese===
高傲的希尔德堡与奥特龙
正坐在她的闺阁里；
倘若她们听从这位夫人的话，
两人都将忘却各自的诸多愁苦。

===Original===
Hartmut, the son of Ludwig,
 went through the palace hall;
 To him a friendly greeting
 was given by one and all,

===Chinese===
路德维希之子哈特穆特
走过宫殿的大厅；
人们向他致以友好的问候，
不论尊卑，无一例外，

===Original===
 Alike both high and lowly
 from their seats arising.
 None than he was braver;
 no worth or greatness e’er in him was missing.

===Chinese===
高贵的与卑微的人
都从座位上起身。
没有谁比他更勇敢；
价值与伟岸从不曾在他身上缺席。

===Original===
He by Gudrun, fair lady,
 to seat himself was told;
 And neither of the others
 her greeting did withhold.

===Chinese===
美丽的贵妇古德伦
请他就在那里落座；
其余两位女子
也没有吝惜对他的问候。

===Original===
 Then said Queen Hilda’s daughter:
 “I beg you to be sitting
 Near my faithful maidens,
 who washed with me for your knights, as was befitting.”

===Chinese===
随后王后希尔达的女儿说道：
「我请你坐到
我忠实的少女们近旁，
她们曾同我一起为你们的武士洗衣，那是合乎身份的事。」

===Original===
“This in scorn you bid me,
 fair and lovely queen!
 Whatever wrong was done you
 truly gives me pain:

===Chinese===
「你这分明是在奚落我，
美丽可爱的王后！
无论加诸你身的是何等冤屈，
都着实令我痛心：

===Original===
 ’Twas by my mother’s wishes
 that this from me was hidden;
 To keep it from my father,
 and from his knights as well, were all men bidden.”

===Chinese===
那是遵照我母亲的意愿，
这事才背着我施行；
人人受命瞒住我的父亲，
也不让他麾下的武士知晓。」

===Original===
To him the maiden answered:
 “My wish I may not hide:
 I now, in truth, Sir Hartmut,
 must speak with you aside.

===Chinese===
少女回答他道：
「我的心愿无从隐瞒：
爵士哈特穆特，说实话，
我此刻必须与你单独一谈。

===Original===
 I and yourself, we only,
 may hear what I am saying.”
 Hartmut then bethought him:
 “May God now grant she is not falsely playing.”

===Chinese===
只有我与你二人，
能听我要说的话。」
哈特穆特心中暗想：
「但愿上帝保佑，她不是虚情假意。」

===Original===
No one else but Fru-te
 allowed she to come near;
 Then the high-born maiden
 said in Hartmut’s ear:

===Chinese===
除弗鲁特一人之外，
她不许任何人靠近；
随后这位出身高贵的少女
凑在哈特穆特耳边说道：

===Original===
 “If you to me will hearken,
 and do what I shall tell you
 With ready heart and freely,
 now of all your sorrows I will heal you.”

===Chinese===
「倘若你肯听我的，
甘心乐意地
照我的吩咐去做，
此刻我便医好你全部的忧伤。」

===Original===
“Well I know your wisdom,”
 then young Hartmut said;
 “Of aught that is unworthy
 I need not be afraid.

===Chinese===
「你的智慧我素来深知，」
年轻的哈特穆特随即说道；
「任何不光彩的事，
我都无须担心。

===Original===
 My heart for nothing wishes,
 unless to do your bidding:
 Gladly, high-born lady,
 to all that you shall say will I give heeding.”

===Chinese===
我的心别无所求，
只想遵你的吩咐而行：
出身高贵的夫人啊，
你说的一切我都乐于听从。」

===Original===
She said: “My wish I tell you,
 and now your life would cheer;
 I, and my kinsmen with me,
 will give you a helpmeet fair.

===Chinese===
她说：「我把心愿告诉你，
如今要让你的生命振作；
我，连同我的亲族，
要为你择一位美好的佳偶。

===Original===
 To keep both land and honor
 you may thus be seeking,
 And of the hate we bore you
 none shall evermore a word be speaking.”

===Chinese===
如此你便可以求取
国土与名誉两全，
至于我们对你怀过的仇恨，
今后再无人提起一言。」

===Original===
“Who is it, say, fair lady,
 that you for me will choose?
 Ere yet my love I give her,
 life would I rather lose

===Chinese===
「请告诉我，美丽的夫人，
你要为我择定的是谁？
在我把爱给她之前，
我宁肯先舍去性命，

===Original===
 Than ever that my kinsmen
 her with scorn were eying;
 For me it were far better
 that I in death upon the field were lying.”

===Chinese===
也不愿我的亲族
用轻蔑的眼光看她；
对我而言，倒不如
战死沙场来得痛快。」

===Original===
“I will give your sister Ortrun,
 the maid beloved and fair,
 To be a wife to my brother,
 himself to me most dear.

===Chinese===
「我要把你的妹妹奥特龙，
这位可爱而美丽的少女，
许给我的兄弟为妻，
他于我最是亲爱。

===Original===
 You must wed with Hildeburg,
 of a king the well-born daughter:
 Never a dearer maiden
 you in the world could find, where’er you sought her.”

===Chinese===
你则须迎娶希尔德堡，
一位国王的高贵女儿：
这样可爱的少女，
你走遍天下也再难寻见。」

===Original===
“If this indeed may happen,”
 then young Hartmut said,
 “And now your brother Ortwin
 shall take that lovely maid,

===Chinese===
「倘若这当真能成，」
年轻的哈特穆特说道，
「而你的兄弟奥特温
又将娶那位可爱的少女，

===Original===
 My dear-loved sister, Ortrun,
 and she to him is wedded,
 Then I will woo fair Hildeburg;
 thus hate will end, nor longer shall be dreaded.”

===Chinese===
我亲爱的妹妹奥特龙，
与她结为连理，
那么我便去追求美丽的希尔德堡；
仇恨就此了结，再无所畏惧。」

===Original===
She said: “To this I’ve brought him;
 his troth to her he gave.
 If now ’twould make you happy
 your father’s lands to have,

===Chinese===
她说：「我已劝成他；
他对她许下了婚约。
倘若重新得回
你父亲的国土能让你快慰，

===Original===
 And again within his castles
 that you should soon be living,
 You well may wed with Hildeburg,
 and there the queenly crown to her be giving.”

===Chinese===
不久又能住进
他那些城堡之中，
你大可与希尔德堡成婚，
并在那里把王后般的冠冕加于她。」

===Original===
He said: “That pledge I gladly,
 and on it give my hand;
 As soon as the king of Ortland
 shall with my sister stand,

===Chinese===
他说：「那个誓言我欣然接受，
并为此伸出我的手；
一旦奥特兰之王
与我妹妹站在一起，

===Original===
 And both the crown have taken,
 then I, no more forbearing,
 Will, with lovely Hildeburg,
 among our men our lands and fiefs be sharing.”

===Chinese===
两人都戴上了王冠，
我便不再迟疑，
要与美丽的希尔德堡
在我们的部众中分封我们的土地与封地。」

===Original===
When he his word had plighted,
 then said the high-born maid:
 “Now will I do gladly
 a further friendly deed;

===Chinese===
他许下誓言之后，
那位出身高贵的少女说道：
「如今我还甘愿
再做一件友好的善事；

===Original===
 Unto the lord of Karadie
 for a wife will I be giving
 The sister of King Herwic,
 that she with him may evermore be living.”

===Chinese===
我要把赫尔维克王的妹妹
许给卡拉迪之主为妻，
让她与他
长久相守，永不分离。」

===Original===
I ween that never hatred
 was smoothed as now was done:
 Brave knights who long were foemen
 now became as one.

===Chinese===
我想，从未有过仇恨
化解得像如今这般彻底：
久为仇敌的勇敢骑士们，
如今亲如一体。

===Original===
 Fru-te, the lord of Daneland,
 thought it right and fitting
 Soon to send for Ortwin;
 also the Moorland king must them be meeting.

===Chinese===
丹麦兰之主弗鲁特
认为宜当尽快
派人去请奥特温；
摩尔兰之王也须与他们相会。

===Original===
When they to court were coming,
 finest clothes they wore.
 The news Gudrun had told them
 others to Wâ-te bore;

===Chinese===
他们来到宫廷时，
穿的都是最精美的衣裳。
古德伦告知他们的消息，
旁人又带给了瓦特；

===Original===
 To Irold, too, they gave it,
 as soon as he came thither;
 This aside they talked of,
 and fitting speech long time they held together.

===Chinese===
伊罗尔德一到，
他们也都告诉了他；
他们在一旁商议此事，
长时间地共同斟酌着得体的话语。

===Original===
Then spake the aged Wâ-te:
 “Peace we can never know
 Until Ortrun and Hartmut
 to Hilda, the queen, shall go,

===Chinese===
随后年迈的瓦特说道：
「不到奥特龙和哈特穆特
走到王后希尔达面前，
我们就永无安宁，

===Original===
 And ask of her forgiveness,
 down at her feet low bending.
 Only if she allows it,
 can we be friends, and hatred have an ending.”

===Chinese===
向她跪伏在她脚下，
乞求她的宽恕。
只有她允准了，
我们才能修好，仇恨才有尽头。」

===Original===
Then spake Gudrun, the high-born:
 “This I can truly say:
 To them is she not unfriendly;
 Ortrun wears to-day

===Chinese===
随后出身高贵的古德伦说道：
「这一点我确可言明：
她对她们并非无情；
今天奥特龙身上穿的，

===Original===
 Such clothes as by my mother
 to me and my maids were given.
 I’ll gladly gain forgiveness;
 in me they all may trust, from home now riven.”

===Chinese===
正是我母亲赠给我
和我的少女们的衣裳。
我甘愿为她们求取宽恕；
她们如今有家难归，尽可信赖我。」

===Original===
Within a ring of maidens
 Ortrun then they set,
 And with her also Hildeburg,
 of birth both high and meet:

===Chinese===
随后他们让奥特龙
坐在少女们围成的圈中，
希尔德堡也陪在她身旁，
两人都门第高贵、堪相匹配：

===Original===
 Ortwin then and Hartmut
 led them out to wed them.
 “I hope,” said Lady Hilda,
 “that now, forever, we our friends have made them.”

===Chinese===
奥特温和哈特穆特
随即领她们出来成婚。
「我希望，」希尔达夫人说道，
「从今往后，我们永远把他们当朋友相待。」

===Original===
When to his side young Ortwin
 did the maiden Ortrun bring,
 Lovingly and kindly,
 he took a golden ring,

===Chinese===
年轻的奥特温
把少女奥特龙领到身旁时，
满怀爱意与温情，
取出一枚黄金戒指，

===Original===
 And this upon the finger
 of her fair white hand he fitted.
 Then far off were driven
 the many woes that late her life had greeted.

===Chinese===
把它戴上了她白皙
美手的手指。
近来缠绕她生命的
种种愁苦，顿时被驱赶到远方。

===Original===
Hartmut around fair Hildeburg
 then his arms did throw;
 Each on the hand of the other
 did a golden ring bestow.

===Chinese===
哈特穆特随即把美丽的希尔德堡
拥入自己的臂弯；
他们又在彼此手上
互赠了一枚黄金戒指。

===Original===
 The lovely maid was blameless,
 and sorrow gave him never;
 Of him and of fair Hildeburg
 nothing their faithful hearts thro’ life could sever.

===Chinese===
这位可爱的少女无可挑剔，
从未给他忧愁；
至于他和美丽的希尔德堡，
一生没有任何东西能分开他们忠贞的心。

===Original===
Then said Queen Hilda’s daughter:
 “Herwic, my lord most dear,
 Say, does the land of your fathers
 lie to us so near

===Chinese===
随后王后希尔达的女儿说道：
「赫尔维克，我最亲爱的夫君，
请说，你父祖的国土
离我们可近么，

===Original===
 That men could bring your sister,
 if this by us were needed,
 Here to my mother’s kingdom,
 that she to the lord of Karadie may now be wedded?”

===Chinese===
倘若我们需要，
人们能否把你的妹妹
接来我母亲的王国，
让她与卡拉迪之主完婚？」

===Original===
To her King Herwic answered:
 “This will I say to you:
 Your men, if they will hasten,
 in twelve days’ time can go;

===Chinese===
赫尔维克王回答她道：
「我可以这样对你说：
你的人马若肯兼程赶路，
十二天的工夫便能抵达；

===Original===
 But if any to your kingdom
 the maiden would be leading,
 Ill luck, I ween, awaits him,
 unless with him my own good knights be speeding.”

===Chinese===
但若有人想把少女
领到你们的王国，
我看厄运就会候着他，
除非我的好骑士们随行护送。」

===Original===
Then answered Hilda’s daughter:
 “Your help, I beg you, grant;
 By doing this, of happiness
 you nought shall ever want.

===Chinese===
希尔达的女儿随即回答：
「我求你允准相助；
做成这件事，
你的幸福将永无匮乏。

===Original===
 To your men both food and clothing
 my mother will be giving;
 Only bring us the maiden,
 that I may thank you, long as you are living.”

===Chinese===
你的人马的粮秣衣装，
都由我母亲供给；
只求把少女带来，
让我终生感念你的恩义。」

===Original===
To her then said Lord Herwic:
 “How can she be clad?
 The mighty lord of Karadie
 a waste of my kingdom made;

===Chinese===
赫尔维克大人对她说道：
「她能有衣裳可穿么？
威震四方的卡拉迪之主
把我的国土蹂躏殆尽；

===Original===
 There he burned my castles,
 and of her clothes bereft her.”
 Then said the king of Moorland:
 “Her would I woo, if only a smock were left her.”

===Chinese===
他烧毁了我的城堡，
也剥夺了她的衣裳。」
摩尔兰之王随即说道：
「哪怕她只剩一件衬衣，我也要向她求亲。」

===Original===
To bring the maid then Herwic
 a hundred warriors sent;
 He bade his men to hasten
 when on their way they went.

===Chinese===
于是赫尔维克派出
一百名武士去接那位少女；
他吩咐部下上路之后
务须快马加鞭。

===Original===
 He begged that Wâ-te and Fru-te
 would with them go riding:
 This was to them a burden;
 but yet the worthy knights both did his bidding.

===Chinese===
他又恳请瓦特和弗鲁特
与他们同行：
这于二人不啻负担；
但两位可敬的骑士还是照办了。

===Original===
With greatest speed they hastened,
 both by day and night,
 Until they found the maiden.
 Wâ-te they feared would fight﻿—

===Chinese===
他们日夜兼程，
以最快的速度赶路，
终于找到了那位少女。
他们唯恐瓦特动武——

===Original===
 ’Gainst this did Herwic’s liegemen
 give their careful heeding.
 Soon from her home the lady,
 with four and twenty maids, the knights were leading.

===Chinese===
赫尔维克的封臣们
对此倍加提防。
不久，骑士们便把那位夫人
连同二十四名少女领出了她的家门。

===Original===
By Wâ-te they were guided
 from the castle down to the sand:
 Two ships they found, with row-boats,
 lying by the strand;

===Chinese===
瓦特一路引导着他们，
从城堡直下沙滩：
他们发现两艘船，附带小艇，
泊在岸边；

===Original===
 One of these they seized on,
 and, helped by breezes blowing,
 They fast away were sailing:
 throughout twelve days they to their homes were going.

===Chinese===
他们夺下其中一艘，
借着吹拂的微风，
飞快地扬帆驶离：
整整十二天，他们都在向家园进发。

===Original===
When to the land of the Hegelings
 they had brought the maid,
 Many knights bethought them
 over the sand to speed,

===Chinese===
当他们把少女
带回黑格林人的国土，
许多骑士都动了念头，
要策马掠过沙滩，

===Original===
 To meet the lovely lady,
 and all with banners hasted.
 They who had brought the maiden
 had kept their oaths, nor from the task had rested.

===Chinese===
去迎接那位可爱的夫人，
个个擎着旗帜疾行。
接回少女的这些人
谨守了誓言，从未半途歇息。

===Original===
How could any maiden
 a better welcome find?
 Gudrun went forth to meet her,
 and gave her greeting kind;

===Chinese===
天下哪位少女
能受到更好的迎接？
古德伦走上前去迎她，
向她致以亲切的问候；

===Original===
 Hilda, with many ladies,
 to see the maiden hasted:
 Nor came King Herwic’s sister
 all alone, though with fire her land was wasted.

===Chinese===
希尔达带着众多女眷
也赶来与少女相见：
赫尔维克王的妹妹
也并非孤身前来，尽管她的国土曾被火焚。

===Original===
She from home was followed
 by full three hundred men.
 Now when the kingly Herwic
 his sister met again,

===Chinese===
她身后跟着足足
三百名从家乡来的人。
当王者气度的赫尔维克
再次与妹妹相逢，

===Original===
 He, to show her honor,
 rode forward, proudly dashing;
 So did many others:
 loud were the shields of the knights together clashing.

===Chinese===
为向她致敬，
他骄傲地纵马向前；
许多旁人也如法而行：
骑士们的盾牌碰得铿然作响。

===Original===
Four kings both rich and mighty
 rode to meet her there;
 Thereon the knights ’gan wrangle
 which of the ladies fair

===Chinese===
四位豪富而威武的国王
策马到那里迎她；
骑士们随即争论起
这些美丽的女子中

===Original===
 Was loveliest and fairest.
 Long their time they wasted,
 For all alike were worthy;
 on this at last their wordy war they rested.

===Chinese===
谁最可爱、谁最美丽。
他们为此争论了许久，
因为个个都同样出众；
这场舌战最终就此罢休。

===Original===
The fair Gudrun then kissed her
 and those who with her came.
 They walked along the seashore,
 till a tent was seen by them,

===Chinese===
美丽的古德伦随即亲吻了她，
也亲吻了随她而来的人们。
他们沿着海岸漫步，
直到望见一顶帐篷，

===Original===
 With richest silken hangings;
 while they stood thereunder,
 What now to her should happen
 gave to Herwic’s sister greatest wonder.

===Chinese===
帐篷垂挂着最华美的丝绸；
他们伫立帐下时，
即将降临到她身上的事
让赫尔维克的妹妹惊讶不已。

===Original===
Now the king of Karadie
 forthwith to come they bade;
 Then they asked the maiden:
 “Will you this man now wed?

===Chinese===
于是他们即刻宣召
卡拉迪之王前来；
随后他们问那少女：
「你愿意现在就嫁给此人么？

===Original===
 Kingdoms nine most mighty
 have for their master owned him.”
 With him were knights full many,
 yellow in hue, now standing all around him.

===Chinese===
九个最强大的王国
都尊他为主君。」
随他而来的武士众多，
个个肤色金黄，环立在他四周。

===Original===
His father and his mother
 were not of faith the same;
 But him, so light in color,
 one might a Christian name.

===Chinese===
他的父亲和母亲
信奉的并非同一信仰；
但他肤色如此浅淡，
人们尽可称他为基督徒。

===Original===
 Like to gold, spun finely,
 the hair on his head was lying:
 She would choose unwisely
 if she to him her love were now denying.

===Chinese===
他头上的头发
宛如精纺的黄金；
她若此刻拒绝
把爱给他，那便是不智的选择。

===Original===
She was slow her love to grant him,
 as oft one sees a maid;
 But she to him was given.
 The worthy knight then said:

===Chinese===
她迟迟不肯把爱许他，
正如人们常见少女的那样；
但她终究被许给了他。
那位可敬的骑士随即说道：

===Original===
 “So well I like this lady,
 from love I ne’er can free me.
 Never will I leave her,
 and as her husband men erelong shall see me.”

===Chinese===
「我是这样喜爱这位夫人，
再也无法从爱中脱身。
我永远不会离开她，
人们不久就会看到我成为她的丈夫。」

===Original===
At last this knight and maiden
 each their troth did plight:
 Both of them scarce waited
 till day should turn to night,

===Chinese===
最后这位骑士与少女
各自许下了婚誓：
两人几乎等不及
白昼转入黑夜，

===Original===
 When, from others hidden,
 they should their bliss be owning.
 Soon, ’mid knightly warriors,
 daughters of four rich kings were hallowed for the crowning.

===Chinese===
好避开旁人耳目，
独自享有他们的幸福。
不久，在威武的骑士们中间，
四位富王的女儿被祝圣，以备加冕。

## Tale XXXI How the Four Kings Were Wedded in Hilda’s Land / 第三十一诗章 四位国王如何在希尔达的国土上成婚

===Original===
A great wedding festival is held, at which the four kings, Herwic, Ortwin, Hartmut, and Siegfried, try to surpass each other in generous giving. Hartmut with his bride, Hildeburg, leaves for Normandy.

===Chinese===
人们举办了一场盛大的婚庆典礼，四位国王——赫尔维克（Herwic）、奥特温（Ortwin）、哈特穆特（Hartmut）与西格弗里德（Siegfried）——在慷慨馈赠上争相胜过彼此。哈特穆特偕同他的新娘希尔德堡（Hildeburg）启程前往诺曼底。

===Original===
Then the kings were hallowed,
 as in days of yore;
 Also there were knighted
 five hundred men or more.

===Chinese===
于是众王接受了祝圣，
一如往昔的岁月；
此外还有五百名上下的男子
当场受封为骑士。

===Original===
 Now in Hilda’s kingdom
 the folk high times were having;
 It was at Matelan castle,
 before the walls where the sea the sands was laving.

===Chinese===
此刻在希尔达（Hilda）的王国里，
民众正欢度着盛会；
地点在马特兰（Matelan）城堡，
就在海浪冲刷沙滩的城墙外面。

===Original===
There the fair Queen Hilda
 to all fine clothing gave.
 How, in the sight of ladies,
 rode Wâ-te old and brave!

===Chinese===
美丽的王后希尔达在那里
向众人赐下了华服。
剽悍的老瓦特（Wâ-te）在贵妇们眼前
骑乘得多么英武！

===Original===
 How Irold, too, and Fru-te
 of Daneland, rode before them!
 One heard the spear-shafts broken,
 as these they lowered, and in the onset bore them.

===Chinese===
伊罗尔德（Irold）与丹麦兰（Daneland）的弗鲁特（Fru-te）
也策马驰骋在他们前头！
只听得矛杆折断之声阵阵，
那是他们压低矛尖、冲锋陷阵之际。

===Original===
Lightly the wind was blowing,
 but the dust was dark as night;
 Yet to the maidens’ clothing
 the knights gave heeding slight,

===Chinese===
风儿轻轻吹拂，
扬起的尘土却暗如夜色；
不过对于少女们的衣裳，
骑士们并不怎样顾惜，

===Original===
 Altho’ ’twas soiled and covered
 with the dust thick flying.
 Before the ladies seated,
 riders bold in many a tilt were vying.

===Chinese===
尽管那衣裳已被
漫天浓尘沾染裹覆。
在端坐的贵妇们面前，
骁勇的骑手们正一场接一场地争胜比武。

===Original===
Now at length the maidens
 were left no longer there;
 They, with the queenly Hilda,
 were led to a window near,

===Chinese===
后来众少女
终于不再被留在那里；
她们与王后般的希尔达一起，
被引到近处的一扇窗前，

===Original===
 Where the daring champions
 their eyes on them were feeding:
 Beside the four betrothèd,
 a hundred well-clothed maids they were thither leading.

===Chinese===
在那里，胆气豪壮的武士们
把目光在她们身上流连：
除了四位订了婚的少女，
人们还往那里领来了一百名衣饰精美的侍女。

===Original===
Many wandering players
 there let their skill be shown;
 The best that each was able,
 how gladly was it done!

===Chinese===
众多四处游走的艺人
在那里献演绝技；
凡是各人拿手的本领，
都演得何等欢畅！

===Original===
 When early mass was ended,
 upon the next day’s morning,
 And God by them was worshiped,
 knights of the sword again to their games were turning.

===Chinese===
次日清晨，
早弥撒诵毕，
他们敬拜过上帝之后，
佩剑的骑士们又转向了比武的游戏。

===Original===
Of uproar and of gladness
 where could more be found?
 Of many tunes and singing
 the halls gave back the sound.

===Chinese===
论欢腾与喜庆，
哪里还能寻得更多？
无数曲调与歌声，
在厅堂里久久回响。

===Original===
 Until four days were over,
 there the high times lasted:
 Well-born throngs were gathered,
 nor oft the hours in idleness they wasted.

===Chinese===
直到四天期满，
盛会都在那里延续：
名门望族的众人齐聚一堂，
他们极少让时辰白白虚掷。

===Original===
An open-handed giver,
 that day was Herwic seen.
 He knew the wandering players,
 who there had come again,

===Chinese===
那一天，赫尔维克显得
是位出手大方的赠予者。
他知道那些四处游走的艺人
又来到了这里，

===Original===
 Were bent on growing richer,
 and well for this were striving;
 Herwic meant, in kindness,
 that all, while there, should gain an easy living.

===Chinese===
一心想多攒些财货，
也正为此竭力卖艺；
赫尔维克出于仁慈，
有意让所有人在此期间都轻松挣得生计。

===Original===
First the lord of Sealand
 flung his gifts around
 With willing hand so freely
 that thanks from all did sound

===Chinese===
西兰（Sealand）之主率先
把赠礼向四方抛撒，
出手如此甘愿慷慨，
以致谢声不绝于耳——

===Original===
 Who saw his love and kindness,
 or heard about it later:
 In ruddy gold King Herwic
 the worth of full a thousand pounds did scatter.

===Chinese===
无论是亲眼见过他仁爱厚意的人，
还是事后听人传述的人都如此称谢。
赫尔维克王以赤红的黄金
撒出了足值一千镑的厚赐。

===Original===
Clothing, too, was given
 by his friends as well as kin;
 Horses finely saddled
 many there did win,

===Chinese===
他的友人和亲族们
也纷纷赐下衣裳；
鞍辔精美的骏马，
许多人在那里赢得，

===Original===
 Who before not often
 on such steeds had ridden.
 When this was seen by Ortwin,
 in giving then he would not be outbidden.

===Chinese===
他们从前可难得
骑上这样的良驹。
奥特温见此情形，
在馈赠上便不肯让人占了先。

===Original===
He, the king of Ortland
 finest clothes now gave:
 Since then, if better clothing
 knights did ever have,

===Chinese===
这位奥特兰（Ortland）之王
当即赐出了最精美的衣裳：
打那以后，骑士们可曾
穿过比这更好的衣物，

===Original===
 Forsooth we cannot tell you﻿—
 it never reached our hearing.
 He and all his followers
 stood bereft, erelong, of much that they were wearing.

===Chinese===
说真的，我们无从奉告——
这事从未传入我们耳中。
转眼之间，他和他的全体随从
身上穿戴的许多衣物都已赠空。

===Original===
No one now could reckon
 what store of clothing good
 Was given by those from Moorland.
 There fine horses stood,

===Chinese===
如今谁也无法算清
摩尔兰（Moorland）来的人
赐下了多少上好的衣裳；
那边还立着精美的骏马，

===Original===
 Soon to be given also﻿—
 such indeed is the saying:
 Those who were to have them
 for better never hoped, nor e’er were praying.

===Chinese===
不久也将一并赐出——
人们的说法确是如此：
那些即将得到骏马的人，
从未盼过更好的，也从未如此祈求。

===Original===
All were now made richer,
 both the young and old.
 Then, too, was seen King Hartmut;
 nought would he withhold,

===Chinese===
这时人人都变得更富足了，
无论年少的还是年长的。
随后人们又见到哈特穆特王；
他什么都不肯攥在手里，

===Original===
 As though his home and kingdom
 had not in war been wasted:
 They saw him give so freely,
 that greater love and kindness none e’er tasted.

===Chinese===
仿佛他的家园与王国
不曾毁于战火：
人们见他赠得如此慷慨，
从未有人领受过比这更深的爱与仁厚。

===Original===
By him and his friendly kinsmen
 who thither with him came,
 And there were held in bondage,
 how readily by them

===Chinese===
他和随他而来的亲善亲族
——他们曾在那里
被囚为质——
出手是多么爽快，

===Original===
 Was given what was left them,
 that any from them wanted!
 By Hartmut and his followers
 all that could be asked was gladly granted.

===Chinese===
凡是有人想要、他们尚存的东西，
无不相赠！
凡向哈特穆特和他的随从开口求取的，
无不欣然获允。

===Original===
Gudrun, the lovely maiden,
 a friendly will e’er bore
 To Hildeburg of Ireland,
 with whom, in days of yore,

===Chinese===
美丽的少女古德伦（Gudrun）
向来怀着友善的心肠，
对待爱尔兰（Ireland）的希尔德堡——
想当初，

===Original===
 To wash upon the sea-sands
 the clothes she oft was bearing.
 I ween no pains she slighted
 that Hartmut’s love her friend might now be sharing.

===Chinese===
她常常提着衣物
到海边的沙滩上去洗濯。
我想，为让她的朋友如今能分享哈特穆特的爱，
古德伦不曾吝惜过任何辛劳。

===Original===
Gudrun then bade her steward
 a hoard of goods to take
 For those who shared her kindness.
 Men of this would speak,

===Chinese===
古德伦随即吩咐她的管事
取出一批财物，
赐给那些曾受她恩待的人。
人们后来谈起此事，

===Original===
 And say in wealth to give them
 she would ne’er be wanting;
 Heavy gold and silver,
 and clothes, could she to all her friends be granting.

===Chinese===
都说在赏赐他们财物上，
她从不落在人后；
沉甸甸的金银，
还有衣裳，她都能赐给全体友人。

===Original===
Before his seat upstanding,
 the Sturmisch lord was seen,
 Clad so well and richly
 that never king nor his men

===Chinese===
只见施图尔姆兰（Sturmisch）之主
从自己的座前起身，
他衣着如此华美富有，
以致无论国王还是国王的部众，

===Original===
 Finer clothes or better
 at any time were wearing.
 None long time were waiting
 who hoped that day his kindness to be sharing.

===Chinese===
都从未在什么时候
穿过更好更美的衣裳。
凡盼着当天分享他恩惠的人，
谁也不曾久等。

===Original===
Above all others, Wâ-te
 gave such clothing there
 That truly never better
 a king was seen to wear;

===Chinese===
瓦特出手压过在场众人，
他在那里赐出的衣裳，
说实话，从未有哪位国王
穿过比这更好的；

===Original===
 With gold and gems it sparkled,
 o’erhung with richest netting:
 Such clothes with him he carried
 when on his way to court he was forth setting.

===Chinese===
衣上金珠熠熠闪动，
还垂覆着最华贵的网衣：
他启程上朝的时候，
随身携带的便是这样的衣裳。

===Original===
In every one of the meshes
 lay a costly stone,
 However one might name it;
 thereby it could be known

===Chinese===
每一只网眼里
都卧着一颗名贵的宝石，
任凭你怎样称呼它；
由此人们便能知晓，

===Original===
 That in the land of Abalie
 the gems therein were fitted.
 To Wâ-te and his followers
 all gave the hand, and them with thanks they greeted.

===Chinese===
这些宝石是在阿巴利（Abalie）之地
镶嵌上去的。
众人纷纷向瓦特和他的随从
伸手致贺，并向他们称谢。

===Original===
None of those there gathered,
 who saw the clothes that day,
 Could of the brave old Wâ-te
 this truth indeed gainsay﻿—

===Chinese===
在场的众人，
凡当天见过那些衣裳的，
对剽悍的老瓦特
都无法否认这一实情——

===Original===
 That beyond the gifts of princes
 his were far outreaching.
 Of wealth he soon was master
 who for these gifts his hand was now outstretching.

===Chinese===
那就是：论馈赠之豪，
他远远超出了诸王。
凡为此刻伸出手来求取这些赠礼的人，
很快就成了财富的主人。

===Original===
Willingly did Irold
 let them see his mind,
 That he to none was grudging
 gifts of any kind.

===Chinese===
伊罗尔德也甘愿
让人看清他的心意：
无论哪一种馈赠，
他对谁都绝不吝惜。

===Original===
 Good care of Hilda’s riches
 was Fru-te ever taking:
 He was a faithful steward,
 and long of him thereafter men were speaking.

===Chinese===
弗鲁特对希尔达的财物
向来照料得妥帖：
他是一位忠诚的管事，
此后许久人们还在称道他。

===Original===
The high times now were ended,
 and all their leave would take.
 Then ’twas allowed to Hartmut,
 as well his worth bespake,

===Chinese===
盛会至此告终，
众人都要辞行。
这时哈特穆特获得应允——
一如他的品行所昭示——

===Original===
 His peace to gain forever;
 to this Gudrun had brought him.
 Then for their home they started;
 each happier went than he had erst bethought him.

===Chinese===
从此永远赢得他的太平；
这全是古德伦为他玉成。
随后众人启程还乡；
每个人走得都比原先预想的更加欢欣。

===Original===
With friendly love, Queen Hilda
 bade them all farewell;
 With her, Gudrun and Hildeburg
 went, with kind goodwill,

===Chinese===
王后希尔达怀着友善的情谊，
向众人一一作别；
古德伦和希尔德堡怀着
善意的真情随她同行，

===Original===
 Far beyond the castle,
 with all their maids-in-waiting.
 There took they leave of Hartmut,
 when he at last was on his way forth setting.

===Chinese===
带着全体侍女，
直走到城堡之外很远的地方。
就在哈特穆特终于登程上路之际，
她们在那里与他作别。

===Original===
A guard Queen Hilda gave them
 across the land and sea;
 Great was the host that Herwic
 and Ortwin now set free,

===Chinese===
王后希尔达给他们派了一路护卫，
陆路海路全程相送；
赫尔维克和奥特温
此刻开释的那队人马为数众多，

===Original===
 Whom, long held in bondage,
 they now were homeward sending;
 Full a thousand followers
 Hartmut brought to his land when the war was ending.

===Chinese===
这些人久遭囚禁，
如今正被送返家乡；
战事收束时，哈特穆特
带回他国中的随从足有一千名。

===Original===
Everywhere the ladies
 one another kissed.
 Many now were sundered
 who long each other missed,

===Chinese===
贵妇们到处
彼此亲吻道别。
多少人就此分离，
日后长久地彼此思念，

===Original===
 And nevermore thereafter
 might again be meeting.
 The high-bred Ortwin and Herwic
 went with them to the boats that for them were waiting.

===Chinese===
从此以后
再不能重逢相见。
出身高贵的奥特温与赫尔维克
陪他们走到候命的船边。

===Original===
Irold must be their leader,
 while they did homeward fare.
 Then by the king ’twas bidden
 that he the word should bear

===Chinese===
他们归家的一路上，
伊罗尔德须做领队。
随后国王吩咐，
让他把口信捎给

===Original===
 To Horant, lord of Denmark,
 how they the land were leaving:
 Soon Irold to the warriors
 guidance and guard unto their homes was giving.

===Chinese===
丹麦之主霍兰特（Horant），
告知他们即将离境：
不久，伊罗尔德便为武士们
引路护送，直到各人的家门。

===Original===
The time, or late or early,
 in truth I cannot tell,
 When they for their home in Kassian
 did at last set sail.

===Chinese===
那时刻是晚还是早，
说实话我无从奉告，
只知他们为返回卡西安（Kassian）的家园，
终于扬帆启航。

===Original===
 The folk, now faring thither,
 were nought but gladness showing;
 After many sorrows,
 God on them was fullest bliss bestowing.

===Chinese===
这群驶向故乡的人们，
满心只流露着欢喜；
历尽重重忧患之后，
上帝把最圆满的福祉赐给了他们。

===Original===
Irold said to Horant,
 when he reached the Norman land,
 That he by the king was bidden
 homeward to lead the band.

===Chinese===
伊罗尔德抵达诺曼人的土地后，
对霍兰特说，
是国王吩咐他
把这队人马领回故乡。

===Original===
 “To leave to them their kingdom,”
 he answered, “it is fitting,
 They home have come so gladly;
 I, too, to see my land with pain am waiting.”

===Chinese===
「把王国留还给他们，」
他答道，「这是理所应当，
他们回乡回得这样欢欣；
我也正焦心地盼着见到我的国土。」

===Original===
Then they welcomed Hartmut,
 and to him his land did leave;
 But how he swayed his kingdom
 I now no knowledge have.

===Chinese===
随后众人迎回了哈特穆特，
把他的国土交还给他；
至于他后来如何治理王国，
我如今一无所知。

===Original===
 With all his friends, then Horant
 quickly homeward hasted,
 And left the land behind them;
 Denmark they reached, nor many days they wasted.

===Chinese===
霍兰特随即带着他的全体友人
匆匆踏上归途，
把那片国土抛在了身后；
他们抵达丹麦，没有耽搁多少天。

===Original===
There we now will leave them,
 and only this will say:
 That never from a wedding
 homeward took their way

===Chinese===
如今我们就在此搁下他们，
只想再说一句：
从未有一场婚礼，
送走过如此欢欣归乡的

===Original===
 Happier knights and kinsmen
 than now from there were going:
 Only the men of Karadie
 tarried still in the land, their gladness showing.

===Chinese===
骑士与亲族，
胜过此刻从那里离去的人们：
唯有卡拉迪（Karadie）的人马
仍留在那片国土上，喜气洋洋。

## Tale XXXII How They All Went to Their Homes / 第三十二诗章 众人如何各归各家

===Original===
Siegfried, king of Moorland, goes home, taking his bride, Herwic’s sister. Herwic returns to his home with Gudrun, after she has bidden farewell to her mother, Hilda, and to her brother, Ortwin, and Ortrun, his wife.

===Chinese===
摩尔兰（Moorland）之王西格弗里德（Siegfried）携着他的新娘——赫尔维克（Herwic）的妹妹——归返家园。古德伦（Gudrun）辞别母亲希尔达（Hilda）、兄弟奥特温（Ortwin）以及奥特温之妻奥特龙（Ortrun）之后，随赫尔维克回到了他自己的家中。

===Original===
Now with the friendly Hegelings
 none would tarry more.
 Soon on the way to Alzabie
 they Herwic’s sister bore,

===Chinese===
如今，在这友好的黑格林人（Hegelings）中间，
谁也不愿再多作逗留。
很快，在通往阿尔扎比（Alzabie）的路上，
他们载上了赫尔维克的妹妹，

===Original===
 Shouting all for gladness
 that they the maid were bringing;
 While, on their watery pathway,
 with proud and happy hearts, the knights were singing.

===Chinese===
个个欢声高呼，
喜迎他们载来的这位少女；
而骑士们在水路之上，
怀着骄矜畅快的心，一路歌唱。

===Original===
Queen Hilda gave, at parting,
 a kind farewell to them.
 Though rich were Herwic’s followers
 when first to her they came,

===Chinese===
临别之际，王后希尔达
向他们亲切地道了别。
赫尔维克的随从们
初来她这里时虽已豪富，

===Original===
 Yet gifts she gave full many
 to them, when homeward faring.
 When one is seen so lavish,
 the name of a wonder-worker is he rightly bearing.

===Chinese===
然而当众人踏上归途，
她仍给了他们丰厚的馈赠。
世人见一人如此豪奢，
他便确然配得上行奇迹者之名。

===Original===
Gudrun then spake to her mother:
 “May blessings on you be!
 Mourn not for the fallen;
 by both my lord and me

===Chinese===
古德伦随即对母亲说道：
「愿福泽降临于您！
不要为殒身的人哀恸；
我夫君与我两人

===Original===
 Shall love to you be given:
 no more you need be feeling
 Heaviness or sorrow;
 your woes shall Herwic’s kindness now be healing.”

===Chinese===
都将向您奉上敬爱：
您从此再不必体味
沉重与哀愁；
您的伤痛，如今自有赫尔维克的仁爱来医治。」

===Original===
To her Queen Hilda answered:
 “Dearest daughter mine,
 If you would make me happy,
 henceforth must friends of thine

===Chinese===
王后希尔达回答她道：
「我最亲爱的女儿，
你若想让我欢喜，
从今以后，你的友人们

===Original===
 Come to the land of the Hegeling
 thrice to see me yearly;
 Else must I greatly sorrow,
 and never can bear the loss I feel so nearly.”

===Chinese===
须得来到黑格林人的国土，
一年三次前来看我；
不然我必将深深哀伤，
再也承受不住这切身所感的失落。」

===Original===
Then said Gudrun, the high-born:
 “Mother, it shall be done.”
 At once, with smiles and weeping,
 and glances backward thrown,

===Chinese===
出身高贵的古德伦随即说道：
「母亲，这事定会办到。」
她当即含着笑与泪，
一步一回首，

===Original===
 She left the castle of Matelan,
 with many a friendly maiden.
 Her sorrows now were ended:
 nought before did ever maids so gladden.

===Chinese===
离开了马特兰（Matelan）城堡，
众多亲昵的少女相随。
她的哀苦至此终结：
从前的岁月里，从未有什么让少女们这般欢欣。

===Original===
Hither men brought horses,
 saddled and fitly bred,
 To bear her hence with her maidens;
 these their keepers led:

===Chinese===
人们牵来了马匹，
鞍鞯齐备、血统精良，
好载她偕众少女登程；
这些马由马夫们牵引：

===Original===
 Light were all the breastplates,
 and golden-red each bridle.
 I ween the ladies wished not
 longer far from home to linger idle.

===Chinese===
所有的胸甲都轻巧，
每条辔头都金红锃亮。
我料想这些女眷们
不愿再远离家门，闲荡耽搁。

===Original===
Many, with hair down-flowing,
 and decked with gold, rode there;
 Methinks from tears and sorrow
 none could then forbear,

===Chinese===
许多少女长发披垂，
周身金饰，骑行在那里；
我想，此时任是谁人
都难忍住泪水与悲伤，

===Original===
 Who must at last from Ortrun
 and from her maids be parted.
 Should Ortrun be unhappy,
 Gudrun would then be sad and heavy-hearted.

===Chinese===
因为人们终究要同奥特龙
和她的少女们别离。
奥特龙若是不幸，
古德伦便会哀伤而心情沉重。

===Original===
Ortrun, betrothed to Ortwin,
 then her thanks did give
 To fair Gudrun, the queenly,
 that she had granted leave

===Chinese===
已与奥特温订婚的奥特龙
随即献上她的谢意，
献给美丽而王后般的古德伦，
感谢她慨然允准

===Original===
 To hold the Norman kingdom
 to Hartmut, her knightly brother:
 “Gudrun, may God reward you!
 my cares are gone, I ne’er shall know another.”

===Chinese===
让诺曼人的王国
归她那骑士般的兄弟哈特穆特（Hartmut）执掌：
「古德伦，愿上帝报偿你！
我的忧烦已消，从此再不会另生忧烦。」

===Original===
To her mother Hilda, also,
 Ortrun her thanks did say,
 That she in Ortland’s kingdom
 the crown should wear one day,

===Chinese===
奥特龙也向母亲希尔达
道出了她的谢忱，
因为她将在奥特兰（Ortland）的王国里
终有一日戴上王冠，

===Original===
 Together with King Ortwin,
 and there be called his lady.
 Then said to her Queen Hilda
 that she to grant her this was ever ready.

===Chinese===
与奥特温王同享尊荣，
在那里被尊称为他的夫人。
王后希尔达随即对她说，
这件事她向来乐于应允。

===Original===
Ortwin then and Herwic
 each to the other swore,
 With strong and steady friendship,
 that they forevermore

===Chinese===
奥特温与赫尔维克
随即彼此立誓，
凭着坚贞不渝的友谊，
誓愿他们从今往后

===Original===
 Would sway with right and honor
 the lands to them belonging,
 And ever would be earnest
 to seize and slay whoe’er was either wronging.

===Chinese===
都以公道与荣耀
治理各自拥有的疆土，
而且将永远尽心竭力，
捕杀任何冒犯二人中任一人之徒。
